[p. 001]

ṛjuvimalā-pañcikā

dravyāṇām
 | atraivamākṣipati-yadi vṛttena saha saṃbandho’sti tadā’sau
kimiti na kīrtitaḥ | atha na tadā’sminneva sthāne kimityadhikāralakṣaṇasyārambha
ityanenābhiprāyeṇāha---atha vṛttena saha saṃbandhakathanaṃ kimityatra tu na
kriyate
 | sarvatra kṛtasya sambandhakathanasyātrākaraṇe heturvarṇanīya ityarthaḥ | uttaraṃ---
sambandhābhāvāditi brūmaḥ
 | kathaṃ tarhyasminneva sthāne adhikāralakṣaṇārambhaḥ ?
ata āha---pañcalakṣaṇāpekṣo hyadhikāraḥ
 pañcānāṃ pramāṇādīnāṃ lakṣaṇamapekṣate
adhikāraḥcintāprārambhāya na punaravagataya iti | āśayamabuddhvā codayati---pañcalakṣa-
ṇāpekṣe hyapekṣā | evaṃ tarhi pañcāpi kīrtanīyāni
 | pramāṇabhedaviniyo-
gopādānakramalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttaṃ idānīmadhikāralakṣaṇaṃ vartayiṣyāmaḥ ityevaṃ pañcāpi
kīrtanīyāni | pariharati---na kīrtanīyāni
 | evaṃ tarhi yasmāttairapi pratyekaṃ
saṃbandho nopapadyate | viniyogopādānakramāstāvat adhikārāpekṣā eva1 | grāhaka-
1. pekṣaiva - mā.

[p. 002]

pūrvako viniyogaḥ parārthe neṣyate | tathā sādhikārasya vidherutpattiviniyoga-
prayogākṣepaḥ | adhikārākṣepastu siddhe sati adhikāralakṣaṇasaṃbandhe bhavati iti
vakṣyāmaḥ | kramaḥ punaḥ prayuktyuttarakālabhāvī | adhikārapūrvakaḥ śāstrabhedo’pi
nādhikāraṃ prati hetuḥ | abhinne’pi śāstrārthe śabdato’dhikārāvagateḥ | nanvabhinne’pi
śāstrārthe’nuṣṭhānasāmarthyābhāvādadhikāro nopapadyate | satyam | tathāpyadhikārapūrvakaḥ
śāstrabhedo’pi lakṣaṇasaṃbandhaśarīrāvagatiḥ śabdata eva bhavati | yadi paraṃ śāstrabhedaṃ
vinā na nirvahati anuṣṭhānaparyantatvādadhikārasyeti nirvāhe hetuḥ śāstrabhedaḥ adhi-
kārasyāvagame tu śabda eva hetuḥ | tathā pramāṇalakṣaṇamapi nādhikāraṃ prati,
sāpekṣe’pi hetuśabde ‘devadatta gāmānaya’
 ityādiṣvadhikāradarśanāt | ataḥ pratyekaṃ
pañcabhirapi saṃbandho nopapadyate | nanvevaṃ samuditaiḥ saṃbandho bhaviṣyati tatrāha---
na ca samudāyo nāma kaścidarthāntarabhūto vidyate
 | yadi samudāyo’rthāntara-
bhūto vidyate tadā pratyekamasaṃbandhe’pi kadācit samudāyenāpi saṃbandhaḥ saṃbhāvyeta |
na caitadasti | tasmāt pañcānāmapi kīrtanaṃ nopapadyate | yadi pañcabhirapi sambandho
nāsti kathaṃ tarhi pañcalakṣaṇāpekṣo hyadhikāra ityucyate | uttaraṃ---paraspa-
rāpekṣeṣu pañcasu parisamāpteṣu adhikārasyāvasara iti pañcalakṣaṇāpekṣo hyadhikāra
ityucyate | na punarhetutaḥ
 | asyāyamarthaḥ---adhītavedasya prathamaṃ tāvat vedārthaji-
jñāsaivotpadyate | ataḥ codanālakṣaṇo’rtho dharma ityanena codanaikapramāṇakaḥ kāryarūpo
vedārtha ityukte punariyamapekṣā jāyate-kathamanapekṣā codanā pramāṇaṃ ? yena codanaika-
lakṣaṇa[ḥ/?/]kāryarūpo vedārtho bhavet iti | tataścānapekṣatvasādhakaṃ pramāṇalakṣaṇamārabdha-
vyam | ukte ca pramāṇalakṣaṇe punariyamapekṣā jāyate-kiṃ tatpramāṇaṃ bhinnamutābhinna-
miti | ataḥ dvitīyādhyāyena śāstrabhedaḥ pratipādyate | pratipādite ca śāstrabhede
śāstrārthā api bhinnāḥ | tatreyamapekṣā punarāpatati-kimete śāstrārthāḥ anyonya-
saṃbaddhā uta asaṃbaddhā iti tatra śeṣalakṣaṇena saṃbandhaḥ pratipādyate | pratipādite ca
śrutyādinibandhane śāstrārthānāṃ parasparasaṃbandhe punariyamapekṣopajāyate---kiṃ śrutyādi-

[p. 003]

bhireva parasparasaṃbandhaḥ utāparamapyasti kāraṇamasya ? tatropādānalakṣaṇaṃ aparamapi
kāraṇamiti tatpratipādanāya upādānalakṣaṇamārabdhavyam | aupādānikaviniyoga-
cintāyāṃ ca prakṛtāyāṃ aparamapyaupādānikaṃ sarvameva cintitam | upādānanibandha-
nāyāṃ ca prayuktau cintitāyāṃ kathamiyaṃ nirvartate nirvahati ekatvātkartuḥ anekatvācca
padārthānāṃ ityapekṣodaye kramato nirṇīyate | tatra ca kaḥ krama ādartavya ityapekṣāyāṃ
kramalakṣaṇāmārabdhavyam | evaṃ parasparāpekṣatvāt pañcānāṃ na tāvadadhikāraḥ eṣāmavasare
nipatitumarhati | kintu eṣu samāpteṣu kiṃ kramaparyante upadeśārthe1 adhikārī vidyate
neti jijñāsodeti | tataścādhikāralakṣaṇamārabdhavyam | evaṃ cintāsiddhayarthaṃ pañca-
lakṣaṇānyadhikāro’pekṣate yāvattāni na cintyante tāvadadhikāracintāvakāśa eva
nāstīti | na punarhetubhāvena pañcalakṣaṇānyadhikāro’pekṣate | tena saṃbandhābhāvāt
saṃbandho nocyate | anyatrāvasarālābhāt idānīṃ cāvasaralābhāt asminneva sthāne’dhi-
kāralakṣaṇamārabhyata ityākṣepapratisamādhānāya hetuhetumadbhāvenāpekṣā nāsti kintvava-
saralābhāyāpekṣeti darśayati---na kramaparyanta upadeśo’dhikārasya hetuḥ |
kintu kramaparyanta upadeśe kaścidadhikārī vidyate neti cintā
 | nanūpadeśa-
balenaivādhikāro’vagamyate upadeśasyādhikārāvinābhāvitvāt ityanenāśayena pṛcchati-
kimanadhikāro’pyupadeśo vidyate
 yena bhavānnopadeśasya adhikāraṃ prati hetu-
tāmāha | uttaraṃ-bāḍhaṃ vidyate devadattaḥ kāṣṭhaiḥ sthālyāmodanaṃ pacatītyevamādiṣu
pramāṇāntarapramitārthaviṣayeṣu
 pramāṇāntarapramitārthaḥ viṣayo yeṣāṃ vākyānāṃ teṣūpadeśo
niradhikāro’pi vidyate | na tatra kaścinniyujyate
 | kathaṃ na niyujyata ityatrāha---
pramāṇāntarapramitaviṣayatvādeva
 | yasmāt pramāṇāntarapramita eṣāṃ viṣayaḥ tasmā-
dityarthaḥ | bhavatu pramāṇāntarapramitaviṣayatā tathāpi kimityadhikāraśūnyatā |
1. mā. apadeśārthe |

[p. 004]

adhikāraśūnyatābhāvāt kathamanuṣṭhānasiddhirityatrāha---lakṣaṇāntareṇa hi pratītasya
svarūpasya śakteśca kathanamātrameva dravyādīnāṃ vartamānāpadeśaiḥ kriyate
 |
arthādarthitvenopadeśasiddhiḥ
 upadeśārthamanutiṣṭhati | nanu yadyarthādarthitvenopadeśārtha-
siddhiḥ kimarthaṃ tarhyadhikāra iha pratipādyata ityatrāha-iha punarupadeśādeva śaktijñānaṃ
iti viśeṣaḥ
 | upadeśavyatirekeṇātra śaktijñānaṃ nāstīti sādhikāra upadeśo
varṇanīyaḥ | anyathā śaktijñānābhāvenānuṣṭhānāsiddhiḥ | tasmādupadeśo’dhikāra-
vyabhicāritatvāt nāyamadhikāramavagamayatīti hetutvābhāvānnāsti saṃbandhaḥ | tasmānna
saṃbandhābhidhānaṃ
 bhāṣyakārasyeti | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ svargakāma evodāhni/?/yate na
punaḥ grāmakāmādayo’pīti
 | sarveṣāmapyadhikārapradarśanārthaṃ sarvāṇyevodāharaṇīyānīti
bhāvaḥ | pariharati---kiṃ vā sarvamevodāharaṇīyaṃ
 yenaivaṃ pṛcchasi ? nyāya-
siddhayarthaṃ tu ekamevodāharaṇīyaṃ
 ekasminnapyudāharaṇe nyāyaḥ pradarśayituṃ śakyate
yataḥ | pratyutodāharaṇāntaramakiñcitkaram | ataḥ ekamevodāharaṇīyamiti | ākṣeptā
āha---ucyate, satyamevaṃ atra punaḥ svargaśabdavācyanirūpaṇamukhena pūrvottara-
pakṣau nirūpayati bhāṣyakāraḥ
 | tatra ekenāpyudāhṛtena sarvodāharaṇeṣu nyāya-
vyutpattirbhavati yadi sarvavyāpī vicāro bhavati | iha tu svargaśabdasya prītimad-
dravyavacanatayā pūrvapakṣo nirūpyate | rāddhāntaśca prītimātravacanatayā | na ca
grāmakāmādiṣvetatsambhavati
 prītyapagamepi grāme grāmaśabdaprayogāt
 | asyārthaḥ-
prītyapagame dravye svargaśabdasyāprayogāt prītivacanaḥ svargaśabda ityuktvā rāddhāntaḥ
kṛtaḥ | na cāyaṃ prakāro grāmakāme saṃbhavati ; prītiṃ vināpi grāme grāmaśabdaprayogāt
ato dravyamātravacano grāmaśabdaḥ iti rāddhānto na labhyata iti | grāmakāmādiṣvadhi
-

[p. 005]

karaṇāntaraṃ vā vaktavyam
 yadyadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇamabhipretam | sādhanābhidhānaṃ vā
dravyavacanatvāt grāmādiśabdānāṃ
 yāgādisādhanatābhidhānamaṅgīkaraṇīyam | kartṛ-
viśeṣaṇābhidhāyakatvaṃ vā āśrayaṇīyam | atrocyate sarvāṇyevodāharaṇīyāni |
pradarśanārthaṃ tu svargakāmapadamudāhṛtam
 | tulyanyāyatayā sarvāṇyevodāharaṇāni |
vastutaḥ svargakāmapadaṃ tu pradarśanārthamudāhṛtam | nanu ca kāmyamānābhidhāna-
nibandhanapūrvottarapakṣau na grāmakāmādiṣu saṃbhavata ityuktam
 | kāmyamāno yaḥ
svargaḥ tadabhidhānanibandhanaṃ yadi svargaśabdo dravyavacanaḥ tadā pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ yadā prītivacanaḥ
tadā rāddhāntaḥ iti grāmakāmādiṣu na saṃbhavata ityuktaṃ dravyavacanatvāt grāmakāmādīnām |
samarthayitā āha---tadayuktam
 | ākṣeptā āha---kathamayuktam
 | samarthayitā
āha-itthamayuktam | kāmyamānatayā svargaśabdaḥ kāmyeṣu vartata ityuktam
 |
asyārthaḥ-prītivacanaḥ svargaśabda iti yaduktaṃ tenedamuktaṃ bhavati-kāmyamānatayā kāma-
nāvyāpyatayā kāmanāṃ prati pradhānatayā svargaśabdaḥ kāmyeṣu sraka्candanādiṣu vartata
iti | prītirhi kāmyamānā bhavati | tena prītivacanatayā kāmyamānābhidhāyakatā
uktā bhavati | evaṃ grāmakāmādiṣvapi draṣṭavyam
 | grāmakāmādiṣvapi kāmyamāna-
tayaiva grāmaśabdo vartate | na hyakāmyaṃ kāmanāyuktaṃ bhavatīti
 grāmakāma ityatra
grāmaḥ kāmenecchayā yuktaḥ saṃbaddhaḥ pratīyate | sa ca kāmyamānameva bhavitumarhati |
yata eva kāmyamānaṃ kāmanāyuktaṃ bhavati ata eva deśavacanatāmabhyupagamya svarga-
śabdasya tathāpi kāmanāyogāt
 prītimaddravyavacana evāyaṃ svargaśabda iti
kṛtaḥ pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ-pūrvapakṣavādinā prītimaddravyavacanaḥ svargaśabda ityukte
coditaṃ-upamānāt svargaśabdaḥ prītimaddravye prayujyata iti | punaśca samāhitaḥ

[p. 006]

mukhyārthāntarābhāvāt nopamānena prayujyata iti | punaścoditaḥ-deśaviśeṣe śītoṣṇā-
dirahite svargaśabdaḥ prasiddha iti | parihṛtaṃ ca tādṛśe deśe pramāṇābhāvāt na tatra
svargaśabdo mukhya iti | punaścoktaṃ-yadyapi tādṛśopadeśo’sti tathāpi prītimada्dra-
vyavacanaḥ svargaśabdo varteta | tatra cāyaṃ hetuḥ kāmanāyogo’tra svargaśabdārthasyāva-
gamyate | na ca prītirahitasya kāmanāyogo’sti | rāddhānte’pi na hyasatyāṃ prītau
kāmanā vartate dravya iti prītiparameva
 svargaśabdasya prayogaṃ darśitavān na
punardeśo nirākṛtaḥ
 | satyāṃ prītau dravye kāmanā pravartate ityanayaiva
yuktayā rāddhānte’pi svargaśabdasya prītiparameva prayogaṃ darśitavān bhāṣyakāraḥ na
punardaśo nirākṛtaḥ | yadi hi svargaśabdasyābhidhānabalenaiva prītivacanatā syāt
tadā prayatnena deśavacanatāṃ nirākṛtya prītivacanatā sādhitā bhavet | na ca
deśanirākaraṇe yatnaḥ kṛtaḥ | tasmāt kāmanāsaṃbandhabalenaiva prītivaca/?/natā
svargaśabdasya bhāṣyakārasyābhimatā | akṣarārtho’pyevaṃ varṇanīyaḥ---yasyaiva prīti-
sādhanasya dravyasya vaktā svargaśabdaḥ tadeva dravyaṃ yadā na prīteḥ sādhanaṃ bhavati
tadā svargaśabdena nābhidhīyata ityarthaḥ | evaṃ ca sati yathā svargaśabdaḥ kāmanāyogena
prītiparaḥ | evaṃ grāmakāmādayo’pīti prītiviṣayatā
 prītiḥ viṣayaḥ
pratipādyo yeṣāṃ te prītiviṣayāḥ teṣāṃ bhāvaḥ prītiviṣayatā tayā prayuktāḥ
 kāmanā-
yogāt na puna
ryāgaṃ prati sādhanatayetyuktaṃ bhavati
 rāddhāntitā | tena sarvatra
tulyo nyāyaḥ | yata eva kāmyamānaviṣayo’yaṃ vicāraḥ na svargamātraviṣayaḥ ata
eva svargaḥ sādhyo yāgo vetyupanyasya punaḥ kāmaḥ sādhya iti darśayati

kāmyata iti kāmaḥ sāmānyena kāmyamātraṃ sādhyata iti vadāmaḥ | anyathā hi svargaḥ
sādhyata ityevocyeta | yadā sarvāṇyevātrodāharaṇāni tadā kiṃ prītiḥ svargaḥ uta
dravyaṃ ityanena granthena | etaduktaṃ bhavati---kiṃ kāmaviṣayatayā svargaśabdaḥ

[p. 007]

prayuktaḥ uta kāmyamānatayā kartṛviśeṣaṇamiti
 | kiṃ kāmyamānaviṣayatayā
kāmyamānāvasthāpratipādakatayā prayuktaḥ svargaśabdaḥ uta kāmyamānatayā kāmanāṃ
kartṛviśeṣaṇabhūtāṃ viśiṃṣan kartṛviśeṣaṇamiti | tatra prītiḥ kāmyamānā-
vasthā’bhipretā | kāmyamānatayā kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ dravyamabhimatam | nanu ca dravyapakṣe
svargasyopasarjanatāmāha bhāṣyakāraḥ na kartṛviśeṣaṇa[tva]mityatrāha---tadā hi
 kartṛ-
viśeṣaṇatve hi kāme kāmyaṃ svargādirupasarjanaṃ bhavati
 | tenopasarjanatāprati-
pādanena kartṛviśeṣaṇataivoktā | yadyupasarjanatve kartṛviśeṣaṇatā tarhi siddhānto na
labhyata ityanenābhiprāyeṇa codayati---nanu ca svargakāma ityatropasarjanībhūta
eva kāmaḥ
 kāmyasvargādiḥ pratīyate
 | pariharati---etadevātra vicāryate |
kiṃ yathābhidhānaṃ
 viniyogasaṃbandhaḥ uta yathāviniyogamabhidhānamiti
 |
kiṃ svargakāmābhidhānānusāreṇa svargasya kāmanāyāśca saṃbandhaḥ, abhidhānaṃ hyetadanya-
padārthaparaṃ tadanusāreṇa ca saṃbandhe guṇataiva svargasya | uta yādṛśaḥ kāmyakāma-
nayorviniyogaḥ parasparasaṃbandhaḥ pratīyate tadanurodhenābhidhānamiti | kāmyakāmanayorhi
saṃbandhe kāmyaḥ pradhānabhūta iti | tenopasarjanataivātra vicāryate | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī
yathābhidhānaṃ viniyogamicchan upasarjanatāmāha | rāddhāntī tu yathāsaṃbandhamabhi[da]dhānaḥ
anupasarjanatāmāheti | atra codayati---kiṃ yathāviniyogamabhidhāne bahuvrī-
hirayaṃ na bhavati
 | ayamabhiprāyaḥ-yathāviniyogepyabhidhāne bahuvrīhirevāyaṃ bahuvrīhiścedu-
pasarjanatā duṣpariharā | yadi hi yathāviniyogapakṣe bahuvrīhirayaṃ na syāt tadaivānu-
pasarjanatā syāt | na caidasti | pariharati---kena voktaṃ nāyaṃ bahuvrīhiriti
 |
codayati---yadi bahuvrīhirupasarjanaṃ tarhi kāmaḥ kāmyaḥ padārthāntara
bhūtapuruṣa-
paratvādasya samāsasya | yata eva cārthāntarabhūtapuruṣaparatā asya samāsasya ata

[p. 008]

evākhyātagatayā kartṛsaṅkhyayā’sya saṃbandhaḥ yajeta svargakāmaḥ yajeran
ṛddhikāmāḥ
 ityekavacanabahuvacanadarśanāt | yadā copasarjanaṃ kāmyaṃ evaṃ ca sati
saṃśayo’nupapannaḥ
 siddhāntavacanavyaktyabhāvāt | na ca bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ
svārthepi bahuvrīhismaraṇamasti
 yena bahuvrīhipakṣe’pi kāmyameva pradhānaṃ bhavet |
samādhātā āha---ucyate
 | vayamapi svārtha eva na bahuvrīhiṃ vadāmaḥ
 |
kintu parārtho’yaṃ bahuvrīhiḥ, uta svārtha iti
 | samasyapadapratipādyābhyāṃ
padārthābhyāṃ paro yaḥ puruṣaḥ tadartho’yaṃ bahuvrīhiḥ, uta samasyamānapadapratipādya-
kāmyārthaḥ iti vadāmaḥ | ākṣeptā āha---kathaṃ punaḥ parārthaḥ kathaṃ vā svārthaḥ
 |
samādhātā āha---siddhārthastu parārtho bhavati
 | yadi svargakāmādiśabdaḥ siddhārtho
bhavati tadā parārtho bhavati | atha sādhyārthaḥ tadā svārtho
 bhavati | sa evāha-
tatredānīṃ na vidmaḥ
 | kiṃ siddhārthasyopādānaṃ
 samuccāraṇaṃ parārthatayā
 uta
sādhyatayopādīyata iti | ākṣeptā āha---anyapadārthaśca sādhyārthaśceti nanu
vipratiṣiddham
 | sādhyaṃ hi anyārthaṃ na bhavati | tataścānyapadārthaśca sādhyārthaśceti
parasparaṃ viruddham | samādhātā āha---nātra vipratiṣedhaḥ yataḥ sādhyopyasau

svargādiḥ anyārtha eva puruṣārtha eva | tena sādhyārthatā anyārthatā cāvipratiṣiddhā |
uktaṃ ca
 ṛṣiṇā ‘phalaṃ ca puruṣārthaṃtvāt’
 iti phalasya puruṣārthatvam| tasmādupa-
pannārtho’yaṃ
 bhāṣyakārīyaḥ saṃśayaḥ---kiṃ prītiḥ svargaḥ uta dravyāmiti |

[p. 009]

nanu caivamapi saṃśayo nopapadyate
 yasmāt kartṛviśeṣaṇamidaṃ pratīyate
ākhyātagatakartṛsaṅkhyāsaṃyogāt
 - yajeta svargakāma iti | nanu cādhikāri-
viśeṣaṇe’pi svargasiddhāvastyeva svargakāmasya kartṛtvaṃ tenākhyātasaṅkhyāsaṃyogo bhaviṣyatī-
tyatrāha---tadākhyātakartari ca saṅkhyāṃ smarati bhagavān pāṇiniḥ iti
 |
asyārthaḥ-yasminnākhyāte saṅkhyā śrūyate tasya ākhyātasya yaḥkartā tatra saṅkhyāṃ
smarati pāṇiniriti | svarge ca sādhye svargasiddhau svargakāmasya kartṛtvaṃ na
yāgasiddhau
 tena yāgagatayā saṅkhyayā yogo na syāt yadi yāge kartā svargakāmo na
syāt | tena yāgaṃ pratyeva svargakāmaḥ kartṛtayopadiśyate | kasmāt pāṇiniḥ (na)
smaratītyatrāha---na cānyatra vyāpṛtasya ākhyātāntaragatayā saṅkhyayā
yogo bhavati - yathā pacatīti pathataḥ
 | samarthaḥ padavidhiriti pāṇineḥ smaraṇam |
svārthasaṃbandhaśca sāmarthyam | anyatra vyāpṛtasya ca ākhyātāntaragatayā saṅkhyayāṃ
saṃbandho na bhavati, yathā - pacatītyanayā saṅkhyayā pathato yogo na bhavati |
tenākhyātāntaraga[ta](tā) saṅkhya[yā] anyatra vyāpṛtasya saṃbandho nāstīti pāṇine-
ranusmaraṇam | iha tu yajeta svargakāma ityekavākyatā’vagamyate
 | paraṃ na
parasparasaṃbandhaṃ vinā ekavākyatā bhavati | ataḥ kartṛviśeṣaṇam | kasmāt siddhārtha
evāyaṃ bahuvrīhiḥ na sādhyārthaḥ ?
 ākṣeptaiva yuktyantaramāha - etadevātra yuktam

siddhārthaṃ nāmeti | na hi sādhyārthaṃ nāma prātipadikaṃ bhavatīti bhāvārthādhi-
karaṇe
 bhāṣyakāreṇoktam | ato yāgasyai
vākhyatopāttasya kartavyatātrāvasīyate
 |
bhāvārthatvāt
 sādhyārthatvāt yajatiśabdasya
 | yuktyantaraṃ cāha---na ca
sādhyavādino’pi yāgena sahaikavākyatā nāsti
 | sādhyamapi svargaṃ yo vādī

[p. 010]

vadati tasyāpi mate yāgena saha svargakāmasyaikavākyatā na nāsti | api tvastyeva |

asti cettarhyekasminneva vākye sādhyadvayāvagatirna
 yujyate |
atra kaściccodayati---nanu yathā vrīhīn prokṣati iti saṃskāre vrīhigate
sādhye’pi prokṣaṇena
 ākhyātopāttena sādhyabhūtenāsyaikavākyatā yathā
tadvat ihāpi bhaviṣyatīti
 | ākṣeptā pariharati---maivaṃ vocaḥ tatra hi
kārakatayā vrīhayaḥ saṃbaddhāḥ saṃskārabhājo bhavantīti
 | asyārthaḥ- yuktaṃ
‘vrīhīn prokṣati’
 ityatra | yasmāttatra prathamāvasthāyāṃ kārakatayā vrīhayaḥ prokṣaṇena
saṃbadhyante | tacca prokṣaṇaṃ paścāddrīhiṣu saṃskāramārabhate | tena bhāvisaṃskārayogi-
kārakatayā vrīhayaḥ prokṣaṇaṃ prati dvitīyāntena śabdenopādīyante | iha punaḥ na
yāgasvargayoḥ
 dvayorapi sādhyayoḥ anyataḥ
 pramāṇāntarāt kriyākārakasaṃbandha-
siddhiḥ
 yataḥ parasparasaṃbandhaḥ syāt | nanu mā nāma pramāṇāntarāt kriyākārakasaṃbandhaḥ
setsyati vedenaiva yūpaṃ chinattīti vadatā avasāyiṣyata ityatrāha---nāpi yūpavadi-
haivāvasīyate
 | pṛcchati---kimiti
 | nāvasīyate | yadyutpādyasyāpi kāraka-
yogo bhavati
 | utpādyasyāpi yūpasya cchede yadi kārakatayā saṃbandho bhavati, tadā
kimiti vede tadvadeva kriyākārakarasaṃbandho nāvasīyata ityarthaḥ | uttaraṃ---yadi bhavati

utpādyasyāpi kārakayogaḥ bhavatu na punarutpādyasyā
sadbhaūtasya kriyāṃ prati	 kārakatā-
yogo bhavati | codayitā āha---kathaṃ tarhi yūpaṃ chinattīti
 | asati yūpasya
kārakatve dvitīyāntaṃ padaṃ dvitīyāyāḥ kārakavibhaktitvāt | samādhātā āha---
chedanena saṃbaddhaṃ yūpaṃ karotītyarthaḥ
 yūpaṃ chinattītyasya vākyasya | etaduktaṃ bhavati-
1. mavatveva-mā.

[p. 011]

atrāpi khadirādereva chedanasya saṃbandhaḥ | ta eva tu khadirādayaḥ chedanena kāraka-
tayā saṃbandhāt chedanena saṃskriyante | saṃskārayoginaśca yūpaśabdenocyante chedanena
yūpīkriyanta iti | yadi tatrāpi pramāṇāntarāvagata eva kriyākārakabhāvaḥ kathaṃ tarhi
codanālakṣaṇo yūpa iti prasiddhiḥ tatrāha---alaukikatvāt
 loke yūpaśabdavācyatayā
saṃskṛtānāṃ khadirādīnāmaviditatvāt codanālakṣaṇo yūpa ityucyate
 | abhi-
prāyamajānan pṛcchati---saṃbandhaśca kriyayā alaukikaśceti kimidaṃ ?
 kriyā-
kārakasaṃbandhasya sarvasya laukikatvāt | uttaraṃ---yūpaśabdābhidheyatayā’laukikatvam
 |
vaidikaśabdaprayogavaśenaiva yūpaśabdasya svārthe vyutpatteḥ | na punaḥ chedanasaṃbandhitayā’-
laukikatvam | ato’trāpi kārakasyaiva khadirādeḥ chedanena kiñcitkriyate
 |
ato vrīhīn prokṣatītyanena tulyatā | svargasya punaḥ
 sādhyabhūtasya yajyarthaṃ prati
kārakatvamevālaukikam
 | upasaṃharati---tasmāt vrīhīn prokṣatītivat
saṃbandho nopapadyate yajeta svargakāma
 iti | yathā vrīhīn prokṣatīti saṃbandha
upapadyate na tathā yajeta svargakāma ityatropapadyata ityarthaḥ | na ca saṃbandhāntaraṃ
śabdavit smarati | smaran vā pramāṇābhāvāt
 nirmūlasmaraṇānupapatteḥ vihanyeta

iti | siddhāntavacanavyaktyabhāvena saṃśayamākṣipyopasaṃharati---tasmātsaṃśayamatra
devāḥ kariṣyanti
 yadi kartavyaṃ maṃsyante |

samādhatte---atrābhidhīyate | syādetadevaṃ yadi pramāṇacatuṣṭayātagamya
eva saṃbandhaḥ syāt
 | tena pramāṇacatuṣṭayenāvagatamapi śabda eva kiṃ saṃbandhāntaraṃ
bodhayati netīti yukta eva saṃśayaḥ |

[p. 012]

ākṣeptā āha---uktaṃ śabdasya prāmāṇyamanapekṣatvāditi
 | tena tatprāmāṇya-
balenaiva saṃbandhāntaraṃ setsyatīti nādhikaraṇamārabdhavyam | samādhātā āha---satyamuktaṃ

prāmāṇyam | tadevātrāvasīdati
 | kathaṃ ? uttaraṃ---adhikārasiddhau prayogasya

anuṣṭhānasya siddhiḥ prayogasiddhau
 satyāṃ upadeśasiddhiḥ
 | kāryarūpaḥ upadiṣṭo
niyogārthaḥ sa siddhyatīti | anyathā anuṣṭhātaryasati kāryameva tanna syāt | tataścā-
kāryaṃ kāryatayā pratipādayan śabdaḥ pramāṇaṃ na syādityasminnadhikaraṇe adhikāralakṣaṇa-
saṃbandha[ndhā]sādhake sati prāmāṇyamevāvasīdatīti prāmāṇyanirvāhārthamidamadhikaraṇamārabdha-
vyam | ākṣeptā āha---evaṃ tarhi pramāṇalakṣaṇamevedaṃ prāptaṃ
 | na lakṣaṇāntaram |
samādhātā āha---bhavati ca lakṣaṇāntaramapi sat pramāṇalakṣaṇam pañcalakṣaṇa-
śeṣatvāt adhikārasya
 | viniyogopādānakramalakṣaṇaśeṣatvaṃ utpādakatayā darśitam |
veda prāmāṇyatadbheda lakṣaṇaśeṣatvaṃ tu tannirvāhakatayā, adhikāraṃ vinā pramāṇatvānirvāhāt |
asati tannirvāhe tadbhedo dūrāpeta iti | yadi pramāṇalakṣaṇamidaṃ bhavati kathaṃ tarhi
lakṣaṇāntaramidaṃ vyapadiśyate
 ? samādhatte---evaṃ vyapadiśyate satyapyadhi-
kāre pramāṇākṣepaḥ pramāṇalakṣaṇe samāhitaḥ
 | ihedānīṃ sa evādhikāraḥ upa-
padyate neti vicāryate
 | etaduktaṃ bhavati - sāpekṣatayā śabdasyāprāmāṇyaṃ pramāṇalakṣaṇe
parihṛtam | iha tu sthitepyanapekṣatve’dhikārābhāvenākṣipya pratisamādhīyate | yadi
ca yāvadaprāmāṇyenaiva śabdasya adhikāra ākṣipyeta tadā’’kṣepahetubhūtāprāmāṇyanirā-
karaṇenedaṃ na lakṣaṇāntaraṃ vyapadiśyeta | nacaitadevaṃ, tadāha---na cāprāmāṇyenaivādhi-
kārākṣepaḥ kiṃ tarhi ? adhikārasaṃbandhānavagamāt
 | ākṣeptā āha---nanu
cānavagamo’pyaprāmāṇyameva
 | tenānavagamenāpyākṣepaḥ aprāmāṇyenaiva | samādhātā

[p. 013]

āha---satyameva
manavagamo’dhikāre’prāmāṇyam na punaḥ śabdārthamātre
| tenādhi-
kārivyatiriktakāryamātrāvagamakatvāt asti prāmāṇyamiti nāprāmāṇyenākṣepaḥ | na
caiṃva tarhi yadyanavagame’pi prāmāṇyaṃ tadā kathaṃ pramāṇalakṣaṇe[’] prāmāṇyaṃ nirākṛta-
mityatrāha---pramāṇalakṣaṇe punaḥ śabdo’rthaṃ na spṛśatītyayamartho nirākṛtaḥ |

sarvathaivārthāsparśitve tadaprāmāṇyam | viśiṣṭārthāpratipādakatve tu arthāntarapratipādaka-
tayāpi prāmāṇyamupapadyata eva |

tasmādupapanno lakṣaṇāntaravyapadeśaḥ | ataḥ pañcabhirlakṣaṇaiḥ kārya-
mātrāvagatiruktā na punarasyedaṃ kāryamityuktam | asatyāṃ caitasyāmavagatau
kāryamātrāvayatiḥ tathāpi bhavantī na tathā bhavati anuṣṭhānābhāvāt
 |
yāvadidaṃ mama kāryamiti nāvabudhyate na tāvat kaścit pravartate | ato yadyapi kārya-
metadityavagatirbhavati tathāpyasau kāryasyānuṣṭhānaṃ vinā kāryatvānupapatteḥ avagamyamāna-
śūnyatayā anavagatireva syāt | tasmādasyedaṃ kāryamiti siddhaye’dhikāralakṣaṇa-
mārabdhavyam | nanu caturthe’satyapyadhikāre phalakalpanā uktā sa svargaḥ
syāt
 sarvān pratyaviśiṣṭatvāditi | asyārthaḥ-kimartho’yamadhikāraḥ pratipādyate ?
yāvatā vināpyadhikāreṇa phalata evānuṣṭhānaṃ bhaviṣyati yathā loke
 | adhikāraṃ vinā
phalāvagatireva na syāditi cet, na, vināpyadhikāreṇa viśvajidādiṣu caturthe phala-
kalpanāyā uktatvāt iti | pariharati---satyaṃ phalakalpanenoktā | adhikāra-
saṃbhave tu sā adhikāranibandhanaiva
 na pramāṇāntaranibandhanā | nanvadhikāra
eva tatra tatra na śrūyate kathaṃ tannibandhanā kalpanetyatrāha---aśrute’pyadhikāro

[p. 014]

vidyate ityuktam
 tatraiva | tenāśrutepyadhikāre’nvitābhighānabalena klṛpte paścāttanni-
bandhanā phalakalpanā | asaṃbhave punaradhikārasya kasyāśrutasya kalpanā
 |
yadi punaradhikāra eva na saṃbhavet kasyāśrutasya kalpanā bhavet | adhikārasambhave hi
adhikāriviśeṣaṇatayā phalakalpanā bhavet | tadasaṃbhave tu kasya kalpanā bhavati |
nahi kāryaṃ phalamapekṣate yena phalatayā kalyeta svargaḥ | tasmādanyato’nuṣṭhāna[nā]siddhe-
radhikāro vaktavyaḥ | evaṃ tāvacchabdasya pramāṇyāt śabdaikagamyasaṃbandhaḥ asti nāstīti
saṃśayaḥ samarthitaḥ | punarākṣepamāha---nanu cātra bhavanto vaiyākaraṇāḥ kecit
kartāramevādhikṛtaṃ manyante | yo yasmin kartṛtayā’vagamyate sa tatrādhikṛta
iti vadantaḥ | nādhikāro nāma kaścidarthāntarabhūtaḥ
 kartṛtvāt | saca kartā
kriyayā saṃbadhyate iti manyante
 | tena kriyākārakasaṃbandha eva parasparasaṃbandhaḥ
nāparaḥ saṃbandhaḥ śabdenāpi pratipādyate | brāhmaṇādayaśca kartṛtayā’vagamyante
smṛteḥ | dvijātīnāmadhyayanamijyādānaṃ iti
 | manvādismṛtirapi brāhmaṇādīn
kartṛtvena pratipādayati nārthāntaratayā ‘dvijātīnāmadhyayanaṃ ijyā dānaṃ’
 iti ‘kartṛ-
karmaṇoḥ kṛti’
 iti kartari ṣaṣṭhīsmaraṇāt | kartṝṇāṃ ca phalakalpanā ātmane-
padādibhirliṅgaiḥ
 | kartṝṇāṃ hi phalakalpanā | na punaḥ kartṛvyatiriktānāṃ ‘kartrabhi-
prāye kriyāphale’
 iti smaraṇāt | ayamevāsau bādaripakṣaḥ
 | bādarerapi sammato yaḥ
pakṣa iti | yaduktaṃ tṛtīye---dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣveva saṃbandhāvagamaṃ manyante na
yāgaphalapuruṣeṣviti | evaṃ ca kriyāsaṃbandha upapanno bhavati
 yadi kartā
svargakāmaḥ evaṃ kriyayā saṃbadhyate anyathā hyadhikṛtasya kriyayā saṃbandho durbhaṇaḥ
 |
avagamyate ca saṃbandhaḥ, anyathā hyekavākyatā na syāt | yatassaṃbandhāntaraṃ nāsti tena

[p. 015]

svargakāmādīni padāni kartṛviśeṣaṇānyeva ‘bhinne juhoti’
 itivat
 |
yathā bhedenena darśapūrṇamāsakartā viśeṣyate tathā svargakāmādibhirapi | iyān viśeṣaḥ
homasaṃbandhārthaṃ darśapūrṇamāsakartā bhedanena viśeṣyate | svargakāmanayā tu nānyatra saṃbandha-
yitumiti | codayati---nanu caivaṃ sati
 kartṛniyamavidhānāt kartrantara
bhūtānā-
mṛtvijāmanupraveśo na prāpnoti
 niyamena nivartitatvāt | pariharati---na
prāpnotu nāma | naitāvatā
 kartrantarānanupraveśamātreṇa[ā] saṃbandhaḥ syāt | adṛṣṭa-
pūrvasya kalpanā’’śrayituṃ śakyate | vacanādvā
 ṛtvigvaraṇabharaṇavidhānāt kartranta-
rānupraveśa upapanno bhaviṣyati
 yata eva ca kartṛparāṇyetāni ata eva ca kāraka-
vibhakteranutpattiḥ | tiṅābhidhānāt saṅkhyāyāḥ prathamāyāścotpattiḥ |
anyathāhi
 kartṛvyatiriktasyādhikāriṇaḥ vibhaktyantaraṃ vā mṛgyaṃ śeṣalakṣaṇa-
ṣaṣṭhyā vā bhavitavyaṃ yajeta svargakāmasyeti
 | yuktyantaraṃ cāha---kartṛnirde-
śaścāyaṃ anvayavyatirekābhyāmavagamyate - yajeta svargakāmaḥ ijyate svarga-
kāmena iti
---anabhihitāyāṃ ākhyātena kartṛsaṅkhyāyāṃ tṛtīyānvayāt, abhi-
hitāyāṃ ca vyatirekāt | yadi hi kartari tṛtīyeyaṃ syāt tadaiva yujyate nānyathā
kartaryeveyaṃ tṛtīyeti kartṛnirdeśa evāyaṃ svargakāma iti | anyathāhi kartṛnirdeśo na
syāt | tadāha---bhāvasādhanepi
 svargakāmenejyate ityādau tṛtīyā na syāt
akārakatvāt svargakāmasya | yatpunaridamuktaṃ
 adhikāraṃ kartṛbhābā[va]vyatiriktaṃ

[p. 016]

vinā niṣphalaṃ karma bhavati na ca niṣphale pravartanta iti | tatra brūmaḥ karma-
svābhāvyāt
 karmaṇo’yaṃ svabhāvaḥ yadabhūtaṃ kiṃcidbhāvayatīti | tatra yāgādikamapi
karmāśrutaṃ kimapi bhāvayiṣyati | tacca
 karmaṇāmabhyudayāya
 bhaviṣyatīti abhyudaya-
rūpaṃ kimapi bhaviṣyati abhyudayaḥ(tvaṃ)
 viśiṣṭaśarīrendriyādiprādurbhāvaḥ nityeṣviva
bhavatpakṣe’pi
 [yasya] svargakāmādīnyadhikāriviśeṣaṇāni tasyāpi mate jīvanādhikāre
phalāśravaṇe’pi karmasvābhāvyāt yathā kimapi phalamabhyudayarūpaṃ kalpyate tathehāpi
bhaviṣyati | evaṃ tāvat asatyapi vyatirikte’dhikāre phalataḥ pravṛttirityuktam | idānīṃ
vināpyadhikāraṃ vināpi phalaṃ pravṛttirbhaviṣyatītyāha-kartavyatāvagamācca karmānuṣṭhānam
 |
yaddhi kartavyatayā’vagamyate tatra pravṛttiḥ na punaḥ phalasambandhāt | phalasaṃbandhe

hyaṅgīkriyamāṇe pramāṇāntarāvagamyatā prāpnoti
 vedārthasya phalata eva kartavyatā-
vaṃgamāt | tataścānapekṣatvāditi heturuddhṛta
 unmūlito bhavati sāpekṣatvaprasaṅgāt

pramāṇāntarānavagamyamānārthatayā hi anapekṣatvam pramāṇāntarāvagamyārthatve tu sāpekṣatā
prāpnoti vedārthasya | tataścānapekṣatvaṃ prathamapāde uktam | upasaṃharati---ataḥ karma
kartavyaṃ kartṛbhiriti anadhikāraḥ
 karturevādhikāraḥ | evamākṣipte saṃśaye samādhātā
āha---ucyate, nāyaṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ śreyān svargakāmapadaṃ hyasmin pakṣe yathā na
saṃbadhyate tathopariṣṭādvakṣyāmaḥ
 | anyathānupapadyamānaḥ svargakāmaśabdaḥ saṃbandhāntara
mavagamayatīti bhāvaḥ | punarākṣeptā āha---yadyevaṃ
 yadi svargakāmaśabdo’nyathā nopa-
padyate kathaṃ tarhi saṃśayaḥ- kiṃ dhātvarthaḥ sādhyatayopadiśyate uta kāma iti
 |

[p. 017]

asyārthaḥ---ākhyātapadasaṃbandhāt yāgo’pi kartavyatayā pratibhāti, kāmanāsaṃyogāt
kāmo’pi | tatra kaḥ sādhyatayā saṃbadhyatāmiti sandehaḥ | kāmyata iti kāmaḥ
svargādiḥ | nanu dhātvarthe sādhye
 dūṣaṇamidamuktaṃ svargakāmapadamanyathā

kartṛviśeṣaṇatvena na sambadhyata
 iti | samādhātā āha---na na sambadhyate
, api
tu sambadhyata eva | kutaḥ? aṅgatayā hi ‘samudraṃ manasā dhyāyet’
 itivat kāmanaṃ
kartavya prāpnoti
 | yadi dhātvarthaḥ sādhyo bhavati tadā tadaṅgatayā samudrasya
dhyānavat svargādikāmanāpi kartavyā bhaviṣyati | tena svargakāmaśabdo’pi kāmanāme-
vāṅgatayā samarpayan saṃbadhyata iti | nanvevaṃ satyubhayoḥ kartavyatā uktā bhavati
 |
dhātvarthakāmanayoḥ tataḥ kathaṃ saṃbandha ityarthaḥ | samādhātā āha---na, satyamuktā,
guṇapradhānabhāvastvatra cintyate
 | ekasya guṇatayā kartavyatā, aparasya pradhānatā |
punaścodayati - yadi svargakāmanāpi sādhyā kathaṃ tarhi siddhārthatayā svargakāma-
śabdasya pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | pūrvapakṣo’pi sādhyārthatayaiva
 tathā | bhūtaṃ bhavyāyopa-
diśyata iti ca kathaṃ bravīti
 bhāṣyakāraḥ | samādhātā āha---nāyaṃ doṣaḥ
yasmāt
bhūtamevātropadiśyate | na cet pramāṇāntaraprāptamupādānasidhyarthaṃ kriyate
 |
anyathā upādānāsaṃbhavāt | upadeśe tu bhūtameva
 | tena saṃbandho’pyupadiśyate |
sādhyasādhanabhāvena siddhārthatayā ca pūrvapakṣo’pyanupapannaḥ | bhūtavyapadeśaśca | yata
evamatra sandeho na---kiṃ yāgaḥ sādhyaḥ uta kāma iti | ata eva caikādaśe
yadadhikaraṇaṃ tadapyapunaruktaṃ nyāyataḥ
 | atra hi yāgaḥ sādhyatayā nopadiśyata
ityuktam || tatra sthite’pi yāgasya sādhyatvenānupadeśe svargakāmādeḥ kartṛviśeṣaṇatvaṃ
1. avyavadhānasambandhata-mā.

[p. 018]

nirākṛtam | tadatra ayaṃ mūlabhūtassandehaḥ---kiṃ svargakāmapadaṃ niyojyasamarpakaṃ
uta neti | tatra yadāhi niyojyaviśeṣaṇaṃ tadā kāmyamānasya sādhyatā svārthatā ca
kāmyamānatayā copādānaṃ yāgasya ca sādhanatā | kāryaviśeṣaṇa pakṣe tu kāmyasiddhayarthatā
parārthatā ca | kāmanāyāḥ sādhanatayopādānaṃ yāgasya ca sādhyateti | evaṃ sthite
viśaye pūrvapakṣavādī bhūtaṃ bhavyāyeti pūrvapakṣitavān | śābdoktābhiśca
yuktibhiḥ
 abhyuccayarūpābhiḥ | asyārthaḥ---ākhyātopāttatayā yāgaḥ sādhyatayā
pratīyate | na ca sādhyena sādhyasya saṃbandho yujyata iti bhūtārthatayaiva svargakāma-
śabdo’nvayaṃ yāti | tenātra kartṛviśeṣaṇataiva | vaiyākaraṇena ca yā yuktaya uktāḥ na

kartṛvyatiriktaḥ kaścinniyojyo nāmāstīti tābhiśca kartṛviśeṣaṇateti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

bhāṣyaṃ vyākhyātuṃ ākṣipati---idamaparaṃ cintyata iti kathaṃ ? svarga-
śabdaḥ kiṃ prītivacanaḥ uta dravyavacana iti
 | asyārthaḥ-yadi yāgassādhyaḥ
tathāpi kathaṃ dravyavacanatā svargaśabdasya | tathā yadi svargaḥ sādhyaḥ tathāpi kathaṃ prīti-
vacanatā | yo hi śabdaḥ svaśaktyā ca yadvacano’vagamyate tadvacana eva yuktaḥ na
punaḥ sādhyatāsādhanatābhyāṃ vyavasthāpyate | tathā dvyaṣṭavarṣādyupanyāsaḥ deśāpa-
lāpo’bhyupagamaśca nātīvopayujyate
 | yadi yāgasya sādhyatayā dravyavacanatā
svargaśabdasya, tadā kiṃ “dvayaṣṭavarṣāḥ striyaḥ svarga”
 ityevamādinā prayogopanyāsena |
tathā deśāpalāpo’pi pūrvapakṣiṇo niṣprayojanaḥ deśasyāpi dravyatvāt dravyavacanatā hi
tasyābhimatā | tathā yadi deśo nirākṛtaḥ tadā abhyupagamaḥ kimartha iti |
samādhatte---nanu coktamasmābhiḥ kiṃ kāmaviṣayatayā svargaśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ uta
dravyaparatayeti
 asyārthaḥ---nātra vācakatā cintyata iti | [kiṃ]tarhi ? prayogaḥ |
tena sādhyatvāsādhyatvavicāraḥ tasya heturbhavati | yadā svargaḥ sādhyaḥ tadā prītipara-
tayā prayogaḥ yadā ca yāgassādhyaḥ tadā dravyaparatayeti prītiśabdaḥ | nacātra kāmya-

[p. 019]

mānāvasthā’bhipretā | yadyevaṃ deśāpalāpe kiṃ prayojanaṃ ?
 dravyaparatayā yadi
prayogaḥ pūrvapakṣavādino’bhimataḥ evaṃ tarhi deśāpalāpe kiṃ prayojanaṃ ? deśasyāpi
dravyatvāt | uttaraṃ---sādhanasiddhiḥ
 prayojanam | kāmanāyā yāgaṃ prati sādhana-
bhūtāyāḥ siddhiḥ prayojanam | sa kila deśo jīvadbhirnopalabhyate | na cānupa-
labdhasyopādānopasarjanatā samudravadbhavati
 | yathā samudrasyopalabdhasya dhyānopa-
sarjanatā saṃbhavati tathā’nupalabdhasya deśasya kāmanopādānopasarjanatā na saṃbhavatīti |
ato dvyaṣṭavarṣādyupanyāsaḥ peśalaḥ
 | tasyopalabdhasyopādānopasarjanatvasaṃbhavādabhyupa-
gamepi deśasya | yadyapi kevalasukhaśravaṇādi
tyanayā yuktyā tathāpyasmatpakṣo
na duṣyati iti pakṣasiddhayarthavacanameva
 | deśasya svargatve kadācidasmākaṃ pakṣo
na siddhayatīti manyase tadapi nāstītyuktaṃ bhavati | tasyāpi kevalasukhaśravaṇārthāpatti-
siddhasya kāmanopādānopasarjanatvasaṃbhavāt | sāmānyāvagame tu vyavahārāsiddheḥ
sukhasādhanatvamātraṃ parigṛhyata ityupasaṃhāraḥ
 | kaściddeśo’stītyetāvatāpyavagamena
kāmanāvyavahārasiddheḥ sukha sādhanamātrameva ca kiṃ[na]gṛhyata ityupasaṃhāraḥ kṛtaḥ |
ataḥ pūrvapakṣavādī yāga evopadeśaṃ manyate
 | yāgaḥ sādhyatayopādīyata ityarthaḥ |
yata eva yāgaḥ sādhyaḥ ata eva ca “bhāvārthāḥ karmaśabdāḥ tebhyaḥ kriyā
pratīyeta”
 iti samarthitaṃ bhavati | anyathā
 cādīnāmapi sādhyārthaḥ syāt tadā
ukto virodhaḥ
 | saṃprati pūrvapakṣasāraṃ niṣkarṣayituṃ codayati---nanu cāsmin
pakṣe yattat kimapi bhaviṣyati tathā bhaviṣyati | na hyakārakādhāraṃ kriyā-
1. mā. tatra

[p. 020]

phalamupapadyate
 | na cādhārabhūtaṃ phalasya karmāravyaṃ kārakamupalabhyate | nanu ca
somaṃ yajatītyevamādiṣu kārakamupalabhāmahe
 | devatā karmabhūtā yāge tadgataṃ phalaṃ
bhaviṣyati | nirākaroti---tadidamayuktam navame nirākṛtatvāt prādhānyasya

yadi yogaṃ prati pradhānabhūtā devatā bhavet tadā syādevam | navame nirākṛtaṃ devatā-
prādhānyaṃ vigrahādinirākaraṇena | itaśca devatāyāḥ prādhānyaṃ nāstītyāha---nanu
anirjñātapuruṣārthasya sākṣāt praṇāḍikayā vā saṃskāro nopapadyata iti
saktvadhikaraṇe uktam
 | sākṣāt praṇāḍikayā vā anirjñātapuruṣārtho’yam | tasya
saṃskāro nopapadyata iti savatvadhikaraṇe darśitam | na ca devatā puruṣārthatayā kena-
cijjñāpitā | nanu ca devatā manuṣyān pālayantīti prasiddhiḥ
 | tena puruṣārtha-
tayā devatā jñāpiteti nirmūlatvāt vaṭayakṣaprasiddhivat nādaraṇīyetyuktaṃ navama
eva | nanu ca deśasyeva yāgopadeśaḥ pramāṇaṃ bhavitumarhati asyāḥ prasiddheḥ
 |
yathā deśaviśeṣasya sukhamātrāyatanasya svargapadābhidheyasya yāgopadeśaḥ tathā devatāyāḥ
manuṣyapālanakartṛtvasyāpi bhaviṣyati | tanmūlā ceyaṃ prasiddhiriti | sa evāha---
avaśyaṃ caitaddeva
tāprādhānyaṃ nityārthamabhyupagamanīyam
 | tatra hi kriyāphalamevābhyu-
dayo’bhyupeyaḥ | pariha rati---naitadupapadyate upadeśo hi yāgaṃ kuryādityetāva-
tyarthe pramāṇaṃ na punaḥ puruṣasyopakārako yāga iti
 |

[p. 021]

codayati---nanu cānuṣṭhānābhāvāt tathāpi bhavatūpadeśaḥ [na] tathā
bhaviṣyatītyuktam | na hyanupakārake kaścitpravartata ityuktam
 | pariharati---
nanu cedamapyuktaṃ kartavyatāvagamāt pravartanīyaṃ nopakārāvagamāt sāpekṣatva-
prasaṅgāt iti
 | yadā ca kartavyatāvagamamātreṇa phalaṃ vināpi pravṛttiḥ | evaṃ ca
nityapratibandhako’pyayamapratibandhaka eva
 | nityādhikāro bhavatā pratibandhako
darśitaḥ sa pratibandhaka eva na bhavati | tatra phalānabhyupagamāt | bandhaka upagraho
yadīti lokaprasiddhiḥ(?) | evamukte ca pūrvapakṣavādī svābhiprāyasāramāviṣkaroti---
yadyevaṃ bhavatu tarhi sarvamevedaṃ nityavadanuṣṭheyaṃ phalamapi tathāpyasmatpakṣaḥ
siddhayatyeva
 yāgaḥ kartavyatayopadiśyate kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ ca svargakāmapadamiti | yato
yāgaḥ kartavyatayopadiśyate ato na kāmaḥ
 kāmyaḥ svargādiḥ sādhyo bhavati
bhūtatayā’vagamāt
 | yadi svargaḥ sādhyo na bhavati kathaṃ tarhi nityaṃ kāmya-
miti yājñikā bhedenopadiśanti
 sarvasya nityatvāt kāmo’syā nimittamiti |
kāmyaṃ godvayaco’saṃkhyāparimāṇāśvāderyat iti yat | samādhatte---nirmūlatvāda-
sadupadeśo bhaviṣyati
 yājñikānām | punarākṣipati---yadi yāgassādhyaḥ evaṃ
tarhi tadevedamāyātaṃ karturadhikāra iti
 | sa evāha---nirākṛtaṃ ca kartra-
bhidhāne svargakāmapadaṃ na saṃbadhyata
 ityekādaśādye | samādhatte---satyaṃ nirākṛtaṃ
nirupādānopasarjanatvaṃ
, nirupādāne kāme upasarjanatvaṃ nirākṛtam | sopādā
-

[p. 022]

nāyāṃ
 tu kāmanāyāṃ upasarjanatvaṃ
 samudravat sthāpitameva | guṇatvenābhi-
dhānāt
 | kāmanāyāḥ karmaṇi guṇatvenābhidhānāt | pṛcchati---kaḥpunarayoḥ

sopādānanirupādānapakṣayorviśeṣaḥ
 | ubhayatrāpi kartṛviśeṣaṇatvāt | uttaraṃ---uktaṃ
sopādānatvaṃ nirupādānatvaṃ ca
 | ekatra kāmanā upādeyā | anyatrānupādeyeti
viśeṣaḥ | praṣṭā āha---satyamuktaṃ pratītau viśeṣaṃ pṛcchāmi | pratītau cet
saṃbandhopagamato viśeṣo’napekṣaḥ kiṃ viśeṣābhidhānena
 | uttaraṃ---satyaṃ yadi
viśeṣānapekṣaḥ saṃbandho’vagamyate | sa tvanupādāne nāvagamyata ityuktam
 |
tasmā dviśeṣo vaktavyaḥ | upasaṃharati-tasmānna kartṛto’dhikṛtatvaṃ nāma pṛthagasti
kiñcit | vaktavyo vā
 kartṛtvādhikṛtatvayorviśeṣaḥ | nanu ca pravartamānaliṅā-
dibhiḥ pravṛttiḥ kriyate yathādarśitaṃ ṇijutpattau kurukurviti
 | asyārthaḥ-pravartamānasya
pravartakalakṣaṇe prayojakavyāpāre ṇijutpadyate | tatra liṅādayaḥ prayujyamānā dṛśyante yathā
kurukurviti yathā | ato liṅādayo’pi pravartamānasyaiva pravṛttiṃ kurvanti | tena vede
pravṛttisiddhayarthaṃ kartṛtvātirekyadhikṛtatvaṃ nāma pṛthak vācyamiti | kurukurviti
saṃbhrame dvirvacanam | yata eva pravartamānasyaiva pravartane liṅādayaḥ ata eva ca vedaivāsau
mayaitatkartavyamiti uyāyaṃ tu na veda iti bhāṣye’pyasti
 | anena sāmānyena
pravṛttaḥ upāyadarśanena viśeṣe pravartata iti darśayan bhāṣyakāraḥ pravṛttapravartane liṅādaya
iti darśayati | darśitaṃ ca ‘abhicaran yajeta’
 iti samāmananti nābhicarita-
vyamiti
 | anenāpi abhicāre tatsādhane ca sāmānyapravṛttau viśeṣe pravartata iti
darśitam | pariharati-na tāvadidaṃ śakyate vaktum
 pravartamānasyaiva pravartane

[p. 023]

liṅādaya iti lakṣaṇāśravaṇāt (lakṣaṇe śravaṇāt) | vidhau hi liṅādayaḥ śra/?/yante |
pravartakaśca vidhiḥ natvidamaparaṃ pravṛttasyāpravṛttasya ceti | prayogavyabhicārācca
 |
prayoge vyabhicāra iti | apravṛttapravartane liṅādiprayogāt | etadeva vivṛṇoti-
lakṣaṇaṃ tāvat pravartamānasyāpravartamānasya veti na viśeṣamācaṣṭe | prayogo’-
pyājñādiṣu apravṛttasyaiva dṛṣṭaḥ | nanu pratipannājñasyaiva liṅādayo bhavanti
 |
asyārthaḥ - yaḥ pratipannājñaḥ yenājñā pratipannā aṅgīkṛtā yadi sa eva taṃ pratyājñāṃ
dadāti | tena sāmānyena pravṛtasya ājñayā pravartana[ā/?/t]pravṛtteḥ pravṛttapravata/?/nājñāmapi
prayogamiti codayati(?) | pariharati---na pravṛttapravartane prayogaḥ āmantraṇādiṣu
vyabhicārāt
 | āmantraṇe hi apravṛttapravartane prayogo dṛṣṭaḥ | ājñāyāmapyapravṛtta-
pravartana eva prayogaviśeṣe’pravṛttasyaiva pravṛttiḥ kriyate
 | nanu sāmānyataḥ
pravṛtta eva viśeṣe pravartate | tatrāha---na ca sāmānyataḥ
 pravṛttiḥ liṅādibhyo’-
vasīyate | viśeṣaniṣṭhatvāt
 śabdārthasya | tena yā pravṛttirviśeṣaviṣayā liṅādibhiḥ
kriyate sā apravṛttasyaiva | yā tu sāmānyataḥ pravṛttā na tatra liṅādervyāpāra iti |
nanu pravṛttimātraṃ liṅādibhiḥ viśeṣaviṣayasaṃbandhe hi viśeṣaniṣṭhateti | tatrāha---
anvitābhidhānaṃ ca padānāmuktaṃ tadbhūtādhikāraṇe
 | tena viśeṣaviṣayatānvitā-
meva pravṛttiṃ liṅādayo vadanti | yatpunaridamuktaṃ ṇijutpattau kurukurviti darśayatīti
atrocyate---na śabdaprayogamātrāt śabdārtho’vagantuṃ śakyate ananyathā-
siddhadarśanaṃ hi śabdārthaniyame
 hetuḥ | vācakatāṃ vinā yasya prayogasya
1. apravartasānasya |

[p. 024]

darśanaṃ nopapadyate sa prayogo vācakatāṃ niyamayati | yasya tu lakṣaṇayā gauṇyā
vā vṛttyā sāmānyavācitayā darśanaṃ tato na vācakatādhyavasānam | pravartakatā-
mātrābhidhāyakatayā ca pravṛttapravartane prayoga upapadyata eveti na tato
vācakatāyā niścayaḥ | kiṃñca sarveṣāmeva kāraṇavatāṃ
 kāryāṇāṃ yadavyabhicāri

tadeva kāraṇamuktamityuktam
 | tena vyabhicāritayā pravṛttapravartanamasya prayogasya na
kāraṇam | yacca yacchabdaprayoge kāraṇaṃ tat tadvācyam | ato na pravṛttapravartanam
liṅādīnāṃ vācyam | nanu caivaṃ sati ājñādīnāmapyanaikāntika eva prayogaḥ

ājñādīnāmapi apravṛttapravartanātmakānāṃ yaḥ prayogasso’pi vyabhicārī | ṇijutpattau
pravṛttapravartanāt | tasmādubhayorvyabhicārāt śabdārthaniyame kāraṇaṃ vaktavyam |
pariharati---idamucyate pravartanaṃ liṅādayo na vyabhicaranti sarvatrāvagateḥ
pravartamāne ca ṇijutpattiviṣaye apravṛtte cājñādau | ato’nvayavyatire-
kābhyāṃ tāvanmātrameva śabdārtha iti śakyate vaktum | yatpunaḥ ‘vedaivāsau’

iti ‘abhicaran yajeta’
 iti ca darśitaṃ tat sthite vyatirikte’dhikāre
kāmasaṃyukteṣu prāptyaprāptivivakṣayoktam | iha tu vyatirikto nāstī-
tyuktam | tasmādadarśanaṃ tat
 | asyārthaḥ---etadadhikaraṇasiddhānte sthite
satyetaducyate kāmasaṃyukteṣu karmasu adhikārāpāditasādhanabhāveṣu phalābhiprāyeṇaiva
sāmānyataḥ pravṛttiḥ prāptā viśeṣatastvaprāptetyuktam | iha tvadhikāra eva kartṛvyati-
rekeṇa nāstītyākṣepaḥ | tenāyaṃ granthaḥ pratipakṣatayopanyasituṃ pūrvapakṣiṇaṃ prati na

[p. 025]

śakya[ḥ/?/](m) | yadi ca pravartamānasyaivāyamupadeśaḥ tadā upadeśārthataiva hīyate
asyedam sādhanamiti
 siddhakarmaśaktisamarpaṇaparatvāt
 vākyasya | kasmāt punarevaṃ-
paratvepyupadeśasyārthavattā nāstītyāha---na sādhanasvarūpe1 śābdaṃ pramāṇamastī-
tyuktam, kiṃ tarhi ? anuṣṭhāna eva
 | uktamidaṃ codanāsūtre kārya evārthe
śabdasya prāmāṇyaṃ vyutpannamiti | vyutpattyapekṣaṃ ca śābdaṃ prāmāṇyamabhyupagantavyamiti
na sādhanarūpe prāmāṇyam | kintvanuṣṭhāna eva | tasmādapravṛttapravatanamupadeśārtha
iti yuktam
 | yata evāpravṛttapravartana eva liṅādīnāmupadeśakatvaṃ ata eva ca
śabdavidaḥ pravartamānasya hetvabhidhānaṃ ṇico manyante | apravṛttasyaiva liṅā-
dīnām
 | pravartamānasya hetoḥ pravartakasya(ā/?/pi) abhidhānaṃ ṇicā kriyate | apra-
vṛttasya pravartakābhidhānaṃ liṅādibhiriti śābdā manyante | anyathā ṇico liṅādīnāṃ
ca tulyamabhidhānaṃ syāt | yato’pravṛttapravartanameva liṅādyarthaḥ tasmāt kartavyatāva-
gatirevānuṣṭhānahetuḥ na punaḥ phalābhilāṣaḥ
 | kartavyatāvagatipūrvaṃ ca phalam |
tathā ca bhāvārthādhikaraṇe kuryādityuktvā svargakāmapadasaṃbandhāt svargaṃ
bhāvayedityuktam | na svargakāmaḥ svargaṃ kuryāditi
 phalāvagamapūrvakaṃ kartavya-
tāvagamo noktaḥ | avaśyaṃ caitadevaṃ
 yathā vināpi kartavyatābhyupagamo bhavatītyabhyupa-
gantavyaṃ, anyathā yāvajjīvādiṣvanadhikāraḥ syāt
 | kartavyatāvagatirna syāt
ityarthaḥ | kutaḥ sādhyāntarābhāvāt
 | yadi kartṛtvātirikto’dhikāro nāsti sarveṣā-
madhikāraśūnyatayā samatvāt prayājādīnāmāgneyādīn pratyaṅgataiva na syāt | tadāha---
1. mā. na sādhanabhūtaṃ

[p. 026]

nanu caivamaṅgasaṃbandho na prāpnoti
 | pariharati---na prāpnotyaṅgasaṃbandhaḥ
yadi śrutyādīni na pramāṇāni
 | nanu śrutyādīnāṃ prāmāṇyamadhikārāyatta-
mityatrāha---na caiṣāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ sādhyāntaraprayuktaṃ
 nityeṣvapi sāṅgopadeśāt |
yatrāpi phalaṃ nāsti nitye tatrāpi aṅgasaṃbandhāya(di) śrutyādītyabhyupagamāt | svargā-
dīnāmaphalatve’pi prayājādyaṅgasaṃbandho bhaviṣyati | etaccāpātamātreṇoktam | paramārthottara-
māha---yadi ca na prāpnoti
 aṅgasaṃbandhaḥ kiṃ kurmaḥ?
 pramāṇānusāriṇo vayaṃ
yadi pramāṇato na yāgasaṃbandho bhavati na bhavatu | uktaṃ cāsmābhiḥ
 bādarimatānu-
vartibhiḥ pṛthak prayojyā eva bhavantu prayājādaya iti | ye punaḥ dṛṣṭārthāḥ

dravyaguṇasaṃskārā niyamāyopadiśyante naivānaṅgaṃ karturadhikāre’pi
 | tadidaṃ
bādarerapi sammatam | pūrvapakṣamupasaṃharati---tasmāddhātvartha evopadeśaḥ na kāme
 |
yāga eva kartavyatāvagame’pi kathaṃ nityeṣu
 pratiṣedheṣu phalaśūnyeṣu anuśāsyā iti
 |
anuśāsyā iti rūpasiddhau yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ “etistu śāsvṛ”
 iti kyap bhavati |
saphale’pi cāpravartamānāḥ kaṃ daṇḍanumarhanti
 ye phalāyopadiśyante na svarga
ityartha(?) | ayaṃ ca kriyāvākyārthapakṣo lokavyavahārānuguṇyāya | ye ca [phalāvagame
satyapi] na pravartante teṣāṃ kartavyam | ataḥ pravṛttyavagatireva pravartane hetuḥ
 |
na phalam | pravṛttyavagame jāte’pi apravartamānāḥ śrutamutsṛjanti kartavyaṃ na
kurvantīti | ataḥ sutarāṃ
 tvatpakṣa eva śāstrasyānanuṣṭhāpakatvam
 | asmatpakṣe

[p. 027]

---nanu ca kartradhikārapakṣe niyogo’narthakaḥ
 | kathamanarthakaḥ ? uttaraṃ---kartā hi
vyāpṛto vyāpṛtān vyāpārayiṣyatīti vā kārakavyapadeśaṃ labhate
 | varta-
mānena bhūtena bhāvinā vā vyāpāreṇa kārakavyapadeśaḥ na punaḥ kālatraye’pi vyāpārā-
bhāve | vyāparanti iti prakṛtya(na)ntaraṃ bhauvādikam na punaḥ pṛṅ vyāyāme iti
taudādikaḥ | tataḥ kimityāha tatra brāhmaṇādayo yāgādiṣu kiṃ vyāpṛtavanta
iti kartṛvyapadeśaṃ labhante | atha vyāparantīti athavā vyāpārayiṣyantīti |
yadi tāvadvayāpṛtavantaḥ kiṃ niyogaḥ kariṣyati | atha vyāparantīti
tathāpyanarthaka eva
 dvayorapi pravṛttatvāt pravṛrttthaṃ ca niyoga ityānarthakyam | atha
vyāpārayiṣyantītyapyabhiprāyaḥ
 | dūṣayati---prāṅ‌niyogādvijñānamidam
kutaḥ ?
 yasya niyogasaṃbandho’sti sa tadvaśāt vyāpārayiṣyatīti jñāyate | yasya
punarniyogasaṃbandho nāsti sa(ha)[na]vyāpāriṣyatītyetacchakyaṃ vijñātum | niyogādeva
bhaviṣyatīdaṃ jñānamiti cet | na tarhi karturniyogaḥ
 niyuktasya kartṛtvaṃ
niyogasaṃbaddhasya kartṛtā prāpnoti tasmāt karturadhikāro durbhaṇaḥ
 | pariharati---
nanu caivaṃ sati sarvakārakasaṃbandhāpalāpaḥ prāpnoti
 | prāṅ‌niyogasaṃbandhāt
tatrāpi kārakatvāyogāta | kārakaśūnyāsu ca kriyāsu kiṃ niyogaḥ
kariṣyati | athānuṣṭheyatayā sarvakārakagrāmamavagamya tasya niyogo

[p. 028]

bhaviṣyatīti kartaryapi tulyam | evaṃ kartṛkartṛkaṃ yat karma tat tenaiva
kartavyamiti kariṣyamāṇābhiprāyeṇa
 ca saṃbandhasiddhiḥ | yo yatra kartṛtvenāvagamyate
tattenaiva kartavyam | anyathā kartṛniyamāvagamabādhaḥ | tataśca kariṣyamāṇābhiprāyeṇa
kartṛtvena saṃbandhaḥ siddhayati | ataḥ kartraradhikārepyanavadyam | avaśyaṃ caitat

yatkartṛkaṃ yatkarma tat tenaiva kartavyamityabhyupagamanīyam
 | siddhāntī kārakāṇāṃ
kārakātirekiṇīṃ daśāṃ nāṅgīkaroti yathā karturadhikāre’pi anavadyarūpāt | teṣāmapi

saṃbandhasiddhayarthaṃ hi yasmānnaivānyaḥ saṃbandho vijñāyate
 viśiṣṭakārakaniyamavatīṃ
niyogasiddhiṃ muktvā
 | etaduktaṃ bhavati---yathā karaṇaviśeṣaniyamāt tadeva karaṇaṃ
tathā kartṛviśeṣaniyamāt sa eva kartā bhaviṣyati | upasaṃharati---tasmādasaṃbandho
vā prāṅ‌niyogādabhyupagamanīyaḥ
 | atha bhaviṣyadavasthāpekṣayā niyoganimittaṃ
kārakāṇāṃ saṃbandhaḥ tadā tannimittaiva
 sā prāgapi
 viniyogakartṛsaṃbandhāvagati-
riti
 | yadi punarekasyāpi kārakasya saṃbandho nābhyupagamyate tadā kārakaśūnyāsu
kriyāsu niyogānupapatteḥ niyogaikaviṣayasya sthitaṃ prāmāṇyamuddharaṇīyam
 | na ca
tadyuktaṃ sthitatvādeva iti | atha kaściccodayati---nanu rājyādiṣu prāgadhi-
kārāt kriyākārakasaṃbandho dṛṣṭaḥ
 | tena kimiti kariṣyamāṇābhiprāyeṇa saṃbandha-
siddhayā abhirucyate | kīdṛśe laukike prayoge ityatrāha---evaṃkartṛke
 rājye |
madhye pṛcchati---kathaṃkartṛke ?
 viddaccharābhijanakartṛke bhavānadhikriyata ityevaṃvidhe
daṇḍanītyādiśāstravartini prayoge viśiṣṭakartṛviṣayaṃ prāguktvā adhikāraḥ kvaciducyata
iti | pariharati-yuktaṃ rājyādiṣvatīteṣu
 puruṣeṣu tadguṇasya
 vidhyādiguṇaśaktīnāṃ
kartṛbhāvadarśanāt
 | asyārthaḥ - rājyādiṣu yukta evāyaṃ prayogaḥ pūrvapuruṣāṇāṃ

[p. 029]

yatkartṛkatvamavagataṃ tadabhiprāyeṇocyate viddaccharābhijanakartṛka iti | tathā saṃprati
na tasya tādṛśe viṣaye kartṛtvapradarśanāyedamucyate bhavānadhikriyata iti | tena yukta-
meva tatra bhūtakartṛpradarśanam | iha punaḥ niyogādṛte puruṣāntaraṃ
 kartṛtvena na
śakyate vaktuṃ
 tatpūrvakatvāt sarvapuruṣāṇāṃ pravṛtteḥ | tena bhaviṣyadabhiprāyeṇa kartṛ-
saṃbandhavarṇanam | daṇḍanītyādiṣu punaḥ darśanāntarapūrvakatvādupadeśasyopapadyetāpi
anubhūtakartṛkepi viṣaye puruṣaviśeṣasyādhikāraḥ
 | kartrantarakartṛke karmaṇi
kartrantarasaṃbandhānupapatteḥ na kartṛpratipādanamupapadyata iti kartṛtvātiriktatā adhikṛta-
syocyate ataḥ karturanyo’dhikṛta iti tatrāha---tatrāpi ca arhatyarthakathana-
mātrameveti kartradhikāra eva; nādhikārāt kartṛtvam
 | asyārthaḥ-viddaccharābhijana-
ityasyārthaḥ yatra rājye viddaccharo’bhijātaḥ kartṛtvamarhati tatra bhavataḥ kartṛtvamiti na
kartrantarāvarodhadoṣaḥ | ataḥ kartaivādhikṛtaḥ | (karturiti) kartradhikāre ca svarga-
kāmitvaṃ kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ na phalaviśeṣaṇamiti siddham
 | evaṃ ca sarvaṃ
sarvasyānuṣṭheyam
 | yadi hi niyojyaviśeṣaṇāni svargakāmādīni padāni syuḥ
tadā bhedenaiva kāmanopādānāt sarvaṃ sarvasyānuṣṭheyaṃ na syāt | yadā tu
kartavyatāvagamamātreṇa kāmanāmaṅgabhūtāmupādāya karma kartavyaṃ tadā sarvaṃ karma sarvasya
puruṣasyānuṣṭheyam tathā bhāgaśo vidyopadeśo’yam | evaṃ ca bhāgaśaḥ bhāgena karmopa-
deśena vidyopadeśo’yaṃ sarvapuruṣasya sarvakarmānuṣṭhānāt kṛtsnasiddhiḥ
 codita-
kṛtsnārthānuṣṭhānasiddhiḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati---sarvasya sarvānuṣṭhānaṃ vadan eka evānuṣṭhā-
teti darśayati | tenātmaikyapradarśanamidaṃ anuṣṭhānopadeśamukhena | tadidamuktaṃ
---

[p. 030]

sarvamidaṃ brahma bhāgaśa upadeśamukhena jñānato’nuṣṭhānataścopadiśyata iti
 | tata-
stattvaṃ prakāśayediti vidhānāt | karmavidhīnāṃ tattvajñāne viniyuktatvāt jñānato
bhāgaśaḥ upadeśamukhenātmaikyamupadiśyate---sarvasya sarvamanuṣṭheyamiti aikātmyamanuṣṭhānata
upadiśyate | yata evāsmāt karmopadeśāt aikātmyamavagamyate ata evodgāraḥ
kartradhikāravādināṃ ‘asatye vartmani sthitvā tatassatyaṃ prakāśayet’
 iti
 |
sarvopadeśānāprayaṃ vidhiriti manyate | tatastattvaṃ prakāśayediti sarvopadeśānāṃ

tattvaprakāśanāyāyaṃ viniyogavidhiḥ, sarvopadeśāstattvaprakāśane viniyuktā ityarthaḥ | tena
tatvaprakāśane viniyuktatvāt karmopadeśapratibhāso’vidyaiva anyaparatvādvākyasya ‘ta ime
satyāḥ kāmā anṛtāpidhānā’
 iti śruteḥ
 | bhāgaśobhāgaśa upadeśo’nṛtamabhi-
pretaṃ tattvaprakāśane viniyuktatvāt | ato’nyaparādvākyāt karmopadeśapratītiranṛtamiti |
tathā ca rāgādinibandhanaśca vyavacchedo
’nṛtamabhipretam satyāḥ kāmādaya iti
śravaṇāt | tasmāt kārakādhikāradarśanāt adhikārāntaradarśanaṃ vyāmohaḥ | anena
prakṛtamupasaṃhṛtam | yata eva ca kartradhikāraḥ ata eva ca dharmaśāstrakārāḥ pathanti-
kāmātmatā na praśasteti
 kāmasiddhiṃ phalasiddhimavaidikīṃ manvānāḥ
 niṣphalatvāt
kāmanāyā(yāṃ) apraśastāṃ(statāṃ) kāmātmatāmāha | kāmyo hi vedādhigamaḥ karma-
yogaśca vaidika
 iti jñāne karmaṇi ca viniyogaṃ manyate
 | yadā aikātmya-
lakṣaṇatattvapratipattiparatayā sarvopadeśā viniyuktāḥ tadā vihitatvāt kāmyo vedādhi-
gamo vedārthādhigamaḥ | yadā ca (na) karmopadeśamātrapara[rā] bhāgaśa upadeśāḥ tadā
vede’pi vihitatvāt karmayogo’pi kāmya eva | “sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tajjalā-

[p. 031]

niti śānta upāsīta ityamumeva kartradhikārapakṣaṃ brāhmaṇavādo dyotayati |

kathaṃ dyotayatītyāha---kāmye hi
 kartavyatayā upadiśyamāne na sarvakartṛsādhya
karma bhavati | bhedena
 adhikāriviśeṣaṇabhūtakāmadarśanāt | ataḥ sarva-
syaikyaṃ na siddhayati |
 sarvasya sarvānuṣṭhānapakṣe sarvo’nuṣṭhātā eka iti siddhayati
sarvasyaikyam | tataśca puruṣa evedaṃ sarvamityādayo mantrārthavādā anupapannā
na syuḥ |
 nanu ca atrāpi pakṣe bhedavādānāṃ kā gatirityāha---medavādāstu māgaśa
eva cedupadeśo yaḥ karmaviśeṣaviṣayaḥ tadāścita iti parihṛtam | ekatvaṃ
tu pāramārthike bhede kimāśritaṃ bhaviṣyati |
 yata eva kartratirekī niyojyo
nāsti ata eva bhagavān pāṇiniḥ pacyādiṣu
 dhātuṣu kriyākārakavyatirekeṇa
na niyogārthe niyojyaṃ nāma karmāntaraṃ smarati | nanu 1yujirapi pacyā-
diṣveva paṭhyate
 tena hi pūrvasyaiva yujeḥ karmasmaraṇāt pāṇininā niyojyo’pi
smṛta eva | pariharati---satyaṃ paṭhyate na punaḥ yujeḥ karma yāgādiṣu karteti |

etaduktaṃ bhavati | pūrvaṃ niyojyasya sataḥ paścāt kartṛtvamiti na smarati | e(ka)ta-
marthamagṛhītvaiva punaścodayati---nanu ca bhavataivedamuktaṃ kartā hi niyujyata iti |

evaṃ ca vadatā tadidamuktaṃ bhavati yāgādiṣu kartā yujeḥ karmeti |
 paraharati---
yadi yujeḥ sādhanaṃ kartṛrūpaṃ niyujyata ityucyate siddho naḥ pakṣaḥ
 yadā kartuḥ
niyojyatvaṃ smarati tadā siddhaḥ eva pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ kiṃ tarhi idamuktaṃ na smarati

1. tipūrvakayujerityarthaḥ---mā.

[p. 032]

bhagavān pāṇiniriti | akārakābhiprāyaṃ taduktam |
 akārakabhūtaṃ niyojyaṃ
na smaratītyarthaḥ | etadeva vivṛṇoti---tatra kāme sādhye pūrvaṃ tāvat niyogaḥ
tataśca niyogāt kartṛtvaṃ
 niyoga ityarthāntarāpattiḥ arthāntare cānavagatastati
rastītyuktam | tadidaṃ kāmināṃ phalāśaṃsanaṃ ākāśe bakabandhana(mivaṃ)-
prabhaṃ kāmināṃ moha ityuktam | tasmāt kartradhikāraḥ sūribhiruktaḥ bhūtaṃ
bhavyāyopadiśyate itya
nayā yuktyā | yata eva kāmopayogena[kāmo’pi niyogena na]
sādhyate ata eva cākhyātabhedopyupapadyate kiñcidguṇakarma kiñcitpradhāna-
karmeti | kāmye punassādhye sarvākhyātānāṃ guṇabhāvāpattiḥ
 phalaṃ prati guṇa-
bhūtatvāt | tataśca tāni dvaidhamiti sūtrārtho’nupapannaḥ syāt | nanu ca nityābhi-
prāyaḥ sūtrārthī bhaviṣyati |
 nityānāṃ phalābhāvāt | na nityābhiprāyaḥ

sūtrārthaḥ | tatrāpi
 nityeṣvapi kāraṇaṃ arthāntarāpattidarśanāt “yaistu dravyaṃ
cikīrṣyata”
 iti guṇataiva prāpnoti |
 asyārthaḥ---yadā kāmādhikāre yāgā guṇa-
bhāvenākhyātenocyante tadā nityādhikārepi guṇabhāvenaivākhyātenābhidhānaṃ kartavyam |
anyathā’nekārthaprasaṅgāt | na ca guṇabhāvo nirviṣayaḥ kārakāṇāṃ viśeṣadarśanena

[p. 033]

tadarthatayā guṇabhāvopapatteḥ | asmatpakṣe punaḥ dravyaparatayā upadeśe guṇabhāvaḥ |
kriyāpare hyupadeśe prādhānyamiti sūktam |
 nanu dravyacikīrṣāmātreṇa sūtrakāro
guṇabhāvamāha na punardravyaparatvenopadeśasya, tatrāha---cikīrṣyata iti copadeśābhi-
prāyeṇa |
 yadeva hi śāstrālocanayā pradhānaṃ pratibhāti tadeva cikīrṣyate nānyaditi |
tena yāgādiṣu kriyāparatve upadeśasya pradhānakarmatā | dravyaparatve tu guṇakarmateti
siddhaṃ dvaividhyam |

atrābhidhīyate | sa nāma kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ bhavati yasya viśeṣaṇa-
bhāva[ve]kāraka upapadyate | iha punaḥ prītyapagame tadeva tu dravyaṃ na svarga-
śabdābhilapanīyam |
 ataḥ[na] prītivacanaḥ svargaśabda iti | kimato yadyevaṃ ?

uttaraṃ---idaṃ tato bhavati---iṣṭaṃ kāmasya viṣaya iti | tataḥ kiṃ ? neṣṭaṃ
paraniṣpattaye bhavati |
 iṣṭarūpā prītiḥ yāganiṣpattyarthā na bhavati | iṣṭārthaṃ hi
sarvam |
 etadeva vivṛṇoti---etaduktaṃ bhavati---iṣṭasaṃyukte hi vākye tadartha-

katayaiva hyanvayo’nyeṣāṃ padārthānām |
 atastathātra vākyārtho varṇayitavyaḥ
yathā iṣṭaniṣpattyarthaḥ itareṣāṃ padārthānāmanvayo bhavati | tatra yadi kartṛviśeṣaṇatā’’-
śrīyate samudravadupasarjanatā svargasya syāt | niyojyaviśeṇatve tu vakṣyamāṇanyāyena
kāmyasiddhiparyantatvānniyojyabhāvasya iṣṭārthe padārthānāṃ [anvayo] bhavatīti niyojya-
viśeṣaṇatā svargasyāśrayaṇīyā | yadidamasmābhiruktaṃ ‘neṣṭaṃ paraniṣpattaye’
 iti
tadidamuktaṃ---prītirūpatvāt kāmasaṃbandhācca iṣṭārthaścānvayo’nyeṣāṃ padārthānāṃ
“karmāṇyapi jaiminiḥ phalārthatvāt”
 “phalaṃ ca puruṣārthatvāt”
 iti |

[p. 034]

iṣṭatva deva phalasya na kratvarthaṃ
 phalamityartha | phalaṃ ca puruṣārthatvādityatra codayati---
nanu ceṣṭaḥ svarga ityuktam
 prītirūpatvāt kāmasambandhācca iṣṭārthaśca nvayo’nyeṣāṃ
padārthānāmityuktam | sa ca puruṣārtha iti vipratiṣiddham |
 pariharati---
apuruṣārthasya vā kathamiṣṭatā
 icchāviṣayo hīṣṭa ityucyate | ātmasaṃbandhinī ca
prītiriṣṭeti nāpuruṣārthasyeṣṭatā | evaṃ tarhi puruṣārthamevedaṃ sarvamityuktaṃ bhavati

neṣṭārthatā | rāddhāntī parituṣṭa āśāste---satyamevaṃ prājño’pyāyuṣmān
bhava |
 punaścodayati iṣṭopanyāsastarhi kimarthaḥ ?
 śāstrasya puruṣārthatvādeva
niyojyaviśeṣaṇatā svargakāmasyocyatāmiti bhāvaḥ | uttaraṃ neṣṭārthatvādṛte
puruṣārthatā sidhyati
 śāstrasya | puruṣārthenaiva śāstreṇa bhavitavyamiti nāsti pramāṇam |
iṣṭatvādeva tu svargasya puruṣārthatā śāstrasya sambhavatīti sārtha eveṣṭopanyāsaḥ
yadidaṃ puruṣasya karmajanyaphalayogitayā karmaṇi śeṣitvam sa cāyamadhikāra
ityucyate |
 īśvaravacano hyadhikṛtaśabdaḥ | asmin grāme ayamadhikṛtaḥ īśvara
iti gamyate | tenaiśvaryamevādhikāraḥ karmaṇi | yata eva ca kasyacit karmaṇyapyaiśvarya-
masti ata eva tulyepi kartṛtve svāmikarmakaravyapadeśaḥ |
 yadi kartṛtvāti-
riktaṃ svāmitvaṃ na syāt | tasmādadhikārādeva svāmivyapa/?/deśaḥ | parārtha karmakāritvācca
karmakaravyapadeśaḥ | atra codayati---nanu ca yāge naiva karmakarassambhavati |
 svatyāga-
rūpatvena yāgasya svayameva karteti na puruṣāntarādyanupraveśassaṃbhavati | pariharati---
na nāma padārthe
 yāgamātre saṃbhavati | śāstrārthe tu
 adhikārasādhanībhūte yāge
anekapadārthopakṛte padārthāntarānuṣṭhānasiddhaye saṃbhavatyeva
 karmakaraḥ | padārthāstu

[p. 035]

sāmarthyenāpi vyavatiṣṭheran |
 kañcideva kaścideva padārthaṃ karoti na sarvaḥ sarvam |
nanu ca bhāvārthāḥ karmaśabdā ityuktam |
 asyārthaḥ-bhāvārthāḥ karmaśabdā ityatra
bhāvārthānāṃ sādhyatvamuktam na ca sādhyadvayamekasmin vākye saṃbadhyata iti | na ca
kāme sādhye yāge kartṛtvamupapadyata iti
 kāmye svargādau sādhye yāge kartṛtvaṃ nopapadyata
iti yāge kartṛtvābhāvāt kartṛsaṃkhṅyāvācinī yajeḥ parā kiñcidvibhaktirna yujyate | kathaṃ
svargaśabde iṣṭavacane grāmādiṣvadhikārasiddhiḥ
 grāmādiśabdānāmiṣṭavacanatvābhāvāt |
anantaraṃ codyaṃ pariharati---uktaṃ kāmaviṣayatayā svargādyuya/?/nyāso na kāma-
viśeṣaṇatayeti |
 etaduktaṃ bhavati-kāmaviṣayavacanatayā svargādīnāṃ siddhānto’bhi-
mataḥ, sacāyaṃ prakāraḥ grāmādiṣvaviśiṣṭaḥ iti | nanu cānvayavyatirekau svarga eva
saṃbhavataḥ |
 pariharati---uktamasmābhiḥ viṣayapradarśanārthāvanvayavyatirekau |

anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ hi prītivacanatayā svargaśabdasya kāmaviṣayatayā svarga ucyata
iti | kāmasaṃyogāttu grāmādīnāṃ viṣayatvasiddhiḥ |
 na hyakāmyaṃ kāmanā-
marhatītyuktam | tasmātteṣvapi siddho’dhikāra iti | dvitīyaṃ codyamanubhāṣate---yadapi
kāmye sādhye yāge kathaṃ kartṛtvamiti |
 pariharati---kena voktaṃ yāge kartṛtva-
miti | yāge tvadhikāraḥ tatsiddhayarthaṃ cārthāt kartṛtvaṃ
 īśvarataiva |
niṣpattimantareṇa kriyāyāḥ phalabhāvo(ve) nopapadyata iti yāgasiddhau puruṣasya kartṛtvam |
yata eva yāgepyarthāt kartṛtvaṃ ata eva saṅkhyāniveśaḥ
 | yadi svargasiddhau kartuḥ
sato yāge’dhikāraḥ tadā karturadhikāra ityucyate | yāge kartussato’dhikāro nāsti |
kintu yāge’dhikṛtasya kartṛtvamiti tena nāsti doṣaḥ | lokavyavahārānusāritayā

[p. 036]

svapakṣe’bhyuccayamāha---loke’pi cānyatrādhikṛto’nyat sādhayati |
 tathā
rājakarmasvadhikṛto’mātyaḥ svārthaṃ sādhayati | tatsiddhayarthaṃ tu rājakarmaṇi
kartṛtvam |
 prathamaṃ codyamanubhāṣya pariharati---yadapi bhāvārthāḥ karmaśabdā iti
yatrādhikāraḥ
 tebhyaḥ phalāvagatirityuktaṃ bhavati |
 asyārthaḥ - yadidaṃ yāgasya
sādhyatvamuktaṃ bhāvārthāḥ karmaśabdā ityanena tenedamuktaṃ bhavati yeṣu yāgeṣu bhāvārtheṣvadhi-
kāraḥ tebhyaḥ phalāvagatiriti | etadeva vivṛṇoti-tena sādhanena svārthamabhinirvarta-
yedityarthaḥ |
 tenādhikārī viṣayabhūtena bhāvārthena sādhanena svārthamabhinirvarta-
yedityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati - bhāvārthaḥ sādhanatayaiva saṃbadhyate | sādhanasya hi
tādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ yat kartṛvyāpāraviṣayo bhavati | yathā paraśuḥ | udyamananipātanalakṣaṇo hi
kartṛvyāpāraḥ paraśuviṣaya eva na sākṣāt dvaidhībhāvaviṣayaḥ | tathāpi dvaidhībhāvassādhyaḥ
sādhanameva paraśuḥ | evamihāpi puruṣaprayatnasādhyau svarganiyogau | na ca tayoḥ
sākṣāt puruṣaprayatnaviṣayatā | kintu yāgādeḥ sādhanasya tathā sati abhūtasvabhāvatvāt
bhāvārthānāmutpattiranena puruṣaprayatnena | na caitāvatā sādhanatvahāniḥ sādhyatvahāniḥ
sādhyatvāpattireva vā, niyogasya phalasya sādhyatvāvagamāt | tena sādhanabhāvāpattyaiva
yāgādeḥ kartṛvyāpāraviṣayatayā sādhyatā bhāvārthādhikaraṇe pradarśitā | kathaṃ punaḥ
anyaviṣayasya kartṛvyāpārasyānyasādhyatā bhavet ? uddiśya pravṛttatvāt | niyogaṃ phalaṃ
coddiśya kartṛtvavyāpāraḥ pravartate | nāntarīyakatayā tu sādhanībhūtabhāvārthaviṣayo
bhavati nāntarīyakakartṛvyāpāraviṣayatvādeva bhāvārthānāṃ anīpsitakartavyatā iṣyate |
yata eva bhāvārthāḥ sādhanatathaiva saṃbadhyante ata eva tṛtīyāsāmānādhikaraṇyāt
ud‌bhidādīnāṃ nāmatā uktā |
 anyathā hi tṛtīyāntānāṃ bhāvārthani(i)ṣṭhatvāna-
vagamāt icchāsāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ(na)syāt | yadi svarga eva sādhyaḥ tarhi kimidamuktaṃ

[p. 037]

nāmnaḥ sakāśāt kartavyatā nāvasīyata iti | kena voktaṃ tadanyathā
,
prātipadikaṃ kartavyatāṃ nāvagamayatyeva | kathaṃ tarhi kāmasya kartavyatā ?

uttaraṃ---iṣṭatvāvagamāt
 iṣṭaviṣayatvādityuktam | nanu yadi niyojyo’sau vākyārthe-
’nvayī syāt tadā iṣṭatvāvagamāt niyojyaviśeṣaṇatayā’nvayaḥ syāt na cāsā-
vastītyāśaṅkya mūlayuktimāha---avaśyaṃ caitadevamabhyupagantavyam |
 kartṛvyati-
rikto niyojyo’vaśyāṅgīkaraṇīyaḥ | na hi kriyākārakasaṃbandhamātrāvagamā-
danuṣṭhānāvagatiḥ | devadattaḥ kāṣṭhaiḥ sthālyāmodanaṃ pacatītyevamādiṣu
 varta-
mānāpadeśeṣu satyapi kriyākārakasaṃbandhe anuṣṭheyamityavagamābhāvāt | nanu ca
liṅādibhyo’nuṣṭhānāvagatirbhaviṣyati |
 devadattaḥ pacet iti liṅādiyukta-
vākyaśravaṇe pravṛttidarśanāt anuṣṭhānāvagamaṃ ca vinā tadasaṃbhavāt liṅādibhyo’nuṣṭhā-
nāvagatirbhaviṣyatīti vijñāyate | kasya liṅādayo’nuṣṭhānaṃ jñāpayanti iti |

asyārthaḥ-yadyapi liṅādayaḥ kāryabodhakatayā anuṣṭhānāvagamaṃ kurvanti iti yaśca
svasaṃbandhitayā’nuṣṭhānamavabudhyate sa pravartate | tena yasyānuṣṭhānaṃ jñāpayanti liṅādayaḥ
so’dhikārī tāvadaṅgīkaraṇīyaḥ | yadarthaṃ hi yatkarma tasya tatra kartavyatāvagatiḥ |
yasya niyojyatvaṃ tasyādhikāritā | tena pūrvaṃ niyojyatvena devadattādīnāṃ saṃbandhaḥ |
paścādadhikāritāyāmanuṣṭhānāvagatiḥ | tato’nuṣṭhānamiti |

nanu ca kartarītyuktam |
 karturanuṣṭhānaṃ liṅādayo’vabodhayantītyuktam |
pṛthak pariharati---ayuktamuktam | itaretarāśrayaprasaṅgāt |
 yasyānuṣṭhānāvagatiḥ
tasya kartṛtā | yasya ca kartṛtvaṃ tasyānuṣṭhānāvagatirityuktam | tathā ca karturni-
yogo’narthaka iti |
 yadi ca kartṛtvaṃ yāvatsampannaṃ kimaparaṃ kāryatvāvagamena |

[p. 038]

itaretarāśrayaṃ vivṛṇoti---yadi hi niyogāt1 kartṛtvaṃ kartṛtve ca niyoga iti
itaretarāśrayāpattiḥ |
 tasmānniyoga eva pūrvamaṅgīkaraṇīyaḥ tato niyogāt
kartṛtvaṃ mantavyam | niyojyāvagatistu anyataḥ evāyamatra |
 niyojyo
nāma kāryasya boddhā kāryaṃ prati boddhaṛtayā svargakāmādaya upādīyante | loke ca
saṃbodhanavibhaktiyogāt boddhaṛtayā’nvayo devadattādīnām | tena lokavyavahārānu-
sāreṇāpi niyojyo vākyārthānvayīti siddham | yadīṣṭasaṃbandhānniyojyaviśeṣaṇa-
samarpakatā evaṃ tarhi yāvajjīvādiṣu kathaṃ ?
 siddhāntī pṛcchati---kathaṃ vā’tra
na bhaviṣyati ?
 pūrvapakṣī tvāha---siddhavadupadeśājjīvanasya
 yadi sādhyatayā
jīvanamupadiśyate tadā sādhyatvāvagamāt tadānuguṇyena niyojyaviśeṣaṇaṃ syāt |
yadā tu siddhavajjīvanamupadiśyeta tadā na niyojyaviśeṇatā | rāddhāntaikadeśī
vadati---tvayaiva tarhyuktaṃ na jīvanaṃ kartṛviśeṣaṇatayā’nveti siddhatvādeveti |

dūṣayati---yadi tāvat siddhatvādeveti niṣpannatvādevetyabhiprāyakaṃ ayuktamidaṃ siddha-
meva hi kārakaṃ viśeṣaṇatayā’nveti na sādhyam |
 atha siddhatvādavagatatvāditi
mataṃ tatrāha---yadi nāma viśeṣaṇaphalamatra na sambhavati na punaranvayo’pi anvayācca
phalāvagatiśśābdī, na phalavato’nvayaḥ sāpekṣatvaprasaṅgāditi |
 yadi phalamava-
gamyānvayo varṇyate tadā phalāvagamāpekṣaṃ vedasyāvabodhakatvamiti sāpekṣatā syāt |
tasmāt kathamatrādhikāro
 jīvanādiṣviti vaktavyam

ayamucyate---yāvajjīvamiti yāvacchabdo nānveti kartṛviśeṣaṇa-

1. tanniyogāt

[p. 039]

pakṣe | kathaṃ ? na yāvajjīvaṃ prayogassaṃbhavati
 phalārthino yāvajjīvaṃ prayogassaṃ-
bhavati | (yadi)vītāyāṃ phalecchāyāmaprayogāt |
 yadi jīvanaṃ kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ bhavati
tadā svargakāmādhikārānupraveśitā, nādhikārāntaram | naṃ ca phalārthitayā yāvajjīvaṃ
prayogassaṃbhavati | yadi (jīvi)hi vītāyāmapi phalecchāyāṃ prayogaḥ syāt tadā
jīvanaṃ prayoge vyāpyeta | na caitadasti | tasmādyathā jāvanavyāptiḥ prayoge (na)syāt
tathā vācyam | nimittabhūte ca jīvane vyāptirupapadyata iti nimittatvameva jīvana-
syābhyupeyam | tacca
 jīvananimittaṃ[ttatvaṃ] adhikṛtasaṃbandha upapadyate |
 adhi-
kārāyattatvādanuṣṭhānasya na kartṛsaṃbandhe nimittatvopapattiḥ | kartṛsaṃbandhe hi yāvade-
voktaṃ yajeteti tāvadeva jīvatā yaṣṭavyamiti |
 tena na nimittatvopapattiḥ |
nanu mābhūnnimittatvaṃ kimetenetyatrāha---yāvacchabdānupapattirdarśitā |
 asati
nimittatve (na)kartṛviśeṣaṇaniyojyaviśeṣaṇasandehe niyojyaviśeṣaṇataiva nyāyyā prāthamyā-
dityāha-na ca prākkartṛtvasaṃyogāt niyojyāparijñāne kartṛtvasiddhirityuktam |

api ca kartṛviśeṣaṇapakṣe ānarthakyāt pakṣāntarāvalambenāpi (na) prayojana-
vattā vaktavyeti nyāyavido manyate |
 tenārthavatī niyojyaviśeṣaṇatā’’śravaṇīyā |
yathā avaśyāśrayaṇīyatayā niyojyaviśeṣaṇatā(yā) svargakāmādīnām | na tarhi
iṣṭatvādadhikārasiddhiḥ |
 rāddhāntī āha---na nāma
 yadyapyavaśyāśrayaṇīyatayā
niyojyaśravaṇaṃ tathāpīṣṭatvaṃ adhikāriviśeṣaṇatve na na hetuḥ, api tu hetureveṣṭatvena | na
hi akriyārthatā akriyārthatvena cādhikāriviśeṣaṇamiti neṣṭam | paraniṣpattaya iti
syāt kriyārthatā | akriyārthatve ca tadarthataiva kriyāyāḥ tadarthā ca kriyā niyojya-
viśeṣaṇatve bhavatīti niyojyaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ niścīyate | upasaṃharati---iti siddho

[p. 040]

naimittike nitye cādhikāraḥ | yatpunaridamuktaṃ 1sādhanatvenetareṣāmiva kāra-
kāṇāṃ kartṛtvasiddhiriti |
 yathā karaṇādīni kārakāṇi sādhanatvenāvagamyante
tathā kartāpi sādhanatvenaivāvagamyate | ataśca kriyāsaṃbandhitayaiva niyojyāva-
gatiriti |
 anubhāṣya dūṣayatiḥ---ayuktamidaṃ, anuṣṭhānato’pi kriyāsaṃbandho-
’vagamyate saṃbandhāntarasyāvidyamānatvāt |
 asyārthaḥ---karturhi kriyāsaṃbandho
’nuṣṭhānanibandhanaḥ | na cānuṣṭhānāvagatiṃ vinā’nuṣṭhānamasti | na cādhikāraṃ
vinā’nuṣṭhānāvagatiḥ karmaṇi | na ca svasaṃbandhikāryabodhaṃ vinā’dhikāraḥ | tena
niyojyatā prāgāśrayaṇīyā | tasmāt yathoktameva nyāyyam |
 iṣṭatvāvagamāt
svargādeḥ, iṣṭārthatayā ca sakalapadārthānvayāt niyojyaviśeṣaṇatā svargakāmāde(ryu)ruktā,
tāmakṣamamāṇa āha---karmaṇi niyujyamānasya
 svargakāmasya kāmasiddhau yāgaḥ
karaṇamiti kasya śabdasyātra nimittabhāvaḥ ?
 asyārthaḥ-lokavyavahārānusāri-
tayā karmaiva kāryatayā liṅādibhiravagamyate | niyojyo’pi ta deva kāryamavabudhyate na
karmaṇyeva niyuktiṃ tathā ca sati karmaṇi niyujyamānasya svargakāmasya kāmasiddhau
kāmyamānaphalasiddhau yāgaḥ karaṇamiti kasya śabdasyātra vyāpāraḥ ? na kasyacidapīti
bhāvaḥ | tena niyojyaviśeṣaṇatve’pi neṣṭārthatayā sakalapadārthānvaya iti na tadbalena
niyojyaviśeṣaṇatayā niścīyate | tadeva prapañcayati-yajeteti yajistāvat
 yāga-
mātramācaṣṭe, na punastasyānyasaṃbandhitām | vibhaktirapi tatrākhyātī yāga-
sādhanaṃ kartāraṃ
 vaiyākaraṇamatyā tadantargatāṃ vā saṅkhyāṃ
 mīmāṃsakamatenācaṣṭe na
punaryāgasyānyārthatāmapi | svargakāmapadamapi ca svargaṃ puruṣasyeṣṭatayā jñāpa-

1. sādhyatvenetareṣāmiva

[p. 041]

yati na punaryāgaṃ prati prādhānyaṃ svargasya kāmasya
 vā ityanādaraḥ | vibha-
ktistu prathamā’vyatiriktārthaiva
 na prādhānyāvabodhikā | jyotiṣṭomādayastu
nāmatvāt yāgābhidhānaparatantrāḥ
 tatsāmānādhikaraṇyena nāmamātrāvagamāt |
na ca yajeteti śabdaḥ yāgaṃ karaṇatvenācaṣṭe ityuktam | tasmānnirmūlamevedaṃ
yāgena svargaṃ sādhayediti
 jñānam | yo’pyatra niyogārthaḥ
 kāryātmakaḥ sa kāme
vā puruṣaṃ niyuṅkte yāge vā
 | kāmo vā kāryatayā’vagamyate yāgo vā |

saṃbandhāvagatiḥ
 |

nanu ceṣṭatvaṃ nānyathopapadyate yadi na yāgāt kāmasiddhirityuktam |

 icchāviṣaya-
tvādeva tadiṣṭaṃ bhavati
 | āśaṅkyati---iṣṭaṃ sādhyatayā’vagamyata iti cet

 vetyavagamyate | sādhyatvepi cānyasādhyasyaiva kāmanā-
yogo na viruddhayate | tasmādvaktavyamatra nimittaṃ yena yāgasya kāraṇatā’vagamyate
varaṃ pūrvapakṣa evāstu

 iti (6-1-3) asādhana-
bhūte kāmino niyojyatayā saṃbandho na syādityarthaḥ | nirnimittatāṃ kāmasya nirāka-
rīti---padamatra nimittamiti śakyate vaktum
 | svargakāmapadameva yāgasya sādhya-
tayāsvargamāha | yajeteti padameva svargasādhanatayā yāgamāha | nanvatraivaṃvidhārthatā na

pratyuktam
 | anvitābhidhāyīni padānyuktāni tenānvayayogyatayā svargādipadānāmevaṃ-
bhūtārthatvamasti | evaṃ tarhi tadbhūtādhikaraṇa evaitatparihṛtam
 | tadbhaūtādhi-
karaṇe hi vṛddhavyahārasiddhārthābhidhāyakatayā upādānamityuktam | tenātrāsiddhā evaṃ-
vidhārthatā nopapadyate iti tenaivedaṃ parihṛtaṃ pratyuktamityasyārthaḥ | samādhatte---asaṃbaddhaiva
padapadārthāvagatirityetāvatparihṛtam
 | asaṃbaddhānyeva padāni padārthāścāsaṃbaddhā
eva ityetāvat tatra parihṛtaṃ bhabati | kuta ityāha---pratibhāsyānyāpohāvāpoddhāra-
pratipakṣatayā tadadhikaraṇam | na punaḥ padaiḥ padārthamātrābhidhānapratipattaye
 |
nimittaśūnyapratibhāsamātraṃ saṃsṛṣṭavākyārthāvagatiriti | tathā anyāpohaviṣayo vikalpa-
mātrametaditi | tathā vidyamānapadārthāvāpoddhāreṇa vyutpannā vākyārthamavagamayanti
ityetāvatā pūrvapakṣaṃ nirākartuṃ tadadhikaraṇam | na punaḥ padairanvayavyatirekānu-
vidhayipadārthamātramabhidhīyate ityevaṃ param | tadanenānvayābhidhānāt anvayānavagati-
rnirākṛtā | tasmādyāgena svargaṃ sādhayediti yuktam
 | ato niyojyaviśeṣaṇatve
iṣṭārtha evānvayo nirvahatīti tadevāśrayaṇīyam | eṣā cātra darśanasthitiḥ---codanāsūtre
kāryaṃ vāvayārtho na siddhamiti sādhyate | tasya tu kāryasya svasaṃbandhitayā boddhā
niyojyo’stīti ṣaṣṭhādye vyutpādyate | kāmini ca niyojye kāmakāryayoḥ sādhyasādhana-
bhāvo’stīti bādaryadhikaraṇavyutpādyamevātrocyate, niyojyāvagamanibandhanatvāt | tacca
kāryaṃ kāmasādhanatvānvayayogyamapūrvaṃ kriyātireki ityapūrvādhikaraṇe sthāpyate | tena ca

iti | kimidaṃ ‘asādhakaṃ tu tādarthyāt’

 iti | asādhakamidaṃ vākyaṃ

syādityarthaḥ | yadi ca yāga eva kartavyatayā codyate tadā niyojyābhāvāt saṃbhūya-
