vaktumityuktam
 | na padavyatireki vākyaṃ na ca kasyacit padajātanimittatā |
tena nimittaśūnyeyamavagatiḥ | pariharati---yata evaikasya na nimittatā’sti ata
eva sarvasyetyucyate
 | parasparānvitābhidhāyitayā sarvāṇyeva padāni asyārthasya
nimittaṃ bhavanti, anyathā’nvitābhidhānānupapatteḥ | yadi sarvapadajātaṃ parasparānvayabodhe
nimittaṃ kathaṃ tarhi vākyādhikaraṇe pratipadanimittabhāva uktaḥ atrāha---pada-
padārthavivecane hi pratipadanimittabhāva ityucyate
 | anviteṣu padārtheṣu
ekaikasya padasya nimittabhāvo vṛddhavyavahāre vyutpannaḥ | tatpratipādanadvāreṇa
vākyārthe punaḥ sarvameva nimittam
 | vākyārtho hi anvitānekapadārthaviśiṣṭaḥ
sacaikasya padasya na śakyaṃ pratipādayitumiti sarvasya tatra nimittabhāva ityuktaṃ tatraiva |
niyoganiyojyasaṃbandhena kiṃ saṃbandhābhidhānaṃ
 saṃbandhapratipādakaṃ śabdaṃ smarati

pāṇiniriti yaduktaṃ tadayuktaṃ śakyate hi ṣaṣṭhīsmaraṇena sarvamidamupasahartum
 śeṣaṃ
karmādibhyaḥ (pratipādakaṃ vyatirekiṇaṃ) vyatirekapratipādakaṃ smaratā sarvamevopasaṃhṛtaṃ
bhagavateti śakyate vaktum | paramārthatastu abhidhānaṃ punaḥ saṃbandhiśabdānāmani-

[p. 045]

yatameva bhagavataḥ
 pāṇineḥ sarvasaṃbandhaśabdo bhagavatā smaryata iti niyamo nāsti
kṛttaddhitasamāseṣvadarśanāt
 | santamapi kṛtaṃ kelimarābhiṃ(?) taddhitamapi yantrādi
samāsamapi adhītapūrvaṃ idaṃ bhagavānna smarati | atassatāmapi śabdānāṃ smaraṇādarśanāt
[a]smaraṇena na śabdābhāvo’rthābhāvo vā vaktuṃ śakyata iti | evaṃ ca niyoga-
siddhayarthatvāt vākyārthasya phalataḥpravṛttirityetat parihṛtaṃ bhavati
 | yadi hi
anyataḥ phalamavagamyeta tadā tadvaśenaivādhikāre siddhe pravṛttiriti niyoganiyojya-
saṃbandho nāvagamyate | yadā tu niyogasiddhiḥ kathaṃ syāditi vākyena vākyārthaḥ
pratipādyate tadā tadanuguṇakalpaneti na phalataḥ prathamapravṛttiḥ | yadi phalaṃ niyoga-
labhyameva kathaṃ tarhi phalaṃ na vidhīyata iti tantre vyavahāraḥ
 | uttaraṃ---
niyogārthatve’vagate nimittacintāyāṃ
 padārtho hi vākyārthapratipattau nimittam |
tena taccintāyāṃ idamanyataḥ prāptaṃ idamanyata eveti kalpanā, na punaḥ vākyārthe
kiñcit prāptaṃ nāma
 niyogānvitatayā na kiñcit prāptaṃ nāma dadhnā juhotītyatra
pradarśitam
 | ekagranthenāha---padavākyaviṣayamaviviktavijñānamatrākulīkaroti
mandadhiyaḥ
 | padaṃ vākyaṃ ca ye viviktaṃ na jānanti teṣāṃ mandadhiyāmeṣā ākulatā
kathaṃ tadeva prāptamaprāptaṃ ceti | ye ca punaḥ padavākye vivikte jānanti te padārthā-
vasthāyāṃ prāptāyāṃ vākyāvasthāyāṃ cāprāptatāṃ niścinvantyeva | ekagranthenāha---na padaṃ
nāma vākye kiñcidasti na padārthāḥ kecana vākyārthe
 | vākyāvasthāyāṃ padaṃ
nāma nāsti | vākyāvasthāyāṃ padārthā nāma kecana na santi | kutaḥ ? viśeṣāva-
gatirhi vākyārthaḥ
 | arthaviśeṣo’vagamyamāno vākyārthaḥ tadavagatihetuśca vākyam
 |
ato viśeṣāvagamāvasthāyāmavagamyamānatayā vākyārthe padārtho nāsti | viśeṣāvagama-
1. sandhātuṃ

[p. 046]

daśāyāṃ cāvagamakaṃ padaṃ nāsti | tena vākyārthe sarvamevāprāptaṃ padārthavibhāgaṃ darśayan
idamāha---nimittakalpanāyāṃ tu padaṃ padārthāśceti vyapadeśaḥ
 | kaścideva varṇa-
samudāyaḥ kañcidarthamanvayitayā’’cakṣāṇo vākyārthe nimittībhavati | tena tadabhi-
prāyeṇa padapadārthavyapadeśaḥ | tena tāmāśritya prāptatayā yadi vākyārthadaśāyāṃ padārthā
nāma na santi evaṃ tarhiṃ padapadārthajñānamanṛtamevetyuktaṃ bhavati śābdairiveti
 |
pariharati---na nimittatā’nṛtamiti badāmaḥ | kintvavagamyamānāvasthāyāṃ

viveko nāsti vākyārthe avagamakatayā ca vākye
 |

yata eva padapadārthā nimittatayā santi ata evoktaṃ
 padebhya eva hi padārtha-
pratyayaḥ padārthebhyo vākyārthasya
 | prameyatvādekasya pratītyavacchitannaḥ pratyaya iti
nimittanaimittikabhedāśrayam
 | padāni svārthānanvitān vadanti vākyārthe nimittatāṃ
yānti iti nimittanaimittikabhedāśrayameva taduktaṃ, na punaḥ pramitau bhedaḥ ekasya
vākyārthasya prameyatvāt ekasya pratītyavacchinnasya pramāpakatvāt | śābdaistu nimitta-
mapyapahnutamiti viśeṣaḥ
 | kasmāt ? teṣāṃ hi mate pratyastamitāvayavavibhāgaṃ vākyaṃ
vācakam | mīmāṃsakamate tu padānyevārthadvāreṇa vākyārthamavagamayantītyuktaṃ tadbhūtā-
dhikaraṇe | tadidaṃ vākyapadīyamityuktam
 | vaiyākaraṇamate hi vākyameva kevalaṃ,
padaṃ nāma nāsti | tena tadbhūtādhikaraṇameva vākyapadīyamiti tatraivoktam | vākyārtha-
mātṛkāyāṃ
 cāsmābhiriti |

atra codayati---yadi niyogo’nuṣṭhānākṣepakatayā’vagabhyate tadā niyojyaṃ
vinā’nuṣṭhānānupapatteḥ niyojya āśrīyate | na ca niyogo’nuṣṭhānākṣepakaḥ niyoga-
śabdāvagamyamānasya tadāha---bhāva
 .... .... .... to liṅādi | abhidheyasyāpi
yā’nuṣṭhānākṣepakatāvagatiḥ sā kālpanikī | anenābhiprāyeṇāha---kiṃ nimittapūrvasya

[p. 047]

yajeḥ vidhyartho vācyaḥ iti
 na tāvadvācya iti śakyate vaktum | vācyaścetkimi-
tyanuṣṭhānaṃ na pratipādayati | pariharati---asti kiñcidvācyam
 | kiṃ tadvācyaṃ ?
svarūpato vācyo na pramāṇata iti
 viyogavaśena nidhyarthaḥ svarūpato vācyaḥ
na pramāṇataḥ | dharmamātraṃ niyogaśabdaḥ[bdena] ucyate | anuṣṭhānapramāṇabhāvāliṅgitastu
gharmī liṅādibhiḥ | kutaḥ punarayaṃ vibhāgo’vagamyate ata evetyatrāha---kāryāva-

gamyatvāt [yatra]pramāṇakāryamanuṣṭhānarūpaṃ avagamyane tatra pramāṇato’bhidhānaṃ, yatra tu na,
tatra svarūpato’bhidhānamiti vibhāgaḥ | yadi pramāṇabhāvo nāsti kathaṃ tarhi kṛdantenābhi-
dhānamiti tatrāha---abhidhānasya padārthamātraniṣṭhatvāt
 | padārthamātratayā svarūpa-
mātreṇābhidhānopapatteḥ na svarūpeṇa liṅādibhirabhidhānam | tasmālliṅādibhireva
pramāṇato’bhidhānaṃ na kṛdantairiti niścīyate | tathā vyavahāradarśanāt vyava-
hārapūrvakatvācca padārthāvagateḥ
 liṅādiyuktavākye’nuṣṭhānadarśanāt kṛdantaśravaṇe
cādarśanāditi | ataḥ kalpanāpakṣo’pi pratyuktaḥ
 | uktena nyāyena kalpanārthe
pramāṇābhāvāt | yadi ṣaṣṭhyā smṛtyupasaṃhāraḥ kathaṃ tarhi svargakāmo yajeta iti
prathamā
 prātipadikārthamātranimittā prathamā niyojyasyātiriktatayā’tra na prātipadikārtha-
mātratā | ataḥ śeṣatvāt ṣaṣṭhī vā syāt aśravaṇaṃ vā vibhakteḥ
 yadi ṣaṣṭhyapi na
yuktā | ucyate | naiva niyogāpekṣeyaṃ prathamā, kiṃ tarhi kārakāpekṣā
 |
niyogāpekṣāyāṃ (vi)niyojyaḥ prātipadikārthātirikta iti prathamā na syāt kartṛbhāvā-
pekṣāyāṃ cākhyātenaiva karturabhihitatvāt śuddhaḥ prātipadikārtha iti prathamopapattiḥ |

[p. 048]

nanu ca
 niyogāpekṣāyāṃ hi niyojyapūrvakatvāt kriyākārakasaṃbandhasya
tatsaṃbandhinyaiva vibhaktayā bhavitavyam
 | prathamopanipātiniyojyamātrāpekṣayā
nibhaktyā bhavitavyam , na paścādbhāvikārakāpekṣayā | satyamāha bhavān
 niyojyaḥ
prathamabhāvīti kintu na liṅvācye niyoge vibhaktissaṃbhavati
 | nanu ṣaṣṭhī
śeṣatvalakṣaṇā saṃbhavati tatrāha---saṃbandhamātrāpekṣiṇī ṣaṣṭhī na vyāpāraviśeṣā-
pekṣiṇī
 | asyārthaḥ - kriyākārakagarbhaḥ śeṣasaṃbandhaḥ | tatra kriyākārakabhāvaṃ
tiraskṛtya tad‌dvārā pariniṣpanne saṃbandhe ṣaṣṭhī prayujyate | na punarvyāpāraviśeṣasaṃbandhe
tasyāśeṣatvāt karmādirūpatvāt | liṅādibhiśca viśeṣo’vagamyate
 niyojyaniyo-
ktṛbhāvaḥ | yadi niyojyatayā svargakāmasyāvagatiḥ tarhi
 karmatvāt dvitīyā bhavatu
 |
na, dhātunā anupāttattvāt niyogavyāpārasya
 | niyuktilakṣaṇo[ṇā]hi dvitīyā
na bhavati satyapi(na)[ca] vyāpāravyāpyatve | kuta ityāha-dhātūpāttavyāpāre hi
dvitīyādayaḥ
 | tadeva kuta ityāha---bhāvāpekṣitvāt kārakābhidhānasya
 |
kārake hi dvitīyādayaḥ | bhāvāpekṣayā ca kārakāṇyabhihitāni vaiyākaraṇaiḥ | tathāpi
kimityāha---dhātūpāttasya bhāvatāmicchanti
 | dhātvarthādeva kuta ityāha---
kevalaḥ śuddho bhāva ityabhidhīyate
 iti vadantaḥ | tenādhātvartharūpatvānniyukteḥ na
bhāvarūpatvam | abhāvarūpatve ca na kārakatvam | akārakatve ca na dvitīyeti |
tasmāt paścādbhāvyapi kartṛbhāvo’tra vibhaktyutpatteḥ hetuḥ | prātipadikārtha-
mātratayaiva niyogasiddhiḥ
[ddheḥ] | vibhaktiṃ vināpi svargakāmaprātipadikārthaparyālo-
canayaivoktena nyāyena niyogasaṃbandhasiddhiḥ svargakāmādīnām | yata eva prātipadi-
kārthamātratayaiva niyogasiddhiḥ ata eva cābhihite karturekatve na dvitīyāprasaṅgaḥ
 |
yadi kartā nābhihitaḥ syāt tadā paratvāt tṛtīyayaiva dvitīyā bādhitā satī na bhavet |
abhihite kartari tṛtīyāyāmasaṃbhavantyāṃ yā dvitīyā bhavati tatreyamaparā yuktiḥ | prāti-

[p. 049]

padikamātravalenāpi karmatvasiddhiriti | ata eva ceti cakāro hetvantarasamuccaye |
ata eva lakāreṇābhidhānasiddhiśca | yata eva niyojyatayā svargakāmaḥ svargakāmaprāti-
padikaparyālocanayā pratibhāti ata eva lakāreṇābhidhānasiddhiśca | anyathā dhātureva
kāryamabhidadhat niyogamabhidadhyāt lokavyavahārānusāreṇa | svargakāme hi phala-
paryante niyojye tadanvayayogyatayā dhātvarthātiriktaṃ kāryaṃ lakāreṇābhidhīyata iti tatra
tatroktam | yadi hi svargakāmaprātipadikaparyālocanayā tasya niyojyatvaṃ na pratibhāsate
tadā niyojya āśrīyamāṇo’pi lokavyavahārānusārikriyākāryatvānurūpo jīvati navā
na phalaparyanta āśrīyate | ka[tata]ścāpūrvakāryābhidhāyikā[tā] liṅāderna syāt iti
darśanasthitiranusaraṇīyā |

nanu svargasiddheḥ yāgātmakabhāvadvayasaṃbhavāt yadyapi yajeḥ kartṛtvamabhihiṃta
tathāpi svargasiddhau kartṛtvasyānabhihitatvāt tṛtīyā syādeveti tatrāha---bhāvadvayo-
panyāsaśca pratyuktaḥ dhātvantarānupādānāt
 | svargasiddheḥ dhātvantareṇānupādānāt
na tenāpi kriyākārakavibhaktiyogāḥ | nanu yadi kartṛtvamapi svargakāmasya tadā
kartṛviśeṣaṇatvamapi svargasyāsti | tena svargo’pi guṇabhūta iti tatrāha-guṇapradhāna-
bhāvaśca kārakābhiprāyeṇetyetadapi parihṛtam
 | kārakābhiprāyeṇa kevalaṃ guṇa-
pradhānabhāvaḥ svarganiyogayoḥ | niyojyadaśāyāṃ tu pradhānataiva svargasya guṇataiva yāgasyeti
na doṣaḥ | yata eva kārakāvasthāyāmiyaṃ vibhaktiḥ na niyojyāvasthāyāṃ ata eva
kāmigatā saṅkhyā’tantram ākhyātagataiva nādrīyate
 | yadi hi kāmigatā
saṅkhyā niyojyāvasthāyāmeva syāt tadbalenaiva ekasyādhikāraḥ siddhayet nākhyāta-
gatāyā ādaraḥ syāt | yadā tveṣā adhikārapūrvakakartṛdaśāvasthaivāśrīyate tadā nādhi-
kāraniyamāyālamiti |

1. mā. vyāpriyate

[p. 050]

yadi svargakāmo niyojyatayā saṃbadhyate na kartṛtayā idamaparaṃ tarhi kathamakā-
minaḥ pravṛttirnāstīti
 | yāvatā akāmino’pi pravṛttyā bhāvyam | nanu kāma-
sidhyarthatvātpravṛtteḥ kathamakāminaḥ pravṛttirāśaṅkyate ityatrāha---niyogaparatvācchā-
strasya
 niyogasiddhayarthā hi kāmitā na niyogaḥ kāmasiddhayartha ityuktam | niyoga
eva kāryatāprayojyaḥ | tasya kartrā vinā siddhayanupapatteḥ | kāmanāviśeṣaṇatvena
tasya lābhāt niyogārthā kāmitā | ato’kāmino’pi pravṛttiḥ syāt | pariharati---
niyogārthasiddhayarthatvāt kāmanāyāḥ
 | niyogārthasiddhayarthe vā kāmitve kathama-
kāminaḥ pravṛttiḥ ityāśaṅkyate ? codayitā’’ha-naivāśaṅkyate aṅgatā tu kāminaḥ
prāpnoti
 | niyogaṃ prati kāmino’ṅgatā prāpnotītyucyate na ceṣyate mīmāṃsakaiḥ |
pariharati---aviveko’trāparādhyate bhavataḥ | dhātvarthāpekṣo hyaṅgāṅgibhāvo
neṣyate
 dhātvarthamapekṣya niyojyasyāṅgatā neṣyate dhātvarthasya pradhānatā[ca] | niyogā-
pekṣayā tu iṣyata eva | nanvevaṃ svargo guṇa eva syāditi tatrāha---guṇapradhāna-
bhāvo’pi tadapekṣa eva,
 dhātvarthāpekṣa eva ukto
 bhāṣye | niyogasiddhau tu sarvaṃ
tadanuguṇamiti kena neṣyate
 | niyogasiddhidaśāyāṃ tatsarvaṃ tadanuguṇamiti kasyātra
vipratipattiḥ ? yadi sarvaṃ niyogārthaṃ tadā parasparaniyogārthānāṃ tādarthyaṃ na syādi-
tyatrāha---tādarthyaniyamastu pradarśitaḥ kuryādityādinā
 | bhāṣye’pyāha
“kimapi bhāvayet”
 iti | bhāṣyamarthato vyākhyātam | yadi niyojyaviśeṣaṇāni tāni
kathamidānīmetāni vākyāni “ta ime satyāḥ kāmā anṛtāpidhānāḥ
“sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahme”
tyevamādīni
 | uttaraṃ---uktā anyaparāṇāṃ mantrārtha-

[p. 051]

vādānāṃ pratipattiḥ
 | uktā anyaparāṇāṃ mantrārthavādānāṃ pratipattiḥ gatiruktā
gauṇī lākṣaṇikī vā | tenaiṣāmapi sā bhaviṣyati | nanu cānyaparataivopaniṣa-
dvākyānāṃ nāsti
 kāryapratipādakasya vākyāntarasyāśravaṇāt; uttaraṃ-yadi na kārya-
pratipādakaṃ vākyāntaramasti svataśca kāryāśravaṇaṃ tasya tarhi siddhārthaparatvaṃ siddhārtha-
paratve ca
 śabdasya tatra prāmāṇyavyutpatteḥ
 anuvādakatvamaṅgīkaraṇīyaṃ, anuvādaśca
pramāṇāntarāpekṣa iti pramāṇāntarāpekṣitaiva prāpnoti | codayitā āha---tarhi
mṛgyatāṃ pramāṇāntaraṃ iti
 | siddhāntyāha---yadi tatra praṇāṇāntaramasti tadā
prāmāṇāntarasadbhāve vā kā no bādhā | śāstrārthastāvadayam yathaivopavarṇi-
to’smābhiḥ
 | na ca siddhārthasya pramāṇāntarāpekṣatve sādhyārthasyāpi tadbhavitumarhati |
apekṣākāraṇābhāvāt | itarasya tu siddhārthataivāpekṣāheturityukto viśeṣaḥ | coda-
yati---nanu vedāntārthaviṣaye’dvaite pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavāt tena pramāṇāntareṇa śāstrārtho
virudhyate bhedenāvagamyamānatvācca
 śāstrārthasya | pariharati---na khalu kaści-
dvirodhaḥ
 kāryasiddhaviṣayatayā bhinnaviṣayatvena virodhābhāvāt tena pramāṇāntareṇa na
śāstrārtho virudhyate | siddhāntavādyevāha---atha virodhaṃ manyase
 viniyogasavya-
pekṣatvāt niyogasya viniyogaviṣaye ca pramāṇāntarāṇāmavatārāt | dūṣayati---na
tarhi pramāṇāntaramasti
 | kutaḥ ? etasmādeva śāstreṇa virodhāt
 pramāṇāntarasya
kalpayitumaśakyatvāt | kasmāt punaḥ pramāṇāntaratvaṃ nāstītyāha---na cānumānā-
dṛte ekatā
 sarvasya jagataḥ śakyate vaktum
 | tataḥ kimityāha---āgamavirodhe
ca nānumānamātmānaṃ labhata iti naiyāyikāḥ
 | nyāyavyavahāriṇaḥ yathā

[p. 052]

yāgīyā hiṃsā na dharmaḥ hiṃsātvāditi | prakṛtamupasaṃharati---tasmādvākyaśeṣāṇi bhavantu
upaniṣadvākyāni svatantrāṇi vā adhyayanamātraparyavasāyīni
 | pramāṇāntarā-
bhāve siddhārthatayā vivakṣitārthāni | nātra saṅkaṭaṃ kiñcit
 | pakṣadvaye’pi
rāddhāntasiddheḥ | yatpunaḥ dharmaśāstrakāraiḥ paṭhitaṃ “kāmātmatā na praśastā”

iti
 | anubhāṣya pariharati-paṭhitamevaitat | kenavoktaṃ na paṭhitamiti
 | codayitā
āha---yadi paṭhitaṃ bhavatu tarhi svargādīnāṃ na prāśastyam
 mṛṣābhūtatvāt |
rāddhāntyāha---bhavatva
prāśastyaṃ yadi sādhyatayā
 svargādeḥ pratiṣedhaḥ
 svargo na
bhavatītyuktam evaṃ yadi pratiṣedhaḥ syāt | atha tu ātmaguṇo’yamupadiśyate

akāmātmakena puruṣeṇa bhavitavyamiti kāmanāpratiṣedhaḥ yathā nahiṃsyāditi

hiṃsātmakakriyāpratiṣedhaḥ
 tadā nānarthakyaṃ
 prayojanavattvameva svargādisiddheḥ
 |
nirṇayastvanayoḥ tata eva
 smṛtivākyapaurvāparyālocanayaiva anveṣyaḥ
 kriyāpratiṣedha
eva smṛtāviti bhāvaḥ | atra bhāṣyaṃ “vākyādevāsmādavagacchāmaḥ”
 ityādi
tadākṣipati---yatpunaridaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa yāgaprādhānye’pi phalasiddhiṃ darśayatā
upanyastaṃ “kāṣṭhānyāhartukāmo’raṇyaṃ gacchet”
 iti
 | kathamatra kriyā-
prādhānyaṃ manyate pratyuta tumun pratyayāt
 gamirevānyārthe vartate | tasmādyathā
-
bhavatyayaṃ dṛṣṭāntaḥ tathā vaktavyam
 | asyārthaḥ---bhāṣyakāreṇedamuktaṃ kimarthamatīva
svargaprādhānye prayatyate yāvatā yāgaprādhānye’pi kāmyamānasya phalasya yāgāt siddhi-

[p. 053]

ravagamyata eva ‘svargakāmo yajeta’
 iti | asmādeva vākyāt yathā kāṣṭhānyāhartu-
kāmo’raṇyaṃgacchet ityasmādeva vākyāt prādhānye’pi gamanasya gamanāt kāṣṭhāharaṇa-
siddhiravagamyate; yāgāt siddhiravagamyate eva svargakāmo yajetetyasmādeva vākyāt |
yathā kāṣṭhānyāhartukāmaḥ(neyate ?) ityuktam | tasmāt gamirevāhartumiti tumun
śravaṇāt guṇabhūto vartate | “tumun ṇvalau kriyāyāṃ kriyārthāyāṃ”
 iti smaraṇāt |
tasmādviṣamo dṛṣṭāntaḥ | samādhatte---idamucyate kāmaśabdaprayogāt tumunnartho’trā-
nyārthe vartate
 | tathā caitadamarthyamavagamyata ityabhiprāyaḥ
 | atyārthaḥ-āhartu-
kāma iti puruṣapradhānatvāt nirdeśasya tumunnartho gamiṃ prati guṇabhūto vartate | tena
gameḥ prādhānyamasti | prādhānye’pi ca gatyarthasya kāṣṭhāharaṇasya tatassiddhiravagamyata
iti dṛṣṭāntaṃ dadato bhāṣyakārasyābhiprāyaḥ | asya tu yāgaprādhānye’pi phalamavagamyata
iti pakṣasya parihāraḥ satyaṃ
 phalasiddhiravagamyate | sā tu na śabdataḥ
 | kiṃ tarhi ?

prāmāṇāntareṇa
 araṇyagamanasya kāṣṭhāharaṇaphalatvadarśanāt | na ca yāgasya svargaṃ-
prati sādhanatvamavagamyata ityataḥ kāmaprādhānya eva phalasiddherupapattiḥ | adhikaraṇārtha-
mupasaṃharati---tasmādadhikṛtasya kartṛtvamiti siddham
 | atrādhikaraṇe pūrvapakṣottara-
pakṣayoḥ saṃkṣepaḥ---tatra pūrvapakṣī na loke niyojyo nāma kaścidvākyārtho[rthe]nvayī
pratīyate | nanvasati niyojye kathamanuṣṭhānaṃ ? yāvaddhi karma anuṣṭheyatayā nāvagamyate
tāvanna prekṣāpūrvakāriṇo’nutiṣṭhanti | niyojyatve tu sati yasya niyogastasyādhikāraḥ |
adhikṛtasya cānuṣṭhānāvagamāt upapadyate cānuṣṭhā(nāt)nam | ucyate | asatyapi
niyojyatve phalataḥ eva pramāṇāntaramadhigamya tatkarmaṇi phalarthino’dhikārāvagamāt
upapannamanuṣṭhānam | [na]niyojyatve kaścicchabdo’stīti |

rāddhāntī manyate---satyamevaṃ loke tathāpi vede kāryapratipādakatvāt liṅā-
dīnāṃ, anuṣṭhāne ca sati kāryasya kāryatvopapatteḥ anuṣṭhānasiddhayarthamadhikāro’bhyupa-
gamanīyaḥ | na ca pramāṇāntarasamadhigataṃ phalamatrāsti yato’dhikāraḥ syāt | tasmā-
dadhikārasiddhayarthaṃ svargakāmādīnāṃ niyojyatvena saṃbandho varṇanīyaḥ | kaḥpunarniyojyo-
nāma ? yaḥ kāryato’vabudhyate sa niyojyaḥ | etena kāryaṃ prati boddhaṛtayā svarga-

[p. 054]

kāmādaya upādīyante | na cākāmopāyaṃ kāmī kāryatayā pratipattumarhatīti kārya-
kāmayorupāyopeyabhāvaḥ | na ca kālāntarabhāvini kāme yāgaḥ kāryatayā niyojye-
nānvayaṃ yātīti yāgātiriktakāryābhyupagamāt yāgastaṃ prati sādhanatvena saṃbadhyate |
tataśca puruṣasya yatkāryaṃ tatsādhanaṃ yāga iti tatraiśvaryaṃ puruṣasyopapadyate | tataśca tatra
tatrānuṣṭhānāvagaterupapannamanuṣṭhānam | tatra nityeṣu kevalaniyogakāryatvāt tatsādhanatvena
yāgeṣu puruṣasyādhikāraḥ | kāmādhikāreṣu tu phalaparyantaṃ niyogasādhyatvāditi viśeṣaḥ |
atra ślokā bhavantiḥ---

loke kartṛtva(tat)saṃbandhaḥ puruṣaḥ phalasādhanam |

mānāntareṇa vijñāya karmādhikurute svayam || 1 ||

tato’nuṣṭheyatāṃ jñātvā svārthaṃ karmānutiṣṭhati |

niyojyatvamatastatra tenāṃśenopayujyate || 2 ||

loke tasya pratīstaya vedepyavagatiḥ kutaḥ |

na hi vyutpattirahitaḥ śabdenārtho’bhidhīyate || 3 ||

satyamevaṃ liṅādīnāṃ kāryārthatvaṃ na varṇitam |

na cānuṣṭhānaśūnyasya kāryatvamupapadyate || 4 ||

anuṣṭhānāvabodhācca karmaṇyaiśvaryabandhanaḥ |

aiśvaryaṃ ca na yāgādau phaladarśanasaṃbhavaḥ || 5 ||

tataḥ kāryamakāryaṃ syāt aiśvaryeṇa vinā nṛṇām |

na ca kartṛtvamātreṇa karmaṇyaiśvaryasaṃbhavaḥ || 6 ||

aiśvaryeṇaiva kartṛtvaṃ yāvatkarmasu dṛśyate |

kārye boddhaṛtayā tatra kāmī saṃbadhyate yadi || 7 ||

tadā kāryaphalaprāptiḥ kāryādhīnā’vasīyate |

na ca kṣaṇāpavṛttena karmaṇā śakyate phalam || 8 ||

kālāntare janayituṃ tasmātkāryaṃ tato’dhikam |

tena kāryeṇa saṃbandhaḥ sādhanatvena karmaṇaḥ || 9 ||

svakāryasādhane karmaṇīśvaraḥ puruṣastataḥ |

tathā ca sati karmāpi samāsāditasādhakam || 10 ||

[p. 055]

labdhānuṣṭhānamityevaṃ kāryaṃ kāryatvamṛcchati |

pravṛttibījajātāyāḥ kāryavyutpattivīrudhaḥ || 11 ||

niyojyatvaṃ phalāsaṅgi kumudaṃ vikasatyadaḥ |

tena vyutpattiviraho na doṣayātra jāyate
siddho ve(do)de niyojyo’taḥ kārye boddhaṛtayānvitaḥ || 12 ||

phalārthatvāt karmaṇaḥ

nanu tiryagādiṣu vedasya pramāṇataiva nopapadyate

cintā
 | asyārthaḥ-yo niyoge kāryātmani boddhaṛtayā saṃbadhyate sa niyojyaḥ,
so’dhikārī | na ca tiryaścaḥ śabdārthe bodhaśaktivikalāḥ śabdaikasamadhigamanīye
kārye boddhaṛtayā saṃbaddhauṃ yogyāḥ | ataśca teṣāṃ na niyojyatvasaṃbhavaḥ | tathā devatā-
nāmṛṣīṇāṃ ca caitanya(lābhāt)ā/?/bhāvāt bodadhṛtvānupapattiḥ | atastiryagādiṣu

adhikāriviśeṣaṇaśabdā ityarthaḥ | indratvādikāmo yāgādīnanutiṣṭhatīti | anu-

bhavatīti | atra cetihāsaṃ darśayati tathāca śatakraturindraityaitihāsikāḥ
 |

pagatā | asyārthaḥ-yādṛśaṃ darśanaṃ bhavatāmabhimataṃ tasyānupapattiḥ devatāyajanta iti

[p. 058]

bhavatāṃ darśanaṃ abhimānaḥ | na caivaṃ sati devatā yajate pūrvotpannasya devatātvāt idānī-
madhunātanasya yajamānatvāt | ye’pi na puruṣāste’pi na taṃ yajante tadijyameva
pūrvotpannamindraṃ yajeran | evaṃ ca yamebeṣṭvā pūrvotpanno’pīndro hūtaḥ sa evāparairapījyate |
tataśca karmabhirutpannānāmanijyamānatvāt na devatātvaṃ syāt | ataḥ karmabhiḥ devatā-
bhāvo bhavatītyapi pakṣo nopapadyate | tasmāt karmajanyadevatā yajata ityetattāvat nāsti |
tadāha---tadbhāvo bhedaścetyanupapattiranuṣṭhāne
 | asyārthaḥ-adhunotpannasyendrasya
puruṣeṇejyamānatvāt devatābhāvaḥ svayaṃ cānyadevatāyājitvāt pūrvadevatāto bhedaḥ iti
nedamanuṣṭhānamupapadyate puruṣāṇāmapi taddevatādījyayājitvaprasaṅgāt | ato devatā
devataiva ṛṣirapi ṛṣireva na punaḥ prāpyaḥ prāpako vā
 | uktena nyāyena
karmotpannasyānijyamānatvāt avaraṇītvācca | karmabhirdevatābhāvaḥ ṛṣibhāvaśca na
bhavatīti svabhāvenaiva devatā’ṅgīkaraṇīyā | ṛṣirapi na punaḥkarmaprāpyaḥ nāpi
karmaphalaprāpakaḥ yathā na prāpakaḥ tathā navame vakṣyāmaḥ
 devatādhikaraṇe
phaladātṛtvanirākaraṇāt | prāpyatā tviha niṣidhyate | yadapyukta kathamapi
[a] viṣṇukramake yāge’dhikāra iti sādhikārapūrvakatvāt prakaraṇādīnāma
 |
asyārthaḥ---sādhikārasannidhisamāmnātānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ tādarthye sati śrutyādibhistadīyeṣu
padārtheṣu viniyogaḥ kriyate | prakaraṇādīnāṃ tu svarūpe viniyoge cādhikārapūrvakatva-
meva | tenādhikāradaśāyāṃ viniyogasyānupapannatvam | yadyapyanuṣṭhānasamarthasyaivādhikāraḥ
tathāpi paṅg‌vādīnāmadhikāro na śakyate vārayitum a dhikāravaśenaiva tu viniyogaḥ pratya-
dhikāraṃ vyavatiṣṭhate---yadā yatsamartho’dhikārī tasya teṣāmaṅgānāṃ viniyoga iti |
yata evaṃ kramaparyante’vasthite śāstrārthe yāgamātrādhikāramapekṣate na punarviśiṣṭasyaivā-
dhikāraṃ tatra kāmimātrasyaiva śaṅkopapadyate | ato rāddhāntepyadhikārasiddheḥ

nādhikṛtasya teṣāmaṅgānāṃ viniyoga iti avirodhāt viniyogānyatvaṃ śakyate
kalpayitumiti nānupapattiḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---prakaraṇādayo’dhikāramātramapakṣente na

[p. 059]

punaḥ viśiṣṭasyaivādhikāraḥ tatra kāmimātrasyādhikāreṇa labdhātmakaiḥ viniyogapramāṇaiḥ
viniyukteṣvaṅgeṣu tadaṅgayukte’nuṣṭhāne samarthasyaivādhikāraḥ kāmino niścīyate | yadi tāvat
viniyogānusāreṇādhikāriviśeṣe niścīyamāne’dhikāro’va(vi)ruddhyate [tadā]
yathādhikāraṃ viniyogāśrayaṇena nānārūpatā viniyogasya kalpyeta | nacaivamasti |
na hi sāmānyaśrutiḥ viśeṣe’vasthāpyamānā śabdārthatāṃ jahātīti | viṣayaṃ ca darśayan
nidarśitāveva pūrvapakṣottarapakṣo iti na punaḥ pradarśyata iti sthitamadhikaraṇam

adhikārapūrvaka’/?/vādviniyogasya yathādhikāraṃ vyavasthāyāṃ sāmarthyānusāreṇa viniyoga iti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | anirdhāritaviśeṣeṇāpyadhikāriṇā (sādhikāriṇaḥ) sādhikāritvopapattau
viniyogasiddheḥ tadanusāreṇa ta(sa)tsamarthādhikāraviśeṣāśrayaṇaṃ nyāyyamiti rāddhāntaḥ |

yo hi nānutiṣṭhati kathamasāvadhikārī bhavati anuṣṭhānaparyantatvādadhikārasyeti |

 avijñāte

hatvā bhrūṇahā bhavatīti |

[tadā kimātreyīgrahaṇeneti] darśayitumātreyīgrahaṇamupapannam | ta(dā)thā parikrayādi-
bhirasvatvapradarśanaṃ striyā abhyuccayaḥ | yathā krayavikrayayoginī strī svavatī na bhavati
gavādivat | ataḥ svādikrayāyoge svadravyatyāgātmake’samarthetyanadhikāriṇī |

atrocyate---jātistu bādarāyaṇa iti
 | narajātimātramatrāpyadhi-

[p. 061]

kriyate na liṅgādviśeṣagatiradhikārasyetyarthaḥ
 | na jāteradhikāro’cetanāyāḥ
kintu tajjātibhājāṃ puṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ cādhikāraḥ | nanu cāpannakartṛbhāvo na niyukta
iti saṅkhyāvat liṅgamapi liṅgāntaraṃ nivartayatītyuktam
 | satyamuktaṃ, tattva-
yuktam saṅkhyā saṅkhyātagatā karmaṇi saṃṅkhyāntaraṃ nivartayatīti yuktam
 |
liṅgaṃ punaḥ aśrūyamāṇatvādayuktaṃ tannivartayatīti
 | asyārthaḥ---yajetetyākhyāte
kartṛsaṅkhyā pratīyate tayā(tha) viṣayo viśeṣyate tenaikakartṛkaviṣaye ekasyaivānuṣṭhāna-
sāmarthyamiti tasyaiva tatrādhikāraḥ yathā rājajātīyasya rājasūye rājakartṛke | liṅgaṃ
punarākhyāte na śrūyate tena tadviśiṣṭastadviṣayo na pratīyate tena liṅgaviśeṣa-
rahite viṣaye strīpuṃsayoradhikāro ghaṭate | yattvidaṃ prātipadikagataṃ liṅgaṃ
tadadhikārapūrvakamidamavagamyate iti nādhikāraṃ nivartayitumarhati yathādhikārameva tu
varṇyate | atassūktaṃ bādarāyaṇasyeti
 | bhāṣya eva tāvat saṅkhyā saṅkhyāntaraṃ
nivartayati | evaṃ tarhi sahaprayogo na prāpnoti
 saṅkhyāntarapūrvakamavagamyata
iti nivṛtteḥ | sahaprayoge hi strīpuṃsayoḥ dvitvasaṅkhyā syāt tataścaikasaṅkhyā
bādhyeta | sahaprayoge cāsati na striyā adhikāra upapadyate vidyāvirahāt
śadravaditi manyate | tadidamuktam---sthitaṃ tāvadaparyavasitamiti
 sahatva-
paryantatvāt stra/?/yadhikārasya yata eva sahatvaparyantaḥ stra/?/yadhikāraḥ ataḥ
 stra/?/yadhikāraṃ
nivartayituṃ pṛthagadhikāraṃ pūrvapakṣavādī darśitavān
 | rāddhāntavādī tu naiva
kramaparyante viṣaye saṅkhyāvirodhaḥ iti manvānaḥ sahādhikāramuktavān
 |
asyārthaḥ kramaparyatoviṣayoḥ | tatra ca striyāmanupraviśantyāmapi saṅkhyāvirodho nāstīti

[p. 062]

matvā sahādhikāramuktavān | yadi hi kramaparyanto viṣayo na bhavati kintu yāgapadārtha
eva tadā yāge kartrantarānupraveśena saṅkhyāvirodhaḥ syāt | yadā tvanekapadārthātmako
viṣayaḥ śāstrārthīṃbhūtaḥ tadā vyāpārāntareṇāpi kartrantarānupraveśaḥ saṃbhavatīti na saṅkhyā
viruddhayate | kathaṃ punaḥ saṅkhyāvirodho nāsti
 ? uttaraṃ---sahiteṣu hi
dvivacanabahuvacane bhavataḥ
 samāso vā; yathā devadattayajñadattau pacata ityādiṣu | na
punassahārtheṣu
 devadattena saha yajñadatto gata iti | iha tu strī sahabhāvenānupraviśati iti
“dharme cārthe na nāticaritavyā”
 | “yatkiñcitkartavyaṃ tadanayā saha”
 iti
vacanāt | yata eva sahiteṣu samāsaḥ ata eva cetaretarayoge samāhāre ca samāsaṃ
manyante samāsavidaḥ
 | na punaścārthamātre
 itaretarayoge samāhāre ca dvandvaṃ
manyante yatra sahitabhāvaḥ | na punaścārthamātrepi
 samuccaye’nvācaye ca | yadi
yatrāpi sāhityaṃ nāsti tatrāpi samāso bhavet tadā samuccayānvācayayorapi dvandvaḥ
smaryeta1 | kaḥpunarabhiprāyaḥ
 sahitabhāve samāsaṃ smaratāmityatrāha---ayamabhiprāyaḥ
netaretarabhāvamanāpannayoḥ padārthāntarasaṃbandhaḥ
 yatra hi sahitabhāvaḥ tatra hītaretara-
bhāvaḥ | sahayogitvamanāpannayoḥ aprāptayorna padārthāntarasaṃbandhaḥ api tu paraspara-
saṃbandhayoreva padārthāntareṇa saṃbandhaḥ | samāse ca samasyamānayorarthayoḥ parasparānu-
gamanapūrvakaṃ padārthāntarānugamanam | tena sahitabhāve yādṛśo’rthānāṃ saṃbandhaḥ tādṛśa
eva samāsenāpyucyate iti | sāmarthye ca sati samāsa iti yuktam | tatra samāso
yatra ca parasparasaṃbaddhayoreva padārthāntareṇa saṃbandhaḥ | tatra tulyadvārasaṃbandhāt dvibahutvopa-
patteḥ dvivacanaṃ bahuvacanaṃ na yuktam | tena samāso dvivacanabahuvacane | yatra itaretara-
savyapekṣayorarthāntarasaṃbandhaḥ tatra tadviṣayāṇyeva | yatra punaḥ padārthāntarasaṃbandhāpekṣaḥ
cārthaḥ na tatra dvivacanabahuvacane bhavataḥ samāso vā
 | yatra hyekasmin sambandhe
sati punararthāntarasaṃbandhaḥ kriyate na tatraikadvāreṇa sambandhaḥ yathā devadattaḥ pacati yajña-
1. smaryate-mā

[p. 063]

dattaśca iti | na hi tatra yathā devadattaḥ pākena saṃbaddhaḥ tathā yajñadatto’pi punaḥ
sambandhayituṃ śakyate | arthāntarāvaruddhe’rthāntarasambandhitvābhāvāt | tena tatra dvārā-
ntareṇa sambandho’vagamyate | ataścānityasambandhitvāt dvibahutvānupapatteḥ na dvivacana
bahuvacanopapattiḥ | samāsastu sāmarthyābhāve na saṃbhavati | samāse hi paraspara-
sahitānāmarthāntarasambandho’vagamyate iti | tathācārthāntarasaṃbandhānāṃ samuccayāvagateḥ
sāmarthyābhāvaḥ | iha ca patnī yajamānāvaruddhe prayoge paścātsahaśabdena sambadhyate |
nāsau yajamānādbhinnena dvāreṇa yajamānāvaruddhe prayoge kartṛtvenānupraviśati | tena

tāmapekṣya na dvivacanabahuvacanopapattiḥ atulyasambandhitvāt | yatra sahitayoḥ kriyā-
sambandhaḥ tatra dvivacanabahuvacanopapattiriti | tadidaṃ mahābhāṣyakāreṇa darśitam
sahitayoḥ kriyāsambandhābhidhāne dvivacanameva pradarśanārtheṣu vākyeṣu evamanu-
kṛtaṃ bhavati
 | anyathāhi anukāro’pi nopapadyata iti
 | asyārthaḥ---sahitayoḥ
kriyāsambandhābhidhāne dvandvasamāse dhavakhadirau cchindyāt ityādau pradarśanārtheṣu vākyeṣu
vigrahavākyeṣu dvivacanameva prayoktavyam---dhavakhadirau ca iti | evaṃ dvivacanaprayoge
sati sahitakriyāsambandhābhidhāyi samāsapadamanukṛtaṃ bhavati | dvivacanadarśane hi
evamavagamyate---dvitvamastīti | dvitvotpattiśca parasparānvitayoḥ kriyāsambandhe bhavati |

nanvevaṃ sati kathamekasmin dvivacanamucyate | sahitāvasthāyāṃ dvitvasaṅkhyā-
yogāt | dvitvaṃ ca sādhāraṇaguṇaḥ sahitāvasthālakṣaṇārthaṃ ca padadvayam | ata
evārthāntare kathaṃ vṛttiriti codyasyānavakāśaḥ | yadi punardvivacanena prayujyate tadā
anukāropi na syāt, sahitāvasthāyā adarśanādeva | sahabhāvena kriyāsambandhe’-
vagate yajamānasya tatpurassarā patnī kartṛtvena karmaṇyanupraviśantī yajamānādbhinnadvāreṇa
kartṛtāmanubhavati | tadidamuktam---pratikārakaṃ kriyābheda iti
 | anyā
kriyā, anyaḥ yajamānasya dvavyatyāgātmo yāgaḥ, madhyagaṃ ca dampatyoḥ dhanaṃ, kevalo
yajamānastyaktuṃ na śaknoti | atyakte hi patnyāḥ svāmyāt yajamānasyāpi svāmitva-
miti yāgānabhinirvṛttiḥ | tena patnī anumatiṃ kurvāṇā yāge kartrīṃ | yajamānastu
sākṣāddevatāmuddiśya tyāgaṃ kurvāṇo yāge karteti | tadidamuktaṃ---pratikārakaṃ

[p. 064]

kriyābheda
 iti | na hi patnyā anumatiṃ vinā yajamānasya kartṛtvopapattiḥ yāge |
tena sāpi bhavatyeva yāge kartrī, vyāpārāntareṇānupraveśāt na dvitvabahutvopapattiriti
niravadyā | atka bhāṣyaṃ---“dvādaśe vā śate ṣaṭa्pañcāśat”
 iti, tadidamākṣipati---
dvādaśe vā śate ṣaṭa्pañcāśaditi kimidaṃ
 ? idaṃ kila bhaṣyaṃ rāddhānte yuktyantaramiva
darśayati dvādaśe vā śate ṛtvik‌parikrayārthe yajamānena ṣaṭa्pañcāśaditi dīyate,
patnyā ca ṣaṭ्pañcāśaditi | etacca rāddhāntavirodhi | evaṃ hi tulyavyāpārā yajamānena
patnīti dvitvāpatteḥ ekavacanavirodhaḥ | ekadeśyāha---vayamapi brūmaḥ kimidamiti
 |
ākṣeptā āha---kimasadevedaṃ
 ? samādhātā āha---na
 asaditi brūmaḥ bhāṣyānartha-
kyāpatteḥ | arthamāha---dvādaśe śate yadi vibhāgaḥ syāt syādetadevaṃ
 ekavacana-
virodha iti | iha punaḥ anya eva patnīvyāpāra iti sahārthatāṃ darśayati na
sahitārthatām
 | asyārthaḥ---rāddhantavādī evaṃ brūte yadi dvādaśe śate ekena
ṣaṭpañcāśaddīyate apareṇāpi ṣaṭpañcāśaddīyata iti dānavibhāgaḥ syāta tadā ekavacana-
virodhaḥ syādityadhyāhāraḥ | patnī vyāpārastvanya evānumatilakṣaṇaḥ | tena sahārthena
patnyā anupraveśena[śo na] sahitārtheneti | darśitaṃ cedamanumatilakṣaṇaḥ patnīvyāpāra
iti | anyatarānicchāyāṃ tyāga eva na saṃvarteta iti
 | upasaṃharati---
tena śrutyā sahatvasmṛteravirodhāt saha kartavyamityasya vacanasya ayathārthatā
na śakyate’vagantum
 | śrutivirodhe hi tathā syāt | na cāsāvasti | evaṃ ca
patnīśabdassamarthito bhavati
 | yadi sahaprayogaḥ patnyā na syāt tadā patnyadhi-
kāro bhajyetaiva | tataśca yajñasvāmitvaṃ striyā nāstīti yajñasvāmistrīviṣaye ‘patyurno-
yajñasaṃyoge’
 iti patnīśabdo’samañjasaḥ syāt | karmaviśeṣāścāvekṣaṇādayaḥ samarthitā
bhavantīti | anyathā hi ‘patnyavekṣitabhājyaṃ bhavati’
 ityanupapannaṃ syāt | liṅgamuktam

1. bhisadanaṃ-mā

[p. 068]

vacanatā | adhikaraṇaśabdena śabdasya vṛttiviṣayo’bhidhīyate---yathā dhavakhadirau
iti samāsāntargatayoḥ dhavakhadiraśabdayoḥ vā .... .... na tayorekaikena padena
dhavakhadirayorabhidhānam | nanu naitadupapannam saṃbandhagrahaṇānusāreṇa śabdo’rthamabhidhatte |
na ca dhavaśabdo’rthadvaye gṛhītasaṃbandhaḥ | yadi cārthadvayamabhidadhyāt tadā padāntara-
prayogo’narthakaḥ | na ca lakṣaṇayā’rthadvayāvagamakatvaṃ, anupapattiṃsabandhayorabhāvāt |
padāntaraprayogaścānarthaka ityuktam | seyaṃ yugapadadhikaraṇatā duḥkhā ca durupapādā
ceti | atrābhidhīyate
 - sahitabhāve dvandvo vartate | samasyamānapadānatirekī ca
samāsaḥ | tena ekaikameva prātipadikaṃ sahitāvasthāpannasvārthaṃ lakṣayati | svarūpa-
mātrāvasthite svārthe’bhidhānaśaktiḥ śabdasya, avasthāviśiṣṭāvasthā lakṣyate | sahita-
bhāvaścārthāntareṇa vinā na bhavatīti dvitīyapadārtho’pi tenākṣipyate | ataḥ siddhaṃ
yugapadanekārthapratipādakatvam | ata eva ca dvandve padadvaya(miti)[mapi]lākṣaṇikārtha-
miti vadanti | yaścāsāvartho lakṣyate tasya viśiṣṭasyāvagatiḥ padāntarāyatteti na
padāntarānarthakyam | tadapavādtvāccaikaśeṣasyeti
 | dvandvāpavāda ekaśeṣaḥ vṛkṣāḥ |
yadyevaṃ tataḥ kim
 ? uttaraṃ---idaṃ tato bhavati dvayoridamabhidhānaṃ tantreṇa
vasānau iti tatra ca dvau pumāṃsāveva pratīyete
 | arthāntarasya vācakābhāvāt
 |
vasānāviti tāvat aukārāntena śabdena ekaḥ pumān sadvitīyo’bhidhīyate | tathā
aparo’pi pumāneva tantrāvṛttyā’bhidhīyate | tatra dvitīyasyāpekṣāyāṃ pumāneva tantrā-
bhidhānenopasthāpito gṛhyate na strī tasyā vācakābhāvāt | nanu cādadhīyātāmiti
dvivacanānupapattiḥ ākhyāte
 puṃdvayasyotpattiśiṣṭaikakartṛkaviṣaye’nupraveśānupapatteḥ |

 yadyutpattiśiṣṭaguṇāvarodhaḥ | kā naḥ pīḍā

 iti smarati bhagavān pāṇiniḥ

yāpyanadhikāra ityuktamante |

idamatrādhikaraṇe vicāryate-kimupanayanamadhyayanāṅgaṃ uta ācāryakaraṇavidhyaṅga-
miti | yadyadhyayanāṅgaṃ tadā kāmaśrutiprayuktamadhyayanaṃ śūdrasyāpi kāmaśrutiṣvadhikāraḥ |
athācāryakaraṇavidhyaṅgamupanayanaṃ tadā ācāryavidhiprayuktamadhyayanaṃ traivarṇikānāmeva kāma-
śrutiṣvadhikāra iti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī manyate-svādhyāyo’dhyetavya ityasya sannidhau
śrutaṃ aṣṭavarṣaṃ brāhmaṇamupanayīteti upanayanaṃ copanīyamānasannidhānakārakatayā tadarthaṃ
pratibhāti | tasya ca kāryākāṅkṣā’sti | kimanenopanayanasahakṛtena kartavyamiti |
tathā svādhyāyo’dhyetavya iti kartrapekṣāsti | tenopanīta evādhyayane kartā | adhyayanaṃ
ca vidyāprāptyartham | prāptayā ca vidyayā kāmaśrutiṣu vidyāsādhyāsu prayojanam |
ataḥ kāmaśrutaya eva vidyāṃ prayuñjānā adhyayanaṃ upanayanaṃ ca tadaṅgabhūtaṃ prayuñjate |
anupāttavidyānāṃ ca kāmaśrutiṣu vidyāprayojikāsu adhikāra iti kāmitvāviśeṣāt
śūdrasyāpyadhikāraḥ syāt | sa cādhikāra upanayanaparyanta iti | upanayane cāpyadhi-
kāra iti svāmitvaṃ śūdrasya | nanu cātmanepadaprayogāt upanayīteti tasyācāryakaraṇe
vartamānādupanayatervidhānāt ācāryakaraṇavidhyaṅgamevopanayanaṃ prayuñjāna ācārya-
karaṇavidhiḥ adhyayanamapi prayokṣyate | upanayanasya hi upanīyamānāsattidvāreṇācārya-
karaṇavidhyupakārakatā syāt adhyāpanena cācāryo bhavati | na cādhyāpanamadhyayanaṃ vinā
bhavati | pratīyate copanītakartṛkamadhyayanaṃ tenādhyayanaṃ kurvāṇameva māṇavakamupasādayat
upanayanamaṅgamiti bhavati adhyayanaṃ prati ācāryakaraṇavidheḥ prayuktiḥ | tataśca yeṣā-
mevopanayanaṃ teṣāmevādhyayanaṃ teṣāṃ vidyāvatāmadhikāra iti śūdrasyānadhikāra iti |

nanu ca dravyaṃ na prayuṅkte karmavidhirityuktam
 | tasmādadhikāro[re] na
ghaṭata eveyaṃ cintā
 | yathāsthite karmabhede’nāhitāgneḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---caturthā-
dhyāye idamuktam kāmaśrutayo dravyaṃ na prayuñjata iti | tena adravyaḥ kiṃ adhikriyate
neti cintaiveyaṃ nopapadyate rāddhāntasyāsaṃbhavāt | yadi hi adravyo’pyadhikriyate tadā
adhikāre sati vidhayo’pi dravyārjanaṃ prayuñjīran | na ca prayuñjata iti sthitam |
ato’dravyasyādhikāro nāstīti phalataḥ caturthe pratipāditam jīvanārthopāyārjitadravya
evādhikriyata iti arthādupapadyate yataḥ | yathā dvitīye karmabhede sthite prāpakeṣu
ādhānavidhiṣu satsu anāhitāgneradhikāre niraste śūdrasyādhikāracintā ghaṭate tatheyaṃ

cintā na ghaṭata iti vaidharmyadṛṣṭānto’yam | kathaṃ tathā na ghaṭata ityatrocyate---
na cānyat prayojanamasti upanayanavat
 caśabdo hetau | yathā pūrvādhikaraṇe

śrutayaḥ śaknuvanti adravyaṃ nirasituṃ uta neti
 yathā anāhitāgniṃ karmaśrutayaḥ
nirasyanti tathā kimadravyaṃ nirasyanti uta neti | kiṃ punarnirāse bījamiti atrāha
dravyopādānamanākṣipantya iti
 | yadi dravyopādānaṃ(mā)nākṣipanti tataḥ pūrvopātta-
dravyamadhikurvate anyathā dravyopādānākṣegprasaṅgāt | tatra pūrvavādino’bhiprāyaḥ-
anāhitāgnimivādravyaṃ dravyopādānamanākṣipantya karmaśrutayaḥ adhikartuṃ na
śaknuvanti
 iti | tasmādanadhikāro’dravyasyeti
 |

atrābhidhīyate---na śakyata etadanāhitāgnimiva adravyaṃ nirūpa-
yitum
 | asyārthaḥ---svābhāvikī śaktiradhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ; adravyasyāpi ca
dravyasādhyakarmānuṣṭhānaśaktirasti adravyasyāpi dravyaṃ bhavatyeva | yadi punarevaṃ nirūpayet
asya dravyaṃ na bhavatyeveti tadā’tyantamaśakyatvādadravyasyānadhikāraḥ syāt | yathā

[p. 078]

śūdrasyāgnivirahiṇo’tyantāśakteranadhikāraḥ | tatra hi śūdrādijātiravacchedikā
vidyate evaṃ
 jāteragnayo na santīti iha punaḥ ādānamaniyataṃ kadācidupādatte
 |
kadācinnopādatte sa eva
 | tena svābhāvikī puruṣāṇāṃ dravyatāśaktirasti | tasmā-
nnaikāntato dravyanirāsassaṃbhavati
 | tena svābhāvikī śaktiḥ[a]dravyasyāpi tāva
dadhikāro’sti | adhikṛtasya jīvanāyeva karmaṇopyupādānaṃ prāptaṃ
 yuktamityarthaḥ |
nanu copādānaśeṣanirāsaḥ
 caturthādhyāyoktaḥ evaṃ samud्vṛtto bhavati
 | pariharati-
noda्dhriyate
 | nimittatvena hi asmābhiridamucyate
 | na śeṣitayā
 prayojana-
bhūtatayā puruṣasya jīvanamiva dravyopādāne karmāpi nimittaṃ na punaretadeva śeṣītyarthaḥ |
yata eva śeṣatā dravyārjananiyamasya neṣyate ata eva tatrāpyuktam karmārjitamapi
ārjito vrīhireva
 | ihāpyuktam jīviṣyati vinā dhanenetyanupapannam iti
 |
caturthe hyevamuktam vrīhibhiryajeteti vrīhimātramaṅgatayā vidhīyate, na punaḥ pratigrahādyapi |
tena karmārthamapi laukikabhojanakarmārthamapi yadi vrīhirupārjyate tadā vrīhibhiryāgo bhava-
tyeva tena vaiguṇyaṃ nāsti | tathā atraivādhikaraṇe uktaṃ ‘jīviṣyati vinā dhanenetyanu-
papannaṃ’
 iti | tena jīvanopārjitena yakṣyata ityuktam | nanu cedamayuktaṃ---jīviṣyati
vinā dhanenetyanupapannamiti | etatkilānenoktam | yo jīvettasya jīvanānupapattyā
dravyamasti tena yāgaḥ kariṣyata iti | taccānupapannam---dravyeṇāpi vinā pratiniyata-
karmakāle jīvanasaṃbhavāt | tathā kratvanaṅgenāpi dravyeṇa kodravādinā jīvanopapatteḥ |
tathāpi kenāpi jīvato nāvaśyaṃ dravyamasti tena nirviṣayamevedamadhikaraṇamiti pṛcchati-
kimanenoktam
---iti | uttaraṃ---idamanenoktam aniyatamidamupādānaṃ na
nirāsāyālamiti
 | etaduktaṃ bhavati-jīvato dhanābhāva ityanupapannam | vyabhicārāt
tenāniyatamupādānam | ato’dravyasyāpi śaktirastīti nādhikāranirāsaḥ | adhikara-

[p. 079]

ṇārthamupasaṃharati---tasmājjīvanāyeva karmaṇopyupādāne yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ
 | adra-
vyasyādhikāro[re]dravyārjanaprayoktṛtvaṃ karmaṇāṃ prasajyate | taccaturthe nirākṛtamiti
pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ | adravyasyādhikāre caturthoktavirodho nāsti | śeṣatā hi tatra pratiṣiddhā na
punaḥ prayojanatāpi bhojanādivat prayuktiriti rāddhāntaḥ adravyasya svābhāvikī
śaktirastītyadhikāro yukta(me)eveti siddhametat | svābhāvikaśaktipratipattyarthaṃmeva
cātra niyatatā dravyavattāyā uktā |

|| aṅgahīnaśca ||

pūrvapakṣamāha---kiṃdharmā ?
 - asyārthaḥ - na tāvat adravyadharmā śakyate vaktuṃ
akalyāṇāṅgaḥ | yasmāt adravyasyāpi dravyasaṃbhavayogyatā dravyavattākālavadaviśiṣṭā,
akalyāṇāṅgasya tu rogavattayāśaktirapagataiva |

auṣadhādiprayoge punaḥ śaktirutpādya iti tasyāmavasthāyāṃ śaktirupagateti nādravya-
tulyatā | tasmānna jñāyate kiṃdharmā aṅgahīna iti | rāddhāntamāha---adravyadharme-
tyarthaḥ
 | sa eva hi kadācidaṅgahīno bhavati kadācit kalyāṇāṅgaḥ
 |
asyārthaḥ---yo na rogeṇa kāryaṃ prati vyāpṛtaḥ sa eva tasya cikitsāyāṃ satyāṃ punaḥ
kāryaṃ kurvannupalabhyate | tathā ca rogeṇa naiva śaktiḥ nāśitetyavagamyate | ciki-
tsāyāṃ satyāṃ kāryadarśanāt | tena śakteḥ satyā eva pratibandhabhūto rogaḥ cikitsa-
yā’panīyata iti kalpyate | kaḥpunarniścayaḥ nāśotpādau na staḥ pratibandhāpagamāveva
kevalau iti ? ucyate | vināśo nāma ātyantikopalabdhivicchedaḥ | sa ca
śakternāsti punaḥ kāryeṇopalambhāt | utpādopyatyantānupalabdhasya punarupalaṃbhanaśaktiḥ |
prāgatyantānupalabdharogasya purataḥ kāryeṇauvopalambhāt iti | tataśca tirohitaśakterapi
1. p 1382

[p. 080]

śaktirastīti yukta evādhikāraḥ ityadravyadharmaiva cititsyarogaḥ : tasmādadhikṛtena
satā cikitsādiṣu yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ dravyopādānavat
 | yasya punarapratisamādhe-
yaivākalyāṇāṅgatā [sa]śakyate nādhikṛta iti vaktuṃ śūdravat | śaktināśaśca ātya-
ntikopalambhavicchedaḥ | yathāhi tatra śūdratvaṃ nirāsahetuḥ nityāvagamyatvāt tadvada-
pratisamādheyatvamapīti tulyam | yathā śūdrasyāśaktiḥ atyantamanapāya t jāteḥ tathā-
naṣṭaśaktikāṅgyogitvamapīti | yadā putaḥ pravṛttayāga evāṅgairhīyate tadā kathaṃ ?
yāgaṃ kurvāṇasya(kasya) akalyaṅgatā vartate yadā tadā cikitsāvā abhāvāt katha-
madhikāra iti | ya eva dravye’pyupāyaḥ sa evātrāpi bhaviṣyati yayā prabṛttaprayoga-
madhya eva yo dravyarahitaḥ tasya yathā’dhikāro’napagataḥ tathā’kalyāṇāṅgasyāpīti bhāvaḥ |
sa cābhyupāyaḥ pratinidhāvupadiśyate |

|| atryārṣeyasya ||

nanu kimidaṃ bhinnaṃ vākyaṃ uta ekamiti cintanīyam
 | atra kila
bhāṣyakāraḥ prathamaṃ tāvadidaṃ cintayati ‘ārṣeyaṃ vṛṇīte’
 iti sāmānyeta vidhiḥ
tena prāptānyekasya dvayostrayāṇāṃ ca varaṇāni ekaṃ vṛṇīte ityādibhiḥ pradarśyante |
tathā ‘na caturo vṛṇīte’
 ‘na pañcātipravṛṇīte’
 ityābhyāṃ caturṇāṃ atītapañcānāṃ ca
varaṇaṃ pratiṣidhyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ rāddhāntastu
 ārṣeyaṃ vṛṇīte ityārṣeyavaraṇacodanā |
trīn vṛṇīte iti tatraiva saṅkhyāvidhānam | ‘ekaṃ vṛṇīte’
 iti cāvayutyānuvādaḥ |
‘na caturo vṛṇīte’
 na pañcātipravṛṇīte’ iti nityānuvāda ityetattāvadatra na cinta-
nīyam | kutaḥ ? arṣeyaṃ vṛṇīte ityukte dvayostrayāṇāṃ ca varaṇaṃ prāptameva | upā-

[p. 081]
 
dānaśeṣatvādekasaṅkhyāyāḥ | tena dvitvasya tritvasya cāvaśyaṃ vidheyatvameva yuktam |
tayośca vihitayorekatvasya prāptirnāstīti tadapi vidheyam | tathā ekatvadvitvabahu-
tvānāṃ vidhānato viniyogaśeṣatvāt caturādīnāṃ prasaktireva nāstīti kathaṃ pratiṣedha
iti | tasmādidamatra cintanīyam---ārṣeyavaraṇe codite kimekatvādīni trīṇyapi
vidadhati bhinnānyetāni | [ni] yogaśeṣatvāt caturādīnāṃ prasaktireva nāstīti kathaṃ
pratiṣedha iti | tasmādidaṃ śāstrāṇi uta tritvavidhāyakamekamidaṃ śāstramiti |
tatrāvayutyānuvāde ‘ekaṃ vṛṇīte’
 ‘dvau vṛṇīta iti tasyaiva stutyarthau | tathā ca na
caturo vṛṇīta iti lāghavena stutyartho nityānuvāda iti | tathā caitadeva
darśayan bhāṣyakāraścodayati---“nanu caikaṃ vṛṇīta ityapi viśeṣavacanamasti”
 iti
prathamaṃ yadbhāṣyakāreṇopanyastaṃ tat mandāpyāśaṅkā nirākaraṇīyetyanenābhiprāyeṇa | tasmāt
kimidamekaṃ uta bhinnamiti cintanīyam | tacca śāstrabhedābhedopayogi, nādhi-
kāropayogi
 | tena lakṣaṇasaṅgatirnāstītyarthaḥ | samādhatte---satyamevaṃ sa tu bhedā-
bhedo’pyadhikāra eva viśeṣamādadhāti ityadhikāra eva cintyate
 | asyārthaḥ---
yadyapi śāstrabhedābhedāvapi bhavataḥ tathāpyadhikāralakṣaṇa eveyaṃ cintocitā | yasmāt
bhede sati ekārṣeyasya cādhikāro bhavati abhede tu tryārṣeyasyaiveti | evaṃ sati
bhavati manuvat bharatavat amukavat iti yajamānasyārṣeyaṃ vṛṇīteti | tenaivaṃ
prayogakāle yajamānasyārṣeyāṇi varaṇīyāni-manuvat bharatavat śāṇḍilyavat asitavat
devalavat ayaṃ devadattaśarmā yajata iti | tatra yasya yābanta ārṣeyāḥ tāvantastasya
varītavyāḥ | tatra ekatvadvitvayorapi vidhiḥ syāt, tadaikārṣeyadvayārṣeyāṇāmapi adhi-
kāro bhavati ārṣeyavaraṇāṅgopasaṃhāro samarthatvāt | ārṣeyavaraṇaṃ hi kathaṃbhāvā-
vasthayā vihitaṃ syāt na samuccayena, asaṃbhavāt | yo hi ekārṣeyo yajamānaḥ tasyai-
kārṣeyavaraṇaṃ vihitam | dvayārṣeyatryārṣeyayostu yathāyathaṃ dvayārṣeyatryārṣeyavaraṇaṃ na punaḥ
sarveṣāmeva sarvāṇi varaṇāni vihitāni, tathāvivasya yajamānasyāsaṃbhavāt | yadā
punaḥ tritvameva kevalaṃ vidhīyate tadā tryārṣeyāṅgayukte karmaṇi tryārṣeṃya eva
yajamāno’dhikārī syāt ityadhikāraviśeṣāpādakatvāt yāvatsaṅkhyākalpaneti |

asyārthaḥ---ekaṃ vṛṇīte ityevamādīnāṃ laḍantatvāt sākṣādvidhirna śrūyate | na ca
vidhimantareṇa tritvādīnāmanuvādatvenopapattiriti vacanāni tvapūrvatvāditi vidhiḥ
kalpayitavyaḥ
 | na ca kaścidviśeṣo’stīti yāvatsu saṅkhyā
 tāvatsu vidhireva
kalpayitavyaḥ | ityevaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate | na yāvatsaṅkhyaṃ
 vidheyakalpanā

[p. 082]
 
bhavitumarhati | kutaḥ ? parasparasaṃbandhāvagateḥ
 | yadi parasparasaṃbandhaḥ saṅkhyā-
śabdānāṃ na syāt tadā bahavo vidhayaḥ kalpyeran | avagamyate tu parasparasaṃbandhaḥ |
tatra yasyāḥ saṅkhyāyā vidhāvāśrīyamāṇe naikavākyatā’paipi tadvidhīyata
iti nyāyāt
 triṣu ca vidhīyamāneṣu anuvādopapatterekavākyopapatteriti | atra
codayati---nanu ca yuktaṃ ‘na caturo vṛṇīte’
 ‘na pañcātipravṛṇīte’
 iti
triṣu vidhīyamāneṣu anuvādopapatteḥ ekavākyatvaṃ vidhānābhāvādeva varaṇā-
bhāvasya prāptasyānuvādaḥ ‘ekaṃ’
 ‘dvau'
 iti tu nānuvādo ghaṭate | aprāptatvā-
dbhinnānyetāni vākyānītyuktam || ata eva pañcānāṃ varaṇaṃ nyāyyameveti
manyāmahe
 | yadi hyekavākyatvaṃ bhavet tadā ‘na caturo vṛṇīte’
 ‘na pañcāti-
pravṛṇīte’
 iti anāvṛttavākyaśravaṇāt pañcānāṃ na varaṇaṃ kalpyeta | yadā kalpyeta tadā
tadarthamapi vākyaśeṣasyāvṛttyā sakṛcchravaṇaṃ bādhyeta | yadā tu bhinnāni vākyāni
tadā vākyaśeṣasya sakṛcchravaṇabādhe siddhe pañcavidhisaṃbandhitayāpi āvṛttiḥ kalpyamānā
nānupapanneti pañcānāmapi varaṇaṃ nyāyyamevetyuktam | kathaṃ punaḥ pañcānāmapi varaṇa-
prāptirāṅkyate ? itthaṃ, naño’nuvāda upapannataro bhavati na caturo vṛṇīte na
pañcātipravṛṇīte iti | yadi pañcānāmapi varaṇaṃ na syāt tadā itthaṃ nañarthasya

varaṇābhāvasyānuvāda upapanno bhavati
 pañcānāṃ tu varaṇābhāve sati pañcatikrameṇa
varaṇābhāvānuvādo nistātparyaka eva syāt | ataḥ pañcānāmapi varaṇaṃ nyāyya- 
meveti | atrocyate---uktamasmābhiḥ sakalapadaikavākyatā | nanu ca ekaṃ
dvau iti anuvādāsaṃbhavāt anupapannaivetyuktam
 | uttaraṃ---na nāma triṣvanu-

vādaḥ na ca saṅkhyā saṅkhyāntare’nuvartata iti nyāyāt
 | nāmaśabdo’ṅgīkāre |
etaduktaṃ bhavati---na saṅkhyā saṅkhyāntare vartata iti nyāyena tritve ekatva-
dvitvayorabhāvādanuvāda āñjasyena na ghaṭate ityaṅgīkurmaḥ | yatpunaridaṃ nañ-
vākyaṃ tatkena saṃbadhyate sarvaissaṅkhyāvākyairaviśeṣāditi cet | siddhā
tarhyekavākyatā
 | yadi sarvaiḥ saṅkhyāvākyaiḥ naña्vākyasya saṃbandhaḥ tadā siddhā
ekavākyatā syāt | yasmāt na naña्vākye’sya pratyekaparisamāptau
pramāṇamasti | kathaṃ nāsti
 ? uttaraṃ---sakṛcchravaṇāt pratyekaparisamāptau pramāṇaṃ
nāsti | yadi hi
 sakṛcchravaṇepi pratyekaparisamāptiḥ syāt
 tadā ekatvādīnā-
mapi
 ‘na caturo vṛṇīte’
 ‘na pañcātipravṛṇīte’
 iti pratyekameva
syānnaño’bhyāsaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---ekatvādīnāmapi kṛte naño’bhyāsaḥ syāt |
tataścaivaṃ syāt ekaṃ na vṛṇīte na trīn vṛṇīte iti | bhavatvevaṃ, kā naḥ pīḍeti |

 | prayojanaśūnyo’bhyāsaḥ syādityarthaḥ | kiñca

 sarveṣāṃ vihitapratiṣiddhatayā
vikalpaḥ prāpnoti

 | sakṛcchravaṇādityarthaḥ | anyathā
hi sakṛcchravaṇādbādhitaṃ syāt | nanvevamapi bhavatveko vidhiḥ vākyaśeṣasya sakṛdanu-
mānānuguṇyāt sa tu trayāṇāmeveti kutaḥ ityatrāha---trayāṇāṃ ca na saṃbhavati

ekasya dvayorvā vidhāvāśrīyamāṇe trayāṇāmanuvādo na saṃbhavati | trayāṇāṃ tu vidhā-
ne’vayutya ekasya dvayoścānuvādo ghaṭata iti | atastrayāṇāmeva (vā)vidhānam
 |
adhikaraṇārthamupasaṃharati - atastrayāṇāmeva varaṇaṃ tryārṣeyasya cādhikāra iti
sthitam

bhavatu atryārṣeyāṇāmapyadhikāra iti niravadyam |

|| vacanāt ||

atra pūrvapakṣavādī vidvatsādhyatvādādhānasya upanayanasādhyatvāt vidva-
dbhāvasya yepāmevopanayanaṃ teṣāmevādhānavidhirupapanno bhavatīti manvānaḥ
naisargikānāmevānena nimittena ādhānamiti varṇitabān
 | asyārthaḥ---
traivarṇikānāmevopanayanaṃ yeṣāṃ copanayanaṃ teṣāmeva vidyā ācāryakaraṇavidhiprayuktatvāt
adhyayanasya | yeṣāmeva vidyā teṣāmevādhānaṃ yuktam vidvatsādhyatvādādhānasya | tataśca
traivarṇikānāmeva rathakaraṇena nimittena ādhānāntarametadvidhīyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
ye punaḥ rathakārā brāhmaṇādayaḥ teṣāṃ vasantādikālakramādhānavidhānaṃ
bhaviṣyati pāriśeṣyāditi sarvamupapannam | yatpunaratra bhāṣyaṃ traivarṇikānāṃ

[p. 085]

śilpopajīvitvaṃ niṣiddhamiti tadayuktamiva pratibhāti niṣedhātikramaṇādapyā-
dhānavidhānaṃ [na] naivopapadyate
 ye pratiṣedhātikrameṇa śilpamupajīvanti teṣāmapyā-
dhānavidhānamupapadyata eva puruṣārthatvāt pratiṣedhasyāgnayarthatvācca
 ādhānasya
sādhyabhedena ādhāna-śilpopajīvitvapratiṣedhayoraṅgāṅgi[bhāvā]bhāvāt na pratiṣedha
ādhānāṅgaṃ, kiṃ tarhi ? puruṣārthaḥ | na ca puruṣārthātikrameṇādhānasya vaiguṇyaṃ
śakyate vaktum
 | yadi vaiguṇyaṃ syāt tadā atikrāntaniṣedhānāmādhānaṃ na syāt |
na cāsti vaiguṇyamiti samarthante | satyamevaṃ, aucityamaṅgīkṛtyaitaduktam

ye ucitācārāstraivarṇikāḥ te tāvat śilpopajīvino na saṃbhavantītyanenā-
bhiprāyeṇaitaduktam | yata evaucityāśrayaṇenaitaduktaṃ na punassarvathā śilpopajīvi-
tvāsaṃvena ata eva brāhmaṇādīnāṃ niyatakālataivādhānasya ādhānāntarā-
saṃbandhe heturuktaḥ
 | vasantādikālataivādhānasya ādhānāntarāsaṃbandhe heturuktaḥ |
sarveṣāmeva traivarṇikānāmaviśeṣeṇa vasantādikālakādhānaśravaṇānnādhānāntarasaṃbandho yukta
ityarthaḥ | iyamapi cābhyupagamyayuktireva | rathakaraṇena nimittenādhānāntarasaṃbhavāt |
paramayuktimāha---jātyantaropanyāsaśca traivarṇikanimittaḥ paramārthato heturuktaḥ
 |
rūḍhyaiva rathakāraśabdena jātyantarasyābhidhānaṃ yogabalenaiva traivarṇikānām | rūḍhiśca
yogādbalīyasīti | evaṃ hi smṛtiḥ---“māhiṣyeṇa karaṇyāṃ tu rathakāraḥ prajāyate”

iti vaiśyāṅganāyāṃ kṣatriyajo māhiṣya ityucyate | tadāhuḥ---

“māhiṣyograu prajāyete viṭa्śūdrāṅganayornṛpāt”
 | karaṇaśca śūdrāyāṃ vaiśyena
jātaḥ | taduktaṃ ‘śūdrāyāṃ karaṇo vaiśyāt’
 iti | tena māhiṣyena karaṇyāṃ jātasya
rūḍhyā rathakāraśabdo vācaka iti tasyaivedamādhānam | yat punaḥ aviduṣo nādhāna-
mityuktam asati vacane tadupapadyate vacane punaḥ kā nāmeyamanupapattiḥ
---yadā

[p. 086]

śrṛṅgagrāhikayā rathakārasyādhānaṃ vihitaṃ tadā kācidanupapattirnāstītyarthaḥ | vidyāpi
tadbalena bhaviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ | yadā vidyāpyasti tadā vidyālābhāya kimanupanayane-
nāpi teṣāṃ saudhanvanāṃ bhavatu?
 uttaraṃ---yadi vacanamasti bhavatu upanayanādikaṃ
asati vidhāne strīṇāmivādhānavidhānādevāyaṃ bhaviṣyati
 | tāvanmātreṇai-
vādhānavidhānopapatteḥ nopanayanākṣepe sāmarthyamastīti | adhikāralakṣaṇamadhikaraṇamiti
lakṣaṇasaṅgatisiddhaye adhikāraviśeṣādhāyakenānena bhavitavyamiti pṛcchati---kasya
punaratra rathakārādhāne’dhikāraḥ ? rathakārasyeti brūmaḥ | rājña iva rājasūye
 | atra
codayati---nanu cāyaṃ kartṛnirdeśo nādhikāranirdeśaḥ
 varṣāsu rathakāra ādadhīta
iti rathakāraśabdaḥ kartṛnirdeśaḥ rathakārajāterupādeyakartṛnirdeśaḥ rathakārajāterupādeya-
tvānnādhikāranirdeśaḥ | ekagranthenāha---yathaiva brāhmaṇādayo vasantādikālakeṣu
kartṛtvena nirdiśyante nādhikṛtatayā evamatrāpi
 | rathakārādhikāravādyāha---yadi
brāhmaṇādayaḥ kartṛtvena nirdiśyante nādhikṛtatayā evaṃ tarhi tatkartṛkeṣvādhāneṣu
kasyādhikāraḥ ?
 ayamāśayaḥ---yathā brāhmaṇādīnāmadhikāraḥ tathā rathakārasyāpi
bhaviṣyati | adhikārāpavādyāha---na kasyaciditi brūmaḥ
 | adhikāriṇo’śravaṇāditi |
rathakārādhikāravādyāha---kiṃ tarhīdamuktaṃ traivarṇikānāmadhikāra iti
 | apaśūdrādhi-
karaṇe traivarṇikānāmadhikāra ityuktam | tenopādeyāpi brāhmaṇādijātiḥ adhikāri-
viśeṣaṇameveti manyate | uttaraṃ---svargakāmasya traivarṇikasya jyotiṣṭomādiṣva-
dhikāra ityuktam
 | jyotiṣṭomādiṣu svargakāmo’dhikārīti sthitam | sa tu svargakāmo
brāhmaṇādirnānya ityuktam | na punarjātirevādhikāriviśeṣaṇam | adhikāravādyāha---
yadi niradhikāraṃ evaṃ tarhi aṅgamādhānaṃ
 kratūnāṃ syāt | itara āha---nāṅgaṃ agnaya-

[p. 087]
 
rthatvāt
 | asyārthaḥ---niradhikāratve’pyādhānasya na karmārthatvam agnayarthatvenāvagamāt |
nanu karmārthāgnimukhena avadhātādivat karmārthatā na syāt ityatrāha agnayo hi
karmārthāḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---agnayaḥ siddhāḥ santaḥ karmaṇi viniyujyante na puna-
rviniyuktānāmevotpattiriti tṛtīye pradarśitam | tena karmāsaṃbandhāgnayutpādakatvānna
karmārthatā ādhānasya | yadyanaṅgamādhānaṃ kathaṃ tarhi traivarṇikasvargakāmo jyoti-
ṣṭomādiṣvadhikriyata iti śakyate vaktum anaṅgatvādādhānasya
 | yadi
hi aṅgamādhānaṃ syāt tadā’traivarṇikasyāśakyatvāt traivarṇikasyādhikāraḥ syāt |
uttaramāha---anagnitvādatraivarṇikānāmanadhikāra ityuktam | na punaḥ traivarṇikā
evādhikriyanta iti
 | asyārthaḥ---yadyapyanaṅgamādhānaṃ tathāpyatraivarṇikasyānadhi-
kāraḥ | asyārthaḥ---jātiradhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ na bhavati ityevaṃvidhe’bhyupagame sidhya-
tyeva jyotiṣṭomādiṣu adhikāraḥ
 svargakāmādīnām | anupādeyaviśeṣaṇaviśi-
ṣṭānāmadhikāriṇāṃ vidyamānatvāt | ādhāne punaḥ
 tadvidhānasyāparasyāśravaṇāt jāte-
ścādhikāriviśeṣaṇatvābhāvābhyupagamāt kasyādhikāraḥ
 | na ca niradhikāraṃ
bhaviṣyatīti vācyam, niradhikārasya karmaṇo’nuṣṭhānāpatteḥ | ekagranthenāha---sarva-
syeti prāpte jātyaṅgāsaṃpatteḥ traivarṇikānāmeva bhavati | yathā svārājya-
kāmasya kṣatriyasyaivādhikāro rājasūye śrūyate
 | ‘rājasūyena svārājyakāmo
yajeta’
 iti rājavyatirikte kartari rājajātyasaṃpratipatteḥ rājña evādhikāraḥ
tathehāpi brāhmaṇajātisaṃpattaye brāhmaṇādīnāmādhāne’dhikāraḥ | yadi brāhmaṇajāti-
saṃpattaye brāhmaṇādīnāmadhikāra ādhāne evaṃ tarhi
 atrāpi rathakārādhāne’pi evaṃ

jātyasaṃpatteḥ rathakārasyaivādhikāra iti siddham
 | tena sādhūktaṃ rathākārasyeti brūma
iti | āhitāgnitayā ca jyotiṣṭomādiṣvadhikāro draṣṭavyaḥ
 | yadā rathakāra

[p. 088]
 
ādhāne’dhikārī tadā agnimān jātaḥ agnimattā ca jyotiṣṭomādiṣvapi tasyādhikāra-
mākṣipati | ākṣiptādhikāranirvāhāya vidyā prayujyata iti labdhavidyo rathakāro jyoti-
ṣṭomādi kariṣyati | kathaṃ punaḥ karmādhikāraṃ vinā ādhānādhikāro na paryavasyati |
svarūpamātreṇāpuruṣārthatvāditi | nanu ca brahmasāmādīnāmabhāvāt kathaṃ rathakārasya
jyotiṣṭomādiṣvadhikāra ityatrāha---yāni punarviśeṣavacanāni rāyovājīyaṃ
ityevamādīti tānyasya na saṃbhavanti na punaradhikāraṃ nivartayitumutsahante
 | 
yeṣāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ viśeṣavacanamasti teṣāṃ viśeṣeṇa brahmasāmādīni | yeṣāṃ tu
nāsti teṣāmaniyamaḥ na punaradhikāra eva tāvannirākṛtaḥ | niradhikāravādī adhikā-
ravādinaṃ pṛcchati---yadi tāvat ādhāne’dhikāraḥ paryeṣaṇīyaḥ tadā kiṃ punarādhāna-
vidheḥ viśvajita iva kalpya evādhikāro na bhavati
 | asyārthaḥ---yathā viśva-
jiti agnividyāsādhye tadvattayā traivarṇikānāmevādhikāro nāṅgīkriyate jāteradhikāri-
viśeṣaṇatvānupapatteḥ, tathā ādhāne’pi na jātiradhikāriviśeṣaṇībhavitumarhati iti
kalpya evādhikāro yuktaḥ tatra dravyārjaṃnavat agnayutpādakādhāne agnayarthina eva
eeśvaryalakṣaṇādhikāraḥ kimiti na bhavati niyojyatā tvatra nāstyeva niyogābhāvāditi |
uttaramāha---bhavatu na kaściddoṣaḥ
 tathāpyadhikaraṇasya lakṣaṇasaṅgatisti | rathakārasyai-
vāgnayarthino’dhikārāt | evaṃ tāvat adhikāra(na)maṅgīkṛtya paramatamuktaṃ saṃprati
svamatamāha---kintvatrāvadhātādīnāmiva niradhikāramevedaṃ sa prayojanatvādanu-
ṣṭhānamicchanti
 | asyārthaḥ---nātrādhikārakalpanayā prayojanaṃ niradhikāratve’pyanu-
ṣṭhānasiddheḥ agnibhiḥ prayojanabhūtaiḥ prayojanavattvāt | agnīnāṃ ca karmārthatvāt karmabidhayai
[vidhinaiva]vānuṣṭhānaṃ prayujyate iti kimadhikārakalyanayā prayojanam ? niradhikāramevā-
dhānam | na ca lakṣaṇasaṅgatirnāsti; ādhāne kartṛtayā rathakārasyāgnivattayā jyotiṣṭo-
mādiṣvadhikārapratipādanāt kartṛtaivādhāne rathakārasyānyathā nopapadyate svarūpamātrasyā-
gnīnāmapuruṣārthatvāditi | śeṣamuktam
 |

[p. 089]
 
|| sthāpatiḥ ||

atrāpi vidvattāpekṣayā ādhānāpekṣayā ca pūrvapakṣavādī ṣaṣṭhīsamāsa-
meva nyāyyaṃ manyate
 | asyārthaḥ-yadā sthapatiścāsau niṣādaśceti karmadhārayo gṛhyate
tadā niṣādasyaivādhikāra ityāpadyate | yadi ṣaṣṭhīsamāsastadā niṣādavyatiriktasya |
tatra ca niṣādasyaivādhikāro na ghaṭate, vidyāvirahādagnayabhāvācca | ato vidyāpekṣayā
agnayapekṣayā ca ṣaṣṭhīsamāsa eva yuktaḥ | bhavati hi kaścit traivarṇika eva niṣā-
dānāṃ svāmī
 | sthapatiśabdaḥ svāmivacanaḥ ato
 vidyāgnayanurodhāt tasyaiva
traivarṇikasyādhikāraḥ | atrābhidhīyate | nāyaṃ ṣaṣṭhīsamāsaḥ | kasmāt ?
uttaraṃ-ṣaṣṭhīsamāse hi niṣādaśabdo lakṣaṇayā sthapatau vartate | karmadhāraye punaḥ
svārtha eva padadvayaṃ
 vartate | asyārthaḥ-yaḥ śabdaḥ śrūyate sa evārthapratipattau nimittaṃ
bhavati | na ca samāse niṣādasthapatau ṣaṣṭhī śrūyate | kintu niṣādaśabda eva kevalaṃ, sa
ca śrutyā jātiviśeṣaṃ pratipādayati | na tu jātiviśeṣasaṃbandhinam | ṣaṣṭhīsamāsa-
pakṣe ca niṣādasaṃbandhinaḥ kārye’nvayo na niṣādasya | tatra niṣādaśabdenaiva niṣādasaṃbandhī
sthapatiḥ lakṣaṇayopasthāpanīyaḥ | karmadhāraye punaḥ niṣādasyaivānvayaḥ | niṣādaśabdaśca
śrutyaiva niṣādaṃ śaknoti vaditum | śrutilakṣaṇāviśaye ca śrutirnyāyyeti karmadhāraya
evāyaṃ na ṣaṣṭhīsamāsaḥ
 | idānīṃ pūrvapakṣamāviṣkaroti---nanu cedamanyāyyamucyate

lakṣaṇāyā vartata iti yāvatā śrutyaiva jñāyata iti bhāvaḥ | kathamanyāyyam ?
taddarśayati---vāvacanaṃ hi samarthayatā bhāṣyakāreṇa vākyavṛttyorekārthatvamabhyupa-
gataṃ bhagavatā pāṇininā
 | samāsādhikāre ‘ve’
ti smṛtam | samāsa eva nityaṃ

[p. 090]

mābhūt vākyamapi yathā syādityevamarthaḥ | tatra yadi samāsasya vākyasya cārthabhedo
bhavet tadā arthabhedādeva samāsena vākyasya nivṛttirnāstīti kiṃ vāśabdena iti
matvā bhāṣyakāreṇa vāvacanaṃ samarthayitukāmena vākyasamāsayorekārthatvamuktam |
tatra yadi ṣaṣṭhīsamāse ṣaṣṭhyarthavācakaśśabdo nāsti tadā vākyasamāsayorekārthatva-
meva na syāt | asti caikārthatā | ato’nyāyyamevedaṃ lakṣaṇayā vartata iti |
rāddhāntī tu vadati---kena vā’nekārthatvamuktam
 lakṣaṇābhyupagamepi anekārthatā
nāstīti bhāvaḥ | bhāvamabuddhvā pṛcchati---nanvidamuktaṃ niṣādaśabdo lakṣaṇayā
vartata iti
 | pariharati---prayojanamaṅgīkṛtyaitaducyate
-‘kena vā’nekārthatva-
mukta’
miti yadidamasmābhirucyate tatprayogamaṅgīkṛtya | etaduktaṃ bhavati |’

 | vākye
savibhaktikasya śabdasya, samāse vibhaktirahitasya ekasminnevārthe prayogaḥ | tena
vākyasamāsayorekārthatvamucyata iti | etadevāha---atra ṣaṣṭhīsamāse sthapatau

niṣādasaṃbandhini niṣādaśabdaḥ prayujyate | tasya ca lakṣaṇayā prayogasiddhiḥ |

idamākūtaṃ---vākyasamāsayorekārthatvameva prakāśamānaṃ bhidyate | vākye vācaka-
vṛttyā’rthapratipādakatvaṃ, samāse lakṣaṇāvṛttyeti | codayati---kiṃ lakṣayati ?

niṣādaśabdaḥ | uttaraṃ---ṣaṣṭhyarthaṃ lakṣayati | nanu ca yadi ṣaṣṭhyarthaṃ lakṣayati evaṃ sati
vyadhikaraṇasamāsānāṃ sādhutvānvākhyānamanupapannam | vācakaniyamārthaṃ hi
bhagavato’nuśāsanaṃ pāṇineḥ pravṛttam
 | apabhraṃśānu1vācakaśabdayoḥ pravṛttayoḥ vāca-
kāneva niyamayituṃ bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ śabdānuśāsanaṃ pravṛttam | lakṣaṇāpakṣe ca nāyaṃ
vācakaḥ
 tataścāvācakatvādanuśāsanaṃ na yujyate | anuśāsti cācāryaḥ | tenāva-
gacchāmo vācaka eva samāso’pīti | lakṣaṇāvṛttipakṣe ca vāvacanānarthakyaṃ prakāra-
1. anuvācakāpabhraṃśayoḥ---mā

[p. 091]

bhedenaiva vākyasamāsayoḥ prayogopapatteḥ | tena vāvacanasyārthavattvāt vācakatvamevā-
śrayaṇīyamiti | tadāha---ata eva ca kātyāyano’pi vāvacanānarthakyamukta-
vāniti | tasmādvaktavyo viśeṣaḥ
 samāsasyāpabhraṃśāt gāvyādeḥ yena [a]vāca-
katvepi samāso’nuśiṣyate na gāvyādayaḥ śabdā iti | na cāsti viśeṣaḥ anyo
vācakatvādṛte iti | rāddhāntī vadati---nanu cāyameva viśeṣaḥ lakṣaṇayāpi
vyavahāro loke dṛśyate gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ prativasati kūpena gargakūlamiti
 |
asyārthaḥ-yeṣāṃ loke prayogaḥ tadanuśāsanaṃ vyākaraṇam | lakṣaṇayāpi ca loke
vyavahāro’sti | tena lakṣaṇayā vṛttasyāpi anuśāsanaṃ [na]virudhyate | gāvyādayastu
loke na prayujyanta iti prayogāprayogāveva viśeṣaḥ | tenānuśāsanama[na]nuśāsanaṃ
ceti | atra pūrvapakṣī---nāyaṃ prayogo’nuśāsanavirodhī
 | asyārthaḥ-vācanikā-
nāmevānvākhyānāya yat pravṛttamanuśāsanaṃ tadayaṃ gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa ityādiprayogo na
ruṇaddhi | yadi hyayaṃ prayogo lakṣaṇayā vṛttasyāpyanuśāsanamāpādayet tato vācanikānā-
mevānuśāsanamiti viruddhayeta | asmin prayoge lakṣaṇāvṛttasyānuśāsanāpattiḥ | kutaḥ ?
padāvadhikaṃ hi bhagavato’nuśāsanam

yatredameva vadanti tatra nimittaṃ bhavatyeva vācakamidamiti vācakasyaivānuśāsanamiti
nānuśāsanavirodhaḥ | nanu ca lakṣaṇāpekṣamevānuśāsanamayaṃ prayoga āpādayati | lakṣaṇā-
vṛttādeva gaṅgāśabdādvibhaktyanuśāsanāt iti atrāha---na ca vācakasaṃbandhinya eva
vibhaktayaḥ iti smarati, prātipadikāditi vacanāt
 | vibhaktiḥ vācikaivānuśiṣṭā

vadati---asadidamuktaṃ na

lakṣyata ityuktameva | na hi ṣaṣṭhyā’nabhidhīyamānasya ṣaṣṭhyarthatā bhavati | tena...lakṣitaṃ

ata eva suktaṃ yadi ṣaṣṭhyā’bhidhānaṃ lakṣyata ityuktaṃ syāt syāda्doṣaḥ
 |
alakṣitāyāmapi ṣaṣṭhyāṃ .... .... .... .... .... ṣaṣṭhyarthaṃ ityucyate | ato’nyat
ṣaṣṭhyarthatvena darśanāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu ca lakṣaṇāpakṣe “tadbhaūtānāṃ kriyārthatvena samāmnāyaḥ”

iti virudhyate yathā yāni hi padāni yadarthapratipādakatayā lokaprasiddhāni tathābhūtānyeva
vākyārthaṃ pratipādayantītyuktam | tat tyaktaṃ syāt gaṅgādipadānāṃ kūlārthe prayogā-

 iti tadbhaūtānā-
mityabhyupagamo na viruddhayate

lakṣaṇāyāmapi prasiddhamevārthamabhidadhadeva padaṃ vākyārthamavagamayati | ayaṃ tu
viśeṣaḥ---kvacitsvena rūpeṇābhidhānaṃ kvacittenaivābhihito’rtha iti nābhyupagamavirodhaḥ |
nanu yadi loke ṣaṣṭhyartho lākṣaṇikaḥ kathaṃ tarhi bhagavān ṣaṣṭhīprasaṅge hi lopānuśāsanāt
lopasya ṣaṣṭhyāśca samānārthatā syādeveti atrāha---na hi bhagavato’nuśāsana-
virodhaḥ prayogānuśāsanaṃ hi vyākaraṇam | kvacidabhidhānena nirūpyate
kvacidekārthatayeti na kaścida्doṣaḥ
 | kvacidabhidhānavṛttyā prayogaḥ kvacillakṣaṇā-
vṛttyā prayogaḥ vyākaraṇe’nuśiṣṭaḥ | tenāyamarthaḥ---vākyasthāne’pi samāsaḥ prayoktavyaḥ |
ekārthā ca lakṣaṇāvṛttirabhipretā abhidhānena sahaikaprayojanatvāt | yata evobhayathāpi
prayogaḥ ata eva bhāṣyakāraḥ asmadāyatte prayoge kimityavācakaṃ prayokṣyāmahe

iti | na hi aprasaktau avācakaprayogasya etadupapadyate tasmādavācakasyāpyasti prayoga-
siddhiḥ | yadyubhayorapi prayogaprasaktiḥ tarhi ko viśeṣo vācakāvācakaprayogayoḥ ?

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ

puruṣārthaika
 | pūrvapakṣavyaktyasaṃbhavena saṃśayamākṣipati---nanu ca svargakāmādhi-
karaṇa idamuktam
---naiveyamādhikārikī saṅkhyā, kiṃ tarhi ? kartṛsaṅkhyai-
veyam bhāvāntarānupādānāt iti | evaṃ ca kathaṃ pariṣado’rthinyā adhikāra
iti saṃśayaḥ |
 asyārthaḥ - yadya dhikārikī saṅkhyā syāt tadā adhikāra eva
bahutve sati pariṣado’rthinyā adhikāraḥ syāt naiveyamādhikārikā saṅkhyā | kiṃ
tarhi ? kartṛsaṅkhyaiveyamiti
 svargakāmādhikaraṇe darśitam | kutaḥ punariyamādhi-
kārikī saṅkhyā na bhavati bhāvāntarānupādānāt
 | yāga eva kevalo bhāvānta
upāttaḥ | tadapekṣeyamākhyātagatā saṅkhyā | na ca yāge puruṣasya niyojyatvaṃ, kiṃ
tarhi ? kartṛtvam | tena kartṛtve saṅkhyaiveyaṃ na niyojyasaṅkhyā | yaśca niyojyassa
evādhikārī iti | adhikāriṇo’pi neyaṃ saṅkhyā tena saṃśaya evāyamanupapannaḥ |
ucyate | uparitane’dhikaraṇe ākhyātagatasaṅkhyā vivakṣitā sthāsyati |
sthitāyāṃ ca kartṛsaṅkhyāvivakṣāyāṃ kiṃ yathākartṛtvamadhikāro bhavati |
utānyathaiva kartṛtvamanyathācādhikāra iti
 | etadeva vivṛṇoti---na kartṛgatā
saṅkhyā adhikārepi itaretarayogamāpādayituṃ kṣametyarthaḥ | tatra pūrvapakṣa-
vādino’bhiprāyaḥ
 | adhikārasya kāmanāyogāt kartṛtvamukhena cādhi-
1. 1387

[p. 096]

kārāvāpteḥ 1adhikāra[śca bhāgena] itaravyāptayā ca niyamyate | kāmitayā
cādhikāra iti pariṣado’rthinyā adhikāra ityuktam
 | asyārthaḥ - yo’yamṛddhi-
kāmādhikārastasya kāmanāyogāt kāmanāsaṃbandhāt kāmanāhetutvāditi yāvat | yaśca
kāmanāhetuḥ so’dhikāraḥ | tatra kāmasya siddhiḥ avikārārthā | tasya prāptiḥ
karturmukhena kartuḥ phalaprāptirityarthaḥ | kartṛtve vā itaretarayoge phalamapi itaretarayuktā-
nām yathābhūtā phalāvāptiḥ tathābhūtaiva kāmanāpyāśrayaṇīyā | tena bhāgaśaḥ kāmanayā
pariṣadaḥ ṛddhikāmanā adhikāriviśeṣaṇam | yathābhūtā kāmanā tathābhūta evādhikāra
iti pariṣado’rthinyā adhikāra iti | atrābhidhīyate | na kartṛtvaṃ karmaprāptiṃ
niyantuṃ śaknoti | yathābhidhānamāptimavagacchāmo na punaryathāpadārthāntara-
saṃbandham
 | yādṛśamabhidhānaṃ svargakāmādikaṃ tādṛśaṃ,[na]punaḥ svargakāmādipadāt
svargaikadeśakāmanā gamyate | kintu kṛtsnakāmanā | tena kṛtsna eva svargaḥ sarveṣāṃ
bhavati | na punaḥ padārthāntarabhūtakartranusāreṇa phalāvāptirvarṇanīyā | kutaḥ? svargakāma-
padāvagamyatvāt | tasyā avagatāvā(a)pteśca kartṛsaṃbandhāt
 svargakāmapadārtha-
phalāvāptiravagamyate avagatāvāptiścādhikārī kartā bhavati | tenottarakālabhāvi kartṛtvaṃ,
na kartṝṇāmitaretarayoga ityarthaḥ | evaṃ tāvat itaretarayuktānāmeva kartṛtvaṃ na kartṝṇābhi-
taretarayoga iti kṛtvā’dhikaraṇaṃ varṇitam | saṃprati kṛtvācintāmuddhāṭayati---kiñca
na kartṛtvamapi atra nirapekṣatāṃ vihanti kartṝṇāṃ hyatra bahutvamavagamyate na
bahūnāṃ kartṛtvam | kimato yadyevaṃ
 ? etadato bhavati | kartāro bahavaḥ na

[p. 097]

bahavaḥ kartā
 | adhiṣṭhānamātrabheda eva kartṛtvaṃ tu na bhidyata ityevaṃ na bhavati kiṃ
tarhi sahādhiṣṭhānena kartṛtvameva bhidyata ityarthaḥ | tena kārtsnyenaikaikasya kartṛtvāt
ekaikasya kṛtsnaṃ phalamiti | yathā kartradhikāre’pi phalāvāptau na pariṣado’dhikāraḥ kiṃ
tarhi ekaikasyaiveti | atra bhāṣyeṇa sahakriyamāṇe satra iti vadatā sahakartṛtvamuktam
sahakartṛtve naikaikasya kartṛtvamityanenābhiprāyeṇa codayati---nanu sahakartṛtva-
matreti
 | pariharati---sahaśabdo’trārthasahatvaṃ lakṣayati
 | asyārthaḥ - yadyapyatra-
śabda[taḥ] kartṝṇāṃ sahatvaṃ na pratīyate tathāpi arthasahatvamatrāpyastītyetadabhiprāyeṇa
sahatvamiti | kutaḥ etat ? bhāṣyādeva
 | yata eva mahābhāṣyakāraḥ [āha]---ekaika-
ścātra karteti | yuktiṃ cāha---ekaikasya svātantryavivakṣāyāṃ
 kartṛtvavivakṣāyāṃ
ākhyāte saṅkhyāyogāt
 saṅkhyāvācitvāt | kiñca yadyatraika eva kartā syāt tadā
ekavacanameva syāt na bahuvacanam tena bahavaḥ kartāraḥ | tathā’paramapi bhāṣyakāreṇoktam-
śaknotyekaikasya svātantryavivakṣāyāṃ---iti
 | tato’pi gamyate ekaikaḥ karteti |
ekaikasya svātantryavivakṣāyāṃ ākhyāte saṅkhyāyogāditi yaduktaṃ tadupapādayitumāha---
nyāyyā ceyamavagatiḥ
 kartāro bahava iti | yasmācchabdāt vibhaktayo bhavanti

tadarthasya saṅkhyāyogāditi yaduktaṃ tadupapādayituṃ avagamayantīti
 || utsargastā-
vadayam | nacākhyātārthaḥ saṅkhyāṃ bhajate | asattvabhūtattvāt atastadākṣiptāni
vyāvṛttānyeva kārakāṇi saṅkhyāṃ bhajante tadavasthānāmevākhyātārthenākṣepāt
na kārakaśabdābhidheyāni
 kārakāṇāṃ dravyāṇāṃ ye śabdāḥ tadabhidheyāni dravyamātrāṇi
na saṅkhyāṃ bhajante ityarthaḥ | nāmavibhaktayaḥpunaḥ saṅkhyābhājaḥ prātipadikārthā

[p. 098]

eveti tadgatāmeva
 prātipadikārthagatāmeva saṅkhyāmabhinirvartayanti | ata evāgnī-
ṣomau devatā, devatātvaṃ hi kārakāvasthā, tasyāmavasthāyāmagnīṣomaprātipadi-
kādutpannā dvivacanavibhaktiḥ na saṅkhyāmāha kintu prātipadikāvasthāmātrāyā-
miti | ata evādhiṣṭhānamātrasya dvitvaṃ, devatātve punarekatvamiti
 eta-
ddarśitam---satyaṃ hi vai devateti vadatā | nanu karmaśabdādapi vibhaktirbhavantī karmasaṅkhyā-
māha na kārakasaṅkhyāmityāśaṅkāmapākareti-utpattau ceyaṃ saṅkhyā | ataḥ kārakagataiva
mantavyā | kimidamutpattāviti
 ? ākhyātagateyaṃ saṅkhyā nopapadagatetyarthaḥ |
tathāpi kimityāha---upapadagatā hi karmagatāpi pratīyate sattvavadabhidhānāt
tisra āhutīrjuhotītivat
 | ākhyātagatā tu asattvabhūtatvāt ākhyātārthasya
tadgatā saṃbhavantī tadākṣiptakārakagatā bhavati | evamapi samabhivyāhāro na dūrānta-
ritaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ | tasmādekaikasya svātantryamavagamyate svatantraḥ
karteti ca smaraṇāt
 | tasmādekaikasya kartṛtvamiti | nanu bahutvaśravaṇāt sāpe-
kṣāṇāṃ kartṛtvamavagamyata ityatrāha---bahutvaṃ punaḥ 1kartuḥ sāpekṣatāmāpādayati

1. kartṛsāpekṣatāṃ

[p. 099]

kintu tadākṣiptena kartrā kathaṃ tarhi yugapadabhidhānaṃ ? kathaṃ ca ekaśeṣaḥ ?

ye hi ekaśeṣeṇa yugapadabhidhīyante teṣāmeva vibhaktibhiḥ saṅkhyocyata iti
bhāvaḥ | pariharati---mābhūt yugapadabhidhānaṃ yugapadākṣepo bhaviṣyati
 tathāpi
yugapadavagamasiddheḥ | ekaśeṣastu yadyabhidhānādṛte na saṃbhavati
 śabdaviṣayatvaṃ
tanna nāma
 bhavatu tathāpyayameva śāstrārthaḥ nedaṃ tantraṃ ekadeśābhihitasyaiva saṅkhyā-
yoga iti pramāṇābhāvāditi manyate ataḥ yathā kartṛ
tvepyekaikasya kṛtsnaphalaprāpteḥ
na pariṣado’rthinyā adhikāra iti siddham

|| prayoge ||

atra bhāṣyaṃ---“nanu tṛtīye uktaṃ śāstraphalaṃ prayoktari”
 ityādi | tadākṣi-
pati---kimidaṃ śāstraphalaṃ prayoktarīti kastenāsya virodhaḥ ? kathaṃ vā
tenedaṃ labhyate
 yadi hi tenāsya virodho bhavet punaruktatā vā tadā yujyeta ‘nanu
ca tṛtīye uktamiti’
| na caitad‌dvayamapyasti iti | samādhatte---yadā prayoktṛtvaṃ
karmaṇi śabdārthaḥ tadā bacanasya vivakṣā prāpnoti | vivakṣite vacane aviva-
kṣitābhidhānaṃ viruddham
 | asyārthaḥ---śāstraphalaṃ prayoktarīti prayoktṛtvaṃ karmaṇi
śabdevocyata ityuktam | prayoktṛtve sati tasyopādeyatvāt saṅkhyāyā apekṣitatvena
vivakṣā prāpnoti | tataścāniyatasaṅkhyānāṃ yāgāśaṅkā virudhyata iti virodhāmi-
prāyeṇākṣepaḥ | yata eva virodhābhiprāyeṇākṣepaḥ atassamādhānakāle idamuktaṃ “tadevā-
kṣipyata”
 iti | kathaṃ punarākṣepaḥ ? niyojyasya hi karmaṇi ‘idamasya’

ktam
 | nanu svargakāma ityekavacanaśravaṇāt saṅkhyāyuktasyaivādhikāra iti atrāha---

 tadanukāritvāt svargakāmo

tathāpi kartā kimiti eko na bhavatītyatrāha---niyojyasya bhāvārthasaṃbandhaḥ
kartṛtvamityetaduktaṃ na bhāvasaṃbandhasya niyoga ityarthaḥ | tasmānna bhāvagatā
saṅkhyā vibakṣāmarhati adhikārapadagateva
 | yadi hi adhikārapadagatā vivakṣyeta
tadā liṅgamapi vivakṣitaṃ syāt iti striyā adhikāro na syāt | kiñca phalasya

paratvādbākyasya
 | bhavatvanyaparaṃ vākyaṃ tathāpi kimityāha---ekārthaṃ caikaṃ vākya-
mityuktam | ato viṣaṃ bhakṣayediti ca na vivakṣyate

iti | ato na vivakṣitetyucyate | pratipattibhramastu sāmānyato dṛṣṭanibandhanaḥ syāt |

na karma vā saṅkhyāpyavivakṣitā śakyate vaktum
 | yathā karma avivakṣitamiti
na śakyate vaktuṃ na tathā saṅkhyāpītyarthaḥ | kutaḥ ? viśiṣṭa eva hi karmaṇyadhi-
kāro bhavati yathā dravyaviśiṣṭakarmaṇyadhikārāvagamāt na dravyāntareṇa kriyate
tathā kartṛsaṅkhyāviśiṣṭe karmaṇyapyadhikārāvagame na saṅkhyāntaramāśrīyate

viśiṣṭaṃ yatkārakaṃ kartrākhyaṃ tasyā yā saṅkhyā tayā viśiṃṣṭa caitatkarma tena tadviśiṣṭatā-
saṃpattaye saṅkhyā ādaraṇīyetyarthaḥ | nanu ca bhavatu viśiṣṭaṃ karma tathāpi vākyaṃ
tatparaṃ na bhavati ityatrāha---na ca svargaḥpuruṣasyetyevaṃparaṃ tadvākyaṃ kiṃ tarhi
asmin karmaṇi asyādhikāra iti

|| prakramāttu ||
kathaṃ punaratra saṃśayaḥ ? katha vā na saṃśayaḥ ? phalaparyantaḥ kāmādhikāra
ityuktam
 | tatra yadi kāraṇāntareṇa phalaprāptau satyāṃ nivṛttaḥ kāmaḥ
 kathañcidvā
anyato
 doṣadarśanādeḥ tadadhikāra eva nivṛtto bhavati | niradhikārasya
cānuṣṭhānaṃ na śabdāt nāpyanyato’vagacchāmaḥ | tasmādapratiṣṭhitamevemaṃ
saṃśaya paśyāmaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ-na nimittaparyantaḥ kāmādhikāraḥ kintu phalaparyanta
ityuktam | yadi nimittaparyantaḥ syāt tadā kāmanā utpannā satī nimittamiti
nivṛttāyāmapi kāmanāyāṃ prayogaḥ parisamā[pyaḥ](pta) syāt | phalaparyante tu kāmanā-
nivṛttau puruṣasya karmaṇyaiśvaryanivṛttiriti kinnibandhanamanuṣṭhānam | ato vītāyāmapi
phalecchāyāṃ prayogaparisamāptisaṃbhāvanā’nupapanneti | ucyate | idamatra saṃśayadvāram |
kartavyatāpūrvakaṃ hi karmādīnāṃ phalapuruṣasaṃbandhamuktavanto’dhikārajñāḥ
 |
asyārthaḥ-yo’yaṃ phalapuruṣayoḥ sambandhaḥ sa kartavyatājñānapūrvakaḥ na punaḥ phalapuruṣa-
saṃbandhajñānapūrvikā kartavyatāvagatiḥ | phalakāmasya hi puruṣasya vidhyarthaḥ kartavya-
tayā’vagamyamānaḥ kartavya eva na bhavati yadi phalasiddhirnākṣipatīti | evaṃ phala-
puruṣayoḥ sambandho’vagamyate na punaranyathā | tena phalapuruṣasaṃbandhāvagamāt prāgeva
tāvat kartavyatā’vagamyate | tathāpi kimityāha-pravṛttaścet kartavyatāyāṃ kāritaḥ
śāstreṇetyavagacchāmaḥ
 | kartavyatājñānena karmakartavyatāvagamanibandhane’nuṣṭhāne
pravṛttiḥ kartavyatāvagame jāte tanmātranibandhanatvāttadanuṣṭhānasya pravṛttaḥ puruṣaḥ śāstreṇaiva

[p. 104]

pravṛttiyogyamarthaṃ jñāpayatā puruṣo’nuṣṭhānaṃ kāryate | nānyathā | tena kartavyatayā
vidhyarthaṃ bodhitavatā śāstreṇa kāritamevānuṣṭhānamiti | nanu copakramamātre bhavatu śāstrasya
vyāpāraḥ ityatrāha---śāstraṃ copakramāderapavargaparyantasya svārthasyābhidhānam | ato

phalārthatā karmaṇo nopapadyata ityuktam
 | iṣṭalakṣaṇaṃ hi phalam | yadā tadiṣṭaṃ na

pravṛttikāle kartavyatāvagamena prayojanam | tasyāṃ cāvasthāyāṃ satyāṃ kāmanāyāmadhikāra-

ataḥ paramadhikārānuvṛttiḥ kvopayujyate
 | nivṛtte’pyadhikāre nānuṣṭhānaṃ nivartata

nāvagamyata
 iti | na punaradhikārādṛte śāstraṃ kāraṇamityavagantuṃ śakyate

manuṣṭhānam | nanu pravṛttau nimittatayopapannamanuṣṭhānamityuktam
 | uttaraṃ---nāsādhyaṃ

kāriviśeṣaṇaṃ tadā jātamātrasyāsāmarthyāt kathaṃ jātamātrasyādhikāraḥ ucyata ityāha
---nanu caivaṃ sati na jātamātrasyādhikāraḥ |
 pariharati---etaduktaṃ bhavati
nopanayanamapekṣate iti |
 anupanītaparo’yaṃ jātamātraśabda iti yāvat |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate upanayanottarakālamevādhikriyata iti | kā yuktiḥ?
svādhyāyādhyayanottarakālaṃ hi vaidikaiḥ padārthaiḥ adhikriyate | vaidikaśca
pāthagaṇa iti smṛtipāde uktam | svādhyāyādhyayanaṃ tu upanītasya |
 tenopa-
nayanottarakālamācāreṣvadhikāra iti anena bhāṣyārthasvarūpamuktam | akṣiyati-bhavatu
svādhyāyādhyayanamupanītasya | padārthāstu yathā yasyāvagamyante upanīta-
syānupanītasya vā tasyaiva te na bhavanti yathā niṣādasthapateriṣṭiḥ |
 atra
codayati-nanu ca tatra
 sthapatīṣṭau svādhyāyavirodhāt
 karmadhārayavirodhāt evamabhyu-
pagatam
 niṣādasyaivādhikāra iti | iha tu ācāragaṇe svādhyāyāvirodhāt adhīta-

[p. 114]

vedānāmevādhikāro bhaviṣyati kāmivat
 yathā kāmināmadhītavedānāmevādhikāraḥ
tathehāpi | anyathā tatrāpyanadhītavedānāmadhikāranivṛttiḥ syāt | pariharati---ayukta-
midaṃ
 adhītavedānāmevādhikāra iti pramāṇābhāvāt | yattu kāmināmivetyuktam
tatrāha---tatra hi adhītavedasaṃbhave na adhyayanaprayuktau pramāṇamasti viduṣāṃ
kāmināmityuktam | iha punaramantrasādhyatvāt ācāragaṇasya naitacchakyate
vaktuṃ
---anadhītavedo nādhikriyata iti yasmānmantraprayuktiratra nāsti ityuktam | tato-
’nadhītavedasyāpi adhikāro yuktaḥ | kiñca na copanītamātra evādhītavedo
bhaviṣyati |
 yadyadhītavedasyādhikāraḥ yadyupa/?/nītamātra evādhītavedo bhavet tata evaṃ
syāt | na caitadasti | tasmādupanītādhikāre vaidikatva/?/pradarśanaṃ śaṅakyameva | atrābhi-
dhīyate---ayamabhiprāyo bhāṣyakārasya | prāgupanayanādvidhayaḥ pituḥ dārviho-
mikasya vopalabhyante | upanayanaṃ cācāryasya |
 ayamāśayaḥ---yāvantaḥ keci-
dvidhayo vaidikāḥ te sarve upanītasya sataḥ nānupanīto’dhikārīti piturevādhikāraḥ |
dārvihomikasya vā kartu
kāmasya | bālakasyopanayane ācāryo’dhikārī kartā
ca | taduttarakāla tu saṃskṛtaviṣayā vaudikā vidhayaḥ śrūyante | tanmūlāśca
smārtā apīti te’pi taduttarakālabhāvina eveti bhagavān bhāṣyakāro
manyate
 sarvatra hi dṛṣṭānusāryanumataṃ vaidikāśca vidhayaḥ pratyakṣāḥ upanītaviṣayāḥ smṛtyanu-
mitā api tathaiva bhavitumarhantīti bhāvaḥ | tasmātsūktaṃ upanītasyādhikāra iti |

|| abhyāsaḥ ||

kathamatra pūrvapakṣamāśaṅkate ? yathā kālāṅgatvāt karmaṇo vaiguṇyamāpadyate tasmādā-
gate kāle’nuṣṭhānaṃ kartavyam tatrāha---asminnadhikaraṇe sarvaśaktyadhikaraṇe sthite
bhavati saṃśayaḥ kiṃ kālaśūnyaḥ śaucaśūnyaśca prayogo hi jīvananimittatayā
kartavyaḥ uta neti |
 asyārthaḥ---sarvaśaktayadhikaraṇe idamuktaṃ adhikārabalenāṅaga-
tyāgenānuṣṭhānaṃ kartavyamiti | tadihāpi jīvananimittakaḥ prayogaḥ kimadhikārabalena
uṅgabhūtakālaṃ parityajya kartavyaḥ ata neta cintā | tatra pūrvavādino’bhiprāyaḥ---
nimittabalenāṅagatyāgo’bhihitaḥ tasmāt yāvannimittaṃ prayogaḥ |
 asyārthaḥ---
jīvananimitto jīvanādhikāraḥ kālābhāve’pi jīvanaṃ nimittabhūtamasti iti tadbalenāṅaga-
bhūtakālaparityāgenānuṣṭhānaṃ kartavyamiti ‘‘juhudhi juhadhi’’
 ityeva hotavyamiti |

kimiti juhudhi juhudhi ityeva hotavyamiti | ābhīkṣaṇyena hotavyamityarthaḥ | tathāhi
---kriyāsamabhihāre tavyapratyayārthe juhoterloṭ‌vidhīyate tasya sthāne hiśabdādeśaḥ
sa ca loṭa ādeśaḥ | tathāca sūtraṃ---‘‘kriyāsamabhihāre loṭ loṭo hisvau vā ca
taddhvamoḥ tataśca ‘ābhīkṣaṇye dve bhavataḥ’
 iti dvirvacanam | tathā ‘yathāvidhyanu-
prayogaḥ pūrvasmin’
 nimittatavyapratyayāntasya juhoteranuprayoga iti | nanu ca āhāra-
vihārakāle na śakyate prayogaḥ kartumiti |
 pariharati---yathā śakyate
tathā’bhyasanīyaḥ
 yena prakāreṇāhāravihārakāle ca prayogaḥ śakyate kartuṃ sa prakāra
āstheyaḥ | nanu ca śaucādi tyaktaṃ syāt atrāha---śaucamapyaṅagameva |
 ataḥ kāla-
1. p. 1403

[p. 116]

śocaparityāgenāpi prayogaḥ kartavyaḥ |
 rāddhāntamāha---atrocyate nābhyā-
sāpekṣaṃ śāstram śaktasyāpi nimittāvagamopapatteḥ |
 asyārthaḥ---yathā śakyate
tathā kartavyamiti yo’yamabhyāsaḥ tadapekṣamidaṃ śāstraṃ na bhavati | sarvāṅgopasaṃhāre
śaktasyāpi nimittāvagamopapatteḥ | yadi hi sarvāṅgopasaṃhāre śaktasyādhikāre jīvanasya
nimittatāvagamo nopapadyate tadā evaṃ syāt | nacaitadasti | tasmānnāṅgabhūtaśaucakāla-
[tyāge] pramāṇamasti | nanvadhikārabalena tyajyate ityatrāha---pratipanne hi nimitte-
’ṅgatyāgo’bhihitaḥ tasminnadhikaraṇe |
 yādṛśaṃ nimittaṃ tādṛśe pratipanne sati
aṅgatyāgaḥ sarvaśaktayadhikaraṇe darśitaḥ | iha punaḥ nimittameva nābhyāsāpekṣaṃ
pratīyate |
 jīvanamātrameva nimittatayā na pratīyate | yadi hi abhyasāpekṣaṃ śāstra
evaṃ tarhi nimittaṃ pratīyeta | tadā jīvanamātrameva nimittaṃ syāt śaucānapekṣam | na caita-
dasti abhyāsābhāve’pi nimitāvagamopapatteḥ | yadi hi jīvanaśruteranadhikārāt nābhyā-
sāpekṣaṃ śāstraṃ evaṃ tarhi kālaśūnyaṃ kriyatām |
 kālasya puruṣaviśeṣaṇatvānupapatteḥ
aṅgamātratvāt tena tattyāgenāpi bhavatyanuṣṭhānam | neti brūmaḥ
 kālaśūnyaṃ nimittaṃ naiva
pratīyate nityayuktatvāt kālasya |
 nityasaṃbandha[āt] kāle karmaṇi śaktasyādhi-
kārāt tatkālameva jīvanaṃ nimittaṃ anyasmit kāle tadupasaṃhārasamarthatvāt | atra
codayati---nanu ca sarvāṇyevāṅgāni nityayuktāni |
 asyārthaḥ---sādhikāri-
sannidhau niravikāritayā śravaṇāt tadarthatvenopādīyante | na ca kaścidviśeṣaḥ kālasya
nityatvenopādānaṃ itareṣāmanityatveneti | tena sarveṣāṃ nityānvaye sati kimidaṃ
nityayuktatvātkālasyeti | pariharati---satyaṃ nityayuktāni na punaraṅgāntara-
tyāgena pratīyante |
 yasminnapi kāle tāni na santi tasminnapi kāle puruṣasya

[p. 117]

tadupasaṃhārasāmarthyamastīti satyadhikāre tattyāgenāpi yuktamanuṣṭhānam | kālaḥpunaḥ
kālāntaratyāgena nimittamavagamayati śaucamiva |
 asyārthaḥ---sarvaśaktayaghi-
karaṇena aṅgatyāgenāpyanuṣṭhāne gauṇāvayavatve prāpte - aṅgaśāstrāṇāmatyantabādho nāsti |
kālasya tu punaḥ tyāge atyantabādhāpattireva syāt niravayavatvāt kālasya śaucamiva |
yathā tasyaiva śaucaṃ aśaucaparityāgena nimittamavagamayati tathetyarthaḥ | tasmādanyathānupa-
padyamānakālānurodhena nimittatā’vasīyate | yata eva kālānurodhena nimittatā’-
vasīyate tasmānna kālaśūnyanimittamavagacchāmaḥ | na cānavagamanimittaṃ
bhavitumarhati |
 bhāṣyamākṣipati---bhāṣyakārastu kālameva nimittamāha---
"na kālo guṇaḥ nimittaṃ hyetat’’
 iti | taccāyuktam jīvanasya nimitta-
tvāt |
 nimittatve kālasya kāmye prayoge kālasyopādānaṃ na syāt anaṅga-
tvāt |
 samarthayate-tasmādayamatra bhāṣyārthaḥ---na kālo guṇo nimittamanava-
cchindannityarthaḥ |
 nimittamanavacchindannityetadadhyāhṛtya bhāṣyagrantho vyākhyeyaḥ |
nimittānavacchedako guṇo na bhavati kintu nimittāvacchedako guṇa iti yāvat |
uktanimittaṃ hyetaditi yaduktaṃ tadātmakālīnajīvananimittatvābhiprāyeṇa | na sākṣā-
nnimittatvābhiprāyeṇa | etacca tasmādāśritakālasya yāvajjīvanaṃ prayoga ityanena
bhāṣyeṇa prakaṭīkṛtam |

|| teṣāmautpattikatvāt ||

kathaṃ punaratra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ ? yāvatā nimittasyāvṛttau naimittikasya

[p. 118]

bhavitavyamiti āgate kāle iti yuktam | evamatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ---kālānu-
rodhena nimittāvagatiḥ aṅgaṃ ca kālaḥ sakṛdanuṣṭhānena kṛtamaṅgaśāstramiti
nimittamapi tathaiva, tadanurodhitvāt |
 asyārthaḥ---kālastāvadaṅgam sakṛccopasaṃhāre
kṛte kṛtamaṅgaśāstramiti na punaḥ punaraṅgaśāstramāśrayaṇīyam | kālānurodhe ca pūrvādhi-
karaṇe nimittasaṅkoco’varṇi | tena sakṛdupasaṃharaṇīyāṅgabhūtakālānurodhena nimittamapi
sakṛdeva | anyathā tadanurodhitā na syāditi kriyāvat | rāddhāntastu naitadyuktam-
sakṛdevānuṣṭhānaṃ kartavyamiti | utpadyamānaṃ hi karma kālasaṃyuktamevotpanna-
miti tatkālīnaṃ jīvanamavagamayatīti nimittamityuktam na punarvidheyatāmapi
nimittasyāpādayati |
 asyārthaḥ-yadi nimittatvamasmābhiḥ pūrvādhikaraṇe kālasyoktaṃ
tat kālasya nityayuktatvāt tadupasaṃhārasamarthasyaivādhikārāt kālāntare ca niyamena
tadupasaṃhārāsāmarthyāt balāt tatkālameva jīvanaṃ nimittamavagamayatītyanayā yuktyā |
na punaḥ nimittasya vidheyatāmapyāpādayati | avihite ca
 tasminnimitte avidheyabhūte
cetyarthaḥ | nimittāvṛttau kālopasaṃhārasāmarthye ca sati kṛtaḥ śāstrārtha iti na

 punaradhikāravṛtteḥ | tasmāt tatkālīnaṃ

|| tathāntaḥ kratu ||

nanūktaṃ nimittāvṛttau naimittikasyāvṛttiriti | tena bhedanādyāvṛttau naimittikā-
nāmapi homādīnāmāvṛttiruktaiva | kena punarviśeṣeṇa vā idaṃ punaratidiśyate
asati viśeṣe[na]tulyatā bhavati
 api tu ekataiva syāt | ayamasti viśeṣaḥ |
kratvarthatvādbhedanādinimittānāṃ homānāṃ sakṛtkṛtenaiva prayogavacanopakāra-
siddheḥ kṛto’dhikāraḥ iti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |
 asyārthaḥ-yadyapyetāni naimitti-
kāni tathāpyeṣāṃ kratvaṅgatvāt kratūpakārāyānuṣṭhānam | ekaścāyaṃ kratuprayogaḥ
sakṛtkṛtenāpi cāṅgena pradhānopakāranirvṛtteḥ nivṛttāvapi na punaranuṣṭhānam prayojanā-
bhāvāt | agnihotrādīnāṃ tu na prayogavaśenānuṣṭhānaṃ, kintu nimittabaleneti yuktaṃ
paunaḥ punyam |

rāddhāntastu satyamevaṃ kratūpakārāyaiva anuṣṭhānaṃ tathāpi punaḥ punarbheda-
nādyutpattau punaḥ punaradhikāraṃ vārayituṃ tāvadupakāro na kṣamaḥ | nanu
bhavatu punaḥ punaradhikāraḥ tathāpi niṣprayojanatvātkimadhikāraḥ kariṣyati ?

nānuṣṭhānāntaraṃ prayojayituṃ śavanotīti bhāvaḥ | pariharati---na niṣprayojanatā |
adhikārāddhi prayojanaṃ na prayojanato’dhikāra ityuktam
 anapekṣatvasiddhaye
pramāṇalakṣaṇe | asyārthaḥ-yadā adhikāraḥ āvṛttaḥ tadā vidhānamāvṛttam | yathāvihi-
tasya ca prayojanakalpanā | tena yāvadvāraṃ nimittamāpatati tāvadvāraṃ naimittikenānuṣṭhitena
kratūpakāro niṣpadyate nānyatheti kalpyate | tasmādyāvadadhikāgmatrāpi prayogaḥ
iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ yāvatpradhānaprayogaṃ hi saḥ | tadadhikāratvādasya agni-

[p. 120]

hotrādibhiḥ naimittikānām |
 iyān viśeṣaḥ te svatantrā eva prayoktavyāḥ
bhedanādihomāstu yāvat pradhānaṃ prayujyate tāvatprayoktavyāḥ pradhānādhikṛtasyaiva bheda-
nādihomeṣvadhikārāt | pradhānābhāve’dhikārābhāvāt |

|| ācārāt ||

nanu svargakāmādīnāmapi adhikāriviśeṣaṇatvāt adhikārāvṛttau prayogā-
vṛttiryukteti kathaṃ pūrvapakṣa ityāśaṅkayāha---gurvanugamanādiṣu nāgamanamadhikāri-
viśeṣaṇamiti pūrvapakṣavādī
 nimittasyāpi gurvāgamanādeḥ adhikāriviśeṣaṇatāṃ na
manyate pūrvapakṣī | nimittenāpi vākyārthe’nvayopapatteḥ gurumāgatamabhyutthānena

prīṇīyāditi vidhāṃna manyate | sakṛcca kṛtaṃ prīṇanamiti kṛtaḥ śāstrārthaḥ iti |

rāddhāntamāha---na hi adhikāriviśeṣaṇatvādṛte nimittatā saṃbhavatīti tadva-
dityatideśaḥ |
 asyārthaḥ-anuṣṭhānaṃ prati nimittabhāva eva adhikāriviśeṣaṇatvena
vinā na saṃbhavati adhikāramātranibandhanatvāt anuṣṭhānasya | tasmāt yāvat nimittaṃ
tāvat adhikāriviśeṣaṇamiti balādaṅgīkaraṇīyam | ata eva sūryasaṅkāntyādeḥ[diḥ]
puruṣasaṃbandhābhāvāt asaṃbaddhasya ca viśeṣaṇatvānupapatteḥ adhikāriviśeṣaṇatvāsaṃbhavānna
nimittam | api tu kālatayaivānvayaḥ snānādīnāmiti mantavyam | tasmādāgamana-
syādhikāriviśeṣaṇatvāt āvṛttāvadhikārāvṛtteḥ punarabhyutthānaṃ kartavyamiti |

|| brāhmaṇamya ||

nanu cātra pūrvapakṣa eva nopapadyate na hi vacanamadṛṣṭvā pūrvapakṣaḥ

 nyāyaparatvādasya śāstrasyeti | atrocyate |

 | yāvatkiñcit vākye

bhavati-siddhaṃ niṣpādakatayā sādhyaṃ phalatayeti, jīvanaṃ siddham | tasmātsādhana-
bhāva eva yukta iti manyate
 | sādhyasya tu phalatayā’nvitasyānyathā phalatvānupapatteḥ

tayā jñāpayati

 nirmūlaṃ darśana

rniyoga iti

tu niyojyasya sataḥ adhikāriṇaḥ kartṛtvaṃ tadā’nayā niyojyaviśeṣaṇameva jīvanam |

1. adhikārakalpanā viśvajinnyāyena---mātṛkā.
2. kriyāyāṃ---mā.

ścāyaṃ yāvacchabdādityuktam
 svargakāmādhikaraṇa eva | kartṛviśeṣaṇatāyā anupa-
pattiśca avyavacchedakatvāt svargavat | yathā svargasya kartṛviśeṣaṇatā na saṃbhavati
ityekādaśādye nirūpitam tathā jīvanasyāpi nājīvan kaścitkaroti | yacca sādhya-
tayā’nvitasya svargasya paścānniyojyaviśeṣaṇatvamiti tadapi nopapadyata iti darśayati---
niyogācca sādhyaṃ
 svargādiṣvapi niyojyaviśeṣaṇasyaiva sato’rthātsādhyatvamiti
darśitam | tasmānna sādhyatā niyoge niyojyaviśeṣaṇatve hetuḥ
 | nanu ca
bhavatu niyojyaviśeṣaṇatā jīvanasya tathāpi kathamavaśyakartavyatetyāha---niyojya-
viśeṣaṇaṃ cādhikāriviśeṣaṇamityuktam
 niyojya evādhikārī bhavatīti |

nopapadyata iti
 tadanusārādapi niyojyaviśeṣaṇatvameva yuktam | tasmānnityānyevai-
tāni karmāṇi nimittanityatvāditi siddhaṃ
 darśanaṃ mūlasaṃbhavānna vyāmoha ityarthaḥ |
