[yadi]syāt tadā vākyasya saṃbhūyakāritvāt tadanurodhenāpi adhikāripadaṃ vyākhyāyeta
vākyāntaraśeṣatvāt | tannirapekṣārthabodhakatvāt yājavajjīvacodanāyā brāhmaṇasyaivādhi-
kāravarṇanaṃ na yuktam mūlāpekṣatvāt | darśanasya mūlabhūtayāvajjīvanānusāreṇa brāhmaṇa-
grahaṇamupalakṣaṇatvena varṇanīyamiti |

iti mahopādhyāyaśrīśālikanāthakṛtāyāṃ ṛjuvimalāyāṃ pañcikāyāṃ

ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ

[p. 124]

ṛjuvimalāpañcikā

|| ṣaṣṭhādhyāsya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || sarvaśaktau pravṛttiḥ syāt tathābhūtopadeśāt || 1 ||

asminnadhikaraṇe gambhīrasya nyāyamahāhṛdasya upari bahavo laghudhiyaḥ plavanta
iti nyāyavicārameva tāvadāditaḥ ārabhāmahe---

tatra tāvat kecit
 evaṃ codayāṃbabhūvuḥ---yathā jīvanādikamadhikāri-
viśeṣaṇaṃ tathā sakalasāṅgapradhānakarmakalāpānuṣṭhānasāmarthyamapi | tatra yastāvat
kārtsnyena yatprayogamupasaṃhartuṃ prabhavati tadviṣayo nāyaṃ vicāraḥ---kiṃ aṅgatyāgenāpya-
nuṣṭhānaṃ kartavyaṃ uta neti | yastu na prabhavati tasyānadhikāra eva śūdrasyevāgnihotrādau |

yadapi
 caike samādhānadhiyā’bhidadhati na-pravṛttāvevedaṃ yathāśaktyanuṣṭhāna-
mucyate | kintu pravṛttasya | tena sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthyenādāvadhikṛtasya yadyantarā
śaktiḥ vyāpadyate tadā prāktanādhikāradārḍhyenānuṣṭhānaṃ parisamāpyata eva | ata eva
nityeṣvagnihotrādiṣu śūdrādīnāṃ nānuṣṭhānam | adhikārasamadhigamasamaya eva teṣāma-
śaktatvāditi |

tadapi
 jātuṣamivābharaṇaṃ vipratipannajanamanīṣānalaśisvā[yāṃ] vilīyate |
tathāhi---adhikāriviśeṣaṇayogāt adhikārī, pravartate ca | āprayogāpavargaṃ
cādhikāranuvṛttiranuṣṭhānasamāptaye’bhyupagamanīyā | anyathā hi nivṛtādhikārasyānuṣṭhānaṃ
na śabdataḥ, nāpyanyataḥ pramāṇāt syāt | tatra madhye prayoge’pi satyāmaśaktau śakte-
radhikārāṃśaniveśitvānnivartata evādhikāraḥ | nivṛttādhikārasya ca kinnibandhanamārabdha-
prayogaparisamāpam | śiṣṭavigarhaṇābhayāditi cet; tarhi naimittikakāmyayoḥ na kaści-
dviśeṣaḥ | nimittaparyanteṣu cedamadhikaraṇamityācāryāḥ | tasmādārambhasamaye prayoga-
madhye vā śaktivirahiṇāmadhikārābhāvādanuṣṭhānamayuktamiti |

yadapi
 kaścidāha---yannityaṃ kartavyaṃ tat sarvāṅgayuktamiti asaṃbhavānnityeṣu
yathāśaktyanuṣṭhānameva śāstrārthaḥ | tena sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthya vināpi nityeṣvadhikāra
iti

[p. 125]

tadidaṃ mīmāṃsātantramanādriyamāṇasya svamanīṣikākalpitam | tathāhi---
yathāviniyogamadhikāra iti nyāyasyāvagatirnāsti | tena jīvato’pi yaccoditaṃ tat
yasyaiva coditaṃ tat tasyaivāvaśyakartavyamiti nānyasya | nityānvitāṅgayuktaṃ ca karma
anuṣṭhānopasaṃhārasamarthasyaiva tadavaśyaṃ kartavyam; aśaktasya kimāyātam | na cāsaṃ-
bhāvyo’yaṃ śāstrārthaḥ kadācit sarvāṅgopasaṃhāraśaktisaṃbhavāt | anyathā kāmyānāṃ
karmaṇāmanavakāśatvāpātāditi yāvat | yatkiñcidetat | yadi ca yo yāvacchaknoti
nityakarmaṇi tasya tāvadeva coditamiti syāt tadā śūdrādīnāmagnividyāviyo-
gitvāt tadrahitamevānuṣṭhānaṃ coditamiti teṣāmapyagnihotrānuṣṭhānaṃ syāditi sakalācāra-
virodhaḥ |

tathedamapare
 vadanti---iha ca nimittaparyanteṣu phalaṃ nāstīti na tatprayukta-
manuṣṭhānaṃ tathāpi niyogasiddhiṃ prati vivādābhāvāt svasiddhayarthaṃ niyoga evānuṣṭhāna-
mākṣipatīti samāśrayaṇīyam | anyathā tadasiddheḥ | niyogasiddhistu yathācoditakṛtsnā-
nuṣṭhānalabhyeti kṛtsnasyaivānuṣṭhānamākṣipati na vikalasya | na cedamāśrayituṃ yuktam-
kadācitsakalasya sādhakatvaṃ kadācidvikalasyeti | sakalasyāpi kalāpasyādhikāra-
vidhinā yugapadgaṛhītatvāt | tathā gṛhītasya sādhakatvakalpanāt | yadi vikalasyāpi
sādhakatvaṃ tadā aṅgavat pradhānamapi parityajya kimityanuṣṭhānaṃ[na]syāt |

api ca kāmyeṣu karmasu kasmādvikalānuṣṭhānaṃ neṣyate | phalāsiddheriti cenna,
niyogāyattatvātphalasiddheḥ | niyogādeva kālāntarabhāviphalamiti mīmāṃsakapravarāḥ
pratipedire | yadi ca vikalādeva niyogasiddhiḥ atrāpi kiṃ na syāt | athātra
sakalasādhyatvaṃ niyogasyāśrīyate, nityeṣvapi kimayaṃ niyogo rājājñāyā nirvāsitaḥ ?
tadidamadhikaraṇaṃ śraddhāvipralabdhamatīnāmevādaraṇīyamiti |

atra prabhākarasevāhevākajaḍimānamāracayanti | satyaṃ śaktiradhikāri-
viśeṣaṇaṃ tathāpyeṣā cintā yujyata eva | dvidhā hi pumāṃsaḥ tirohitaśaktayaścāti-
rohitaśaktayaśca | tatra[ye]atirohitaśaktayaḥ teṣāṃ yadyapi śaktirasti[tathāpi](teṣāṃ)te
pratibandhakavaśāt śaktiphalamanuṣṭhānaṃ na labhante | kathaṃ punasteṣāṃ śaktirastīti gamyate ?
phalagamyā hi sā phalābhāve kiṃ tasyāṃ pramāṇam ? atrātridhīyate---ya eva prāk
kāryaṃ kuvannupalabdhaḥ sa eva punarantarā na kṛtavān | punaśca kāraṇāntarasamupanipāte
kurvannupalabhyate | tatra kiṃ śakternāśotsādau kalpyeyātām uta tirodhānatadvigamāviti |

[p. 126]

tatra dharmikalpanāto dharmakalpanā laghīyasīti pūrvaśaktivinaśapurassaro na śaktyanta-
ropajanaḥ kalpyate | kintu antarā kāryānudayadarśanāt pratibandhameva manyāmahe |
punareva kāryodayadarśanāt tadapagama eva kalpanāmarhati | tasmādanuṣṭhānābhāve’pi
tiraskṛtā śaktirastīti sugamametat | tatra śakteradhikāriviśeṣaṇatvāt tirohi-
tāyāmapi ca sadbhāvāviśeṣāt tirohitaśaktīnāmapyadhikāro bhavatyeva | anuṣṭhānā-
tprāgbhāvinī hi daśā puruṣasyādhikāraviśeṣaṇam nānuṣṭhānameva, tasyādhikārāyattatvāt,
prāgadhikārādasaṃbhavāt | tena śaktirevādhikāriviśeṣaṇam | sā ca tirohitaśaktī-
nāmapyasti ityukto’dhikāraḥ | ye tu svabhāvata evoktāḥ śūdrādayaḥ atyantocchinna-
śaktayaḥ nāndhādayaḥ teṣāṃ śaktyabhāvādeva nādhikāraḥ | tatra ye tirohitaśaktayaḥ
teṣāmadhikāre sati kasyacidaṅgasyānuṣṭhānābhāve sati kimadhikāradārḍhyasadbhāvādanuṣṭhānaṃ
bhavatu uta aṅgāsadbhāvādananuṣṭhānaṃ (teṣāṃ śaktayabhāvādeva nādhikāraḥ | tatra ye
tirohitaśaktayaḥ teṣāmadhikāre sati) iti cintā | sā ceyamupakrame prayogamadhye ca
tulyā |

tatra pūrvapakṣavādī manyate---yadyapi pratibaddhaśaktīnāmadhikāro bhavati tathāpyasau
nivartata eva sādhanavaikalyāt | kṛtsnānuṣṭhānasampādyo nimittaparyante viniyoṃgaḥ
pratīyate | yadā ca kṛtsnamanuṣṭhīyate tadā vikalatvāt sādhanasya niyogasiddhirnāsti
tadabhāvācca na puruṣasyādhikāraḥ | niyojyasaṃbandhiniyogalakṣaṇe kārye sādhanatayā
karmaṇāmadhikāra ityutpannāpyadhikārapratītiḥ vilayamupaiti | yadi nitye vikalādapi
niyogasiddhiḥ tadā kārye’pi tathā syāditi | tathā pradhānaparityāgenāpi anuṣṭhānaṃ
prāpnoti aṅgaissaha sādhanatve pradhānasyāpyaviśeṣāditi |

rāddhāntī tvāha-satyamadhikāro yāvadadhikāravidhinā prayujyate tāvataiva vini-
yogārthanirvṛttiḥ na yāvadviniyuktaṃ tāvataiva; bādhavat | sarveṣāmaṅgatve satyapi keṣā-
ñcidaṅgānāṃ bādhe yāvantyevādhikāravidhinā’nuṣṭhāpyante tāvadbhireva tatsiddhiḥ tathehāpi
adhikāravidhireva sādhanabhāvamupakalpayati na pramāṇāntaram | tena yāvatprayuṅkte
tāvataiva tatsiddhiṃ gamayati | yathāsaṃbhavinaśca prayuktiḥ nāsaṃbhavinaḥ | tena satyadhikāre
yāvatsaṃbhavaṃ prayuktāṅgasampādya evādhikāravidhiḥ nimittaparyanteṣviti kalpyate |

yattūktaṃ---kāmyo’pi tathā syāditi | tatra brūmaḥ-kāmye phalaparyantānvayitvāt
adhikārasya phalaṃ prati karaṇībhūta eva yāgādirviṣayaḥ | nacetikartavyatārahitasya

[p. 127]

phalaṃ prati karaṇatā’sti | kṛtsnā cetikartavyatā | tenaikadeśābhāve’pi itikartavyatayā
śūnyatayā karaṇatvānupapatteḥ akaraṇībhūtasya viṣayatvamiti | itikartavyatāvirahe kevala-
viṣayāt viṣayābhāve ca niyogābhāvāt tadabhāve cādhikārābhāvāt nānuṣṭhānasiddhiḥ |
ata eva pradhānaparityāge’pyanuṣṭhānaṃ syāditi nāśaṅkanīyam | nityeṣu phalāntarā-
bhāvāt na karaṇībhūtasya viṣayatvamiti itikartavyatāvirahe kevalaviṣayapratyabhijñānāt
niyogāpatteḥ adhikāro yuktaḥ | tadbalena cānuṣṭhānamapīti granthārthaḥ |

nimittaparyantamatrādhikāraṃ cintayatyācāryaḥ
 iti kāmyeṣu neyaṃ cinteti
darśayati | cintāsvarūpamāha---kimaṅgairanupasaṃhniyamāṇaiḥ nimittatvamevāpaiti

kimaṅgairanupasaṃhniyamāṇaiḥ kṛtsnānuṣṭhānasādhyasya vidhyarthasya asiddheḥ adhikārābhāvā-
jjīvanādeḥ (na) nimittatvamevāpaiti satyapi tasminnananuṣṭhānāt | [u]ta nimittatvaṃ
nā(|nnā)paiti anupasaṃhniyamāṇairapyaṅgaiḥ uktena nyāyena niyogena niyogārthasiddheradhikāre
sati nimittatvamanuṣṭhāne yujyata eva |

svavacanameva vivṛṇoti---etaduktaṃ bhavati-kimadhikārāvagamo bhavati |
utādhikārānavagamāt kathañcidanuṣṭhānamiti
 |

yadā’dhikārāvagamo bhavati tadā nimittatvamevāpaiti | yadā tu adhikārāna-
vagamāt kathaṃcidanuṣṭhānaṃ tadā nimittatve nāpaiti | nanu ca yadā’ṅgānuṣṭhānāsaṃbhavaḥ
tasyādhikāra eva nāsti | yasya cādhikāraḥ tasya ca kṛtsnānuṣṭhānameva saṃbhavatīti
cinte/?/yaṃ na jñāyate kīdṛśī[ti] tadāha---

nanu ca saṃśaya evāyamaprakaṭīkṛtārthaḥ iti | ayaṃ prakaṭyate-sarvāṅga-
viśiṣṭe karmaṇi adhikṛtasya kadācit prayogaśaktau sāmarthyānapagame’pi
cinteyamiti viṣayaviśeṣapradarśanenaiva saṃśayo vyākhyātaḥ
 |

yasya sāmarthyaṃ nāpagataṃ tasya sarvāṅgaviśiṣṭe karmaṇyadhikṛtasya prayoga[ā]śaktau
kiṃ nimittatvamevāpaiti uta neti cintā | kathaṃ punaḥ sāmarthyānapagamo’śaktiśca |
śaktireva hi sāmarthyam | tena sāmarthyamanapagataṃ aśaktiśceti parasparaviruddham |
samādhatte---

daivānmānuṣādvāpi tiraskārādaśaktiḥ na punarājānataḥ, svabhāvaśakti-
rastyeveti sāmarthyānapagamo varṇyate
 |

[p. 128]

daivādrogādeḥ mānuṣāt abhighātādeḥ śaktestiraskāra iti aśaktirityu-
cyate | etattu prāguktameva | tasmāt kadācidaśaktiriti sūktam | nanu tiraskārepi
śakteḥ anadhikāra eva atrāha---

sāmarthyaṃ cādhikārahetuḥ nopadhānavyapekṣam
 |

sāmarthyaṃ svābhāvikamevādhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ na punaḥ upadhānasāpekṣam | upadhāna-
śabdena upādhibhūtaṃ vastvabhimatam | pratibandhāpagamaṃ cikitsādi | apratibandha ca
sāmarthyaṃ nādhikārehatuḥ kiṃ tarhi svābhāvikam | na hi kāryāt prāk apratibandhatā
śakteḥ śakyate vaktum | kāryaṃ cānuṣṭhānameva | tena satyanuṣṭhāne apratibandhaśakti-
niścaye sati adhikāraḥ, adhikāre cānuṣṭhānamiti itaretarāśrayatā syāt | tasmāt
svābhāvikī śaktiradhikāriviśeṣaṇam | adhikṛta eva pratibandhavigamāvapi tathā |
yathā nayanācchādanapakṣmavigamaḥ(mau) ājyāvekṣaṇādiṣu iti bhavati saṃśayaḥ---
kimupadhānādrogādeḥ pravṛttiśaktirivādhikāro’pi virudhyate uta upādhinirapekṣatvāt
apravṛttāvapyadhikṛta eveti | atra pūrvapakṣavādī paśyati---

satyamanupādhikamevājānataḥ svabhāvata eva yatsāmarthyamanupādhikaṃ
anapekṣitapratibandhavigamaṃ adhikārahetuḥ kintu pravṛttyarthatvāttasya upādhi-
vihatāyā api pravṛtteḥ hetorakiñcitkaraṃ vidyamānamapi
 |

asyārthaḥ---sāmarthyasya pravṛttiḥ prayojanam | tatra yadi satyapi sāmarthye
pravṛttirnāsti tadā vidyamānaṃ akiñcitkaram | tasmin satyapi pratibandhavaśena
pravṛttyanupapatteḥ | asatyāṃ pravṛttau kṛtsnānuṣṭhānasya saṃpādanīyasya vidhyarthasyāsiddheḥ
nivartata evādhikāraḥ | tasmāt sarvāṅgopasaṃhāreṇaiva [aṅgīkaraṇī]yā pravṛttiḥ | atrai-
vādhikārakāryasya niyogasyābhirnivṛttiḥ naikadeśaprayogāt anyathā ko viśeṣaḥ phala-
paryanteṣvadhikāreṣu ? asāvapi phalaparyanto’dhikāraḥ anaupādhika eva upādhinira-
pekṣa eva | na ca pravṛttyaśaktau kāryakṣamo bhavati, tathābhūte sakalāṅgayukte’dhi-
kṛtatvāt |

atrābhidhīyate | phalaparyante viśeṣaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ nimittaparyanteṣvadhi-
kārānapa(va)gamo varṇyate pravṛttāvaśaktāvapi | yadi pravṛttāvaśaktiḥ kathaṃ
tarhi varṇyate ? itthaṃ varṇyate | anaupādhike adhikārāvagame hi nimittatva-

[p. 129]

mapahnotuṃ śakyam | bhavati hi aśakterautpattikyāḥ śūdrādīnāṃ nādhikāraḥ
na punaḥ svābhāvikasāmarthyavaśenopajātasyādhikārasya pratibandhavaśenāśakte-
radhikāravighātaḥ(m)
 |

tasmādanapagatādhikārasya yathāśaktyanuṣṭhānamanuṣṭhānavido manyante evaṃ
yathāśaktyanuṣṭhānaṃ nityeṣvasti
 |

ata evātra nityādhikāre gauṇo mukhyaśca śāstrārtha ityācakṣate
mīmāṃsakāḥ
 |

codayati---

kimidaṃ kūśmāṇḍabhūtaṃ pralapyate asaṃbandhaṃ, ekadeśasya śāstrārthasya
gauṇatvaṃ mukhyatvaṃ ceti
 |

pariharati---

bhavānevātra pralāpī
 |

na mīmāṃsakāḥ pralāpinaḥ | kathamiti pṛcchati | uttaraṃ---avihanyamāne
hi aśaktāvapyadhikāre yāvacchaktyā cānuṣṭhāne pratīyamāne arthākṣipte ca
saṃbhavati śrautāpāye ca nopadānaṃ virudhyate
 |

nanu yadi śrautaṃ tyajyate tadā’paramapi kimiti na tyajyate ityatrāha---

yāvadaśaktistāvannopādīyate | tathāpi śakyatyāgeṣu na kiñcana
pramāṇamasti śakyopādāna [iva] iti
 |

anena hi yathāśaktyavagamo hetuḥ | tena kiñcittyājyaṃ kiñciccopādātavyam |
punaścodayati---gauṇasya kimāyātam
 |

bhavatu arthasya kimāyātaṃ ? yadyapyarthānnīvārādi upādīyate tathāpi kathaṃ
gauṇatvaṃ ?

pariharati---

tadeva hi ārthamabhyupagamya na śakyate upādātuṃ
 yāvat gauṇatā vrīhi-
śāstrārthasya nocyate tāvannīvārāṇāṃ nopādānaṃ saṃbhavati | nanu ca kiñcidvikala-
vrīhiśāstrārthagrahaṇenāpi karmasiddheḥ kartha gauṇatetyatrāha---

[p. 130]

na hi nīvārāṇāṃ na pratītyā bhūyo’vayavasāmānyamupapadyate | nīvārāneva
hyupā/?/dadānaḥ bhūyovayavasāmānyopādānaṃ śaknoti kartuṃ nānyathā | nīvāreṣu vopādīya-
māneṣu siddhaṃ gauṇatvam | tasmātsupṭhūcyate gauṇo mukhyaśca śāstrārtha iti |

anyatra kathamiti atrāha---

ārādupakārakāṅgahāne’pi kasyaciccopādānāt sadṛśabuddhirbhavati
 |

abhinne’pi dharmiṇi yathā[loke](yoge)kasyacidanekaguṇasaṃbhṛ(bhū)tasya dṛṣṭasya
punaḥ kālāntare kaiścidgauṇairviyuktasya draṣṭāro bhavanti vaktāraḥ sa ivāyaṃ dṛśyata iti |
avasthāvato’bhede’pyavasthānāṃ bhinnānāṃ sādṛśyamupapadyata eveti | tasmādubhayaṃ siddhaṃ
gauṇamukhyatā yathāśaktyanuṣṭhānaṃ ca | darśanamuktam | ‘tadeva yādṛk‌kīdṛk‌ca
hotavyaṃ’
 iti vacanamidaṃ kasmānna bhavati yena darśanamucyate
 | uttaraṃ---
(sa)[na] yādṛk‌kīdṛk‌śabdārthau vidhātuṃ śakyete vihitāpekṣatvādanayoḥ
 |

vihitadravyamapekṣya yādṛk‌kīdṛk‌śabdārthau vidheyau nānyathā | tatra yadi tāvat
dravyaṃ viśiṣṭameva vihitaṃ tadā na yādṛk‌kīdṛk‌śabdārthau vidhātuṃ śakyete | atha
dravyamātraṃ vihitaṃ na viśeṣaḥ tadāpi prāptatvādvidhānaṃ nopapadyate | tasmāt nyāyānu-
vādāviti sūktam |

|| 2 || kāmyeṣu caivamarthitvāt || 8 ||

nanvekādaśe darśitaṃ kāmādhikāre sakalāṅgopasaṃhāra eva kartavya iti |
kimaparamiha cintyata iti | atrāha---

iha vikṛtayaḥ udāharaṇam | ekādaśe yadadhikaraṇaṃ tatprakṛtiviṣayam
 |

iha tu vikṛṣu cintā kena viśeṣaṇena ? uttaraṃ---

na hyatrāṅgopadeśaḥ | kiṃ tarhi ? kāryato hi saṃbandhaḥ
 | vikṛtiṣu
aṅgāni nopadiśyante | kintu kāryeṇa karaṇopakāralakṣaṇenābhisaṃbadhyante |

kaścaivaṃ sati viśeṣaḥ ? ayamasti viśeṣaḥ | upadiṣṭe hi karmaṇyadhi-
kāraḥ | upadeśāvagatipurassaro’tideśaḥ | vikṛtiṣu ca pradhānamātra-
mevopadiśyate | ataḥ kāryābhisaṃbandhānāmaṅgānāṃ kadācidanuṣṭhānāśakteḥ
adhikāro nivartate iti śakyate vaktuṃ upadeśaviṣayatvādadhikārasya
 |

[p. 131]

upadiśyata ityupadeśaḥ | evaṃ caupadeśikasyādhikārasya na saṅkocaḥ kṛto
bhavati | kriyatāṃ ko doṣa ityatrāha---

na hi kāryeṇa karaṇopakāralakṣaṇena prākṛtān padārthānatidiśatā
pramāṇabhūtenopadeśasya saṅkoco nyāyyaḥupadiṣṭārthasiddhayarthatvāt kāryasya |
upadiṣṭo’rthaḥ kathaṃ nāma siddhayedityevamarthaṃ kāryaṃ pramāṇamāśrīyate | tena
yadarthameva yajjātaṃ tasya tena saṅkoco[na]nyāyyaḥ | na hi guṇānurodhena
pradhānaṃ saṅkocamarhati |

idamatrākūtam-yadyapi sāṅgapradhānaviśiṣṭasya niyogasya kāryabhūtasya tadanuṣṭhāna-
yogyameva saṃbandhībhavitumarhati | ākāṅkṣasannidhiyogyatānāṃ sambandhahetutvāt |
tathā ca bhavatu nāma tathāvidhasyaivādhikāra iti ayogyasya nādhikāraḥ | yastu śakta
eva1tirohitaśaktiḥ kiñcidātideśikamaṅgamupasaṃhartuṃ na śakroti tasyāpi vikṛtiṣu
kāmyāsvapi nānuṣṭhānamanupapannam | na ca tatraivaṃ śakyate vaktuṃ - phalaṃ prati karaṇī
bhūteṣvadhikārāt setikartavyatākasya ca karaṇatvāt aṅgābhāve karaṇatvānupapatteḥ
viṣayatvābhāve cādhikārāsaṃbhavāt ananuṣṭhānamiti, upadiśyamānaviṣayatvādadhikārasyeti |

atrābhidhīyate---yadi kāryanirapekṣo’dhikāraḥ phalaparyanteṣu syāt
syādetadevam |

asyārthaḥ---phalaparyanṣu karaṇībhūteṣu adhikārāt setikartavyatākasya karaṇa-
tvāt kāryeṇa ca pramāṇena vinā vikṛtiṣvitikartavyatāyā abhāvāt kāryasamarpiteti-
kartavyatānvayalabdhakaraṇabhāva evopadiṣṭe pradhāne sādhikāra iti atideśikopa-
saṃhāreṇānuṣṭhānam | yadi paraṃ phalaparyanteṣvapi karaṇopakāranirapekṣa evādhikāraḥ
syāt tadaivātideśikasyāpi aṅgasya tyāgaḥ syāt | na caitadasti tadāha---yathā ca
sakārya eva sakaraṇopakāra eva upadeśopadiṣṭe pradhānepyadhikriyate phalārthī
tathā ekādaśe vakṣyāmaḥ |

kāmyanimittādhikārayorviśeṣamaparamāha---

yathā ca nimittaparyanteṣu nimittāpagamo mābhūditi viguṇamapi
kartuṃ yuktam |

1. atirohita---mā

[p. 132]

anuṣṭhānaṃ prati nimittatayā pratīyate | yadi cānuṣṭhānaṃ na kriyeta nimittatva-
mevāpaiti | na ca tadyuktam | [ato]viguṇamapi kartuṃ yuktam | anuṣṭhānaṃ prati nimi[ttaṃ]
phalāvagatiḥ ananuṣṭhāne’pi 1phalāvagamāvirodhāt | pratyuta viguṇānuṣṭhāne sā phalā-
vagatirna saṃbhavati sādhyatvāt phalasya sādhyasya setikartavyatākakaraṇāyattatvāt iti
sarvāṅgopasaṃhāra eva atrāpi vikṛtiṣvapi nyāyyaḥ |

|| 3 || kriyāṇāmāśritatvāt dravyāntare vibhāgaḥ syāt || 11 ||

nanu ca gauṇamukhyatveśāstrārtha ityuktam | na punastatpratyayādṛte’nuṣṭhānaṃ
saṃbhavati | yadi mukhyaśāstrārthaikadeśo’pi nānugṛhyate tadā kimananuṣṭhānene | gauṇā-
vagatāvapi tadityevānuṣṭhāne hetuḥ | tasmāt tatpratyaye yatraḥ kartavyaḥ |

pūrvapakṣamāha-nanu cānumānīkī kriyetyuktam | svarūpāgrahaṇe tu kathaṃ tatpratyayaḥ
 |

anumānena kriyā’vagamyate anumānācca svarūpāvagame ca pratyabhijñāyā anu/?/-
pattiriti kathaṃ kriyāyāṃ tatpratyaya iita |

rāddhāntamāha---śāstrāvagamyatayā tatpratyayābhidhānaṃ na svarūpeṇe-
tyadoṣaḥ |
 yadyapyānumānikī kriyā tathāpi tasya dravyāntareṇa kriyāyāḥ tenaiva
śabdenābhidhānam auṣādhikatvena śāstrāvagamyatayā tatpratyayaḥ na punaḥ svarūpeṇetyadoṣaḥ |
śāstrāvagamyatvaṃ cānuṣṭhānahetuḥ na svarūpāvagatiriti sūktaṃ śāstrārthānanvayitvam | nanu
svarūpe pratyabhijñānaṃ kimiti nocyata iti
 suhṛdbhāvena kathayati | svarūpa-
pratyabhijñānaṃ tu na pratyakṣādṛte’stīti prathamapāde uktam buddhikarmaṇī abhihite-
’pratyakṣe iti |

atra yadi śāstrāvagamyatayā tatpratyabhijñābhidhānaṃ kathaṃ tarhi sve sve
prameye(na)[tena]tena pramāṇena pratyabhijñāpyastītyuktam |
 bhavati pratyakṣeṇa
pratyakṣagrāhye śāstrāvagamye ca śāstreṇetyāpatitam | pariharati---

naitadevam darśanāntarasya smaraṇapūrvikā hi pramitiḥ pratyabhijñetyuktam |
pūrvadarśanānusandhānena pratītiḥ sā pratyabhijñā tadevedamiti |

1. phalāpagama-mā

[p. 133]

anumānādiṣu punaḥ na darśanāntarapūrvikā pratītiḥ, viṣaye liṅgādimātrabhāvya-
manumānādikam | tena pūrvadarśanānusandhānaṃ tatra nāstīti | pratyakṣaṃ tu pūrvadarśana-
saṃskārasacivendriyajanyatayā pūrvadarśanānusandhānenotpadyata iti yuktaṃ pratyabhijñānam |
ato viṣayāntarānavagatimātram | atrocyate | na, pratyabhijñābalasyaivaṃjātīya-
kasyopādhibhūtatvāt |
 śabda eva paraṃ tatra pravartate na punaḥ pratyabhijñā’stīti |

|| 4 || śrutipramāṇatvācchiṣṭābhāve nāgamo’nyasyāśiṣṭatvāt || 13 ||

nanu sarvaśaktyadhikaraṇe’yathābhūtamapi kartavyamevetyuktam |
 tena
vrīhyādīnāmapi parityāgenānuṣṭhānamuktameva | ataḥ kathamatra pūrvapakṣāśaṅkā ? uttaraṃ---
tenaivamuktam |
 yathāśrutamapi kartavyamityetāvaduktam | yadi śrutāntarāsaṃbhave’pi
śrutāntaraṃ kartavyamevetyuktam athāpyetadadhikaraṇaṃ na kartavyam vrīhiśrutyarthāsaṃbhavepi
puroḍāśaśrutyarthaḥ kartavya eva |

ekadeśī samādhatte---evaṃ tarhi prayojanamidaṃ tasyādhikaraṇasya |

dūṣayati---prayojane’rthyamāne pūrvapakṣeṇa kiṃ ?

kṛta evātra pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ | tena prayojanamidaṃ na bhavati | paramasamādhātā āha---
ya[dī]daṃ sarvaśaktyadhikaraṇasya prayojanamātraṃ na bhavati evaṃ tarhi kartavya
evātra pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ |
 sa evāha---uktaṃ dvitīye śāstrāntaratayā dravyavidhayo
nāvagamyanta iti |
 nirapekṣasādhyabhede[vā]’nubandhamede vā śāstrāntaratā bhavati |
na dravyavidhīnāṃ pradhānavidhikāryānapekṣaṃ kāryāntaramasti | na cānubandhabhedaḥ | tena
śāstrāntaratā tāvanna śakyate vaktum | tathā dvādaśe vakṣyāmaḥ | kāryeṇa vikalpaḥ
na śāstrāntaratayā |
 kāryeṇa vikalpo vakṣyate | sa na śāstrāntaratayā bhavati |
śāstrāntaratve hi vikalpānupapatteḥ | kutaḥ ? kāryānyatve hi śāstrānyatvaṃ nānyathā |
tena śāstrānyatve sati balāt kāryānyatvāpattiḥ | ekārthāstu vikalperannityuktaṃ[na]
nānārthānām | tena śāstrāntaratā tāvat dravyavidhīnāṃ nāsti |

bhavatvevaṃ tataḥkim ? idaṃ tato bhavati---āgneyo’ṣṭākapālaḥ
“vrīhibhiryajeta”
 iti caikaṃ śāstram
 ekaśca śāstrārthaḥ kāryasyānantarasya śrutya-
ntarasya tyāgo’yam yasya śrutyantarasya tyāgena śrutyantarārtho’nuṣṭhīyate |

upasaṃharati---tasmādyuktaṃ yatsamidho yajatītyeva
mādyarthaparityāgenā-
gneyo’ṣṭākapāla ityasyārthasyānuṣṭhānam | anubandhabhedena śāstrabhedāt śāstrāntaratyāge-

[p. 134]

nāpi śāstrāntarārthasyānuṣṭhānaṃ yuktam | yuktameva | vrīhibhiryajeteti punaśśāstrānta-
rānupapatteḥ tadabhāve nānuṣṭhānamiti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |

atrābhidhīyate---satyaṃ nedaṃ śāstrāntaram puroḍāśaśāstrāt vrīhi-
bhiryajeteti |
 yata eva śāstrāntaraṃ na bhavati ata evānuṣṭhānaṃ puroḍāśa-
śrutyā’stīti śrutadravyaparityāge’pi anuṣṭhānamucitam |

yadi puroḍāśaśrutyaiva dravyamākṣipyate kiṃ tarhi vrīhibhiryajetetyanena
kriyate |

uttaraṃ---niyamo vrīhīṇām |
 puroḍāśaniyogāṅgatve nirjñāte niyoga-
siddhayarthatayā vrīhīṇāmevopādānam anyasādhakatānavagamāt | avihiteṣu vrīhiṣu
kiyāmātrasiddhaye yatkiñcid dravyamupādīyata iti niyamaḥ prayojanaṃ bhavati | na hi
niyamaḥ śāstrārthatvaṃ (kāmaṃ) vihanti puroḍāśavidheḥ | yadi hi niyame sati puroḍāśavidheḥ
śāstrārthatvaṃ vihanyeta tadā niyamābhāve puroḍāśaśrutyartho nānuṣṭhīyeta | na cāyaṃ(a)
śāstrārthatāṃ vihanti | kutaḥ ? niyamo hi anirjñāte kārye ātmānaṃ labhate | na ca
śāstrādṛte kāryāvagatiḥ | tasmātsiddhaṃ niyamābhāvepyanuṣṭhānam | niyamanirapekṣatvā-
tkāryasya niyamamantareṇāpi śāstrasyārthavattvāt | kathaṃ niyamanirapekṣatetyatrāha---
niyamena hi tadapekṣyata iti |
 atra na tena niyamaḥ | tasmādaśāstrāntaratve’pi
tadabhāve niyamābhāve’nuṣṭhānasiddhiḥ | yatpunardravyāntaravidhānumaktam tatparihṛtam |

|| 5 || na devatāgniśabdakriyamanyārthasaṃyogāt || 18 ||

kena punaḥ prasaṅgetedamadhikaraṇamāyātam | yadi tāvat dravyaguṇacodanā-
bhāve’pi śāstrāntaramapi pradhānaṃ kartavyamityucyeta pūrveṇaivādhikaraṇena gatamidam |
athaiṣāmabhāve’kartavyatāmeva manyate tat pūrvādhikaraṇena sarvaśaktyadhikaraṇena ca
viruddham | pūrvādhikaraṇe hi pradhānaśāstrārtho’nuṣṭheya ityuktam | tathā sarvaśaktyadhi-
karaṇena ca viruddham prayājādikarmābhāve’pi pradhānaṃ kartavyamiti sarvaśaktyadhi-
karaṇe pratipāditam | ucyate | sāmānyaṃ taccikīrṣā hi ityanena sadṛśe pratinidhau
prāpte tadapavādārthamidamadhikaraṇam | keṣu cinneṣyate sadṛśapratinidhiḥ yeṣu ca neṣyate
tānīmāni pratyudāhriyante---na devatāgniśabdakriyaṃ ityanena sūtreṇaiva sadṛśapratinidhya-
pavādo’yam | ata eva karmāpyatrodāharaṇam | tatrāpi pratinidhyabhāvasya saṃbhavāt |

[p. 135]

yeṣāṃ tu mate pūrvādhikaraṇokte dravyāntaropādāne tatpratiṣedhārthamevedamadhikaraṇaṃ teṣāṃ
mate karmodāharaṇamasaṃbaddhaṃ syāt |

nanu ca devatābhāve’pi ananuṣṭhānameva pradhānasyāpi manyante kecit |

tena pratinidhyapavādamātratā tvatra nāstīti | kintu karmaṇo lopapratipādanārthameva |
kecichabdaṃ prayuñjānaḥ asminnarthe’nāsthāṃ darśayati | etacca vakṣyāmaḥ | samādhatte-nānta-
rīyakametadbhaviṣyati |
 devatāpratinidhau pratiṣiddhe nāntarīyakametadbhaviṣyati
karmāpi tyājyameveti | kathamidaṃ nāntarīyakamityatrāha---yuktyantarābhidhānāt
devatāvirahe karmārthatyāge anyārthasaṃyogāt iti |
 ato mūlādhikaraṇayukteḥ
yuktyantarasya bhāṣyakāreṇoktatvāt | atraiva pratipādayiṣyāmastadyuktyantaram | tatra
pūrvapakṣavādī sadṛśacikīrṣāṃ manyate | tāvatā ca lābhena kiyānapi śāstrārtha-
ssaṅgṛhīto bhavati |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---na devatādiṣvetatsaṃbhavati yo hyuddeśassūrya iti |

upādīyamāneṣu [nīvāreṣu]vrīhyavayavasāmānyalābho labhyate | naivamatra kiñcillabhyate |
‘vrīhibhiryajeta’
 iti vrīhīṇāṃ sādhanabhāvo’vayavaparyantaḥ tena sādhanībhūtasādhāraṇā-
vayavalābhena tatra śāstrārthaikadeśānugrahaḥ | devatāyāṃ tu tannāstīti | na hi bhāsva-
ratvādiguṇaparyanto’tra śāstrārthaḥ iti |

nanu āhavanīye juhoti ityayaṃ śāstrārthaḥ agnayantare’pyupādīyamāne
prāyeṇānugṛhyata evetyatrāha-evamāhavanīyādiṣvapyagniṣu na dravyāntare kiñjit
sādṛśyaṃ sādhāraṇarūpamuglabhāmahe |
 codanālakṣaṇatvādāhavanīyaśabdavācyasya
svarūpasya | ādhānādijanyasyātīndriyasyāhavanīyaśabdavācyatvāt tasya kaścidbhāgo
dravyāntaropādānenānugṛhīta iti nāsti pramāṇam | atha śabdamātramupādīyate na
mantrasādṛśyaṃ kiñcidupāttaṃ bhavati | mantratayā copādānaṃ na śabdatayeti vyarthaṃ śabda-
mātrasyopādānam |

evaṃ karmasvapi sādṛśyābhāvānna kiñcitsādṛśyamupādātuṃ śakyate | karmaṇāma-
tīndriyāṇāṃ sādhāraṇamasādhāraṇaṃ vā rūpamavagantuṃ na śakyata iti sādṛśyābhāvānnānyo-
pādānam | ida nīṃ devatābhāve tadyuktyantaramāha---

devatāyāṃ punaḥ dravyāntare upādīyamāne āstāṃ tāvat sādṛśyaṃ,
devatātvamapi nāstīti | ko’bhiprāyaḥ evaṃ vadato bhāṣyakārasya ? aya-

[p. 136]

mabhiprāyaḥ-tadabhāve kevalaṃ karmānuṣṭhānamapi naiva saṃbhavatīti manyate |
 devatā-
bhāve yāgasyāniṣpatau kevalaṃ karmānuṣṭhānamapi na saṃbhavati |

nanu ca ‘agnihotraṃ juhotīti’
 ityavagamāt śāstrārthasya devatāviśeṣābhāve
sāmānyataḥ siddhe kimityananuṣṭhānam ? yathāha devānāṃ priyaḥ-yadi devatātvaṃ nāma
sāmānyamasti, prāgviśeṣacodanāyāḥ tacca nāsti caiva hi, yasya viṣayaścodyate saiva
tasya devatā nānyetyuktam | ataḥ sāmānyena devatā nāsti svarūpato viśeṣato’pi
tāvat devatā bhavati śabdamātropakāriṇī hi setyuktam | kutaḥ tasyāṃ sādṛśyaṃ
sāmānyaṃ vā ? yadi devatā sā | tathā mantre śabdāntaropādāne na kiñcitsādṛśya-
mupalabhāmahe, varṇasaṃhatyātmano mantrasyāpi kāryata upagamāt | svabhāvato hi varṇāḥ
parasparavilakṣaṇā eva | teṣāṃ mantrabhāvaḥ kāryasamadhigamya eva sa varṇasaṅghātāntare
upātte’nugṛhīta iti na śakyate vaktum kramānyato nāsti | evaṃ tarhi devatāyā
vinā yāgānupapatteḥ agnihotraṃ juhotīti śāstrārthaḥ parityakto bhavati |
 uttaraṃ-
coditāgnayabhāve yadi na śakyate saṃpādayitumiti tadā kimanyada-
tastyāgācchreyaḥ |

yadiśabdaṃ prayuñjāno’trāpyanāsthāmāha | yadi devatābhāvepi yāgānapāyaḥ tadā
bhavatvanuṣṭhānam | atha yāga eva na saṃvarteta tadā bhavatu tyāgaḥ[kimetena] (kiṃ naitena)
nirbandhena | tasmānna kevalaṃ sādṛśyaṃ nāsti devatāyāṃ sāmānyamapi nāstīti, tadabhāve
cānuṣṭhānamapi nāstīti | tadidaṃ nāntarīyakam |

|| 6 || pratiṣiddhaṃ cāviśeṣeṇa hi tacchrutiḥ || 20 ||

pūrvapakṣamāha---

sādṛśyalābhāt sādhāraṇāvayavalābhāt sadṛśapratinidhiriti taccikīrṣā
hīti vakṣyāmaḥ | tena māṣā api pratinidheyā iti |

nanu ca pratiṣedhaḥ śrūyate na māṣā yajñasambandhāḥ kartavyā iti | tena
kathaṃ pratinidhiriti |

pariharati---

satyaṃ śrūyate yatra bhāṣā ityeva sambadhyante tatrāsau pratiṣedhaḥ | iha
punaḥ śrutadravyasadṛśā iti pratinidhīyante na bhāṣā iti |

[p. 137]

ayamāśayaḥ---yatra bhāṣāṇāṃ yajñasaṃbandhaḥ tatrāsau pratiṣedhaḥ | kutaḥ ? yajña-
saṃbandhapratiṣedhāt iha tu bhāṣāṇāṃ yajñasaṃbandho nāsti | śrutasaṃpādakatayā iha bhāṣāṃ
nāntarīyakāḥ | tasmānnātra pratiṣedhaḥ kramata iti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ |

atrābhidhīyate | satyaṃ sadṛśā iti pratinidhīyante te na bhāṣā iti
na punaḥ bhāṣabhāvo’pahnotuṃ śakyate |
 yadi bhāṣabhāvo’panhūyeta tadā pratiṣedho
nāvataret | na ca tasyāpahnavo’sti | sa evāha---pratinidhau bhāṣabhāvo na
vivakṣitaḥ
 iti | etadapi satyaṃ, kintu pratiṣedho bhāṣabhāvaviṣayatayā bhāṣabhāvasya
bhāṣatvasya viṣayatayā bhāṣajātyāśrayatayā pratiṣedho vārayituṃ na śakyate | nanu bhavatu
bhāṣajātyāśrayatayā yajñasaṃbandhastu nāstīti atrāha---

tasya ca nāntarīyakayajñasaṃbandhānapahārāt saṃbhavo na nivārayituṃ
śakyate |

nanu ca vikalpaḥ prāpnoti vihitapratiṣiddhatvāt tataśca kathamekāntenānu-
pādānamiti atrāha---na cātra vikalpo’pyāśaṅkyate śrutasaṃbhavamātrākṣepāt
pratinidhānasya |
 ye śrutasya dravyasyāvayavāḥ teṣāmākṣepo’vaidhaśrutasya viṣayasya
saṃbhavāt | pratiṣedhasya śrautasya ca bhāṣatvasya pratiṣedhaviṣayasya saṃbhavāt bhinnaviṣayatvānna
vikalpaḥ | tasmāt pratiṣedhātikramāt bhayāt na pratinidheyā bhāṣā iti |

|| 7 || tathā svāminaḥ phalasamavāyāt phalasya
karmayogitvāt || 21 ||

adhikāriṇamācikṣipsuḥ pṛcchati---kasyāyaṃ pratinidhiḥ kartavyatayā
cintyata iti |
 kimayaṃ pratinidhirupādeyo neti cintyate ||

uttaraṃ---yasya karmaṇyadhikārastadīya iti |
 ākṣeptā āha---evaṃ cet
svāmyapacāre ko’nyaḥ pratinidhatte |
 yadi svāmyevāpacaritaḥ kastarhi pratinidhi-
mupādatte | ekagranthenāha---tadidaṃ bālajalpitamiva pratibhāti |
 samādhatte---
astyatra pratinidherviṣayaḥ patnīyajamānayoranyatarāpacāre’nyataraṃ pratinidhātuḥ
nīvāravat |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---adhikṛtayoḥ sahatvaṃ karmaṇi |
 pratyekamadhikṛtayoḥ
strīpuṃsayoḥ pratyekameva prayoge prāpte sahatvaṃ coditam | tataḥ kiṃ ? idaṃ tato bhavati |

[p. 138]

adhikṛtaḥ karma kurvan yajamānākāratāmāpannaḥ | na karturadhikāra ityuktam |

adhikṛtasya sataḥ kartṛtvam tatrāyaṃ sahabhāva ityuktaṃ bhavati |

codayati---satyamevaṃ kārakamātravyāpāraḥ anyatagapekṣitaḥ |
 śakyate
ca kārakamātravyāpāro’nyenāpi saṃpādayitum | pariharati---sahatvaṃ tābhyāma-
pekṣitam na sahitabhāvaḥ |
 adhikāravaśaprāpte’nuṣṭhāne sahatvamātramapekṣitaṃ, na
sahakartṛtvaṃ karmaṇaḥ | kasmāt sahabhāvo’pekṣito na sahitatvamiti atrāha---netare-
tarayuktayoḥ kartṛtvaṃ kartṛtve ca sahabhāvaḥ |
 ekavacanaśravaṇāt netaretarayuktāḥ
kartāraḥ | kintu kartṛtva eva sahabhāvaḥ patnīyajamānayoḥ | tasmāt nādhikārādṛte
pratinidhānaṃ saṃbhavati | adhikārāyattaprayogasahatvasiddhaye pratinidhānamiti nādhikāra-
mantareṇa tatsiddhiḥ | adhikṛtaścenmukhya eva na pratinidhiḥ tasmādapratinidheyā svāmitā |
so’yaṃ kāryabhūtasahaprayogāsapatteḥ na pratinidhiḥ | sahatvameva parityajya anuṣṭhānaṃ
kimiti na bhavatīti nāsyādhikaraṇasya viṣayaḥ | tathā’dhikṛtenāpariṇetrāpi sahatvamiti
strī na yajatītyapi |

|| 8 || bahunāṃ tu pravṛtte’nyamāgamayedavaiguṇyāt || 22 ||

satreṣvidānīṃ cintyate-kiṃ kasyacidapacāre pratinidhāya satriṇaḥ prayogaṃ
parisamāpayanti uta neti |
 kasya citsvāmino’pacāre kiṃ svāminoḥ svāminaṃ
pratinidhāya satraṃ samāpayanti uta neti cintā | pūrvapakṣamāha-na hyasvāmī yajamāno
bhavatītyuktamadhastāt | pratinidhīyamānaścāsau yathā na svāmī tathā
vakṣyāmaḥ | tasmādatrāpi pūrvavat pratinidhānābhāvāt
 ‘ye yajamānāsta
ṛtvija’
 iti vacanāt yajamānairevārtvijyaṃ karma kartavyam | svāmī ca yajamāno bhavati
yo hi phalabhāgī sa yajamāna ucyate | na cāsvāmī phalabhāgī | asvāmī cāyaṃ
pratinidhīyamāna iti uttarādhikaraṇe vakṣyate | tasmāt puruṣāntare’pi pratinidhīyamāne
tatkāryāsiddheḥ pūrvavadatrāpi pratinidherabhāvaḥ |

atrocyate---atra hi sahitatvamapekṣyate na sahabhāvamātraṃ itaretara-
yuktānāṃ kartṛtvena śravaṇāt | ataḥ tatsiddhayarthaṃ itarakārakavat kartrantaraṃ
pratinidhātavyam |
 bahukartṛke karmaṇi ekaikasyādhikārāt kartṛtvamupādeyam |
kathaṃ tarhi pratinivivācoyuktiḥ ? svāmisthāne nipātāt |

[p. 139]

nanvevaṃ tarhi anadhikṛtamapyupādāya prayogaprārambhaḥ syāt | bhavatu na doṣaḥ
svāmini pravṛtte’pacārite puruṣāntaropādānamapi avaiguṇyānnayāyyamityucyate |

nanvasvāmino yajamānatā nāsti | ayajamāne cānuṣṭhātari ārtvijyasyaiva
vaiguṇyaṃ syāt | ucyate | ye yajamānā iti pradhānakartṛtayaivārtvijyeṣu viniyogaḥ
[na]phalabhāgitaiva vivakṣitā | vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | na cātmanepadāt tasyopādāna-
śeṣatā | yathānirjñātapradhānakartṛmātralābhena viśeṣaṇasyānapekṣitatvāt iti |

|| 9 || sa svāmī syāt tatsaṃyogāt || 23 ||

nanu ca kartṛtvenopādānamityuktam | kathamatra svāmyāśaṅkā ?
 yo
hyārabhya parisamāpayati sa phalabhāgī yaśca phalabhāgī sa svāmī | tena pratinidhīyamāna;
svāmī na bhavati iti kathaṃ svāmyāśaṅketi | samādhatte---satyamuktaṃ, tathāpyāśaṅkā
kācidvidyata eva | adhikāramukhena kartṛtvamuktam | aiśvaryaṃ hi karmaṇi
pratītya kartṛtvaṃ bhajate puruṣaḥ | ato’nadhikṛtasya kartṛtā nopapadyata iti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
 yadā’dhikṛtatvaṃ tadā kartṛtvaṃ, athānadhikṛtaḥ tarhi na kartṛtvamiti
bhāvaḥ |

rāddhāntamāha---satyaṃ, anadhikṛtasya kartatvaṃ nāsti | itaraiḥ punaḥ
kartṛtvamātramupādīyate saṅkhyāpūraṇāya | tasyāḥ karmaṇi śravaṇāt |
 ayamāśayaḥ-
adhikṛtakartṛtvamāpannaiḥ anadhikṛtasya sāmadānādibhiḥ śakyamāpādayituṃ, kartṛtvam |
kartṛtvenetareṣāṃ prayojanam, tāvanmātreṇaiva saṅkhyāpūraṇāditi | tasmādārthakartṛtvamātra-
mevopādīyate śrautasyānupayogāt | ārthaṃ śrautamadhikṛtatvaṃm | tayorārthamevopādīyate |
tasyaiva copayogāt | itarat śrautaṃ aupayogyeveti |

|| 13 || vacanāccānyāyyamabhāve tatsāmānyena pratinidhi-
rabhāvāditarasya || 31 ||

nanu abhāvidhipratinidhiniyamayoḥ na viśeṣamupalabhāmahe | tathāhi-abhāve
vidhirabhāvavidhiḥ | sa hi abhāve vidhiḥ upādānasāmarthyābhāve adhikāre ca līyamāne
kasyacitpratinidherupādānāprasakte’pi vidhiḥ na niyamaḥ | api tu vi(dhe)dhireva |
yatra punaḥ aniyamenānekopādānasaṃbhave vidhānamekasya asau niyamavidhiḥupādāna-
sāmarthye satyadhikāre coditābhāve’nekapratinidhiprasaṅge’pi ekasya vidhiḥ niyama-
phalam | tatra saṃśaya upapadyate---kiṃ somopādānāsāmarthye etadvidhānaṃ utānekaprati-
nidhiprasaṅge niyama iti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī ‘na vindet’
 iti asāmarthyamava-
gamyata iti manyate |
 na vindediti bhāvaḥ | ata idamavagamyate yadi somaṃ
labdhuṃ na śaknuyāditi | tena vedanasāmarthyaṃ nāstītyavagata ityuktam | na punarapacarita
ityetasmādavagamyate | ato’sāmarthyeṃ vidhirabhāve vidhireva |

atrābhidhīyate | na śakyate’sāmarthyanirūpaṇam adravyavatkartuṃ na
vidyaḥ ko’sāvasamarthaṃ iti |
 yathā adravyādhikaraṇe uktam-avidyamānadravyasyāpi

[p. 142]

dravyopādāne sāmarthyamasti iti | tathā anupāttasomasyāpi somalābhasāmarthyamastyeva |

1. anupādeyaḥ - mā

[p. 143]

ayamāśayaḥ---sarve hi dravyavighayo niyamārthā eva | tena somābhāve

miveti | evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---

yatrāpi pratinidhiniyamaḥ śrūyate tatrāpi na dravyasyāyaṃ niyamaḥ |

pūtīkasvarūpasyāpyayaṃ niyamaḥ | kintu tadgatā hi ye śrutadravyasadṛśāḥ śrutadravya-
sādhāraṇā avayavāḥ ta eva niyamyante | anyathā hi pūtīkasvarūpavidhiḥ syāt na
pratinidhiniyamaḥ aprasaktatvāt pūtīkasvarūpa(syā)grahaṇasya | pratinidhiniyamaścāyaṃ
pratipāditaḥ apekṣitavidhoḥ | yato’yaṃ pratinidhiniyamaḥ ato’trāpi arthena tulyaṃ
mukhyasadṛśamevopādātavyam | evaṃ tvatra nyāyyam---mukhyasadṛśamapi tadevopādātavyaṃ
yatra pūtīkasādhāraṇāḥ somāvayavā vidyante, na punaryatra pūtīkasadṛśāḥ somāvayavā
vidyante tadupādeyam | somāpacāre teṣāṃ niyatatvāt | tatsomāvayavasadṛśaṃ ca
pūtīkasadṛśaṃ copādeyamiti |

apekṣitavidhānāt tadgatāvayavā eva niyamyanta iti rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 16 || mukhyādhigame mukhyāmāgamo hi tadabhāvāt || 35 ||

 pratinidhisaṅka-

 | satyamasau saṅkalpaḥ | kintvasāvakratvarthaḥ | yadyapi nīvārāssaṅka-

|| 17 || pravṛttepīti cet || 36 ||

pravṛtte punaḥ karmaṇi pratinidhinā śrutadravyopalabdhau kiṃ śrutamevopādā-
tavyaṃ uta pratinidhinaiva samāptiriti
 pratinidhau keṣucitsaṃskāreṣu pravṛtteṣucintā |

nanvatra kecana saṃskārāḥ kṛtāḥ punaḥ kartuṃ na śakyante | tena tadanugrahāya

prayogasampattaye | tena pradhānaṃ dravyamevādaraṇīyam | saṃskārāstu nīyantāmiti bhāvaḥ |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---upādānepyetaccintyate nopātte |

viniyogamupādānamiti viniyogānurodhena śrutamevopādīyate | yatropādīyate yatro-
pādānasamaye viniyuktaṃ na labhyate tata upādātavye kimapyupādīyate | upātte ca
tasmin punarupādānāntarāsaṃbhavāt tenaiva prayogassaṃmpādanīyaḥ | upāttena prayoganirvṛttau

kṛtamiti | tasmāt pratinidhinaiva sampādayitavyamiti |

|| 18 || dravyasaṃskāravirodhe dravyaṃ tadarthatvāt || 37 ||

1. tena na - mā

[p. 145]

rāddhāntastu---satyaṃ saṃskāraśravaṇaṃ kintu śrutasya saṃskāraśravaṇam |
śrutābhāve punaḥ na ca saṃskārāḥ | śrutadravyāśritā hi saṃskārāḥ kratoraṅgam
tadabhāve teṣāmanaṅgataiva syāt | tasmāt śrutadravyamupādadataḥ yadi nāma
saṃskārā na bhavanti na bhavantu nāma tadabhāve’pi pradhānārthopapatteḥ |
 na hi
saṃskāro dravyasya dvāram | tena saṃskārābhāvepi dravyaśāstrārtho niṣpadyate | dravyābhāve
punaḥ na saṃskāraśāstrārthopapattiriti śrutasyaivopādānam | upādadatirūpasiddhau yatnaḥ
kartavyaḥ, ‘‘āṅodonāsya viharaṇe’’
 (pā. sū 1-3-20) ityātmanepadaprāpteḥ |

|| 19 || arthadravyavirodhe’rtho dravyābhāve tadutpatterdravyāṇā-
marthaśeṣatvāt || 39 ||

nanu yatra punaḥ prayojanameva śrutena nopapadyate | tatra śrutamapyaśrutameva
prayojanārthatvācchrutasya
 | ataḥ prayojanasiddhyarthaṃ samarthasyaivopādānamatreti pūrvādhi-
karaṇasya pratyudāharaṇamātrametat |

|| 20 || vidhirapyekadeśe syāt || 40 ||

nanu ca saṃskārābhāve’pi pradhānamupādātavyamityanenaitadgatam | kima-
bhyadhikamatra cintyate |

asyārthaḥ - śeṣakāryāṇi dravyapratipattyarthāni dravyaṃ prati guṇabhūtāni | tena
tadabhāve’pi dravyamevopādātavyamiti | uttaraṃ---idamabhyadhikam - puroḍāśasyā-
vadānaśrutayo bhavanti | na ca pradhanāmātrasaṃbhave puroḍāśatvopapattiriti
niyataparimāṇatvāt puroḍāśasya |
 asyārthaḥ - pūrvārdhādavadyatītyavadānaṃ puroḍāśe
śrutam | na ca dvayavadānamātraṃ puroḍāśo bhavati niyataparimāṇābhivyaṅgayatvāt puroḍā-
śākṛteḥ | tena puroḍāśe asamarthatayā nīvārā evopādātavyāḥ | puroḍāśānurodhena
vrīhīṇāṃ tyāgo yukta iti | tasmānnīvārāṇāmevopādānam | puroḍāśānurodhena vrīhīṇāṃ
tyāgo yukta iti | tasmānnīvārāṇāmevopādānaṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |

rāddhantastu satyaṃ puroḍāśasyāvadānaṃ, na punastat puroḍāśārtham |
kiṃ tarhi ? kratvartham |
 puroḍāśo dvayavadānaṃ prati guṇībhūta eva nāpekṣyate |

[p. 146]

nanu bhavatu dvyavadānaṃ prati puroḍāśasya guṇabhāvaḥ vrīhīn prati pradhāna[bhāva] eva |
vrīhayo’pi puroḍāśanirvṛttyarthāḥ | puroḍāśāstu dvyavadānārthāḥ | tena pradhānabhūta-
puroḍāśānugrahāya guṇabhūtānāṃ vrīhīṇāṃ tyāgo yukta iti |

ucyate | puroḍāśaprakṛtidravyatayā’ṅgabhūtā vrīhyādayaḥ sākṣāt dvayavadāne
saṃbadhyante | tanmayatvāttasya | tena bahiraṅgatvāt puroḍaśasyaiva tyāgaḥ antaratvāṅga-
hvrīhīṇāmanugraha iti yuktaḥ | ataḥ puroḍāśo’pi yatra guṇabhūta iti yāvat
nāpekṣya(kṣa)te [tatra] kiṃ punaḥ śeṣakāryāṇyaprayojakāni | tasmāt pradhānamātra-
nirvṛtyarthā vrīhaya evopādātavyā iti | darśanamuktam |

iti ṛjuvimalāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasya

tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ

[p. 147]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

|| ṣaṣṭhādhyāsya caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || 1śeṣād‌dvyavadānanāśe syāttadarthatvāt || 7 ||

nanu yasya havīṃṣi naśyeyuḥ ityādike vacane avadānanāśe dravyāntara-
vidhānārthe sati rpūvapakṣāśaṅkābhāvāt kathamidamadhikaraṇamārabdham |

samādhatte-asatyapi vacane nyāyādayamartho labhyata iti pradarśayitum
 |
vacanavyatirekeṇa hi dravyāntaramāgamayitavyam, na śeṣādavadeyamityayamartho nyāyenaiva
labhyate | tena caikasya dravyasya pūrvārdhaṃdvayaṃ vā saṃbhavatīti darśayitumidamadhikaraṇamārabdham |

yadi nyāyenaivāyamartho labhyate vacanamidānīṃ kimartham ?
 uttaraṃ---
dravyaviśeṣādhānārthamiti brūmaḥ |
 nyāyataḥ puroḍāśāntarāgame prāpte dravyaviśeṣa-
syājyasya lakṣaṇayā aprāptasya vidhānārtham | yadi vacanametat nyāyata eva dravyāntarāgame
prāpte dravyaviśeṣavidhānārthaṃ evaṃ tarhi ‘nirdeśāt’
 iti sūtraṃ
 (6-4-2)
‘yasya havīṃṣi’
 ityetannirdeśābhiprāyeṇa kathaṃ vyākhyātam
 | na hyanena vacanena
dravyāntarāgamo nirdiśyate |

samādhatte - dravyaviśeṣavidhānaṃ tu śeṣāditi jñāpanārtham
 | asyārthaḥ-
yadyapyetat dravyaviśeṣavidhānārtham tathāpi(vi)śeṣā(ṇā)nnāvadeyamiti jñāpayati | evaṃ
dravyāntaravidhānādeva | tena pūrvapakṣanirākaraṇārthametaduktamiti bhāvaḥ | śeṣanirdeśastu
pratyukto bhāṣye śeṣakāryāṇāmaprayojakatvānna tadanurodhena dravyāntaramupādeyamiti |

|| 2 || api vā śeṣabhājāṃ syādviśiṣṭakāraṇatvāt || 3 ||

kathaṃ punaratra pūrvapakṣāśaṅkā śeṣādeva punaravadeyamiti | na hi
śeṣakāryāṇi dravyaṃ prayuñjate
 | yadi hi dravyaṃ prayuñjīran tadā tairdravyasya prayukta-
tvāt tadakaraṇe dravyopādānavaiyarthyāt punaravadānaṃ syāt | na caitadasti | samādhatte-
sthita evāprayojakatve pratipattīnāmiyamāśaṅkā avirataśśāstrārthaḥ śeṣasya
1. 1429

[p. 148]

vidyamānatvāditi
 | na punaḥ prayojakatvamāśaṅkyate | pradhāne(tu tena)hute śeṣaḥ
sviṣṭakṛdādibhiḥ pratipādyata iti śrutam | tasya śāstrārthasyāviratireva | tena
punaravadeyamiti |

rāddhāntastu kāryārthatvācchāstrāṇāṃ śāstrārthānāṃ kṛtatvācca kāryasya
nānupapattiḥ śakyate vaktum
 |

yadyapi śeṣaśrutiraviśiṣṭā tathāpi kāryārthamanuṣṭhānaṃ dravyapratipattiḥ sviṣṭakṛdādīnāṃ
prayojanam | yacca dravyaṃ sviṣṭakṛdādibhiḥ pratipādyaṃ tat(da)vinaṣṭam tena pratipādya-
dravyābhāvāduparataḥ śāstrārthaḥ | yadi paraṃ śeṣakāryāṇāṃ prayoktṛtvāt punaḥ karaṇaṃ tacca
nāstītyuktam | pratipattyantaravidhānācca punaradānaṃ na kartavyamiti |

|| 3 || 1nirdeśāccheṣabhakṣo’nyaiḥ pradhānavat || 4 ||

keyamadhikāre cintā kathaṃ vā nāsti ?

uttaraṃ - śeṣacinteyaṃ ke bhakṣayitāra iti na punaḥ kasyādhikāra iti |

samādhatte---atrocyate - adhikāracintaiveyam | kāryaviniyuktakartṛ-
mato’dhikāraḥ utāviniyuktakartṛkasyāpīti
 | etaduktaṃ bhavati - yajamānasyai-
vādhikāraḥ cintyate | kiṃ[ye]viniyuktā ṛtvijaḥ tadyuktasyaivādhikāraḥ uta kartrantara-
yuktasyāpīti |

pūrvapakṣavādī tu aśvamedhādiṣvaśakteriti iḍāyāṃ na viniyuktakartṛ-
niyamādaviniyuktakartṛkasyāpyadhikāra iti manyate |

asyārthaḥ-yadi viniyuktakartṛkasyaivāyamadhikāraḥ tadā’śvamedhe śeṣabhakṣaṇasyā-
śakyatvāt anadhikāra eva syāt | tathā ‘yajamānapañcamā iḍāṃ bhakṣayanti’
 iti
vacanaṃ ṛtvijāmiḍābhakṣaṇe niyamārthaṃ ‘iḍaiva taireva bhakṣayitavyā’
 iti | taccaivamupa-
padyate yadi vacanāntareṇānyasyāpi bhakṣaṇaṃ prasajyate | na ca tadvacanaṃ yajamānaprāptyarthaṃ,
tasyāpi kartṛtvena prāptatvāditi na punaḥ viniyuktavyatiriktānāṃ viniyoga iti
śakyate vaktum śrutyādīnāmabhāvāt |

1. 1431

[p. 149]

[aviniyuktakartṛkasyāpyadhikāra iti śakyate vaktuṃ na punaraprakṛtānāṃ
viniyoga iti tadviniyoge śrutyādīnāmabhāva iti]

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---viniyuktakartṛkasyaivādhikāra iti | kutaḥ ?
na hyaśrutenādhikāraḥ śakyate vaktum | śrutaviṣayatvādadhikārāṇām
 | yādṛśo
viṣayaḥ śrūyate tadanurūpo’dhikāro varṇanīyaḥ | ṛtvijāṃ ca karmakaratvenopādānāt
tatkartṛkabhakṣayukto viṣayaḥ pratīyata iti tadyuktasyaivādhikāraḥ |

yatpunarvacanaṃ tatprāptyarthaṃ
 karmakaratvena ṛtvijāmupāttatvāt bhakṣo’pi taireva
prāpto na yajamānena, tatprāptyarthaṃ vacanamupapadyata iti darśitam | aśvamedhe punaḥ
tadvadevādhikāraḥ kāryārthaḥ punaranyopādānamiti nādhikārāntaraṃ prayuñjīta |
aṣi hi viniyuktakartṛkameva kāryamiti śabdādavagamyate
 |

etaduktaṃ bhavati-[vini]yuktasyādhikāraḥ tathāpi tāvanmātreṇaiva kāryasāmarthyāt
na sahāyatvenāprakṛtānāmupādānāditi |

|| 4 || arthasamavāyāt prāyaścittamekadeśe’pi || 10 ||

ihaitāvacchrūyate ‘bhinne juhoti’
 na punaḥ kṛtsnabhinne ekadeśabhinne
vā iti | tatra saṃśayaḥ kiṃ bhinnasyaivayaḥ saṃskāraḥ uta bhedenāpagato’dhi-
kāra iti
 | anena bhāṣyakārīyasya sandehasya karaṇe saṃdehaḥ kṛtaḥ | nanu ya[dya]tra
bhedanavato’dhikāraḥ tadā adhikārāntaratve sati darśapūrṇamāsāṅgatā na syāt | tataśca
prakaraṇabādha iti adhikṛtādhikārādaṅgatvasiddhiḥ |

pūrvapakṣamākṣipati---atrocyate - bhedanavato homaścodyate ‘tasmāt bhinne
juhoti’
 iti
 | bhedanamātramatra śrūyate na punaḥ parityaktasāmarthyasya aparityaktasāmarthyasya
vā bhedanavat iti viśeṣo’vagamyate | aviśeṣe ca parityaktasāmarthyasya saṃskārānupa-
patteḥ bhinnasaṃskārakatve niraste bhedavato homaścodyata iti vadāmaḥ |

samādhatte --- pūrvapakṣavādī tu kāryata eva viśeṣo’vagamyata iti manvānaḥ
samarthasaṃskāraṃ manyate | evamārādupakārakakalpanā ca kṛtā bhavati |
adhikaraṇāśaṅkānivṛttiśca na vaktavyā bhavati
 | ayamāśayaḥ-sannipatyopakārā-
dārādupakāratāṅgasaṃśaye sati sannipatyopakārāśrayaṇaṃ nyāyyam | antaraṅgatvāt |

[p. 150]

tena homasyāpi bhinnasaṃskāratvena sannipatyopakārakatvāśrayaṇaṃ nyāyyam | na ca
paratyaktasāmarthyaṃ saṃskāramarhatīti kāryaparyālocanayā parityaktasāmarthyaṃ bhinnaṃ pratīyate |
saptamī tviyaṃ agāre gāvo vāsyantāmiti ca draṣṭavyā | tenādhikaraṇabhūtasyaiva
saṃskārāt tannivṛttirapi vaktavyā na bhavati | rāddhāntamāha---atrocyate-satyamevaṃ,
kintu atra saptamīnirdeśāt na karmabhāvo bhinnasya
 | na hi karmabhāve saptamī
bhavati | yattu ‘agāre gāvo badhyantāṃ’
 iti tat pramāṇāntaratastathāvagamāt |
atra tu na tathā | kintarhi nimittabhāva eva saptamīyam tena bhinnasya nimittatā |
yuktyantaraṃ cāha---saṃskāryatve ca na nityasaṃskārasya aṅgabhāvaḥ śakyate
pratipattum bhinnasyānityatvāt
 | homastāvat nityamaṅgam | tadanurūpaṃ ca
ca dvāramāśrayaṇīyam | yadi ca bhinnasaṃskāro dvāraṃ syāt tadā bhodanasyānityatvāt
tatsamādhānadvāro homo’pi na nityaḥ syāt | atasso’yamananvayī śāstrārthaḥ syāt |
nimittabhāve evam | nāyaṃ doṣaḥ | nityānityasaṃyogalakṣaṇo doṣo na bhavati sati
nimitte tvaṅgatvāvagamāt naimittikamevāṅgam na punaḥ saṃskārapakṣe nimitte’sati aṅga-
homa iti śakyate vaktum | na ca nimittamadhikāriṇāṃ mate upapadyata iti darśitam |
tena bhedavato homaścānyata iti |

codayati---atha kasmādadhikaraṇasaptamyeveyaṃ na bhavati |
 uttaraṃ---na
bhavati āhavanīyabādhaprasaṅgāt
 |

sarvahomeṣvāhavanīya[sya] prāptatvāt bhavatu bādha eveti cet tatrāha---anyathā
vopapatteḥ
 nimitte saptamī(te) tvenāpyupapattau na bādhaḥ śakyate vaktum | atha
kasmānnimittanaimittikasaṃbandha eva na cocyate kimadhikāriviśeṣaṇena bheda-
nimittamiti
 | etāvadeva kimiti pradhānahoma eva na jñāpyate kimityaparaṃ
homāntaramāśrīyate tatra ca bhedanamadhikāriviśeṣaṇamiti |

uttaraṃ---siddhaviṣayo hi saṃbhavati | na ca siddhaviṣaye vedasya pramāṇya-
mastītyukte bhavati
 | yadi vede’pi siddhānuśāsanaṃ syāt tadā nimittanaimittika-
bhāvamātre vākyaṃ paryavasyet | na caitadasti anuṣṭhānopadeśakatvāditi homānuṣṭhāna-
vidhireva | homānuṣṭhāne cādhikāriviśeṣaṇasyaiva nimittatvamityaktam | tasmādbhedanavato
homaścodyata iti siddham | śeṣamuktam

|| 5 || kṣāme tu sarvadāhe syādekadeśasyāvarjanīyatvāt || 17 ||

[p. 151]

darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ śrūyate---atha yasya puroḍāśau kṣāyataḥ taṃ yajñaṃ varuṇo
gṛhṇāti | yadā taddhaviṣkaṃ karma santiṣṭhate atha tadeva havirnirvapet yajño yajñasya
prāyaścittiriti | tatrāyamarthaḥ-yasya yajñasya puroḍāśau kṣāyataḥ taṃ yajñaṃ varuṇo gṛhṇāti
yadā taddhaviṣkaṃ karma santiṣṭhate tadā tadeva haviḥ punarnirvaptavyamiti | yasya puroḍāśau
kṣāyata iti pūrvādhikaraṇanyāyena kṛtsne caikadeśe ca kṣāme nimittasya haviḥkṣāṇa-
lakṣaṇasya vidyamānatvāt | yasya puroḍāśau kṣāyata iti vacanavihitena naimittikena
bhavitavyamiti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | yadi kṣāmasaṃskārārthametat tadā ekadeśakṣāma eva
prāyaścittaṃ syāt na kṛtsnakṣāme | kṛtsnakṣāme’pi nimittasaṃbhavāttatrāpi yuktameva | na
ca kṛtsnekṣāme ityapi viśeṣaḥ śrūyate | [ataḥ] ubhayasminnapi kṣāme prāyaścittaṃ
yuktam | taddhaviḥ [saṃsthā] vyapadeśo nirviṣaya evaitatsyāt (?) | nanvevamapyeka-
deśakṣāma eva prāyaścittaṃ syāt | śabdaścaivamupapanno bhavati | yadi hi
akṛtsnakṣāme etadvacanaṃ syāt tadā taddhaviḥ santiṣṭhata iti saṃbhavati
 |
ayamarthaḥ-sarvadāhe tu dagdhe haviḥ saṃsthānānupapatteḥ taddhavirvyapadeśo na ghaṭate sarvadāhe
tu havirantaramupādāya prayoge kriyamāṇe taddhavirvyapadeśo nirviṣaya eva syāt |

nanvaivamapi ekadeśadāha eva prāyaścittaṃ syāt na kṛtsnadāhe | na hi tadā
taddhavirvyapadeśo ghaṭata iti | satyam | tata haviḥ yasya taddhaviḥ iti vyapadeśaḥ
ekadeśadāha evopapadyate | tathāpi ārtyadhikaraṇanyāyena yajñasaṃsthaiva nimittaṃ na punaḥ
tena haviṣā saṃsthānaṃ nimittaṃ, tenānyena [a]dahatāpi haviṣā kriyamāṇe naimittikaṃ
yuktameveti | kintu yadi sarvadāha evaitadvacanaṃ syāt tadā taddhaviḥ śabdāt
prāyaścittamityuktaṃ tadanvāruhyavādamātram |

evaṃ prāpti’bhidhīyate---aparihāryatvādekadeśakṣāṇasya sarvakṣāṇanimittaṃ
prāyaścittamiti yuktam
 yasya puroḍāśau kṣāyata iti viśeṣavacanaṃ sarvadāha evopa-
padyate eka deśadāhe tu sarvasya saṃbhavāt viśeṣavacanaṃ nopapadyate | tasmāt kṛtsnakṣāṇa-
prāyaścittamiti |

atra codayati---yāvatpākaṃ hi yā kṣāṇatā sā bhavatvaparihāryā
pākānaupayikī tu yā kṣāṇatā sā kimiti nimittaṃ na bhavati
 | yāvatyā
kṣāmatayā vinā na pāka eva niṣpadyate tāvatī kṣāmatā’vaśyaṃbhāvinī mābhūnnimittam |
yayā vināpi pāko bhavati tāvatī kādācitkā nimittamastu | sa caikadeśakṣāme’pi

[p. 152]

saṃbhavati | laukikā api hi anīpsite pākānaupayike dāhe kṣāmaśabdaṃ prayuñjate
dagdho guळḥ dagdha odana iti | tenānīpsitaṃ pākānaupayikaṃ kṣāṇamiti |

pariharati---[satyaṃ] yathā bhavānāha, anīpsitaṃ pākānaupayikaṃ kṣāṇa-
mabhipretamiti | tadeva na jñāyate kiyadīpsitamiti
 | tena īpsitapākā-
naupayikatvena viśeṣo jñātuṃ na śakyate | ākṣeptā āha---kimiti na jñāyate |
puroḍāśasiddhirapekṣitā | yāvatā pākena puroḍāśaḥ siddhayati tāvān pāka
īpsitaḥ
 |

samādhātā āha---satyaṃ puroḍāśasiddhirīpsitā saiva tu na jñāyate
kiyatā kṣāṇena tu sidhyati kiyadanupayogīti
 | ayamāśayaḥ - yāvatā pākena
puroḍāśaḥ siddhayati tāvānīpsitaḥ (tāvatānīpsitaṃ) tena yāvāneva puroḍāśasiddhi-
vighātakārī pākaḥ tāvānevānīpsitaḥ | sa ca sarvadāha eva | tāvatā hi puroḍāśa-
buddhirapaiti | laikike punaḥ loka eva pramāṇaṃ īpsitānīpsitatve | tatra niyamo
nāsti pratipuruṣamīpsitabhedāt | tasmāt suṣṭhūcyate---aparihāryatvādekadeśa-
kṣāṇasya sarvakṣāṇa eva prāyaścittamiti
 | tacchabdaparihārastūktaḥ anyenāpi-
haviṣā saṃsthāpyamānaṃ karma taddhaviśśabdena lakṣayituṃ śakyata iti |

|| 6 || 1yathāśrutīti cet || 22 ||

nanu coddiśyamānasya viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitamiti grahādhikaraṇe uktam |
tenaivādhikāriṇo’pi viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitamityuktam | tena punaruktatvādanā-
rabhyamevādhikaraṇam |

pariharati---satyaṃ kena voktaṃ na pratipāditamiti | yadi pratipāditaṃ
kiṃ tarhi anenedaṃ anena kimadhikriyamāṇaviśeṣaṇaṃ vivakṣyate neti
cintyate
 | asyārthaḥ---uddiśyamānasya viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitamiti tatroktam |
iha tu adhikriyamāṇasya viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitamiti pratipādyate | tena nāsti
paunaruktyamiti bhāvaḥ |

nanu uddiśyamānaviśeṣaṇavivakṣābhidhāne nedamupalabhyata iti atrāha---

nacādhikāryuddiśyate | kiṃ tarhi karmaṇyadhikriyate | anyā adhi-
kāritā anyā coddiśyamānateti manyate
 | vidheyasya viṣayatāpattiruddheśyatvaṃ,
karmaṇi svāmitvamadhikriyamāṇatvam | tena yatra vidheyārthatvenoddiśyate tadgatā sā
cintā | atra tu niyojyagatā cinteti viśeṣaḥ |

atra pūrvapakṣavādī padadvayaviśiṣṭāmārtiṃ adhikriyamāṇaviśeṣaṇatayā
manvānaḥ padadvayaviśiṣṭārtiṃ nimittamityāha---yasyobhayaṃ havirārtimārcche-
diti
 | ākhyātenārtirucyate | sā ca havirubhayapadābhyāṃ yugapat viśeṣyate
kraya ivāruṇaikahāyanībhyām | (te)viśiṣṭā cāsau puruṣaviśeṣaṇamiti | ubhayaviśeṣaṇa-
viśiṣṭā ārtiḥ nimittam | ṛcchatiśca dhātuḥ āṅpūrvaḥ ārtau vartate ārtiśabdo’pi
ktinnantaḥ tasmādeva vyutpādyate | upasargādṛti dhātau (pā. sū 6-1-11) iti
vṛddhau kṛtāyāṃ raparatve ca rāllope ilope; ārtimārcchediti tu yaducyate tadā odana-
pākaṃ pacatītivat draṣṭavyam | rāddhāntavādī tu satyaṃ, sarvaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭo’dhi-
kārī gamyate | na punarekapadībhūtaiḥ viśeṣaṇaiḥ viśeṣyate samāsābhāvāt
ekapadībhūtāni etāni na bhavanti | tataḥ kiṃ ? idaṃ tato bhavati adhi-
kriyamāṇaviśeṣaṇaṃ hi phalatayā nimittatayā vā gamyate | na karmaṇaḥ
punaḥ punaḥ svaviśeṣaṇatayā
 svasyātmanaḥ yā viśeṣaṇatā tayā nāvagamyate |
viśeṣaṇamātramāha---paryavasāyī na bhavatīti
 | kintu karmaṇaḥ phalatvena
nimittatvena vā’vatiṣṭhate | taccedānīṃ phalaṃ vā nimittaṃ vā | yathaivādhi-
kṛtaviśeṣaṇatayā’vagataṃ tathaiva bhavitumarhati | svapadārthaprayuktaṃ ca viśeṣaṇatayāvagataṃ
na tatkāryādṛte śakyata ekīkartum | svakīye padārthe ca prayuktaṃ padajātaṃ adhikāri-
viśeṣaṇatayā’vagataṃ na śakyate kāryādṛte upādeyādṛte ekī kartum | yathā kraya upā-
deyatvena coditaḥ ekībhūtaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭo’vagamyate | na tathehāvagantuṃ śakyate
anupādeyatvādārteḥ | na cedaṃ kāryaṃ nimittaparyantatvādasyādhikārasya | na ca nimitta-
mupādeyaṃ bhavati | ato nedaṃ parasparasaṃbandhanimittatvenopādātuṃ śakyate | ekaṃ
cedaṃ vākyam | tasmādanyataradatra nimittatvena pratipattavyam | nobhayam
 |
havirārtirnimittaṃ nobhayaṃ viśeṣyatvāddhaviṣaḥ | ānantaryācca | tena haviṣi
ārte nimitte ubhayapadamanuvādaḥ---yadyekapadībhāvāvagamābhāvāt ubhayatva-
viśiṣṭaṃ havirnimittaṃ tadā kiṃ havireva nimittaṃ
 ? uttaraṃ---na ārtiviṣayabhūta-
miti brūmaḥ
 |

[p. 154]

nanu ca ārtirapi naiva haviṣā ekapadībhūtā kathaṃ tadviṣayabhūtasya viśeṣaṇatā |
naiṣa doṣaḥ | bhāvāśrayatvānnāmnaḥ | na haviḥ[ṣaḥ] puruṣeṇa saṃbandhaḥ | abhāvārthānāṃ
kriyākārakasaṃbandhapūrvaka eva parasparasaṃbandhaḥ | parasparaṃ punaḥ nāma saṃbandhi | ekapadī-
bhāva eva hetuḥ tenārtisaṃbandhāt puruṣasaṃbandhaḥ | tenā[rta]rtimeva haviḥ puruṣasya
viśeṣaṇamiti ārtiviśeṣaṇībhūtameva haviḥ nimittamiti siddham |

nanu vākyādapi parasparasaṃbandho nāmnāmavagamyate---yathā devadattasya
gāva iti
 | pariharati---bāḍhaṃ sa tu vaibhaktikaḥ | vibhaktiśca dvayoreva
saṃbandhamācaṣṭe, na punaranyenāpīti
 | autsargikamidaṃ, tena havirārtyaiva saṃbadhyatāṃ,
nobhayatvena | tasmādārtiviṣayaṃ havirnimittaṃ viśeṣyā [tvā]dānantaryācca | ārtiviṣayamiti
napuṃsakanirdeśaḥ havirviśeṣaṇatvāt |

nanu cobhayaśabde nimittatvena parigṛhyamāṇe haviṣo’pi anupraveśaḥ
siddho bhavati | ubhayatvasya nityasāpekṣatvāt | ataḥ sakalapadānuguṇyena
tathaiva sambandha āśrīyatām
 | yasyobhayaṃ(haviḥ) ārtimārcchet kiṃ tadubhayamitya-
syāmapekṣāyāṃ havireva parigṛhyate |

pariharati---asaṃbandhajño bhavān padārthajātasya viśeṣaṇaṃ hi viśeṣya-
mapekṣamāṇaṃ viṣayatayā’pekṣate
 nātmano’vacchedakatayā | ubhayatvaṃ saṅkhyā | sā ca
ārtiviṣayatayā bhavituṃ nārhati iti ārtiviṣayaṃ dravyamevāpekṣate | tena yadyubhayatvaṃ
havirapekṣate tathāpi viśeṣaṇatvena naivāpekṣate | kiñca ārtiviṣayatayā ārtiviṣayabhāve
ca tasya tadeva nimittaṃ bhavati tadāha---na cedamavacchedakaṃ, apitu viṣayaḥ |
viṣayaḥ punaḥ viśeṣaṇanirapekṣaḥ nimittatāmāpadyate iti havirārtimātrameva
nimittaṃ vyartha evobhayapadākāṅkṣopanyāsaḥ
 | yadidaṃ haviṣo dravyatvena
ārtiviṣayatvaṃ nobhayatvasya tadidamuktaṃ---mṛṣyāmahe na haviṣo viśeṣaṇamiti

akāryatayā anupādeyatayā cādhikṛtaviśeṣaṇānāṃ yāvantaḥ parasparasaṃbandhāḥ pradarśyante
yāvatsaṃbhavatsaṃbhavatāmidameva nirākaraṇamityuparamyate samāsaṃ vinā parasparasaṃbandhānāṃ
viśeṣaṇatvānupapattiriti idameva nirākaraṇaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇāpīti iyatāmupanyāso nirā-
karaṇasya vyāptipradarśanārtha eva kṛtaḥ | na hi pramāṇādṛte nimittasamāhārassiddhayatīti
siddhaṃ havirmātraviṣayārtirnimittamiti |

|| 7 || homābhiṣavabhakṣaṇaṃ ca tadvat || 24 ||

atrāpi dvayornāmnoḥ parasparasaṃbandho nāstīti adhikriyamāṇa-
viśeṣaṇatvepi pratyekaṃ nimittabhāvaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---abhiṣutya hutvā bhakṣayatī-
tyatra dvayoḥ vatvāntayornāmnoḥ parasparasaṃbandho nāstīti yadyapi vastusthityā parasparasaṃbandha-
yorapi puruṣaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ saṃbhavati tathāpi śabdasāmarthyānusāreṇa pratyekameva nimittabhāvaḥ
‘abhiṣutya bhakṣayanti’
 tathā ‘hutvā bhakṣayanti’
 iti | tena ye abhiṣavamātre’pi
kartāraḥ te’pi bhakṣayanti | ye’pi homa mātre te’pīti | na cobhayapadavadavivakṣā
saṃbhavati
 | yathā ubhayapadamavivakṣitaṃ tathā’bhiṣave nimitte hutveti na vivakṣitaṃ
anuvādamātram | na ca śakyaṃ vaktuṃ haviṣo’nyasyobhayatvaṃ nimittaṃ bhaviṣyatīti |
anyasyānupādānāt haviṣaścopādānāt tadviṣayatvamevobhayatve pratīyate | iha puna-
raviśeṣādubhayaṃ nimittam | abhiṣave nimitte homasya nimittatā na prāptā | tathā
home nimitte’bhiṣavasyeti nānuvādopapattiriti |

nanu cābhiṣutya hutveti saṃbandho bhavati | ktvāpratyayasya samāna-
kartṛkārthatvāt dvitīyena vinā dhātvarthena tadanupapatteḥ
 | tadavaśyāpekṣaṇīye
abhiṣutyeti bhavati | hutveti tu bhakṣayatītyetadapekṣam | tenābhiṣavahomayoḥ paraspara-
sahitayoḥ nimittatvamiti | pariharati---satyaṃ kriyāntarāpekṣitā ktvāpratyayasya
na punaḥ ktvāntakriyāpekṣitā | tasyā apyanyaparatvāt | na hi dvayora-
pradhānayoḥ mithassaṃbandho yuktaḥ
 | kintu sarvaṃ pradhānenaiva saṃbadhyate pradhānaṃ ca
bhakṣayatītyetat na tu hutveti | tasyāpi bhakṣayatītyetasmin guṇabhāvāt | tasmādaya-
mabhisambandhaḥ abhiṣutya bhakṣayati, hutvā bhakṣayati iti | kramavidhānaṃ tvatra naiva
saṃbhavati prāptatvāt
 | abhiṣutyaiva homaḥ prāptaḥ huta eva ca bhakṣaṇam pratipatti-
tvāt | nāpyabhiṣavahomayoḥ bhakṣaṇāṅgatvena vidhānam adhikṛtaviśeṣaṇatvāt | nāpi
svārthavidhānaṃ, abhiṣavasya ‘somamabhiṣuṇuyāt’
 iti prāsatvāt | ‘somena yajeta
iti ca homasya | tasmānnimittanaimittikavidhirevāyam | sa ca pratyekamiti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

atrābhidhīyate---samānakartṛkatāvagamāt ktvāntānāmabhiṣavahomayo-
ścānyataḥ prāptatvāt ekakartṛkatvasyaiva abhiṣutya hutveti codyate |

[p. 156]

asyārthaḥ ktvāntena śabdena samānakartṛkatā ucyate | homābhiṣavau ca
samānakartṛkau siddhau | tena sa eva samānakartā ucyate | tenāyamarthaḥ sampadyate---
abhiṣavahomayoḥ dvayoḥ kartā bhakṣayati iti | atrāyaṃ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

nanu ca dvayoḥ ktvāntayoḥ parasparasaṃbandho nāvagamyate | anyārtha-
tvāt vyatiriktavibhaktyantarayuktayoriva
 | yathā aruṇayaikahāyanyeti ca
prātipadikāvyatiriktakaraṇatvābhidhāyitṛtīyāntayoḥ parasparasaṃbandho na bhavati | tathā
ktvāntayorapi na yuktaṃ, avyatiriktaprathamāntayorbhavatyeva saṃbandhaḥ na vyatirikta-
vibhaktyantayoḥ | pariharati---

satyaṃ, yatra pramāṇāntaraṃ nāsti tatra pradhānakriyayaiva ktvāntasya
saṃbandhaḥ | yatra punaḥ pramāṇāntarāvagatai(re)va tadviṣayatā na tatra viṣayānta-
rāvagatirbhavati pramāṇāntareṇa ekavākyalakṣaṇena
1, yatra ktvāntayorekakartṛviṣaya-
tā’vagatā na tatra kartrantarāvagati | yadi hi ekaviṣayatā na syāt tadā eka-
vākyatā na syāt | ekavākyatā cāvagamyate | tenaikavākyatvāt tayoḥ ktvāntayo-
rapi parasparasaṃbandhaḥ | yathā kṛṣṇasya devadattasya gāva iti | kintvekavākyatā-
balāt kṛṣṇaguṇasya devadattasyaiveti saṃbandho bhavati | evamatrāpi abhiṣutya hutveti
ekakartṛviṣayamevādhikāriviśeṣaṇamavagamayati ekavākyatvāt | adhikṛtamukhena ca
viśeṣaṇasya(ā)vidhyanvayāt siddhā samāhārasya nimittatā | samāhṛtaviśeṣaṇa-
viśiṣṭasyā dhikārāt siddhā samāhṛtayoreva nimittatā | na cobhayapadavat nivartayituṃ
sāmarthyaṃ nāstīti śakyate vaktum, bhinnadvāratvāt padadvayasyāsya kartarīti | tatra
punaḥ vidhidvārakameva padadvayamiti viśeṣaḥ abhiṣavahomayostvananyatantratvāt nivartayituṃ
sāmarthyamasti | abhayapadaṃ havirviṣayameva | tena havirmātrārtinimittaṃ na śaknoti
nivārayitum | ayaṃ tu kevalasya nimittatāṃ śaknotyeva vārayitumiti |

 iti ubhayaśabdasyāgniviṣayatvāt havi(ṣu)

|| 9 || 1pañcaśarāvastu dravyaśruteḥ pratinidhiḥ syāt || 28 ||

nirvapatisaṃbandhāt pratinidhiniyamaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī
 | aindraṃ
pañcaśarāvamodanaṃ nirvapet iti nirvapatiśravaṇāt sānnāyye vipanne ca dravyāntaraṃ
nirvapediti codyate | yadi hi karmāntaraṃ vidhīyate tadā tatra nirvāpasya codakaprāptasya
anuvādakaḥ syāt |

kathamasminnapi pakṣe aindraśabdaḥ ?
 yadyodanaṃ nirvapediti vidhīyate katha-
maindraśabdaḥ | na hi so’pi vidheyārtho’vakalpate vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | uttaraṃ---
prāptatvādanuvādaṃ manyate
 | pratinidhīyamānaṃ dravyamaindrameva prāptaṃ indrasya tasmin
karmaṇi devatābhāvāt | nanu ca mahendro’pi[mahendrasyāpi] tatra devatābhāvāt; na
cendraśabdena mahendro’bhidhātuṃ śakyate | ataḥ kathamanuvāda iti tatrāha---

prāptatvānmahendraśabdāśravaṇamapi adoṣaḥ
 nāvaśyaṃ sarvameva prāptamanuvadita-
vyamiti bhāvaḥ | atrocyate | yataḥ prāptaṃ nāvaśyaṃ sarvamevānuvaditavyaṃ
vināpi tena vivakṣitārthasiddheḥ
 | ata eva karmāntaramidaṃ; anyathā hi māhendra-
śabdavat aindraśabdo’pi na vaktavyaḥ syāt | uktaścāyam | sa ca yasmin pakṣe’rthavān
sa eva pakṣaḥ āśrayituṃ nyāyyaḥ | tasmādvidhānārthataiva nyāyyā |

nanvevamapi nirvapatiśabdo’narthaka ityatrāha---nirvapatiśabdastu pratya-

 | aindraśabdassarvatrānarthakaḥ nirvapatiśabdastu

|| 10 || 1sa pratyāmanet sthānāt || 30 ||

pañcaśarāva idānīṃ cinatte---kiṃ svatantraṃ karmāntaraṃ uta darśapūrṇamā-
sādhikṛtasyaiva tannāśe tatkāryasādhanamupadiśyate utāmāvāsyāṅgamiti
 |
sthite karmāntara[tve]hi cintopapadyate | idānīmiti vadan pakṣatrayamatra cintyata iti ca
darśayati | tena bhāṣye amāvāsyāṃ pratyāmanediti pakṣaḥ tadā[tathā]kiṃ karmāntaraṃ
svatantraṃ uta tadaṅgameveti draṣṭavyam | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī tāvat vinaṣṭasyāṅga-
bhāvo’narthakaḥ | ataḥ tatkāryasyaivedaṃ sādhanamiti manyate | svatantrapakṣo’pi mandatayā
nopanyastaḥ | rāddhāntavādī tu svātantrye tāvadadhikārāntarakalpanaiva doṣaḥ
 |

kāryakaraṇatve’pi pradhānaṃ pramāṇaṃ naivāṅgi aṅgatā punaḥ prakaraṇāmnānādavasīyate |
nanu vinaṣṭasya aṅgabhāvo’narthaka ityuktam | aṅgavināśādevāvināśo bhaviṣyati |
haviṣi kila viparnna karmaṇo dravyāntareṇa kriyamāṇasya vaiguṇya saṃbhavati | tacca vaiguṇya-
maṅgavidhānādeva pratisamādhāsyate |

nanu cānyathāpi idaṃ vidhānamupapannamityuktam | kāryakaraṇatayā
vidhānopapatteḥ sa śakyate’ṅgatayā vidhānam
 | uttaraṃ---na prakaraṇe’nyathoṣa-
padyate
 | prakaraṇādaṅgataivāvagamyate | pratyakṣaśca prakaraṇapāthaḥ | kāryasvātantryayostu
kalpanopapadyate | tasmādaṅgataiveti yuktam |

|| 11 || 2viśvajittvapravṛtte bhāvaḥ karmaṇi syāt || 32 ||

āgurataḥ karma codyate | atra saṃśaya iti
 | anena saviṣayatayā[tāṃ]
nyāyasya darśayati viśvajidvā bhavati anyadvā | sarvathā padāgūryamāṇasya karma codyate |
tatrāyaṃ vicāraḥ---āgurata iti rūpasiddhau yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ | gurateranudāttettvāt
ātmanepadaprasakteḥ | saṃśayasvarūpamāha---

kiṃ yadāguritaṃ satrādi tatpravṛttau satyāmeva idaṃ karma uta aviśeṣeṇa
atha[a]pravṛttāveveti
 | anenāviśeṣapakṣo yadyapi bhāṣyakāreṇopanyāsakāle manda-
tvānnopanyastaḥ tathāpi nirākaraṇadarśanādupādātavyaḥ iti darśitam | āguritamiti

śabdasādhutāyāṃ yatna āstheyaḥ | gurīdhātorīditvādiṭpratiṣedhopapatteḥ | viśeṣānu-
pādānādaviśeṣeṇa1 iti prāpte ucyate---

āgūryamāṇasya pravṛttau viśvajit iti | evamaṅgatā bhavati | na nimittaparya-
nto’dhikāraḥ kalpito bhavati | āgūryamāṇebhyaḥ pravṛtte karma vidhīyamānaṃ āgoraṇa-
nimittakaṃ bhavati | tacca na yuktam | adhikārāntarakalpanāprasaṅgāt | pravṛtte tasyeda-
maṅgamiti kalpanā laghvī | aviśeṣe punaḥ nimittaparyantatā | pravṛttyabhāvapakṣe’ṅgataiveti
vipratiṣiddham | svatantratāṅgatvayorvirodhāt |

atrocyate---apravṛttasyai vedaṃ karmā’’guramāṇasya codyate | āgūrya-
māṇasyāgoraṇaviśeṣārthatvāt na śakyate vidheyasyāṅgatā vaktum
 | ‘yaḥ satrā-
yāgurate sa viśvajitā yajeta’
 iti nāyamabhisaṃbandhaḥ ‘satrāya viśvajitā yajeta’
 iti
vyavahitānvayaprasaṅgāt, kintu satrāyāgurate iti | tenāgoraṇaviśeṣaṇatvāt āgūrya-
māṇaṃ pratyaṅgatā caturthībalena vidhīyamānasya na śakyā sampādayitum | tena svatantra-
taiva nyāyyā |

nanu cāsminnapi pakṣe āgoraṇasya nimittaparyantatā kalpanīyā |
tadvaraṃ samabhivyāhriyamāṇasatrāṅgataivāśrīyatām | nimittaparyantatve’pi
svatantrādhikārāntaramāśrayaṇīyam
 | aṅgatve tu tanna bhavatīti | uttaraṃ---
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ āgoraṇasya samāptiparyantatvāt āgūryamāṇakāryaparyantataivā-
śrīyate
 tasmāt pūrvādhikṛtakāryaparyantataiveti na kāryāntaraṃ kalpitaṃ bhavati | āgoraṇa-
mudyamanaṃ, udyamanaṃ ca samāptyartham samāptiśca kāryaparyantataivocyate udyamanaṃ ca tasya
bhavati | tena satratā kāryatayā pratipannā | tasya viśvajidvidhīyamānaḥ tatkāryaparyanta
evāvasīyate | ataḥ kāryāntarakalpanā nāsti | tadevaiśvaryaṃ viśvajitāpi puruṣasya
yataḥ sādhyate tasmādayameva pakṣaḥ śreyān viśvajitā satrakāryaṃ kuryāditi |

|| 12 || 2vatsasaṃyoge vratacodanā syāt || 34 ||

keyamadhikāre aṅgāṅgicintā | yadi kālalakṣaṇā yadi vā śrautārthaḥ
ubhayathāpyaṅgapradhānacintaiva
 | ekatra pakṣe śrutyā vatsasya vratāṅgatayā vidhiḥ |

|| 13 || 1kālaścetsannayatpakṣe talliṅgasaṃyogāt || 41 ||

nanu caiva puroḍāśānāmapi anapāyo hi kālasyetyuktam | kimatrā-
bhyadhikaṃ cintyate | na, paunaruktyamāśaṅkyate yo’pyeṣa puroḍāśaḥ paurṇamāsyā-
masti asomayājinaḥ tasyāmasatyapi agnīṣomīye puroḍāśe puroḍāśayormadhya-
vartikālaḥ upāṃśuyājasaṃbandhī vidyata evetyuktam | tadvadihāpi
 | asānnā-
yyayājino vatsāpākaraṇābhāve’pi tatkālasyāpāyo nāstīti tasyāpi kālavidhiryukta
eveti siddha eva rāddhāntaḥ |

kimarthamidamadhikaraṇamārabhyate ? astyatra viśeṣaḥ---adhikāramukhenai-
tatkālavidhānam
 | adhikṛtasyaivāyaṃ kālavidhiruktaḥ | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī kālopa-
lakṣaṇapadārthayuktasyaivādhikṛtasyeti manyate | kālopalakṣaṇena vatsāpākaraṇena yukta-
syaivādhikāriṇaḥ kālavidhirayaṃ yuktaḥ | na hi anyasya tatkālo vidhātuṃ śakyate |
tadabhāve tatkāla evāsau na bhavatīti bhāvaḥ |

rāddhāntavādī tu - satyaṃ adhikṛtasyaivāyaṃ kālavidhiḥ | kālasaṃbhava-
mātre tvadhikṛto’yam
 | tasya hi kālo vidhātavyaḥ yasya hi kālaḥ saṃbhavati tena
saṃbhava evā dhikṛto’yaṃ tasya kālavidhiḥ | sa ca kālo vatsāpākaraṇarahitasyāpyasti |

|| 14 || 1prastare śākhā śrayaṇavat || 43 ||

atrāpi viniyogapratipakṣatayā’dhikāra eva cintyate
 | aṅgavidhau
aṅgabhūtaśākhāvidhau viniyogaḥ | kālavidhau prastarapraharaṇakāle śākhā praharaṇaṃ prati
vidhīyamānā’dhikṛtasyaiva vidhiḥ | na punaḥ guṇārthe tṛtīyeyaṃ śaṅkyate | upapada-
lakṣaṇā hīyaṃ na kārakalakṣaṇā | sahaśabdaśravaṇāt sahaprayoganibandhanaiveyaṃ
tṛtīyā na kārakalakṣaṇā | tena kathaṃ śākhāyāṃ guṇabhāvaśaṅkā
 |

satyaṃ upapadavibhaktiriyam aprādhānyaṃ tu anyathā nopapadyate
 |
tena guṇabhāvaṃ manyate | ‘sahayukte’pradhāna’
 iti apradhānavācinaḥ prātipadikāt iyaṃ
tṛtīyā | tena śākhā apradhānabhṛtā guṇabhūtaivāvasīyate |

atra codayati---bhavatvatra prādhānyaṃ sahabhāve, mā bhūt prastare
 | yadyapya-
prādhānye sahārtheyaṃ tṛtīyā, tathāpi tadaprādhānyaṃ sahabhāve |

kimidaṃ sahabhāva iti? sa pradhānaṃ yasya tu prasiddha eva kriyāsaṃbandhaḥ, yasya na
tadapradhānaṃ tena praharaṇasaṃbandhaḥ śākhāyā ucyata iti | bhavatvaprādhānyaṃ śākhāyāḥ
prastaraṃ pratiguṇībhāvo na yuktaḥ
 | samādhate-tatsiddhistviha na saṃbhavatīti hi
paśyan pūrvapakṣavādī sahabhāvamavivakṣitaṃ manyate
 | śākhāyāḥ praharaṇasambandhasyāpi
aviditatvāt prastarasya viditatvāt sahabhāve’prādhānyaṃ na saṃbhavatīti avivakṣita
evātra sahabhāvaḥ | tasmāt kārakatayaivātra guṇabhāva upapadyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntastu---sahabhāvāvivakṣā na vibhaktibalena yuktā | vibhakta-
yo’pi vyatyayenāpi smaryante
 | asyārthaḥ - yadi śākhāśabdaḥ tṛtīyānto gṛhyeta
1. 1455

[p. 162]

prastaraśabdaśca dvitīyāntaḥ tadā śākhāyāḥ aprādhānyāt anupapatteḥ sahabhāvo’vivakṣitaḥ
syāt | sā ceyamavivakṣā vibhaktibalena | na ca vibhaktiḥ sahaśabdamapi vivakṣitaṃ
kartuṃ śaknoti | vibhaktivyatyayenāpi prayogadarśanāt | tenātra vibhaktervyatyayena varṇanā
‘prastareṇa śākhāṃ praharati’
 iti | upapadyate cātra sahārthaḥ nopapadyamānasyāvivakṣā
yuktā | kiñca kṛtaprayojanā ca śākhā vatsāpākaraṇena | kṛtaprayojanānāṃ ca
pratipattau satyāṃ na kāryāntarakalpanā’vakalpate iti | prastarapraharaṇamapi kāryāntaraparam
na śākhāyāḥ (kāryāntaraṃ) kalpayituṃ śakyate - viniyuktaviniyogavirodhāditi |
sahayoga evāyaṃ pratipattavya iti vibhaktivyatyaya ityevātra nyāyyaḥ | upakāraścātra
viparītaḥ tṛṇamayaprastaraḥ śākhāyāṃ dahyamānāyāṃ upakartumarhatīti śāsvāprastare prayojana-
muktam | pūrvapakṣe amāvāsyāyāmasannayatā śākhāupādeyā prastarapraharaṇāṅgatvapakṣe |
pratipattipakṣe tu yatraiva sānnāyyāṅgabhūtā śākhā’sti tatraiva pratipādyā bhavatīti |

iti ṛjuvimalāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasya
caturthaḥ pādaḥ

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasya pañcamaḥ pādaḥ

|| 1 || abhyudaye kālāparādhādijyācodanāsyādyathā
pañcaśarāve || 1 ||

atreyaṃ viṣayaśuddhiḥ---amāvāsyāyāmeva darśārthena vediḥ kriyate | agni-
praṇayanādikaṃ vratādikaṃ ca yajamānasaṃskāraḥ dadhyarthaśca dohaḥ | pratipadi darśaḥ pravartate
iti | eṣa tāvat pāramārthiko’nuṣṭhānaniyamaḥ | yasya tu yajamānasya kutaścit
gaṇanabhramanimittā caturdaśyāmevāmāvāsyābuddhirupajāyate sā tasyāmamāvāsyāyāṃ karma
pravartayati | tasya candramasā havirabhyudīyate | tatredaṃ śrūyate ‘yasya havirniruptaṃ’

iti | tena yajamānenābhyuditena haviṣā amāvāsyāyāmeva nimittādhikāraṃ parisamāpya
punastadahareva vedyuddhananādi karma kṛtvā pratipadyeva darśaḥ kartavya iti | abhyudaye-
[ṣṭiri]ti naimittikamidaṃ karmāntaraṃ vidhīyate | athavā tadevāpanītadevatāke iviḥ
punaḥ devatāntaraiḥ saṃyujyate iti |

pūrvapakṣe niyogaḥ, rāddhānte tu adhikṛtasya prayogaviśeṣacodaneti | adhi-
kāralakṣaṇena(ṇa na) karmasaṃbandhitā śāstrasya
 | anena adhikaraṇasya lakṣaṇasaṅgati-
māha | akṣarārthastu darśāt karmāntaraṃ vidhīyata iti | ayamatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | atra
ca viniyogo dvaitīyaḥ karmāntarasyādhikāre viniyoga upakrānto darśitaḥ | karmaṇaḥ
prayogaviśeṣo’yaṃ codyata iti rāddhāntaḥ | tatra ca prayogaviśeṣacodanā, prayogaviśe-
ṣaścādhikṛtasyeti adhikāriviśeṣāśrayatvāt adhikāralakṣaṇamevedamiti bhavati lakṣaṇa-
saṅgatiḥ | nanu ca yadyapi pūrvapakṣe karmasambandhitā śāstrasya tathāpi
kimityadhikāradvārā cintā na bhavatīti
 |

atrāha---karmasaṃbandhe hi adhikṛtadvārā cintopapadyate | tasmin
sati adhikārāt | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī karmāntaraṃ vidhīyata iti manyate |
tredhā taṇḍulān vibhajet iti taṇḍulavibhāgo’trāvagamyate na dadhipayasoḥ

asyārthaḥ | yadi(devataiva na)[devatābhyaḥ] haviṣāṃ vibhāgaḥ syāt tadā apanīta-
devatānāṃ haviṣāṃ devatāntareṇa saṃyogaḥ śakyate kartum | tataśca prakṛtānāmeva karmaṇā-

[p. 164]

mayamanuṣṭhānaviśeṣaḥ codyata iti rāddhāntasiddhirbhavati | iha tu taṇḍulān vibhajeta iti
taṇḍulavibhāgaḥ śrūyate na dadhipayasoḥ | evaṃ ca taṇḍulānāmevāyaṃ sthaviṣṭhādibhirviśeṣaiḥ
viśiṣṭo vibhāgo’vagamyate na devatāyāḥ | sthūlamadhyamasūkṣmatvādibhiḥ taṇḍulā
vibhaktavyā iti pratīyate | [na]punardevatābhyo vibhajediti | tataśca pūrvadevatābhyo’na-
panītatvāt na śakyate taddhaviḥ devatāntareṇa yojayitumiti | karmāntara vedaṃ vidhīyate |
pūrvaṃ tu karmabhāvāt adhikārabhāvāt parityajanīyameva | dadhyādayastu laukikāḥ santaḥ
yadi karmāntare’pi vyāpriyante tadā na kaściddoṣaḥ |

yuktyantaraṃ cāha---caruśca dadhipayasossaṃyoge devatāvibhāge nopapadyate
 |
asyārthaḥ---ekendrādisaṃbandhitvena yadyapidaghnastaṇḍulānāṃ ca saṃyogaḥ tathāpi carutvaṃ
kathaṃ labhyate? tadapi na vidhīyate | vidhīyate cet tadā vihite karmaṇi guṇadvaya-
vidhānānupapatteḥ karmāntaravidhirevāpatati | na hi devatāntaraṃ carutvaṃ ca śakyate
vidhātumekena vākyena vihite karmaṇi |

nanu saṃyuktayoḥ dadhitaṇḍulayoḥ pāke sati, tathā payastaṇḍulayośca pāke sati
carutvamarthaprāptameva sat anūdyate(saḥ)saptamyarthenetyāśaṅkyāha---puroḍāśārtha---
tayā hi pākastatropadiṣṭaḥ | caroraśra(va)paṇāt
 | darśapūrṇamāsopayogi yacchra(va)
paṇaṃ vihitaṃ tat prathanottarakāle puroḍāśākṛtike dravye sampanne sati puroḍāśa-
saṃskārārthaṃ na taṇḍulānāṃ caroraśrapaṇāt | yadi tatra caruḥ śrūyate tadā taṇḍulānāṃ
pāko vidhī[yeta](yate) | tasmāt karmāntaravidhipakṣa evaitatsarvamupapadyata iti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

kathaṃ punaratra asmin pakṣe ātañcanābhyāsaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ | evamata
śruyate---“yadi bibhīyādabhimodeṣyatīti mahārātra eva havīṃṣi nirvapet phalīkṛtai-
staṇḍulaiḥ paryupāsīta ardhaṃ havirātañcanasya nidadhyāt, ardhaṃ na, yadyabhyudiyāt
tenātacya pracaret yadi na tena brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayet”
 iti | yaḥ khalu yajamānaḥ
āśaṅkate-pravṛttā’māvāsyā tatraiva kimatra caturdaśī uta pañcadaśīti | [sa tasyā](satyā)
meva rātrau vrīhīnnirupya avahatya, phalīkṛtya udīkṣamāṇa āsīta | sāyaṃdohana-
saṃpāditaṃ ca dadhi dvidhākṛtya ardhaṃ dadhi ekāntaritakariṣyamāṇasya kṛte anantaraṃ sāyaṃ
dohaṃ sampādya dadhyātañcanāya nidadhyāt | ardhaṃ caivameva sthāpayet | tatra yadyabhyudayaḥ
syādevameva sthāpitenārdhena dadhnā naimittikādhikāraṃ nirvartya nihitenārdhena dadhnā sāyaṃ

[p. 165]

dugdhe payasi ātañcanaṃ kṛtvā dadhyutpādya anyedyuḥ darśamabhinirvartayet yadi tannābhyu-
diyāt yadevaṃ nihita(camevaṃnita)mardhaṃ dadhi tenārdhena dadhnā darśameva kuryāt | ātañcanāya
nihitaṃ ca dadhi ātañcanasyānarthakatvāt ātañcanenāsyāpa[sa]raṇīyaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanena
pratipādayediti | tatra ca yadi karmāntaramidaṃ naimittikaṃ bhavet | tadā yattaddadhyasti
taddarśārthabheveti (nanu) [na] darśārthenāpareṇa dadhnā kṛtyamastīti ātañcaṃna na kriyeta
tatrātañcanābhyāsadarśanaṃ nopapadyate | yadā tvetat tadevāmāvāsyākarma(a)pravṛttaṃ tatra
tadā anenaiva dadhnā darśārthopārjitena kartavyamiti aparedyuḥ kvacitprāptasya1 darśasya
kṛte ātañcanena prayojanamastīti yukta ātañcanābhyāsaḥ | pariharati---

vācaniko bhaviṣyatīti
 | asyārthaḥ---yadyapi tad dadhyasti tathāpi
anyaddadhi ātacya pracaritavyamiti vācanikamevaṃ bhaviṣyatīti nyāyenaivāsmin

 |

hi janitaṃ dadhi darśe viniyuktam | ataścātañcanābhyāsadarśanaṃ nopapadyate iti
itarasminnāpādanapakṣe’pi vācanika eva
 | ayamarthaḥ---tenaiva dadhnā darśaḥ

 | āpādanapakṣe hi viniyogamātraṃ tasya dadhnaḥ

 | asyārthaḥ---nāyaṃ sthaviṣṭhādibhirviśeṣaiḥ taṇḍulānāmeva
1. vidhiprāptasyeti yuktaḥ pāthaḥ

[to’]prāptatvāt |

kuto byastataṇḍulānāṃ traividhyena vibhāgaprāptiḥ ?
 uttaraṃ---devatā-
sambandhavidhānataḥ
 | yadā sthaviṣṭhādīnāṃ bhedena devatātrayasambandhaḥ tadā arthā-

tacca havirmātra na taṇḍulaśabdena viśeṣṭuṃ śakyate | vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt |

gamyate | prakṛtānyeva ca dravyāṇi darśākhyena karmaṇā saṃbaddhāni devatābhyo viṣeśeṇā-
panītāni devatāntarairyuktānīti prakṛtakarmaṇi pratyabhijñānāt na karmāntaramavagamyate |
na ca kālābhāvenādhikārābhāve karmasvarūpābhāvaḥ | naimittikādhikāre tānyeva karmāṇi
codyante | tataśca tasmin kṛte nimittādhikāre nityādhikāra1nirvṛttyarthaṃ bhūyastānyeva

 |
puroḍāśārthatvātpākasya tadvidhau ca vākyabhedaprasaṅgaḥ | yadi prāptakarmānuvādena vidhiḥ
guṇavidhiḥ karmasvarūpāśritaḥ syāt, tadā’nekaguṇavidhānādvākyabhedaḥ syāt | yadā tvayaṃ
kālabhrameṇādhikārabhrāntyā pravṛttasyābhyudayasyādhikṛtasya vibhāganimittako devatābhyo
vibhāge sati tasyaiva yāgasya prayogaviśeṣaścodyate tathāsati na vākyabhedaḥ | kathamityāha
---sa evāyaṃ puroḍāśaḥ tāṃ tāmavasthāṃ nītaḥ
 iti | anuvādo’yam | asyārthaḥ-
yo’sau puroḍāśaścoditaḥ tasya puroḍāśasvarūpatayaiva prayogo’pi prāptaḥ | samprati abhyudaye

pāke’rthāccarutā | saptamyarthaśca sampadyata evetyanuvādoya’m “dadhani caruṃ”

carum”
 iti | yata eva ca prayogaprakāracodanayā’nuvā(do)dānupapatteḥ prakṛtakarmānu-
