 |

bhedābhedalakṣaṇe hi dvitīye’dhyāye yadyeṣā cintā kriyate tadā karmasvarūpā-
śritatvāt tatraiva guṇavidhiḥ syāt | tatrānekaguṇavidhānāt vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt
karmāntaramevedaṃ syāt | adhikāralakṣaṇe tu cintāyāmadhikṛtasya prayogaviśeṣaścodyata
iti puroḍāśa evāyaṃ prayujyata iti pākasyāpanītatvāt arthaprāptaiva carutā saptamyarthena
sahānūdyata iti vākyabhedābhāvādupapadyata evābhedaḥ | tenādhikāralakṣaṇa idaṃ cintyate |
tasmāt pañcaśarāvādibhyo vilakṣaṇamidaṃ naimittikamiti siddham | pañcaśarāvādibhyo-
havirārtyā karmaiva vinaṣṭamiti karmāntaraṃ codyate | iha kālāparādhe’pi haviṣo’vinaṣṭa-
tvāt karmaṇi avinaṣṭe prayogāntaramidaṃ naimitikaṃ codyate | tasyaivāyaṃ prayoga iti |
prakṛtadravyapratyabhijñānena idamavagamyata iti sustham | śeṣamuktam |

|| 2 || upāṃśuyāje’vacanādyathāprakṛti || 10 ||

amāvāsyāyāmapyupāṃśuyājo vidyata iti kṛtvācinteyam | kathaṃ
punaratra pūrvapakṣā’’śaṅkā yāvatā prayoga evedamuktaṃ tredhā taṇḍulān vibhajet
iti havirmātrasya vibhāga iti
 | tenopāṃśuyājahaviṣo’pi devatāpanaya iti |

uttaraṃ---pratisamādhānadarśanāt pūrvapakṣāśaṅkā
 | yathādadhipayaḥpuroḍā-
śānāmapanītāyāṃ pūrvadevatāyāṃ aparadevatāsambandhaḥ tatheha nāsti | na ca devatāṃ
vinā yāgo bhavati ityapanayaḥ eva(vaṃ)devatāyāḥ | tasmātpūrvapakṣī tāvatsamādhānama-
vibhāgaṃ manyate |

rāddhāntastu---vibhāgāviśeṣāt sarvatra vibhāgaḥ
 | yatra samādhānaṃ tatra
samādhiḥ | yatra tu nāsti samādhānaṃ tatra vibhāgānta eva naimittikaḥ prayogaḥ |

nanu ca devatāvibhāgānte yāgaḥ prayukto na bhavati | debatoddeśena hi
dravyatyāgo yāgaḥ | sa ca devatāyāmanuddiṣṭāyāṃ prayukto na bhavati
 |

pariharati---kathaṃ śakyate vaktuṃ na prayukta iti ? vācanikatvāda-
syārthasya
 | asyārthaḥ---ya evāsau yāgaścoditaḥ tasya devatāvibhāgātmaka eva

[p. 168]

prayogaḥ kartavya iti vacanenedamucyate | tyāga eva tu devatoddeśo yāgaḥ | sa ca
upādhivigame’pi nānupapannaḥ |

nanu yadyupādhirna pratīyate kathamavagamyate sa evāyaṃ yāga iti |
prakṛtasyaiva prayogavidhānāt puroḍāśa iva carvavasthe
 | yāgo’pi hi vibhāgānta
evāvasīyate prayoganiṣṭhatvādadhikāraśāstrāṇām | adhikāraśāstrāṇi tāvat prayoganiṣṭhāni
prayogameva codayanti | na prayojyaviṣayaśśeṣaḥ | tena yāgasyaivāyamīdṛśaḥ prayojyasya
prayogaśeṣo jñāyata iti na doṣaḥ | yathā puroḍāśa eva carubhūta iti | karmasvarūpa-
vidhau hyayaṃ doṣaḥ
 devatoddeśaṃ vinā yāgāsiddhilakṣaṇaḥ | nanu ca devatāpanaye sati
yākāciddevatā kathyata iti, atrāha---na ca sāmānyena devatākalpanā vacanāva-
gamyatvāt devatāyāḥ
 | etadeva kuta ityatrāha---yā yasya haviṣaḥ codyate sā
tasya devateti sthiteḥ | tena devatābhāvaṃ
 kalpayituṃ na śakyata iti | 1artha-
vibhāgānta eva prayogo vidhīyata iti |

|| 3 || nirupte syāt tatsaṃyogāt || 12 ||

niruptaśabdaśravaṇāt nirupta eva haviṣi udite naimittikamiti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ
 |

nanu ca ārtyadhikaraṇenaiva gatamidam
 | yathā ārtyadhikaraṇe ubhayatva-
viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitaṃ, kiṃ tarhiṃ havirmātrasyārtirnimittaṃ ihāpi havirmātrasya abhyudayo
nimittaṃ niruptagrahaṇamavivakṣitameveti pariharati---na tena gatamidamadhikaraṇaṃ pravṛtta-
śabdasyāśravaṇāt
 | na hi svarūpeṇa haviṣo’bhyudayaḥ saṃbhavati kiṃ tarhi ?
prabṛttasya | na cātra pravṛttaśabdaḥ śrūyate 1tena punaḥ śrutyavagatameva niruptatvaviśeṣaṇamastu
tasyaiva śrutatvāt |

rāddhāntamāha---satyaṃ pravṛttaśabdo na śrūyate tathāpi yasyābhyudi-
yāditi pravṛttyavagamāta na viśeṣaṇaviṣayameva vyavasthāpayituṃ kṣamam
 |
yasyābhyudiyādityukte pravṛttatvaṃ haviṣo’vagamyate | pravṛttaṃ hyabhyudayo bhavati | tenābhyudi-
1. artha vibhāgataḥ- mā
2. kena-mā

[p. 169]

yādityasminneva pade pravṛttāvagamāt niruptaviśeṣaṇamaparanaimittikaṃ na viśeṣamava-
sthāpayitumarhati yasyābhyudiyāditi---[bhyupa]gamāditi | tasmāt pravṛttaśabde śrute
cāśrute ca na kaścidviśeṣaḥ | darśanamuktam
 |

|| 4 || anirupte’bhyudite prākṛtībhyo nirvapedityāśmarathyaḥ
taṇḍulabhūteṣvapanayāt || 16 ||

nirupte’nirupteca abhyudite prākṛtībhyo devatābhyaḥ agnyādibhyo
nirvāpa iti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate
 taṇḍulabhūtānāmeva devatāpanayaśravaṇāt | nanu
ca pravṛttamātra havirabhyuditaṃ nimittamityuktam | tena pravṛttamātrasya devatā-
panayāt kathaṃ prākṛtībhyo nirvāpa iti
 | pariharati---satyamuktam
 |

nanu naimittikaṃ tu taṇḍulameteṣviti vadāmaḥ
 yadyapi pravṛttamātraṃ havirnimitaṃ
tathāpi devatāvibhāgātmakaṃ naimittikaṃ taṇḍulaśabdaśravaṇāt | taṇḍulāvasthāyāmeveti
prāga[na]panītatvāddevatāyāḥ prākṛtībhya eva nirvāpaḥ |

atrocyate---naimittike’pi taṇḍulā atantramityuktam | vibhāga-
mātravidhānāt
 | devatābhyo havirvibhajedityukte havirmātrasya vibhāgo’vagamyate
tena taṇḍulagrahaṇamavivakṣitamityuktam | abhyuditaṃ tu haviḥ vibhajanīyamiti śāstrārthaḥ |
tasmāt naimittikebhyo nirvāpa iti rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 5 || vinirupte na muṣṭīnāmapanayastadguṇatvāt || 17 ||

yadavasthasyaiva haviṣo’bhyudayaḥ tadavasthayaiva devatāpa(nna)nayo’stu
tatpūrvako devatāntarebhya eva nirvāpaḥ
 |

nanvevaṃ avaghāto’pi tarhi kimiti dadhipayasā yuktānāṃ na bhavati |
ucyate | kṣodiṣṭhādibhedabhinnānāṃ dadhyādisambandhādavaghātottarakālameva
dadhipayassaṃsargaṃ kṛtvā pākaḥkartavya iti | vinirupte sāminirupte prākṛtībhya
eva nirvāpaḥ pravṛttaḥ | pariśiṣṭamapi muṣṭayantaraṃ prākṛtībhya eva nirvaptavyam

nirvāpe prākṛtīnāmevābhipravṛttatvāt | na ca padārthāntaraṃ muṣṭaya iti pañcame uktam |
evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate-apanayanamavagamyate
 durdarśassa(ma/?/)bhavati | tasmādvaikṛtīnāṃ
apanaye kṛte sati āpāditahavissambandhānāmuddeśenaiva nirvāpaḥ kartavya iti prāpte
rāddhānto’bhidhīyate---

[p. 170]

tūṣṇīṃ nirvāpaḥ kartavyaḥ apanayāvagamāt ekatvācca nirvāpasya (na
niruptapayassambhavaḥ) |
 asyārthaḥ - pūrvadevata muddeṣṭuṃ na śakyate | apanītatvāt |
na devatāntaramapi yojayitu śakyate, caturmuṣṭinirvāpasyaikapadārthātmakatvāt | na ca
muṣṭayantaraṃ pṛthak‌padārthaḥ | nacaikasmin padārthe devatādbayaṃ sambhavati, anyonyavirodhāt |
na ca devatāvayavasya devatāsambandhaḥ ityanuddeśā parisamāptiḥ |

|| 6 || sānnāyyasaṃyogānnāsannayataḥ syāt || 21 ||

nanu carvavasthasyāvidhānāt naivātra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ sambhavati
 | yadi sānnā-
yyādhikaraṇe carvavasthā vihitā syāt tadā sānnāyyābhāvena na syāt asānnāyyayājino
naimittikam | na carvavasthā vihitetyuktam |

samādhate-astyatra kācidāśaṅkā | nahyasati śṛte dadhani vā carvavasthā
puroḍāśasya sambhavati iti manvānaḥ sānnāyyayājina eva naimittikaṃ
sambhavatīti pūrvapakṣayati
 | anuvādabhūtāpi carvavasthā sānnāyyayājina eva
sambhavatīti tasyaiva naimittikamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntastu---udakenāpi carvavasthāsaṃbhavāt nimittasya cāviśeṣa-
śravaṇāt naimittikasya ca devatāsambandhamātraparatvādadhikaraṇārtho na
vivakṣitaḥ iti
 |

yadyapi vivakṣitā carvavasthā, tathāpi udakenāpyasau sampadyata eveti aviśeṣeṇa
śrutaṃ naimittikaṃ na śakyate viśeṣeṇa sānnāyyayājinyavasthāpayitum | paramārthatastu
devatāntarasambandhamātraṃ naimittikam | adhikaraṇārthastvarthaprāpta iti | avivakṣitārthatvāt
carvarthasya yatrānuvādassambhavati tatrāsau bhaviṣyati | na punastabdalena vidhireva saṅkoca-
yituṃ nyāyya iti asannayato’pi naimittikamityuktam |

|| 7 || sāmyutthāne viśvajit krīte vibhāgasaṃyogāt || 25 ||

nanu cāsannayato’ṣyuktameva | anantaraṃ tenaiva akrītarājakasyāpīti
labhyate kā atra abhyadhikāśaṅkā yā nivartyate
 | yathā pūrvasmin adhikaraṇe
’viśeṣeṇa nimittaśravaṇāt asannayato’pi naimittikamityuktam | tathā’trāpi sāmyu-
tthānasyāviśeṣeṇa śravaṇāt akrītarājakasyāpi naimittikena bhavitavyamiti labdhameva |
kimityadhikaraṇāntarāmbhaḥ |

[p. 171]

samādhatte---astyatrābhyadhikāśaṅkā-tatra hi taṇḍulaśravaṇāt adhi-
karaṇārthasyāvivakṣitatvāt yuktamasannayato’pīti | iha punaḥ akrītarājako
naiva śrūyate ‘somamapabhajya’
 iti ca krītarājakāvagamāt vibhāgasya cānya-
trānupapatteḥ naimittikānurodhena nimittasaṅkocaṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate---yathā
agnī ādadhīta iti |
 asyārthaḥ---taṇḍulaśabdaśravaṇāt auṣadhe’pi naimittikāva-
gamāt asannayato’pi cauṣadhasambhavāt, adhikaraṇārthasya vākyabhedaprasaṅgenāvivakṣita-
tvāt, arthaprāptatvāccāsannayato’pi naimittikamiti yuktam | iha punarakrītarājakasya
śravaṇameva nāsti | pratyuta naimittike somamapabhajya viśvajitā yajeteti śrūyate | tena
somavibhāgaviśiṣṭasya naimittikasya vidhānāt krītarājakasyaiva tathābhūtanaimittikānuṣṭhāna-
sambhavāt | yathā ca tatrodakenāpi carvavasthā asānnāyyayājino’pi śakyate vaktuṃ
tathā iha nākrītarājakena somavibhāgaśśakyata iti viśiṣṭanaimittikānusāreṇa krīte
rājani sāmyutthānaṃ nimittamiti kalpyate | yathā---yasyobhāvagnī anugatau
ityatra |

atrocyate | satyaṃ, yuktamādhāne 1ekopāyatvena avagamāt ādhā-
nasya | somamapabhajyeti iha sarvadravyavibhāge prāpte lakṣaṇayā nāvagamyata
iti na nimittasaṅkocāyoktam |

asyārthaḥ - yadi naimittikamevedaṃ somavibhāgaviśiṣṭaṃ syāt kiṃ yatra bahūnāṃ
sahapravṛttānāṃ madhye ekīkṛtadhanānāṃ sāmyutthāne prāpnotyevāyaṃ dravyavibhāgaḥ | sa ca
lakṣaṇayā somamapabhajyetyanūdyate | yo hi krītarājakastasyāpi somavibhāgaḥ prāptatvāt
na vidheya eva kintvanūdyata iti siddhe’nuvāde lakṣaṇāpi na doṣaḥ | tasmāt pravṛttā-
nāmutthāne viśvajiditi krītarājakānāṃ ceti yuktam |

|| 8 || dīkṣāparimāṇe yathākāmyaviśeṣāt || 27 ||

jyotiṣṭome bahūni dīkṣāparimāṇāni śrūyante | tatra saṃśayaḥ | kiṃ
tatra sarvaiḥ parimāṇaiḥ adhikriyante, uta ekenaiveti |
 anenādhikaraṇasyā-
dhyāyasaṅgatimāha | yathāviniyogamadhikārāvagamāt sarvairiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

1. ekotpatyatvenānava-mā

[p. 172]

sarveṣāṃ parimāṇānāṃ viniyuktatvāt sarvairevādhikāraḥ tadā dvādaśarātrīrdīkṣito bhṛrti
vanvītetyayaṃ 1[pākṣiko]dvādaśadīkṣa eva draṣṭavyaḥ |

rāddhāntamāha---dvādaśarātrīrdīkṣito bhṛtiṃ vanvīteti nityaśravaṇāt na
pakṣāntare upapadyata iti |
 dvādaśaparimāṇa evādhikāraḥ dvādaśarātrīrdīkṣitasya
bhṛtivananaṃ nityavadadhikāraśravaṇāt | parimāṇāntarapakṣe ca tadanupapatteḥ dvādaśapari-
māṇenaivādhikāraḥ | parimāṇāntarāṇi tu vikṛtāvutkṛṣyante |

kathaṃ punaradhikāravaśāt viniyogotkarṣaḥ
 yāvatā viniyogavaśenaiva tu
kimityadhikāro na varṇyata iti bhāvaḥ |

pariharati---adhikāraparyantatvādviniyogaśāstrāṇāmiti yuktam |

pradhānatvādadhikārasya tadanurūpo viniyogī nyāyya iti | tiryagadhikaraṇe tu adhi-
kārāvirodhāt yathāviniyogamadhikāravyavastheti darśitam |

|| 9 || paurṇamāsyāmaniyamo’viśeṣāt || 30 ||

dīkṣādhikāre paurṇamāsīśravaṇe saṃśayaḥ---kimaviśeṣeṇa yā kācit
paurṇamāsī grāhyā uta kācit viśiṣṭaiva caitrī māghī veti |

tatra pūrvapakṣavādī aviśeṣaṃ manyate, vākyaśeṣasāmarthyayorvirodhāt |

vākyaśeṣe caitrīsaṅkīrtanāt caitrīpaurṇamāsīti pratibhāti | teṣāmekāṣṭakāyāṃ krayassampadyata
ityasya sāmarthyāt mādhīti | tena virodhāt anayoḥ parasparavyāghātenāsādhakatvāt
aniyama eva | yā kācitpaurṇamāsī grāhyā |

aparastvāha---na hi sāmānyajñānaṃ vākyaśeṣaṃ vihantumarhati tena
caitryeva grāhyā |
 yadyapi paurṇamāsīśabdāt sāmānyajñānaṃ tathāpi vākyaśeṣāt
viśeṣo’vagamyate | nanu liṅgena krayasāmarthyena tadviruddhamityuktam | liṅgaṃ tarhi
niyāmakaṃ bhavatu yena tadapodyate |

aniyamavādyāha---etadeva na vijānīmaḥ anayoḥ vākyaśeṣasāmarthyayoḥ
kiṃ kena bādhyata iti |
 sundopasundanyāyena parasparamanayoḥ parāhatayoḥ
aniyama eva |

1. yaḥpādaḥ - mā

[p. 173]

rāddhāntamāha---ucyate | bāḍhaṃ jñāyate liṅgena vākyaśeṣo bādhyate |

sāmarthyaṃ hi adhikāre’bhyarhitam | vinā ca sāmarthyenādhikārābhāvāt | tena sāmarthyasya
valīyastvāt vākyaśeṣo’podyate | vākyaśeṣasya guṇavādenāpyupapatteḥ |

nanu ca mādhyāṃ kāryasambandhinyāṃ caitryāḥ stutiranupapannā | tatrāha---
vidheyāntarastutyarthatayaivāyamupanyāso jartilavat |
 yathā payovidhau jartilastutiḥ
tathā māghīvidhau caitrīstutiḥ | caitryeva sādhvī māghīṃ tvapekṣya nāpi jaghanyeti
tasmāt sāmarthyenaiva viśeṣagrahaṇam |

|| 10 || dīkṣākālasya śiṣṭatvādatikrame niyatānāmanu-
tkarṣaḥ prāptakālatvāt || 38 ||

iha pākādīnāṃ yāvaddīkṣākālaṃ paryudāsaṃ manvānaḥ pūrvapakṣavādī
anutkarṣaṃ manyate | dīkṣito na dadāti ityanena
 yāvaddīkṣā puruṣasya śāstrataḥ
tāvat paryudāsaḥ pākādīnām śāstrataśca niyatakālaiva dīkṣā | tena 1dīkṣīnmoca-
nasya 2utkarṣepi pākādayaḥ kartavyā eveti |

rāddhāntastu---nāyaṃ kālaparyudāsateti3 | yadi kālaṃ payudasyeta
bhaveyuḥ pākādayaḥ na tvayaṃ kālaparyudāsaḥ |
 dīkṣitasya hi atrānadhikāraḥ |
dīkṣitatvaṃ cānuvartata eva [ā] avamṛthāt | tasmāt yāvat dīkṣitaḥ tāvat paryudāsa
eveti yuktaḥ pākādīnām |

|| 11 || tatra pratihomo na vidyate yathā pūrveṣām || 40 ||

kālotkarṣe kiṃ pratihomāḥ kartavyā neti cintyate
 ityanenākṣeptumanu-
bhāṣate |

ākṣipati---kathaṃ punaratra pratihomāśaṅkā ? dīkṣitaḥ paryudasta ityuktam |

asyārthaḥ---kartavyasya akaraṇe pratihomakriyā sambhavati | na ca dīkṣitasyādhikāraḥ
pākādiṣvasti | ato’kartavyānāmevākṛtatvāt na pratihomāśaṅkā yuktā | samādhatte---
prāk‌pramādānniyataṃ nityaśāstraṃ manyate’
pramāda iva | asyārthaḥ-nityaśāstrāṇi
1. dīkṣāmo - mā 
2. utkarṣopi - mā
3. paryudasyeteti - mā

[p. 174]

homādiviṣayāṇi niyatāni prāk‌pramādāt | yāvat pramādo na nipatati | avabhṛtho-
tkarṣahetuḥ dīkṣitatvānuvṛttikāraṇam | tāvatpuruṣasya homādayaḥ kartavyā eva | yathā-
tataśca pramāde’pi āpatite dīkṣitena ye homādayo na kṛtāḥ te kartavyā eva santo na
kṛtāḥsyuḥ | tasmāt pratihomāḥ kartavyā iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntastu---satyamapramāde niyataḥ kālaḥ | anadhikārāttu dīkṣita-
syākaraṇameva homānām | adhikāradvāratvānniyamasya |
 asyārthaḥ---yasya
pramādo nāsti tasya homānāṃ niyataḥkālaḥ | yasya punaḥ pramādo nipatati tasya
dīkṣitatvenānadhikārāt pratihomānāmakaraṇameva adhikāradvāratvānniyamasya | 1niyamo
hi homānāṃ adhikārivaśenaiva | yasya hi jīvanavato nityamadhikāraḥ tasya
nityamanuṣṭhānam | na cādhikāraḥ prāgeva bhavati |
 kintu aharaharadhikāro-
’pyāvartate | dīkṣitasya adhikāraḥ paryudasta iti adhikārābhāvānna homānāṃ karta-
vyateti kimu |

|| 12 || pratiṣedhāccordhvamavabhṛthādiṣṭeḥ || 42 ||

dīkṣonmocanottarakālaṃ pramādādiṣṭyutkarṣe sati aparaṃ dīkṣitasya
pratihomaḥ kartavya iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
 asyārthaḥ---‘dīkṣito na dadāti na juhoti
na pacati’
 iti dīkṣitasya paryudāsaḥ kṛtaḥ | kṛtāvabhṛthaśca unmuktadīkṣo na
dīkṣitaḥ, yadyapi punarādheyasammiterṣṭina kṛtā | tenāparyudastatvāt pramādādiṣṭyutkarṣe’pi
homādiṣvadhikārāt ye homā na kṛtāḥ teṣāṃ pratihomāḥ kartavyā iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

etayā punarādheyasasmitayeṣṭyeti kramamātraṃ vacanam | tena kramānu-
grahāyodavasānīyāṃ kṛtvaiva pratihomāḥ kariṣyanta iti manyate |

rāddhāntastu satyaṃ nāsti paryudāsaḥ| adhikāro’pi naivāsti |
adhikārāntaraśravaṇāt punarādheyasammitayeṣṭyeti |
 asyārthaḥ---jīvanamiva
kṛtodavasānīyatvamapi adhikāriviśeṣaṇānupraveśīti akṛtodavasānīyasyānadhikāraḥ |
na tatrānadhikṛtasya niyamo’stīti na kartavyāḥ | samānakartṛkatā hi ktvāpratyayasyārthaḥ |
na ca paraspara[ā]sambandhayoragnihotrodavasānīyayoḥ samānakartṛkatā sambhavati | tatra yadi
kṛtodavasānīyaḥ puruṣaḥ agnihotre’dhikārī bhavati tadā’dhiṣṭhānaikyenāpi tāvat
samānakartṛkatā nātīva dūrojjhitā bhavati | kramaparatve sā atyantabādhitā syāt |

[p. 175]

|| 13 || pratihomaścet sāyamagnihotraprabhṛtīni hūyeran || 43 ||

ekasmin kāle paryavasthitānāṃ pratihomānāṃ kramaniyamapramāṇā-
bhāvāt aniyama iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntastu yataḥpramādaḥ tataḥ prabhṛtyeva
kartavyā iti |
 atipannapratisamādhānamatipannakrameṇaiva kartavyamiti bhāvaḥ |

1atra keṣāṃ citsāyaṃ, ṣoḍaśini prātariti vyavasthāpradarśanārthaṃ ca ṣoḍaśinaḥ
pṛthak grahaṇam |

|| 15 || prāyaścittamadhikāre sarvatra doṣasāmānyāt || 45 ||

‘bhinne juhoti’
 ‘skanne juhoti’
 iti śrūyate | tatra sandehaḥ-
kiṃ nimittaparyanta evāyamadhikāraḥ uta kāryaparyanta iti |
 anena bhāṣya-
kārīyasaṃśayo vyākhyātaḥ | yadi hi bhedanavataḥ, svatantro’yamadhikāraḥ syāt |
atha tu kāryaparyantaḥ, prakṛtakratūpakāraparyanto’yamadhikāraḥ tadā darśapūrṇamāsayoreva
bhedane sati homaḥ tadaṅgabhūtaḥ kartavya iti |

asādhyatvādadhikāriviśeṣaṇasya nimittaparyanta evāyamadhikāra iti
pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |

yatra hi sādhyamadhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ bhavati tatra hi kāryaparyanto’dhikāro bhavati |
atra tu na sādhyamadhikāriviśeṣaṇamiti na kāryaparyantatā yuktā | sā(tu)caivamanupakāra-
[ka]syāṅgasyānupapatteḥ sarvatra jyotiṣṭomādiṣvapi bhedanenaiva tatrāhitāgnerhomā bhavanti |

nimittāviśeṣāt anāhitāgneḥ kimiti na bhavati ?
 uttaraṃ---
homavidhānāt | na hyāhavanīye homaḥ saṃbhavatīti |
 naimittikānurodhena nimitta-
saṅkocaḥ ādhānavat | nātra tirohitamiva kiñcidasti |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---kāryaparyanta evāyamadhikāraḥ |
 kutaḥ ? darśa-
pūrṇamāsādhikārasyaiva prakṛtatvāt | niyogaikyāvagatirna śakyate’pahantum |

1. ataḥ mā

[p. 176]

asyārthaḥ-bhinne juhoti iti bhedanaṃ nimittatayā śrūyate | na sākṣādadhikāriviśeṣaṇa-
tayā | nimittabhāvastvadhikāraikanibandhane’nuṣṭhāne’nyathānupapanna iti arthādadhikāri-
viśeṣaṇatā kalpanīyā | darśapūrṇamāsādiniyogaśca sākṣācchrutādhikāraḥ | tatsannidhau
yanniradhikāramavagamyate tenāpi sa evānvitaḥ upanīyata iti ekaniyogatā tāvadava-
gamyate | kathaṃ ca sā bhavati ? yadi karaṇopakāraṃ nirvartayati (iti) nānyathā |
tena kāryaparyantatvāddarśapūrṇamāsāṅgamevedam |

nanu caivaṃ sati adhikāravirodhaḥ prāpnoti yasmādanyadevādhikāra-
nimittam |
 adhikārasya nimittamadhikāranimittaṃ adhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ bhedanam
anyacca kāryaṃ darśaṃpūrṇamāsopakāralakṣaṇam | svargādikaṃ adhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ caikameva |
ucyate | na virodhaḥ | adhikāro hi adhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ niyojyatāmā-
pādayati | niyuktasya ca kāryamavagamyamānaṃ nādhikāraṃ niruṇaddhi |
tasyaiva tadavagamāt |
 yadi kāryaṃ kalpyamānamadhikāriviśeṣaṇameva nirundhyāt tato
virodhāt na kāryamāśrīyate | avirodhe tu kāryakalpanā nānupapannā | tasyaivādhikṛtasya
kāryāvagamāt nādrīyetetyatrāha---jīvane punaḥ kāryāntarānavagamāt nimitta-
paryantataiva yuktā |

nanu kimiti 1nimittaṃ kāryamavekṣate | jīvane’pi kimiti na kalpyate |
atha tu nāpekṣate kathaṃ tarhi yatra kāryaṃ svīkriyata ityanenābhiprāyeṇa pṛcchati---
atha kasmānna parikalpyate kāryaṃ ? ucyate | na niyogaḥ kalpanāṃ viṣahate |
niyogasiddhayaiva caritārthatvāt |
 jīvanādhikāre’pi (vi)niyogaḥ kāryabhūtaḥ
pratīyate | tenaiva caritārthatvāt na paraṃ kāryaṃ kalpyate | pratīyamānaṃ(na)punarnā-
panhotuṃ śakyate svārthaviśeṣāt
 yathā hi---niyogo’tra sādhyabhūtaḥ pratīyate tathā
karaṇopakāro’pi | tena pratīyamānasyāpahnavo’nupayuktaḥ | yasmāt kāryaparyanto’ya-
madhikāraḥ tasmāt darśapūrṇamāsādhikṛtasyaiva bhedanavato’dhikāraḥ, nāgnihotre
jyotiṣṭome vā | adhikṛtasyopadeśātideśayoḥ pramāṇayorabhāvāt |

|| 16 || vyāpannasyāpsu gatau yadabhojyamāryāṇāṃ tatpratīyeta || 48 ||

1. nimittakārya

[p. 177]

adhikaraṇamadhyāyasaṅgatiṃ darśayan kasya vyāpattyeti na vidma iti vadannava-
tārayati---vyāpattimato’psu praharaṇaṃ śrūyate---‘vyāpannamapsu praharet’

 iti |
vyāpattimataḥ puruṣasyādhikṛtasyāpsu praharaṇaṃ śrūyate | na jñāyate kasya vyāpatti-
mata iti |
 kiṃ tat vyāpannaṃ yasya vyāpattyā vyāpattimān puruṣa iti na jñāyate |
yadi vyāpattiradhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ kathaṃ tarhi vyāpannasyaiva praharaṇam | ekamidaṃ
vyāpannapadaṃ nādhikāriṇaṃ (karma)viśeṣṭuṃ karma ca 1samarpayitumalamityarthaḥ |

uttaraṃ-tatkathamityapekṣāyāmadhikārāvagamāt
 | asyārthaḥ-karmasamarpaṇārtha-
meva vyāpannapadamāmnātam | arthāt tasyādhikāriviśeṣaṇatvam | kena prakāreṇa ?
ucyate---tadvayāpannasyāpsu praharaṇaṃ kathaṃ sambhavati | yadi vyāpattimaddravya evādhikārī
bhavati evamarthādadhikārikalpanāyāṃ na doṣaḥ | atra ca vyāpannaśabdena kiṃ grahītavya-
mityajñānameva pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ|

rāddhāntastu---kiṃ taditi viśeṣānavagamāt yāvatkāryakāraṇatayā
kiñciddūṣitam |
 etadeva vispaṣṭayati---śiṣṭānāṃ kāryaṃ pratyayogyaṃ kṛta-
mityartho vyāpannaśabdasya |
 yata evaṃ vyāpaṃnna atastadapekṣo’yamadhikāraḥ |
śiṣṭānāṃ kāryaṃ prati ayogīkṛtaṃ dravyaṃ yasya tasya vyāpannapraharaṇamiti siddham |

|| 17 || vibhāgaśruteḥ prāyaścitaṃ yaugapadye na vidyate || 49 ||

apacchede saṃśayaḥ-kimekaikenādhikriyate uta dvābhyāmapīti |
 anena
bhāṣyakārīyasya saṃśayasya kāraṇamupanyastam | yadyekaikenāpacchedenādhikriyate tadā
dvayoryugapat apacchede prāyaścittaṃ na kartavyam | 2ataḥ dvābhyāmadhikriyate |
apacchedaśabdena cātra vibhāgaphalā kriyā’bhipretā |

saṃśayamākṣipati---nanu ca dūyoḥ kathaṃ saṃbhavaḥ?
 dvayoḥ apaccheda kriyayoḥ
kathaṃ sambhavaḥ | ekaikevetyarthaḥ | samādhatte---etadevātra vicāryate kiṃ yugapat-

1. samāpayituṃ
2. atha-mā

[p. 178]

kāle ekaivāpacchedakriyā puruṣadvayasamavāyinī bhavati | tataśva tasyā ekena vyapadeṣṭu-
maśakyatvāt anyatarasya vyapadiṣṭe cāpacchede prāyaścittavidhānānna bhavitavyaṃ
prāyaścittena uta apacchedakriye nirapekṣayoreva puruṣayoḥ pratyekaṃ parisamāpte |

yadi paramekakālamātravartinyau tataścobhābhyāṃ vyapadiṣṭāvapacchedau yugapat dvausta
iti bhavitavyameva prāyaściteneti |

tatra pūrvapakṣavādī kālaikyādekamevāpacchedaṃ manyate |
 kālaikyanibandha-
no’yaṃ bhramaḥ ekaiveyamapacchedakriyā puruṣadvayasamavāyinīti manyate | ekadravyamaguṇaṃ
karmeti ca prakriyāmātraṃ padārthavidāmityavalopaḥ, anyatareṇa puruṣeṇa vyapadeṣṭumaśa-
kyatvāt ubhayādhiṣṭhānamekakartṛtvam | tasmāt dvābhyāmevāyaṃ vyapadiśyate |
anyatareṇa puruṣeṇa vyapadiśyamāno’pacchedaḥ adhikāraheturityuktam | ato na
yaugapadye’dhikāraḥ ekayaiva kriyayā’numitayā phalopapatteḥ na kriyāntarānumāne kāraṇa-
mastīti gūḍho’bhiprāyaḥ |

atrocyate---na dvābhyāṃ vyapadeṣṭuṃ śakyate’pacchedaḥ dvayadhiṣṭhānā
apacchedakriyā ekā na bhavati, itaranirapekṣatvāt kartṛbhāvasya |
 tasmādi-
taretarayuktayoḥ kartṛtvamiha na bhavati | kartṛtvābhiprāyeṇa nirapekṣatokteti siddhāntyabhi-
prāyamabuddhvā codayati---kathaṃ punarapacchedo na dvitīyamapekṣate |
 apādānabhūta-
dvitīyānapekṣā’pacchedakriyaiva nopapadyata iti bhāvaḥ |

uttaraṃ---apekṣata eva dvitīyamapaccheda iti sanmātratayā’pādānatvena |
na tu vyapadeśyasya |
 tathā na kartṛtayā’pekṣate | vyapadeśaścātra kartṛtayā’pekṣate |
vyapadeśaścātra kartṛbhāveneti | vyapadeśyavyadeśatāniṣedhena kartṛtvaṃ niṣedhati |

kathaṃ punaḥ pratyekamubhayasaṃpādye dve kriye kathyete ? ucyate | ekaikā-
pacchedadaśāyāṃ yādṛśamekaikagatatatphalaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tathābhūtameva dvayorapi avagamyata
iti dvayorapi kriyānumānamaviruddham |
 ataḥ samarthitamidaṃ bhagavataḥ kāśyapasya
sūtraṃ “ekadravyamaguṇaṃ saṃyogāvibhāgeṣvanapekṣakāraṇaṃ karma”
 iti | (1-1-17)
karmalakṣaṇam | tasmāt yugapajjñāpitāyāmapi siddhamekaikasyādhikārahetutvaṃ tattaddhaviḥ
prati |

|| 18 || tatra vipratiṣedhādvikalpaḥ syāt || 51 ||

[p. 179]

idamidānīṃ cinyate | kiṃ naimittikayorvikalpaḥ uta samuccaya iti |
yadā sthitaṃ yaugapadye(na) nimittadvayamastīti tadā naimittikadvaye cintopapadyate kiṃ
samuccayaḥ uta vikalpa iti | tatra virodhāt vikalpa iti vadāmaḥ |
sarvasvādākṣiṇyayoḥ virodhāt yugapadupasaṃhartumaśakyatvāt vikalpa eva nyāpya iti
rāddhāntaḥ | atha kasmāt tadanurodhena naimittikāṅgadvayānurodhena nāvṛttiḥ
kalpyate pradhānasyaiva, tena dvayamapyupasaṃhariṣyate |

pariharati---nāṅgānurodhena |
 atha kasmāt dvirna pradhānasyāvṛrttiryuktā ? |
pradhānaṃ hi aṅgasya prayojanaṃ nāṅgaṃ pradhānasya | tenāṅgānurodhena pradhānasyāvṛttirnopapadyate |
aṅgatā caivaṃ vidhānāt naimittikānāmuktā vācanikyāṃ punaḥ tato dvitīyaṃ naimittikaṃ
bhaviṣyatīti cet | neti [brūmaḥ nā] pacchede(ḥ) [prāyaściyoḥ] samuccayaḥ, prathama-
prayogāṅgatvena(ā) nirjñānāt | yadeva hi prathamaprayogāṅgatayā vijñātaṃ tadeva punaḥ
ta(tra)tra kartavyam, na prathamaprayoge dvayoraṅgatā, kintvekasyaiva | tenāṅgatvād‌dvitīyasya
punasta(āva)[trāpya]pyananupraveśaḥ |

nanu yathā vrīhiyavayordvayorapi viniyogādaṅgatā tathehāpi dvayoraṅgatā kimiti
na bhavati iti atrāha---prayoge’pyadhikāradvārā viniyujyate na karmaṇītyukta-
mabhyudayeṣṭyadhikaraṇe |

yatpunaḥ nimittasambandhenādhikāriṇamāśritya vidhīyate tatprayogāṅgatayaiva
vidhīyate | na ca prayoge dvayoranupraveśasaṃbhavaḥ | karmaṇi tu viruddhānāmapi sambhavo
bhavatyeva prayogabhedāśrayatvena | tasmādvikalpaḥ | ekaikasya viniyogādekāṅgateti |
so’yaṃ śāstravikalpaḥ |

|| 19 || paurvāparye pūrvadaurbalyaṃ prakṛtivat || 54 ||

keyamatra bādhacintā |
 anenāsaṅgatiruktā | ayuktā ca sā

pradarśita eva |

prayogāya bhavati na karmajñānāya |
 jñāte hi karmaṇyadhikriyate | tenādhikāra-
dbārakamidaṃ prayogāntaraṃ vidhīyate ityatraiva cintitatam |

2. raṇaṃ
3. aprā-mā

[p. 181]

prayogaḥ paraṃ nivartate | tataścottara eva prayogaḥ pūrvasya bādhako yuktaḥ | aparaḥprayogaḥ
pūrvamabādhitvā (anena) na pratyetu śakyate na punaḥ pūrvaḥ paramabādhitvaiva na pratīyate |
ato’nupajātavirodhitaivātra prayoge bādhyatve hetuḥ upajātavirodhitā bādhakatve |

nanu pūrvādhikāre bhūte uttarādhikāraśāstrameva[na]pravartate ityatrāha---pratiprayo-
gāvadhāraṇīyaṃ
 cādhikāraśastramityuktam | ataḥ kālato’pi yatrāvadhāraṇaṃ daśame
tatropajātavirodhitaiva bādhakatve heturiti darśitam |

yadyupajātavirodhitayā’yaṃ bādhaḥ tadā dāśamika evāyaṃ bādha iti
punaratrāsaṅgatiḥ
 | pariharati-satyaṃ hetuḥ na punardvārataḥ
 | upajātavirodhitā yo’yaṃ
bādhe hetuḥ sā tulye tu dāśamika eva syāt dvārantu(stu)bhinnam | tatra hi prayojyāva-
dhṛtiḥ dvāre hetuḥ | iha na prayogāvadhṛtiriti viśeṣaḥ | prayojyāvadhāraṇaṃ prayojye’pi
tatra kṛtakāryatayā prayojyāntaraṃ bādhate | prayogaḥ prayogāntaraṃ aśakteḥ, adhikārā-
śrayā ca codanā prayogaviśeṣaḥ | codanā karmasvarūpacodaneti pradarśitam | tene-
haivāyaṃ bādhaścintanīya iti sarvamupapannam |

evāyam | yathā daśame’syāntarbhāvo na bhavati tathā’nantaramevoktamiti |

|| 20 || yadyudgātā jaghanyaḥ syāt punaryajñe sarvavedasaṃ dadyāt
yathetarasmin || 55 ||

pūrvaṃ pratiharturapacchede sarvavedasadakṣiṇe prayoge prāpte paścādudgāturapacchedaḥ

naimittikaṃ; punastataḥ pūrvavaddakṣiṇādānaṃ ca
 | anena bhāṣyoktaviṣayaḥ spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ |

tatra saṃśayaḥ | tatra taddadyāt yatpūrvasmin dāsyan syāditi vacana-
balāt kiṃ pratiharturapacchedanimittakaṃ sarvavedasaṃ dadyāt uta prakṛtameva dvādaśa-
śatamiti
 | anenāpi saṃśayaḥ spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ |

tatra pūrvapakṣavādī kratunimittakaṃ svābhāvikaṃ dāsyannityucyata iti
manvāno dvādaśaśataṃ dadyādityuktavān
 | asyārthaḥ-yat pūrvasmin prayoge

[p. 182]

deyatvena prāptaṃ taddeyamityukte yatsvābhāvikaṃ deyaṃ tadeva pratīyate nirapekṣatvāt |
na naimittikaṃ nimittasāpekṣatvāt | sāpekṣanirapekṣayorhi nirapekṣe pratītiḥ śīghratareti
svābhāvikadvādaśaśatameva punastato dātavyam |

rāddhāntastu prayogābhiprāyaṃ dāsyannityucyate, na karmābhiprāyam
asyārthaḥ-1atra pūrvasminnityanena pūrvaśavdena na karmocyate ekatvāt karmaṇaḥ pūrvā-
paravyapadeśābhāvāt | kintu prayoga ucyate | tenāyamarthaḥ-yatpūrvasmin prayoge
dātavyaṃ taddadyāditi | pūrvaprayoge ca prakṛtikarturapacchedanimittakaṃ sarvavedasameva teno-
ttarasyāṃ kratau deyam
 |

nanu ca pūrvadānābhiprāyametat, tatra dvādaśaśatamapyasti | yatkila
pūrvadāne deyaṃ tadabhipretyedamucyate yatpūrvasmin dāsyan syāt taddadyāt
iti
 | tatra pūrvaṃ deyaṃ dvādaśaśatamapyasti | pūrvaṃprayoge hi viniyogato dvādaśaśata-
mapyasti sarvavedasaṃ ca | ataḥ kathaṃ sarvavedasasyaiva dakṣiṇātvam |

pariharati---yadyapi dvayamasti tathāpi dvayoranugrahāt sarvavedasameva
 |
tasmin dīyamāne dvayamapi dattaṃ bhavati | dvādaśaśatādapyadhikābhiprāyaḥ sarvavedasa-
śabda uktaḥ | tenobhayānugrahaḥ | ūne hi ubhayānugraho na bhavet | etaccātrābhyupa-
gamyavādamātraṃ tathāpīti vacanāt | mūlayuktimāha---na cātra pūrvatāmā-
tramabhipretam | kintu svaprayogābhiprāyaiveyaṃ pūrvatā sā ca sarvavedasa-
dakṣiṇaiva
 | asyārthaḥ---yasyaiva prayogasya yaḥ punaḥ stutiḥ sa evātra prayogaḥ
pūrvaśabdenoktaḥ na pūrvatāmātram | dvādaśaśataprayogaśca pūrvataḥ | tadupamardena tu
pratiharturapacchedanimittakaḥ parataḥ sarvavedasadakṣiṇaḥ prayogaḥ | tasyaiveyaṃ punaḥ stutiriti
sarvavedasameva deyam |

nanu sāpi sarvavedasadakṣiṇā tatra bādhitā
[na]kintu pūrvaprayoge bādhitā
(na punarastīti yukte prayoge) tatra taddadyāditi vacanāt sarvathā na bādhitā kintu
pūrvaprayoge bādhitā punastato deyā | tasmānnātra naimittikaṃ bādhitam | kālamātrasya tu
apahāṇyā pūrvaprayoge dakṣiṇā sā[na]tadānīmeva deyā | kintu punastutau deyā iti
1. yatra-mā

[p. 183]

kālamātrasyotkarṣaḥ | na dakṣiṇābādhaḥ | tataśca yaiva sā prāgbhāvī pratiharturapaccheda-
nimittā saivotkṛṣya dātavyeti |

|| 21 || ahargaṇe yasminnapacchedastadāvarteta karmapṛthaktvāt || 56 ||

prayogaviṣayatvādadhikārasya dvādaśāhaviṣayaṃ ca (yaśca) ekaṃ prayogaṃ
manvānaḥ sarvāvṛttiṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | punaryajñena yajeta iti prayoga-
viṣayo’yaṃ naimittiko’dhikāraḥ prayogasyāvṛttimāha
 dvādaśā[hā]tmakaścāya-
mekaprayogaḥ iti
 tasyaivāvṛttiriti pūrvapakṣavādyāha | rāddhāntastu---karmaviṣaya-
tvādadhikārasya dvādaśāhe ca karmabhedāt pratikarma naimittikamiti karmaṇa
evāvṛttiḥ na karmagaṇasya
 | yasminnimittamupapadyate tadīya eva prayogaḥ punarāvartate |
ekakarmaṇi cedaṃ nimittaṃ, na karmagaṇe dvādaśāhe | ekaikānāṃ karmaṇāmavāntaraprayogāśca
bahava eva, tena karmaṇa ekasyaiva prayogāvṛttiḥ na karmagaṇasya |

akṣarārthastu---karmaviṣayatvādadhikārasya adhikriyate’ne’neneti vyutpattyā nimitta-
madhikāraśabdenocyate | ataḥ karmaviṣayatvamadhikārasyaivoktam |

iti ṛjuvimalāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasya
pañcamaḥ pādaḥ

[p. 184]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

|| ṣaṣṭhādhyāsya ṣaṣṭhaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || sannipāte’vaiguṇyāt prakṛtivattulyakalpā yajeran || 1 ||

idānīmidaṃ śrūyate
-adhikṛta viśeṣāṇāṃ śrūyate | tatra bahukalpeṣu karmasu
samānakalpānāmevādhikāraḥ uta bhinnakalpānāmapīti
 | anubhāṣitamākṣipati-
nanu cādhikṛtaviśeṣaśravaṇe saṃśaya eva nāsti
 | va[ā]siṣṭhādayo’pyadhikṛtatvenaivātra
sambadhyante, na kartṛtayā prathamopanipātitvādadhikārasya | tataśca nārāśaṃsakalpenaiva
teṣāmadhikārasiddhiriti | itareṣāṃ tanūnapātkalpeneti samānakalpānāmevādhikāro
yuktaḥ | na bhinnakalpānāṃ vaiguṇyāt | samādhatte---satyaṃ kratvarthānapāyādbhavati
saṃśayaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---prayājādayastāvatkratvarthā eva | na puruṣārthāḥ | kratoścaitadubhaya-
maṅgam | tataścaikānuṣṭhāne’pi kratoravaiguṇyam | avaiguṇye va kratoradhikārasiddhirapyadhi-
kṛtānāmiti | atra codayati-kratvarthānapāye kathaṃ punassaṃśayaḥ
 yāvatā bhinnakalpā-
nāmapyadhikāra ityayameva pakṣaḥ śreyāniti |

uttaraṃ---satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ
, viparyayameva pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | kathaṃ ?
kratvarthaścennāpaiti na vaiguṇyaṃ śakyate vaktum | vaiguṇyāccādhikāravidhātaḥ svamanīṣi-
kayā | asmiṃśca pakṣe vasiṣṭhādiśravaṇamanuvādamātram | kratorvasiṣṭhādisambandhitvāt |
yataḥ kratorekopādānatve’pi avaiguṇyenāyaṃ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | evaṃ ca sati kāryasiddhiḥ
sāmānyamātratayā śirovaditi pradarśitam | yathā ṛtapeye ghṛtavratavidhānāt ghṛtaduhi
nivṛttāyāmanyayāpi gavā kāryasiddhiḥ | tathā’nyenāpi dvitīyena prayājenānyaprayāja-
kāryasiddhirityetāvanmātreṇāśiravaditi dṛṣṭāntaḥ |

nanu aṅgāntarānupasaṃhāre vaiguṇyamityatrāha---kāryārthatvādupadeśopa-
saṃhārasya tasya kāryasiddhāvanupasaṃhāre’pi adoṣa iti
 | yadvā ubhayasaṃbhave
naikamapi nimittaṃ bhavatīti bṛhadrathantaravaditi pradarśitam | bhinnakalpodayapuruṣasambhave
vasiṣṭhādayo viśvāmitrādayaśca nimittaṃ na bhavanti ekaikasya nimittatvāt | yathā
bṛhat rathantaraṃ ca pratyekaṃ nimittamiti ekasya na nimittatvamiti
naimittikavyāghātaḥ
 | ayamabhiprāyaḥ---upadeśe’pi tāvadanupasaṃhāra uktaḥ | yadvā

[p. 185]

upadeśa eva bhinnakalpānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ samāhāraḥ nāstīti | kena punarviśeṣeṇā-
śiravaditi yasmāt yajamānasammitaudumbarī bhavati ityupanyastam | atrāpi
kāryasiddhistulyaiva | uttaraṃ---satyaṃ, kintu parihāre’bhyadhikā śaṅkā’sti
 |
tāmanena dṛṣṭāntavyājenopanyasya parihartuṃ bhedena punarupanyāsaḥ | kā punarasau ? tāmāha---
kārakatayaiva kimiti yajamānaśabdo na pravartate ‘ṛtvigvat’
 tena(na)kratva-
ntaragatenāpi yajamānena sammānaṃ prāpnoti
 | yadā svāmitayā yajamānaśabdo
vartate tadā katvantaravartinā sammānaṃ na bhavati | tasyāsvāmitvāt | kartṛmātraparigrahe tu
tasyāpi kartṛtvāt tenāpi sammānaṃ śakyate kartuṃ tannirākaraṇārthaṃ dṛṣṭāntāntaro-
pādānam |

nirākaraṇamāha---na kartṛmātratayā grahaṇam adhikṛtamukhenopādāna-
sambhavāt | adhikārapūrvakameva kartṛtvamiti tatpurassarameva kartṛtvaṃ parigṛhyate
 |
tenādhikṛtena sammānaṃ kartavyamiti na kratvantaragatayajamānāntaraparigrahaḥ ātmanepadānu-
grahācca svāmyeva grāhyaḥ | na kartṛmātram | ato’dhikṛto yajamāna iti | sthitam |
upādeyatvāt sammānaṃ prati bahavo na kurvantīti viśeṣo dṛṣṭāntenopādānasya | prathame
punaḥ śirovaditi vyākhyāne yathā puruṣaśīrṣamupadadhāti iti śravaṇāt smṛtibādhaḥ |
tathehāpi kalpāntareṇa kalpāntarasya bādhaḥ iti | asminnapyaprāptireva draṣṭavyā nimitto-
pasaṃhāravat | kratvarthatve hi śavaśīrṣasya sparśane na pratiṣedhaḥ pravartate | tena dṛṣṭānte-
nātrāprāptireva draṣṭavyā | yathā nimittopasaṃhārapakṣe prāptau ca kalpāntarasya pramāṇaṃ
nāstīti ayukto’yaṃ pakṣaḥ | ata evāparitoṣāt dvitīyā vyākhyā kṛtā |

atrābhidhīyate---nedamāśiravaditi śakyate vaktuṃ, nāpi nimittopa-
saṃhāravat
 | etaduktaṃ bhavati-nāpi nimittopasaṃhāravadaprāptiḥ, nāpyāśiravaditi kṛta-
kṛtyatā sambhavati | kathaṃ tarhi ? adhikṛtaviśeṣaṇaṃ hi etat vasiṣṭhādīnāṃ bhṛgūṇāmiti
ca | tathāpi kimityāha---adhikāradvāraṃ ca prayoge karmasvarūpe sambhavati-
tyuktam
 | adhikṛtasambandhena prayogaviśeṣo’yaṃ vidhīyate na karmasvarūpam | tathā
ca prayoge kṛtakṛtyatāprāptirapi nāstīti samānakalpānāmevādhikāraḥ ekaprayogatvāditi
sthitam |

|| 2 || vacanāttu dvisaṃyogastasmādekasya pāṇitvam || 12 ||

[p. 186]

rājapurohitau sāyujyakāmau yajeyātāmiti śrūyate | tatra saṃśayaḥ---kiṃ
samāsārthāt dvivacanaṃ uta prātipadikārthāditi | yadi samāsārthadvitve dvivacanaṃ tadā
rājñaḥ purohitau dvau yajeyātāṃ (uta)prātipadikārthadvitve tadā rājā purohitaśca
yajeyātāmiti |

nanu itarasminnapi pakṣe samāsārthādeva dvivacanaṃ dvandvo’pi hi samāsa
eva
 | uttaraṃ-satyaṃ samāsaḥ na tu samāsārthe dvivacanaṃ, samāsināveva dvivaca-
nārthena sambadhyete itaretarayuktau
 | itaretarayoge hi dvayordundvaḥ | tatra samāsa-
vartibhiḥ padaiḥ svārthāḥ itaretarayuktā eva pratipādyante | na punaḥ padadvayena kaścidarthaḥ
pratipādyate | yadgataṃ tad‌dvitvaṃ hi vacanenocyate | yatra punaḥ samāsārtha eva dvivacanaṃ
tatraikavacanameva bhavati | yathā samāhāre | samāhāre hi yo dvandvaḥ tatra samāhniyamāṇa-
marthadvayaṃ samāhāralakṣaṇārthamucyate | tataśca tasyaikārthyāt ekavacanameva bhavati samāhāre |
samāhāre hi yo dvandvaḥ tatra samāhriyamāṇe(ma)rthe dvivacanagrahaṇaṃ dvivacanopalakṣaṇārtham |

nanvevamitaretarayogasyāpi ekatvādekavacanaṃ prāpnoti | na ca dṛśyate,
ke[te]nābhiprāyeṇa pṛcchati---kimita itaretarayogo na samāsārthaḥ
 yena tatraika-
vacanaṃ na bhavati | atha samāsārthe’pi tasminnekavacanaṃ na bhavati samāhāre’pi na
syāditi bhāvaḥ | uttaraṃ-na samāsārtha iti brūmaḥ upādhimātraṃ hi sa tadā bhavati
samāsināvevetaretarayuktaḥ [ktau] samāsārthaḥ samāsasaṃjña
kābhyāṃ padābhyāmucyate |
yata evetaretarayoge samāsināvevocyete, ata eva ca yugapadadhikāraṇatā ekaśeṣaśca
evaṃvidhaviṣaya eva na samāhāraviṣayaḥ |

kathaṃ punaryugadapadhikaraṇavacanatā ? na tāvat vācakavṛttyā, sambandhagrahaṇā-
bhāvāt | padāntaraprayogavaiyarthyācca | na ca lakṣaṇāvṛttiḥ sambhavati anupapatterabhāvāt |
svārthahānaprasaṅgācca | atrocyate | lakṣaṇāvṛttyā yugapadadhikaraṇavacanatā | tathā
hi - dhavakhadirau hi cchindhi ityatra dhavaśabdo dhavamātraṃ bravīti sa dhavārthaḥ, khadira-
sahitāṃ svāvasthāṃ lakṣayati avasthāvasthāvatoḥ sambandhāt | dvivacanaśravaṇaṃ cānupapadya-
mānaṃ lakṣaṇāhetuḥ | tarhi prātipadikārthadvitve sambhavati | na ca dvandve padadvaya-
vyatirekī dviśabdo’sti | samāsapade eva sahitāvasthasvārthamātrapratipādanapare | tadā
tayorapi pratyekaṃ dvitvābhāvāt kimāśritya ghaṭatāṃ dvivacanam | yadā tu samāsapade
itaretarayuktasvārthapratipādanapare bhavataḥ, tadā tathābhūtayorarthayoḥ dvitvaṃ samavetamiti dviva-

[p. 187]

canopapattiḥ | samāse tu pratyekapadābhihitayorarthayoḥ samāhāralakṣaṇatvādupapannameka-
vacanamiti na yugapadadhikaraṇavacanatāyāṃ pramāṇamasti | ekaśeṣe ca tantrābhidhānāt[na]
parasparasahitārthapratipattiḥ | itaretarayoge ca parasparasahitārthapratipattiriti tadapabā/?/da
ekaśeṣaḥ tadviṣaya eva, na samāhāraviṣayo dvandvaḥ pakṣe |

yadā yugapadadhikaraṇabalāt idamāpadyate uktena nyāyena sahitavacanatā, evaṃ
cet purohitārthagatameva dvitvaṃ nyāyyam | anyathā hi purohitaśabdo rājanyapi
syāt | tatra lakṣaṇāprasaṅgaḥ syāt | kathaṃ punarekasminnevoccāraṇe purohite rājani ca
vartamānaḥ purohitaśabdo lakṣaṇayā vartata iti śakyate vaktum | purohite tu śrauta
eva saḥ | na ca śrauto lākṣaṇikaśca sakṛduccārito bhavati
 | pariharati---satyaṃ
yugapadadhikaraṇaśravaṇabalādidamāpadyate | uktena nyāyena yadā sahitāvasthā-
lakṣaṇatvena yugapadadhikaraṇaṃ tadā śraute lākṣaṇike ca tātparyaṃ bhavati
 |
paramārthataśca yanna śrautārthaḥ lākṣaṇika eva | tathā ca darśitaṃ dhavasvadirau ceti | na hi
yugapadadhikaraṇavacanatāṃ vinā dvivacanamupapadyate | ataḥ śraute saṃbhavatyapi lakṣaṇāmaṅgī-
kṛtya heyatvenāyaṃ pakṣaḥ upanyasyate | yadyapi purohitārtho’pyasti tathāpi lakṣaṇā
prāpnotīti | anena doṣeṇa pūrvapakṣavādinā heyatvenāyaṃ dvandvapakṣa upanyastaḥ |

nanu vasānau iti dvitvaṃ puṃstvaṃ ca eṣaśabdo vyabhicarati evamatrāpi
purohitadvitvaṃ caiṣaśabdo na vyabhicaratīti
 dvitīya’rthe pratipādakatvāt
naiva lakṣaṇāprasaṅga iti | pariharati---vacanābhiprāyaḥ | taduktaṃ puṃstvaṃ dvitvaṃ ca
aukārāntadvivacana na vyabhicaratītyuktam
 | na prātipadikābhiprāyam |
prātipadikasya hi yugapadadhikaraṇatvena vinā dvivacanānupapattiḥ | yathā rājapurohitaṃ
ceti | tena yatra dvivacanaṃ tena prātipadikena itaretarayuktaḥ svārthaḥ pratipādanīyaḥ |
na ca lakṣaṇāṃ vinā tadupapadyate | tasmātsuṣṭhūcyate lakṣaṇeti | lakṣaṇātve heyo’yaṃ
pakṣa iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

nanu ṣaṣṭhīsamāsapakṣe’pi rājaśabdo lākṣaṇika eva ṣaṣṭhayarthalakṣakatat
 |

pariharati---bhavati | kā no hāniḥ? purohitaśabdamaṅgīkṛtya lakṣaṇā-
tvena na hātavyo’yaṃ pakṣa ityuktam
 |

[p. 188]

nanu ca ṣaṣṭhīsamāsapakṣe’pi tasya lakṣaṇātvamanyaparatvādityāha---uttarapada-
pradhāno’yaṃ ṣaṣṭhīsamāsa iti
 |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---satyamevam | kintu ekatvāt purohitasya
ṣaṣṭhīsamāse’pi dvivacanānupapattireva sambandhiśabdatvāt purohitaśabdasya
dvayoḥ sambandhitvenābhāvāt
 | asyārthaḥ---purohitaśabdo’yaṃ sambandhiśabdo-
’yam | na caikasya rājño dvo purohitau sambandhinau sambhavataḥ | tena ṣaṣṭhī-
samāse’pi arthābhāvāt dvivacanānupapattireva | tena varaṃ lakṣaṇaivāśrīyatāṃ dvivacanopa-
pattyartham |

nanu ca viniyogadarśanāt dvau purohitau bhaviṣyataḥ yathā añjalividhānāt
dvau hastau vyāpāryete tatrāha---yatpunarañjalivat ityuktaṃ tadatra na sambhavati |
asambandhitayaiva dvitīyasya sambandhino’bhāvāt | purohitaṃ vṛṇīta ityeka-
vacanaśravaṇāt
 | nanu bhavatu sambandhiśabdaḥ purohitaśabdaḥ kāryāntare’pi niyoga
eva sambandhitāmāpādayiṣyati | yo yasya rājñaḥ tāni tāni karmāṇi karoti sa tasya
purohito bhavati iti sambandhidvayasambhavāt tatrāha---utpattiśca sambandhe hetuḥ na
kārye viniyogaḥ etadapekṣatvādviniyogasya
 | asyārthaḥ-paraṇotpannaḥ purohito
bhavati na punaḥ kāryāntaraviniyojyaḥ purohitatvāpekṣatvāt kāryāntaraviniyogasya |
tadatra saṅgrahārthaḥ-viniyogataḥ purohitatvaṃ tasya tu varaṇenopasaṅgrahaḥ | tataśca
dvayorviniyogadarśanāt dvayoḥ saṅgrahaḥ viniyogārthatvāt saṅgrahasyeti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntastu purohitasya sataḥ viniyogānuvṛttita eva purohitatvam | utpattau ca
ekavacanaśravaṇāt eka evotpadyata iti dvayoḥ rājñoḥ sambandhinoḥ purohitayoḥ
sāyujyaṃ na bhavatīti pradarśitaṃ bhāṣye | tasmāllakṣaṇaivātra jyāyasī arthasambhavāt |
liṅgamuktam
 |

|| 3 || satrāṇi sarvabarṇānāmaviśeṣāt || 16 ||

nanu ca sarvavarṇānāmiti nopapadyate ārvijye yajamānaśravaṇāt
 |
asyārthaḥ-prakṛtau brāhmaṇakartṛkamārtvijyaṃ pratipannam | iha tu ārtvijye yajamānaḥ
kartṛtvenocyate ‘ye yajamānāsta ṛtvija’
 iti śravaṇāt | yadyabrāhmaṇo’pi satre’dhi-

[p. 189]

kārī syāt tadā tasyārtvijye kartṛtve sati brāhmaṇakartṛtvaṃ bādhyeta | brāhmaṇe hyadhi-
kāriṇi na kaścidbādhyate | nacābādhenopadeśopapattau bādho nyāyya iti brāhmaṇānāme-
vādhikāro yuktaḥ satreṣu |

na ceyaṃ vacanavyaktiratra cintyate-kiṃ ye yajamānāḥ ta ṛtvija iti
yajamānoddeśenārtvijyaṃ saṃskārakatayā vidhīyate uta ārtvijye yajamānāḥ kartṛtvena
vidhīyanta iti |

tatra yadi yajamānasaṃskāraḥ tadā ārtvijyaṃ ṛtvigbhireva kartavyamiti sarva-
varṇānāmadhikāro bhavet | yadi tu yajamānānāmārtvijye kartṛtvavidhiḥ tadā brāhmaṇānāṃ
pūrvoktanyāyenādhikāra iti | tena prāpyavacanavyaktyāśrayaṇena sarvavarṇānāmadhikāraśaṅkā
yuktaiva | na ceyamiha cintā yuktā kartuṃ, adhikāralakṣaṇe’saṅgatatvādviniyoganirṇayasya |
tasmādasaṅgatatvavādināṃ cintāṃ vinā pūrvapakṣo niryuktika iva pratibhāti |

ucyate | ayamabhiprāyaḥ pūrvapakṣavādinaḥ, yathādhikāramaṅgavacanaṃ
tatpūrvakatvāt svayaṃkartṛbhāvasya
 | asyārthaḥ-yajamānānāṃ sakāmārtvijyavidhānāt
viniyogaḥ | yajamānānāṃ yajamānatā ca satyadhikāre | tena pūrvasiddhādhikāropa-
jīvitvāt ayamaṅgavidhānasya adhikāravaśena varṇanam[na] punaretadvaśenādhikāraḥ |
adhikāraśca svarūpatastrayāṇāṃ nāma vibhāti | teṣāṃ cārtvijye’pi viniyoga iti
upadeśenātideśikī brāhmaṇakartṛtā bādhyate | tathā ca liṅgaṃ brahmasāmavidhāne
varṇatrayadarśanam |

atrocyate
-kena voktaṃ yathādhikāramaṅgavacanam iti | kintu nādhikāro’dhi-
kārāntaraṃ nivartayituṃ kṣamaḥ bādhakāraṇābhāvāt asāvapyadhikāra eva brāhmaṇānāmārtvi-
jyamiti brāhmaṇakartṛkatvenārtvijyaṃ iti śrūyate | ārtvijyaśabdena ṛrtvikkarmāṇyucyante |
“yadyat brāhmaṇakarma taduktaṃ....karma hīnena na kartavyaṃ”
 iti smaraṇāt kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ
niṣidhyate | nāyamārtvijye niṣādādhikāro yathā nātikramyate tathā satreṣvadhikāro
varṇanīyaḥ | na cātra kratvarthatayā viṣayabhedenādhikāradvayāvirodhaḥ | dvayorapyadhikārayoḥ
puruṣārthatvāt | yadyapi ca satreṣu nāṅgāni ṛtvikkartṛkāṇi tathāpi tānyārtvijānyeva
prakṛtau teṣāmārtvijyena jñātatvāt taddevatāvat | idamadhikaraṇaṃ ārtvijye yajamāna-
māśritya varṇitam |

[p. 190]

yatpunaratra bhāṣye ārtvijyena yajamānasaṃskāranirākaraṇaṃ tatpratikaroti---
yadi paramārtvijyaṃ adṛṣṭāyaiva codyate svāminām
 | asyārthaḥ-yadi svāmināma-
dṛṣṭāya saṃskārāyārtvijyaṃ codyate tadā evaṃ sati satre sarveṣāmadhikāraḥ syāt | na ca
tadastītyuktaṃ bhāṣye | tasmādadhikārānurodhena brāhmaṇānāmeva satreṣvadhikāra iti
siddham | darśanamahīne caritārtham | tatra varṇatrayasambhavāt |

|| 4 || vāsiṣṭhānāṃ vā brahmatvaniyamāt || 24 ||

nanu cedamadhikaraṇaṃ vacanamadṛṣṭvā pūrvapakṣaṃ kurvatāmanārambhaṇīyameva
 |
vaiśvāmitro hoteti vacane sati vāsiṣṭhānāmeva satramiti pūrvapakṣābhāvāt nedamadhikaraṇa-
mārambhaṇīyamevetyarthaḥ |

samādhatte---satyamevaṃ, kintvatra kalpāntarāśaṅkānirākaraṇāya pūrvapakṣa-
pradarśanam | kā punaratra kalpāntarāśaṅkā ?
 vāsiṣṭhe brahmaṇi hotāpi vāsiṣṭha-
samānakalpa eva bhavatu vāsiṣṭho vā | ubhayathāpyavaiguṇyāt | tathā hotaryapi (vi)vaiśvā-
mitra eva tatsamānakalpo vā bhavatvityāśaṅkate-dvayorvacanayorvidyamānatvāt kalpa-
dvayena bhavitavyamiti
 | tannirākaraṇārthamidamadhikaraṇam | na punarvāsiṣṭhānāṃ
tatsamānakalpānāmevādhikāra iti tannirākaraṇārthametat | vasiṣṭhavacanasyānyārthatvena
niraste vāsiṣṭhe punarhotraiva niyamo bhavatīti siddhaṃ avāsiṣṭhānāṃ atatsamānakalpānāṃ ca
satram | vaiśvāmitratatsamānakalpānāmeva satramiti |

|| 5 || vihārasya prabhutvādanagnīnāmapi syāt || 27 ||

kathaṃ punaranāhitāgnyāśaṅkā ? sthite svāgniniṣṭhaṃ karma kartavyamiti
nyāye parakīyānāmagnīnāmāhavanīyatvābhāvāt
 | samādhatte---liṅgadarśanā-
śaṅkayā prasaṅgaṃ manvānasya pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ
 | sārasvate satre “pararathairvā ete
svarga lokaṃ yanti ye’nāhitāgnayaḥ satramāsate”
 ityanāhitāgnīnāṃ satradarśanāt |
tathā “agnaye vivicaye puroḍāśamaṣṭākapālaṃ nirvapet yasyāhitāgneranyairagnibhiragnayaḥ
saṃsṛjyeran”
 iti saṃsargadarśanāt | āhavanīyenaiva kāryaniṣpatterapi anāhitāgnera-
pyadhikārāśaṅkā |

atrocyate---nātra prasaṅga upapadyate tatkāryāparijñānāt
 | yadi hi
āhavanīyakāryaṃ jñāyate tadā parakīyenāpi tatkṛtamiti prasaṅgaḥ syāt | na ca

[p. 191]

tajjñāyate | homanirvṛttiḥ nāhavanīyakāryaṃ kiṃ tarhi agnidravyamātrasya | liṅgasya
parihāraṃ vakṣyati | 1pātrāṇāṃ punaḥ svaiḥ ityaśravaṇāt pūrvapakṣavādī prasaṅgaṃ
manvāno yasya kasyaciditi bravīti
 | asyārthaḥ---yathā svāgniniṣṭhakarma kartavya-
miti sthite prasaṅgo nāsti tadā[thā]tarhi yatra svairiti na śrūyate nāpi nyāyataḥprāpyate
tatra prasaṅgena bhavitavyaṃ yathā pātreṣu iti | pūrvopajīvanenotthānāt antarāgarbhiṇītvam |

rāddhāntavādī tu satyamatra prasaṅgo vidyate sa tu dīrghakālatvāt satrāṇāṃ
āyuṣaśca parimāṇājñānāt anyairapi tadarthamutpannaiḥ kṛtameva tadbhavatīti pātrā-
ntaropādānameva nyāyyamiti | manyata iti sambandhaḥ
 |

satyamatra prasaṅgo vidyate sa tvayukta iti vākyaśeṣaḥ | kutaḥ ? dīrghakālatvāt
satrāṇāṃ, āyuṣaśca parimāṇājñānāt kadācit satrecet pravṛtte vyāpadyeta yo’tra
sthāyī tasya pātrāṇāṃ satre vyāpṛtānāṃ pratipattilopaḥ syāt | pratipattau vā satra-
kriyāvaiguṇyam | anyairapi ca satrārthamupāttaiḥ satraṃ kṛtameva bhavati ityanayā rāddhānta-
vādī manyata iti yojyam | ādhastyasya liṅgamuktam prāyaścittamāpadi syāditi
 |
sārasvate darśanaṃ tu bhāṣye na parihṛtam |

tatrāha---sārasvate darśanaṃ tu satre yadyanyathā nopapadyate tadā vacanaṃ
tarhi bhaviṣyati | na ca tāvatā sarvāṇi satrāṇyevānāhitāgnīnāmapi
bhaveyuriti bhāvaḥ |

|| 7 || puruṣakalpena vā vikṛtau kartṛniyamaḥ syāt yajñasya
tadguṇatvādabhāvāditarān pratyekasminnadhikāraḥ syāt || 36 ||

kathaṃ punaḥ adhvarakalpādiṣu sandehaḥ sāmānyenādhikāre śrūyamāṇe kiṃ
vaiśyasyaivādhikāraḥ uta trayāṇāṃ varṇānāmapīti saṃśayaḥ
 | itthaṃ saṃśa(ye)[yaḥ]
kimadhvarakalpādiṣu adhikāro niyamyate sāptadaśyavacanena vaiśyasyaivādhikāraḥ uta
pāñcadaśyapratyāmnāyena sāptadaśyaṃ kratvarthatayā codyate iti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī adhi-
kāraniyamaḥ sādhīyān iti manyate | sāptadaśyaṃ kila vaiśyanimittakaṃ tenaitadvacanenaivaṃ darśa-
1. atra juhādīnāmaprayuktatvāt ityādhikaraṇārambho draṣṭabyaḥ | antarāgarbhiṇītvāt
na pṛthaguktiḥ |

[p. 192]

yati yathā sāptadaśyaṃ bhavati tathā kuryāditi | vaiśye cādhikāriṇi tadbhavati | tena vaiśya-
meva niyacchati | nanu pāñcadaśyapratyāmnāyena kratvarthatayā sāptadaśyavidhireva1 kasmānna
bhavatītyatrāha---kratvarthe hi vaiśyakartṛke sāptadaśyasya codakaprāptatvānna vidhāṃna
sambhavatīti
 | na cātra tatparyudāsaḥ śrūyate tena vidhivaiṣamyaṃ syāt ekasyaiva
vidheranuvādakatvaṃ ceti | tasmādadhikāra eva kasmānna bhavatītyadhikāraniyamaḥ |

rāddhāntastu kratvarthena svarūpavidhiravagamyamāno nādhikāraṃ niyantuṃ
kṣamaḥ | nanu ca vaiśyakartṛke vidhānaṃ na sambhavatītyuktam
 | samādhatte---
na sambhavati yasmānna kāryataḥ prāptirūpaṃ vihanti
 | kāryato hi nimittasāpekṣā
vaiśyakartṛke’pi prāptiḥ | upadeśastu nimitānapekṣatayā vidadhāti | yathā tryanīkāyāṃ
anyokteṣvāmnānaṃ guṇārthamityāśaṅkya nimittanirapekṣatayā aindravāyavāgra[tā]vidhānam |
tena vaiśyakartṛke’pi vidhāyakatvameva | yathā ca tatra vaikṛte nimitta[ā]bhāvepyanuṣṭhānaṃ
bhavatīti vidhiprayojanaṃ tathehāpi brāhmaṇarājanyakartṛke prāptiḥ vidhiprayojanam |
tasmātsarveṣāmadhikāra iti sthitam |

evaṃ tāvat vacanaśravaṇenādhikaraṇasthitiḥ2 |

ekaṃ prakṛtau saptadaśa vaiśyasyānubrūyāditi, aparamapi adhvarakalpāyāṃ saptadaśa-
sāmidhenīranubrūyāt iti | tṛtīyamāśritya vicāraṃ kurvanti taddūṣayati---anārabhya
cintā tu na śāstrataḥ kāryato vopapadyata iti nādṛtā
 | prākṛtavacanaṃ kāryataḥ
prāptāvupayujyamānaṃ cintanīyam | vaikṛtaṃ tu vacanaṃ śāstrataḥ prāptāvupayujyamānaṃ cinta
nīyam | vaikṛtaṃ tu punarārabhyavacanamiti taccintā nādṛtā bhāṣyakāreṇa |

iti ṛjuvimalāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasya
ṣaṣṭhaḥ pādaḥ

1. vidhereva-mā 2.sthitikam

[p. 193]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasya saptamaḥ pādaḥ

|| 1 || svadāne sarvamaviśeṣāt || 1 ||

viśvajiti sarvavedasaṃ śrūyate | vedasaśabdaḥ svaśabdasyārthe vartate | svaśabda-
ścāyaṃ jñātidhanādiṣvaviśiṣṭaḥ ātbhīyavacano’yaṃ svaśabdaḥ | ātmīyatvaṃ ca jñātidhanā
diṣvaviśiṣṭaṃ ityaviśeṣeṇa sarvatra svaśabdo vartate | na ca viśeṣe kiñcana pramāṇamasti
etaddeyaṃ etanneti | sarvaśabda śravaṇāt | tasmadyāvadasya svaṃ tāvatsarvamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

atra ca sarvasyātmīyasya dānaśravaṇāt dadātiḥ parasvatvāpādane vartate na punaḥ
svatyāge tasyāśakyatvāt | atra codayati---kaściddānā(dā)bhimānī | nanu
yathaivātmanaḥ svaṃ tathaiva parasmā āpādanīyam
 | na ca pitrādayaḥ parebhyaḥ
pitṛtvenāpādayituṃ śakyante | ucyate | svaṃ tāvaddeyam | dīyamānasya ca yathaiva
parasvatvāpattirbhavati tathaiva parikalpanīyam
 | yena rūpeṇātmanaḥ svatā tenā-
pādayituṃ na śakyate [yena rūpeṇa śakyate] tenāpādayiṣyate | na ca kaścidvirodhaḥ |

rāddhāntastu svaśabdastāvadayaṃ naikārthaḥ sambhavati svabhāvabhedāt |

asyārthaḥ---jñātidhanātmīyavacano’yaṃ svaśabdaḥ sa caikayā śaktyā na vartate | kiṃ
tarhi gośabdavadanekārthaḥ | anātmīye ca jñātau dhane ca prayogadarśanāt jñātiśabdavat
dhanaśabdavacca | tathā’’tmani vartate ātmīye ca | nātmani śrautaḥ ātmīye lākṣaṇikaḥ
iti śakyate vaktuṃ viparītapakṣāṇāṃ sambhavāt | tasmādagṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvādātmanaḥ
ātmīyasyāyaṃ vācakaḥ | na cāyamekārthavacanatayaiva caturṣvapi prayogaṃ labhate svārtha-
bhedāt | jñātiṣu jñātitvāt svatvaṃ dhane dhanatvaṃ ātmīye ātmīyatvaṃ ātmanyātma-
tvamiti naikaṃ sāmānyamasti sarveṣu anekārthaḥ svaśabdaḥ na ca yugapat sarveṣu vartate kintu
kadācitkvaciditi | tatra dadātiśabdasambandhāt dhanaviṣaya eva vartate | dadātirhi
svatyāge parasvatvāpādanātmake vartata iti darśitaṃ caturthe | dhanaviṣaye hi tathā
sambhavati tena sarvadhanaṃ deyamiti śāstrārthaḥ | tena pitrādayo na deyāḥ |

yuktyantaramāha---nacātmajñātyādiṣu yaḥ svaśabdārthaḥ sa śakyate parasmā-
āpādayitum | anyathā hi āpādyamāne na svaṃ parasmā āpāditaṃ bhavati |

[p. 194]

svaṃ sat parasmā āpāditaṃ na bhavati | kiñca viśeṣapramāṇaṃ cātra vidyate
 |
asyārthaḥ---yadyapi svaśabda ātmīyārthatayaiva jñātidhanādiṣu vartate tathāpyātmīya-
viśeṣe’pyatra pramāṇamasti | prakṛtau hi dhanaviṣayameva dānaṃ tasya ceyaṃ sarvatā vidhīyata
iti dhanaviṣayaiva sā sarvasvatā[’va]tiṣṭhate | ātmīyamapi dīyamānaṃ dhanameva deyaṃ
nānyat prakṛtivaditi tasyaiveyaṃ sarvasvatā vidhīyata iti |

|| 2 || na bhūmiḥsyāt sarvān pratyaviśiṣṭatvāt || 3 ||

bhūmau svaśabdo bhavati
 | bhogyaviṣayaṃ dhanaviṣayamityarthaḥ | tena deyā bhūmi-
riti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntamāha---na tu sā parasmā āpādayituṃ śakyate sarvān pratya-
viśiṣṭatvāditi
 | asyārthaḥ---bhūgolakaṃ gopatharājamārgajalādisamanvitaṃ kṛtsnameva
dānuṃ na śakyate gopathādīnāṃ sarvān pratyaviśiṣṭatvāt yathā bhūsvāmī tatra svāmī
tathā’nyo’pi | gamanāgamanādibhogo hi tatra svāmitā, na paraṃ sa ca sarveṣā-
maviśiṣṭaḥ | laukikaṃ ca svatvaṃm | ye tu grāmādikaṃ dadati te’pi vāstukṣetrādika-
meva dadatīti mantavyam | grāmastu tadavacchedaka iti niravadyam |

|| 3 || akāryatvācca tataḥ punarviśeṣaḥ syāt || 4 ||

atrāpi prasajya pratiṣedhāśaṅkayā pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ
 | aśvā na deyā iti prasajya-
pratiṣedhaṃ manyate | tatra vihitapratiṣiddhatvāt vikalpena dānaṃ sarvadānameva prasajya-
pratiṣedhe kāraṇam | tathā sati pakṣe’pi tāvaddānaṃ bhavati | paryupadāsena
rāddhāntaḥ | ekavākyatvabalenāśvavarjitaṃ ca sarvaṃ bhaviṣyati
 |

|| 4 || nityatvāccānityairnāsti sambandhaḥ || 5 ||

atrāpi sarvaśabdānurodhena yāvadbhogyaṃ deyamiti manyate pūrvapakṣa-
vādī
 | śayanāsanādīni yāvanti svaśabdavācyāni tāni sarvāṇi deyānīti |
yasya sarvāṇi na santi tathāpi krītvā krītvā dātavyamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntavādī tu vidyamānenaiva svena svato’dhikārasiddheḥ nāvidyamāno-
pādānamutsahate vidhiḥ kartum
 | yāvadyasya dhanaṃ tāvataḥ sarvatvamucyate | sarva-

[p. 195]

śabdo’yaṃ prakṛtāpekṣaḥ vidyamānaṃ cādhikāriṇaḥ svaṃ prakṛtaṃ nāvidyamānamapi | tena
tasyaiva sarvatvamucyate | tāvatā ca dattenādhikārasiddhiriti nāparamapyavidyamānamapyu-
pādāya dātavyam | na cātra dānacodanetyuktam
 | yadi hi svaṃ prati dānaṃ
codyeta tadā svaṃ dānena sambandhayitavyamiti bhavedapyupādānakalpanā | iha tu svena
dānaṃ kartavyamiti yathopāttenāpi kṛte kṛtameveti nopādānakalpanetyuktaṃ bhāṣye |

|| 5 || śūdraśca dharmaśāstratvāt || 6 ||

dharmopanate cintā---bhogyatvāt deyabhāśaṅkya na dharmopanataḥ parasmai
śakyata āpādayituṃ tadadhīnatvāt dharmopanatasyeti rāddhāntaḥ
 |

asyārthaḥ---dharmopanatasya bhogyatvāt deyatāmāśaṅkya na deya iti siddhāntaḥ |
sa hi dharmaśikṣārthaṃ yasminnupanataḥ tasyaiva bhogyo nānyasya bhogyaḥ śakyate vaktuṃ tathā
tasyānupanatatvāt | sa hi dharmopanataḥ ucyate yo dharmaṃ bruvāṇasyopanataḥ paricāraka-
balena | tasmiṃśca svayameva kelavaṃ bhoktṛtvam na punaḥ paramapi pratyupādātuṃ śakyate |

|| 6 || dakṣiṇākāle yatsvaṃ tatpratīyeta taddānasaṃyogāt || 7 ||

idamidānīṃ sandihyate---kiṃ yatprāk dakṣiṇākālāt svaṃ tadanyatra na
viniyujyate yo(dharmaḥ) yacca bhaviṣyatīti jñāyate tatsarvameva deyaṃ, uta
dakṣiṇākālavartyeveti
 | mayā viśvajitkartavya iti jñānādanyatra na viniyukte tathā
pramāṇatayā yasya bhāvino nirṇayaḥ tadapi deyam | uta yatheṣṭaviniyuktaśeṣavartamānameva
deyamiti | tatra bhaviṣyadvartamānasarvasya bhūtavartamānasarvasya ca svaśabdārthatvāt
deyatāmāśaṅkya avyavasthānāt tatkālavartyeva deyamityavagamyata iti rāddhāntaḥ |
vyavasthito hi śāstrārtho yuktaḥ tena vartamānameva sarvaṃ deyamiti rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 7 || aśeṣatvāt tadantaḥ syāt karmaṇo dravyasiddhitvāt || 8 ||

nanu ca tridhāvyavasthānādacintanīyamevedamadhikaraṇam
 | samādhatte---
satyaṃ tathāpi karmāpekṣo dānādhikāraḥ na dānāpekṣaṃ karmeti imamarthaṃ pradarśa-
yitumidamupanyastam
 asyārthaḥ-vibhāgo nāstīti kṛtvā nyāyavyutpādanamevaitat
kiyace | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī dānāpekṣaṃ karmeti manyate | sarvasvaṃ hi sākṣāddeyatvenopa-

[p. 196]

deśena vihitaṃ bhavati | datte ca tasmin dravyasādhyatvāt karmaṇaḥ tadantataiva yuktā
na śeṣayitavyaṃ kiñcitkāryārthamiti śakyate vaktum teṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ codakaprāptānāmupa-
deśabādhe’samarthatvāt |

rāddhāntastu karmānuṣṭhānārthaṃ sahāyānveṣaṇārthaṃ ca dānamiti pradhānabhūta-
karmānurodhena (na)dānaśāstraṃ varṇayitavyam
 | tena kratvarthavyatiriktadravyameva sarvaṃ
deyamiti | dravyavibhāgastu udāharaṇāpekṣayā draṣṭavyaḥ | atrodāharaṇe tredhāvyavasthā-
panāt tadapekṣo’yamudāharaṇe’paro viśeṣa iti pradarśayituṃ vibhāga upanyastaḥ | na
nyāyāvalopitayā | tasmādanavadyam |

|| 8 || ahargaṇe ca taddharmā syāt sarveṣāmaviśeṣāt || 14 ||

kathaṃ punaḥ nāmnā dharmātideśe balīyasi dvādaśaśatāśaṅkā ?
 saptamādye nāmnā
dharmāṇāṃ padārthānāmatideśo balīyāt liṅgena dharmātideśāt ityuktamiti pūrvapakṣo
nopapadyate | uttaraṃ---gaṇadharmāpekṣayā | gaṇo hi gaṇasya dharmānapekṣate iti
gaṇāntaḥ pātina eva dharmo nyāyyaḥ apekṣitatvāt
 | tena dvādaśāhika eva
vidhyanto yukto naikāhika iti | ekā ceyaṃ gaṇacodanā | tena gaṇadharmākāṅkṣā |
tasmāt sarveṣāṃ dvādaśaśataṃ dakṣiṇeti tulyo dānādhikāraḥ | adhikāraśabdaṃ prakṣipya
lakṣaṇe saṅgatimāha |

rāddhāntastu---satyamekā gaṇacodanā codanīyāstu bhidyante tadape-
kṣayā ca dharmagrahaṇam
 | asyārthaḥ - yadyapyekā codanā tathāpi codanīyānāṃ karmaṇāṃ
bhedāt tadapekṣayācādhikāravidheḥ dharmagrahaṇaṃ karaṇānyapekṣya cādhikāravidhiḥ dharmān
gṛhṇāti | bahūni caitāni karaṇānīti tadapekṣayā pratyekaṃ dharmātideśaḥ tatra kasya-
cinnāmnā, kasyacilliṅgeneti | tasmāt nāmaiva balavat |

kathaṃ punaritareṣāṃ dakṣiṇā
 | yadi sarvasvaṃ ekasminnahani dakṣiṇā,
anyatra dvādaśaśataṃ ekayā ca dakṣiṇayā bhavitavyamiti daśame sthāpitam | ato
virodhāt kathamitareṣāṃ dakṣiṇā | uttaranyāyāntaramidaṃ cintyate - vipratiṣiddha-
dharmāṇāṃ samavāye bhūyasāṃ syāt svadharmatvamityatra
 |

|| 9 || vikāraḥ sannubhayato’viśeṣāt || 18 ||

[p. 197]

nanu ca niyamavidhau sati sarvadānaṃ dvādaśaśatābhyadhikenaiva sarvasva-
dānenādhikṛta iti vaktavyam | kimidaṃ samena vetyucyate
 | asyārthaḥ - bhāṣya-
kāreṇātra vidhāna evamucyate---dvādaśaśatādhikena tatsamena vā’dhikriyata iti | tadanupa-
pannam | yasmāt prakṛtau dvādaśaśataṃ vidhāyāha---evaṃ vā ṛtvija āgamayitavyāḥ
api vā sarvasveneti | evaṃ cokte dvādaśaśatādhikameva sarvasvaṃ pratīyate | yehi
dvādaśaśatena1 nānamante na tarāṃ te nyūnena | tenādhikaṃ sarvasvaṃ tacca viśvajiti
niyamyate | tenādhikenaiva sarvasvenāpyadhikāro na dvādaśaśatasamāneneti |

atrābhidhīyate---samagrahaṇamānatyantarāpekṣayā na dvādaśaśatāpekṣayetya-
doṣaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---‘yo’yaṃ tattulyayā dakṣiṇayā’dhikāra’
 ityūce sa ānantyanta-
rāpekṣayā tattulyamityanenābhiprāyeṇa tattulyādhikāra ityucyate | na dvādvaśaśatāpekṣayā
dvādaśaśatādhikameva kiñcit ānatitulyatvāttulyamityadoṣaḥ |

|| 10 || aparimite śiṣṭasya saṅkhyāpratiṣedhaḥ tacchrutitvāt || 21 ||

nārthalopenātra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---yadyatrāparimitaśabdena tāvat
yasya parimāṇaṃ nāsti tadavayavānusāreṇocyate na tasya dānāsambhavaḥ sarvasya dīyamānasya
parimāṇasambhavāt | athāvayarthatiraskāreṇa aparimitaśabdena bahūcyate tadā avaya-
vārthatyāga eva doṣaḥ | tenāvayavārthalobhena parimitaṃ na deyaṃ ityatrārthaḥ | asamartha-
samāsāśrayaṇe’pi avayavārthānugraha eva heturiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | dānacodanāprakaraṇāt
śrutiścopapannatareti rāddhāntaḥ
 | ‘ekā deyā’
 ityādayaḥ dānacodanāḥ prakṛtāḥ
tatsamīpe ca aparimitaṃ deyamiti | tena dānacodanāsāhacaryāt iyamapi dānacoda-
naiveti mantavyam | tena aparimitaṃ deyamityevaṃ sambandhaḥ | samāsaśrutiścopapannatarā |
samarthasamāso hi śrutita evāvagamyate | tena aparimitaṃ deyamityarthaḥ | aparimita-
śabdena bahūnyucyante |

nanu caivaṃ sati avayavārthalopaḥ prāpnoti iti pūrvapakṣavāraṇam | rāddhāntamāha
---prāptasya prāptirityapeśalam
 | loke hi aparimitaśabdena bahūnyucyante
aparimitamasya dhanamiti bahviti gamyate | tenāvayavārthalopena darśanāt nāyaṃ doṣa iti |
tathā ca pravīṇādiṣu dṛṣṭam
 | pravīṇakuśalaśabdānāṃ rūḍhitvaṃ dṛṣṭam | pravīṇa-
1. dvādaśena-mā

[p. 198]

śabdo hi na gauṇa iti śakyate vaktum | vīṇāvādanaguṇayogena hi vartamānaḥ
prakarṣa eva kevalena vyavatiṣṭhate | tasmāttatrārohan rūḍha ityucyate | evamapyaparimita-
śabdo’pyādhikya eva vartamāno rūḍha iti |

|| 11 || aniyamo’viśeṣāt || 23 ||

āpekṣikatvāt bahvarthasya sahasrasya prakṛtatvāt tadabhyadhikamiti
nyāyyam
 | tatkilāpekṣitaṃ yatpratiyogyapekṣaṃ tasmin sannihite sambhavati tada pekṣa-
mevābhyadhikatvaṃ pratīyata iti |

|| 12 || parakṛtipurākalpaṃ ca manuṣyadharmaḥ syādarthāya
hyanukīrtanam || 26 ||

atra cintyate---kimevamadhikāravidhayo’thavā kratvarthānāmeva stuti-
riti
 | māṣādīnāṃ saṅkīrtanaṃ kimadhikārapradarśanārthaṃ utāraṇyādistutyarthamiti
sandehaḥ | puruṣasaṃyogādadhikāra iti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ | anyathā hi puruṣa-
śravaṇaṃ saviśeṣaṇamanarthakaṃ syāt bahvarthatvāddhi1 puruṣasaṅkīrtanaṃ gotraviśeṣeṇa sahitaṃ
stutimātratve’narthakaṃ bhavati | vikārārthatve sārthakamityadhikāravidhireva |

nanu ‘nivīṃta manuṣyāṇāṃ’
 ityanena tatrāpi puruṣasambandhāt vidhitvamāśaṅkya
stutitvaṃ darśitam ihāpi tathaiveti | neti brūmaḥ | tatra puruṣaviniyogaḥ kratuviniyoge
bādhitaḥ karmāntarāśrayaṇāt | kratuprakaraṇe kratureva kāryaṃ pratibhāti | tatra puruṣa-
dharmatvaṃ syāt tena puruṣasaṃyogo’rthavādamātram | iha punaradhikāramukhena kratvarthavighāta
upapadyate | adhikāre’pi nāṣādīnāṃ kratvarthatvāditi cintyate | ataḥ puruṣa-
śravaṇāt kratoścāvighātāt adhikāravidhirevāyam | viśeṣatastu tadgotrāṇāṃ sāmānyato
vā atadgotrāṇāmapi sāmānyata eva vā viśeṣasya stutyupayogitvāditi | yasmā-
dvārṣṇyenaivaṃ kṛtaṃ tasmādanyo’pi tatkuryāt iti sāmānyata evāyaṃ siddhiḥ |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---satyaṃ na kratunā virodhaḥ na punaradhikārāntaraṃ
pratyetumeva śakyate tadavagamāt
 pākādaya evaṃ kurvantītyetāvanmātramatra śrūyate, na
1. tvādi - mā

[p. 199]

punaranyo’pyevaṃ kuryāditi tenādhikārāvagamāt āraṇyavyatiriktādhikārāntarāvagamāt
na māṣādiviṣayamadhikārāntaraṃ pratyetuṃ śakyate | nanu ca māṣapākādisaṅkīrtanamanartha-
kamityāśaṅkyāha - viniyogasya ca viniyogāntareṇaikavākyatvāt
 | stu-
tyarthatvāt āraṇyaviniyogenaikavākyatvāt stutyarthameva māṣamātre pākādikīrtanam |
ato na viṣayataḥ
 na niyogata ityarthaḥ | viniyogo hi niyogasya viṣayaḥ tathāpi
niyogataḥ sākṣādvidhyaśravaṇāt nādhikārasya nānātvaṃ śakyateṃ pratipattum | tasmāt
stutireveti nyāyyam |

|| 13 || sahastrasaṃvatsaraṃ tadāyuṣāmasambhavānmanuṣyeṣu || 31 ||

kathaṃ punaratra trivṛta iti śravaṇāt saṃśabdārthāśaṅkā ?
 asyārthaḥ---
yadyapi pañcāśataḥ trivṛtaḥ saṃvatsarā iti saṃvatsaraśabdaḥ śrūyate tathāpi trivṛta iti
śravaṇāt ahānyeva pratīyante trivṛcchabdo’hanyeva vartituṃ śaknoti trivṛtstotra-
mukhena savanārthopasaṃhārāt ahareva pratīyate | saṃvatsaraśabdo’pi ahassveva
nāmadheyatvopapattyartho draṣṭavyaḥ | ataḥ kathaṃ saṃvatsaraśabdārtha ityāśaṅkate | ucyate |
dvādaśāhavadetat bhaviṣyati iti
 | yathā dvādaśāhena yajeta ityatra trivṛdādayaḥ
śabdāḥ stotravacanā api ahardvāreṇa sambadhyante evamatrāpi pañcāśataḥ trivṛtaḥ saṃvatsarā
iti yāvadbhiḥ ahobhiḥ trivṛdbhiḥ pūryate tāvatā trivṛtāmahnā trivṛtā iti
vyapadeśo bhavatīti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate
 | asyārthaḥ-dvādaśāhena yajetetyatrāṃdhi-
kāravākye ahaśśabdaśravaṇāt utpattau trivṛdādayaḥ śabdāḥ ahānyevopalakṣayanti
tathāpi adhikāravākye saṃvatsara śravaṇāt utpattau trivṛdādayaḥ śabdāḥ yāvadbhirahobhiḥ
saṃvatsarāṇāṃ pañcānāṃ pañcāśatā pūraṇaṃ bhavati tāvatsu vartate | teṣāmapi stotra-
yogitvāt | ata eva cotpattāvapi saṃvatsara1śabdasāmānādhikaraṇāt trivṛdādi-
śabdānām | ataḥ sahasrasaṃvatsarayoginyadhikāre sthite sahasrasaṃvatsaraśravaṇāt nūnaṃ
keciddīrghāyuṣo vidyanta iti gandharvādyupanyāsaḥ | nahyāśaṅkanīyamarthaṃ vedo vidadhyāditi
nyāyāt vidhibalādeva dīrghāyuṣaḥ kalpanīyāḥ | evaṃ rasāyanakulakalpārghatṛtīyaśatādyu-
panyāsaḥ | tulyatvādadṛṣṭakalpanāyāṃ yadā drādhiṣṭhaṃ adṛṣṭānāṃ kalpanam | tathā rasā-
yanasyāpi jyoga्jīvitatva[vakatva]kalpanaṃ tathā kulakalpanā ardhatṛtīyaśatapuruṣādhi-
kārakalpanā |

1. śabdādasā-mā

[p. 200]

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---trivṛtaḥ saṃvatsarā iti hi śrūyate na trivṛtāṃ
saṃvatsarā iti
 | yadi trivṛtāṃ saṃvatsarā iti śrūyate tataḥ trivṛcchabdena ahakena
saha sambandho’vakalpate | yadā tu trivṛta iti ahānyucyante tadā saṃvatsaraśabdena
mukhyasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ na bhavatīti trivṛcchabdena vā trivṛtāṃ samūho lakṣaṇīyaḥ |
saṃvatsaraśabdo vā gauṇyā vṛttyā ahassu varṇanīyaḥ iti cintāyāṃ vicāritatvāt
saṃvatsaraśabda evānirṇīyamānārthaḥ trivṛcchabdānuguṇyena lakṣaṇayā varṇanīyaḥ |

tathāhi---sāvanenāpi saṃvatsaro’sti ṣaṣṭyadhikaiḥ tribhiranhāṃ śataiḥ | tathā
sāṅkrānto’pi bhavati ādityasya dvādaśarāśibhedanena | tathā nākṣatro’pi bhavati
dvādaśabhiḥ gaṇaparivṛttibhiḥ indoḥ | tathā cāndro’pi tithigaṇanāyāṃ ṣaṣṭyadhikaiḥ
tribhireva śataiḥ sarvatra cātra saṃvatsaraśabdo mukhyaḥ | tenāsau na jñāyate kva prayujyata iti |
tena tadvaśena na padāntaravarṇanā, padāntaravaśenaiva tasya varṇanā | ityato na dvādaśā-
havadetadupapadyata iti saṃvatsaraśabdo’trāhassu nāmadheyopapattyartho draṣṭavyaḥ | pañca pañcā-
śatastivṛta ityetāvadevātra vidhīyate atra tu saṃvatsaraśabdasya vihitānvayitvāt |
arthāntararūpe vṛttirdṛṣṭā | tasmāt siddho’dhikāro vinā kalpanayā sahastreṇānhāṃ
karmāṇi kartuṃ laukikānāmapi sāmarthyāditi sthitam |

iti ṛjuvimalāyāṃ pañcikāyāṃ
ṣaṣṭhādhyāye saptamaḥ pādaḥ

[p. 201]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasya aṣṭamaḥ pādaḥ

|| 1 || iṣṭipūrvatvādakratuśeṣo homaḥ saṃskṛteṣvagniṣu syādapūrvo
’pyādhānasya sarvaśeṣatvāt || 1 ||

atra bhāṣyakāreṇaivamuktam---pakṣāntaramapi vicārayiṣyatīti | yadi ca
pakṣāntaramasti kimiti nopanyasyate tatrābhiprāyamāha---iha pakṣāntarānupanyāse
mandayuktikaḥ pakṣaḥ nānyapakṣapratipakṣatayopanyasanamityabhiprāyaḥ |

atra bhāṣyakāraḥ evaṃ vicārayati---kiṃ pavamāneṣṭisaṃskṛteṣvagniṣu caturhotṛ-
homāḥ utāsaṃskṛteṣvapīti | tadasaṅgatamiti matvā samarthayamāna āha---caturhotṛṣu
homeṣu kimāhitāgneradhikāraḥ utānāhitāgneḥ athavā aviśeṣeṇeti cintyate |

karaṇacintā tu bhāṣyakāreṇaiva kṛttā | tatrānārabhyāivanīyavidhānāt āhitāgneriti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | sarvahomeṣu āhavanīyayoga ityāhavanīyavataḥ sāgneradhikāraḥ | kathaṃ
punaranāhitāgneriṣṭiriti vacanametasmin pakṣe ?
 ato vacanenānāhitāgneriṣṭirbhava-
tīti bhāvaḥ | uttaraṃ---yadagnisaṃyuktaṃ karma tanna bhavatītyabhiprāyaḥ |
 japānāmeṣa vādaḥ
anahitāgnereṣavā iṣṭiriti na homasya | eṣa vā anāhitāgneḥ kriyā yaduteṣṭiriti |
iṣṭiśabdo’niṣṭiṣu gauṇaḥ praśaṃsārthaḥ | aviśeṣāśaṅkā tarhi kathaṃ ?
 uttaraṃ---
āhitāgneradhikāro na śakyate vārayituṃ avaiguṇyāt sarvahomānāṃ
 na tasya
kiñcidvaiguṇyaṃ kleśāsādhyatvena tāvadadhikriyate | anāhitāgnestu vacanaṃ
bhaviṣyati iti |

pitṛyajñavadapyanāhitāgninā kārya iti |
 yathā tena dvayorapyadhikāro
bhavatviti yuktā śaṅkā |

rāddhāntastu yata eva cāviśeṣeṇa vidhānamāhavanīyasya ata
evādhikāriviśeṣaṇamāskanditumarhati |
 viniyogaviśeṣamiva pade juhoti vartmani
juhoti iti yathā | yadi hi caturhotṛhomānevādhikṛtyāhavanīyo vihitaḥ syāt
[syāt] āhitāgneradhikāraḥ | yatastu sāmānyenāhavanīyo vihitaḥ tato hi na

[p. 202]

śaknotyanāhitāgnervācanikamadhikāramāskandituṃ pade juhotīti viniyogāntaramiva |
tena|[nā]hitāgnervācaniko’dhikāro yuktaḥ | eṣā vā anāhitāgneriṣṭiḥ iti vacanāt |

atra ceyaṃ vacanavyaktiḥ---yaiścaturhetṛhomarūpeṣṭiḥ eṣā vā’nāhitāgneriti |
yaduktaṃ āhitāgneravaiguṇyādadhikāro nivartayituṃ na śakyate anāhitāgnayarthaṃ tu
vacanamiti | atra brūmaḥ | uktaṃ ca nādhikāraviśeṣaṃ sādhāraṇavidhirāskandatīti |
viśeṣeṇa cātra anāhitāgnerayamadhikāraḥ | tatra cāhavanīye juhotīti sāmānyavidhirnā-
vataratītyuktam | tasmāt vācaniko’yamadhikāro’nāhitāgneḥ | ato yatra cobhayasaṃyogaḥ
tatraivobhayoradhikāraḥ yathā piṇḍapitṛyajñe | tatra hi a[pya]nāhitāgninā kāryamiti
apiśabdenānvācayaḥ kriyate | atra tu sa nāsti | tasmādanāhitāgnerevaiṣo’dhikāra
iti siddham |

|| 2 || upanayannādadhīta homasaṃyogāt || 11 ||

iha māṇavakahomāścintyante, nācāryasya1, ācāryasyāhavanīyasaṃbhavāt |
siddhāntābhāvaprasaṅgācca | māṇavakakartṛkāśca māṇavakahomā yeṣvācāryasyādhikāro na
śrūyate | iha cintyate---uktamādhastye’dhikaraṇe caturhotṛṣvanāhitāgneradhikāraśravaṇāt
āhavanīyavidhireva bādhita iti | iha punaḥ anāhitāgniśabdābhāvāt āhavanīyasya
ca sarvahomārthatvāt āhitāgnermāṇavakasya svakarmaṇyadhikāra iti tatsiddhayarthamādhānaṃ
kariṣyati | nanu cādhītavedasya gurukulānnivṛttasya kṛtadāraparigrahasyādhānamucyate
tatkathamanadhītavedasyādhānaṃ ? atrocyate | kramamātraṃ hi tat vākyāccāhavanīyasaṃyogaḥ
āhavanīye juhotīti | tenāhavanīyānugrahāt kramo bādhiṣyate | tadāha---
vākyāṅgabhūtaṃ kramamapi bādhata iti punaḥ kramamātram |
 nanu ca saha patnyā
ādhānaṃ śrūyate | asati sahabhāve kathaṃ taducyate | na cākṛtadārasya patnī sambhavati |
ucyate | kriyatāṃ dārasaṅgrahaḥ sahādhānasidhyartham | nanu kathamanivṛttau gurukulāt
dārān kuryāt |
 gurukulanivṛttyantaraṃ dārakriyāvihitā yataḥ | pariharati---
uṃkta kramamātraṃ taditi |
 tasmādāhitāgneradhikāraḥ aiśvaryalakṣaṇaḥ |

1. nācāryatā - mā

[p. 203]

atrocyate | nāyaṃ pakṣaḥ sambhavati | yasmānna dāropayamanamādhānaṃ prayuṅkte |
yadi hyādhānena dāropayamanaṃ prayujyate tadā sahabhāvopapattaye syādapyupayananam | na ca
tadasti rāgasyaiva prayoktṛtvāt kṛtadāragrahaṇasya sambhavāt | atra codayati---kena
voktaṃ prayujyata iti | rāgata eva tvasau kṛtadāra ityuktamasmābhiḥ |
 krama-
syānaṅgatvāt vākyācca durbalatvādityanayā yuktyā | pariharati---satyamāha bhavān |
kintvatrāsti kiñcidvaktavyam |
 upanayanottarakālaṃ hi vaidike’dhikāra iti
pratipāditam, [na]prāgupanayanāt | tadā dārvihomikācāryāṇāṃ vidhaya ityuktam |
tena māṇavakasya vaidiko’dhikāro nāsti | na cānadhikṛtadāraniyamassaṃbhavati | na cā---
kṛtadārasyādhānaṃ ityanāhitāgnerevādhikāraḥ | nanu cāpatyānyārtheṣu dāreṣu niyamaḥ
dharmārthamiti na prāpnuyuḥ | tatra niyamābhāvāt |
 pariharati---kṛtadārāṇāṃ
hi taddharmaniyamaḥ | na hi dharmārtho dārasaṅgrahaḥ | rāgaprayukto dārasaṅgrahaḥ

na dharmaprayuktaḥ | tena dāraniyamaṃ vinā dārasaṅgraho nāsti | tasmātsiddhamanāhi-
tāgneḥ prāgaupanāyaniko vidhiriti |

nanu ca prāgupanayanādadhikāra eva nāstīti tvayaivoktam |
 tena
māṇavakasya homa eva na sambhavatīti kuto’yaṃ vicāraḥ ? uttaraṃ---bāḍhamadhikāro
nāstīti na punaḥ kāryato’pi nāstītyucyate
 svādhyāyādhyayanavadaiśvaryapakṣo’sti |
niyojyalakṣaṇastu nāstītyarthaḥ | kārye cādhāne’nadhikārādanāhitāgneradhikāra
ityuktam | ekatvānniyamasya na punaḥ dārasaṅgraha iti | satyamevam
dvitīyo dārasaṅgraho na syāt yadi tu vacanaṃ na syāt |
 punarādhānavadasti
tu vacanam dharmaprajāsampanne dāre nānyāṃ kurvīta anyatarābhāve kuryāditi vacanasyāpāyo
naivāstīti sthitiḥ | tena vacanāt dvirdārasaṅgraho na viruddhaḥ | darśanamuktam |
piṇḍapitṛyajñavaditi ca parihṛtam |

|| 3 || sthapatīṣṭiḥ prayājavadagnayādheyaṃ prayojayet
tādarthyāccāpavṛjyeta || 20 ||

atrāpi (vi)nā’’havanīyasya vidhiḥ śakyate bādhitumiti pūrvapakṣa-
vādī manyate
 yasmānnātra māṇakasyeva ādhāne’nadhikāraḥ śakyate vaktum | ādhāne
sahabhāvadarśanāt (na)prāptireva prayājādiṣviva darśapūrṇamāsaprakṛtitvāt asya karmaṇasta-

[p. 204]

dvidhānenāhavanīyasya prāpteḥ | tanmukhena cādhānākṣepādāhavanīyasyopāyatvāt | tasmādā-
hitāgnerevādhikāro niṣādasyeti | ucyate | nādhāne tāvadadhikāritvaṃ paśyāmaḥ
vidhānābhāvāt |

nanu darśapūrṇamāsaprakṛtitvenādhānasya prāptiruktā |
 pariharati---satyaṃ,
agnīnāṃ tu sā prāptiḥ teṣāmaṅgatvāt | nanu ca anāhitasya tasyānaṅgatvāt
agnidvārā bhaviṣyatyādhānasya prāptiḥ |
 kratvākṣepakaṃ pariharati---prakṛtāvapi
naivādhānamagnayarthamadhikārakaṃ, kiṃ tarhi ? viniyogataḥ tadagnayarthaṃ
, na
kratvadhikārākṣiptamādhānaṃ prakṛtau | kiṃ tarhi ? viniyogena hi krameṇa tadagnayarthamiti |
na cādhikārādṛte viniyogo’tra labhyata iti | adhikāramapāsya viniyogo’tra
nāsti | kevalamadhikāramātraṃ nākṣipatyādhānamityasaṃskṛteṣvevāgniṣu sthitamidaṃ karma |

nanu ca viniyukteṣvagniṣu adhikāra iti tanmukhenāpyadhikārasya
prāptiḥ | satyam | na(nu)tu punaradhikṛtasyādhāne viniyogānuṣṭhānam |

ādhānenāgnayupādānam | anadhikṛtaścāyaṃ niṣāda ityuktam | tena ādhānenāgnirna
siddhayati niṣādasyetyagnilopena karma kartavyam |

atrāyaṃ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ---

yadyapyanaṅgatayā ādhānamatideśena na prāpyate tathāpyatidiṣṭāhavanīyasiddhayarthaṃ
niṣādasyākṣepo bhaviṣyati |

rāddhāntastu alaukikatvādāhavanīyasya niṣpatternākṣepakam | ato yatkartṛka-
mādhānamāhavanīyādyutpādakaṃ viniyogato’vagataṃ tatkartṛkenaivāhavanīyotpattiḥ nānyena,
mi/?/ṣādakartṛkasyotpādakatvenā1vijñānānnākṣepaḥ sambhavati |

|| 4 || avakīrṇipaśuśca tadvadādhānasyāprāptakālatvāt || 22 ||

abakīṇiṃpaśau kaṃ viśeṣaṃ paśyati āśaṅkāhetubhūtaṃ tasyāpi dārābhāvāt
na sahādhānamupapadyate |
 uttaraṃ---ayamabhiprāyaḥ---tasya dārāḥ saiva bhavatu
niyamātikramāt yayā sahāvakīrṇīṃ |
 dārā iti bahuvacanāt tadbhavati ityeka
1. tvena vi-mā

[p. 205]

vacanāt karma saiveti sambandhāt | tasmāt satsu dāreṣu āhavanīyasambandho naiva
bādhituṃ śakyata iti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |

rāddhāntavādī tu atikrame nirharaṇāyaitatprāyaścittaṃ niyamānusamba-
ndhāditi tādṛśasyādhikārādidamapi pūrvādhikaraṇavad‌draṣṭavyam |
 avakīrṇipaśurhi
avakīrṇinā kṛtadoṣanirharaṇena brahmacaryamevānusandhātuṃ ghaṭayituṃ vihitaṃ prāyaścittatayā |
nivṛttasya ca pāpāt prāyaścittam | tena tāṃ parityajyaivāvakīrṇipaśuḥ kartavyaḥ | tena
tādṛśasyaiva brahmacāriṇa eva tyaktastrīkasyaivādhikārādidamapi māṇavakahomādhikaraṇena
tulyam |

|| 5 || udagayanapūrvapakṣāhaḥ puṇyāheṣu daivāni smṛtirūpā-
nyārthadarśanāt || 23 ||

kathaṃ punaratra smṛtivacane sati pūrvapakṣāśaṅkā
 upanayanādiṣu saṃvatsarādi-
kālaniyamaśravaṇāt | aṣṭavarṣasyārthaḥ aṣṭavarṣaṃ brāhmaṇamupanayīteti śrūyate yasya pūrṇā aṣṭau
varṣāḥ so’ṣṭavarṣaḥ tena niṣekakakālādārabhya pūrve[rṇe]ṣvaṣṭasu varṣeṣu upanayanamiti noda-
gayanādismṛterādaraḥ sambhavatīti |

rāddhāntastu vartamāneṣvevāṣṭasu varṣeṣu sa niyamo nātivṛtteṣvityavirodhaḥ |

yadi hi smṛtiḥ śrutiviruddhā syāt bādhyetāpi smṛtiḥ | na cāsti virodhaḥ |
śrutāvapi vartamānepyaṣṭavarṣe upanayanaṃ śrūyate | tasyodagayanādiśrutyā saha virodho
nāsti | sa hyaṣṭavarṣa ucyate |
 uttaraṃ---aṣṭamo hi tasya varṣo’sti yadi paripūrṇaḥ
tena bhaviṣyatyevāṣṭavarṣaḥ | darśanamuktam |

|| 7 || yācñākrayaṇamavidyamāne lokavat || 26 ||

nopāttadravyasaṃbhave satyapyanyopādānaṃ yuktaṃ, adṛṣṭakalpanāprasaṅgāt
tadabhāva eva 1krayo vidhīyate bhṛtivananaṃ ceti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
 asyārthaḥ-dravyo-
pādānārthaṃ hi yācñādi vihitam | asati dravye dravyopādānaṃ vihitaṃ kartavyaṃ na
satyeva | tasmāt prayojanālocanayā dravyābhāva evāyaṃ vidhiriti |

1. tayorvi-mā

[p. 206]

rāddhāntastu---satyamevaṃ yadi puruṣārthatvena vidhānaṃ syāt | kratvartha
evāyaṃ vidhiḥ | naca kratorupāttaṃ nāma kiñcidasti ityasyaiva dṛṣṭārthatvam |

asyārthaḥ---kratvarthatvena yācñāvidhānaṃ kratuprakaraṇāmnānāt | na ca puruṣārthatvena prāk
kratvarthaṃ dravyamasti puruṣārthayācñayaiva hi kratvarthaṃ kriyate | tasmāt vidyamānasyāpi
yācñayaiva kratvarthaṃ dravyaṃ bhavati nānyathā, tenāsyaiva kratvarthasya sato dṛṣṭārthatvam |

nanu yadi dṛṣṭārthatvaṃ tadā dṛṣṭasyānyena sambhave nityaṃ tarhi yācñā-
derupasaṃhāro na bhavati |
 atrottaraṃ---na caitāvatā dṛṣṭārthatvenānupasaṃhāraḥ śakyate
vaktuṃ, sarvasya yugapadvidhānāt | aikaśabdyāditi vakṣyati | tasmānni-
tyavatkartavyam |
 asyārthaḥ-aikaśabdyāddhetoḥ sarvasya vidhiniṣpattyarthatvena yugapadvi-
dhānam | tena siddhe’pi dṛṣṭe’rthe vidhyarthasya yathācoditāṅgasādhyatvāt nityamupādānaṃ
kartavyam | tenāyaṃ yācñādividhiḥ vidhyarthaniṣpattyarthatvena yācñādivacanāt aprāpta-
vidhirevāstu | niyamastu prayojanamātram | asmin vidhau sati yācñādereva niyo-
gārthasiddhihetutvenābhyanujñānāt nityamupādānam | asati tu tasmin kriyāsaṃbandha-
sidhyarthamanyadupādīyeteti niyamaḥ prayojanaṃ vidheḥ |

|| 8 || tathā bhakṣapraiṣācchādanasaṃjñaptahomadveṣam || 28 ||

kaḥpunaratra viśeṣaḥ ?
 uttaraṃ-ayamasti viśeṣaḥ | puruṣārthadvāreṇānu-
praveśāt | saṃjñapanasya hṛdayādyutpattyarthatvaṃ manvānasya bhavati kācidāśaṅkā |

asyārthaḥ-kratvarthā api bhakṣādayaḥ puruṣārthadvāreṇa kratāvanupraviśantīti yatra paraṃ
puruṣārthopāyo nāsti tatraivaiṣāmaṅgabhāva iti bhakṣādiṣu tāvadāśaṅkā | saṃjñapanahome
tvevam---na saṃjñapanāṅgaṃ homo’vakalpate | prāyaścittasthānīyo hi sa śrūyate mantravarṇa-
śravaṇāt | pratyuta sādgauṇyāyattatvāditi |

rāddhantastu---adhikāravidhinaikavākyatvāt prakaraṇavidhīnāṃ pūrva-
vadanuṣṭhānam |
 asyārthaḥ---prakaraṇe hi vidhīyamānaḥ śrūyamāṇaḥ adhikāravidhyarthaḥ
adhikāravidhiśca tatsambandhaḥ pratīyamānaḥ taireva sadbhirniścīyate | atastadanuguṇadvārā
kalpanīyatve(na) satyapi puruṣārthasiddhiḥ tena bhakṣādijanyapuruṣārthadvāreṇaiva vidhiriti

[p. 207]

parikalpyate tathā saṃjñaptahomo’pi saṃjñapanamātranimittataḥ, na vaiguṇyapratisamādhānārthaḥ |
mantravarṇastu vidhyānuguṇyenaiva varṇanīya iti bhakṣāṇāmapi nityamanuṣṭhānam |

|| 9 || anarthakaṃ tvanityaṃ syāt || 29 ||

atrāpi tarhi adhikāravidhinaikavākyatvāt nityamanuṣṭhānaṃ tena
vinā vidhyarthāsiddheḥ |
 na cedaṃ vācyaṃ anuṣṭhīyamānamidaṃ prayojanaśūnyamiti
nānuṣṭheyamiti | yasmāt yaḥ sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasamarthaḥ so’pi kāmī bhaviṣyati | na hyanena
yatraiva prayojanamasya tasyaivādhikāraḥ |

rāddhāntamāha-ucyate | svārthaparityāgenaiva hi vidhānameva na pratīyate
dvāraniyamasyāpekṣitatvāt | na cāvihitasyānuṣṭhānaṃ yuktam |
 tasmādartha-
śūnyamevaṃvidhaṃ ananuṣṭhīyamānamapi nādhikāraṃ vihanti iti siddhamadhikāravidhirna vihanti |
vināpi tena(āpi) adhikāravidhissidhyatyeva | nityavattasyāviniyuktatvāt | sati
dvāre tasya viniyogaḥ nāsatyapi | yaccedamuktaṃ - yatra puruṣe dvāraṃ na sambhavati
tasyādhikāro mābhūditi tadayuktam | yathāviniyogamadhikāra ityuktam |

asya ca parārthasyānityadvārakatvāt anityamevārthāt viniyoga iti na śakyate
tadvaśenādhikāro nivartayitumiti |

|| 10 || paśucodanāyāmaniyamo’viśeṣāt || 30 ||

lakṣaṇasaṅgatimāha---idamatra cintyate - kiṃ sāmānyena paśumato’dhikāraḥ
uta chāgavata eveti |
 nanu viniyogamukhenāpi eṣā cintopapadyata iti kimityatraiva
cintyate ? tatrāha---viniyoge punaścintyamāne’viśeṣāt viniyogadvayasiddherna
śakyate viśeṣo’vadhārayitumityadhikāra eva cintyate |
 asyārthaḥ - na
viniyoge ghaṭate tathāmantrasyāpi chāgābhidhāyitayā | adhikāre tu parasparavirodhād‌dvaya-
mupasaṃhartuṃ na śaknoti | virodhe sati śakyate paśuviśeṣo mantrabalenāvadhārayitumityatraiva
cintā yuktā | tatra pūrvapakṣavādino’bhipāyaḥ---sāmānyaśabdo’yaṃ paśuśabdaḥ
gavādiśabdavat | tena paśvākṛtimātrasya viniyogāt meṣādiṣvapi
jātyaviśeṣāt yaḥkaścitpaśurupādātavyaḥ |
 nanu mantravarṇena viśeṣe’vasthāna-
mityatrāha---kvacicca viśeṣamantrasya arthavattvamupapannameveti na sāmānyaṃ
viśeṣe’vasthāpayitumarhati | yogādvā
---gamanayogādvā, varṇasāmānyādvā, akṣara-

[p. 208]

sāmānyādvā mantrasyaiva sāmānyārthatā yuktā | pramāṇāntaravaśena hi mantrasyārtho’vatiṣṭhate
ityuktam | viniyuktadvārasmārakatvāt mantrāṇāṃ yathāviniyogaṃt vaṇenā yuktaiva | tasmā
paśumato’dhikāraḥ |

atrocyate | syādetadevaṃ yadyayaṃ paśuśabdaḥ sāmānyavacanaḥ syāt |
kiṃ tarhi aupādhiko’yaṃ sarvapaśūnāṃ pucchavattvopādhikaḥ
 śabdaśabda iva śrotra-
grāhyatvopādhikaḥ sarvaśabdānām |

kasmādevaṃ bhavatītyatrāha---pratyabhijñānābhāvāt
 chāge hastini ca (na)pratyabhi-
jñānena sāmānyaṃ tāvannāsti tadabhāva eva śabdapravṛttimaupādhikīṃ darśayati | sa ca
vāladhirupādhiniyamaḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ pratyabhijñā nāstītyatrāha---yathā ca pratyabhijñā
nāsti evaṃ prathamapāda eva darśitam
 pūrvākārāvamarśābhāvāditi | yasmādayamaupādhikaḥ
sarvapaśūnāṃ pucchavattvopādhikaḥ śabdo vyaktivacanḥ | ato na viśeṣamantropasthāpi-
tamatikrāmati | tatraiva pravartate prayogaikyāt | ekādhikāravidhiparigraho mantrasya,
parigṛhītatbāt | tena ca viśeṣasya sannidhāpitatvāt |

abhyupagamyāpi sāmānyavacanatāṃ brūmaḥ | viśeṣaniṣṭhataiva sāmānyā(pi)nāṃ
yuktā nityavanmantrāmnānasāmarthyāt na mantrasya pākṣikatvaṃ yuktam | na ca sāmānyaṃ
viśeṣe’vasthāpyamānaṃ śabdārthatāṃ jahāti | yadyapi viniyogena virodhaḥ | tathāpi tasya
kāryānuguṇyāt viśeṣāvasthitireva nyāyyā | anuṣṭhānānuguṇo hi viniyogaḥ anuṣṭhā-
nārthatvāt anuṣṭhānaṃ ca mantrānvitamiti tadbaśena viśeṣāvasthāpanameva nyāyyam |
na ca [rūḍhau]sambhavantyāṃ yaugikatvaṃ sambhavatītyuktam | tasmāt suṣṭhūcyate chāgo
vā mantravarṇāt iti | śeṣamuktam |

iti śrīśālikanāthamiśraviracitāyāṃ ṛjuvimalāpañcikāyāṃ
ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasyāṣṭamaḥ pādaḥ
adhyāyaśca samāptaḥ
