ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

|| *navamādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || yajñakarma pradhānaṃ taddhi codanābhūtaṃ tasyaṃ dravyeṣu
saṃskārastatprayuktastadarthatvāt || 1 ||

viśiṣṭakāryāvagamyatvādūhasya viśeṣātideśalakṣaṇaṃ hetuḥ |
 asyārthaḥ---
vikṛtau prākṛtānāṃ padārthānāmatideśena prāptau satyāṃ prākṛtāt kāryāt viśiṣṭaṃ
kāryamavagamyate | tatpramāṇakaścohaḥ | tena bhavati viśeṣātideśa ūhasya hetuḥ |
atreyaṃ prakriyā-ūhyata ityanayā vyutpattyā vikṛtaprayoga ūhaśabdenocyate | sa ca
prākṛtāt kāryādanyasmin kārye’vagate’vagamyate | tathāhi---prakṛtāvavaghātādī-
nāmapūrvasādhanabhūtaviśeṣā1vrīhayo yavāśca kāryatvena pratipannāḥ | vikṛtau nīvāra-
dravyavatyāṃ apūrvasādhanabhāvāviśeṣāt atideśena prāptānāṃ nīvārā eva kāryatvenā-
vasīyante | tataśca teṣvavaghātādayaḥ kriyamāṇā vikṛtā bhavanti | kārakabhedena
kriyāvikārasyāparihāryatvāt |

kathaṃ punarnīvārāṇāṃ kāryatvāvagamaḥ ? upadeśastāvada2pūrvaviśeṣagṛhītadharmāṇāṃ-
viśiṣṭāpūrvasādhanaviṣaya eva | nacā’tra pramāṇāntaramapi prakramate | ucyate | śābdaṃ
pramāṇaṃ dvivighaṃ, upadeśajaṃ kāryajaṃ ca |

kaḥpunarupadeśaḥ ? kiṃ punaḥ kāryaṃm ? ucyate | upadeśo nāma granthasandarbhasya
pāthaḥ | kāryaṃ tu dvārabhūtam karaṇopakārādi | tatra granthasandarbhajaṃ darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ
yajeta svargakāmaḥ vrīhibhiryajeta yavairyajeta vrīhīnavahanti ityādiparyālocanayā’pūrva-
prayuktatāṃ sarvadharmāṃṇāmanusandadhatāṃ vrīhīṇāṃ yavānāṃ cāpūrvaviśeṣasādhanatvaṃ tulyamākalayatāṃ
vrīhiyavakāryakamavaghātādīti jñānamupajāyate | taccedamupadeśajam | śābdaṃ punaśca
* saptamāṣṭamādhyāyayoḥ ṛjuvimalāmātṛkā kutrāpi nopalabdhā ataḥ navamādhyāyaḥ
mudrāpyate---
1. R viśeṣabhūtā
2. P pūrvaviśeṣagṛhītadharmāṇāṃ

[p. 210]

1vikṛtyapūrvāntare dravyāntaravati codakaprāpteṣvaghātādiṣu iyaṃ jijñāsopajāyate---kathamete
prayoktavyā iti | tadarthaṃ ca kīdṛśaṃ kāryaṃ upadeśena prayujyata ityetannirūpyate |

kiṃ darśapūrṇamāsāpūrvasādhanaviśeṣaḥ kāryatayā pratīyate, uta yasyāpūrvasyā-
vaghāto’ṅgaṃ tadīyasādhanaviśeṣa iti | tatra yadi darśapūrṇamāsāpūrvasādhanatā vivakṣitā
tadā nīvārāṇāṃ tadrūpatābhāvāt teṣāṃ kāryatā na syāt2 | atha tu yasyāpūrvasyāva-
ghāto’ṅgaṃ tadīyasādhanatāmātraṃ vivakṣitaṃ tadā’sti tadrūpatā nīvārāṇāmiti teṣāmapi
kāryatā vrīhīnavahantītyādibhireva pratīyate | tatra viśeṣāpūrvāṅgabhāvajñānamidaṃ
nāntarīyakaṃ darśapūrṇamāsāpūrvasādhanaviśeṣaḥ kāryabhūta iti | apūrvaviśeṣasambandhino
hi dharmasya tadīyaṃ kāryaṃ bhavitumarhati apūrvaidamarthyāvirodhāditi | nāntarīyakamapūrva-
viśeṣasādhanatvamavivakṣitat3 | yadīyo’vaghātastatsādhanameva kāryamiti codanārthaḥ |
ataśca vrīhīnavahantītyanayaiva codanayā nīvārāṇāmapi kāryatvamavagamyate | tadidaṃ
kāryanirūpaṇajñānamupajāyata iti kāryajaṃ śābdam | tena nāpramāṇatvaṃ nāśābdatvaṃ
nopadeśatvamiti svayameva bhagavatā vivaraṇakāreṇoditamityanavadyam | ślokamapyudāharanti---

prākṛtasthānapatitapadārthāntarakāryataḥ |

ūhaḥ prayogo vikṛta ūhyamānatayoditaḥ || iti

ākṣepyaṃ bhāṣyamanubhāṣate---trividhaścoha iti |
 ākṣipati---kimidaṃ
traividhyaṃ ?
 vikṛtaprayogātmakatā mantrasāmasaṃskāraviṣaye tulyeti bhāvaḥ | samādhatte-
ucyate---kāryabhedaṃ svasthāne vakṣyāmaḥ | tadbhedāyohyamapi prayogarūpaṃ
bhidyata iti tatraiva vakṣyāmaḥ |
 kāryapramāṇako hi prayogavikāraḥ sa pramāṇa-
bhūtakāryatraividhyāt trividho bhavati |

tathāhi---avaghātādīnāṃ taṇḍulaprakṛtidravyaṃ kāryam, mantrāṇāṃ samaveto’rthaḥ,
sāmnāmakṣarāṇīti | tadvaśena ca trayāṇāmapi vikāro bhavatīti traividhyābhidhānaṃ bhāṣya-
kārasya prayājādiviṣaye ūho na bhavatīti darśayitum | na hi teṣāṃ kaścitprayoga-
vikāro’sti | anyadapyupoddhātādinetyanena bhāṣyamanubhāṣitam | ya(the)didamādyamadhi-
1. p. vikṛtāvapū
2. nāsti
3. mavihitam

[p. 211]

karaṇaṃ upoddhātena prasaktānuprasa(ktyā)ktaṃ īṣṭe bhuṅkta iti anūhasiddhayartha,
devatāprādhānyaṃ prasaktaṃ tatsiddharthamīśitṛtvaṃ, bhoktṛtvaṃ ca (tva)devatāyā anuprasaktamiti |

atra bhāṣyakāreṇāgnihotrāditrayamudāharaṇaṃ gṛhītam | tatra tātparyaṃ pṛcchati-
kiṃ kāraṇa ? kena punaḥ kāraṇenāgnihotrādaya evodāharaṇam ? yāvatā sarvāṇi
karmāṇi dharmavantyudāhāryāṇīti
 |

uttaraṃ---kevalopadeśārthe hi pratiprakaraṇaṃ niyamātmake nirūpite’tideśaḥ
pravartate
 | anyathā hi sarvatraivopadiṣṭatvāt dharmāṇāṃ sādhāraṇatā syāt, nātideśaḥ
syāt | atideśāvagatadharmaviṣayaścoha ityatideśasiddhayarthametānyevodāharaṇāni
nānyat | atra pauruktyaṃ saptamādyena saha paricodyottaravivakṣayaivedamapūrvaprayuktatvamucyata
ityarthaśaḥ parihāro dattaḥ | yaḥpunarayaṃ dvitīyaḥ parihāraḥ etena ceha prayojanaṃ ūhe
vivakṣite iti asāvayuktaḥ yasmāt tatrāpi saptamādye apūrvaprayuktatayaiva dharmāṇāṃ
pratiprakaraṇaṃ niyamaḥ siddhayati nānyathā | tenaitena cohaprayojanamityuktam | sapta-
mādye’pyasya prayojanattvāt | samādhatte---nanu bhāṣye ayajiprayuktatve’pi sati
na pratiprakaraṇaṃ niyamaḥ siddhayatītyanyathāpi niyamaṃ darśayati
 | tenāpūrva-
prayuktatā ekāntataḥ 1tatra na vyutpādyā | punarākṣipati---yadeva bhāṣyakāreṇoktaṃ
tadeva na vidmaḥ kimidamayajiprayuktatvaṃ nāma
 | yajeranyadayajiriti apūrvamevānya-
diti bhāvaḥ |

samādhatte---atrocyate | karaṇaprayuktatvaṃ ayajiprayuktatvamucyate
 |
yajeranyadayajiriti karaṇamucyate tena prayuktatvamayajiprayuktatvamucyate | 2nanu karaṇatā
kena(prayojakamata) [pramāṇena karaṇatvāvagamaḥ] atra āha---kāryakaraṇasambandho’hyatra
niyogalakṣaṇaḥ
 | sādhyabhūtena hi niyogena samabhivyāhāro’nyathā nopapadyate | yadi
niyogaḥ kāryabhūtaḥ karaṇabhūtaśca yajirna syāt iti kāryakaraṇatā niyogenāvasīyate |

kathaṃ punaḥ karaṇaprayuktatve sati pratiprakaraṇaṃ niyama ityata āha---karaṇa-
prayuktatve’pi ca niyogaviśeṣasambandha upapadyata eveti pratiprakaraṇaṃ niyama-
siddhiṃ manyate
 |

1. R tatrāpyūhyā
2. R kena pramāṇena prayojakatvaṃ ataḥ yajisvarūpamevāha-na kāraṇatā na ca tṛtīyāpi
vimaktirasti ya ākhyātapratyayasya na karaṇatāyāḥ smaryate |

[p. 212]

asyārthaḥ-yajiprayuktatve hi darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetyanenādhi-
kāravantaṃ yajimuddiśya samidho yajatītyādibhiḥ samidānīnāṃ vidhānamiti pratiprakaraṇaṃ
niyamo na syāt | karaṇaprayuktatve tu darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmityadhikāravākyāvagataṃ karaṇaṃ
sannidhimātreṇāpūrvamiva dharmāṇāṃ grāhakaṃ tena viśiṣṭameva karaṇaṃ grāhakamiti viśiṣṭāpūrva-
sambandhāt pratiprakaraṇaṃ niyamasiddhiḥ | tena apūrvaprayuktatve karaṇaprayuktatve ca prati-
prakaraṇaṃ niyamasiddheḥ saptamādye apūrvaprayuktatā ekāntena na vyutpādanīyā | atra-
tvadhikaraṇe saiva vyutpādyate tāṃ vinā ūhāsiddheḥ | karaṇaprayuktatvehi dharmāṇāṃ vrīhayo’pi
karaṇabhūtā dharmān prayuñjata iti karaṇabhūtavrīhirūpatābhāve nīvārāṇāmavadhātādyūho na
bhavet | apūrvaprayuktatve tu vrīhisvarūpasyāvivakṣitatvādapūrvasādhanaviśeṣa eva vivakṣita
iti siddhayatyūhaḥ tena saptamādye idaṃ cintyate kiṃ yajiprayuktā utāyajiprayuktā iti |
iha tu ayajiprayuktatvepi kiṃ karaṇaṃ prayojakamutāpūrvamiti cintyate---

tatra pūrvapakṣī karaṇameva svānugrahamapekṣata iti manyate | rāddhāntī tu
 karaṇa-
vat tadanugrahamapi kāryamevākāṅkṣati tasyaiva cikīrṣitatvāditi |

atha kasmādidamevāyajiprayuktatvaṃ tat saptamādye siddhāntatvena na
likhitam ?

uttaraṃ-asthāyitvādasyārthasya
 | sāmānyenāyajiprayuktatvamasthāyi | kintva-
pūrvaprayuktatvameva sthāyi | sthāyī ca pakṣaḥ siddhāntatvenopanyasanīyaḥ śiṣyāṇāṃ bhrama-
nivṛttyartham | ato’yamevāpūrvaprayuktatāpakṣaḥ sthāyī saṃvyavahārārthamatrānīta iti hetora-
pūrvaprayuktatvamuktam | yadyayajiprayuktatvamasthāyi kimarthaṃ saptamādye taccintyata ityupanyastaṃ
tadāha---asthāyinaḥ punarupanyāsaḥ kimarthaḥ ?
 uttaraṃ - punaruktiparihāraśca
 |
ca śabdastuśabdārthe | kimetāvadeva prayojanam ?
 uttaraṃ---anyadastīti
hantipiṣṭayadhikaraṇe vakṣyāmaḥ
 | tatra pūrvapakṣaupayikamidam | athavā anyāmeva
cintāṃ ityetadbhāṣyakāreṇa yajinimittatānirāsārthamuktam | tadayuktam---yaji-
nimittatve hyatideśo’pi na siddhayati na kevalamūhaḥ tasmādūhalakṣaṇa evāya-
mupanyāsa iti na śakyate vaktum
 | asyārthaḥ---darśapūrṇamāsayajernimittatve
sati tadabhāve vikṛtau codakaprāptānāmapi sarvadharmāṇāmevānanuṣṭhānam bhedanābhāva iva
bhedanahomasya | tataśca kimatideśeneti atideśābhāvo’pi |

[p. 213]

na kevalamūhābhāvaḥ tenohalakṣaṇa eva nedaṃ yuktaṃ cintayituṃ, atideśa-
lakṣaṇe’pi yuktā cintā | pariharati---ucyate | satyamāha bhavān atideśo’pi
na siddhayatīti | saptamādyena tvadhikaraṇena idamapaunaruktayam
 | asyārthaḥ---
na vayaṃ brūmaḥ ūhalakṣaṇa eveyaṃ cintopakāriṇīti | kintu punaruktatāparihāro-
payuktaḥ | apūrvaprayuktatve hi pratiprakaraṇaṃ niyamasiddhiḥ iti saptamādyena, iha tu yajerni-
mittatānirāsaḥ kṛtaḥ tenāpaunaruktyam | kiṃ punarasya parihārāntarasya prayojanaṃ ?

uttaraṃ---idameva vakṣyamāṇaṃ prayojanam
 | nimittāntare yajyantare prayuktasyāpi
codakaprāptasya dharmajātasya nimittābhāvādananuṣṭhānaṃ prāptaṃ tasyānanuṣṭhānasya vyudāso
vaktavya ityabhiprāyo varṇakāntaraṃ kurvataḥ so’yamananuṣṭhānavyudāsaḥ parihāraśca tenocyate-
tatreti vākyopanyāse | bhāṣyakāro varṇakakāyopanyāsānantaraṃ parihāradvayamekenaiva
varṇakena varṇitavān idānīṃ kiṃ yajiprayuktā utāpūrvaprayuktā iti cintyata ityevamādinā
bhāṣyeṇa |

atra bhāṣya eva yāgaṃ kuryāditi yajisvarūpaprayuktatoktā | evaṃ yāgena
kuryāditi karaṇaprayuktatoktā | tṛtīyaḥ prakāro varṇa/?/kāntareṇa varṇitaḥ evaṃ
vetyādinā | bhinnaga ityuktamityasyārtha ityetat | varṇakāntaraṃ bhāṣyakāreṇoktam
 |
bhāṣyoktā eva pūrvapakṣā iti yāvat |

|| 2 || saṃskāre yujyamānānāṃ tādarthyāt tatprayuktaṃ syāt || 2 ||

ऩnu coktamapūrvaṃ prayojakam | nimittatve tvādye prayojakatvaṃ varṇakadvaye,
nimittatvaṃ ca tṛtīye varṇake’pūrvasyaivoktam | keyaṃ cintā ? kiṃ hantipiṣi-
prayuktaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ utāpūrvaprayuktamiti | ucyate | pūrvapakṣo’bhyadhiko’tra
vidyate | kathaṃ ?
 uttaraṃ---apūrvaṃ hi sādhyatayā sambadhyamānaṃ dharmāṇāṃ
prayojakaṃ bhavatīti śakyate vaktum
 | asyārthaḥ - yadyapūrva sādhyatayā dharmaiḥ
saṃbaddhamavagamyate tadā taddharmāṇāṃ prayojakamiti śakyate vaktum | yathā ārādupa-
kārakeṣu teṣu prathamamadhikārāpūrvameva sādhyatayā’vagamyate nāvāntarāpūrvamiti tatra
tatroktam | iha punaḥ kriyānirvṛttireva kāryatvenāvagamyate prokṣitābhyāmiti tṛtīyā
nirdeśāt, kriyāpekṣatvāt karaṇabhāvasya | na ca pratīyamānāpi kriyāṃ prati sādhanatā
vivakṣitetyāha---tadaparityāgenaivaidamarthyamuktam
 | pratīyamānāvāntarakāryāpari-
tyāgena dharmāṇāmapūrvaṃ pratyaidamarthyamuktam “ye yatra śrūyante tatra bhavanti”
 ityatra |

[p. 214]

nanu yadi kriyāniṣpattirevaiṣāṃ kāryaṃ tadā apūrvāsaṃsparśitve sati ānarthakyaṃ
prāpnoti niyamasya kriyāyāḥ puruṣaiḥ kāryatvenāvagamyamānatvāt | kiṃ tayā niṣpāditayā ?
pariharati---evaṃ tarhi ānarthakyaparihārāyaivāpūrvamupayujyate | na punaḥ
sādhyatayā
 | asyārthaḥ - na niṣprayojanā kriyāniṣpattiḥ | tatsiddhāvapūrvaniṣpatteḥ |
tena prokṣaṇādīnāmapūrvasiddhirevārtho na punarapūrve sādhye[rvaṃ sādhyaṃ ?] yathā
dīkṣaṇīyāvāṅ‌niyamasya paramāpūrvaṃ prayojanam | na tu tadeva sādhyam | kasmādeva-
mityāha---hantipiṣyorapūrvasambandhitayaiva prokṣaṇavidhāne parihṛta tadānarthakya-
miti nāpūrvaṃ kāryatayā parigṛhyate
 | yau hantipiṣī apūrvaṃ pratisādhanabhūtau tayoridaṃ
prokṣaṇaṃ sādhanatvena grahītum [gṛhītam |]

yaduktaṃ hantipiṣṭayadhikaraṇopayogāya ayajiprayuktatvaṃ tadidamuktaṃ bhavati |
avahantiprayuktatvena, sādhanībhūtāvahantiprayuktatvena parihṛtaḥ | ānarthakyenāpūrva-
prayuktatā śakyate vaktum yata evāvāntarakāryameva dharmāṇāṃ kāryaṃ ata eva ye yatra
śrūyanta ityupapannaṃ bhavati | anyathā’[vaghātādeḥ a]pūrvaprayuktatve satyavāntarakāryasya
kāryadvayavirodhenāvivakṣitatve sati ārādupakārakatvena sarvatraiva āgneyāgnīṣomīyopāṃ-
śuyājeṣu syāt prayājādivat | yasmādavahantiprayuktā avahantidharmāḥ tasmādavahantau
nakhanikṛttanenohitavyāḥ | yo nāma mandadhīḥ yathāśrutagrāhī taṃ prati udāharaṇa-
vyāptimāha---sarvāṇyevaivaṃjātīyakāni pratīyamānāvāntarakāryāṇyudāharaṇāni
vrīhīnavahantītyevamādīni
 | so’yamaṅgasya vrīhyāderdharmaḥ sannapūrvasyopakārako,
nāpūrvadharmo’ṅganāmupakārakaḥ | na caivaṃbhūtaḥ śabdo vidyate yadapūrvaṃ hantipiṃṣibhyāṃ
kriyate tatprokṣaṇena kartavyamiti | yasmāt prokṣitābhyāmavahantīti śrūyate

kriyāniṣpattireva kāryaṃ śrūyate ityarthaḥ | na cāvahantirapi sākṣādapūrvasādhanaṃ yena
tadyuktamavahantiyuktamapūrveṇa saha sāvayavatvena saṃbadhyate | tasmāt kāryāntarānupraveśināṃ
nādhikārakāryeṇa prayuktiḥ sambhavatīti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

atrocyate | nādhikāraprayuktimantareṇopādānasiddhirdharmāṇām | na
vā’nupāttānāmanuṣṭhānam
 | asyārthaḥ-loke hi sarvasya śabdasyānuṣṭhānopādeyaparatā
dṛṣṭā | yadi vā’pūrvaprayuktatā dharmāṇāṃ na syāt tadā teṣāṃ niyogenopādānaṃ
anuṣṭhānākṣepo na syāt | anutpa(pa)nnānāṃ vā adhikārāpūrvānākṣiptānuṣṭhānānāṃ
anuṣṭhānaprayojanābhāvādanuṣṭhānaṃ na syāt |

[p. 215]

atrāyaṃ bhāvaḥ-apūrvasādhanatā avahantyādīnāmeva | sā ca vinā prokṣaṇā-
dibhirna siddhayatīti tadarthatayā prokṣaṇādyākṣepaḥ | tasmāt sarvaṃ dharmajātamapūrvārtham |

nanu caivaṃ sati viniyogavyavasthaiva na siddhayati[na ca avaha]ntyā-
dīnāmeva sāvāntarakāryasyāvivakṣāprasaṅgādityuktam
 | pariharati---kena
voktamavāntarakāryaṃ na vivakṣyata iti
 | punaścodayati-kathaṃ vā vivakṣāyā-
mavāntarakāryasya pradhānakāryasya prayojakatvam
 | pariharati---ucyate |
avāntarakāryaṃ tāvat kiṃ pradhānāpekṣitaṃ sat kāryaṃ bhavati utānapekṣitaṃ tatra
yadyapekṣitaṃ satkāryaṃ tadā svarūpeṇa kāryatvābhāvāt pradhānakāryameva prayojakaṃ,
athānapekṣitaṃ tadā’vāntarakāryameva prayojakam |

evaṃ saṃśayya nirṇayamāha---pradhānakāryeṇānapekṣāyāmavāntarakāryataiva na
syāt
 | tadavāntarakāryamucyate yat pradhānakāryānuguṇatayā kriyate yathā kāṣṭhānāṃ
pākaṃ kartuṃ pravṛttānāṃ jvalanam | na vā vācyamavāntarakāryatā mābhūditi | tanmātrasya
pradhānakāryatve’nuṣṭhānābhāvaprasaṅgāt | tadapekṣāyāṃ tu pradhānakāryāpekṣāyāṃ punastadarthatvena
pradhānakāryārthatvenāvagamāt | tadapāye pradhānakāryāpāye dharmasambandha eva na syāt |
tenāvāntarakāryepa vivakṣite pradhānakāryasya prayojakatvāvidhānāt avāntarakāryamapi
pratīyamānaṃ vivakṣitam | atha tu upadeśavyavasthaiveyaṃ viniyoge naiveyaṃ vyavasthā-ye yatra
śrūyanta iti na punaḥ prayuktivyavasthā vrīhyādīnāṃ prayuktyā vyavasthaiveyaṃ na siddhayatīti
siddhamapūrvaprayuktatvam |

yuktyantaraṃ vā’pūrvaprayuktatāyāmāha-niyamavidhīnāṃ pradhānānupraveśaścaturthe-
darśitaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ-yadyavaghātādīnāmapūrvaṃ sādhyaṃ syāt tadā vidhānamavakalpate
pramāṇāntareṇāpūrvasādhanatvāparijñānāt | vrīhyādisvarūpasambandhitve tu vidhānameva nāva-
tarati prāptatvāt | na ca niyamārthaṃ vidhānaṃ aśakyatvāt | vināpyavadhātaṃ taṇḍulādi-
nirvṛttidarśanāt apūrvānupraveśitve tu niyamavidhiravakalpate taṇḍulanirvṛttyarthaṃ dalanā-
dyākṣipedapūrvaṃ vihite tadevāpūrva sādhanamiti tadevākṣipyata iti | evaṃ tahi niyama-
vidhīnāmapūrvameva kāryaṃ syādityatrāha---tathābhūtopadeśena ca dvāraniyamastṛtīye
darśitaḥ
 | vrīhyādisambandhibhūtā dyupadeśenaiva dvārasyāpi niyamastṛtīye darśitaḥ | tena
dvārakāryamapyāśrayaṇīyam |

[p. 216]

upasaṃharati-ataḥ prokṣitābhyāmavahantītyatra avahantidvāramapūrvaṃ kuryā-
diti śāstrārthaḥ
 | tasmāt tatkāryāpanne anyatrāpi dharmāṇāmūhasiddhiriti |
aviruddhaśca vrīhīnavahantītyupadeśaḥ dvārasyāpi vivakṣitatvāditi sthitam |

evaṃ vā

ihedānīmapūrvārthatvātsarvadharmāṇāṃ paramāpūrvameva[rveṇaiva]prayuktaṃ dharmajāta-
miti pūrvapakṣavādī manyata iti sambandhaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---na laukikarūpārthatā
dharmāṇām vinā dharmaistasya sambhavāt | ato’pūrvārthatvāt dharmāṇāṃ paramāpūrvameva prayoja-
kam | nanvavāntarāpūrvamapyapūrvameva, tena tadapi prayojakaṃ syādityāśaṅkyāha---
sādhikāraṃ vā’pūrvaṃ paramārthataḥ
 | paramārthataḥ sādhikāramevāpūrvaṃ nānyat |
etaduktaṃ bhavati---avāntarāpūrvāṇi nāma na santyeva | kintu sādhikārameva kevala-
mapūrvamasti | kuta ityāha-tacchūnyānāṃ
 adhikāraśūnyānāṃ tadāpatteḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---
adhikāraśūnyānāṃ prayājādīnāmadhikārasannidhau śrutānāṃ tadrūpāpatteḥ | etaduktaṃ
bhavati - niradhikārebhyo yajibhyaḥ parato liṅādayaḥ śrutāḥ adhikārāpūrvamevānuvadantīti |

nanvevaṃ [adhikāra]śūnyānāmapi yāgā eva dīkṣaṇīyādayo dharmajātaṃ prayuñca-
tāmityatrāha---anāpannatadbhāvaiḥ svadharmaiḥ sambandhānna yāgāḥ svarūpeṇa dharmān
prayuñjate
 laukikatvāt dharmasambandhitvānupapatteḥ | (dūrotsāritatāṃ vāhana) nāvaghātā-
divat dvārakāryamatropalabhyate ārādupakāritvādeṣāṃ dīkṣaṇīyādyaṅgānām | tenā-
śaṅkāpi nāsti anyasya prayojakatvaṃ prati sākṣādapūrvasādhanatvādeṣāmiti | atrocyate
---satyamuktamanāpannādhikārāṇāṃ na dharmasambandha iti kintu kāryatvamavi-
śiṣṭamavā
ntaraniyogasyāpi | sādhyamāna evāsau tadāpattiṃ labhate adhikārasādhanatāṃ
labhate | kuta ityāha---na hi asādhyamānasya rūpamavaghātavallokataḥ siddham
 |
yathā laukikamavaghātādirūpaṃ tathā’vāntaraniyogasya rūpam tena tadapi sādhyaṃ vacana-
samadhigamyameva sādhyaṃ ca svabhāvataḥ setikartavyatākasya karaṇasya niṣpādyamiti vacanā-
davagamyate |

nanu avāntaraniyoga eva nāstītyatrāha---nadhikārāpattirbhedaṃ nirūṇaddhi
anubandhajam
 kṣaṇikānāṃ yāgānāṃ sādhanatvānupapatteḥ avāntarāpūrvāṇyaṅgīkriyante anu-
bandhabhedabhinnānīti(māṃ)tadāpattyāpi bhedo na viruddhayate | adhikāre tvabhedo

[p. 217]

varṇitaḥ
 | adhikārāvasthāyāṃ tvabhedaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati - ekamadhikārāpūrvaṃ sādhyam |
tatsādhanabhūtāni tu anubandhamedabhinnāni avāntarāpūrvāṇi yāvadanubandhaṃ bahūni
bhidyanta iti |

nanu bhavantvanubandhabhedabhinnāni apūrvāṇi tathāpi kathaṃ dharmāṇāṃ tadarthatetyāha---
anubandhajaṃ bhedamuddiśya dharmajātamavagamyata ityuktam
 | yāvatyā vācā kāmayīta
tāvatyā dīkṣaṇīyāyāmanubrūyāditi vāṅ‌niyamo dīkṣaṇīyāyāṃ tāvat sākṣātsambandhaḥ
śrūyate | dīkṣaṇīyāsambandhena ca tatpratyāsannatadapūrvasambandho’vagamyate | tenānu-
bandhabhinnāpūrvoddeśena vidhānāt tadarthatā dharmajātasya |

upasaṃharati---tasmādadhikārāpatteravāntarāpūrvasyādhikārārthatā
 | adhi-
kāraprayojanatā dharmāṇāṃ na niṣpādyatayā, avāntarāpūrvaṃ dharmaniṣpādyamiti siddhamavāntara-
niyogārthatvaṃ dharmāṇām | niyogāvagamyasādhyeṣu
 niyogena liṅgādinā’vagamyaṃ sādhyaṃ
avāntarāpūrvaṃ yeṣu vidyate dīkṣaṇīyādiṣvityarthaḥ | avāntarāpūrvābhāvāt avaghātā-
divat dīkṣaṇīyādiṣu paramāpūrvameva dharmāṇāṃ prayojakamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

avaghātādivat dravyādidvāreṇa adhikāraidamarthyānupapatteḥ kṣaṇikeṣu dīkṣaṇīyādiṣu
avāntarāpūrvāṇyaṅgīkaraṇīyāni | tāni cā’laukikatvāt śabdāvagamyaniṣpattīni |
dharmāṇāṃ vākyādibhirupanītānāṃ prayojanamuktam | uktepyarthe kasyacit mandadhiyo
vismṛtirmābhūditi bodhaparihāradvāreṇa uktamevārthaṃ smārayati---nanu ca yāgo’pi
lokata eva siddhaḥ kimidamavaghātādibhirvaiṣamyamiti codyam
 | parihārastu---
kāryaparyantatāmaṅgīkṛtyālaukikatvamuktam ārādupakārakāṇāṃ nābhidheyatayā
 | yāga-
śabdābhidheyatayā laukikatvam kintvavāntarāpūrvaparyantatveneti viśeṣo’vaghātādibhiḥ |

|| 3 || phaladevatayośca || 4 ||

kathaṃ punaratra saṃśayo yāvatā apūrvaprayuktataivātrāpi yukta
 | uttaraṃ---
mantrābhidhānasāmarthyācca taiḥ svarūpaprayuktataivoktā
 | asyārthaḥ---mantrastāvat
śrutyā agnyādisvarūpaṃ svargādisvarūpaṃ cāha | tena mantraliṅgena svarūpaprayuktatā
pratibhāti | codanātaśca lakṣaṇā | mantrasyādhikāracodanayā parigrahītatvāt tadanu-
sāreṇāpūrvasambandhisvarūpalakṣaṇā bhātītyevaṃ saṃśayaḥ | punarākṣipati---tadayuktam |
na codanādṛte’bhidhānaṃ prāpnoti karmasambandhitayā
 | asyārthaḥ - yadi mantro’tra

[p. 218]

codanayā parigṛhyate tadā karmasambandhitayā mantreṇārthābhighānaṃ na prāpnoti | na
vā karmasambandhyabhidhānamatra cintyate, karmasambandhi vā’bhidhānaṃ codanānuguṇamiti
lakṣaṇaiva yuktā | tasmādayuktamevādhikaraṇamidam | karmasambandhitve hyapūrvārthatvamuktaṃ
sarvadharmāṇāmiti ihāpi tadeva yuktam | ato devatādvārakaṃ kāryābhidhānamiti
sthitameva |

atrocyate---astyatra saṃśayahetuḥ | tathāhi-phalaṃ tāvanna karmārtha-
tayā codanāvākyepyavagamyate phalasya puruṣārthatvenāvagamāt
 | tathāpi
kimityāha---yathācoditaṃ ca mantreṇābhidhānamiti svarūpābhidhānāśaṅkā
 |
yatkhalu yathābhūtaṃ coditaṃ tat tathābhūtameva mantareṇābhidhātavyam | phalaṃ ca na karmārtha-
tayā coditamiti na tasya karmārthatvenābhidhānam kintu svarūpeṇaiveti svarūpābhidhānā-
śaṅkā | aprayukteśca karmārthatāṃ manyate | yadi karmārthamabhidhānaṃ na syāt tadā mantrasya
prayuktirna syāditi karmārthatā mantrasya | tasmādupapannaḥ saṃśayaḥ
 |

atra pūrvapakṣavādī yathācoditamabhidhānaṃ nyāyyamiti manyate | ayathā-
coditābhidhāne
 yathā coditaṃ tathā’nabhidhāne hi mantrasyāṅgataiva nopapadyate | asama-
vetārthābhidhānāditi hetoḥ | devatāyāṃ punaḥ dātṛtvāśaṅkayā mukhyamabhidhānam |
phaladātrī devatā | tena sāpi phalakoṭinikṣiptā na karmārthatayā coditā | tena
tasyā api svarūpeṇaivābhidhānamiti vakṣyamāṇaphaladātrī devateti vakṣyāmaḥ |
tasmānna mantre lakṣaṇā kintu śrutyaiva svargā(nya)[dya]bhidhānamiti | atrocyate-yuktamuktaṃ
yathācoditamabhidhānamiti | nanvevameva codatam | kathaṃ coditam ?
 uttaraṃ
---samantrakasya karmaṇaḥ idaṃ phalam
 | asyārthaḥ - yadyapi phalaṃ karmārthaṃ na bhavati
tathāpi tāvat karmasambandhitayaiva cojitam | mantraśca karmāṅgabhūtaḥ anyathā prayuktya-
bhāvāt | evaṃ sati karmadvārakamabhidhānaṃ mantrasya pratīyate
 | asyārthaḥ-abhidhānasya
karmābhidheyaṃ yat tad‌dvārakamāgneyādikarmābhidhānaṃ mantreṇa pratīyate | etaduktaṃ bhavati

---svargaphalābhidhānadvāreṇa karmaivābhidhīyata iti | mantrakāryataḥ pratyutāyamevārthaḥ
śrauto vartate nimittāpekṣayā lakṣaṇetyucyate | yo hi kāryānvayyarthaḥ sa eva śabdārtha
iti | yadyapi nimittaṃ paramālocyate lakṣaṇeyaṃ tathāpi ayameva pratyuta śrauto’rthaḥ |
mukhyastvartho niyogāsaṃsparśādaśruta eva śruteratatparatvāt | tasmānnimittāvasthāyāṃ na
lakṣaṇādoṣaḥ kāryapradhānatvānmantrābhidhānasya | viniyogaparyālocanayā yadeva mantrasya

[p. 219]

svakārye tadanuguṇamabhidhānaṃ, iha ca karmābhidhāne yo mantro viniyukta iti karmasambandhi-
tvena lakṣaṇayaiva tadabhidhānam | atra ca phaladātṛtayā phalāṃśanikṣiptāyāmapi devatāyāṃ
satyāṃ na tatprayuktatā’bhidhānasyetyuktam |

|| 4 || devatā vā prayojayedatithivadbhojanasya tadarthatvāt || 6 ||

idānīṃ dātṛtvādibhiḥ kāraṇaiḥ devatāṃ na viniyogārthāṃ manyate
pūrvapakṣavādī
 | asyārthaḥ-havirdānena guṇavacanaiśca devataivārādhyate | yajerdevatā-
pūjārthatvāt | pūjāyāśca loke pūjyamānārādhanārthatvena darśanāt | tathā guṇavacana-
lakṣaṇāpi stutirloke rājñāmārādhanārthā dṛśyata iti | na ca phalakāmino niṣphalaṃ
devatārādhanamiti devatāyāḥ phaladātṛtvamavagamyate | na ca phalasyānīśānāyāḥ
tadupapadyata iti phalasyāpi seṣṭe iti kalpyate | na ca havira(nu)jñānā devatā
havirdānenārādhyate | na cācetanāyāḥ vigrahavirahiṇyāścaitatsarvamiti bhoktṛtvaṃ
caitanyaṃ vigrahavattā ceti sarvamarthādavagamyate | sarvathā yāvatā vinā’syārthasyāyogaḥ
tāvatkalpyate | tena pradhānabhūtā devatā na niyogārthā kintu saiva prādhānyenāvagamya-
mānā dharmajātaṃ svārthatayā gṛhṇatī prayojiketi | etadeva vivarītuṃ codayati---

nanu caivaṃ sati sāpekṣatvānmantrāṇāṃ bahu hātavyaṃ bhavati
 | asyārthaḥ-
śabdastāvat vigrahavattādiṣu na pramāṇam | tasyāpūrve kāryātmani prāmāṇyā | tena
pramāṇāntarāpekṣairmantraiḥ vigrahavattvādiko’rtho vaktavyaḥ | na ca tatpramāṇāntaramastītya-
prāmāṇyāpatteḥ sthitaṃ prāmāṇyādi bahu hātavyaṃ bhavatyasmin pakṣe | pariharati---
ārādhanasyautpattikatvaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī
-asyārthaḥ-asminnadhikaraṇe rāddhānta-
siddhau satyāṃ apūrvaṃ vākyārthaḥ siddhayati | yadā tu devatārādhanamevopadiśyate tadā
tatraivautpattikamadhidhāyakatvaṃ ca śabdasya | idamatrākūtam---kāryapratipādakatā loke
śabdasya vyutpannā | tadeva ca kāmī kāryatayā’vabudhyate yatphaladānasamartham |
ārādhanaṃ vā ārādhyadevatādvāreṇa phaladānasamarthamityārādhanameva kāryatayā śabde-
nābhidhīyata iti tatraiva śabdasya prāmāṇyam | yadā vā ārādhanaṃ śabdena pratīyate
tadā tadbalena vigrahavattādike pramīyamāṇe na kiñcidavahīyate smṛtyupacārānyārtha-
darśanāni caivamupapannāni bhavantītyabhyuccayaḥ |

nanu caivamapi atideśo hīyate yaddhi devatāntarārādhanopāyabhūtaṃ na
tat devatāntare bhavitumarhati
 asadṛśatvāditi bhāvaḥ | pariharati---hīyatāmeva

[p. 220]

na kācit kṣatiḥ
 | yata evātideśo hīyate ata evāha yaccāgnau havanaṃ na tat
sūrya iti
 | sūryāgnayorasadṛśatvāt |

kiṃ punaḥ karaṇaṃ sthito’tideśastyajyate ?
 yāvatā sthitātideśānu-
guṇyāt ayameva pakṣaḥ kimiti na hīyate | uttaraṃ---niyogasiddhau vākyārtha-
bhūtāyāṃ atideśo naiba tyajyate
 | yadyarādhanenaivopapadyate kiṃ kurmaḥ etadadhi-
karaṇasiddhāntamaṅgīkṛtyātideśaḥ sthāpito nyāyabalāt | pūrvapakṣavelāyāṃ yadi
nopapadyate kiṃ kurmaḥ ? yata evāyaṃ doṣāvahaḥ pakṣo’ta eva tyājyatayopanyasta
ityarthaḥ | pūrvapakṣamupasaṃharati---smṛtyupacārānyārthadarśanaiḥ pūrvapakṣavādī śrutyā
devatābhidhānamāha
 |

rāddhantastu codanātastāvat yāgeneti pratīyate
 | asyārthaḥ-asyādhi-
karaṇasya pūrvapakṣadaśāyāṃ yāga eva devatāpūjātmakaḥ kāryatayā’vagamyate | yacca kāryatayā
phalakāmenāvagamyate tena phalaṃ siddhayatītyaṅgīkaraṇīyam | tena yāgena svargaṃ kuryāt
svargakāma ityayamarthaḥ svargakāmo yajetetyatra pratīyate |

pratīyatāṃ kā kṣatirityāha---pramāṇāntarābhāvācca dātṛtvādiṣu na vrīhi-
bhiryajetetivat vyavahitā yāgaśrutiḥ śakyate kalpayitum
 | yadi hi
pramāṇāntareṇa phaladātṛtvaṃ agnayādīnāmavagataṃ bhavet tadā devatāvyavadhānena yāgasya
phalaṃ prati sādhanabhāvo’vagamyeta | na ca dātṛtvādiṣu pramāṇamasti | ārādhanabhūta-
yāgakartavyatopadeśa eva pramāṇamiti cenna, itaretarāśrayaprasaṅgāt | siddhe hi dātṛtve
ārādhanaṃ vākyārtho bhavati | ārādhane ca vākyārthe dātṛtvādikalpaneti | tasmāt
yāgasya phalakāminaṃ prati kāryatayopadeśo nopapadyata iti apūrvameva phaladānasamarthaṃ
kāryatayā śabdena pratipādyata iti tadeva vākyārtha iti siddham |

yathā ca dātṛtvādiṣu pramāṇāntaraṃ nāsti tathoktaṃ bhāṣye | smṛtyādīnāṃ
tatrāprāmāṇyāt | tasmādapūrvasya vākyārthatvāt yāgasya ca tadviṣayatvāt yadaikasmāda-
pūrvaṃ tadā itarat tadarthamiti nyāyena devatāpi yāgārthā satī apūrvārthā | tasmādida-
mapi devatāsambandhi karmadvārakameva abhidhānakarmabhūtābhidheyadevatādvārakamapūrvasyaivābhi-
dhānamiti sthitat | śeṣamuktam
 |

|| 5 || dravyasaṅkhyāhetusamudāyaṃ vā śrutisaṃyogāt || 11 ||

[p. 221]

sthite tvapūrvaprayuktatve(pi)viniyogasyāvirodhāt kāmāśaṅkāṃ nivarta-
yitumidadhikaraṇam ?
 asyārthaḥ-hantipiṣyadhikaraṇe sarveṣāmevamādīnāmapūrvaprayuktatva-
muktam | na ca
 vrīhyādiviniyogavaśena tadākṣipyate |

atrocyate - astyatrāśaṅkā |
 tāmāha---yuktaṃ tābat prokṣaṇasya hanti-
piṣibhyāṃ saha sambandhe hantipiṣyoḥ bhāvyārthatayā kāryaparyantatvam |

asyārthaḥ---apūrvasambandho hi yasyāvagamyate tasyāpūrvaṃ prayojakaṃ bhavati | anyathā’ti-
prasaṅgāt | atra ca prokṣitābhyāmulūkhalamusalābhyāmavahantīti prokṣaṇasya yuktamapūrva-
sambandhitvam bhavyārthatayā hantipiṣṭyoḥ sādhyāntarānupraveśāt prokṣaṇasya ca tatsambandhi-
vāt | phaladevatayośca sādhyasambandhitvāt vyapadeśasyāpūrvasambandhitayaiva hi svargaḥ
phalamityucyate tathā’gnirapi devateti na svarūpeṇa tena tatsambaddhasyāpi mantrasya yuktama-
pūrvasambandhitvam tatsambandhitvācca tatprayuktatvaṃ ca | iha tu dravyasaṅkhyādayo na kārya-
paryantasvabhāvāḥ | nāpi sādhyasambandhitayaiva vyapadiśyante | ato na kāryasambandhitayā
dharmavidhānameṣāṃ bhavati |

nanu saṃskāro’narthaka ityatrāha---saṃskṛtānāṃ tu bhavati kriyāntare
viniyoga iti |
 nanu ca tṛtīye teṣāmarthena ityatra viniyuktānāṃ satāṃ vrīhyādīnāṃ
dharmaviniyoga ityuktam | kathamidamucyate saṃskṛtānāṃ satāṃ viniyogo bhavatviti |
pariharati---tadevedamākṣipyate |
 etadeva vivṛṇoti---tatra tṛtīye ye yatra śrūyante
teṣāmeva dharmā iti viniyogavyavasthā pratipāditā |
 kāmāśaṅkāṃ nivartayitu-
mityatrāha---iha siddhena kāryārthatvena virodhamāśaṅkyeti |
 kena hetunā tarhi
sā siddhayatītyatrāha-virodhābhāvena śrutyādivyavasthoktā |
 iha punaḥ śrutyādi-
vyavasthāne’sati kāryagarbho nopapadyate ityākṣipyate |

nanvevaṃ sati
 yadi kā[rya](ryā)garbhatā nāsti tadā ye yatra śrūyante itye-
tadebādhikaraṇamākṣiptaṃ bhavati |
 tathā ca sati pūrvapakṣābhāvenānārambhaṇīyatvāt |
pariharati-na sarvamadhikaraṇamākṣipyate dravyādiṣu punarākṣipyata
 iti sambandhaḥ |
kayā yuktyā ākṣepa ityatrāha - kāryagarbhatā nāstīti virodhāśaṅkāpi nāstī-
tyanayā yuktayā |
 tasmādaviruddha eva vrīhīn prokṣatītyevamādiṣu viniyogaḥ |

nanu caivaṃ sati puruṣārthatā prāpnotyavaghātādīnām | yadi hi kratvarthānāṃ satāṃ
saṃskāraḥ syāt tadā kratvarthatā bhavet yadā tu saṃskṛtānāṃ satāṃ kratvarthatā

[p. 222]

tadānīmakratvarthadravyasambandhāt puruṣārthatā prāpnoti | uttaraṃ---na prāpnoti
puruṣārthatā |
 kasmādevamityāha---śakyate vibhāgo’vagantuṃ amī kratvarthā
amī puruṣārthā iti |
 asyārthaḥ---ye vrīhyādayo niruptāsteṣāmeva prakṛtatvenāva-
ghātādisambandho’vagamyate nānyeṣām | ye niruptāste kratvarthāḥ anye cākratvarthā iti
śakyate vibhāgo’vagantum | ataḥ kratvarthavrīhisambandhānna puruṣārthatāvaghātādīnām |
punarapi codayati---nirvāpe kathaṃ ?
 asyārthaḥ - nirvāpe tarhi kathamapuruṣārthatā |
nahi tatrānyassaṃskāro’sti yatsaṃskṛtadravyasambandhāt apūrvārthatā syāt nirvāpasyā-
grimattvāt |

pariharati---tatrāpi nirvāpapṛthaktvāt siddhamapuruṣārthatvam |
 dvāvetau
nirvāpau staḥ yaḥ puruṣārtho yaśca kratvarthaḥ | kaḥpunaḥ kratvarthaḥ ? yaḥ kratvarthe dravye kriyate
yasya kratuḥ prayojanam tatkratvarthaṃ dravyam | tasmādaviruddha eva viniyogaḥ kāryeṇeti
akāryaprayuktatāṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | atrocyate---atrāpi kāryaprayuktataiba
nyāyyā |
 anyathā hi yadi kāryaprayuktatā na syāt tadā niyamaniyogo na sampāditaḥ
syāt | niyogo hi tadevānuṣṭhāpayati | yacca niyogasiddhāvaṅgaṃ yena ca vinā’ṅgasiddhireva
na syāt | tatra tāvat pūrvapakṣe avaghātādayo niyogasiddhau nāṅgabhūtāḥ | na ca tairvinā
aṅgasya kasyacidasiddhiḥ | avaghātādivyatirekeṇāpi puroḍāśasiddheḥ | tataścāva-
ghātādiniyamavidhiḥ nānuṣṭhitaḥ syāt | anuṣṭhānaparyantatā cā’vagamyate | tenāpūrva-
prayuktatā’’śrayaṇīyā | yaccādhikārāntaraṃ vā kalpyaṃ avaghātādiviṣayaṃ, na ca sannidhānāt
kāryabhedo’vagamyamāno bhinna iti śakyate vaktum | adhikāravidhisannidhau niradhikāreṣu
śruteṣu sa evādhikāro buddhau viparivartata iti nādhikārāntarakalpanā śakyā | tasmā-
nnādhikārāntarakalpanāṃ sahate niyogo yathā sannihitādhikārapraveśo bhavati tathā varṇanīya-
miti tatprayuktataiva nyāyyā | tatprayuktatve ca vrīhiśabdo’pi tatsādhanaviśeṣalakṣaṇārtha
eva varṇanīyaḥ | yata eva prathamamapūrvagrahaṇaṃ paścāttadīyasādhanaviśeṣaviniyogaḥ ata
eva darśapūrṇamāsāvudāhṛtya viniyogavyavasthoktā grāhakapūrvakaṃ viniyogaṃ darśayitum |

nanu viniyogavyavasthāyāmapūrvakāryatā virudhyate kāryadvayānupapatterityatrāha---
kāryavirodhinī ca seti teṣāmarthenetyatra pratipāditam |
 avahantyādiṣvapi
naiva padārthānuguṇatā heturapūrvaprayuktatve prokṣaṇādīnām | kintu niyamaniyogasiddhireva |
tasmāt tulyatvāddhetoḥ na vaiṣamyam prokṣitābhyāmavahantītyasya vrīhīnavahanītyādinā |

[p. 223]

tadevamihādhikaraṇe sannipatyopakārakāṇāṃ grāhakapūrvako viniyoga iti pratipādyate
ārādupakārakāṇāṃ tu saptamādye ityavahitena manasā1 brahmavidyevānusandheyam |

iyamabhyuccayayuktiḥ | puruṣārthatā’vahantyādīnāṃ prāpnotīti sādhayituṃ pūrvapakṣoktaṃ
samādhānamanubhāṣya dūṣayati-yadapi ca prakṛtalakṣaṇaṃ prakaraṇamaṅgīkṛtya kāryagarbhatā
’panīyate
 tadapanayanaṃ niyamaniyogasiddherayuktamityuktam | nirvāpe cāsambhavāt prakṛta-
lakṣaṇasya prakaraṇasya | na hi tatra kiñcit prakṛtamasti yena kratvarthatā syāt |

nanu nirvāpabhedādeva sambhava uktaḥ | sa eva
 nirvāpabhedo bhinneṣu na sambha-
vati | na hi nirvāpatvāt kratvarthapuruṣārthabhedo vrīhīṇāmastītyasambhavaḥ prakṛtalakṣaṇasya
prakaraṇasya | nirvāpe ya eva vrīhayaḥ puruṣārthāsta eva kratvarthena nirvāpeṇa kratvarthāḥ
kriyante | pūrvapakṣāvasthāyāṃ tu nirvāpasyākratvarthatvāt na sambhavatīti puruṣārthadravya-
sambandhānnirvāpaḥ puruṣārthaḥsyāt | tasmin puruṣārthe prokṣaṇamapi prakṛtaniruptavrīhisambandhāt
puruṣārthamityanena krameṇa sarveṣāṃ puruṣārthatā | śeṣamuktam |

|| 6 || deśabaddhamupāṃśutvaṃ teṣāṃ syācchrutinirdeśāt
tasya ca tatra bhāvāt || 20 ||

sthite apūrvaprayuktatve kiṃ dīkṣaṇīyāvāṅ‌niyamavat dharmasambaddhānā-
madhikāreṇaikatāpattiraṅganiyogānāṃ uta adhikārāpannānāmayaṃ dharmasambandha

ityanena bhāṣyakārīyaḥ saṃśayagrantho vyākhyātaḥ | yadi hi dharmasambaddhānāṃ satāmavāntara-
niyogānāmadhikāraniyogasambandho dīkṣaṇīyāvāṅ‌niyamavat tadā avāntarāpūrvaprayuktatvaṃ,
athādhikāraṃ prati sādhanībhūtānāmevāyaṃ dharmasambandhastadā avaghātādivat paramāpūrva-
prayuktatvam | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī yajñaikadeśasambandhaṃ manvānaḥ adhikāraikatāpa-
nnānāmayaṃ dharma iti manyate |

asyārthaḥ---‘‘tsarā vā eṣā yajñasya tasmāt yatkiñcit prācīnaṃ tenopāṃśu
caranti’’
 ityatra yajñaśabdasya prācīnaśabdena saha sambandhaḥ anyathāhi tsarāśabdena
sambandhe arthavādapadaśeṣatā syāt | prācīnapadasambandhe tu vidhipadaśeṣatāsāmānyāt
pravṛttiviśeṣakaratvāt | tasmādyajñasya yatprācīnaṃ tenopāṃśu carantīti yajñasambaddhānāmevāyaṃ
1. anūdyasanghe---A

[p. 224]

dharma iti paramāpūrvaprayuktatāṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | deśena hi tadāpattirlabhyata ityabhiprāyaḥ |
avāntaraniyogapare tu vākye deśaniyogasambandho’nyārthaḥsyāt |
 asyārthaḥ-
yajñasya prācīnamiti yo’yaṃ deśaniyogasambandhaḥ pratīyate tatra niyogasambaddhāvasthā-
nyavāntarāpūrvāṇi pratīyante | tena tadavasthānāmeva dharmasambandhaḥ | yadi svarūpamā-
treṇāvāntarāpūrvāṇāṃ dharmasambandhārthameva vākyaṃ syāt tadā sambandhāvasthā svarūpamātro-
palakṣaṇārthā satī anyārthā syāt | na ca svārthatve sambhavati anyārthatā nyāyā ||

|| 6 || praṇītādi tatheti cet || 23 ||

rāddhāntopakramamidamadhikaraṇam | tatra rāddhāntinā avāntarāpūrvaprayuktyarthaṃ
dṛṣṭānto dattaḥ | yathā praṇītāḥ praṇeṣyan vācaṃ yacchati iti vāṅ‌niyamo’vāntarā-
pūrvaprayuktaḥ tathedamupāṃśutvamiti |

atra pūrvapakṣabhāṣyaṃ rāddhāntavādyuktavāṅ‌niyamavaiṣamyapradarśanārthaṃ yajñasya śrutatvā-
diti | yaktuṃ tatra vāṅ‌niyamasyāvāntarāpūrvaprayuktatvam yajñasambandhāśravaṇāt | iha ca
yajñasya prācīnamiti śratatvāditi | punaśca vāṅ‌niyamenaiva saha[sāmyo](svasyo)papattyarthaṃ
rāddhāntaikadeśe sthitvā ‘‘nanvidānīmevoktaṃ’’
 ityuktam | yajñaśabdaśca yajñaṃ tanvāte
ityudāhṛtaḥ | ‘‘yadadhvaryuyajamānau vācaṃ yacchataḥ tatprajāpaterhṛdayaṃ gatvā yajñaṃ
tanvāte’’
 ityarthavādagato yajñaśabda udāhṛtaḥ | yajñaṃ taniṣyantāvadhvaryuyajamānau vācaṃ
yacchata iti kena kena vākyārthapradarśanapareṇa |

atra codayati---naivopakrame yajñaśabda udāhṛtaḥ na cānantaramuktaḥ |

rāddhāntopakrame hi praṇītāḥ praṇeṣyan vācaṃ yacchati ityeva tadudāhṛtam tathānubhāṣyeṇa |
sūtrepi praṇītāvaditi ? cet ityatrāpi yajñaśabdo noktaḥ kathamidamucyate nanvidā-
nīmevoktaṃ yajñaṃ taniṣyantāviti |
 parihāramāha---nanvidamacodyam |
 eta-
duktaṃ bhavati---nanvidānīmevoktaṃ svayā pūrvapakṣiṇā yatrāpi yajñaśabdaḥ śrūyate sa dharma-
vidhānāyaiva yuktaḥ pravṛttiviśeṣakaratvāt na stutaya iti | asti cātrāpyayaṃ yajña-
śabdaḥ ‘yajñaṃ tanvāta’
 iti | idamatrābhipretam---nāyaṃ bhāṣye sambandho-nanvidānī-
meva yajñaṃ taniṣyantāvadhvaryuyajamānau vācaṃ yacchata ityuktamiti | kintu nanvidānīmevoktaṃ
tvayā yatra yajñaśabdaḥ śrūyate sa dharmavidhānāyaiva na stutaya ityadhyāhṛtena sambandhaḥ |
punaścāsti cātrāpi ayaṃ yajñaśabdo yajñaṃ taniṣyantāviti tenātrāpi yajñasambandhāvasthā-

[p. 225]

nāmeva vāṅ‌niyamasambandha ityatrāpi paramāpūrvaprayuktataiva syādityevaṃ bhāṣyamuktvā puna-
rvaiṣamyābhidhānamaduṣṭam | satyamatrāpi yajñaśabdosti vidhiśeṣaśca | tathāpi vistāra-
sambaddho’sau | tena yajñaṃ vistārayituṃ vāṅ‌niyamaḥ kartavyaḥ | padārthasambandhe ca
yajñasya vistāra iti padārthasambandha eva yuktaḥ | sa paramāpūrvasambandhaḥ |

kathaṃ punarasmin pakṣe vīpsāvagatiḥ ? yatkiñcicchravaṇāt bahūn
prati vyāptumicchā pratīyate sā’vāntarāpūrvārthatve’bakalpate | paramāpūrvārthatve
ca bhāgadharmo’yam | ekaścāsau bhāga iti vīpsāvagamo nopapadyate |
 uttaraṃ---
arthaprāptatvādavivakṣetyabhiprāyaḥ |
 arthaprāptānuvādo’yamityarthaḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ
prāptirityāha---deśasambandhepi hi padārthamukhenaivādhikārasambandha ityupapadyate
vīpsā |

atra rāddhāntopakrame tasya ca tatra bhāvāditi sūtrāvayavaṃ vyācakṣāṇena sanni-
patyopakārapradarśanaṃ kṛtaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa | paraṃ na kevalārādupakārakasyaivāvāntarāpūrvasyāyaṃ
dharmaḥ | kintu tasya ca sannipatyopakārakasvāpūrvasyāyaṃ dharmaḥ tatra deśe dvayorbhāvāditi |

tatra codayati---nanu caibamapi sūtrārtho gacchatyeva |
 tasya cāvāntara-
niyogasya tatra bhāvāt tatprayuktamupāṃśutvamityarthaḥ sūtrāvayavaḥ | pariharati---satyamevaṃ
sūtrārtho gacchati nirākaraṇīyastvartho nāsti |
 yannirākaraṇārtho’yaṃ sūtrāvayavaḥ
syāt | na cāsāvasti yannirākaraṇārthaḥ sūtrāvayavaḥ | tena mandaprayojanatā syāt |

kathaṃ nirākaraṇīyo’rtho nāsti ?
 uttaraṃ---tasyāvāntarāpūrvasyābhāvo
vispaṣṭatvānna śakyata āśaṅkitum |
 yadi hi avāntarāpūrvasyābhāvāśaṅkā syāt
tadā tannirākaraṇārthaṃ sūtrāvayavaḥ syāt na cāsāvasti | yuktyantaraṃ bāhyaviśeṣeṇa
cāprāptirvaktavyā | na kevalamārādupakārakasya ubhayavidhasyāyaṃ dharma iti vaktavyam |
yadi hi tathā nocyeta tadā kasya cinmandasya kevalārādupakārakadharmatvāśaṅkaiva syāt
iti yathābhāṣyameva sūtravyākhyānaṃ yuktam |

idānīṃ rāddhānto’bhidhīyate---dharmavatāmekatāvagatiryuktā | kutaḥ ?
ekatāpannānāṃ dharmasambandhe pramāṇābhāvāt |
 nanu ca yajñasya prācīnamiti sambandhāva-
gamaḥ pramāṇamupanyastam | pariharati---tulyamavāntarāpūrvasambandhe’pi ekatvādya-
jñasya
 prācīnamityupalakṣaṇārthametat | tenopāṃśu carantīti dharmavidheḥ tenopalakṣaṇatayā

[p. 226]

yajñasambandho nānupapannaḥ | yuktyantaraṃ cāha---stutyā vā’’nantaryeṇa sambandhāva-
gamāt |
 tasmādityanena śabdena vyavadhānādyajñaśabdaḥ prācīnaśabdena saha na
sambadhyate, kintvānantaryeṇa tsarāśabdenaivāsya sambandhaḥ | tena yajñasambaddhānāṃ
upāṃśutvaṃ na pratīyate |

nanu stutisambandhe’pi śākunikadṛṣṭāntena yajñārthamevopāṃśutvaṃ pratīyate | yathā
śākunikasya tsarā śakunipratyayāya yajñasyāpi tsarā yajñaprāptayarthaivetyāśaṅkyāha---
dṛṣṭena cārthena stutyupapattiḥ |
 padārthadharmatve hi dṛṣṭenaivārthena yajñaprāptayarthatvaṃ upāṃśu-
tvenāśithilaprayatnai sukhena padārthān aṅgabhūtānupakurvadbhiḥ sukhena yajñaḥ prāpyate |
padārthasambandhābhāve tu adṛṣṭenopakāreṇopāṃśutvasya yajñaprāptayarthatā syāt |

rāddhānte’bhyuccayamāha---avaśyaṃbhāvitvādyajñapadārthasambandhasya sākṣāddeśa-
sambandhasyāvidyamānatvāt varaṃ prathamaṃ padārthasambandha eva |
 yaccoktaṃ pūrvapadārtha-
sambandha eva | yaccoktaṃ pūrvapakṣe vīpsārtho’nuvāda iti tatrāha---vīpsārthāvivakṣāyā-
mayameva doṣo yaduta tasya satyāṃ vivakṣāyāṃ avivakṣeti |
 atassiddhaṃ dharmavatā-
madhikārāpattiriti |

atra bhāṣyakāreṇoktaṃ---‘‘nanu dīkṣaṇīyādiṣvanyadeva svarāntaramastī’’
ti |
tadasambaddham | na hi tat pūrvapakṣe rāddhānte vā upayuktam ityāśaṅkyāha---nanu
dīkṣaṇīyādiṣvanyadeva svarāntaramasti vihitaṃ ityetena granthena yā’tra prāptaya-
prāpticintā viniyogāviniyogacintā dīkṣaṇīyādiṣu pradhāneṣu viśeṣa-
vihitaṃ svarāntaramastīti naitadasambaddham |
 svarāntarāvaruddhe upāṃśutvasyā-
viniyogaḥ |
 tadaṅgāpūrveṣu tu viniyoga iti sā kāryabhedasiddhayarthā | anyatra
tṛtīye siddhā saṃvyavahārārthaṃ likhitā | samyagrāddhāntena vyavahartumidamuktam | śeṣaṃ
saha prayojanenoktam |

|| 7 || agnidharmaḥ pratīṣṭakaṃ saṅghātāt paurṇamāsīvat || 26 ||

kimidaṃ kāryacintayā dravyāntaraṃ cintyate ?
 asmin ṣaṭke kārya-
taścintā | tathā’sminnadhyāye tatsiddhayarthaṃ dvārakāryacintā kāryārthā | tena ṣaṭke
adhyāye ca neyaṃ dravyāntaracintā saṅgateti | uttaraṃ-kāryasiddhayamarthamidaṃ dravyāntaraṃ
cintyata iti bramaḥ |

[p. 227]

kiṃ tatkāryaṃ yadarthaiṣā cintā ?
 uttaraṃ---agniṃ prokṣatīti kimasya
pratīṣṭakaṃ kāryaṃ utaikameveti |
 yadi dravyāntaramasti tadā tadeva prokṣaṇīyamiti
ekaṃ kāryam | dravyāntarābhāve pratīṣṭakaṃ kāryam | tadā hi iṣṭakā eva prokṣaṇīyāḥ
tadarthaṃ dravyāntaracintā | pūrvapakṣamāha---atra dravyāntaravidāṃ ye avayavebhyo’va-
yavibhūtaṃ dravyāntaraṃ vadanti vaiśeṣikāḥ teṣāṃ pakvāmasaṃyoge dravyāntaraṃ na
bhavatīti
 rāddhāntaḥ |

atra ceṣṭakāḥ pakvāḥ purīṣamapakvaṃ tena tatsaṃyoge dravyāntaraṃ nāsti | nacaivaṃ
pratyakṣaikabuddhiḥ kathamupapādanīyetyabhiprāyeṇāha---kā punaratropapattiḥ pratyakṣabuddheḥ ?
 |
uttaraṃ---samudāyālambanaivātrākṣajā
 buddhiḥ pulakādiṣvava na punararthāntarāpekṣiṇī |
avayavabhūtābhyaḥ iṣṭakāmyo’rthāntaramavayavidravyamapekṣya vā buddhirna bhavati yathā devadattā-
diṣviti vaidharmyadṛṣṭantaḥ | tasmānna dravyāntaram |

rāddhāntastu bacanāttāvadavagamyate iṣṭakābhiragniṃ cinuta iti |
 anyathā
hi iṣṭakābhiścinuta iti syāt | yathāyamabhiprāyaḥ avyatireke’pi vyatirekaśabdaḥ-
prayujyate yathā śilāputrakasya śarīramityevamādiṣu iti | satyaṃ yatra spaṣṭa evābhāvaḥ tatra
hi dravyāntarābhāvāt vyatirekābhāvāt avyatirekabadbhāva eba nyāyyaḥ | iha punaḥ
devadattavat iṣṭakānirapekṣaiva citau buddhirutpadyate iti vyatirekavadbhāvakalpanā |

yathāhi devadatte pāṇyādyavayavanirapekṣā ekabuddhiḥ avayavisadbhāve pramāṇaṃ tathā-
trāpyayamastīti nāvayavyanupapattiḥ śakyate vaktum | pakvāmasaṃyoge’pi dravyāntaramutpadyata
iti darśanabalādaṅgīkaraṇīyam | tena spaṣṭatvāt dravyāntarasya na vyatirekadbhāvakalpanā
kintu vyatireka eveti samāptamadhikaraṇam |

yastu gautamamatāśrayaṇena sakalāvayavidravyāntarāpahnavaḥ sa vaiśeṣikairniṃrākṛto-
’smābhiśca jātinirṇaye
 | tadāha---keśoṇḍrakanayaśca dravyāntaravidbhireva
pratyuktaḥ |
 yathā avidyamānaḥ keśoṇḍrakaḥ taimirikasyāvabhāti tathā’vayavyapīti yo’yaṃ
keśoṇḍrakanyāyaḥ sa vaiśeṣikairnirākṛtaḥ | yattvanumānamākarṣaṇamuktaṃ yatrāvayavī vidyate
tatraikasminnavayave ākṛṣyamāṇe avayavāntaramapi ākṛṣyate yathā devadattaḥ | iha ca
yaiveṣṭakā ākṛṣyate saivāyāti neṣṭakāntaramapi | tadākarṣaṇamatrānumānaṃ liṅgamuktam |
tat sthāvareṣvanaikāntikatvāt pratyuktam | vṛkṣādau yaiva śākhā ākṛṣyate saivāyāti

[p. 228]

nānyeti | tena yatrāpyavayavī tatrāpyetadastīti naikāntato’vayavinirākaraṇe samartham |
tasmādabhinnaṃ kāryaṃ prokṣaṇasyeti siddham |

prayojanamuktam |
 paurṇamāsīnidarśanamapi yatpūrvapakṣiṇoktaṃ yathā caturhotrā
paurṇamāsīmabhimṛśediti pratyekamabhimarśanaṃ tathā’gnidharmeṇāpi pratīṣṭakaṃ bhā(ṣya)vyam |
tadapi parihṛtam | yuktaṃ tatra dravyāntarānārambhāt, iha tu dravyāntaramārabdhamiti |

|| 8 || patnīsaṃyājāntatvaṃ sarveṣāmaviśeṣāt || 29 ||

ūhacintāyāṃ kaḥprasaṅgo’valopasya |
 yadi sarveṣāmahnāṃ patnīsaṃyājāntatā
tadā parastāttanānāmaṅgānāmavalopa eva | yadā tu prāguttamādahnaḥ tadā nāvalopa ityava-
lopacinteyamūhalakṣaṇe na saṅgatā | bāghacintātprakatvāditi bhāvaḥ | uttaraṃ---na
khalu kaścidavalopasya prasaṅgaḥ |
 kintu avalopa evātra na cintyata iti
hṛdistham |

yadyavalopo’tra na cintyate kathaṃ tarhīdamadhikaraṇam | uttaram---
itthamadhikaraṇam | ayamupadeśo’valopasya uta kāryasyopasaṃhāra iti |

asyārthaḥ-kimaprāpta evāṅgalopo’yamupadiśyate atha kāryasya karaṇopakārasyopasaṃhāraḥ |
sakṛdanuṣṭhitairaṅgaissiddhiḥ |

nanvanyathaiva bhāṣye cinteti yo manyate taṃ pratyāha---yadyayama[va]lopopadeśa-
stadā sarveṣāmahnāmaviśeṣāt patnīsaṃyājāntatā | atha kāryopasaṃhṛtistataḥ
prāguttamādahnaḥ patnīsaṃyājāntatā |
 uttame tu patnīsaṃyājebhyaḥ(yasyāḥ)parastā-
ttanānāmapyanuṣṭhānaṃ sarvārthatvena |

nanvevamapi kā’tra lakṣaṇe saṅgatirityatrāha---upadeśe
 avalopopadeśapakṣe
yathopadeśaṃ kāryakalpanā |
 kāryaprāptirupadeśavaśena bādhanīyetyarthaḥ | tataśca
vikṛtiṣvapi tathaiva kāryam patnīsaṃyājāntaivāṅgarītiḥ | upasaṃhāre tu vikṛtiṣvapi
yathākāryameva tadantatā | sahānuṣṭhānasampattistadantatāyāḥ kāryam | tena yadupottamamahaḥ
tatra tatra tadarthaṃ tadantatā | dvirātra ekasyānhaḥ patnīsaṃyājāntatā, trirātre tu dvayoriti
yathākāryamanuṣṭhānamityūhe saṅgatiḥ |

tatra pūrvapakṣastāvadupadeśābhimānena |
 avalopopadeśo’yamityabhimānena
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

[p. 229]

nanṛpadeśena kathaṃ sarveṣāṃ tadantatetyatrāha---na hyupadeśe kiñcinniyāmaka-
mastīti sarveṣāṃ tadantatopadeśaḥ |
 itiśabdaḥ samāptau | aparaḥ pakṣo
rāddhāntābhāsabhūtaḥ
 bhavatvayamupadeśastathāpi asaṃsthitaśabdaśravaṇāt prāguttamādahnaḥ
patnīsaṃyājāntatā | evamasaṃsthiterhetutvamupapannaṃ bhavati | evaṃ śrūyate-patnīsaṃyājā-
ntānyahāni santiṣṭhante na barhiranupraharati | asaṃsthito hi tarhi yajñaḥ ityasaṃsthā-
hetukā patnīsaṃyājāntatā kīrtyate | prāguttamādahno yajñasyāsaṃsthā | tena tatraiva
tadantatopadeśa iti |

imaṃ rāddhāntābhāsamasaṃsthānasyārthavādatvena nirākṛtya punaraviśeṣa
uktaḥ |
 asyārthaḥ - patnīsaṃyājāntatāstutiriyam | yadi hi patnīsaṃyājāntatā
na syāt tadā cireṇa yajñassantiṣṭhate tena saṃsthito’pyasaṃsthitaprāyaḥ syāt | tasmāda-
valupyantāmaṅganīti | tenāviśeṣāt sarveṣāṃ patnīsaṃyājāntateti |

rāddhāntastu naivāyamavalopopadeśaḥ sambhavati | prāptatvāddharma/?/jātasya |

tadavalope prāptabādhaprasaṅgāt | na cābādhe sambhavati bādho nyāyyaḥ | mūlayuktimāha---
aikaśabdyāda(dya)hargaṇasya dvādaśāhalakṣaṇasya kathamitikartavyatāvistāraḥ saṃhara-
ṇīya ityasyāmākāṅkṣāyāṃ
 upasaṃhāravidhirevāyamavagamyate, nāprāptasyāvalopasyopa-
deśaḥ | dvādaśāhena prajākāmaṃ yājayedityekaḥ prayogavidhiḥ pratyahaṃ vihitamiti-
kartavyatākalāpaṃ yugapatprayuṅkte | tatprayuktyanusāreṇetikartavyatākalāpastāvat sahānuṣṭhā-
tavyaḥ | sa kena prakāreṇeti prakāraviśeṣākāṅkṣāyāṃ idaṃ vacanamupasaṃhārasya | upasaṃ-
hniyate sahānuṣṭhīyate yata itikartavyatākalāpo yena patnīsaṃyājāntatāvasthānena
tadvidhiravagamyate | apekṣitavidhānāt | yadā vedaṃ patnīsaṃyājāttatāvasthānaṃ iti-
kartavyatopasaṃhāraprayojanaṃ tadā prāguttamādahno vyavatiṣṭhate | evaṃ ca dṛṣṭārthaiva stuti-
rupapannā bhavati | ‘‘patnīsaṃyājāntānyahāni santiṣṭhante na barhiranupraharati asaṃsthito
hi tarhi yajñaḥ’’
 iti | yatrāsaṃsthā tatra patnīsaṃyājāntatāvagamyate sā cānena
nyāyena prāptā samāptiḥ pramāṇapratipannatayaiva stutirupapannā bhavati | anyathā yathāva-
gamyamānārthābhāvenānupapannā syāt | vidhānaṃ ca viparītam | itarasmin pakṣe vākyena
vidhānam | ahnāṃ hi samāptirastyeva | tatra copapadārthavidhānaṃ syāt | asmatpakṣe tu
saṃsthānameva vidhīyata iti śrutyā vidhānam | śeṣamuktam |

[p. 230]

|| 9 || abhyāsaḥ sāmidhenīnāṃ prāthamyāt sarvadharmaḥ syāt || 33 ||

nanu ca kāryaśūnyatvādṛco dharmasambandha eva nopapadyate | kathaṃ prayukti-
ścintyate |
 atra kilādhikaraṇe kimayaṃ ṛgdharmaḥ uta sthānadharma ityanena prakāreṇa
prayuktireva cintyate | ayamabhyāsaḥ ṛk‌prayukta uta sthānaprayukta iti | tacca na
yuktam | akāryatvādṛcaḥ | kāryaṃ hi dharmāṇāṃ prayojakaṃ bhavati nākāryamiti |
ucyate | kāryayogamaṅgīkṛtyaiṣā cintā na ṛṅmātratayā |
 etaduktaṃ bhavati-
yāvatkāryamasyāssādhanabhūtāyāḥ kimayaṃ dharmaḥ, sāmidhenyanuvacanatayaiva uta prathamavacana-
tayaiveti | triḥprathamāmanvāheti yadi pravovā jāsambandho’bhyāsasya tadā darśapūrṇamāsādhi-
kāragṛhītatvāt yadetadīyaṃ pravovājāsādhyaṃ kāryaṃ tatra niviṣṭāyāstasyāḥ ayaṃ dharmaḥ
syāt | yadā tu prathamavacanasambandho’bhyāsasya tadā prathamavacanakāryaniviṣṭāyā iti |

tatra pūrvapakṣavādī vadati---avaśyaṃ strīliṅgavivakṣā kartavyā | prāthamyamātra-
vivakṣāyāṃ hi padavarṇaṃdharmo’pyāśaṅkyeta ṛca eva dharmo na syāt | tena prathamoddeśena
vidhāne liṅgaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ vivakṣaṇīyam | ato liṅgenaiva saṃśayavyāvṛtteḥ vivakṣitaṃ
liṅgamityevaṃ
 vadatīti sambandhaḥ | kena punarliṅgamabhihitaṃ vivakṣitamityāha---striyā-
mabhidheyāyāmityapi pakṣo vidyate |
 tena pratyayenāpyate strītvam | tadevaṃ strītvasya
vivakṣitatvāt taddharmo’yam | ṛk‌strī, tasmāttaddharmo’bhyāsa ityevaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate
 |

nātra liṅgavivakṣopapadyate, prāthamyasyānuvādāsambhavāt vidhāne ca
vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt |
 yadi striyamuddiśya dharmavidhistadā prāthamyaṃ tāvat nānṛdyate
aprāptatvāt | nahi prathamaiveyaṃ ṛk | na ca prāthamyaṃ vidhīyate vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt |
tasmādvaraṃ strītvamavivakṣitamastu |

nanu tadapi kathamanvīyata ityatrāha---strītvaṃ punaḥ ṛgviṣayatvāt anu-
vacanasya nityaprāptatvāt anṛdyata ityadoṣaḥ |
 nanu padavarṇaśaṅkāpi prāpnotītyuktam
tena nāpi strītvamatra nityaprāptam | pariharati---na padavarṇaṃśaṅkā arthaniṣṭhatvāda-
nuvacanasya |
 arthapratipattyarthaṃ hi anuvacanaṃ tena yadevārthaṃ pratipādayati tasyaivānu-
vacanaṃ vidhīyate | vākyāccārthāvagatiḥ
 anekapadasaṅghātātmano vākyādevārthāvagatiḥ
na punarekasmātpadādvā | tena vākyasyaivānuvacanaṃ prāptam vākyaṃ ca ṛk | tena prāptaṃ
strītvamanūdyata ityuktam | striyāṃ yatprātipadikaṃ vartata ityasyārtha ityanenābhiprāyeṇa striyāṃ

[p. 231]

yatpratipadikaṃ vartate tasmāṭṭābādaya ityuktaṃ viśeṣaṇabhūtatvācca strītvasyānuvacanasambandho
nāstītyāha---na ca viśeṣaṇaṃ kriyayā sambadhyate kintu viśeṣyatayaiva
 |

nanu cābhidheyapakṣo’pyastītyuktam
 | asyārthaḥ---yadyapi vastusthityā
laṅgasya viśeṣaṇatvaṃ pṛthak‌śabdābhidheyatayā sambandho bhaviṣyati saṅkhyāvaditi |
pariharati---tathāpi viśeṣaṇasvabhāvo naivāpaiti
 | pratyayenāpi strītvamabhidhīyamānaṃ
prātipadikārthaviśeṣaṇatayaivocyate iti tasya kriyāsambaṃndhaḥ | tasmāt prāthamyenaivā-
bhyāsasya sambandho yuktaḥ | yuktyantaraṃ cāha yuktaṃ caitat pañcadaśārthasampattyarthā-
vagateḥ
 | samidhenīnāṃ hi pañcadaśatvasampattyarthe’bhyāse dṛṣṭārthatā bhavati | ataḥ sāmi-
dhenīprathamānuvacanakāryaprayukto’yaṃ dharmaṃ iti sthitam |

|| 10 || iṣṭyāvṛttau prayājavadāvartetā’’rambhaṇīyā || 34 ||

ādyamadhikaraṇam kimārambhaśabdena karmārambhaḥ karmānuṣṭhānopakrama ucyate uta
ide mayā kartavyamityadhyavasānamityevaprārambhaśabdasyārthaṃ vikalpya varṇitam | tatra karmo-
pakramaḥ śabdārtha iti pūrvapakṣayitvā adhyavasānaṃ śabdārthaḥ ekaṃ ca tat sarvadarśapūrṇa-
māsaprayogāṇāmiti rāddhāntitam | tadida pūrvācāryavyākhyānamayuktaṃ manvānena bhāṣya-
kāreṇa anyathā adhikaraṇaṃ vyākhyātam |

kathaṃ punaridaṃ pūrvācāryavyākhyānamamayuktaṃ manyate
 | uttaraṃ---nāyamā-
rambhaśabdaḥ kasyacitpadārthasyaikasya pratiniyatasyādyāmavasthāmāha | kiṃ tarhi ?
sarvapadārthānāmeva ādyāmavasthāmācaṣṭe | tataḥ kiṃ ? idaṃ tato bhavati
 |
upāttasya sannighāvuccāryaprāṇaḥ tasyaivārambhamavagamayati | darśapūrṇamāsasannidhāvayamuccāryate
‘‘āgnāvaiṣṇamekādaśakapālaṃ nirvaped‌darśapūrṇamāsāvārapsyamāṇaḥ’’
 iti | tena tadārambha
mevāvagamayati | idaṃ mayā kartavyamityadhyavasānaṃ punaḥ sarvapadārthānāṃ sarvakarmaṇāmeva
nityanaimittikakāmyānāmāghānottarakālabhāvināṃ sādhāraṇatvāt naikasyādyāvasthā tayā-
’vagantuṃ śakyate | evaṃ hyasau puruṣasya saṅkalpo yāvadāgnisādhyaṃ me karmopanipatiṣyati
tanmayā kartavyamiti na punardarśapūrṇamāsamātraṃ kartavyamiti | tena sādhāraṇatvāt
darśapūrṇamāsārambhatayā nāvagantuṃ śakyate | ato darśapūrṇamāsārambha eva tadupakrama
eva tadī(yo’yaṃ)[yādya]padārthaprayoga evāyaṃ pratipattavyo’sādhāraṇatvāttasya tadārambha
tayā’vagamāt | nāghyavasānaṃ, viśeṣādṛte vyavahārābhāvādadhyavasānasya
 |

[p. 232]

viśeṣābhāve darśapūrṇamāsārambho’yamiti vyavahārābhāvāt | tasmādadhyavasānanimittikā
nārambhaṇīyā | kintu ādyapadārthaprayoganimittikā | tataścāvṛttirevāpadyata iti na
rāddhāntasiddhiḥ ityevaṃ pūrvācāryavyākhyānamayuktam |

nanu bhāṣya[vyākhyā]namapi gatārthamiva dṛśyate | kathaṃ
 ? uttaraṃ---
sarvameva hi nimittamadhikāriviśeṣaṇatayā’nvetīti bhedanādiṣūktam | katha-
mārambhaḥ śeṣitvenāṅkyate
 | asyārthaḥ - bhāṣyakāro hyevaṃ vicārayati kimāra-
mbhaṇīyā darśapūrṇamāsayorādyapadārthaṃ samyañcaṃ karoti uta puruṣamārambhayogyamiti |
tatrārambhasya nimittabhūtatvāt śeṣitvāśaṅkā nopapadyate | ukta hi ṣaṣṭhe | ‘bhedanādīni
nimittabhūtāni na śeṣīṇī’
ti | tena gatārthamidamadhikaraṇaṃ bhāṣyakārīyam | pariharati
---astyatrāśaṅkā yadyayaṃ bhedanavadadhikāraḥ parigṛhyate tadā darśapūrṇamāsādhi-
kṛtaviśeṣaṇavat pratiprayogamāvartate ārambhaṇīyā yathā sati bhedane homaḥ
 |
asyārthaḥ - yathā bhinne juhotīti bhedanamadhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ, yadi tathā’’rambhaḥ syāt
tadā darśapūrṇamāsādhikṛ[taḥ] tena viśeṣaṇīyaḥ ārambhavān darśapūrṇamāsādhikṛtaḥ
ārambhaṇīyāṃ kuryāditi | yathā yo bhedanavāt darśapūrṇamāsādhikṛtaḥ sa homaṃ iti |
tataścādhikārāvṛttau pratiprayogamārambhaṇīyā āvartate yathābhedanahomaḥ | pradhānādhi-
kārānupraveśāt avāntarādhikārasya tadāvṛttāvāvṛttiḥ | yadi tu svatantro’dhikāraḥ
syāt tadā ārambhaṇīyāyā āvṛttirna syāt | athaivaṃ na kalpyate
 darśapūrṇamāsādhi-
kṛtaviśeṣaṇaṃ na kalpyate tadā adhikārāntaratvāt kāryāntarakalpanā syāt |
bhavatviti cenna | darśapūrṇamāsaśabdasamabhivyāhārādadhikārāntarānavagatiḥ
 |
ekasmin vākye darśapūrṇamāsāvāgnāvaiṣṇavaṃ ca samabhivyāhniyete | tatra vākyena
sambandho’vagamyate | adhikārāntare ca sa bādhito bhavati | tasmānnādhikārāntarā
vagatiḥ | yata evaṃ nimittabhāvepyāvṛttiḥ tasmādvaramādyapadārthasaṃskāratayaivāyaṃ vidhiḥ
kalpitaḥ syāt kimavāntarādhikāreṇa | ārapsyamāna iti varaṃ śeṣiṇameva samarpayatu |
yadārambhasaṃskārakaṃ tadārambhavataiva kriyata iti puruṣaśravaṇamanuvādaḥ kimavāntarādhikā-
reṇa vṛthā āśritena |

nanu yadi tulyameva pakṣadvayepi prayojanaṃ kena viśeṣeṇāvāntarādhikārastyajyate
ārambhasaṃskāraḥ parapakṣaśca parigṛhyate tadāha---kiṃ punaravāntarādhikāre gauravaṃ
yena sa tyajyate
 | uttaraṃ---na khalu kiñcidgauravaṃ, kintvavāntarādhikāra

[p. 233]

eva na sambhabati | kathamasambhavaḥ
 ? uttaraṃ---nityatvādādyasya padārthasya
nāvāntarādhikārasambhavaḥ
 | darśapūrṇamāsayorhi ādyaḥ padārtho nityabhūtaḥ anityaṃ
ca viśeṣaṇaṃ bhavati | tena tasyādhikṛtaviśeṣaṇāṃśo nopapadyate | bhedanādīnāṃ tu
anityatvāt yukto’vāntarādhikāraḥ teṣāmadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇatayā vidhānametadyuktamiti pūrva-
pakṣavādī manyate | ataḥ pratiprayogaṃ prayājavadāvṛttiriti yuktam |

atrocyate---astyatrāvāntarāvikāraprayojanaṃ sakṛdanuṣṭhānarūpam | yathā ca
tatsiddhayati | na cedamadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇena bhavati ārapsyamāṇa iti puruṣaviśeṣaṇa-
tvāvagateḥ | tadāha---spaṣṭaścāyamadhikāra iti
 | nanu tathāpi kimityārambhaḥ śeṣī
na bhavatītyatrāha-na ca nimittaṃ śeṣī(na)bhavitumarhati śeṣitve pramāṇābhāvāt
 |

nanu nimittametanna bhavati nityatvādādyasya padārthasyetyuktam
 |
pariharati---satyaṃ na bhavati nimittaṃ yadyārambhaṇīyā bhedanahomavat karmāṅgaṃ
kalpyeta
 | yo hyārambhaṇīyāmaṅgamicchati tena darśapūrṇamāsādāvārambha ityāstheyam
nānyathā’ṅgatvasiddhiryataḥ | yaśca darśapūrṇamāsādhikṛtaḥ tasya nedaṃ viśeṣaṇamupapadyate
avyabhicārāt | yastvārambhaṇīyāmaṅgabhūtāṃ necchati na tasya darśapūrṇamāsādhikāro
viśeṣaṇīyaḥ, kintu puruṣātram | na ca sarvaḥ puruṣo darśapurṇamāsārambhavāniti
vyabhicārādyuktaṃ viśeṣaṇam | svatantrādhikāra evāyaṃ na bhavatīti manvāna āha---
kimiti cāsambhāvyamāne cāṅgatve svatantrādhikāre doṣa ukto darśapūrṇamāsa-
samabhivyāhārānnānavagatiruktādhikārāntarasya
 | uttaraṃ---satyaṃ svatantrādhi-
kāre kalpyamāne bhavatyayaṃ doṣo
 darśapūrṇamāsasamabhivyāhārabādhātmakaḥ kintu avā-
ntarādhikāra evāyam | nāyamekāntato darśapūrṇamāsādhikārasaṃsparśyadhikāraḥ | kintu
tadupakārakatvādavāntarādhikāra evāyam | na cādyapadārthanityatayā’sya virodhaḥ |
na cāstyeva nityatayā virodhaḥ | tathāhi---yadi darśapūrṇamāsasamabhivyāhāra
āditastadā tadaṅgamevārambhaṇīyā | tadaṅgatve vā ārambho’pi tadadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇaṃ
bhedanavat | na ca nityasya tadupapadyata iti śaṅkāyāmāha---sarvatra cāṅgāṅgibhāve
niyogaikyaṃ kāraṇam
 | aṅgaṅginorhi niyogaikye sati aṅgāṅgibhāvo bhavati anyathā
bhinnakāryatve aṅgāṅgibhāvānupapatteḥ |

bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi kimityāha---na cātra niyogaikye pramāṇamasti
aprakaraṇādhītatvādārambhaṇīyāyāḥ
 | nanu ārambhasambandhamukhena astyeva tanniyoga-

[p. 234]

pratyabhijñānaṃ yathā parṇamayī juhūrbhavatītyanena abhiprāyeṇāha-kathaṃ nāsti niyogaikye
pramāṇamiti
 | uttaraṃ---yadi hi svatantrasamabhivyāhṛtaṃ
 ādyapadārtharūpaṃ ārambha
ityā[rambhaṇīya]yā sahānveti tadā syāt vainiyogikaḥ ādyena padārthena saha tanmukhena
viniyogaikye | iha punarārambhakarturupasarjanībhūtaṃ viśeṣaṇībhūtaṃ ādyapadārtharūpamārabha-
māṇa ityārambhaṇīyayā’nveti | tatrādhikārānvaye sambhavati kimiti vainiyogikaḥ
pirigṛhyate | adhikāriviśeṣaṇatayaivārambhānvayastadviśeṣaṇatvena darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ |
evamādhikārike sambandhe sati kimiti vainiyogikaḥ parigṛhyate ? prayojana-
bhedo nopapadyate tena parigṛhyata iti ceta |

dūṣayati---sutarāṃ sākṣātpratīyamānādhikāratyāge pramāṇaṃ nāsti |

na ca prayojanabhedo’pi nāstītyāha---asti ca śabdārthe viśeṣaḥ sakṛt saṃskṛto-
’dhikārī sarvaprayogāṅgatāṃ labhate
 | asyārthaḥ---adhikāriviśeṣaṇatghepyārambhasya
sādhyatvenaiva viśeṣaṇatvāt svargavat tatsiddhayupayogitvamārambhaṇīyāniyogasya | sadhi-
kāre vākye puruṣapradhānaḥ sambandhaḥ iti puruṣārthatvādārambhaṇīyāyāḥ puruṣasyaivārambho-
tpattyanuguṇaḥ saṃskāraḥ ārambhaṇīyayā kriyate | sakṛtsaṃskṛtaśca puruṣo darśaṣūrṇamāsāra-
mbhayogyo jāta iti nārambhaṇīyā’’vartate | vainiyogike tu sambandhe[ke]valārambhasaṃskārā-
dārambhasya ca āvṛtterāvṛttiḥ syāt tadāha---vainiyogike punaḥ sambandhe prati-
prayogamāvṛttiriti
 | iha cedamarthasāram---anārabhyādhītā ārambhaṇīyā | tena
na tāvat sannidhānānniyogaikyam | nāpi parṇatāvat padārthānvayadvāreṇa | adhikāre
puruṣasambandhena puruṣārthatvādārambhaṇoyāyā na sākṣādādyapadārthasambandhaḥ | tena puruṣasyai-
vārambhayogyatāṃ utpādayantī saṃskārikā | na punaḥ kratvaṅgaṃ pramāṇābhāvāt |

etenedamuktam---ye prayājādiṣvadhikāramāhuḥ teṣāṃ prayājādayo’pi puruṣārthā
eva bhaveyuḥ na kratvarthāḥ tatrārambhaṇīyāvat prayājādibhirapi apūrvodayānuguṇaḥ saṃskāraḥ
kriyate | tadvacca sakṛdanuṣṭhānaṃ teṣāmapi syāditi | dīkṣaṇīyā tu prakaraṇādhītā |
tena dīkṣiṣyamāṇa iti prakṛtādhikṛtaviśeṣaṇameva | ato niyogaikyājjyotiṣṭo-
māṅgamiti | na ca puruṣasaṃskāradvāreṇāpi kratvanupraveśaḥ svatantrapuruṣasaṃskārāt
sakṛtsaṃskṛtasya sata uttarakālaṃ prayogāṅgatvāt |

yadyevaṃ yadyārambhaṇīyā puruṣasaṃskārikā bhedanahomo’pi kasmāt puruṣa-
saṃskāra eva na kalpyate
 | uttaraṃ-kalpyamāne’pi pratinimittaṃ vyalīkotpattau

[p. 235]

punaḥ prasaṅgavijñānādāvṛttirevātra
 | asyārthaḥ---bhinne juhotīti bhedananimitta-
doṣanirharaṇadvāreṇa puruṣaḥ saṃskartavyaḥ | nimittaṃ pratibhedanaṃ doṣotpatteḥ tannirharaṇārthaṃ
punaḥ homaprasaṅgavijñānamastītyāvṛttireva homasya | punaścedanuṣṭhānāviśeṣaḥ tenedaṃ codya-
macodyameva | tatrāpi saṃskāraḥ prakaraṇāt sākṣādadhikṛtasamabhivyāhā[rāt](yoṃ)
bhedanādiṣu nimittānyathānupapattyā tu kalpitaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---na bhedanādīnāṃ
puruṣasambandho yena puruṣasaṃskārakatā syāt | kintu te kratvarthā eva | yadi teṣu sākṣā
dadhikāraḥ śrūyeta tadā bhavedevaṃ, kintu niradhikārā eva, bhedanādīnāṃ nimittatvena-
śravaṇāt | niradhikārasya ca prakaraṇādhītasya kratvarthatvameva | animittatā tu adhikā-
riviśeṣaṇatāmantareṇa na sambhavatīti kratvarthādhikṛtapuruṣaviśeṣaṇatā bhedanādīnāṃ
kalpyate |

nanu puruṣasaṃskārakatvepi ārambhanimittatvādasyārambhāvṛttau kimityāvṛttirna-
bhavati puruṣasaṃskārabhedanahomavadityāśaṅkyāha---iha punarnimittāntarotpāda eva
na bhavatīti nāvṛttiriti viśeṣaḥ
 | kasmānnimittāntarotpādo na bhavadītyatrāha---
abhinnatvādādyasya padārthasya
 | eṣo’bhiprāyaḥ---ādyapadārthānuṣṭhāne yogyatā puruṣasya
ārambhaṇīyayā kriyate | ekaścāsāvādyaḥ padārthaḥ | tena taṃ prati yogyatā na bhinnā
yogyatotpattyarthatā ca puruṣasyārambhaṇīyānuṣṭhāne nimittam | ekā cāsau | tena nimittā-
vṛttyabhāvānnārambhaṇīyāvṛttiḥ | tatra punarabhinnasya bhedanamiti siddho nimittabhedaḥ |
bhedanāntarotpannavyalīkabhedāt tannirharaṇārthaṃtāpi tatra bhinnaiva | tena puruṣasaṃskārakatve
tatrāvṛttirnimittāvṛtteḥ |

tadidaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa prayogāntarepi saṃskṛta eva labhyate iti samāne’hanī-
tyapi siddhamiti darśitam | darśapūrṇamāsāvarapsyamāna ityatra lṭṛṭpratyayena anadyatanabhavi-
ṣyadvihitena yasminnevāhani darśapūrṇamāsau kartavyau tasminnevāhani kṛtayā’’rambhaṇīyayā
ārambhayogyatā bhavatīti | taccāstyeva | yathā’sau sakṛdārambhaṇīyā kṛtā sā darśapūrṇa-
māsasamānāhassambandhinyeva | yathā caivaṃ tathā daśame yā dīkṣaṇīyādiṣvārambhaṇīyā-
śaṅkā samadhigatā sā somapūrvatvapakṣamāśritya | iṣṭapūrvatve hi saṃskṛtatvāt puruṣasya
nāśaṅkopapadyata ityanavadyam |

|| 11 || arthābhidhānasaṃyogānmantreṣu śeṣabhāvaḥ syāt
tatrācoditamaprāptaṃ coditābhidhānāt || 36 ||

[p. 236]

atra kila samavetārthatve dṛṣṭārthateti manvān āha---nanu ca dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthatvaṃ
mantrāṇāṃ na sambhavatīti saṃśaya evāyuktaḥ
 | yasmād ‘arthaikatvādekaṃ vākyaṃ
sākāṅkṣaṃ cedvibhāge syāt’
 ityuktam | dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthatve cārthabhedaḥ | tena mantraikyameva na
siddhayati | ekasya mantrasya dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthatvaṃ nopapadyata iti rāddhāntavacanavyaktayabhāvāt
saṃśaya evāyamayuktaḥ |

rāddhānte hi agnaye juṣṭaṃ nirvapāmīti samavetārtham pariśiṣṭamasamavetārtha-
madṛṣṭārthamiti prayojanabheda āpadyate | pariharati---ucyate
---naivātra dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthatayā
pūrvottarapakṣau yathāsaṅkhyaṃ kriyete | rāddhānte pariśiṣṭasyāpi nāsti adṛṣṭārthatvamiti
ekamantratvāvirodhaḥ |

kathaṃ tarhi pūvottarapakṣau kriyete
 ? uttaraṃ---samavetārthatayā
 | punaścodayati-
nanu samavetārthatve sati dṛṣṭārthatvamasamavetārthatve tvadṛṣṭārthatvamiti tadevedaṃ
dṛṣṭādṛṣṭārthatvaṃ pūrvottarapakṣayoḥ
 | pariharati---naitadevam | satyaṃ samavetārthatve
dṛṣṭārthatvaṃ asamavetārthatve tvadṛṣṭārthatā na śakyate vastum
 | kasmādityāha---
na parārthasyābhidhānasya kāryāntarakalpanā śakyā
 | stutyarthamapyabhidhānamarthaprati-
pattyarthameva nādṛṣṭārthaṃ śakyate kalpayituṃ pramāṇābhāvāt | etaccārthavādādhikaraṇe uktam |
yadi siddhānte’pi dṛṣṭārthatā kathaṃ tarhi samavetārthābhidhānaṃ pūrvapakṣavādī
manyate
 | yadi hi siddhānte adṛṣṭārthatvalobhena yuktaḥ pūrvapakṣaḥ | uttaraṃ---svārthā-
bhidhānalobhāt
 | na hi svārthatve sambhavati pārārthyaṃ nyāyyāmiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
stutyarthatve hi pārārthyaṃ samavetadevatāprakāśakatve svārthatvaṃ śabdānāmiti svārthatvalobhena
samavetārthatvaṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |

atra codayati - yuktaṃ svārthatvaṃ māntravarṇikasavitrādidevatākalpanāyāṃ

savitrādipadaiḥ tāsāmevābhidhānāt | gauṇakalpanāyāṃ tu kathaṃ ?
 yathāhi gauṇyā
vṛttyā agnireva savitrādipadaiḥ pratyāpyate tadā kathaṃ svārthatā ? gauṇo hi śabdaḥ
parārtha eva | uttaraṃ---atrāpi svārthaḥ prayoga iti siddhaṃ na śabdāntarārtham
stutyarthatayā savitrādipadānāmuccāraṇam
 | kintu tānyeva devatāṃ pratipāda-
yantīti | tena gauṇapakṣe’pi prayogasya svārthatvameveti siddhaṃ svārthatvaṃ pakṣadvaye’pi |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---nātraikāpi kalpanā sambhavati | pārārthyenā-
pyekavākyatvāvagamāt
 | devatāntarakalpanā gauṇakalpanā vā na sambhavati |

[p. 237]

stutyarthatayāpyekavākyatvāvagamāt sarvapadānāṃ gamyatāmekavākyatā tathāpi kimityāha-
gamyamāne caikavākyatve siddhaṃ mantrasya samavetārthatvamiti
 | na pratipadaṃ samavetārtha-
pratipādakatā laghvīti sākṣātpratīyamānaikavākyatā na śakyate’panhotum | tadva-
[śe]na pārārthyameva nyāyyam yathā syonaṃ te ‘ityatrānekavākyatāvagamyate tadā
tadanupraveśasamavetārthabhedena | tathā’trāpi bhavatu ityatrāha---vidyamāne hyarthe vibhajya-
mānasākāṅkṣatvaṃ na nāmāpekṣyate
 | kalpyamāne cārthe pramāṇābhāvānna tadapekṣatā
śakyate vaktum | na hi klṛptāmekavākyatāṃ kalpyo’rthabhedaḥ śaknoti vārayitum |
syonaṃ ta ityatra bhedo na kalpyaḥ | bhidyamānatvāt sadanakaraṇapuroḍāśapratiṣṭhāpanayoḥ |
ataḥ siddhaṃ pārārthye’pi keṣāṃcitpadānāṃ mantrasya samavetārthatvam |

kathaṃ punaḥ savitrādipadānāṃ stutyarthatvamityatrāha---pārārthyaṃ ca darśitaṃ
bhāṣyakāreṇa
 | savitrādipadāni yajamānaparatvena stutau varṇitāni | tatra ca puna-
rāśaṅkitaṃ yadi yajamānaparāṇyetāni evaṃ tarhi yajamānāśraya ūhaḥ prāpnoti | tatra
ko heturityatrāha---yajamānapakṣa ūhāśaṅkā prasavabalāt
 | prasavo’nujñā yajamāna-
sambandhinī kīrtyata iti bahuyajamānaprasavakīrtanāya savitrādipade vacanohaḥ śaṅkitaḥ |
sā cohaśaṅkā’vivakṣāyāmapi prasavasyopapadyata eva prasavakīrtanamiti nādṛtā
 |
nirākṛtetyarthaḥ | yadi vivakṣitatā syāt tadā anyatrāpi kīrtanārthamūhaḥ syāt |
avivakṣitatve tu naitatprakāśane prayojanamastītyanūhaḥ |

|| 12 || guṇaśabdastatheti cet || 38 ||

yadi savitrādayaśśabdāḥ nirvāpaikavākyatvādanyārthāḥ evaṃ tarhi
juṣṭāgniśabdāvapi anyārthāveva | tataścāsamavetārthatvaṃ asamavetārthatve cānūhaḥ |

asyārthaḥ---yadyapyagniḥ samaveta eva karmaṇi tathāpi yajñapatiṃ vardhānitivat
parārthatvāt mantreṇa samavetatvena nābhidhīyate | parārthatā ca nirvāpaviśeṣaṇatvenābhi-
dhānāt | asamavetārthatve ca yajñapatiśabdavadanūhaḥ |

atra bhāṣyakāro’gniśabdasyaivānūhamāha na juṣṭaśabdasya | tatra kāraṇaṃ
pṛcchati---kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇamagniśabdasyaivānūhaṃ bravīti | na juṣṭaśabdasyāpi
 |
uttaraṃ---samavetārthatvepyasambhavāt siddhānte pūrvapakṣe ca juṣṭaśabdenohaḥ
 | tena
viśeṣābhāvāt tasyānūho nocyate |

[p. 238]

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate
---satyaṃ nirvāpeṇaikavākyatvaṃ tathāpi devatā svārthatayaiva
nirvāpamākṣipantī tenaikavākyatāṃ yatīti na pārārthyaṃ śakyate vaktum | nirvāpeṇa
vinā yāgābhāvāt devatā svārthaṃ nirvāpamākṣipatīti na parārtho’gniḥ | apārārthye ca
samavetārthatvaṃ samavetārthatve ca vikṛtāvūha iti siddham | na ca ajuṣṭe juṣṭavacana-
mayuktaṃ nirvāpeṇa juṣṭateti | na prāṅ‌nirvāpādajuṣṭateti pariharati---naivedamajuṣṭe
juṣṭavacanaṃ
 kāryatvena juṣṭārthāvagamāt nirvāpeṇa juṣṭaṃ karomīti sambandhaḥ | na punaḥ
prāṅ‌nirvāpāt juṣṭaṃ santaṃ nirvapāmīti | yata evamabhisambandhaḥ ato’yamāśaṃsāyāṃ
bhūtavaccetyasya viṣayaḥ kāryasyāśaṃsanīyatvāt |

|| evaṃ vā ||

atra mukhye gauṇe vā’bhidhāne’sambhava iti kimadṛṣṭārtho bhavedayaṃ mantraḥ
dhānyamasīti taṇḍulāvāpe uta lakṣaṇayāpi tāvat samavetārthataiva nyāyyeti
cintyate
---dhānyaśabdena gauṇyā vṛttyā mukhyayā vā taṇḍulābhidhānāsambhavāt
taṇḍalāvāpe kimayamadṛṣṭārtho bhavenmantraḥ uta dhānyasambandhitvāttaṇḍulānāṃ dhānyaśabdo
lakṣaṇayā taṇḍulān pratipādayatīti samavetārtha evāyaṃ mantro bhavatviti cintā |

tatra pūrvapakṣavādī prayogasyaidamarthyābhāvāt adṛṣṭārthatāṃ manyate |

asyārthaḥ---yadi lakṣaṇayā dhānyaśabdaḥ taṇḍulān pratipādayet tadā dṛṣṭārtho
bhavenmantraḥ lakṣaṇāpi prayogaidamarththajñāne sati bhavati | na cātra dhānyaśabdastaṇḍulaprati-
pādanaparatvena prayukta iti pramāṇamasti | tena lakṣaṇānupapatteradṛṣṭārtha eva mantra iti |

kathaṃ punaḥ prayogaidamarthyānnāvagamyate dṛṣṭārthatetyatrāha---prayogapradhāno hi
vākyārthaḥ tadabhāve
 aśāvdyeva kāryakalpanā prāpnoti | prayogo hi vākyārthā-
vagame pradhānaṃ kāraṇam prayogābhāve taṇḍulaprakāśanaṃ kāryamiti śabdaśūnyaiva kalpanā |
na ca viniyogo no(so)papadyate adṛṣṭenāpi dvāreṇa tadupapatteḥ | tasmādadṛṣṭārthataiva |
idaṃ cātrabhipretam | viniyogena mantrasya tādarthyamavasīyate na padasya | padatātparya-
mūlā ca lakṣaṇopapattirityabhidhāyakatvenopayogābhāvāt adṛṣṭenaiva dvāreṇa viniyogo
mantrasyeti |

atrābhidhīyate---nādṛṣṭārthatā sambhavati | kasmādityatrāha---na hi lakṣaṇā
lokavatpratipattihetutayā vārayituṃ śakyate | yathā śālayo bhujyanta

[p. 239]

iti
 | asyārthaḥ - yadi lakṣaṇā pratipattihetutayā vārayituṃ pāryate tadā adṛṣṭārthatā
syāt | nacaitatsambhavati lakṣaṇāyā api lokavyavahāre pratipattihetutvena darśanāt
śālayo bhujyanta itivat bhavati loke pratipattihetuḥ | atra tu na sambhavatītyatrāha
-na ca sambhavantyāṃ lakṣaṇāyāṃ viniyogo’dṛṣṭakalpanāṃ sahate
 | tasmāllakṣaṇayā
samavetārtha evāyaṃ mantra iti yuktā kalpanā |

nanu ca prayogābhāvānna padavyāpāravattayā lakṣaṇā śakyate kalpayituṃ
viniyogena mantrasya tātparyāvagamo na padasya | tena dhānyapadaṃ lakṣaṇāvyāpāreṇa
taṇḍulāvagamakamiti na śakyate’vagantum | padavyāpāraścātra cintyate | kasmāditya-
trāha-padavyāpārāśritaścohaḥ satyapi vākyaprayojanatve samavetaprakāśanasya
 |
yadi hi dhānyapadasyopyamānapratipādanaṃ vyāpāra ityavagataṃ bhavati tadā vikṛtau
māṃsasyopyamānasya dhānyaśabdenāvagamayitumaśakyatvāt māṃsaśabda ūhyate nānyathā |
yata eva mantratātparyamātreṇa padavyāpārātmikā lakṣaṇā na siddhayati ata eva keci
llakṣaṇaivātra vivakṣiteti manvānāḥ mṛgo’sītyūhaṃ manvate
 | asyārthaḥ-yāvallakṣaṇā-
paryanto viniyogo na varṇyate tāvat padāśritā lakṣaṇā na siddhayati | tena
lākṣaṇikatvaṃ padasyecchatā evaṃ vaktavyam - lakṣaṇayopyamānaṃ vaktavyamiti viniyogārtha
iti | tataśca vikṛtāvapi mṛgo’sītyūhena bhavitavyaṃ na māṃsamasīti | atha tu lakṣaṇā
na vivakṣitā tadā mantratātparyāvagamāt dhinotīti dhānyamiti yaugikadhānyaśabdā-
śrayaṇena taṇḍulapratipādakatvopapatteḥ māṃsepi yogasya sambhavāt ūho na syāt |
tadāha---

anūhaṃ vā manyate | kutaḥ ? arthavādadarśanāt
 | dhinuhi devāniti
mantrārthavādadarśanāt | bhavatvarthavādaḥ tathāpi kathamanūha ityāha---dhānyaśabda-
prayogasiddhistaṇḍuleṣvarthavādādeva
 | arthadhādagatadevatādhinvanayogāt dhānyamiti
taṇḍulā ucyante tathā māṃsamapi tena prakṛtivikṛtyoryaugikatvādanūhaḥ |

atrocyate---mantraprayojanaṃ samavetaprakāśanamityuktaṃ prathame’dhyāye |
atastadeva tāvatprayojanam
 | yatpunarapadavyāpārādūho nopapadyata iti tadayuktam
kuta ityāha---lakṣaṇāpi tadāśritaiva gaṅgāghoṣādiṣu darśanāt
 | asyārthaḥ-
mantrasya samavetaprakāśanaṃ kāryamiti prayojane’vagate kathaṃ tatsiddhayatītyapekṣāyāṃ
pratipadaṃ śaktivimarśo bhavati | tatra tāvat dhānyapapadavyatirekiṇāṃ padānāṃ vā[na]

[p. 240]

prakāśanasāmarthyamasti dhānyaśabdasya tu lokaprasiddhalakṣaṇāvṛttyāśrayaṇena upyamāna-
taṇḍulaprakāśanadvāreṇāvāpapratipādakatā sambhavatīti tadāśritā lakṣaṇā’’śrīyate |

yasmānmantratātparyānusāreṇeyaṃ lakṣaṇā na punaḥ lakṣaṇaiva vivakṣitā tasmānnātra
lakṣaṇā vikṛtau, ātmāyattatvātprayogaptya ārṣeṇa dhānyaśabdena lakṣaṇayāpi māṃsasyābhi-
dhātumaśakteḥ tadyuktaṃ tāvat padāntaraṃ prakṣeptavyam | tatra mukhye sambhavati ko nāma
lākṣaṇikaṃ prakṣipet tadāhuḥ---

‘ṛjumārgeṇa siddhayantaṃ ko’rthaṃ vakreṇa sādhayet’
 iti | ārṣe tu prayoge
kiṃ kriyatām ? | tathaiva prayoge yasyāmapi vikṛtau dhānyaśabdo lakṣaṇayāṃ varṇayituṃ
śakyate tasyāmapyanūha eva prakṛtāviva ārṣeṇaivābhidhānasiddheḥ | nāpyayaṃ yaugiko
dhānyaśabdaḥ śakyate rūḍhereva prayojanasiddheḥ rūḍhyā hi yaugikatve parihṛte tadāśritā
lakṣaṇaiva śreyasī na yaugikatvam | yatra tu lakṣaṇayāpi prayojana[ā]siddhiḥ tatra
bhavatyeva yaugikāśrayaṇam kāryapradhānatvānmantrārthasya yato’yaṃ dhānyaśabdo na yaugikaḥ
tasmāt stutimātramarthavādaḥ, na tu śabdapravṛttaye yogopasthāpakaḥ |

nanu ca dhānyaśabdasya taṇḍulapratipādanaparatayā prayoga ityanavagame kathaṃ
lakṣaṇetyatrāha---na cātra prayogeṇa prayojanam
 | kasmādityatrāha---samaveta-
prakāśane hi sa iṣyate
 | samavetamarthaṃ padaṃ prakāśayatu ityetadarthaṃ prayoga iṣyate |
taccānyenāpi prakāreṇa siddham | mantratātparyāvagamenāpi pūrvoktarītyā siddhamityatra
prayogaḥ | yadi lākṣaṇiko’pi mantreṣu śabdasyārtho gṛhyate gauṇamukhyopa-
cārastarhi mandaprayojanaḥ syāt
 | uttaraṃ---na mandaprayojanaḥ, avagatau
tulyaprayojanatvāt
 | gauṇatvamukhyatvāvagatyavasthāyāṃ tulyaṃ prayojanam | nimitte
viśeṣa ityuktaṃ tṛtīye | nimittāvasthāṃyāṃ viśeṣo mukhyasya nirapekṣaṃ nimittaṃ gauṇasya
1mukhyāvagatisāpekṣaṃ sādṛśyamiti gauṇamukhyayorviśeṣaḥ | imameva viśeṣaṃ darśayituṃ
gauṇamukhyavicāraḥ | anena viśeṣeṇa liṅgaviniyojye viśeṣo na punargauṇamukhyavicāre-
ṇaitaduktam mukhya evāvagatirheturnānya iti | tasmāllakṣaṇayāpi samavetārthatvaṃ mantrāṇā-
miti siddham |

|| 13 || codite tu parārthatvāt vidhivadavikāraḥsyāt || 40 ||

1. R mukhyā evā

[p. 241]

kathaṃ punariḍopahūteti iḍāmupahvayasveti śrutyā viniyuktasya mantrasya
kāryāntaraṃ yajñapativṛddhiprakāśanamāśaṅkyate
 | uttaraṃ---viniyoga evātra
cintyate | kiṃ yajñapatiṃ vardhānitīḍopāhvāne viniyuktaṃ neti
 | asyārthaḥ---
pratīkenāyaṃ śrauto viniyogaḥ | tatra kiṃ yajñapatiṃ vardhānityapi iḍopāhvāne viniyoge
sati cintā |

tatra pūrvapakṣī liṅgaprakaraṇābhyāṃ viniyogāntaraṃ manyate | yajñapatitvena yajamā-
nasya vṛddhiviṣayatvena yajñapatiṃ vardhāniti tāvat prakaraṇapāṭhāt prakaraṇino’ṅgam |
śaknoti cedaṃ yajñapatiṃ vṛddhiviśiṣṭaṃ vaktum | asti ca tādṛśo yajñe | tatprakāśanaṃ
ca kratorupakārakaṃ protsāhajananāt | tena tatraivāsya viniyogo bhavati | kathaṃ
punarupakramopasaṃhārayorantarnipatitaṃ vākyāntaraṃ kalpayituṃ śakyate paraspara-
sākāṅkṣatvena padānāmekavākyatvāvagamāt
 |

uttaraṃ---viniyogavaśānmantrāṇāṃ yajuṣi kalpanā | vākyatvena nānya-
padākāṅkṣayā syonaṃ te sadanaṃ ithi pradarśitam
---asyārthāḥ---yajuṣi yā
mantrāṇāṃ vākyatvena kalpane yadekaṃ vākyamiti sā viniyogavaśāt yāvatāmekatra
viniyogaḥ padānāṃ tāvatāmekārthatvaṃ ekārthatve caikavākyatvaṃ na punaranyapadāpekṣayā
vākyatvakalpanā | yāvanti parasparāpekṣaṇīyapadāni tāvantyekaṃ vākyamiti vākyamiti saiṣā
kalpanā | yathā syonaṃ te ityatra satyapi sākāṅkṣatve viniyogabhedādbhinnavākyatvaṃ
tathehāpi viniyogavaśāt mantravākyabhedaḥ tasmādvibhāgaḥ vibhāgāt kāryabhedaḥ kārya-
bhedāccohabhedaḥ yajamānadvitve bahutve coho’nyathā cānūho yajñapatiśabde pariśiṣṭabhāge
iḍābhede nohabhedaḥ |

evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate---spādetadevaṃ yadi viniyogāntaraṃ yajamāna-
vṛddhiviṣayaṃ syāt | iha tu kalpyam | na ca kalpyo’rthaḥ siddhasyārthasyā-
vivakṣāyāṃ heturbhavati
 | asyārthaḥ---upakramopasaṃhārābhyāṃ iha tāvat yajñapatiṃ
vardhānityasya padanibandhanaikavākyatā’vagamyate iḍopahvāne ca śrutyā upakramopa-
saṃhārau viniyuktau | tadekavākyatayā tasyāpi tatraiva viniyogaḥ pratīyate |
yajñapativṛddhau tu liṅgena viniyogaḥ kalpyaḥ | na ca klṛptaḥ kalpyena bādhituṃ
śakyate | tasmānmantrata eva mantramevālocya yadekavākyatvamavagamyate tadeva
yajuṣa ekavākyatve hetuḥ | yatsyonaṃ ta ityuktaṃ tatra prakaraṇena viniyoge sati

[p. 242]

liṅgenaiva yajuṣo vibhāgo bhedena sambandhaḥ | sadanakaraṇe pratiṣṭhāpane ca liṅgena
viniyogaḥ | ato yuktametat padākāṅkṣānirapekṣaṃ yajuṣo dvitvaṃ parikalpyate | iha
punaḥ liṅgaṃ prakaraṇaṃ ca śrauta eva viniyoga upapadyata iti siddhaṃ pārārthyaṃ iḍopāhvāna-
paratvaṃ ca | tataścānūhaḥ | kenāpi yajamānenopalakṣaṇasiddheḥ vidhivat | yajamāna-
sammitaudumbarītyekenāpi sammāne kṛtārthamiti pradarśitaṃ bhāṣye ||

|| 14 || vikārastatpradhāne syāt || 41 ||

nanu sūktavākādhikaraṇe phalamidaṃ āyurāśāsanaṃ āśāsyamānamāyuḥ
phalamityuktam | tenātra phalabhāgitayā na parārtho yajamāna iti kathamatra
pūrvapakṣaḥkriyate
 |

samādhatte---satyaṃ phalamidaṃ, natu yajamānena sambadhyamānasyāsya
kaścidviśeṣo vidyate
 | phalamapyetadyajamānena sambadhyamānaṃ na guṇatayā sambadhyate
tena ta yajñapatimityanena na kaścidviśeṣaḥ yasmāt nāvaśyaṃ phalavattvāya yajamānenā-
yurāśāsanaṃ viśeṣaṇīyaṃ kartṛtayaiva phalamabhisambadhyate tathāpi mantrārthopapatteḥ | na ca
phalavatā phalaṃ prati pradhānabhūtenāyurāśāsanaṃ viśeṣaṇīyaṃ kevalaṃ kartṛtayaivāśāsanānvaye’pi
mantrāvākyasyārthopapatteḥ | ato yajamānasammitaudumbarīvaditi nānyo yajamāno
nāpi phalavattayetyasamavetaṃ manyate
 | yathā yajamānasammitaudumbarī bhavatītyatra
anyo yajamāno na gṛhyate | na ca phalitayaiva samyadhyate, kintu kartṛtayaiva tathehāpi |
tena samavetasyāpi yajamānasya parārthatvenābhidhānānna samavetārthatvaṃ
mantrasya
 | tataścānūho yajñapatiśabdavat |

atrocyate---avaśyamatra 1phalitaivāśāsanaheturiti mantavyam
 |
asyārthaḥ---pha़labhāgitaivāśāsanahetuḥ tena yadyapi āśāsane kartṛtvaṃ tathāpi phala-
bhāgitayā prādhānyādapārārthyamityūhārham | kutaḥ phalabhāgitaiva āśāsanahetuḥ ?

uttaraṃ---yatra hi anyataḥ phalāvagamaḥ tatra kartṛtayāpi mantrārtha upapadyata
ityuktam | iha tu mantrādeva phalakalpanā | sa cet phalavattayā na sambadhyate

mantraścet phalavattāṃ na bodhayati tato mantrāt phalakalpanaiva vihanyeta | tadvidhāte ca
1. phalatayaiva

[p. 243]

haratau mantrasyāṅgatvaṃ nopapadyata ityuktaṃ tṛtīye | mantro hi phalaprakāśanena karmaṇyaṅgatāṃ
yāti | aphalatve tu kiṃ bodhayan aṅgatāmiyāt | tasmādaparārthatvādyajamānasya sama-
vetasya prakāśanaṃ tataścohasiddhiḥ |

|| 15 || asaṃyogāttadartheṣu tadviśiṣṭaṃ pratiyeta || 42 ||

agneyī subrahmaṇyetyatra cintyate---kiṃ harivadādayo’pyatrohitavyā neti |
tadarthamidaṃ cintyate---samavetārthā harivadādayaśśabdāḥ utāsamavetārthāḥ |

kathaṃ punaḥ samavetārthāḥ kathamasamavetārthāḥ?
 uttaraṃ---yadīndrasya dravyasya
svarūpaṃ prakāśayanti tadā samavetārthāḥ
 | yadi darbhaistṛṇīta haritairitivat guṇābhi-
dhānadvāreṇendra eva prakāśyate tataḥ samavetendraprakāśakatvāt samavetārthatvam | atha
stutiṃ kurvanti tato na samavetārthāḥ
 yadi harivattādikaṃ indraṃ stotumucyate tadā-
’samavetārthāḥ |

tatra rāddhāntavādī stutimeva nyāyyāṃ manyate stutyaiva nigadārthopa-
patteḥ
 | itarathā hi guṇaparatvena lakṣaṇā syāt | rāddhāntopakramo vyākhyātaḥ |

pūrvapakṣyāśaṅkā punaḥ arthavādāt tatsvarūpāvagatyartho nigada iti
buddhirutpadyate
 | indrasvarūpapratipattyartho’yaṃ nigadaḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ harivattādayaḥ indra-
mupalakṣayituṃ kṣamāḥ na hi teṣāmindrasambandho’vagataḥ yathā haritasya darbhasambandha iti |

atrocyate-arthavādāt guṇasambandhāvagamāt bhavatīndrasvarūpāvagatiḥ
 |
kaḥ punarasāvarthavāda ityāha---pūrvāparapakṣāviti darśitam
 |

rāddhāntastu arthavādo’pi tāvat stutyaivopapadyate svarūpaparatve’rtha-
vādataiva hīyeta
 | yo’yamarthavādaḥ indrasambandhiharivattādipratipādanaparaḥ so’pi tāvat
stutyaivānvaye sati arthavādo bhavati | yadi pratīyamānārthasvarūpaparo bhavati tadā artha-
vādataiva hīyate | yasmāt stutyarthena vidhīnāmityarthavādatvamuktam | yadi cedaṃ vastu
yathāpratīyamānamevāśrīyate tadā ādimattā syāt kāṇvāyanādyarthapratipādakatvāt
ādimattāprasaṅgaśca vedasya syāt | tasmādayamartho yathāśruto na grāhyaḥ |

evaṃ tāvadindrasyaivaṃrūpatā nāstītyuktamabhyupagamyāha---tasmādyadyapīndraḥ evaṃ-
svarūpaḥ tathāpi harivattādayaḥ stutyarthā evamu[yu]ktāḥ | śrutistābhyāṃ

[p. 244]

hīdaṃ haratīti copapadyate
 | indrasya harivattāṃ prati tābhyāṃ hīdaṃ sarvaṃ haratītyupa-
pattiryuktirucyate | tena harivattaiva yuktyā pratipādyata iti jñāyate | evaṃ cābhyupa-
gamyavādamātraṃ mūlayuktimāha | yatra punaḥ svarūpepi pramāṇābhāvaḥ tatra kimanyadataḥ
stuteḥ | yathā ca svarūpapratipattaye nālamarthavādaḥ tathā vyākhyātaṃ bhāṣye | tasmāt
harivadādīnāṃ noho’samavetārthābhidhānāt |

atra bhāṣyakāreṇānūhaṃ darśiyitvā uktaṃ---‘‘tattvetanna rocayante yājñikāḥ’’

iti | tadākṣipati---na rocayante iti kathane kiṃ prayojanaṃ siddhāntānupa-
yogāt | yājñikānuṣṭhānavirodhena ca pratyuta kṣatihetutvāt
 |

samādhatte---evaṃ bacanārtheṣu vyāmoha upapadyate
 | ato vipratipattau
jijñāseyamityarucipradarśanena yājñikānāṃ vacanārtheṣu vyāmoho’stītyucyate | ato
vipratipattau jijñāseyaṃ kriyata iti |

nigadāntaravidhāne tu na doṣaḥ | athavā yajñikānāmarucipradarśanena nigadā-
ntarameva āgneyī subrahmaṇyetyatrāstīti pradarśayati | tena na kaściddoṣo nyāyasya |

|| 16 || asaṃyogāttadartheṣu tadviśiṣṭaṃ pratīyeta || 42 ||

athedamanyadudāhriyate---nanvatra samavetatvepi guṇānāṃ mantrasya
stutyarthataiva nyāyyā śruteḥ | na ca stutyarthatve’dṛṣṭārthatvaṃ, prarocanayā
dṛṣṭārthatvopapatteḥ
 | somavikrayiṇo hyekahāyanīstutau prarocanā jāyata eveti
dṛṣṭārthatvam | svarūpe punarabhidheye’ṅgīkriyamāṇe ekahāyanīmātrasya codatatvāt aco-
ditasvarūpābhidhāne’dṛṣṭaṃ kalpanīyam | tasmāt pūrvapakṣa eva nopapadyate | pūrveṇaivādhi-
karaṇena gatārthatvāt |

atrocyate - ayamabhiprāyaḥ pūrvapakṣavādinaḥ sambhavati
 | ekāhāyanī-
svarūpāpanhavaḥ śakyate kalpayitum | nanu adṛṣṭārthamekahāyanīsvarūpaparatvamityatrāha-
yathāpratipannasya ca prayojanakalpanā
 | yathābhūtaṃ vastu pratīyate ya[ta]thābhūtasya
prayojanaṃ kalpyaṃ na punaḥ prayojanavaśāt arthapratipattiḥ | tena yadi dṛṣṭaṃ nāsti
bhavatvadṛṣṭaṃ, kintvatra dṛṣṭamapyastītyāha---bhavati ca svarūpādapi prarocanetyadṛṣṭa-
kalpanāpi parihṛtaiva sutarāṃ svarūpāvagameva vikreturbhavati prarocaneti
 |

[p. 245]

atra codayati---stutāvapi prarocanā | svarūpāvagame punaḥ gauravādaraḥ
kiṃkṛtaḥ?
 | pariharati---ananyavyāpāratākṛtaḥ
 | na hi svarūpaparatve sambhavati
parārthakalpanā ātmānaṃ labhate | svarūpāvagamapakṣe hi tasyai śṛtamityādipadānāṃ
svarūpaparatvam | stutau parārthastutiparatvam | na caitannayāyyam | svārthaparatvasambhavāt |
tasmātpūrveṇa vaiṣamyam pramāṇānavagamādindrasvarūpasya | iha tu pramāṇāntareṇa svarūpā-
vagamāt guṇipratipattyarthaṃ hi guṇavacanaṃ ityūha eva nyāyyaḥ |

hāyanyāḥ
 | tena stutyarthamevāsadapyāropaṇam | bhāvinīṃ pravṛttimaṅgīkṛtyocyata
iti cet

 tarhi nigadasya siddhamasama-
vetābhidhānam |
