|| 17 || liṅgaviśeṣanirdeśātsamānavidhāneṣvaprāptā sarasvatī
strītvāt || 45 ||

nanu cātra prātipadikārthasyaiva pradhānatvāt naiva liṅgavacanānurodhena
saṅkoca upapadyate samānavidhānepi pāśasyeva
 | yathā pāśasya prādhānyānnotkarṣo
mantrasya | tasmāt pūrvapakṣa evātra jyāyāniti |

atrocyate - syādetadevaṃ yadi liṅgādvacanācca niṣkṛṣṭāntaraṃ sarvanāma-
śabdenābhidhīyate
 | asyārthaḥ - sannihitavacanatvāt sarvanāmnāṃ yādṛśaṃ sannihitaṃ
tādṛśamevābhidhīyate | liṅgavacanayuktaṃ ca sannihitamiti tathābhūtameva prātipadike-
nāpyabhidhīyate iti viśiṣṭaviṣayatvāt prātipadikasyaiva viśiṣṭe viniyogaḥ | pāśe
punaryuktaṃ pāśamātrābhidhānāt |

nanvatrāpi sarvanāmnā vacanaviniyuktena vibhaktirahitena sannihitamātraṃ
saṅkhyāliṅgaviyuktamucyate yadvastuvṛttena saṅkhyāvalliṅgavacca
 | etaduktaṃ bhavati-

[p. 246]

yadyapi sannihitasya vastunaḥ saṅkhyāliṅge staḥ tathāpi sarvānāmamātreṇa vibhaktirahitena
paśutvamevocyate vibhaktyā tu saṅkhyāliṅgayorabhidhānam | tena prātipādikena meṣyā
api sannihitatvādabhidhānaṃ śakyate kartum | prātipadikaṃ ca liṅgādvalīyaḥ | tasmāt
prātipadikārtha evānena mantreṇābhidhīyata iti liṅgavacanānurodhena saṅkoco yuktaḥ |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate - satyamevaṃ saṅkhyāvalliṅgavacca yattadevābhidhīyate
sarvannāmnā
 | nanu saṅkhyāliṅgavibhaktibhiśca saṅkhyā’bhidhīyate ṭābādibhiśca strītvaṃ
tathāpi tvatra[kiṃ]liṅgavaditi cintāyāṃ sannidhānādvayaktireva pratīyate na punaḥ paśutvam |
vyaktiścet sarvanāmnā pratīyate na liṅgavacanaviyuktā śakyate pratipattuṃ,
tasyāstadyuktatvāt pāśavat paśutvavacca
 | yathā pāśatvaṃ pāśaprātipadikena
saṅkhyāliṅgarahitaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate evaṃ paśutvavacca, yathā paśutvaṃ paśuprātipadikena
vyaktimātraṃ liṅgasaṅkhyāviyuktaṃ sākṣācchabdena sannidhāpitaṃ prāsmai paśave agniṃ bharata
ityevaṃvidhe vākye pratyetuṃ śakyate tathā vyaktirna śakyate |

nanu vyaktimātraṃ saṅkhyāliṅgayorāśrayabhūtaṃ pratīyatāṃ tasyāpi sanni-
hitatvāt
 | pariharati - keyaṃ vyaktimātratā nāma yā sarvanāmnocyate
 |
uttaraṃ---sannidhānāviśeṣāt sannihitavyaktimātraṃ pratīyatāmiti
 | yā vyakti-
rliṅgasaṅkhyānvitā tasyāḥ svarūpamapi sannihitaṃ, tena svarūpasyāpi sannidhānāviśeṣāt
bhavatyeva sarvanāmnā’bhidhānamiti rāddhāntī vadati | sannihitaṃ cetsāmānyaṃ na tat
sarvanāmaprātipadikābhidheyam sannihitavacanatvāt sarvānāmnām
 sannihitamātraṃ
yadi sāmānya tadā sarvanāmnā na śakyate vaktuṃ samānyasya vāstavasannidhānā-
bhāvāt | vyaktireva hi vāstavena sannidhānena sannihitā bhavati | sāmānyaṃ tu
śabdena sannidhāpyate | yadītyasaṃśayepi saṃśayavacanam | tasmāt vibhāgaśaḥ prāti-
padikenāśrayamātraṃ vibhaktyā saṅkhyā liṅgamiti vibhaktyartho na śakyate’navagantuṃ
ṛte paśuśabdasāmānādhikaraṇyāt | tacca nāsti | ataḥ sakalaprātipadikārthāvagateḥ
savibhaktikaprātipadikārthāvagatireva sannidhānopādhinānveti | vākye saliṅgasaṅkhyā
vyaktiḥ sannidhānopādhinā idaṃśabdenābhidhīyamanā asya vākyasyārthaḥ | ato na
strīliṅgayuktaṃ dravyamātraṃ vākyārthe’nvitatayā śakyate pratipattumityanimittamapadārthaḥ
sārasvatī | animittatve cāpadārthatve ca tādarthyaṃ mantrasya | tasmādyuktameva strītvāt
sārasvatī adhriguvacanamaprāpteti | tārtīyapaśvadhikaraṇapūrvapakṣaprayojanavicāro’yamityuktaṃ
bhāṣye | tena prayojanaṃ na vaktavyam |

|| 18 || āmnātādanyadadhikāre vacanādvikāraḥ
syāt || 50 ||

keyaṃ vādhacintā aprāpticintā vā ?
 | anenāsaṅgatimāśaṅkate | yadi
irāpadena girāpadaṃ prāptameva bādhyate iti tadā dāśamikī cintā | atherāpada-

prāptayarthaṃ itīhaiva cintopapadyate
 | asyārthaḥ - girāpadakārye guṇavacana irāpadamanena

yajñāyajñīye sānni stutirūpaṃ kāryaṃ kartuṃ pravṛtte girāpadena vinā kartumaśakteḥ girāpade
ākṣipyamāṇe eva tatkārye irāpadaniyamāt tadevopādīyate | tena kāryavaśene-

śyate | kintu tadapi kāryādevohyate |

|| 17 || apragāṇāncabdānyatve tathābhūtopadeśaḥ syāt || 54 ||

kathaṃ punaratra saṃśayaḥ ? yāvatā apragītasya stutisādhanatvānupapatteḥ
pragāṇenaiva bhavitavyam
 | uttaraṃ---taddhitanirdeśādeva
 | evadeva vivṛṇeti---
pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ avikṛtādirāpadādayaṃ taddhitaḥ matau chaḥ
 sūktasāmnoḥ
ityasminnadhikāre vimuktādibhyo’ṇ iti matvarthe sāmaviṣaye’ṇ‌pratyayo’yam | sa
cāvikṛtādirāpadāt | tasmādavikṛtamevedamirāpadam | yadi hi vikṛtaṃ prayujyate tadā
vikārānurūpastaddhitaḥ syāt | vikāre sati ā irā itiśabdarūpaṃ, tattu vimuktādiṣu
na paṭhitamiti matau cha iti cha eva syāt | na cāsāvasti | tasmādavikṛtamirāpadaṃ
prayoktavyamiti |

kaḥpunaratra taddhitārthaḥ
 | pṛcchataścāyamāśaḥ-vikārārtha evāyaṃ taddhitaḥ |
gānarūpaśca vikāraḥ prāpta evānūdyata iti | uttaraṃ---irāpadayuktaṃ gānaṃ kartavyaṃ
matvarthe’yama(ya)ṇ‌pratyaya ityarthaḥ | tasya pratipadoktatvāt lakṣaṇapratipadoktayoḥ
pratipadoktasyaiva grahaṇāt |

nanu cāpragītatvādyoga eva gānayoga eva na sambhavati
 | pariharati-
sambhavati tadyogaḥ, tatkāryakaraṇatvāditi
 | ayamabhiprāyaḥ-sāmakāryakaraṇatvā-
ttasya bhavati sāmnastatsambandhitvaṃ tasmādapragītopadeśo’yaṃ taddhitāntopadeśena jñātaḥ |
rāddhāntavādino’pi taddhitanirdeśādeva svarūpamātropadeśānavagatiḥ
 | asyārthaḥ-

[p. 250]

nāyaṃ vimuktāditvāttaddhitaḥ | kintu tasya vikāra iti | yathā caivaṃ tathā’tra vakṣyāmaḥ |
vikārataddhite’smin svarūpamātropadeśo nāvagamyate | gānapratyākhyānena svarūpamātro-
padeśo nāvagamyate | ko’bhiprāya
 iti pṛcchato’yamabhiprāyaḥ---bhavatu vikārataddhitaḥ
tathāpi apragāṇarūpo vikāro vidhāyiṣyata iti |

uttaraṃ---vikāravidhāne hi svarūpavidhānānupapattiḥ vikārasya svarūpasya
ca vidhāne vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt
 | bhavatu tarhi vikāramātravidhānaṃ ityatrāha---
na cāvihite svarūpe vikāramātravidhānamupapadyate
 | nirāśrayasya tasyānupapatteḥ |
na ca vācyamaupādānikaḥ svarūpavidhirbhaṃviṣyati irāpadavikāro hi asatīrāpadopadeśe
nopapadyata iti arthādirāpadākṣepa ityavaśyāśrayaṇīyaprathamopanipātisvarūpavidhinaiva
vidherupapatteḥ vikārasya gītisvarūpasya arthaprāptānuvādasambhavāt | tasmādvikārasyāyamanu-
vādaḥ | svarūpamātraṃ tu vidhīyate |

kena punaḥ prāptasyānuvādaḥ ? kāryata eva
 | na hyavikṛtaṃ sāmnaḥ kāryakaraṃ
bhavati | kāryaṃ hīrāpadaṃ vihitam | kathaṃ ca tatsāmakārye bhavati yadi pragītākṣara-
kāryaṃ bhavati | tasmāt kāryata eva gānaṃ prāptasanūdyate |

kasmāt punaḥ avikārataddhito’yaṃ uktametat vimuktāditvādayaṃ taddhita
ityatrāha---vacanasaṃyogācca nārthāntare taddhita āśaṅkanīyaḥ
 | na girāgireti
brūyāt iti prakramāt vacanamuccāraṇamavagamyate | tenerāvacanamiti vacanasambandhānna
vimuktāditaddhito’yam | sāmni viṣaye hi sa taddhitaḥ | na cerāpadoccāraṇamātraṃ
sāma, kiṃ tarhi ? sāmāvayavaḥ | yaccoccāraṇaṃ gītātmakaṃ bhavati tacchabdavikārātma-
kamiti vikārataddhita evāyam | tasmādvikārānuvāda evāyam | yato’yaṃ
svarūpamātropadeśoṃ na gītiprakhyātaṃ ata evāvikṛtāt taddhitotpattiradoṣaḥ | pratyuta
vikṛtāttaddhitotpattireva doṣaḥ | vikṛtasya vikārābhāvāt |

atha viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ kasmānna bhavati
 apragāṇarūpavikāraviśiṣṭamirāpadaṃ
kasmānna vidhīyate | uttaraṃ---sakṛttaddhitayorubhayoḥ sambandhāvagatiḥ
 kriyā-
kārakayoriva kārye yasya vidhānaṃ tasya kutaścitkārye vidhānam yathā somena yajete-
tyadhikāra vidhau | tatra ca parasparānvayāvagame sati viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ yathā somena
karaṇabhūtena avacchinnāyā yāgakriyāyāḥ | iha ca stutikārye irāpadasya vidhānam |

[p. 251]

na ca vikāro viśeṣaṇam | kintu vikāraṃ prati tasya viśeṣaṇatvam | tena kṛttaddhiteṣu
pratyayārthapradhāneṣu pratyayārthaviśiṣṭaprakṛtyarthavidhānaṃ nāsti viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭabhāvānavagamāta |

kiñca yugapadvidhānamapi kriyākārakavannopapadyate prakṛtiprāptisavyapekṣā hi
vikārā bhavanti | kāraṇakartṛnirdeśāśca ṇvulādayaḥ kriyāprāptisavyapekṣāḥ | prāptāyāṃ
prakṛtau tatsavyapekṣasya vikārasya viśeṣaṇatvena vidhānaṃ nāprāptāyām | tathā kriyāyāṃ
prāptāyāṃ ṇvulādayo’kriyāviśeṣaṇatvena kartrādīnyapayeyuḥ nānyathā | pratyayārthasyaiva
pradhānatvāditi nāsti viśiṣṭavidhānam | tasmāt prakṛtividhānameva nyāyyam na
viśiṣṭavidhānamiti |

yattu prayojanaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇoktaṃ praprapadaṃ praprīpadaṃ vā prayoktavyaṃ
pūrvapakṣe rāddhānte ca praprīpadameveti | tat pūrvādhikaraṇasyaiva pradarśitamiti
pratibhāti
 | tattvayuktamiheti bhāvaḥ |

samarthayate---yatpunarubhayorapyadhikaraṇayoḥ iti na doṣaḥ
 | kimiti
punarirāpadaṃ parityajya anyatprayojanamuktaṃ tadāha---kathamiti
 | uttaraṃ---tadge-
yamityupadeśena vikalpo nāpragītāśaṅkā
 | upadiṣṭatvānna vikalpaḥ tadgeyaṃ āī
rācādākṣāsāi gānavidhānācca nāpragītāśaṅkā | praprapade punaḥ ubhayamidaṃ nirasanīyamiti
tadevādhikaraṇadvayasya prayojanam |

iti śrīmahāmahopādhyāyaśālikanāthamiśrakṛtau
ṛjuvimalāyāṃ pañcikāyāṃ navamādhyāyasya
prathamaḥ pādaḥ

atra bhāṣyakāreṇa kiṃ gītiviśiṣṭaṃ mantravākyaṃ sāma uta gītimātrameveti
vicārya varṇakāntaramārabdham | kiṃ nityānyūhasāmāni utānityānīti | tatra varṇi-
te’pi kasmādvarṇakāntaramityatrāha---atra dvitīyādhyāyacintayā sahārthāntara-
varṇanaṃ saptamādhyāyena sahāpaunaruktyakathanārtham | iha punaruttaravivakṣayaiva
parihāraḥ | atra pūrvottarapakṣāvaparihāryāveveti adhikaraṇāntaravarṇanā
 |
asyārthaḥ---yasmādiha navame uttaravivakṣayaiva punaruktiparihāraḥ atrāpi parihāre pūrvottara-
pakṣaracanā kartuṃ na yuktā | na ca tāva(sā)parihāryāvevetyadhikaraṇāntaravarṇanaṃ kartuṃ
śakyate, sūtrakāreṇa sākṣāt kṛtatvāt tasmādetaddoṣa1parihārāya varṇakāntaraṃ kṛtam |

kasmātpunaratrottaravivakṣayaiva parihāro yāvatā dvitīyenādhyāyena sahānyathaiva
paunaruktyaṃ parihṛtam | dvitīye hi pragītepi mantravākye sāmti mantratrevidhyaṃ siddhayati |
2tadarthaṃ ca gītiṣu sāmākhyetyuktam | tena naitena a(ta)tra prayojanaṃ gītimātraṃ sāmeti
tatrāha---dvitīyena saha yadarthāntaravarṇanaṃ tatsaptamādhyāyacintayā saha[ā/?/]
paunaruktyārthaṃ
, saptamādhyāyacintayā tu yatpaunaruktyaṃ tasyottaravivakṣayaiva parihāraḥ |

nanu nityānityavicāro’yamihāsaṅgata ityatrāha---idaṃ cādhikaraṇamuttara-
cintārthamaupoddhātikamiti
 | yadi nitya ūhaḥ tataḥ auk‌thikyamūhasāmasvarūpa-

vasthitasvarūpakathanāya na kāryaupayikatayā kāryabhūtohopāyatvena na prārabdhavyam |
atha tu kṛtaka ūhastato yathānyāyaṃ yathāvacanaṃ cohaḥ kartavya iti tatparamauk‌thi-
kyamārabdhavyam |

dhānavyāpṛtāyā eva ṛco guṇabhūtaṃ sāmeti bruvate
 | asyārthaḥ---akarmakāle
sāmnaḥ prayuktatvāt punaḥ karmaṇi prayogo na syāditi saṃskārakarmapakṣe dūṣaṇamāpadyate
taccedama(ca)dūṣakṣam | ye ca saṃskāratāṃ sāmno manyante tepyarthābhidhānaṃ kurvantyā ṛcaḥ

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate-guṇabhūtaṃ sāma; kutaḥ ? yasmādṛgakṣarāṇāmarthābhi-

yitumaśakyatvādrathantarādibhiḥ sambandho na syāt | saṃskārakarmatve(hi)tu guṇābhi-

yatpunaścandraṃ gāyeti tadviniyogavaśādavivakṣitamarthābhidhānaṃ praiṣe hi sāmaprayo-
gārthameva puruṣaḥ preṣyate tena sāmaprayogaparatvāt praiṣaviniyoga(para)sya tadvaśādarthābhidhānaṃ
avivakṣitaṃ tena yukto dvitīyānirdeśaḥ | kiṇvamavahantītivadvikāraḥ yathā kiṇva-

2. R. prakṛtā eva
3. R. vadityuktam
4. R. aupādhika

mavahantītyatra vidyamāno’pi kiṇvavikāro na vivakṣitaḥ tathehāpyarthābhidhānaṃ; nacaitāvatā

paratvānna sāmasvabhāvavidhātitā | yatpunarbahiradhyayanaṃ tadanūhyānāṃ sāmnāṃ svādhyāya-
vidhikāritameva | tasya ca svādhyāyavidheḥ puruṣārthatve satyapi nādṛṣṭārthatā, dṛṣṭo hi
tasyārthaḥ karmāvabodhanaṃ nāmeti nyāyāt | tatrākṣarāṇāmarthāvabodhārthamadhyayanaṃ gītestu
svarūpābhyāsāya | na hyanabhyastaṃ sāma karmaṇi prayoktuṃ śakyate | ūhyānāṃ punarvacana-

bhyāsasya naivādhikārāntaramastītyadoṣaḥ

1śiṣyācāryasambandhaḥ tathāpyabhyāso dṛṣṭārtha eva | vacananibandhanā hi yā prāptiḥ
nyāyanibandhanā ca sā saṅkaṭāvagameti | nyāyavacanavidā ūhitānāṃ sāmnāmabhyāsaḥ |
ato na tatrāpi karmakāle punaḥ prayogepyadhikārāntarakalpanā | tena guṇakarmatvena(pi)
yukta eva bahiḥ prayogaḥ | tasmādgauṇabhūtaṃ sāmeti siddham |

nanvevamabhyāsārthepi saṃskāre kṛte punassaṃskāro nopapadyata ityatrāha---piṣṭapeṣaṇa-
nyāyastu viniyogāntarādabhyāsācca parihṛtaḥ

manavasthānācchakyatvācceti | yadi bahiḥ prayoge(ku)kṛtaḥ saṃskāraḥ karmakālaṃ yāvadava-

|| 3 || tṛce syācchrutisaṃyogāt || 14 ||

 (utpatti bṛhatyostvā) pūrvapakṣavādī | yadi
yāsau raurave yaughājaye ca viṣṭāra paṅ‌ktiḥ tāṃ svīkṛtya prāgāthikaṃ kriyate | tadā
gāyatrī bṛhatī triṣṭup paṅ‌ktibhiḥ tricchabdastvaṃ śrutamatikramya(te) utpattibṛhatyostvā-
naya paneṅ‌ktiparityāgādupapadyata eva tricchandastvamiti pūrvapakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntastu paṅ‌ktayā bṛhatīkāraṃ gānaṃ kartavyamiti kāryatvenāpi bṛhatyāḥ
tricchandastvamupapannaṃ bhavatīti | yadi hi paṅ‌ktirūpatvenāsau stotre vyāpriyate tato
bhavet tricchandastvavirodhaḥ | nacaitadasti | tāmādāya pragādhena bṛhatīdvayaṃ sampādya
sāmaprayogāt | śeṣaṃ pūrvavat | nanu ṣaṣṭistriṣṭubho mādhyandinaṃ savanamiti liṅgaṃ
pūrvapakṣe’pyupapannaṃ trichandastvavat paṭhitapaṃktiparityāgāt | yadi hi ṛgantarāgamanena
trichandastvaṃ labdhaṃ tadā nāsti paṅ‌ktipragāṇamityāśaṅkyāha---nāsyāsvānīyamānāsu
prakaraṇapaṭhitāḥ śakyante’prayojyatvena vaktuṃ, apragītatvameva kevalamāsāṃ
syāt na punaraprayogaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---prakaraṇapaṭhitānāṃ grāhakagṛhītānāṃ yadyapi
gānaṃ nāsti tathāpi laiṅgikaḥ kvāpi viniyogo bhavati | tena nāprayogaḥ savanagatark
parigaṇanā | na stotraparigaṇanāni na pūrvapakṣe liṅgānupapattiḥ apragītatve tricchanda-
sttvopapattiḥ stotragatachandogaṇanā neyaṃ, tena kāryāntaraprayoge’pi trichandastvādhirodhaḥ
iti upapannaṃ liṅgaṃ pūrvādhikaraṇe’pi tulyam |

evaṃ vā

atredānīṃ ko viśeṣaḥ ? uttaraṃ---ayamasti viśeṣaḥ
 yadyonyāṃ tadutta-
rayoriti gāyatrībhyāṃ śāstraṃ sampannamiti nānuṣṭup‌kāraṃ gānakartavyatā’vagamyata iti
pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |

ekaṃ sāma tṛce kriyata iti yadyonyāṃ taduttarayoriti ca gāyatrībhyāmeva śāstraṃ
sampannam | kimarthaṃ pragrathanenānuṣṭumaṃ kṛtvā gātavyamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ ? nanu cānuṣṭubhe
tṛce bhavataḥ ityasya virodhaḥ ekayāpyanuṣṭubhā tasyopapatteḥ |

[p. 264]

rādhāntavādī tu manyate---kāryatvepi uttarayoriti saṃjñitvaṃ dvitvaṃ cāviruddha-
miti na kāryāvagamaḥ śakyate bādhitum śyāvāśvāndhīgave ānuṣṭubhe tṛce bhavata iti
śruteḥ tisro’nuṣṭubhaḥ pratīyante sambhavastu(su)tāsāṃ pragāthe nāstyeva | na ca kiñcidvi-
ruddhayate | tenānuṣṭuṃ kṛtvā gātavyamiti pratīyate | tasmādatrāpi prāgāthikaṃ gānam |
śeṣamuktam
 |

evaṃ vā

atrādhikaraṇe kiṃ pragāthaḥ kartavyo neti cintyate | na kartavyo gāyatrībhyā-
meva tṛca uttarayoriti copapatteḥ iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

ānuṣṭubhe tṛce bhavata iti śruteḥ tisṛṇāmapyanuṣṭuptvāvagamāt kāryaḥ pragādha
iti rāddhāntaḥ | bhāṣye tu utpattyanuṣṭuvāgamābhidhāne varamāgamo na tu pragādha iti
pradarśayitum |

evaṃ vā

nanu cātrāpi catuśśatamaindrābārhatāḥ pragādhā iti codanāyāmeva śravaṇāt
pragādhenaiva bhavitavyam | kathamatra pūrvapakṣāśaṅkā | atrotyate - ṛk pragrathanenāpi
pragādhaśabda upapadyata eva tṛca uttarayoriti ca sarvamupapannaṃ bhavatīti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

dvayṛcānāṃ hi taccaturuttaraṃ śataṃ tenānyasmāt dvayṛcādekāmṛcamādāya tṛcaṃ
sampādya gānaṃ kariṣyati tena pragāthaśabdastṛcaśabda uttarayoriti copapadyate |
kimaparaṃ pādapragrathanena |

rāddhāntastu anyā anyā ṛco bhavanti tadeva sāmetyūhasya ca darśanāt
pādapragrathanenaiva gānaṃ kartavyam | pragādhatvasiddhaye hi tādṛśaṃ pragrathanaṃ yuktamāśrayituṃ
yena darśanavirodhona bhavati iti pādapragrathanenaiva gānaṃ nyāyyam | tṛca uttarayoriti ca
kāryepyārtha upapadyata evetyuktam | śeṣamuktam
 |

|| 7 || arthaikatvādvikalpaḥ syāt || 29 ||

kathaṃ punargīterviniyuktatvādupāyāḥ samuccīyante ityāśaṅkate | yadi hi te’pi
viniyuktā bhaveyuḥ tadā samuccīyanta iti yāvat | na ca te viniyuktāḥ gītereva

[p. 265]

viniyuktatvāt | uttaraṃ---na hi upāyānanupādāya gītiḥ śakyate upādātuṃ
ityupādānataḥ prāptiṃ manvāno gṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatayā samuccayamāha pūrvapakṣī
 |

rāddhāntastu---ayameva viśeṣaḥ yaduta eka evābhyupāya upāyāntaranirapekṣo
gītyupādānaṃ nirvartayati | nacopāyānugrahāya sāmopādānābhyāse pramāṇamasti |
sakṛdupāttenāpi sāmnā kāryasiddheḥ | kāryaṃ ca dharmāṇāṃ prayojanaṃ nopāyāḥ | tasmāda-
gṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvaṃ vikalpahetuḥ na samuccaye iti siddho vikalpaḥ ||

evaṃ vā

nanu cedamadhikaraṇamanupapannārthamiti vakṣyate | yadi tāvadidaṃ vacanaṃ tadopa-
deśacinteyam nāsmin lakṣaṇe’syāvatāraḥ | atha vikṛtāvidaṃ vacanaṃ tato’narthakaṃ prakṛtau
stuteḥ sāmasādhanatvāvagamāt prakṛtivadeva sāmnā stutiḥ prāpnoti | pratyuta
ṛgvidhānārthamevedaṃ yuktaṃ sāmnaḥ prāptatvāt ṛco’prāptatvāt |

atrocyate-prakṛtau vikṛtau vā bhavatu parasparanirapekṣakāryanirvṛttyarthamidamadhi-
karaṇaṃ yadi ṛcāmapi stutau viniyogaḥ syāt tadā sāmanirapekṣameva kāryamṛcāṃ syāt
sāmaviniyogapakṣe ca ṛṅ‌nirapekṣasāmna eva kāryamiti yadetadāpyate tannikaraṇārthamida-
madhikaraṇam |

nanu ca yadyetadvikṛtau vacanaṃ tadā’narthakaṃ ṛgvidhānārthaṃ ca syāditi pūrvamuktaṃ
tatrāha---na ca prakṛtivikṛtyorniyamaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---vikṛtāvapi yadi (vikṛtau)
stutau sāmavidhānārthametatsyāt tadā prakṛtigatāsvapi stutiṣu codakataḥ sāmaprāpti-
raviruddhā | vikṛtihiṃ pradhānabhūtātkarmaṇo dharmān gṛhṇāti prakṛtyaṅgāpūrvaṃ tu stuti-
viṣayaṃ vaikṛtastuterdharmagrāhīti na kutaścidviśeṣaḥ | tena prakṛtāvapi yatsāmnaḥ stuti-
darśanaṃ tadetadvacanapūrvakameveti nānarthakaṃ vacanam | na ca ṛgvidhānārthaṃ vacanam |
kasmāt prakṛtivikṛtyorniyamo nāstītyāha---yadeva yatrābhyadhikaṃ tadi-
tareṇākāṅkṣyate | prakṛtiliṅgaviśeṣādviśeṣo’vagamyate | naiva tasyāḥ
prakṛtirasti
 | prakṛtigatasyāpi stotrāpūrvasya sādhanākāṅkṣā vaikṛtāpūrvasya sāmasādhanaṃ
vihitam | stutitvaṃ ca codanāliṅgam | tena yukto’tideśaḥ | yata eva paraspara-
nirapekṣakāryanirvṛttyarthamidamadhikaraṇaṃ ata evedamasmin lakṣaṇe contyate upadeśe
viśeṣābhāvāt | pūrvapakṣāvasthāyāṃ hi upadeśato vikalpo nāsti | śrutito viśeṣā-

[p. 266]

bhāvāt nāsyāṃ śrutau samuccaye vikalpe vā viśeṣaḥ, arthataśca viśeṣābhāvādubhayoḥ
sādhanabhāvasya samuccayenāpi ghaṭanāt aruṇaikahāyanīnyāyato vā dvārabhedena samuccayā-
virodhāt | kāryatastu vikalpa eva prāpnoti | ekaikasya viniyogenāṅgatvādekai-
kopādāne’pi tatsiddheḥ | tena kārye vikalpo’yamiti asmin lakṣaṇe saṅgatiḥ |
kāryacintārūpatvāt | ūhasiddhayarthaṃ hi avāntarakāryacintā prastuteyam |

api ca avāntarakāryacintā tannirākaraṇārthaṃ
 vikalpanirākaraṇārthaṃ
ihaiva cintanīyam | tatraiva pūrvapakṣavādinā avaśyaṃ nindayaivāsurānvavāyasya nimittabhāva
iti kiṃ padāntarāpekṣayeti bhavatu vākyabhedaḥ | tasmāt pūrvapakṣavādī vikalpaṃ manyata
iti sambandhaḥ | yadi nindayaivāsurānvavāyasya vākyārthāvagame nimittabhāva iti niścayaḥ
syāt tadaikavākyataiva bhavet | yadi tu anindayā stutyā copapadyate tadā nāstiḥ
padāntarāpekṣāyāṃ pramāṇamiti vākyabhedāt ṛcāmapi stutau viniyogaḥ tathā
sāmno’pi | tatra kāryasyaikaikasya sādhyatvādvikalpaḥ | vikṛtāvapi copapannārthamevedaṃ
vikalpapakṣe vacanaṃ, ṛco’prāptāyā eva vidhānāt | tadvidhāne ca sāmna ekāntena
nivṛttirmābhūditi tasyāpi vidhānaṃ yuktaṃ tadāha-ṛgvidhāne hi sāmnā stutirna
prāpnoti
 prakṛtitaḥ prāpterupadeśena vādhitatvāt | tasmādubhayaṃ vidhātavyam |

rāddhāntastu syādetadevaṃ yadyaviśiṣṭo nimittabhāvaḥ syāt 1ayaṃ tu
gamyate viśeṣaḥ | ko’sau ?
 uttaraṃ---iti vakṣyamāṇaḥ
 | kaḥ ? padārthāntareṇa
sāmnā stuvītetyanena hi sambandhitvena vikalpenāsākṣātkṛtatvādasya tadasaṃbandhitvam |
ṛcā stuvīteti kalpanā | na cākalpanāyāṃ sambhavantyāṃ kalpanā sambhavatīti hetoḥ
sāmnaiva stutiḥ | yadi sāmamātravidhānametat vikṛtāvidānīṃ kiṃ prayojanamasya
vākyasya ?
 pūrvapakṣe tu pūrvoktaprakāreṇa vikṛtāvapyetadvacanamarthavat | uttaraṃ---
nāvaśyaṃ prakṛtivikārabhāve niyama ityuktaḥ parihāraḥ
 pūrvameva |

evaṃ vā

abhidhāne viśeṣānavagamāt pragītāpragītayormantrayoḥ pratīkena ca
vidhānāt aviśeṣaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī
 | asyārthaḥ---yathā stutau viśeṣaḥ
pragītenaiva stuteḥ sādhyatvāt 2tasyābhidhānaṃ tu nāpragītenaivopasthānātmakatvābhidhānāt
1. idaṃ-mā
2. tasyā abhidhāne pragīte tulyaṃ

[p. 267]

pragītena cāpragītena ca tulyam | ayaṃ sahasramānava iti (pragītena)pratīkena ṛco
viniyogāt tasyāścobhayāvasthatvādaniyamaḥ | yadi hi etayeti sarvanāmnā viniyoga-
stadā tasya prakṛtaparatvāt pragītāyāśca sāmavede prakṛtatvāt nāpragītayopasthātavyamiti
gamyate | pratīkena cāyaṃ viniyogaḥ | etacchabdastu pratīkopāttāyā ṛca eva
parāmarśakaḥ | yastvatra stutiśabdaḥ so’bhidhānopalakṣaṇayaiva, anyathā sāmnaiva syāt
ṛcāṃ stutāvacoditatvāt |

rāddhāntastu etayeti viśeṣāvagamāt pragītayopasthātavyamiti yuktam
 |
---asyārthaḥ-etayopatiṣṭhata iti viniyogārthaḥ etacchabdaśca prakṛtaparaḥ sāmavede
ca pragītaiva sā prakṛtā | paścāt kayetyapekṣāyāṃ ayaṃ sahasramānava iti viśeṣasamarpaṇaṃ
pratīkenaiva | evaṃ vyākhyāyamāne etayeti pravṛttiviśeṣakaraṃ bhavati |

nanu pratīkagrahaṇāt punarapyaviśeṣa ityatrāha---pratīkagrahaṇaṃ tvaviśiṣṭameta-
yeti yā prakṛtā pragītopasthāpitā tasyā api viniyoge pratīkagraha-
ṇasyāviśeṣaḥ
 |

nanu ca sāmavede apragītāpi paṭhyata iti | ṛk‌pāthacodanāmu-
panyasyārthatayā pragāṇārthatayā na svarūpagrahaṇārthamṛk‌pātha iti nāpragītā
prakṛtā,
 kintu pragīteti | āhavanīyopasthāne pragītāpragītayoḥ avikalpamuktavān |
kutaḥ punaḥ apragītāyāḥ pragāṇārthamevātra pāthaḥ ityatrāha---dṛṣṭe sambhavatyadṛṣṭa-
kalpanānupapatteḥ
 | pragāṇanirvṛttirhi dṛṣṭaṃ prayojanam | na hyagṛhītānyakṣarāṇi sukhaṃ
pragīyante | tasmādyuktamanyārthatvam | ataḥ pragītayopasthātavyamiti sthitam |

evaṃ vā

kaḥpunaḥ gītyupāyaiḥ saha svarāṇāṃ viśeṣaḥ ?
 uttaraṃ---ayamasti
viśeṣaḥ svarāṇāṃ viniyogaśeṣatvam
 | mantro viniyujyamānaḥ sasvara eva viniyujyate
jape nyūṅghe ca svaraniyamadarśanāt | yenaiva hi svareṇa mantrāḥ paṭhitāḥ tenaiva svareṇa
jape nyuṅghe ca prayujyante | anyathāhi niyamo na syāt | nyūṅkhaśabdena ca nirantara-
paṭhitāḥ ṣaḍoṅkārā ucyante | yajñakarmaṇi japanyūṅkhayoraikaśrusyaṃ nāsti yajñakarmaṇyajapa-
nyūṅkhasāmasbiti paryudāsāt |

[p. 268]

kena punarviśeṣeṇa japanyūṅkhyorupādānaṃ ye[na]tānyapi mantrasvaraviśeṣeṇa
paṭhyante teṣāmapi tarhi svaraviśeṣeṇaiva prayogaḥ syāt | anyathā svarānarthakyāditi
tatrāha---yeṣāṃ tu
 nānenaiva prayogaḥ teṣāmudāttādayo’rthaniścayāyaiva | tāno yajñakarmaṇi
iti japanyūṅkhātirekiṇāṃ yeṣāmaikaśrutyena viniyogaḥ | tāna aikaśrutyam | teṣā-
mudāttādayo’rthaniścayāyaiva svādhyāyakāle | etacca na 1pūrvapakṣavādinā uktam kintu
suhṛdbhāvena granthakāreṇa |

nanu viniyoge sati sutarāṃ nātra pūrvapakṣāśaṅkā aikaikaśyena vini-
yogāvagamāt samuccayāśaṅkā mandaiva
 | pariharati---na cātrā[nā]śaṅkā
 |
kintvāśaṅkaiva kārye’bhidhānalakṣaṇe svarāṇāṃ avaśyaṃ vyāpāradarśanāt | abhidhāna-
mātrāt varṇaviśeṣeṇāvadhānasiddheḥ | tenādṛṣṭārthatayā samuccaya eva yuktaḥ | svarato’-
pyarthanirṇayamabhiyuktā varṇayantīti2 rāddhāntaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ-yadyapyanabhiyuktā
lokāḥ svaraviśeṣeṇārthaviśeṣaṃ nāvadhārayanti, tathāpi paṇiniprabhṛtayo’bhiyuktāḥ svara-
viśeṣeṇārthaviśeṣaṃ varṇayantyeva | arthaviśeṣe svaraviśeṣānuśāsanāt “pade’padeśe”

“nivāte vātatrāṇe”
 ityādau | (pā. sū 6-2-7, 8)

|| 8 || sāmapradeśe vikārastadapekṣaḥ syāt śāstrakṛtatvāt || 32 ||

atredānīṃ cintyate---kiṃ gītivat gītyupāyā akṣarābhivyaktyarthā uta
gītiniṣpattyarthā eveti | tatra yadi gītiniṣpattyarthāstadā yathāyonyuttarayorapi prayogaḥ |
tatra yadi gītiniṣpattyarthā gītyupāyāḥ tadā yasmin gītibhāge viṣpādakatvena āī
bhāvaḥ kṛto yonau uttarāyāmapi tasminneva bhāge prayoktavya iti yathāyonyuttarayoḥ
gītyupāyānāṃ prayogaḥ | athākṣarābhivyaktyarthā gītyupāyāḥ tadā yathābhivyaṅgyaṃ prayogaḥ
yonau vṛddhaṃ tālavyaṃ āībhāvenābhivyaktamityuttarayorapi tadevābhivyajyate na yathāyoni
prayogaḥ |

etaduktaṃ bhavati---kiṃ gītibhāgavaśena gītyupāyānāṃ prayoga uta varṇavaśeneti |
tatra pūrvapakṣavādī gītyutpattyarthā upāyā iti manyate | kutaḥ ? gīterhyakṣarābhivyaktiḥ
1. R. pūrvapakṣakālenoktam
2. upadiśantīti |

[p. 269]

prayojanaṃ nopāyānām | etadeva kuta ityatrāha---yadyakṣarābhivyaktyarthā evo-
pāyā bhaveyuḥ kenedānīṃ gītirniṣpādyate aniṣpannāyāśca gīteḥ akṣarābhi-
vyaktyarthataiva hīyate
 | tenopāyā gītiniṣpattyarthāḥ, gītirakṣarābhivyaktyarthā |
ato gītyarthā evopāyā iti yuktam | tena gītibhāgavaśena prayogaḥ |

atrābhidhīyate---yadyupāyā akṣarābhivyaktyarthā na bhavanti tadā
gīterakṣarābhivyaktyarthataiva na prāpnoti
 | kuta ityatrāha---na hyakṣarāśritā
upāyā na santi | yadyakṣarāśritā upāyā na bhaveyuḥ tadā[kiṃ]gītiniṣpādi-
tākṣarāṇyabhivyañjyāt
 | āībhāvādayastu akṣarāśritāḥ | tatra yadyete abhi-
vyaktyarthā na bhavanti tadā’paro’bhivyañjako bhaviṣyati |

nanu yadyupāyā akṣarābhivyaktyarthāḥ tadā gīteranutpattiḥ prāpnotī-
tyuktam
 | pariharati-iyamevotpattirasya yadutākṣarāṇāṃ abhivyaktiḥ svarāṇāṃ

cānekopāyasamāhārātmikā gītiḥ sopāyairanavayavabhūtairniṣpādyate | ato’tra dṛṣṭe’rthe
sambhavati nādṛṣṭārthakalpanā nyāyyetyakṣarābhivyaktirevopāyaiḥ | darśitā cākṣarābhi-
vyaktyarthatā āībhāvena | tālavyamevāībhāvena niṣkṛṣyābhivyajyate ityanyeṣvapi bhāga-
vikāreṣu anekaghā’kṣarābhivyaktyarthatā | kathaṃ tarhi lopāgamastobhādiṣu
 |
lopo hi anuccāraṇātmakaḥ sa kathamakṣarābhivyaktyarthaḥ | tathā āgamastobhāvapyakṣarānta-
rānupraveśātmakatvāt akṣarāṇāmanabhivyañjakau | uttaraṃ---tatrāpyabhidhānātmataivā-
dṛṣṭāthaitaiva dṛṣṭārthatve na viśeṣaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---yadyapi lopādayo nābhivyañjakāḥ
tathāpyakṣarābhivyaktyarthā eva akṣarābhivyañjikāyā gīteḥ viśeṣāpādakatvāt | sa ca
tadāhito viśeṣo gīteradṛṣṭārthaḥ | tadāha---teṣāmapyabhidhānavyaktāvevādṛṣṭārtha-
tvaṃ kalpayitavyam sviṣṭakṛdādīnāmiva dravyadevatāgatam
 | tasmādvarṇavaśena
gānaṃ kartavyam, na bhāga1 vaśeneti siddham |

|| 9 || stobhasyaike dravyāntare nivṛttimṛgvat || 34 ||

nanu stobhasya lakṣaṇamuktvā stobhapradeśāpradeśacintā yuktetyatrāha---idānīṃ
stobhaścintyate---pradiśyante neti | asyā eva cintāyā lakṣaṇasaṅgata-
1. A. gīnivaśena.

tvāt
 | yadyonyāṃ taduttarayorgāyatītyanena vacanena yonisādhyastutikārye uttarayo-
viṃniyogaḥ | tacca stutyākhyaṃ kāryaṃ sāmasādhyamiti kāryeṇa sāmapradeśaḥ ūhātmaka-
stadviśeṣaśca stobhapradeśe iti stobhaprasaṅgena | stobhaśabdena yaducyate tadupariṣṭā-
dvakṣyāmaḥ |

tatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ---gītiśabdena stobhānāmanabhidhānāt ṛgakṣaraiśca saha
stutāvasambandhāt kāryārthatvāccātideśavākyasya na pradeśaḥ stobhānāmiti
 |
yadyonyāṃ taduttarayoriti yadidaṃ atideśavākyaṃ tatkāryārtham | yonikāryaṃ stutyākhya-
muttarābhyāmapi kartavyamiti | tatkāryaṃ yonyāpi gītiyuktayā kṛtamityuttarayorapi
tatsiddhaye gītirākṣipyate | tatra yadi stobhā gītiśabdābhidheyā bhaveyuḥ tadā stuti-
sādhanatvenopadeśāt stutisiddhayarthamākṣipyeran, yadi vā stobhaiḥ vinā ṛgakṣarāṇi na
stutiṃ kuryuḥ; na caitadubhayamastīti na pradeśaḥstobhānām |

atrocyate---gītyatideśādeva stobhānāmapyatideśaḥ svarāṇāmiva

yathā svarāṇāṃ gītyatideśe gīti1bāhyānāmatideśastathā stobhānāmapi | nanu ca stutya-
rthatvānabhidhānānniṣprayojanatā stobhātideśasya

saprayojana evātideśaḥ | uttaraṃ---satyamabhidhāne niṣprayojanatā gīti-

nanu cākṣarābhivyaktireva gītirityuktam | abhidhānārthaṃ cākṣarāṇya-
bhivyajyante | na ca taiḥ stutyabhidhānaṃ sādhyata ityuktam | tasmāt niṣpra-
yojanā gītirapi na niṣpādyā
 | asyārthaḥ-stobhā na stutiṃ sādhayanti kintu
ṛgakṣarāṇyeva | gītiścākṣarābhivyaktirūpā stutau viniyuktā | tatra yānyakṣarāṇi stutiṃ
kurvanti tad‌dvāreṇa tasyāḥ stutisādhanatvam | sāca gītiḥ ṛgakṣaraiḥ vyaṅgyaiḥ
sampāditeti stobhasampādyo gītibhāgaḥ niṣprayojano naivottarayoḥ kartavyaḥ | ucyate |
satyamevaṃ yadi tāvadabhidhānākṣarābhivyaktyaiva sāmno guṇabhāvaḥ yadyarthābhi-
dhānākṣarābhivyaktayātmakasyaiva sāmno guṇabhāvaḥsyāt tadā gītibhāgasya
syānniṣprayojanatā, kintu svādhyāya eva saṃskārakarmatā sāmno vijñātā |
bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi kimityāha---atastathābhūtasyaiva karmaṇi viniyuktasya sāmnaḥ

1. kāryāpekṣaḥ

[p. 274]

madhvaśanaghṛtāśane na1 naimittikatayā saṃsthānasya sambhavato’nyatrādhikārānvayāt | yāva-
dadhikārānvayitā ṣaḍahasaṃsthānasya sambhavati tāvannaiva nimittatopapattiradhikāriviśeṣaṇa-
syaiva nimittatvasambhavāt | adhikāraśceha ṣaḍaha(hā)karaṇavataḥ satriṇaḥ tadārabdha-
kāryāntarepi | viniyogāntare [na]viṣayabhedabhinnaṣaḍahakāryāpattiradhikāraniyogalakṣaṇa eva
kāryāpattiḥ | tataśca prayojanaśūnyānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ paramāpūrvaprayuktānāṃ prāptiḥ | evaṃ nakhāva-
pūtepi kāryāpattiḥ? kuta ityāha---avapūtatā ca phalena saha sambandhaḥ upapadyate’-
nyatra kriyākaraṇāt
 | tasmādatrāpi kṛptakāryāśritatvāt vaikṛtānāmupadeśānāṃ
nakhānāmapi tuṣakaṇavimoka eva sambandhaheturiti kāryāpattiḥ | paridhau paśuṃ niyuñjantīti
niyojanasyāvidhānāt kimanyadato yūpakāryādavagamyate | yadi niyojanaṃ vidhīyeta
tadā na syādapi kāryāpattiḥ | na ceha niyojanaṃ vidhīyate prakṛtitaḥ prāptatvāt |
tataśca yūpakāryāpattiriti taddharmaprāptiḥ |

evaṃ na girāgiretyanenaikavākyatvāt giraṇasya cāsambhavāt na
girāpadakāryādṛte dvayamupapadyata iti kāryāpattiḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---na giretyanena
saha airaṃ kṛtvetyasyaikavākyatvaṃ pratīyate | taccaivamupapadyate yadi girāpadakārya everāpadaṃ
vidhīyate tadā hi kṛtakāryatayā girāpadanivṛttiḥ | sā prāptā satī anūdyate anyathā
sāpi vidheyaiva syāditi vākyabhedaprasaṅgaḥ | tenaikavākyatvabalena girāpadakārya everāpadaṃ
kathaṃ nāma pratīyatāmityevamarthameva na girāgireti brūyādityucyate | na ca śakyamātma-
giraṇalakṣaṇadoṣo’bhidhīyata iti, asambhavāt | yadi ātmaśabdena śarīramevātma-
sambandhāllakṣaṇayocyate, yadi vā ātmaiva śrutyā ubhayathāpi giraṇāsambhavāt | tasmāt
girāpadakārya everāpadasya vidhāne dvayamidamekasmin vākye upapadyate | na girā gireti
brūyāt airaṃ kṛtvodgayamiti kāryāpattestaddharmalābhaḥ |

dharmā bhaveyuḥ
 tadā yaṃ pratyanaidamarthyaṃ tasya(vi)niyogāntarārthatvāt niyojanaidamarthyaṃ

 | asyārthaḥ---yadi dharmāṇāṃ pradhānānāṃ ca eka eva

niyamaḥ syāt | niyogārthānhi dharmānniyogaḥ prayuṅkte | yadi ca niyogasaṃsparśino
dharmā na bhaveyuḥ tadā teṣāmaniyogārthatve prayuktyabhāvaḥ prasajyeta | yata eva niyoga-
saṃsparśināṃ dharmāṇāṃ prayuktiniyamaḥ aniyogārthānāṃ cāprayuktiḥ ata eva paridhidharma-
viruddhānāṃ yūpadharmāṇāṃ takṣaṇādīnāmanavakāśaḥ | itarathā virodhino yūpadharmāḥ paśuyāgena

tadabhāvaśca pūrvapakṣiṇo yuktiriti
 | samādhatte---anyāyya ūho na bhavatīti
1parihāraḥ | kathamanyāyyatvaṃ
 | uttaraṃ---sādhanabhāvena hi abhidhānaṃ
mantrāṇāṃ pratipāditam | na ca paridhau niyojanasādhanatā’stīti parihṛta-
manyāyyamabhidhānamiti ayuktam
 | asyārthaḥ---rāddhāntinā tūhamicchatā dharmoha-
vat mantroho’pi vaditavyaḥ | tatra yūpāyājyamānāyeti paridhau mantre prayujyamāne
yūpābhāvāt yūpaśabdastāvanna prayojyaḥ | yadi paraṃ paridhiśabdaḥ sa ca prayoktumanucitaḥ
paridhiśabdena niyojanasādhanabhūtasyānabhidhānāt | sādhanabhūtaścārtho marntreṇābhi-
dhīyate | tadetad‌dūṣaṇaṃ paridhau yūpadharmāṇāmanūhe parihṛtaṃ bhavati |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate-atrāpyūhena bhavitavyam niyogaikye, niyogaikyaṃ
hi bhavati sambandhahetuḥ | upadeśena punaḥ kāryaprayuktatāṃ vārayati niyoga-
bhedaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ - upadeśena hi dharmāṇāṃ niyogaikyaṃ śeṣiṇo niyogaidamarthyasiddhau
1. R abhiprāyaḥ

[p. 277]

hetuḥ | atadarthatāpanne hi dharmāḥ sambadhyamānāḥ na niyogaidamarthyaṃ labhante, niyogaida-
marthyāpanneṣu sambadhyamānāstu labhante | kāryatastu dharmāṇāṃ prāptau niyogaidamarthyaṃ tāvat
pramitam | pramite tu tasminnanuṣṭhānāya kevalamāśrayāpekṣā sādhyaṃ cāśrayo dharmāṇāmiti
kāryaprayuktatā na śakyate vārayitum | tatkāryatā ca paridherupapāditā | tasmānna
dharmāḥ kartavyatayā śakyā vārayitum |

idaṃ cādhikaraṇaṃ yūpakāryaprayuktā yūpadharmā na yūpotpattyarthā iti kṛtvā cintitam |
yattvanabhidhāno(nāya)mantraḥ iti tatrocyate | kāryaprayuktatve sati sādhanāntarasyāvi-
vakṣitatvāt tathaivābhidhānamiti noho virudhyate,1 tathaivābhidhānasiddheḥ paridhānasādhanasyā-
vivakṣitatvāt niyojanasādhanasya vivakṣitatvāt yūpaśabdena tasyābhidhātuṃ śakteḥ nohaḥ
kartavyaḥ | yadi paśuniyogo yūpadharmān prayuṅkte kathaṃ tarhi satvaktvādī-
nāmanugrahaḥ ?
 uttaraṃ---tathābhūtasya
 paridhi1bhūtasya yūpakārya upadeśāt
 na svarūpa-
vighāte tatkāryasamarthamiti tadavirodhidharmāveśa iti yuktam |

|| 14 || parārthena tvarthasāmānyaṃ saṃskārasya tadarthatvāt || 44 ||

iha punaḥ kāryacodanāpi nāstīti1 pūrvapakṣavādī paridhinānyatvaṃ matvā
dharmā na bhavantītyāha
 | etadeva vivṛṇoti---tatra hi paridhau paśuṃ niyuñjantī-

tyatra paridheḥkāryaṃ coditam | iha punaḥ na dadhani caruścodyate kiṃ tarhi ubhayaṃ
dadhitaṇḍule pradātre | ato na kevalamaidamarthyameva nāsti api tu kāryamapi na codyata
iti vaiṣamyam paridhānāt | atrocyate | kāryaṃ hi dharmāṇāṃ prayojakaṃ na codanā, tadeva
hi kāryaṃ kvacit codanāt yathā paridhau paśuṃ niyuñjīteti | kvacidaidamarthyādyathā

prakaraṇādhīteṣu mantreṣu liṅgaviniyojyeṣu śeṣiṇāmaidamarthyaṃ, kvacidarthādyathā’traiveti na
kaścit kāryakāritve viśeṣaḥ | kāryataścātra prāptiruditā nābhidhānaviniyogābhyāmiti
kārye’bhidhānaviniyogābhyāṃ dharmāśrayasya na dharmasambandhaḥ, kintu kāryayogamātreṇeti
nātra praṇītādharmaprāptiravaidikīti śakyate vaktum | tasmāt kartavyāḥ praṇītādharmāḥ
dadhipayasoḥ |

1. R yujyate
2. R paridhirūpasya
3. nāpyastīti

[p. 278]

|| 15 || ekārthatvādavibhāgaḥ syāt || 46 ||

sthitaṃ kāryaprayuktā dharmā iti | bṛhatpṛṣṭhaṃ bhavati rathantaraṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavatītyatra
kāryabhedo’sti nāstīti vicāryate | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī pṛṣṭhaśabdavācyaṃ dvayorapyekakāryaṃ
manvāna ubhayatrobhayavihitadharmāveśaṃ manyate vrīhiyavayoriva | yathā vrīhiyavayoreka-
kāryakatvāt kāryaprayuktatvācca dharmāṇāṃ ekatrāpi śrutā dharmā ubhayatra bhavanti tathā
atrāpi pṛṣṭhaśabdavācyasya dvayorbṛhadrathantarayorekatvāt tatprayuktairdharmaiḥ ekaikatra śrutaiḥ
ubhayatra bhavitavyam |

rāddhāntastu upadeśe tāvacchabdabhedo vidyate bṛhadrathantaramiti
 | dharmo-
padeśe bṛhadrantaraśabdopādānāt śabdabhedastāvadasti | śabdabhedācca vyavasthā’vasīyate
dharmāṇām | kāryamekamiti cet
 | pariharati---dṛśyate ca kāryabhedaḥ anyathā
kavatīṣu stutirgamyate anyathā bṛhadṛkṣu
 | guṇābhidhānalakṣaṇā stutiḥ sā kavatīṣva-
nyena prakāreṇa | anyena bṛhadṛkṣu | tena vyāpārabhedastāvadavagamyate | pṛṣṭhaśabdā-
bhidheyatvamekamiti cet
 | pariharati---nābhidhānato dharmaprāptirityuktam
 |
tenābhidhānaikyepi na dharmavyatikaraḥ | kiṃ tarhi ? kāryata eva dharmaprāptiḥ kāryataścobhaya-
bheda upalabhyate bṛhadrathantarayorityuktam | karṣaṇachedanayoriva parvavibhāgaphalayoḥ |
na ca phalato dharmā bhavantīti nīvārādiṣu darśitam | kintu kāryataḥ | tasmāta sarva-
kāryabhede sambhavati bṛhadrathantarayoḥ lakṣaṇā ayuktā | ato vyavasthayaiva dharmāḥ kartavyāḥ
vrīhiyavayoḥ puroḍāśaniṣpattau na kāryabheda upapadyate ubhayoḥ puroḍāśanirvartakatvāt
anyathā yavamayaḥ puroḍāśo’nyathā vrīhimaya iti tatrāpi kāryabheda iti
cet
 | uttaraṃ---na sva/?/bhāvaprayuktā dharmāḥ kintu kāryaprayuktā ityuktam
 | svabhāva-
ścātra bhidyate na niṣpattau vyāpāraḥ vyāpāraprayuktā dharmāḥ abhidhānavyāpārānyatvaṃ ca bṛha-
drathantarayoḥ pradarśitam | anyathā kavatīṣu stutiḥ anyathā bṛhadṛkṣviti | ato
vrīhiyavābhyāṃ vaiṣamyaṃ bṛhadrathantarayoḥ |

|| 16 || aprākṛte tadvirodhādvayavatiṣṭheran || 48 ||

sāmāntarasādhane vikṛtiṣu pṛṣṭhe cintā | kimekasya dharmāḥ kāryeṇa
kartavyā utobhayadharmā iti
 | kiṃ vikalpena dharmāḥ kartavyāḥ uta samuccayeneti
sandehaḥ | atra pūrvapakṣavādī prakṛtau kāryabhedāt ihāpi tadvaditi na prāptibhedaṃ

[p. 279]

manvānaḥ kāryeṇecchati
 | asyārthaḥ---prakṛtau kāryabhedāt bhedena prāptiṃ manvānaḥ
pūrvapakṣī ihāpi kāryeṇa prāptiṃ tadvati necchati | anyathā tadvadbhāva eva na syāt |

rāddhāntastu---tadvati kāryaṃ prāpyate nopāyāḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---prakṛti-
vadvikṛtirapyupakāravatī na punaḥ padārthavatī upakārasādṛśyaṃ hyetat tadvaditi | na

padārthasādṛśyamiti | etaddaśamādye darśitam | bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi kathaṃ samāveśo dharmā-
ṇāmityata āha---upāyataśca prakṛtau kāryānyatvamavagatam
 | ṛgbhedāt prakṛtau
stutivyāpāralakṣaṇakāryabhedo’vagataḥ | na cehopāyabhedo’sti upāyāntaravidhānāt |
stutau sāmāntaravidhānena prākṛtarṅ‌nivṛtteḥ ṛgantarāpekṣatvāt tasya caikatvāt |
tasmānna kāryānyatvaṃ śakyate’vagantum | ananyatve ca kāryaprayuktāḥ kena vāryante nakha
ivolūkhalamusaladharmāḥ anyatra virodhāt | yadi paraṃ virodhādeva dharmāṇāmakaraṇam |
ato’viruddhānāṃ samāveśa iti sthitam |

|| 17 || ubhayasāmni caivamekārthāpatteḥ || 49 ||

atrāpi kaṇvarathantaravat ubhayamubhayatra yojyata iti pūrvapakṣavādī
manyate
 | etaduktaṃ bhavati---yat prakṛtāvupāyadvayaṃ vijñātaṃ tasya kārye
ihopāyadvayaṃ vidhīyate
 | etadeva vivṛṇoti---bṛhatkārye bṛhadrathantare,
rathantara kāryepi bṛhadrathantare | tasmāt samāveśo dharmāṇām
 |

rāddhāntastu upāyasamāveśaḥ pratyabhijñānāt
 | asyārthaḥ-yau tau vyāpārau
stutiṃ prati bṛhadrathantarayoravagatau tāveveha samāviṣṭau pratyabhijñāyete | tenopāyayo-
re va samāveśo na kāryayoḥ | na ca samāveśaprayuktā dharmāḥ kiṃ tarhi kāryaprayuktā
ityuktam | tena kāryabhedāt yathāyathameva dharmairvyavasthitairbhavitavyam | tadidamuktaṃ
bhāṣye “sāmnāpi dṛṣṭamadṛṣṭaṃ vā kriyata”
 iti | upāyārthasamāptirdṛṣṭakāryaṃ

stutirvyāpāraviśeṣalakṣaṇā upāyārthasamāptirdṛṣṭaṃ kāryam | samāveśataḥ kimapyadṛṣṭam |
na ca prākṛte kāryasambandhe’vagamyamāne kāryāntarasambandho dharmāṇāmupapadyata iti
vyavasthaiva nyāyyā |

|| 18 || pārvaṇahomayostvaprabṛttissamudāyārthasaṃyogāt
tadabhījyā hi || 51 ||

[p. 280]

atra sūtre pravṛttiśabdaśravaṇādbhrāntaścodayati | nanu ceyamatideśacintā |
kaḥprasaṅgo’syāḥ ?
 | kimanena niṣphalenoktena | bādho’yaṃ nātideśa iti matvā’’ha
-astyatra prasaṅgaḥ kāryapradhānatvādatideśānatideśayoḥ
 | etadeva vivṛṇoti-
yadi hi prākṛtadevatārthatā homayoḥ tadā sauryādiṣvapravṛttiḥ | athārādupakāra-
katvaṃ tadā pravṛttiḥ
 | asyārthaḥ - prakṛtāni yāni karmāṇyāgneyādīni devatābhūtāni
parvaśabdābhidheyāni tadarthatā tatsmaraṇārthatā pārvaṇahomayoḥ syād tadā vikṛtiṣu
apravṛttiḥ, athārādupakārakatvaṃ tadā pravṛttiḥ prayājādivaditi kāryanibandhane pravṛttya-
pravṛttī vicāryete iti tadarthaṃ homayoḥ dvārakāryaṃ cintyate---kiṃ devatāsaṃskāraḥ
kāryaṃ uta karaṇopakāra eva prayājādivaditi | iha cādhyāye dvārakārya-
cintaiva kriyate tannibandhanatvādūhasyetyasti prasaṅgaḥ
 | kaścitatvaviccodayati
-nanvevaṃ sati bādhacintaiveyaṃ prāpnoti kāryābhāvānnivṛttiriti | kimiti
bhavān samādhātā’natideśamāha
 | pariharati---satyamevametat tathāpi kārya-
svarūpamevātra nirṇīyate’rthādvādha ityūhāntaḥ pātinyeveyaṃ cintā
 | anāsthayā
mayā nirbandho na kṛta iti samādhātā darśayati---sarvathā kāryacintārūpatvāt
saṅgatiḥ | arthādbādho bhavatu anatideśo vā iti nāsmākamāsthā
 |

atra kecinmīmāṃsakāḥ tantre svātantryamicchantaḥ svabuddhilāghavamanākalayantaḥ
bhaṅgamāpādayāṃbabhūvuḥ | nanu karmasaṃskārakatvepi homayoḥ kimiti bādho yāvatā devatā-
ntarepi paśupuroḍāśavadūhena pārvaṇahomau kartavyāveveti |

atra prabhākarapādasevānirastasamastajāḍyāḥ
 samarthanamāhuḥ---kāryaprayuktā
dharmā ityuktam | yaccāgneyādīnāmavāntarakāryaṃ paramāpūrvaniṣpattiṃ prati na tat sauryādī-
nām | ata eva ca prakṛtāvāgneyādīnāṃ nānyonyadharmasaṅkara iti |

atra pūrvapakṣavādī kālavacanatvepi parvaśabdasya kālayogāt karmaṇi prayoga
upapadyata iti kālavacanatvaṃ manyate |

rāddhāntastu niyogaikyaṃ na siddhayatīti samudāyavacanataiva nyāyyā
 |
karmavacanatve hi parvaśabdasya tatsaṃskārakatvāddhomayoḥ tanniyogaikyaṃ siddhayati | itarathā
tu na siddhiḥ | niyogaikyaṃ ca gamyate prakṛtatvāt | śakyate ca samudāyārthatvepi karma-
vacanatvepi kāle prayogo vaktuṃ karmayogāt | yadi vā parvaśabdo’yaṃ yaugikaḥ pṛṇāte

[p. 281]

rdānārthatvāt tatra bhāvasādhanatve karmavacanatā | adhikaraṇasādhanatve kālavacanatā | tatra
prakṛtatvātkarmāṇāmiha karmaṇyeva parvaśabdaḥ prayukta iti niścīyate | tadāha-yogādvo-
bhayatra parvaśabdaḥ | vidhyanupraveśastu viśeṣahetuḥ
 |

nanu karmavacanatvepi kimityārādupakārakatvaṃ na bhavatītyāha---prakṛtatvāccā-
rādupakārakatvanirākṛtiḥ
 | prakṛtakarmaṇāṃ saṃskārārhatvāt ārādupakārakatvanirā-
kṛtiḥ | śeṣamuktam

|| 19 || ubhayoraviśeṣāt || 57 ||

prasaktānuprasaktamidaṃ kāryacintāprasaṅgena
 | pūrvādhikaraṇe homayoḥ
karmārthatvaṃ prasaktam | tadanuprasaktaṃ cedaṃ bhedena pārvaṇahomau kartavyāviti |

kaḥpunaḥ pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ ?
 uttaraṃ-prakṛtārthaṃtve sati ekani-
yogakatvādubhayamubhayatreti manyate
 | ubhayatrādhikāraniyogaikyāt tadarthatvāccobhayoḥ
ubhayatrobhābhyāṃ bhavitavyam |

rāddhāntastu vyavasthāyāmapyekaniyogāvirodhāt na kāryaśūnyasyānu-
ṣṭhānaṃ yuktam
 | amāvāsyādevatākasya hi amāvāsyāsaṃskārakatvaṃ pauṇamāsīdevatā-
kasya tatsaṃskārakatvam | yatraiva tatsaṃskāryaṃ tatraiva saṃskāreṇāpi bhavitavyam |
nānyatra | tasmādvayavasthaiva nyāyyā | na ca dṛṣṭe sambhavatyadṛṣṭakalpanaiva nyāyyeti
kāryāntaravirodhaḥ | na hyekasyaiva kvaciddṛṣṭārthatvaṃ kvacidadṛṣṭārthatvaṃ yuktaṃ, yenāmāvā-
syāyāṃ dṛṣṭārthasya homasya paurṇamāsyāmadṛṣṭārthaḥ prayogaḥ syāt | tathetarasyāpī-
taratreti |

|| 20 || prayāje’pīti cet || 55 ||

nāmadheyākṣepamātramidaṃ prakṛtaprasaṅgena
 | asyārthaḥ---yathā pārvaṇahomayoḥ
prakṛtadevatārthatve vikṛtiṣvapravṛttiḥ tatheha prakṛtārthatvepi nāsti vikṛtāvapi kāryasambhavāt |
tena nāmadheyamātramihākṣipyate prasaṅgena prakṛtatvāt | yathā pārvaṇahomayoḥ karmadevatā-
rthatvaṃ tathehāpi samidādīnāṃ devatārthatvaṃ saṃskāryatvaṃ ca |

[p. 282]

rāddhāntastu iha na tathā kalpanāmarhati samidādīnāṃ devatārthatve pramāṇā-
bhāvāt | karmavacanatvepi dvitīyāyā upapatteḥ mantravarṇasyāyathārthatve pramāṇābhāvāt |
tasmādupādeyaivātra māntravarṇikī devateti siddhamārādupakārakatvam |

|| iti śrīmahāmahopādhyāyaśālikanāthamiśrakṛtau ṛjuvimalāyāṃ
pañjikāyāṃ navamasyādhyāyasya
dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ

[p. 283]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

navamādhyāye tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ

|| 1 || prakṛtau yathotpattivacanamarthānāṃ tathottarasyāṃ tatau
tatprakṛtitvādarthe cākāryatvāt || 1 ||

nanu ca ākṛtyadhikaraṇe ākṛtiḥ śabdārtha iti darśitam
 | rāddhā-
ntitam | tasya caitadeva prayojanam
 |

tajjātiviśiṣṭe saṃskārābhidhānaṃ yathā syāditi
 | saṃskāraścābhidhāna
catajjātiviśiṣṭa eva yathā syāt | vyaktipakṣe hi sādhanamātravacanatā | prakṛtau
vikṛtau ca sādhanabhāvāviśeṣādanūhaḥ tasmādākṛtivacanatvasyaitadeva prayojanam-kimatra
pūrvapakṣe’bhyadhikaṃ kriyate ? vrīhiśabdena nīvārāṇāmabhidhānāśakteḥ tacchabda
eva prayoktavyaḥ |

nanu caivaṃ sati yadi vrīhiśabdenāvrīhijātīyasyābhidhānaṃ nāsti
teṣāmarthenetyatrāpi pūrvapakṣo na yuktaḥ
 | yadi vrīhiśabdenājyasānnāyyayora-
pyabhidhānaṃ syāt tadā ājyasānnāyyayorapi auṣadhadharmairbhavitavyamiti pūrvapakṣaḥ sambhā-
vyeta | kevalam vrīhijātīyābhidhāne tu na sambhavatīti | pariharati---anyadeva hi
tatra nirākṛtaṃ anyastatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ
 | kiṃ nirākṛtamityāha---dhātvarthakarma-
vivakṣāyāṃ prayogāṅgatā na prāpnotītyāśaṅkya tatra nirākṛtam
 | vivakṣite
dhātvarthasyaiva karmaṇi vrīhyādau prayojanatvena svīkṛte adhikāravidhyarthatā na syādavadhātā-
dīnām | ato dhātvarthakarma na vivakṣitaṃ kintvārādupakārakatvamevetyāśaṅkya tatra
nirākṛtam | iha punaḥ na kāryāntaramupalabhāmahe prākṛtānmantrakāryāt | prakṛtau hi
haviḥ prakṛtidravyābhidhānaṃ mantrakāryam | ihāpi tadeva, taccohamantareṇāśakyamityūhena
bhāvyam | na cāsamavetārthatvaṃ yena mantrārthasya vivakṣitatvepi asamavetārthatvamagni-
vrīhiśabdayorgamyeta | tadapi nāsti | yataḥ samabetārthatvaṃ agnivrīhiśabdayoḥ sthita-
meva | ataḥ vaktavyamatra nirākaraṇīyam |

tadidamabhidhīyate | satyamākṛtivacanatvaṃ śabdānāṃ sthitaṃ, tathā-
pyatra sādhanabhāvenābhidhānāta prakṛtivikṛtyoścāviśiṣṭatvāt tadbhāvasya

[p. 284]

sādhanabhāvasya nohena bhavitavyam | śabdārthatve’pyākṛtistadavasthaiva bhavati
sādhanatvalakṣakatvāt
 | asyārthaḥ---prakṛtāvapi vrīhiśabdena na svarūpaṃ prakāśitaṃ,
kiṃ tarhi sādhanaviśeṣaḥ sa ca vikṛtāvapyaviśiṣṭaḥ | anyathā hi ūho’pi na
syāt | tataśca prakṛtāvapyākṛteraprakāśyatvaṃ sādhanalakṣaṇopalakṣaṇatvāt | vikṛtāvapi
na nīvārajātiḥ prakāśyeti noho yuktaḥ | nanūpalakṣaṇatve’pi vrīhyākṛtervikṛtau
tadabhāvādupalakṣaṇatvānupapatterupalakṣaṇasamarthanīvārādijātiparyupasthāpanāya jātyantaravacanameva
nyāyyamiti vaktavyaṃ, yajjātīyaṃ hi coditaṃ tajjātīyamevopalakṣaṇakṣamaṃ vrīhijātiḥ
nīvāravyaktiṃ na lakṣayituṃ kṣametyanyatprayoktavyam | na vrīhijātivacanaṃ prayoktavyamiti |

pūrvapakṣī samādhatte---atrocyate-syādevaṃ yadi nāntarīyakasya vivakṣā
syāt svajātyabhidhānenopalakṣaṇīyaṃ sādhanamiti
 | prakṛtāvidaṃ nāntarīyakaṃ,
prakṛtau hi tajjāteścoditatvāt sannikṛṣṭamupalakṣaṇamiti nāntarīyakabhābaḥ | yadi punaḥ
jātyantaracodanā syāt na tadopalakṣaṇaṃ nāntarīyakatayāpi syāt | prakṛtau hi
vrīhijātīyaṃ coditaṃ vrīhibhiryajeteti | mantre’pi tajjātivācaka eva vrīhiśabdaḥ prayukta
iti sannikṛṣṭajātirupalakṣaṇam | yadi punaḥ tatrāpyanyajātīyaṃ coditaṃ bhavet mantre(vā)ca
anyajātivācakaḥ śabdaḥ tadā na sannikṛṣṭasyopalakṣaṇaṃ bhavediti | tadevaṃ sampannaṃ
sannikṛṣṭasyopalakṣaṇatvam | na ca tadvivakṣituṃ nyāyyam | tena sannikṛṣṭasyopalakṣaṇatva-
siddhaye na sbajātivācakaśabdoho yuktaḥ |

kathaṃ tarhi vrīhiśabdena nīvārajātīyasyābhidhānaṃ ?
 uttaraṃ---yathā-
kathaṃcit
 abhidhānakalpanā aindrīvat | tasmānnāntarīyakamabhidhānamavivakṣitam |
ataḥ prākṛtayoreva śabdayoḥ prakṛtau prayogadarśanāt vikṛtāvapyabhidhānaṃ kalpanīyam
tayoḥ kāryataḥ prāpteḥ | śabdāntarasya na punaḥ kāryataḥ(yā)prāptiḥ nāpi prayogata
ityanūhaṃ manyate | samavetārthatvaṃ punaḥ upodbalakamevāsya pakṣasya yasmātsādhanaṃ karmaṇi
samavetaṃ vyakteśca sākṣāt sādhanabhāva iti lakṣaṇārthaiva jātiḥ tena nāntarīyakatvājjāteḥ
avivakṣitatvānna nīvāraśabdaḥ prayoktavya iti |

yuktyantaraṃ cānūhe darśayati---viniyoge’pi ca śabdānāmevopadeśaḥ
 |
asyārthaḥ-mantrasya viniyogo varṇaparyavasāyī varṇātiriktasya tasyābhāvāt yathaivāsau
kāryārthatve’pi prakṛtau na pīḍitaḥ evaṃ vikṛtāvapyasau na pīḍanīya eveti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

[p. 285]

prakṛtāvapi kāryārthameva mantraḥ prayujyate | tatra yathā sa eva śabdaḥ prayujyate tathā
vikṛtāvapi sa eva prayoktavyaḥ aṅgabhūtatvāt |

atrocyate---bhavitavyamūhena | kasmāt ? kāryāntare śaktyantara-
kalpanāyāmagnyādīnāmaindrīvannyāyo na vidyate
 | kāryāntare abhidheyāntare
śaktyantarakalpanāyāṃ agnayādiśabdānāṃ nyāyo nāsti yathendraśabde | kathaṃ nyāyo
nāsti
 ? uttaraṃ---prakṛtāvivātra
 mantreṇātra sādhanaprakāśanaṃ kartavyamipi prāptam |
tadidānīmabhidhānāntarakalpanayā, uta śaktyantarakalpanayeti vicāraṇīyam | tatra
śaktyantarakalpanā na nyāyyā klṛptaśakteḥ śabdasya, prayogāntare punaḥ
śaktyantarakalpanā
 agnayādiśabdaḥ klṛptaśaktiḥ tasya mantraprayogāntare punaḥ śaktya-
ntarakalpanā na yuktā | kutaḥ ? tāvanmātrasyāprayogāt | yadi tāvat
tāvanmātrasya prayogo(na)bhavet tadā balācchaktyantarakalpanā syāta | na ca
tāvanmātrasya prayogaḥ kintu mantrasya
 | tathāpi kimityāha---mantrasya puna-
rabhidhānāntaraprakṣepe’pi na kiñcit dṛṣṭādadhikaṃ kalpitaṃ bhavati
 | nābhidhā-
nāntaraprakṣepe hi kiñcit kalpitaṃ bhavati | śaktyantarakalpanāpakṣe tu sa eva doṣaḥ |
tasmāt gauravācchaktyantarakalpanāparihāreṇa śabdāntaraprakṣepa eva nyāyyaḥ |

nanu ca mantratvameva hīyate | ūhapravaranāmadheyānāmamantratvamuktaṃ yataḥ
 |
pariharati-na mantratvaṃ hīyate | nimittābhiprāyaṃ hi tadamantratvaṃ nābhidhānābhi-
prāyaṃ prakṣepyasya śabdasya
 tadamantratvamuktaṃ na punarabhidhāyakasya vākyasya abhidhāyaka-
tvamautpattika ityatroktam | klṛptakāryatayā prāptiḥ kāryavaśena mantrasyārthavattvameva yuktaṃ
samudāyāpannasya pratyabhijñāyamānatvāt | yata eva ca nimittamātranāśe mantranāśo nāsti
ata eva ca pratinidherabhāvaḥ
 sakalamantranāśe hi pratinidheḥ ātmalābho na nimitta-
mātravināśe sakalamantravismṛteḥ na śakyate tatsadṛśo jñātumiti | nimittamātravināśe tu
tattvameva mantrasya | tasmāt abhidhānāntaraprakṣepo nyāyyaḥ
 na śaktayantarakalpanā |
kutaḥ? abhyāsāpekṣatvāt
 śaktayantarakalpanā abhyāsāpekṣā | yadi hi agnayādipada-
syābhyāso(na) bhavet mantrāntargataṃ tāvatprayujyeta tathā kevalamiti | tathā kevalavacana-
prayoge śaktayantarakalpanā avaśyaṃ kartavyā | anyathā’bhidhānāśakteḥ | nacācoditā-
bhyāsaḥ kārayiṃtu śakyate yatra śabdasyopadeśa iti | satyaṃ, sa tu kāryārtha ityuktam |
kāryataścātra prāptirnopadeśataḥ | tasmādyathākāryamabhidhānam
 kāryānatikrameṇo/?/bhidhānaṃ

[p. 286]

āśrayaṇīyam | kāryataścātra prāptiḥ, kāryaṃ cātra nīvāraprakāśanam | na ca tacchaktaya-
ntarakalpanayā yuktam | anyathāpi tatsiddherityūha eva nyāyyaḥ |

yatpunaridaṃ antarāgarbhiṇīvyākhyānaṃ kṛtvā punarākṣepo’nyathā ca
vyākhyānaṃ tatra yadyākṣepaṇīya evāyamarthaḥ kimityupanyastaḥ
 | kimiti
tatparatvena sūtravyākhyānaṃ kṛtam |

uttaraṃ---upanyastastāvat upanyasanīyatvādeva
 nyāyyatvādeva | yadi
nyāyyaḥ, ākṣepastarhi kasmāt ? uttarasūtrāsambandhaparihārāya
 | 1uttarasūtramasambaddhaṃ
syādityetāvatā doṣeṇākṣepaḥ na punaranyāyyatayā | yadyasmin vyākhyāne
uttarasūtrasambandho na syāt asūtritaṃ tarhi pūrvavyākhyānam
 | pariharati-na
cāsūtritaṃ anekārthasūcanāt sūtrasya | tasmātkauśalapradarśanamidaṃ ākṣepadvāreṇa
sūtrakāreṇa (sya)kriyate
 | sūtrakāreṇātraivādhikaraṇāntaramapi sūtritamityekenaiva
sūtreṇānekārthasūcanāt mahatkauśalaṃ sūtrakārasya pradarśyate | śeṣaṃ saliṅgamuktam
 |

|| 3 || laukike doṣasaṃyogādapavṛkte hi codyate || 9 ||

prāsaṅgikamidamadhikaraṇam
 | yadyekaṃ yūpamityadi vyavasthārthaṃ vacanamiti
pūrvamuktam | idānīṃ prasaṅgenedamadhikaraṇamāyātam | tatrāviśeṣāt
 yadyekaṃ yūpamupa-
spṛśediti aviśeṣaśravaṇāt | ubhayārthatāṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī
 | puruṣārthatāṃ
kratvarthatāṃ ca manyate | asti tvayaṃ viśeṣaḥ laukike’dhikārāntarakalpanā | 2tasmā-
dvaidika evāstu iti pakṣāntaram |

rāddhāntamāha---klṛpta evātrādhikāro doṣaśravaṇāt
 | arthavādābhi-
hito’pi doṣaḥ puruṣārthatve āñjasyenopapadyate | yuktayantaraṃ cāha-iṣṭaśabdasaṃyogācca
iṣṭottarakālasparśaviṣayameva prāyaścittaṃ manyate
 | ucchrayaṇādayaśca neṣṭottarakālāḥ
aparāmapi yuktimāha---aparihāryatvāccocchrayaṇāderna vināpīti śakyate kalpa-
yitum
 | asyārthaḥ---tasmādyūpo nopaspṛśya iti pratiṣedho’yaṃ puruṣārtha evopapadyate |
1. A uttarasūtrasambandho na syādi |
2. A alaukikavaidike

[p. 287]

kratvarthapakṣe vikalpaprasaṅgāt | na ca vikalpo yuktaḥ ucchrayaṇādeḥ aparihāryatvāt |
puruṣārthaścet pratiṣedhaḥ tadatikramaprāyaścittamapi puruṣārthameva | tasmāllaukika eva
sparśane’yaṃ mantraḥ | yattarhīdamuktaṃ praharāneneti punargaṇya[punardaṇḍa]śabdaḥ saṃbadhyata
iti tatkathaṃ ?
 uttaraṃ---itthameva yathā śrūyate tathaivābhiprāyaḥ |

praṣṭā vivṛṇeti---nanu ca syonaṃ ta ityatrāpi ekavākyatāparihāraḥ
prāpnoti
 | pariharati---bhavatyeva vākyārthāvagame
 | arthapratītikāle tadapekṣaka-
tvāt | prayoge tu prayogāvasthāyāṃ tu sa mantrānta(tya)[ram] |

|| 4 || anyāyastvavikāreṇādṛṣṭapratighātitvādavi-
śeṣācca tenāsya || 10 ||

dvitīyatṛtīyapakṣayossāmyaṃ manvāno bhedapradarśanāyāha---pakṣacatuṣṭayamatro-
panyastam | ādyastvayaṃ pakṣo, bahuvacanānta evāvikāreṇa pravartatāṃ
 eka-
vacanānto nivartata eva | dvitīyastu bahuvacanānto nivartate ekavacanāntaḥ pravartate |
sa cohitavyaḥ | tṛtīyastūbhayorapi pravṛttiḥ bahuvacanāntasyāvikāraḥ ekavacanānto
vikṛtaḥ | caturthastu ubhayorapi pravṛttiḥ vikāraśceti | kā yuktiścatuṣṭaye ?
 uttaraṃ---
prathamasya tāvat pakṣasyeyaṃ yuktiḥ vikṛtāva
bhidhānākāṅkṣāyāṃ yadyavikṛtamapi abhi-
dhānaṃ na śaknotyabhidhātuṃ tadā dhikāraniyogo niyuṅkte | sambhavati tvavikāreṇaiva bahuvaca-
nāntasyābhidhānaṃ, prakṛtau pāśamātravivakṣayā bahuvacanāntasya prayogāt sādhudṛṣṭiḥ[ṣṭam]
prakṛtivacca dvipāśakāyāmapi vikṛtau tenaivāvikṛtenābhidhānaṃ sambhavati | ekavacanā-
ntastu ekasaṅkhyākapāśābhidhāyitvāt vikṛtāvapi saṅkhyābhidhānāyohitavyaḥ
prāpnoti | na ca tadyuktaṃ avikṛtenaivābhidhānasiddherna vikāraṃ prayuṅkte |

dvitīyasyeyaṃ yuktiḥ tāmāha---ūhavādyāha
---anūhavādinā nāprāptirekavaca-
nāntasya śakyate vaktum | pāśamātrasya vivakṣitatvāditi tvayaivoktam | tataśca
prātipadikārtha(ma)sya sambhavāt bhavitavyamekavacanāntena mantreṇa | na ca tasyāvi-
kṛtasya prayogassambhavati, anvitasvārthatvāt | prakṛtau samavetārthatvāt sa cedeka-
vacanānto vikṛtaḥ tadā sampannamanvitābhidhānam | etadeva vivṛṇoti---samaveta-
prakāśanamiti hetoḥ kimananvitābhidhānena
 | bahuvacanānteneti vartate |

[p. 288]

tṛtīye tviyaṃ yuktiḥ | tāmāha---ayamabhiprāyaḥ | tvayaivokta
pāśamātrasamavāyānna śakyate mantro
 vinivartayitumiti tena bahuvacanāntenāpi
mantreṇa bhavitavyam | nanu cānanvitābhidhānānna tena prayojanamastītyuktam
 |
pariharati---nānvitābhidhānanyāyaprāptiḥ
 anvitārthābhidhāyyayamityanena rūpeṇa na
prāptiryena tadabhāvādaprāptiḥ syāt |

kathaṃ
 tarhi ? samavetābhidhānena prāptikamityuktam
 | tulyaścāyamartha-
samavāyaḥ ekavacanāntaśca bahuvacanāntaśca mantraḥ pratīyate | na prāptirvārayituṃ
śakyate | ananvitasya prakṛtāvivātrāpi sādhutā bhaviṣyati | ekavacanāntasya punaḥ
svārthenaikatvenānvito yaḥ
 samavetaḥ pāśaḥ tadabhidhānātta
dadhāyakatvāt tathaivātrāpi
sādhutvama
bhidhāne sādhuteti hetorno(n)haḥ |

rāddhāntastu mantradvayaṃ vikṛtaṃ prayoktavyam | nanu cāsamavetābhidhāyi
bahuvacanāntamantra
 [ityuktam] | [tasya kathaṃ]sādhutā bhaviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ | pariharati
-satyaṃ śakyata iha dvipāśake karmaṇi noktam | nanu ca prakṛtivatkuryāditi
ihāpi tadvadeva prāpnoti
 | prakṛtau vivakṣitaṃ samavetārthaṃ syāt | na caitattatra
vivakṣitam | kuta ityāha---satyaṃ śakyata iha dvipāśake karmaṇi noktam | nanu
ca prakṛtivatkuryāditi ihāpi tadvadeva prāpnoti
 | prakṛtau vivakṣitaṃ samavetārthaṃ
syāt nacaitattatra vivakṣitam | kuta ityāha---tatra hi pāśamantrānvayādeva
pāśārtho vivakṣitaḥ vacanayogastu prayogasādhutāsampattyarthaḥ | nābhidhānā-
nvayaḥ | (na ca) yadi ca prayogo’yaṃ sādhuḥ tadā sādhutve sati atrāpi
kena prayogo vāryate
 | pariharati-nātra śakyate sādhutāṃ vaktum
 | kuta
ityāha---sādhutā hi prayogādbhavati | aprayukte’pi lakṣaṇādvā
 | tatra prayoga-
stāvat bahubacanāntasya pāśayornāsti | (pedikaṃ)[vacana]lakṣaṇāpi chandasi vidyate supāṃ
supo bhavantīti | nacohaḥ cchanda ityuktam | tasmādapabhraṃśa evātra bahuvacanāntaḥ |
tena sādhutvāya dvivacanānta ūhitavyaḥ | atra pūrvaprayogāt bhinnalakṣaṇamuktamiti
matvā codayati---kathamaprayuktasya lakṣaṇamiti
 | apratyakṣaprayogasyetyabhiprāyaḥ
yasyāpi prakṛtau pratyakṣeṇa prayogo na dṛśyate tasyāpi lakṣaṇadarśanāt sādhutvamavagamyata
ityarthaḥ | ataḥ prakṛtau prayuktasya pāśadvayamātre vivakṣite mantrārthe kimiti
vacanārtha ādriyate
 | uttaraṃ---sādhutvāyeti
 brūmaḥ | vibhaktiṃ vinā na kevalasya

[p. 289]

sādhutvam | tathāpi kimityāha---asādhoḥ pratiṣedhāt
 | tathā gatārthānāmapi
sādhutvāya laukikā vacanamādriyamāṇā dṛśyante devadattayajñadattābhyāmidaṃ kāryaṃ idaṃ
kṛtamityevamādiṣu | tasmāt sūktaṃ vikṛtayorapi mantrayoḥ prayoga iti |

|| 5 || vipratipattau vikalpaḥ syāt samatvāt guṇe tvanyāyya-
kalpanaikadeśatvāt || 1 ||

pāśamantradvayamudāharaṇam
 | anena pāśamantrasamākhyāmutkarṣopayoginīmāha |
nanu codakaprāptaṃ bahuvacanāntapāśamantramāśritya vicāraḥ kṛtaḥ | nanu
prakṛtāvananupraviṣṭasya kathaṃ codakataḥ prāptiḥ
 | ataḥ kathaṃ punarutkarṣāṃśaṅketyatrāha-
arthāccedamadhikaraṇaṃ
 ādhastyāt pūrvaṃ draṣṭavyam | etadadhikaraṇasiddhamevānupraveśaṃ
gṛhītvā sā cintetyabhiprāyaḥ | sākṣādasaṅgatatvācca pūrvamiyaṃ cintā na kṛtā | idānīṃ
pūrvādhikaraṇaprasaṅgena saṅgatiḥ | atra pūrvapakṣavādī sakalavākyānvayābhāvāt
bahuvacanayuktasya mantrasyotkarṣaṃ manyate
 | asyārthaḥ-liṅgaviniyojyo’yaṃ mantraḥ
tena yatraiva pāśabahutvānvitaṃ pāśonmocanaṃ prakāśyate tatraiva pāśamantrasamākhyayā
labdhasāmānyasambandho viniyujyate iti utkṛṣyaḥtava bahuvacanāntaḥ | nanu pāśavi-
muktyarthatvānmantrayoḥ bahuvacanaviniyuktepi tasyārthasya sambhave notkarṣo nyāyyaḥ |
pāśavimoko’yaṃ mantrābhyāṃ prakāśyaḥ | bahuvacanāntenāpi mantreṇa śakyata
evāsau prakāśayitumiti kathamutkarṣaḥ
 | pariharati---satyaṃ pāśavimokārthaḥ
sa bhavati bahuvacanatyāgena tu
 saḥprakṛtigatena | na cā (vā’)nvitārthasya bahuvaca-
nasya tyāge pramāṇamasti | tasmādyatra karmaṇi sahānvayassamavetārtho’yaṃ mantraḥ tatrānena
mantreṇa bhavitavyam | athāpyavāntaravākyāśrayaṇaṃ syāt | na ca mahāvākye tada-
pāśrayaṇamastīti hetorutkarṣa eva nyāyyaḥ |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---prātipadikāśritaṃ hyatra bahutvaṃ pratīyate
vibhaktito’vagamāt na yathā saṅkhyāśabdādivat svatantram
 | yathā saṅkhyā-
śabdena caturo muṣṭīnnirvapatītyevamādiṣu prātipadikasambandhanirapekṣā saṅkhyā vidhīyate
na tathā vibhakti[taḥ]pratīyate | vibhakti[śrutayaḥ]śrutyā’bhihitaṃ prātipadikāśritameva
svārthamāhuḥ | tataḥkiṃ ?
 uttaraṃ---idaṃ tato bhavati
 pāśārthāśrayi[yī] bahutvasyānvayaḥ
pratīyate | tathāpi kimityāha---pāśārthaśca vimokasambandhitayā pratīya
-

[p. 290]

māno’nena mantreṇa nābhidhīyata iti[na]śakyate vaktum | abhidhīyate cet
so’rthaḥ tadā tadarthatvānmantrasya tadabhi(dhāyakatvā)hito mantrārthaḥ yadi
viniyogāpekṣo mantrārtho bhavet
 | yadi viniyogānapekṣo mantrārthaḥ, prātipadi-
kasthaliṅgasya prathamopanipātāt prādhānyāt prātipadikārthaviṣayasya vibhaktiliṅgāt
balīyastvāt ekasminnapi pāśe mantrasya viniyogāt bahuvacanamavivakṣitārthamantraphala-
miti niścīyate | viniyogataśca mantrārthāvagama ityuktam | anyathāhi kāryārthatā
mantrasya syāt | evameva yasya kasyacit anuvādaḥ prāpnoti | tataśca sāpekṣatvaprasa-
ṅgādaprāmāṇyam | viniyogāyatte tu mantrārthe yāvatyeva viniyogaḥ tāvadeva prakāśyam |
arthāntarasyollāpastu viṣabhakṣaṇavannāntarīyakaḥ ityanena nyāyena avivakṣita eveti
nāprāmāṇyam | tena viniyogāpannameva mantrārthāvadhāraṇam | yata eva prātipadikaliṅga-
nyāyenānutkarṣaḥ ata eva prakaraṇamabhyuccayavṛttitayopanyastaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa
 |
yadi mūlayuktyantaraṃ na syāt tadā prakaraṇameva mūlayuktirbhavet | na ca tasya mūla-
yaktitvasambhavaḥ | śrutiliṅgavākyebhyaḥ aprabalatvāttasya | vākyabalenotkarṣo nyāyyo
yataḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ prakaraṇasyābhyuccayayuktitāpyastītyāha---pratyakṣe hi kartavyatā-
vacanasambandhe’
dhikāravidhisambandhe sambhavati nāpratyakṣeṇādhikāravidhinā
sambandho nyāyya ityabhiprāyaḥ
 |

upasaṃharati---ata āśrayavaśādanuguṇavibhaktyarthaḥ pratipattavyaḥ
saṅkhyā-
śrayabhūtaikapāśānuguṇo vibhaktyartho vijñeyaḥ | sa ca karmamātrapratipattiparo bhavati
 |
sahaikatvena ekasaṅkhyāyā avivakṣitatvāt karmamātrapratītiparo mantraḥ | yadi vā
saṅkhyā vivakṣitā tenaivaikatvena saha mantrārthaḥ |

nanu sahapakṣe’rthāntaravacanatāpi kalpitā bhaviṣyati bahuvacanasyai-
katvamapi | bahuvacanasya vācyābhāva iti kalpitam
 | tataścaikārthatvaprasaṅgo
bahuvacane’pi sambhavati | tataśca svāyattepi prayoge ekasmin bahuvacanaprasaṅgaḥ | pari-
harati---na svāyatte prayoge prasaṅgaḥ aniyoge lokato’vagamāt
 | ekatvādi-
niyamenaikavacanatādyavagamāt | tasmān prayogasādhutaivātrānabhidhānasādhutvam | kathaṃ
tarhi ekatvāvagatiḥ tatrāha---karmamātravacanatvādapi ekatvasiddheḥ
 karmamātre’vagate
nirapekṣatayā ekatvamavagamyate | dvitvādikā hi saṅkhyā apekṣābuddhinibandhanā | yata
ekasmin bahuvacanamavācakam ata eva ca lakṣaṇakārā api ekasmin bahuvacanamiti

[p. 291]

yatnāntaramārabhante | anyathā hi svabhāvasiddhatvādavācyaṃ syāditi | svabhāvasiddha-
tvānna na vācyatvam | kathaṃ tarhi bahuṣu vacanāprāptau ekasmin bahuvacanamiti smaraṇaṃ ?
prāptyarthameva | tasmāt prayogasādhutaiveti arthe samanvayābhāvāt |

|| 6 || apūrve tvavikāro’pradeśātpratīyeta || 20 ||

iha punarekapatnīke prayoge svārthasambandhasambhavānna dvayorbahuṣu vā sādhutā
śakyate vaktum | pāśe hyananyagatitayā tathātvāśrayaṇam | iha tu gatirastīti na
prayogasādhutvāśrayaṇam |

nanu cātrāpi prayogasādhutvāya bhaviṣyati
 | pariharati---na ekasmin
svārthasambhavāt
 na [svārthe] sambhavati prayogasādhutā | tasmāt sādhutvāyaiva dvayoḥ
bahuṣu cohaḥ kartavyaḥ |

atha kasmādekapatnīka evāsya prayogo na bhavati
 | yathā vrīhipakṣe
syonaṃ te iti mantraḥ | tataścoktoho na yuktaḥ | pariharati---naivamupapadyate sarvatra
prātipadikārthasambhavāt pāśavat sarvatraiva viniyogaḥ
 | tatra hi yaveṣu samavetārtha-
taiva nāsti | iha punaḥ dvayoḥ bahuṣu ca patnīprātipadikasya samavetatvamasti iti na
vacanāntarānurodhena niyamaśśakyate vaktum | na ca samavetārthasambhave prayogasādhutā
vacanasyeti ūhena bhavitavyam | atrābhidhīyate---na tāvadekavacanakārye dvitvaṃ
bahutvaṃ vā śrūyate nīvāravat
 | yathā nīvārā vrīhīṇāṃ kārye vidhīyante tathā
ekatvakārye dvitvaṃ bahutvaṃ vā na śrūyate | tataḥ kim
 | uttaraṃ-idaṃ tato bhavati |
tadvatsamavetārthamiti na śakyate vaktum
 | yathā prakṛtau vrīhiśabdaḥ samavetārthaḥ
tathaikavacanamapīti na śakyate vaktuṃ sarveṣāmekadvitvabahutvānāṃ samanvitatvāt | nanu
ca prayogasādhutāpi na śakyate vaktum
 | pariharati---na śakyata eva vaktuṃ
prātipadikārthamātrasya sannahanayogāt dvayoḥ bahuṣu ca samavetārthānavagamāt
 |
asyārthaḥ---patnīprātipadikamātrasyāpekṣitasaṅkhyāviśeṣasya mantreṇa sannahanena yogāt |
dvayoḥ patnyoḥ bahuṣu vā ekatvābhāvāt ekavacanasya samavetārthatvānavagamāt |
aviśeṣeṇaikasminnapi samanvayaśśakyate vaktum |

ekatvapratipattyarthamidamekavacanaṃ na bhavati dvayoḥ bahuṣu ca tadabhāvāt | kintu sarva-
syāpi savyāpāratayā karmatvasambandhārthamiti | ataḥ prayogasādhutaivātrāpi iti na

[p. 292]

yopragavirodhaḥ | nanu caikasmin samanvayo na śakyate parihartum
 | pariharati---
na śakyaṃ nāma prayogaviśeṣāt dvayoḥ bahupu cārthasamanvayāt prayogasādhu-
taivetyadoṣaḥ
 |

|| 7 || vikṛtau cāpi tadvacanāt || 21 ||

atra svāyattaṃ prayogaṃ manvānasya pūrvapakṣaḥ
 | yathā svāyatte karmaṇi
svāyattaḥ prayoga iti mattvā dvivacanamūhitavyam tathehāpi vikṛtau dvipatnyāmapi patnī-
śabde dvicanasyoha eva yuktaḥ svāyattatvātprayogasyeti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntastu prakṛtivat sādhutvasambhavāt [na]svāyatte prayoge pramāṇamastīti |
yadi vaidikena prayogeṇa kāryaṃ na siddhayati tatra svāyattaḥ prayoga āśrīyate | patnī-
śabdaścaikavacanānto dvitve bahutve ca siddhaprayoga iti na svāyattaprayogāvakāśaḥ |
pāśe tu dvitve bahuvacanāntasya prayogādarśanādasti svāyattaprayogāvakāśaḥ | yatpunaḥ
bhāṣye tathaiva ca nāvivakṣā tadvacanenetyuktam | tadayuktam | pāśādhikaraṇavirodhāt
avivakṣitepi vibhaktyarthe yathārthameva vibhaktiḥ prayoktavyeti | tenātrāpi dvivacana-
bahuvacanayoreva prayogaḥ prāpnotīti pariharati---yata evāyaṃ pakṣo dṛṣṭaḥ ata
evāthavetyuktvā
 pakṣāntaramuktam pūrva[ā]paritoṣeṇa | tadvayācaṣṭe-tadvadeva sādhutva-
sambhavāt
 | yathā prakṛtau dvitvabahutvayorapi prayogāt ekavacanasya sādhutā patnī-
śabde tathā vikṛtāvapyanūhaḥ |

|| 8 || adhrigau savanīyeṣu tadvat samānavidhānāścet || 22 ||

samānavidhānamiti kṛtvā cinteyam | anena viśeṣeṇa tadvadati-
deśo’yaṃ na viśeṣāntaraṃ vaktavyam
 | samānavidhānaprayojanametadityarthaḥ |

|| 9 || pratinidhau cāvikārāt || 23 ||

pratinidhau vrīhikāryāpatteḥ mukhye ca vrīhiśabdasya samavetārthatvāta
ūhaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī
 | yadi pratinidhiḥ vrīhayaśca svātantryeṇopadiṣṭā bhaveyuḥ
tadā mantro na bhavedeva | vrīhikāryāpannaśya pratinidhiḥ vrīhiśabdaśca samavetārtha
iti yukta evohaḥ |

[p. 293]

rāddhāntastu na vrīhikārye nīvārā upadiśyante
 | kintu vrīhyupadeśopa-
pattyarthaṃ kiñcidvikalaṃ vrīhyuddeśaṃ sampādayan nīvārāṇāmupādānam | na copadeśopapattau
kāryāpattiḥ tasmā eveti tadabhidhāna eva bhavitumarhatīti | tasmāt pratinidhāvapi
vrīhaya evetīha vrīhiśabdenaivābhidhānaṃ yuktam |

samyañcamarthaṃ buddhayā coditi (?) tadevedamutpatitam | yadi pratipādyamapratipāditaṃ
nirākṛtaṃ ca | adhyāsitatadbhāvo’dhyāropitatadbhāvo vā gauṇārtha iti
 |
adhyāso bhedāgrahaṇam | adhyāropo viparītakhyātiḥ | ubhayaṃ caitadayuktaṃ bhedena
nīvārāṇāmavagaterityuktam |

svābhiprāyaṃ rāddhāntī vivṛṇoti---nāyaṃ doṣaḥ bhūyo’vayavasāmānyenaiva
vrīhīṇāmevopapādanaṃ śaktitaḥ kṛtamiti
 tadabhidhānameva vrīhiśabdena kriyata iti
[na]śabdāntarāpattiḥ | avayavaparyantatvādākṛteḥ sādhanabhāvasya | vrīhyabhāve’vikalā-
vayavopasaṃhārābhāvepi katipayavrīhyavayavasvīkārāya pratinidherupādānamiti vrīhitayo-
pāttānāṃ vrīhiśabdenaivābhidhānaṃ yuktam | na cādhyāropādhyāsau, tatpratyayādavayavāntara-
pratīteḥ | yadi tathaivāvayavo(pāyoḥ)pādānaṃ kimiti tasminnīvāropādānamiti tatrāha
---tadvayatiriktasya vrīhyavayavavyatiriktasyātropādānaṃ
 nāntarīyakam | tairvinā
vrīhīṇāmevopādānāśakteḥ ityadoṣaḥ | tasmānnāyamūhasya viṣayaḥ tatsthānāpattya-
bhāvāditi |

atra pūrvapakṣavādī brūte---vrīhyapacāre’vaśyaṃ (a)mukhye karmaṇi dravyāntaropādā-
ne’vaśyaṃbhāvini sādṛśyena taddravyopāditsāniyama iti | rāddhāntī tu upadeśenaiva
kevalaṃ sampādayituṃ sadṛśamupādīyata iti |

|| 10 || saṃsargiṣu cārthasyāsthitaparimāṇatvāt || 27 ||

atra bhāṣyakāreṇaivamuktam---"nanu prakṛtāvapyanyāyanigada evāyam | dvayo-
ścakṣuṣorekavacanānto yataḥ tenoho na yukta" iti tadākṣipati---yeyaṃ samavetārtha-
paricodanā naivāsāvanūhāyālam | kathaṃ ?
---uttaraṃ---dvayorekavacanaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ
dvipaśukeṣu karmasu adhiṣṭhānabahutve asādhutaiva prāpnoti pāśānitivat
 |
dvayoḥ pāśayoḥ pāśānityasādhu ekasmin darśanāt | tathā’tra dvayordarśanādekavavacanasya
bahuṣvasādhutā | tasmānniṣprayojanaiveyaṃ paricodanā
 | samādhatte---ayamabhiprāya
-

[p. 294]

ścodakasya | samavetatve punarūhaḥ kathaṃ syāditi
 | asyārthaḥ---codakasyāya-
māśayaḥ---adhiṣṭhānabhedenāsamavetārthatvaṃ mayā coditaṃ, samavetārthasiddhaye pūrvapakṣavādī
tejasa ekatvaṃ nāsamavetārthatvamiti parihāraṃ vakṣyati | tathāpi dvipaśuṣvapi tejasa
ekatvāt samavetārthatvepi anūho bhavediti | evaṃ codanāṃ samarthya parihārabhāṣyaṃ
vyācaṣṭe---tadidamayuktam | yadbhavatā coditamanyāyanigado’yamiti saṃsargi-
tvāttejasaḥ satyapyadhiṣṭhānabhede tatsamavetārthatvaṃ manyate 
|

parihṛte codakaḥ svābhiprāyamāha---ayamabhiprāyaḥ---eṣāmanayoriti vā
kṛte mantre asādhutā na śakyate vaktuṃ pāśānitivat iti
 | vaidharmyadṛṣṭānto’yam |
yathā pāśānityasādhutā dvayoḥ tathātrāsādhunā nāsti ekavacanāntasyāpi mantrasyeti
codanā | atra pūrvavādī sambandhibhedāt paśubhedāttejaso bhedaṃ manvānaḥ ūhaṃ vadati |
tejodvaye tejobahutve ca ekavacanāntasyāsādhutvādūho yuktaḥ tejobhedaśca paśubhedāt |
evaṃ pūrvapakṣite rāddhāntacchāyayā paricodayati---adhiṣṭhānato vā kimiti
bhedo na vivakṣitaḥ
 yadi sambandhibhedādbhedaḥ tadā adhiṣṭhānabhedādapi bhedaḥ
syāt | tataśca prakṛtāvapi kathamekavacanam | anyasya bhedepyanyasya bhedo nāstītya-
ṣṭhānabhedo na vivakṣitaḥ | tataśca paśubhedādapi bhedo na vivakṣaṇīya iti parihāra-
bhāṣyaṃ vyācaṣṭe---anupādānādhiṣṭhānayoriti parihṛtam | asyeti punaḥ paśoḥ
rupādānamastīti
 | asyārthaḥ---adhiṣṭhānanibandhano bhedo nāśrīyate tadavacchedena
tejaso’nabhidhānāt asyetyanyena tu śabdena paśorupādānāt paśvavacchedena tejaso’
bhidhānāt tannibandhano yukta eva bhedo vivakṣitum | tasmādūhaḥ |

rāddhāntastu na śabdābhedādūho yuktaḥ | cakṣuśśabdasya yo’rthastejolakṣaṇa-
tasyābhedādekatvādūho na yuktaḥ | bhavatu sambandhinaḥ paśorbhedaḥ tathāpi na śabdānta-
rārthasya cakṣurādeḥ bhedo yuktaḥ | śabdāntarārthasya paśorbhede yadi tejaso
nāsti[bhedaḥ] kathaṃ tasmin cakṣuḥ
 [padaprayogaḥ] uttaraṃ---adhiṣṭhānabhede lakṣaṇa-
yaiva
 | bhinne’sminnadhiṣṭhānatvameva vivakṣitvā loka evaṃ prayuṅkte | cakṣuśśabda-
syādhiṣṭhānaparatvāt tatra tadabhidhitsāvagamāt | iha punaḥ sūryamasya cakṣurgamayatāditi
nādhiṣṭhānābhidhitsā’vagamyate prakṛtigāmitvādadhiṣṭhānakameva hi tadvacanam | nādhiṣṭhānasya
sūryaprakṛtitā kintu tejasa iti tatpuna[tpara]ścakṣuśśabda ityanūhaḥ | liṅgamuktam
 |

|| 11 || ekadhetyekasaṃyogādabhyāsenābhidhānaṃ syāt || 29 ||

[p. 295]

idānīṃ cintyate---kiṃ yathā prakṛtau mantrārtho varṇitaḥ tathaiva vikṛtāvapi uta
prakārāntareṇāpi samavetārthānvayopapatteḥ varṇanīya iti | tatra [yadi]tathaiva vikṛtāvapi
tadā ekadhāsya tvacamācchyatāditi prakṛtau sakṛttvābhidhānāt vikṛtāvapi tadeva kartavyaṃ
ityabhyāsaḥ syāt ekadhaikadhā iti | anyathāpi yadi mantrārthavarṇanā tataḥ sahāyārtha-
tayā’nūha iti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī viniyogāntara[vat] prayogāntarepi mantrasyārthāntaraṃ
na doṣāyeti manvānaḥ prakārāntareṇāpyanūhamuktavān | nanu ca prakṛtivadityasyā-
rthāntarakalpanāyāṃ pramāṇaṃ nāsti [śaktyai] vopapatteḥ upadeśāntare tu pūrvasyānu-
papattikāraṇamarthāntaravarṇanena anyatrāpyanūho [bhavitā] tathā’nūha eva syāditi |
pariharati---ko’yamanūho nāma arthāntaravarṇanahetuḥ ?
 uttaraṃ---ārṣānugrahaḥ
 |
punaḥ pṛcchati---arṣo nāma kaḥ
 ? uttaraṃ-svādhyāyakāle yatsvarūpam
 | rāddhā-
ntyāha---anugṛhītaṃ svarūpaṃ prakṛtau vikṛtau ca gauṇaṃ nāsti kāryatastu
tatprāptyavagamāt yathākāryameva varṇanaṃ nyāyyam
 | klṛptaṃ ca sakṛttvaprati-
pādanaṃ prakṛtau kāryamiti vikṛtāvapi tatsiddhayartha ūha eva yuktaḥ | upadeśe tu svarūpata
eva prāptiriti tadanurūpaṃ kāryam | bhavatu kāryataḥ prāptiḥ tathāpi kimityāha---
kārye cārthāntaraklṛptau pramāṇaṃ nāsti prakṛtiklṛptyaivopapatteḥ
 | tasmādūha evātra
nyāyya iti nyāyavido manyante nārthāntarānugraham |

|| 12 || medhapatitvaṃ svāmidevatasya samavāyātsarvatra ca
prayuktatvāt || 32 ||

atrobhayasya svāmidevatasya nimittatvopapatteḥ padārthatve ubhayaṃ svāmidevataṃ
mantrārtha iti manvānaḥ pūrvapakṣavādī prakṛtāveva bahuṣu dvivacanaika[vacana]darśanāt vikṛtā-
vapi prayogasambhavādanūhaṃ manyate | nanu caikenaiva padārthena medhapatiśabdasya
nimittabhāvasya caritārthatvāt kathaṃ dvayarthatā mantrasya
 ? yameva hi padārthaṃ upa-
sthāpayati medhapatiśabdaḥ sa evaiko mantrārtho yuktaḥ kathaṃ dvayarthatā mantrasya gṛhyamāṇa-
viśeṣatvāt | dvayamapi medhapatiśabdenopasthāpyate
 dviṣṭhānyapi vākyāni bhavantī-
tyabhiprāyaḥ | pakṣāntaraṃ tu satyaṃ dvayamupanyastam | yadi na gṛhyate viśeṣaḥ tatrobhayasya
nimittatvaṃ bhavadbhavet | gṛhyate tu viśeṣaḥ | svāmiparatve dvivacanaikavacanayorupapa-
tyā’rthau bhavataḥ | svāminyekavacanasya dampatyordvivacanasyopapatteḥ | na devatāparatve,
ekatvāddevatāyāḥ | tasmāt svāmiparo’yaṃ mantraḥ tadanusāreṇa cohitavyaḥ | devatā-

[p. 296]

paratvaṃ tu rāddhāntī manyate | evaṃ prātipadikārthoṃ na bhūtapūrvavṛttyā vyākhyāto bhavati |
svāmino hi taddeyaṃ pratyāyitam | na ca bhūtapūrvaḥ sambandhaḥ[yuktaḥ] | na ca bhavatyarthe
bhūtapūrvāśrayaṇaṃ yuktam | yatpunaridaṃ gaṇābhiprāyamekavacanaṃ varṇitaṃ gaṇasyaiva devatātva-
miti smāritaṃ ekavacanāntasyānutkarṣārthaṃ tat pāśabahuvacananyāyenānutkarṣahetau
sambhavati ekasya hetoḥ kena hetunā ayaṃ sambhavati | abhidhānasādhutve prayogasādhutaivā
śrayituṃ na yuktam iti | pāśamantre hi bahuvacanasya prayoge sādhutā abhidhānasādhu-
tvā[t]sambhavati | agatikā gatiḥ | tenābhidhānasādhutvapradarśanāt gaṇābhiprāyameka
vacanaṃ varṇitaṃ, kiṃ prayojanamityāha---ūhe ca viśeṣopapattiḥ prayogasādhu-
tvapradarśanārthatve bahiṣṭhadevatāsu prāpnoti
 | gaṇābhiprāyatve’bhidhānasādhutve sati
sa neti |

nanu dvayadhiṣṭhānāyā devatāyā ekatvepi vacanamanupapannaṃ bhavati
 |
pariharati---nānupapannaṃ ekena hetunā samavetārthatvena
 | kutaḥ punassabhavetārthami-
tyāha---dviṣṭhasyāpyadhiṣṭhānagataikyopapatteḥ
 |

nanu ca yadyapyadhiṣṭhānagatadvitvaṃ samavetaṃ tathāpi kārakagaṃ na bhavati tatprakāśane
mantrasyānarthakyaṃ ityatrāha---samavetārthā
bhidhāne ca mantrasyārthavattoktā adhiṣṭhānamapi
smāritaṃ kārakaṃ smārayatyeveti bhāvaḥ | tasmāddevatāvaśādūhaḥ dvivacanāntasya adhiṣṭhāna-
bahutve ekatve cohaḥ ekabahutvayoriti sthitam |

|| 13 || niyamo vā bahudevate vikāraḥ syāt || 41 ||

atraikavacanāntamantrasya nivṛttiṃ manyate | ekavacanāntatayā ūhena
na tu pravartata eva | kathaṃ manyate
 ? uttaraṃ---dvitve hi ekavacanāntasyāyaṃ
prayoga iti bhrāmyato bahuṣu apabhraṃśaḥ sādhvekavacanamiti bahuṣvasādhutvā-
dūho yuktaḥ
 |

nanu saṃsargidevagaṇo devatetyuktam
 | tenātrāpi tadabhidhāyakamupapadyata
evaikavacanam | pariharati---syāt dvitva eva taditi manyate
 | asyārthaḥ
-dvitve prayogaḥ sthitaḥ saṃsargokterityuktam | tenātrāpi dvitve vā saṃsarge vā tatraika-
vacanam na bahutva iti manyate | tasmāta bahuṣvapabhraṃśa eveti | nivṛttiḥ bhramaḥ yathā
pāśāniti dvayoḥ |

[p. 297]

rāddhāntamāha---pravarteta vā ekavacanāntameva
 | kuta ityaha---saṃsargo hyatra
vivakṣitaḥ (na ca) dvayoḥ bahuṣu vā saḥ
 saṃsargamātraṃ hyatrāviśiṣṭam | sarvo hi
saṃsarga eka eva | tena tatra yuktamekavacanam | abhidhānavipratipattau hi
prayoganiyamo bhavati | na punassampratipattāvapi adarśanamātrāt anyābhidhāyino-
’nyatra punarabhidhānasampratipattiḥ yatraivābhidhāyakatvaṃ tatraiva prayogaḥ tatra tadvidha evānyatra
prayogadarśanamātreṇa niyamaḥ | kuta ityaha---adya jātāyāṃ gavi gośabdasyā-
sādhutāprasaṅgāt
 | na cāsādhutā tasmāt prakṛtivadatrāpyekavacanaṃ sādhu |

|| 14 || arthāntare vikāraḥ syāddevatāpṛthaktvāt ekābhi-
samavāyāt syāt || 43 ||

atra saṃsargamātrāviśeṣāt sādhutvaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | adhiṣṭhānasaṃsargo vā
bhavatu, devatāsaṃsargo vā | sarvathā tāvat saṃsargasyaikatvādekavacanaṃ sādhu iti |

rāddhāntastu devatāsaṃsargaḥ [mantrārthaḥ] na saṃsargamātram
 | aṅgatvāddeva-
tāgatasaṃsargo mantrārthaḥ na saṃsargamātram | tasyānaṅgatvāt | nanu anaṅgasyāpi samaveta-
tvādabhidhānaṃ yuktamityatrāha---aṅge hi samavetārthābhidhānaṃ mantrāṇāmuktam
 | na
sambhavamātreṇa | aṅge hīti ‘nimittātkarmayoge’
 iti saptamī | tenāyamarthaḥ-aṅgārtha-
maṅgaṃ pratyāyayituṃ samavetārthābhidhānaṃ mantraiḥ kriyate | na punassambhavanmātrasaṃsargaḥ yāga-
bhedāt bhinneṣu yāgeṣu yā devatāḥ tāsāṃ saṃsargo na kvacit aṅgam | aṅgabhūtadevatārūpa-
prakāśakaścaikavacanānto mantraḥ | tasmāddevatābahutvādasādhvekavacanam | ata ūha
eveti siddham |

nanu caivaṃ sati ākṛtyadhikaraṇākṣepaḥ prāpnoti | kriyāviśeṣāṇāṃ
cābhidhānadarśanāt na tāvanmantrārthatā siddhayati
 | asyārthaḥ---tathā kriyā-
viśeṣaṇatayaivābhidhānaṃ darśitam | evaṃ ca darśayatā akṛtyadhikaraṇamākṣiptaṃ bhavati |
ākṛteḥ kriyāviśeṣaṇatvāsambhavāt anabhidhānaprasaṅgāt | samādhatte---astyatra
viśeṣaḥ devatābhidhānatve prātipadikasya siddha eva ca nopapattyarthaṃ saṃsargasya
devatātvāśrayaṇam
 | tena bhinnānāṃ yāgānāṃ yā eva devatāḥ tāsāṃ saṃsargo na

[p. 298]

devateti[na]mantrasyābhidheyaḥ | tadabhidhāne mantrasyā[na]nvayaprasaṅgāt | tatra punarā-
kṛtipratipattirevānvayahetuḥ sāmānyarūpeṇānvayāvagamāditi tatraiva prapañcenoktam |
tasmānnāgṛhītaviśeṣaṇeti vā ito vā ākṛtyā samānāvevānvitā kriyāyāṃ na saṃsarga
iti siddhaṃ devatābhidhānamasaṃsargābhidhānam | tasmānnākṣepa ākṛtyadhikaraṇasya |

iti mahopādhyāyaśālikanāthamiśrakṛtāyāṃ
ṛjuvimalāyāṃ pañjikāyāṃ navamasya
tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ

[p. 299]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

navamasyādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ

|| 1 || ṣaḍviṃśatirabhyāsena paśugaṇe tatprakṛtitvādguṇasya pravibhakta-
tvādavikāre hi tāsāmakārtsnyenābhisambandho vikārānna
samāsaḥ syādasaṃyogācca sarvābhiḥ || 1 ||

atra pañcapakṣā upanyasyante | pañcadhā mantrārthapratibhānāt | tatra paśupradhāno
mantrārtha ityekaḥ pakṣaḥ | saṅkhyāpradhāna iti dvitīyaḥ | vaṅkrīpradhāna iti tṛtīyaḥ |
avivakṣita evetyaparaḥ, ṣaḍviṃśatisambandhitāparaḥ pañcamaḥ | asmiṃśca pañcame pakṣe
evaṃ sati sambandhitoktā bhavati ṣaḍviṃśatiṣṣaḍviṃśatiranayoreṣāṃ vaṅkraya iti ṣaḍviṃśati-
pade’bhyaste paśubhyāṃ paśubhi[rvā]pratyekaṃ sambandhitā pratipāditā bhavati | vīpsā tu
vyāptumicchā tena dvirvacanadarśanāt ṣaḍviṃśatisaṅkhyaiva paśusambandhitayā pratipādayitu-
miṣṭeti gamyate | yathā “puruṣaḥ puruṣo nidhanamupaiti”
 iti puruṣe ekavacanohaḥ
sthita iti pratīkānuvādo’yam |

ṣaḍviṃśataya iti vacanohena saṅkhyāsvarūpasya gaṇanāvagamāt | saṅkhyā-
prādhānye1 pratītikaraṇatvenāviniyogādavivakṣaiva prāpnoti mantrārthasyeti [a]vikāreṇaiva
prayogaḥ | yena hi mantreṇa smṛtaṃ kriyate[sa karaṇamantra] nayanakālatvādadhriguvacanasya
tenākaraṇatvādarthasmaraṇe’viniyogādavivakṣitārthamuccāraṇamadṛṣṭārthamityanūhaḥ | vaṅkrīprādhānye
samasya vacanam | samasya vacane hi vaṅkrīgaṇanā pratīyate | tena tāsāṃ prādhānyāvagatiḥ |

kathaṃ punarviniyogavaśāt svīkṛte mantrārthe pañcadhā pratibhāyāt
 |
yāvatā yatraiva viniyogassa eva mantrārtho yuktaḥ | uttaraṃ-prakaraṇamātreṇa viniyoga
mamanvānasya pañcadhā pratibhāti mantrārthaḥ
 | prakaraṇamātreṇaiva mantrasya viniyogaḥ |
na ca śrutyā, nāpi vākyena, samākhyayā, sthānena vā |

nanu caitadayuktam---prakaraṇaviniyojyatve pañcadhā pratibhānaṃ uccāraṇa-
mātreṇaivāṅgatāsiddheravivakṣaiva nyāyyā | kvopayujyante’rthavikalpāḥ ?
 pariharati
1. nya-mā

[p. 300]

---satyamāha bhavān ācāryavānasi ! yadyapi prakaraṇaviniyojyatā tathā
pyarthālocanaṃ yuktameva
 | kadācidālokyamāne mantrārthe kasmiṃścidarthe dṛṣṭārthatā
bhavatīti na liṃṅgaṃ prakaraṇena viruddhayate | prakaraṇasya sāmānyasambandhāpādakatvāt | 
liṅgasya ca viśeṣasambandhāpādakatvāt | liṅgena ca dṛṣṭārthe sambhavati nādṛṣṭārthaṃ
[pra]tyarthi(nī)bhavitumarhati mantrapadārthālocanena liṅgāt kvacilliṅgaviniyogo labhyo na
viniyogādityanekadhā mantrārthālocanā, tatra paśuprādhānye saṅkhyāprādhānyasambandhini
ca prādhānye’nyānuṣṭhāne kiñcidadṛṣṭenopakṛtam | yadi paśavaḥ pradhānaṃ yadi vā saṅkhyā
svarūpeṇa paśuviśiṣṭatayā vā pratipadyamānā pradhānaṃ tatprakāśanasyānupayogitvāt
(a)dṛṣṭopakāritā mantrārthasya na bhavatīti vaṅkrīyapradhānataivopakāriṇī | ‘tā anuṣṭhyo
cyāvayatāt’
 iti sakaloddhāraṇāvagamāt vaṅkrīḥ anuṣṭhya prayatnena śamitāra ucyāvaya-
teti | anena sakaloddharaṇaṃ vaṅkrīṇāṃ pra(ā)tipadyate | anaṣṭhānaṃ
 anuṣṭhayā
prayatnaḥ parasyāstṛtīyāyā bhāvaḥ atassamasyameva vacanaṃ nyāyyam | etāvatīrvaṅkrī-
ruddharateti pratipādayitum |

nanu atrāpi yugapaduddharaṇāśakteśca bahudhā vā’nupaklṛptameva samasyavacanam |
yadi hi tāḥ samasya yugapaduddhriyeran tadā samasyavacanamupa(vṛta)padyata iti sūktaṃ
samasyavacanameva kāryamiti |

|| 2 || aśvasya catustriṃśat tasya vacanādvaiśeṣikam || 17 ||

atra pūrvapakṣaśaṅkā-kevalapadapratiṣedhabalena | [na]catustriṃśaditi brūyāditi
kevalaṃ catustriṃśatpadaṃ pratiṣidhyate | na vaiśeṣikamevāsya vacanaṃ kartavyam |
kathaṃ punarasmin pakṣe na vākyabhedaḥ ? catustriṃśatpadapratiṣedhāt ṣaḍviṃśati-
padavidhānācca vākyabhedaḥ prāpnoti
 | ṛk‌pratiṣedhe tu codakaprāptamantrānu-
vādo’yam ṣaḍviṃśatirityeva brūyāditi | tena[na]vākyabhedaḥ | uttaraṃ---irāpadava-
dbhaviṣyati ityabhiprāyaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ-yathaiverāpade vidhīyamāne vākyabhedo na bhavati
tathā’trāpi nāsti ṣaḍviṃśatipadavidhāne prāpta eva catustriṃśatpadābhāvo’nūdyate |

rāddhāntastu na kevala[pada]pratiṣedho’yam | kutaḥ ? brūyāditi
śravaṇāt
 | asyārthaḥ---prasaktasya hi pratiṣedho bhavati ṛcaśca vacanaṃ prasaktam |
na tu padamātrasya tasyārthapratipādakatvābhāvāt | tadāha - na kevalaṃ padavacane

[p. 301]

arthapratipādane vyāpriyete
 | vākyaṃ hi vacaḥ kāraṇamityuktam | tena prasakta eva
niṣidhyate natu padamātram |

nanu cerāpadavadbhaviṣyati padasyāpi pratiṣedhaḥ
 | pariharati-[na]tathā’tra
sambhavaḥ
 | kasmādityāha---ṣaḍviṃśatirityeva pratīyāt ityukte na catustriṃśatpada-
pratiṣedhaṃ pratīmaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ-na girāgireti brūyādityanuvādo’yam | anuvādaśca
pramāṇāntareṇa varṇyamāno na doṣamāvahati | tena yuktaṃ tatra padamātroccāraṇānuvādaḥ |
iha ṣaḍviṃśatipade vihitepi na catustriṃśatpadanivṛttiḥ prāptā tulyakāryatvābhāvāt---
tatrāpi udgānapadasamabhivyāhārāt
 | tatkāryānupraveśena airaṃ kṛtvodgeyamiti udgānena
gānāvayavenerāpa[dasya]sambandhaḥ | tenodgānakārakaṃ girāpadaṃ nivartate | iha punaḥ
vākyabhedādṛte naikakāryāvagatiriti vaiṣamyam | tena pratiṣedho yaktaḥ nānuvādaḥ |
padapratiṣedho nāvagamyate |

yuktyantarāṇyāha---vākyabhedaścāyuktaḥ
 ityuttarābhidhāyi | vaṅ‌kri(vi)prati-
patteścādṛṣṭārthatvaprasaṅgaḥ
 | yāvatyaśca vaṅkrayaḥ tāvatīḥ anuṣṭhyāditi ṣaḍviṃśatiśabdo
na śaknoti | tasmādṛca eva pratiṣedha iti sthitam |

|| 3 || vaniṣṭhusannidhānādurūkeṇa vapābhidhānam || 22 ||

atrāpi viśeṣaviniyogābhāvāt mantrārtha eva doṣo labhyata iti
tadartho vikalpate
 | saiva dṛṣṭārthatvāśrayaṇahetuḥ gṛddhraṃ vapāṃ manyamānā vaniṣṭhuṃ
mārāviṣṭetyucyamāne’dṛṣṭārthatā mantrasya
 | ulūkavacane hi adṛṣṭārthatā | vapoddharaṇa-
kāle viruddhatā ca vaniṣṭhoḥ lipitulyatvāt | yadyulūkaṃ dhyātvā vaniṣṭhurlevitavyetyu-
cyate tadā’dṛṣṭārthatā | atha ulūkasadṛśo vaniṣṭhurna lavitavya ityucyate tadā
viruddhārthatvādvaniṣṭhoḥ lavitavyatvāt | tasmātpadabhedamātra eva nyāyaḥ | urupadaṃ vistīrṇa-
vacanaṃ kapadaṃ ca samudāyalakṣaṇayā bhedovacanaṃ kaśaśabdopasthāpanamukhena bhedasvivapā-
lakṣaṇā | padabhede ca samāso bhavatu mā vā na kaścitprayojane viśeṣa iti | nirṇaya-
hetvabhāvādanirṇaya iti | yadi punaḥ svarādibhiḥ kaścidviśeṣārtho’virodhena sa āśraya-
ṇīya eva | tadabhāve punaḥna kiñcidvaiyarthyam | icchāto niyamaḥ | tathā cobhayaṃ pradarśitaṃ
bhāṣyakāreṇa |

|| 4 || praśasāsyabhidhānam || 23 ||

[p. 302]

atrāpi saiva vārtā mantrālocanena nirṇīyata iti
 | pūrvasmin yā vārtā
saivātrāpi prayogakalpikā bhavatu 1prayogakalpanābhayāttu pūrvotthitā nirasyate | asivā-
cakatve hi asinā bāhūddhāraṇaprayogaḥ kalpayitavyaḥ nānyathā samavetārthaprakāśana-
mupapadyate | na ca prayogāntaraṃ kalpayiṃtu śakyate svadhitisādhanasya prayogasya klṛptatvāt |
nanu ca prāśastye bāhvorvākyenābhidhīyamāne sūktabhāktvena tayoḥ devatātvaṃ prāpnoti |
atrāha prāśastyaṃ na punarbāhvordevatātvaṃ prakalpayati | kṛtsnoddharaṇatayāpyupapatteḥ

kṛtsna ud‌dhriyate na sakṛt svoddhāraṇo mantraḥ tasya bhāvastattā | yadyapi mantrārthopapa-
tterna devatāpādakatvaṃ sambhavācca kṛtsnayorbāhvoḥ praśastatāyāḥ ayamevārthato nyāyaḥ | kathaṃ
punarasyabhidhānapakṣe ūho bhāṣyakāreṇocyate | ucyate | māntravarṇikādupapannam | māntra-
varṇiko’siḥ | tenaikasminnasau praśasetyekavacanāntaṃ dvayoḥ bahuṣu ca dvivacanāntaṃ bahu-
vacanāntaṃ ca |

|| 5 || śyenaśalākaśyapakavaṣasrekaparṇeṣvākṛtivacanaṃ
prasiddhasannidhānāt || 25 ||

atrāpi dṛṣṭārthe sambhavati nādṛṣṭārthaṃkalpanā pratyarthinī yukteti kṛtsnoddhāraṇa-
taiva2nyā[yyā]śyenākṛtiḥ 1asya vakṣo yathā bhavati tathā kuruteti mantrārtho bhavet |
kṛtsnoddharaṇārthatayā dṛṣṭārthatā bhavati | kṛtsnoddhāraṇe hi tathārūpatā vakṣasaḥ | nanu
kṛtsnoddhāraṇe kathaṃ dṛṣṭārthatetyata āha---anyatra nyūnāvadānaprāpteḥ
 | homārthaṃ hi
yadavadānaṃ kṛtsnoddhāraṇe sati anyūnebhyo bhavati | nanu[mā]bhūdanyūnāvadānākāṅkṣe-

na sambhavati
 | ākāramātropalakṣaṇamitarasmin pakṣe yataḥ | “pakṣoktaṃ

 ityuktaṃ bhāṣye | viśasane ca tatprayojanaṃ kathitaṃ, nohe
 |
ūhe ca kathayitumucitam, anyathā lakṣaṇāsaṅgateriti paricodanā | parihārastu

 | tathā (tā)evaṃ coditānyū-
