|| 6 || prāsaṅgike prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate parārthatvāt
tadarthe hi vidhīyate || 28 ||

nanu ceyamadhikāracintā | tasyāḥ kaḥ prasaṅga ūhalakṣaṇe ?
 pariharati
---kāryatastvadhikāranirṇayo nopadeśa[ta]itīhaiva cintyate
 | yadā jyoti-
ṣmatyā agnireva kāryaṃ tadā agnihotrārthamuddhaṛtāgnyudvānavata evādhikāraḥ | yadā tu
naimittikamidaṃ karmānuṣṭhānaṃ tadā prāsaṅgiko’gnyudvānavato’pyadhikāraḥ | tena kāryata
evādhikāranirṇayo nopadeśāditi kāryacintāyāmūhacintā | pūrvapakṣavādī ārtya-
dhikaraṇanyāyaṃ manvāna aidamarthyamuddharaṇena vivakṣitamiti pūrvapakṣitavān
 |
yathā ārtyadhikaraṇa ubhayatvamavivakṣitaṃ tathehāpyaidamarthyamudvānasyāvivakṣitam | tena
prāsaṅgikodvānepi naimittikameveti pūrvapakṣitam |

rāddhāntastu kāryayogo’nyathā’gnihotre na syāta yadyaidamarthyaṃ
nādriyate
 | asyārthaḥ-udvātāgninirmittaṃ nodvānamātraṃ udvātāgneśca puruṣasyāgnisamā-
dhānameva kāryam | tatra yadi prāsaṅgikepi agnāvudvāte prāyaścittaṃ syāt tadā tasyāgni-
hotrāsambandhātagnihotrasambandho jyotiṣmatyā na syāt tasmādagnihotrārthamuddhaṛtāgnyudvāna
eva ca tatra naimittikamiti |

nanvadhikāriṇo viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitamityuktamārtyadhikaraṇe ityata āha---
viśeṣaṇāvivakṣā ca upadeśa ukto na kārye
 | tasmādvaiṣamyamārtyadhikaraṇena |
vākyabhedādavivakṣitatoktā | kāryataścātrānupraveśaḥ | ato vākyabhedo nāsti | ato’-
nyārthe(vyābhā)prāyaścittam | nanvagnerudvānaṃ nimittaṃ nodvānavānityatrāha-agniviśiṣṭo-
dvānanimittatā
 yathānirastā tathā pradarśitaṃ bhāṣye yasyāgnirudvāta iti śravaṇādagnireva-
nimittamiti | tasmādudvānaparīto’gnirnimittamiti sthite yukto rāddhānta iti sthitam |

|| 7 || dhāraṇe ca parārthatvāt || 29 ||

pūrvoktaṃ sarvamupapadyate nimittatayā[pro]ktam | tādṛśaṃ nāsti | kuta ityāha---
kṛtārthasya hyatra
 dhāraṇaṃ akṛtārthaṃ ca nimittamityuktam
 | akṛtārthasya samādhāna-
mapekṣitam apekṣitārthasamādhānārthamidam | (a)kṛtārthaścāgniratra yadarthamasau uddhaṛtaḥ
dhāryate tadanuṣṭhitameva | ataḥ parārthavadatrāstu | parisamūhanaparihārastūktaḥ prayogā-
ṅgatvena | prayogaḥ parisamūhanādīnāmaṅgatvāt |

[p. 304]

|| 8 || na tūtpanne codanā’prāptakālatvāt || 31 ||

kālo’ṅgaṃ, na cāṅgābhāve pradhānaśāstrārthānyatvaṃ tasmānnāṅgāntara-
nivṛttiḥ
 | asyārthaḥ - yadyapi darśārthoddharaṇe agnihotrārthoddharaṇakālo na labhyate
tathāpi mantraḥ prayoktumucita eva | aṅgābhāvepi pradhānānuṣṭhānadarśanāt āgneyavat
yathā vrīhyabhāvepi āgneyaśāstrārtho na vidyata[lupyata]iti tadanuṣṭhānaṃ tathehāpi yuktam |

rāddhāntastu satyamaṅga(ka)lopastathāpi nimittena tulyam
 | kuta
ityāha---kāle hi sati karma codyate na punaḥ karmaṇi sati kālaḥ
 upādānā-
śakteḥ | tena tadabhāve adhikārābhāvānnedamagnihotrārthameva |

|| 9 || pradānadarśanaṃ śrapaṇe taddharmabhojanārthatvāt saṃsargācca
madhūdakavat || 32 ||

payo na pākārthamiti
 | atra punarbhede[rvede]śabdapramāṇakā vayaṃ śabdataśca
vākyārthatāṃ payaso vidmaḥ na dhātvarthatām | carurāditya iti ādityasambandhaścarora-
vagamyate na payasaḥ, tasya ca pākārthataiva yuktā | na ca pakvā āmāśśakyate
vaktuṃ yena śabdo vihanyeta | bhojanārthasyāpi(stā)pākasādhakatvasya loke darśanāt |
athāpi nāntarīyakyapi pākāsiddhirna syāt sāmānyato dṛṣṭaṃ ca vede na kramata iti tatra
tatroktaṃ niyogārthastvananyaḥ |

|| 10 || abhyudaye dohāpanayaḥ svadharmā syāt pravṛttatvāt || 41 ||

|| 11 || apanayo vā’rthāntare vidhānāccarupayovat || 46 ||

śṛtaśabdaśravaṇāt prāyaṇīyena vaiṣamyam śrutisāmānyatvaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī |
śrapaṇaṃ hi havissaṃskāraḥ pratipannaḥ taddarśanāt | payaso haviṣṭvaṃ manyamānaḥ prāyaṇīya-
payasā vaiṣamyaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | abhyudayeṣṭitulyatvācca vākyānām |

rāddhāntastu nāsati pradeyārthatve saptamyartho’pahnotuṃ śakyate iti yathāśrata eva
vākyārthaḥ abhyudayeṣṭau pūrvāvagatatvāt pradeyatāyāssaptamyarthāpahnavaḥ kṛtaḥ | iha tu tada-
bhāvānna yuktaḥ | [śṛta] śabdo’pi lakṣaṇayāpyanvita eva saptamyarthaḥ payomātra-
lakṣaṇataḥ śabda sa hi payasi haviḥ śravaṇānna[bha]vati | vṛtta ityarthaḥ |

[p. 305]

|| 12 || śrayaṇānāṃ tvapūrvatvāt pradānārthe vidhānaṃ syāt || 45 ||

payasā maitrāvaruṇaṃ śrīṇātīti dṛṣṭārthālābhena pūrvapakṣavādī pradeyārthatāṃ
payaśśabdasya manyate | avarudhyaḥ tṛtīyāntasyāpi devatāsambandhaḥ | saptamyantasyeva
payasaḥ prāyaṇīye |

rāddhānastu na brūmastṛtīyayā devatātvasyāvaruddhatayā deyatvena na sambadhyata
iti | kintu nityadeyasambaddhena somena saṃyogaśravaṇāt tadarthataivāvasīyate | maitrā-
varuṇaṃ gṛhṇātītyetaccodanānusāreṇa somamātrasyaiva devatāsambandhaḥ pratīyate | sa pratīta-
devatāsambaddhaḥ somaḥ śrayaṇārthamihānūdyate tena somārthaṃ payo na pradānārtham | kutaḥ ?
śrutyantare hi deyadravyopalakṣaṇāyaiva devatārītirvidhīyeta | yo vā dīyate vidheyāntarasya
saṃyogāt ta[sya] vidhāne cāvaśyopalakṣaṇīyatvāt | dvi....śe hi śeṣisamarpakaṃ
maitrāvaruṇapadam | tasmāt somasya dvitīyāsaṃyogāt somārthataiva payasa iti nyāyyam |
śeṣamuktam
 |

|| 13 || paryagnikṛtānāmutsarge tādarthyamupadhānavat || 51 ||

kathaṃ punaratra pūrvapakṣo devatāśravaṇe śakyate vaktum
 | īśānāya parasvata
ālabhata iti devatāśravaṇādyāgaḥ vidhinaivādhigamyate | kathaṃ pūrvapakṣāśaṅkā ?
devatāśravaṇasya ca samādhileśaṃ kaṃ punarasau
 [manyate ?] uttaraṃ --- tyāgādṛte
nānyadvidhātuṃ śakyata iti | rāddhānte rāddhāntasthāpanamadhye bhāṣyakāreṇa
śaṅkitavyo vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ - tān paryagnikṛtānutsṛjatītyatra tyāga
eva vidheyaḥ paryagnikaraṇaviśiṣṭaḥ vidheyāntarasyāsambhavāt | yadi hi īśānāya para-
svata ālabhata ityanena yāgo vidhīyate tān paryagnikṛtānutsṛjatītyatra na kiñcidvidheyaṃ
gamyate | paryagnikaraṇasya codakaprāptatvāditi bhāṣyakāreṇoktam | tasmādīśānāyeti
śabdamuccāryālambhaḥ kartavya iti vidhīyate | tadāha---devatāśravaṇaṃ punaḥ svarūpā-
nvayi bhaviṣyati mantravadityabhiprāyaḥ |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---yāgavidhirevāyam | kutaḥ ? na hi arthānvaye
sambhavati svarūpānvayaśśakyate vaktum
 | nanu ca śabde kāryasyāsambhavāt arthe
sampratyayo yuktaḥ | tatrārthe kāryaṃ vijñāyate | iha tu śabdo’stīti tatraiva kāryaṃ
bhavituṃ yuktam |

[p. 306]

rāddhāntavādī vadati-kena voktaṃ yatra śabde sampratyayo’sti tatra na yuktamiti
kintviha śabda eva sampratyayo nāsti | na hyarthāvivakṣāyāṃ pramāṇaṃ śakyate’vagantum |
yatrotsargamṛte vidhānaṃ na sambhavati tatra tatra darśitaḥ sambhavaḥ tadantāṅgarītividhānena |
śeṣaṃ sanidarśanamuktam
 |

|| 14 || ājyasaṃsthāpratinidhiḥ syāt dravyotsargāt || 56 ||

spaṣṭe’pi tadanenotsargavidhāne śeṣasaṃsthāpanaśabdāt bhrāntirupajāyate tadantaṃ
karma na bhavatīti | tataśca pakṣadvayasambhavāt tasyā bhrānterapanayāyedamadhikaraṇamārabhyate |
na caivamapi sandeha eva yuktaḥ | kathaṃ pūrvapakṣādhyavasānamityatrāha---pūrvapakṣo hi
śeṣasaṃsthāna [śravaṇāt]
 etadvalena tadantāṅgarītividhānameva na sambhavati iti
kalpanīyā |

rāddhāntastu parisamāpanīyamevaitadityuktvā punastaccheṣābhidhānaṃ viruddhayate |
tena śeṣābhidhāne gauṇasamanvayaḥ | na ca parisamāptiḥ asamāptāvapi śakyate
vaktum | śabdābhidheyasya pakṣāntare’pyavyāghātāt | pūrvapakṣyāha---yuktaṃ saṃsthānaṃ
karmāntarapakṣe | śeṣaśabdastu kathaṃ ?
 rāddhāntyāha---nanu sādṛśyādevetyuktaṃ
bhāṣye śeṣavaccheṣa ityarthena
 | pūrvapakṣyāha---satyamuktaṃ bhāṣye tadayuktamabhi-
hitam | śeṣaśabdaśrutibhānāt
 | ekagranthenāha---utsarge vā kaḥ pakṣapātaḥ
 |
sa eva kimityanyathā varṇitaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | rāddhāntī uttaramāha---ayamasti pakṣa-
pātaḥ pūrvoktāpekṣonyaḥ śeṣaśabdaḥ
 | na punaḥ pūrvoktaṃ śeṣamapekṣate | śeṣo hyavayavaḥ
pūrvamavayavinamapekṣate na punaḥ pūrvoktaṃ śeṣam | tasmānnirapekṣatvāt utsargaḥ śrautaḥ |
tadapekṣayā tu śeṣaśabdo gauṇa iti nyāyyam | sanidarśanaṃ śeṣamuktam
 |

|| iti mahopādhyāyaśālikanāthamiśrakṛtau
pañcikāyāṃ navamasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||

[p. 307]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

daśamādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ

|| 1 || vidheḥ prakaraṇāntare’tideśātsarvakarma syāt || 1 ||

nanu ca bādhe satyūhena bhavitavyam
 | anena bhāṣyoktamūhabādhalakṣaṇayo-
rhetuhetumadbhāvamākṣipati | yadi hi ūhe sati bādhaḥ syāt tarhi ūhasya bādhaṃ prati
hetutā syāt viparītaṃ tu vidyate bādhe satyūhena bhavitavyam |

pṛcchati---ka evamāha
 - uttaraṃ---evaṃ hyupalabhyate | nivṛtte’gniśabde
sūryapadaprakṣepaḥ
 | agnipadasthāne sūryapadaprakṣepo yaḥ so’gniśabdasyānivṛttau nopapadyate |
tasmādagniśabdabādhe satyūho bhavatītyupalabhyate | samādhātā āha-upalabdhiścet
sāmni saṃskāre vā kimupalabhyate kasya nivṛttau kasya prakṣepaḥ
 ? sāmni
saṃskāre ca kasyacidvādho na dṛśyate | tena sāmasaṃskārohastāvata bādhahetuko na
bhavati | ato bibhīṣikāmātrameva bādhapūrvaka ūha iti |

ākṣeptāramāśaṅkyati---mantre kathamiti cet
 | parihāramāha---tatrāpi
prakṛtivadabhidhānaṃ kuryāt ityanena hetunā yaḥ sūryapadaprakṣepaḥ so’gniśabdasya
nivṛttiṃ nāpekṣate
 | kasmādityāha---abhidhānasampattyartho hi[sūryapada]prakṣepaḥ
nāgniśabdābhāvanimittakaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ-yathā prakṛtau mantreṇābhidhānaṃ kṛtaṃ vikṛtāvapi
tathaiva kartavyamityevamarthaṃ sūryapadaprakṣepaḥ | nahi sūryapadamantareṇa prakṛtivadabhidhānaṃ
sampadyate | prakṛtau pradhānadevatābhidhānaṃ mantreṇa kṛtam | vikṛtau sūryaḥ pradhānadevatā,
nāsau sūryapadamantareṇa śakyate bodhayituṃ ityevaṃ sūryapadaprakṣepo mantre na punaragniśabdā-
bhāvanimittakaḥ tasya prakṣepaḥ | yadi hi agniśabdābhavanimittakaḥ syāt tadā bādha-
hetuka ūho bhavet na caitadevam | tasmānna viparīto hetuhetumadbhāvaḥ | yadyevaṃ
bādho’pi tarhi nohanimittakaḥ tathābhidhāna1nirvṛttyarthaḥ 2prakṣepaḥ | tathā
saurye karmaṇyagneranabhidheyatvādagniśabdasya nivṛttiḥ na punarūhahetuko bādhaḥ

2. A prakṣepāt

[p. 308]

uttaraṃ---mā bhūdūhapūrvako bādhaḥ kā no bādhā?
 na kiñcidasmākaṃ bādhyata ityabhi-
prāyaḥ | ākṣeptā---kastarhi lakṣaṇasambandhaḥ yadi hetuhetumadbhāvo nāsti
 |
samādhātā āha---kāryeṇohaḥ prāptaḥ kāryaśūnyamabhidhānaṃ kimanuṣṭhātavyaṃ neti
bhavati sambandhaḥ
 |

asyārthaḥ---yatprakṛtau padārthānāṃ kāryaṃ vikṛtāvapi tadeva kāryamiti kāryeṇa
haviḥ prakṛtidravyeṇa saṃskāryeṇa haviḥ prakṛtibhūteṣu kṛṣṇaleṣvevāvaghātasyohaprayojanaḥ
prāptaḥ | idānīṃ kṛṣṇalasaṃskāratvenāvaghātasya prayoge niścite kartavyo’vaghātaḥ kārya-
haviśśūnyo bhavati | tuṣavimocanakāryābhāvat ataścintā bhavati kiṃ kāryaśūnyasyā-
vaghātasyānuṣṭhānaṃ kriyatāṃ neti | tatra dvārābhāvat ananuṣṭhānamavaghātasyeti bādho
bhavati | eṣa ūhalakṣaṇabādhalakṣaṇayoḥ sambandhaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati - dvāralopanibandhano
bādhaḥ tena sākṣādūhasya hetubhāvo nāsti, kintu kṛṣṇalasāṃkaratvenāvaghātasya prayogaḥ
kartavya ityūhe niścite sati kṛṣṇale tuṣakaṇavimocanakāryābhāvādavaghātasya dvāralopa-
niścayaḥ | tataśca bādha iti bādhahetubhūtadvāralopaniścayahetutvādastyūhasya bādhaṃ prati
hetubhāva iti | tathā ca svayameva sūtrakāraḥ āgrayaṇe prākṛtānvāhāryasthānapatitayo-
rvāsovatsayoḥ anvāhāryadharmohaṃ darśayitvā dvārābhāvena pākasya bādhaṃ darśayiṣyati |
bhavanti cātra ślokāḥ
 |

vikṛtyapūrvatādarthye kratuśeṣatvasīmani |

prākṛtānāṃ padārthānāmatideśātmake sthite ||

prākṛtasthānapatitavastvantaracikīrṣayā |

prayoge vidite dvāralopaḥ śakyaviściyaḥ ||

dvāralopanimittaśca bādho’to hetusiddhaye |

ūhāpekṣeti sambandho vyakta evohabādhoḥ ||

bādho nāmeti bhāṣyamarthato’nubhāṣya ākṣipati---bādho nāmetyuktvā yanni-
ścitaṃ kāryavijñānaṃ tasya kāraṇāntareṇa
 mithyābhāvo bādha iti bādhasvarūpamupa-
varṇitam | taccedaṃ vipratiṣiddhaṃ pramāṇavido manyante | na hi niścitajñānaṃ mithyā
bhavati | mithyābhāvo’pi jñānasyārthaśūnyatvam | na ca yadvijñānaṃ niścitaṃ tadartha-
śūnyaṃ bhavati | vijñānasya svamahimnaivārthopasthāpakatvāt | viśeṣeṇa tu mīmāṃsakānāṃ

[p. 309]

mate na hi vijñānaṃ mithyā bhavati yeṣāmagrahaṇanibandhanameva mithyātvam | mīmāṃsakā
hi bhedāgrahaṇarūpatayā vijñānasya mithyābhāvaṃ vadanti, na jñānarūpatayā | vivekā-
grahaṇāddhi smṛtijñānaṃ pratyakṣavyavahārapravartakatayā puruṣaṃ vyavahāre visaṃvādamāpādayat
vijñānaṃ mithyābhūtamityucyate | tenāgrahaṇanibandhanameva mithyātvam |

yeṣāmapi mīmāṃsakaikadeśināṃ khyātireva viparītā jñānamevāyathābhūtaṃ te’pi naiva
niścitatvaṃ viparītatve heturiti bruvate | teṣāmapi śuktikāsvarūpaniścayo na rajatatva-
grahaṇe hetuḥ kintu śuktikāsvarūpāniścaya eva | tadetadāha---teṣāmapi ajñāna-
vāsanā viparītatve hetuḥ
 | ajñānena vāsanā saṃskāro’jñānavāsanā | śuktikā-
svarūpājñānena saṃskṛtasya rajatavijñānaṃ bhavati |

yuktaṃ 1cājñānasyaiva mithyātvaṃ, tasyaiva jñānenāpanīyamānasya bādhyatvāt | jñānaṃ
tu na jñānenāpanetuṃ 2śakyate | tena bādhakābhāvānna jñānaṃ mithyeti yuktam | tadāha
---jñānena cā’jñānaṃ vivartyata iti ajñānasyaiva yukto mithyābhāvaḥ | ato
niścitaṃ jñānaṃ mithyeti vipratiṣiddham
 | kiñca prāmāṇye ca suparitiścitatvameva
hetutvena pramāṇalakṣaṇe svayamuditam | “jāgrato buddhiḥ supariniścitā kathaṃ viparya-
siṣyatī”
ti | tena yadvijñānaṃ niścitaṃ tanmithyetyabhidhīyamāne svavacana
-

 | yadyupakārasya padārthasaṃhateśca

tanniścitam | tena niścitatvaṃ prasiddham | aniścitatvaṃ prasādhya mithyātvamidānīmāha
---yasya tu tathā yena prakāreṇa prākṛtena dvāraviśeṣeṇetthaṃbhāvaḥ karaṇopakāro
na sambhavati taddvārābhāvenākartavyatayā mithyetyavagamyate
 | kathaṃ dvārābhāve

tādarthyataḥ
 | caśabdo yasmādarthe | yasmāt prakṛtāvapi dvāranibandhana eva karaṇopa-
kāraḥ na tu tādarthyamātranibandhanaḥ prakṛtivacca vikṛtāvapyupakāraḥ tasmāt dvārabādhe
karaṇopakārāsambhavaḥ | karaṇopakārāsambhave cākiñcitkaratvādananuṣṭhānam | yadi
dvārabādhena karaṇopakāraṃ na janayati kathaṃ tarhi vikṛtau tasya niścaya ityāha---
tādarthye(nā)cāyaṃ niścayaḥ
 uktaḥ yāvaduktaṃ tāvati śabdasya prāmāṇyāt
 |

asyārthaḥ---yadyapi 2dvārabādhena karaṇopakāraṃ na janayati tathāpi vikṛtyapūrvaṃ
prati tasyaidamarthyaṃ śeṣatvaṃ niścitam | tena śābdo niścayo’sti | aidamarthyamātre
śabdasya prāmāṇyāt | dvāraṃ punaḥ pramāṇāntarasiddhameva tuṣakaṇavimocanādikamavaghātā-
dīnām | śābda ca pramāṇamaṅgīkṛtyāyaṃ niścaya uktaḥ | yato dvāraṃ pramāṇāntaravedyamata

ntarabaśenetyarthaḥ
 | ayamāśayaḥ---śābdena pramāṇāntareṇa tādarthyaṃ niścitaṃ pramāṇā-
ntareṇadvārābhāvena karaṇopakārajanakatayā mithyeti parikalpyate | anupakārakasyā-

pramāṇāntaravaśena śābdasya tādarthyasya mithyātvaṃ? śābdajñānena gṛhītaṃ tādarthyaṃ satya-
bhūtameva na tasya mithyātvaṃ śakyate vaktum | yadyapi tadānīmupakāraṃ dvārābhāvena sa

2. A dvārābhāvena

padārtho na janayati, tathāpi prākṛtatvādupakārasya bhavatyevāsau tajjanyaḥ, prakṛtau tena
tasya janyamānatvāt | tenā(ā)tajjanyopakārasambandho vikṛtyapūrvasya | tasmātprākṛtasya
padārthasya luptadvārasyāpi avihatameva tādarthyam | kathaṃ punaḥ pramāṇantaravaśena
śābdasya mithyātvaṃ ?
 uttaraṃ-yata eva mithyātvaṃ nāsti ata eva kāraṇā-
ntareṇa mithyātvaṃ na svata ityuktam |
 kāraṇāntareṇa dvāra(ā)niścayāt |
tadabhāve tadānīṃ karaṇopakārājanakatvāt tādarthe satyapi atādarthyasādhāraṇīṃ karaṇopa-
kārājanakatāṃ pratiyato’nyatra dṛṣṭyā tādarthyasmaraṇamupajāyate tena luptadvāraṃ atadartha-
miti manyate | tena pramāṇāntareṇa dvārasya grāhyatvāt tadabhāve’tādarthyajñānamiva bhavati
na punaḥ śābdenaiva jñānenātādarthyamavagamyate tadeva spaṣṭayati---paropādhikaṃ
mithyātvaṃ svatastu niścayaḥkena vāryate draṣṭṛtvamivātmanaḥ
 | dṛṣṭāntaṃ vivṛṇoti
---nopadhānādṛte na paśyatīti na draṣṭṛtvaṃ hīyate
 | upadhānādindriyādi-
vyāpārādvinā suṣuptyādau viṣayaṃ na paśyatītyetāvatā ātmano draṣṭṛtvaṃ dṛk‌śaktiyogitvaṃ
na hīyate | astyeva dṛk‌śaktiḥ svābhāvikī apaśyato’pi | tathā kāryamupakurva-
to’pi upakārajananaśaktirastyeva luptadvārasyāpi prakṛtau darśanāt jāgraddaśāyāmivātmano
viṣayadarśanāt | tataśca tāvanmātrameva bādha iti |

yadyevaṃ tadeva vaktavyaṃ dvārābhāvādbādha iti tadāha---atha kasmādevameva noktaṃ
dvārābhāvo bādha iti
 | uttaraṃ---naivaṃ, pramāṇasya bādha ityuktaṃ bhavati | nanu
ca naiva pramāṇasya svato bādha ityuktam
 | tena pramāṇasya bādhābhāvāt pramāṇabādho
vaktumeva na yuktaḥ | tena tasyānabhidhānaṃ na doṣa ityarthaḥ | pariharati---upā-
dhito’pi bādho bādha eva sugandhiteva kāminīnām
 | asyārthaḥ---dvārā-
bhāvāvagame tādarthye satyapi yadidamatādarthyasmaraṇamupādhiḥ tato’pi yobādhaḥ so’pi bādha
eva | yathā kāminīnāṃ sugandhidravyāṅgarāgavatīnāṃ śarīrasamavāyigandhāgrahaṇe smaryate
yathā aṅgarāgagatagandhagrahaṇe ca sugandhiteva bhavati | yadi sugandhiteva kāminīnāṃ
pramāṇasya bādhaḥ upacārastarhi ? upacārastu na pāramārthika iti tattve jñānena2
pāramārthiko bādho na bhavati | anyadīyamatādarthyaṃ smaryate yathā’ṅgarāgagataṃ saugandhya-
manubhūyate | tadeva tu saṃyuktasamavāyāt kāminīṣviva bhāsate |

1. R. ukto
2. A. tatve jñāne

[p. 312]

pariharati---na kārye upakāraḥ śakyate vaktum
 | bādhakārye upacāro
nāsti | bādhakāryaṃ pāramārthikamananuṣṭhānātmakam | tatparamārthato bhavatyeva dvārābhāve’nu-
pakārakasyānanuṣṭhānāt | ato’pāramārthikepi bādhe kāryaṃ pāramārthikameva | yathā
saurabhe kāminīnāmapāramārthikepi saurabhakāryaṃ kāmukaprītirūpaṃ pāramārthikameva |

1nanvevamapi bādhakāryameva pāramārthikaṃ na bādha iti pramāṇasya mithyābhāvo bādha
iti na vaktavyamityatrāha---kāryārthaṃ ca svarūpavarṇanam
 | yadidaṃ bādhasvarūpavarṇanaṃ tat
bādhakāryānanuṣṭhānasiddhayarthaṃ tadbādhakāryaṃ pramāṇasya mithyābhāve darśite satīti tadarthamau-
pādhiko’pi pramāṇasya bādha uktaḥ | tasmātsuṣṭhūcyate niścitaṃ mithyeti ca
kārye vibhāgādarśanāt

yaḥ punarayaṃ dvitīyaḥ parihāraḥ na tadeva śāstramarthadvayāya prabhavati ārambhaṇīyā-
kāryamavagamayituṃ tatra cātideṣṭumiti | aṅgatvābhyupagame na saḥ; aṅgatve hyārambhaṇīyā-
śāstramapyaṅgatvakalpanāyāṃ vyāpriyate | nanu cāyamaṅgatvābhyupagamaḥ prayājādera-
pyatideśaṃ vārayati aparipūrṇadharmakasyopakārakasya anatideśāt
 | pariharati
---na, abhāvapradarśanamātraparatvāt
 | ārambhaṇīyāyāmārambhaṇīyā nāsti ityetā-
vanmātraparametat | na cābhyupagame doṣāntarāpattiḥ | tasmātsūktam |

|| 5 || karma ca dravyasaṃyogārthamarthābhāvānnivarteta tādarthyaṃ
śrutisaṃyogāt || 9 ||

yūpotpattyarthatā asyā yūpāhuterna vidyata iti pūrvapakṣavādinaḥ
 khale-
vālyāmapi yūpāhutiḥ kartavyeti vadato’bhiprāyaḥ | kā yuktiḥ yūpānutpattyarthatve ?

uttaraṃ---takṣaṇādīnāmivotpattau na kaścidvayāpāra upalabhyate | tasmāda-
dṛṣṭārthatayā parivyāṇādivat khalevālyāmapi yūpāhutiḥ kartavyā
 | khadirā-
dayo hi yūpībhavanti | tatra ca yat khadirodiṣu vyāpriyate karma tasyaiva khadira-
saṃskāreṇa yūpotpattyarthatvaṃ yuktam | na ca yūpāhuteḥ khadirādau kaścidvayāpāra
upalabhyate | tasmādadṛṣṭārthatayā parivyāṇādivat khalevālyāmapi yūpāhutiḥ kartavyā |

rāddhāntastu satyaṃ na kaścit dṛṣṭa upakāraḥ utpattāvupalabhyate
takṣaṇādiśabdena tūtpattyarthatāmavagacchāmaḥ yūpamaccheṣyatā hotavyamiti
 |

[p. 326]

kathaṃ ? acchośabdo hi āptumityasminnarthe prasiddha ityabhiyuktā upadiśanti | bhavatvā-
ptyarthatā | tathāpi kathamutpattyarthatetyāha---utpattiścāptiryūpasya
 | utpādayanneva
yūpamāpnoti yataḥ nānyena prakāreṇa | yato yūpotpattyarthatā’to na khalevālyāṃ kartavyā |
ayūpe hi paśurbadhyata iti siddham | tena yūpe naiva prayojanamiti yūpārthā satī
na kriyate |

|| 6 || sthāṇau tu deśamātratvādanivṛttiḥ pratīyeta || 10 ||

nanu yūpasyotpādyatvena sarvayūpasambandhaṃ tadutpattyarthamityuktam
 | yasya
yūpamaccheṣyateti yūpasambandho’vagamyate tadyūpotpattyarthaṃ yūpasyotpādyatvādityuktam | tena
sthāṇvāhutiryūpotpattyarthā | samādhatte---satyamuktam tadevedamākṣipyate | kathamā-
kṣepaḥ ? sthāṇumātrasambandhāt sthāṇvāhuteḥ na yūpīyasthāṇusambandho’va-
gamyate
 | kiṃ tarhi ? sthāṇumātrasambandhaḥ tataśca na yūpasambandho’vagamyate | asaṃyuktaśca
prakaraṇena gṛhyata ityārādupakārakatvam | idamatrākūtaṃ---saṃskāravaśena khadirādayo
yūpībhavanti | saṃskāraścāyaṃ laukikaḥ padārthaḥ saṃskārasambandhini vā kriyamāṇena
karmaṇā janyate | na ca sthāṇvāhutiryūpe yūpasambandhini vā kriyata iti asaṃskārikā |
acchaśabdaśca kathañcidvayākhyeya iti | evaṃ ca yūpāhutirapyārādupakārikā |
sāpi hi na yūpasambandhinī | atha yūpasamīpe kriyamāṇā yūpārthā | tatrāha---kiñca
avaśyamevamabhyupagamanīyam yūpāhutiryūpasamīpe kriyamāṇatayā yūpasamba-
ndhinī na bhavatīti | anyathā yūpāhuterekādaśinyāṃ tantratā na syāt
 |
yadi yūpasamīpe kriyamāṇā yūpasambandhinī tadā samīpadeśabhedāt tantratā yūpāhuterna
syāt |

nanu ca yūpāhutiryūpārthetyuktam | satyamuktaṃ tadevāyuktaṃ manyate
 |
tantratā vā hātavyā | yūpotpattyarthatā vā ityabhiprāyaḥ |

rāddhāntastu---accheṣyatīti vacanāt yūpārthaivāvagamyate sthāṇvāhuti-
riti ca yūpasambandhinyeva sthāṇau pratīyate
 | na hi prakṛtaviśeṣaṇahāne pramāṇa-
masti | sthāṇuśabdo’yaṃ yaugiko vyaktivacanaḥ | vyaktivacanāśca sannihitāṃ vyaktiṃ
nātikrāmantīti yūpasambandhyeva sthāṇuḥ | nanu ca yadyapyasau(dāya) [na yūpa] sambandhī
tathāpi tato’pi yuktaḥ kathaṃ tatsambandhena yūpasaṃskāraḥ ityatrāha-taruviyuktasaṃskāre’pi

[p. 327]

tadvataḥ saṃskāramicchanti laukikāḥ
 yathā devadattaśiraso’vatāritāyāḥ srajaḥ
śucideśanidhānena saṃskṛtāyāḥ devadattasya saṃskāraḥ | laukikaścāyaṃ saṃskārapadārtho
’bhyupagato’vahantyādivat | śabdāttu niyamamātram | tena yūpasambandhisthāṇusambandhāt
sthāṇvāhuteryūpotpattyarthatā | yūpāhutestu antikadeśasyāvivakṣā | āpekṣikatvā-
dantikadeśasyetyuktam | tenaikatra kriyamāṇā yūpāhutiḥ kasalayūpāntikeṣu kṛtā
bhavati āpekṣikatvādantikedaśasya | tasmānna doṣastantratāyāḥ | yūpāntikeṣvapi
kriyamāṇāyāḥ yūpāhuteḥ tantratopapatteḥ tantratā yūpārthatāṃ na dūṣayatītyarthaḥ |
darśanamuktam

|| 7 || prayāje ca tannāyatvāt || 14 ||

svāhākāraṃ yajatīti dvitīyānirdeśāt devatābhidhānaṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |

sannipatyopakārakatā karmaṇaḥ syāt tadā tallobhenāvyatiriktārthatā yajatiśabdāt

 | dvivā hi

|| 8 || tathā’’jyabhāgāgnirapīti cet || 16 ||

pūrvavadatrāpi dṛṣṭārthataiva devatāsaṃskāratvenājyabhāgasya nyāyyā ityāśaṅkya, na
dṛṣṭārthatā devatāyā apratyabhidhānāditi rāddhāntitam | yadi hi āgneye yā ca
devatāgniḥ saivājyabhāge’pīti pratyabhijñānaṃ syāt tadā syāt saṃskāratvam | na
cātra pratyabhijñānam | darśitā cāpratyabhijñā āvāhananigade | ‘agnimāvaha’

somamāvaha’ ‘agnimāvaha’
 iti paṭhitam | tatra yadyāgneyasya agnirājyabhāge’pi
devateti syāt tadā prathamena agnimityanena āgneyasya pradhānasya devatā prakāśyā |
somamagnimityanena upāṃśuyājadevatā agnīṣomau prakāśyau tatra ca kramaviparyayaḥ |
devatāntarīye tvājyabhāgīye agniṃ somaṃ ityājyabhāgayoḥ devate prakāśye tadanantaraṃ
ca pradhānadevatā agniriti yuktam | kathaṃ tarhi devatāyā guṇabhāvaḥ ? uttaraṃ---
bhūtatvāt devatāyā bhavyatvācca karmaṇa iti nyāyyatā guṇabhāvasyoktā

nyāyyāṃ guṇabhāvo na vācanika ityarthaḥ |

|| 9 || paśāvapīti cet || 19 ||

atra pūrvapakṣavādī latāsome praṇīyamānāgnyabhidhānaṃ manvānaḥ artha-
vādādapratyabhijñāṃ manyate
 | āsomaṃ vahantītyarthavādāt latāsomapraṇīyamānāgnya-
bhidhānaṃ agnisomaśabdayoḥ paśau manyamānaḥ puroḍāśe ca niyoga[ta]stadvacanatā
nāstīti pratyabhijñāyā abhāvaṃ manyamānaḥ pūrvapakṣavādī ārādupakārakaḥ paśupuroḍāśa
iti vadati | saṃskārakarmapakṣe utkarṣāśaṅkā’tra sautrāmaṇyāṃ vipratipannadevatāttvāt
paśūnāṃ puroḍāśānāṃ ca | yatra sampratipannā devatā tatra puroḍāśānāmutkarṣaḥ syāt |
ārādupakārakatve tvanutkarṣaḥ guṇābhivātaṃ ca tulyam | yathā paśau guṇatvenābhi-

[p. 330]

dhānaṃ tathā puroḍāśe’pīti | kathaṃ tasyāḥ prādhānya mukhyā ca dharmaprāptiḥ anyathā
vaktavyeti guṇabhāve’gnīṣomayoḥ mukhyā ca dharmaprāptiḥ | guṇabhūtayoreva hyagnīṣomayoḥ
prakṛtau dharma upadiṣṭaḥ sa guṇabhāve mukhyo bhavati | prādhānye tu sati guṇakāryakaratvena
dharmaprāptirvaktavyā | sā kleśasādhyā parihṛtā bhavati | puroḍāśepi devatāyā 1guṇa-
bhāvāṅgīkaraṇena |

tadidamuktaṃ bhāṣye---“parājite’pi hetuḥ kenacidviśeṣeṇānuprāṇyata”
 iti |
so’yaṃ viśeṣaḥ |

 | viśeṣe tu pūrvapakṣānuprāṇanahetau tūktaḥ

|| 10 || carurhavirvikāraḥ syādijyāsaṃyogāt || 34 ||

sthālyā mukhyārthatāṃ manvāno’nadanīyena dravyeṇa yāgavirodhāt 
kapālavikāraścaruriti manyate
 | asyārthaḥ-sthā/?/lyāḥ pūrvasiddhatvāt tadvacana eva
1. A aṃśe nira karaṇe guṇabhā/?/vaḥ syāt
2. R arthavādābhidhānatve hetu
3. prakaraṇena

[p. 331]

caruśabdaḥ tadyogādodane vartate | tatrāpi sthālī na haviṣṭvena vidheyā anadanīya-
tvāt | anadanīyena ca yāge kriyamāṇe śiṣṭācāravirodhāt | pākādhāratvena
kapālasthāne sthālī vidheyā | darśana ca darśitamasya pakṣasya | uparitanapakṣāntaramāha
---taddhitārthānupapatteḥ havirvikāra iti sthitam
 | saurya iti taddhitena
devatā haviṣā sambadhyate na pākādhāreṇeti haviṣṭvenaiva sthālyā vidhānam |

rāddhāntamāha---odane’pi ca mukhyārthe sambhavāt tadyogācca sthālyāṃ
prayogopapatteḥ adanīyaśrutivirodhāśca | kvacit śrapayatīti carau śravaṇāt
odanena havirvikriyata iti sthitam
 | asyārthaḥ - yadi sthālīvacanaḥ caruśabdaḥ
syāt tadā haviṣṭvaṃ tasyā vācyam | tataścānadanīyena yāgaḥ kriyeta iti śiṣṭā-
cāro bādhanīyaḥ | odanavacanatve tu nāsti śiṣṭācāravirodhaḥ iti tadvacana eva
caruśabda iti nirṇīyate | sambhavati tadvacanatā | tadyogena ca sthālyāmapi prayogo
ghaṭate | ādhāravacanatā śiṣṭācāravirodhena tyajyate | kvaciccaroḥ śrapaṇaṃ vidhīyata
ityodana eva haviriti sthitam | śeṣamuktam

|| 11 || sa kapāle prakṛtyā syādanyasya cāśrutitvāt || 45 ||

atra yāvadviruddhayate tāvannivartate | na ca kapālasya kaścidvirodhaḥ |
kintu saṅkhyāyāḥ | tasmādaṣṭasaṅkhyāṃ parityajya kapāla eva caruḥ śrapaṇīya
iti pūrva pakṣaḥ
 |

na kapālaḥ prakṛtivadupakartuṃ śaknoti | taddhi pākahetuḥ, pākādhāra-
tvena prakṛtāvupakārakam
 | odanaśca udakagatenoṣmaṇā pacyata ityarthalopādaprāptiḥ
kapālasyāpi saṅkhyāyā iva | idānīṃ pātraniyame prāpte yāsu sthālīṣu somāḥ syuḥ
te carava iti liṅgadarśanāt caruśabdābhidheyāyāśca pakteḥ sthā/?/lyāmevābhinirvṛtteḥ
saukaryāt sthālyāmeva paktavya iti sthitam | etacca suhṛdbhāvakathanamātram, na tu
sthālī viniyuktetyevaṃparaṃ tat | śeṣamuktam
 |

kiṃ bhavati prayojanam ? yadi kapālavikāraścaruḥ yadi vodanena havirvi-
kriyata iti | atra sthālyāḥ punarhavirdhikāre ko viśeṣa iti na cintitam | sujñāna-
tvādavaghātādibādhasya |

|| evaṃ vā ||

atra punaradhikaraṇe prayojanatve peṣaṇādilopaṃ vyākhyāya punaradhikaraṇāntara-
karaṇāt kvacit kiñcidapaneyamastītyabhiprāyaḥ |

nanu ca mandaśaṅkā nāpaneyetyatrāha- mandaviṣeṇāpītī darśitam
 | yo
mandaviṣeṇa vṛścikena dṛṣṭo na mumūrṣati saḥ nācikitsyo yathā tathā mandāśaṅkāvyā-
kulitaḥ śiṣyo’pīti darśitaṃ bhāṣye | tatra bahuṣu anuṣṭhānasambhavaṃ anarthalopaṃ ca
manvānena pūrvapakṣitam | kvacidavirodhenānivṛttiḥ kvacidārthasyaiva codanārthatāṃ manyate |
saṃvapa(caya)nādīnāṃ śakyatayā’nuṣṭhānasambhavamātreṇopakārakatvam | asti cātrānuṣṭhāna-
sambhavalakṣaṇo’narthalopa ityabhimānavān peṣaṇādīnāṃ dṛṣṭārthānāṃ avirodhāt piṣṭakamaya-
syāpi caroḥ darśanāt vyuddharaṇasya cārthasya vidheyatvabuddhayā pūrvapakṣitam |

rāddhāntastu-na śakyatayā’nuṣṭhānam nāpyavirodhāt
 | nāpyartho’ti-
diśyate kiṃ tarhi ? kāryato’nuṣṭhānaṃ dharmāṇāṃ tadabhāvācca nivṛttiḥ | vacanācca
kāryakalpanā | tenārthasya kārye karaṇopakāre’nanupraveśānnātideśa iti sūktam |

|| iti śrīśālikamiśraviracitāyāṃ ṛjuvimalāyāṃ
pañcikāyāṃ daśamasya prathamaḥpādaḥ ||

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

daśamādhyāyasya dvitīyāḥ pādaḥ

|| 1 || kṛṣṇaleṣvarthalopādapākaḥ syāt || 11 ||

kathaṃ punaratra pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ ? pūrvapakṣe’pi hi vacanamapi vidyata eva
 |
samādhatte---na vacanaśatenāpi aśakanīyo’rthaḥ śakyate vidhātum
 | kathama-
śakyatetyatrāha---pacerhi viklṛttiḥ pradhāno’rthaḥ | na cāsau kṛṣṇaleṣu vidyata
iti kimatra vacanaṃ kariṣyati
 | asyārthaḥ - mahābhāṣyakāreṇoktaṃ - pacerhi
viklṛttiḥ pradhāno’rtha iti | pradhāno mukhya ityarthaḥ | dṛśyate ca loke’pi pacyante
taṇḍulā iti viklṛttivacanatā pakteḥ | devadattaḥ pacatītyādāvapi taṇḍulasama-
vāyinyāmeva viklṛtau devadattaḥ kartā, tadadhīnatvāttasyāḥ | viklṛttiśca gandharūpa-
sparśarasādiviśeṣātmikā | tena puroḍāśādīnāmapi pākassiddho bhavati | na ca
taijasānāṃ kṛṣṇalādīnāṃ rūpādiviśeṣestīti pākasyāśakyatvānna vacanaśatenāpi
vidhānamupapadyate | ekagranthenāha---atha tu santāpajananamātraṃ pākaḥ tathāpi
codakenaiva prāpnotīti vacanena kiṃ ?
 viklṛttiphalako yo vyāpāraḥ sa eva yadi
pacerarthaḥ tataḥ prakṛtāvapi sa eva vidhīyate tataścodakenaiva kṛṣṇaleṣvapi prāpnotīti
vidhānamanarthakam | doṣāntaraṃ cāha---avaghātādi ca durnivāraṃ tadā bhavati
 |
yadā vyāpāravacanatā dhātūnāṃ tadā hanterapi udyamananipatanamātrārthatvāt kartavyataiva
pratīyate |

athocyate tuṣakaṇavimocanātmakaphalābhāvāt akaraṇaṃ pāke tulyaṃ
upadiṣṭepi pāke tulyaṃ phalābhāvādakaraṇam
 | tasmādakartavyatāmeva manyamānasya
pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ | vacanamidānīṃ kimarthaṃ yadi pākasyākartavyatā
 | uttaraṃ---
anarthakaṃ ca|
 | avagamyamānārthatayā hi vacanamarthavat, na vacanenārthavatā bhavitavyamiti
pramāṇamasti |

rāddhāntastu satyamāha bhavān phalābhāvāt na kāryataḥ prāptirastīti
 |
vacanaṃ punaḥ na nivārayituṃ śakyate | tena vācanikaḥ pāko bhaviṣyati |

[p. 334]

nanu vacanaśatenāpyaśakanīyo’rtho na vidhātuṃ śakyata ityuktam
 |
pariharati---etadapi satyam aśakanīyo’rthoṃ na vidhātuṃ śakyata iti
 | punaḥ
pūrvapakṣī pṛcchati---kathaṃ tarhi pākasya kartavyatā aśakanīyatayā vidhānā-
sambhavāt
 | siddhāntītvāha---śabdārthasya sambhavāt
 | yo’sau viklṛttiphalako
vyāpāraḥ sa eva pacinocyate pūrvabhāvitvāt | tatkāryatayā lakṣaṇayā viklṛttau paceḥ
prayogaḥ pacyante taṇḍalā iti | evaṃ ca devadattaḥ pacatīti svasamavāyinyeva
vyāpāre devadattasya kartṛtvam | yaścāsau gandhādiparāvṛttihetubhūto’gnyavayavasaṃyoga-
phalako vyāpāraḥ sa eva yadi śabdārthaḥ kṛṣṇaleṣvapi sambhavati | teṣāmapyavayava-
saṃyogopalabdheḥ | uṣṇasparśānumeyo hi teṣvagnyavayavasañcāraḥ | tena śabdārtha-
sambhavāt pākastatra vidhātuṃ śakyate |

nanu vihitasyāpi phalābhāvādananuṣṭhānaṃ kāryaprayuktatvāddharmāṇāmityatrāha---
vihite śabdārthe dṛṣṭamadṛṣṭaṃ vā prayojanaṃ bhavati | yasya dṛṣṭaṃ prayojanamasti
tasya dṛṣṭameva prayojanaṃ
 yathā prakṛtau pākasyāvaghātādīnāṃ ca | yasya tu dṛṣṭaṃ
nāsti tasyādṛṣṭaṃ kalpyate grāhakasambandhanirvāhāya | dvāreṇa vinā tadanupapatteḥ |
yathā prakṛtāveva prokṣaṇasya | tatrāpi dvitīyāśravaṇāt taddravyagataṃ taditi kalpyate |
tenātrāpi kṛṣṇaleṣu ko’pi pākajanyaḥ saṃskāraḥ prayojanamityadhyavasīyate na punaḥ
dṛṣṭaṃ prayojanaṃ nāstīti vidhinivāraṇam | sambhavati ca śabdārthaḥ |

nanu vidhānaṃ satyadṛṣṭaṃ prayojanaṃ vidhānameva prāk prayojanāvagamānnopapadyate
prayojanaparyante vyāpāre puruṣasya niyogādityāśaṅkyāha---yāvat puruṣavyāpāra
eva hi vidherviṣayaḥ na phalopapattirapi
 | svavyāpāre hi puruṣo niyujyate tena
tāvanmātre vihite adṛṣṭaphalakalpanā yuktaiva | viklṛttiśca phalaṃ paceḥ pacyarthasya na
punaḥ sa eva pacyartha iti pūrvamuktam | kathaṃ tarhi mahābhāṣyakārasya vacanamityatrāha-
phalenāpi ca dhātvarthakathanaṃ dhātupāṭhe1 dṛṣṭam yathā gaḍivadanaikadeśe iti
 |
kriyāvacano hi dhāturiti gaḍirapi kriyāvacana eva kriyāmeva vadanaikadeśārambhalakṣaṇāṃ
vadati | tatphalena dhātvarthakathanam vadanaikadeśaphalake vyāpāre gaḍirityarthaḥ | evaṃ kaḥ
paceḥ pradhānārtha iti phalata eva vyākhyānaṃ vaktavyam viklṛttiphalo vyāpāra eva paceḥ
pradhāno’rtha ityarthaḥ |

pāka iti |

prākṛtakāryābhāvādavaghātādīnāṃ nivṛttiḥ
 | asyārthaḥ vyāpāramātre’pi dhātvarthe
tasya prakṛtau dṛṣṭameva dvāraṃ tuṣakaṇavimocanādi tatkāryasyaiva vikṛtāvapi prāptiriti
prākṛtakāryābhāvat avaghātādīnāṃ nivṛttiḥ | tathā pākasyāpi prākṛtakāryapuroḍāśa-
gandhādiparāvṛtterabhāvānnivṛttiḥ | paścāttu vācanikaḥ pāko’paro vidhīyate tasyādṛṣṭaṃ
dvāraṃ kalpyate | taduktaṃ yathopapadyate tathopariṣṭādvakṣyāma iti tadidamuktam |

|| 2 || upastaraṇābhidhāraṇayosmṛtārthatvādakarma syāt || 3 ||

nanu ca vyakta eva rāddhāntaḥ kimarthamidamadhikaraṇamityātrāha-atra nivṛtti-
hetavaḥ parīkṣyante | kimupastaraṇābhidhāraṇayoramṛtārthatvaṃ kṛṣṇaleṣu nivṛttau
hetuḥ svādimno’sambhavāt uta caturavattasyāptyā iti caturavattāptivacana
nivṛttihetuḥ athavā saṃsargābhāvo nivṛttau heturiti rāddhāntābhāsanivṛttyartha-
midamadhikaraṇamityarthaḥ
 |

keciddhi manyante svādimaprayojanamupastaraṇamabhidhāraṇaṃ ca prakṛtau, kṛṣṇaleṣu
svādimno’bhāvānnivartata iti |

tathānye manyante---caturavattaprayojanamupastaraṇamabhidhāraṇaṃ ca taccaturavatta
kṛṣṇalacatuṣkāvadānenaiva sampannamiti kāryābhāvannivartate |

tathānyanmatam - upastaraṇābhidhāraṇe sruci havissaṃsarganivṛttyarthe | na ca kṛṣṇa-
lānāṃ sruci saṃsargo’sti tena tannivṛttyarthaṃ nopastaraṇamabhidhāraṇaṃ ca kāryamiti |
tatraikamamṛtāhutisampādanaṃ nivṛttikāraṇamarthavādatvena parihṛtam | na hi svādimā
paramārthataḥ prayojanaṃ bhavitumarhati | anupayogitvāt | asvādunāpi yāgasiddheḥ |

 sambandhaḥ | kena hetunetyatrāha---

[p. 336]

pradhānāvadeyaviṣayatvāccatussaṅkhyāyāḥ
 | catvāri kṛṣṇalānyavadyatīti yeyaṃ
catussaṅkhyā sā yatpradhānaṃ haviravedayaṃ tadviṣayā nāvadānaviṣayā tena caturavattāvāptirekena
avadānena nāsti |

nanu yadyapyekamavadānaṃ tathāpi tena caturāvattāvāptirarthavādādavagamyata ityatrāha
---āptyarthavādasya catvāri kṛṣṇalānyavadyati tadekamavadānaṃ ityanena

nirākaraṇamiti sambandhaḥ | tathā bhinnaviṣayāvagamāt saṅkhyāyā nirākaraṇam |
catussaṅkhyā bhinnaviṣayā’vagamyate kṛṣṇalaviṣayā, nāvadānaviṣayā | tena 1stutimātra-
metat | kṛṣṇalacatuṣkāvadānābhyāsaśrutivirodhācca nirākaraṇam | catvāri
catvāri kṛṣṇalānyavadyati iti | avadānāntare sati abhyāsa upapadyate | yadi ca
kṛṣṇalacatuṣkāvadānenaiva caturavattasampattiḥ tadā’vāntaraprasaṅga eva nāsti ityabhyāsa-
śrutirviruddhayeta |

nanu sviṣṭakṛdavadānāpekṣayā’bhyāsaśruterupapattirityatrāha-pradhānasya cābhyāsaḥ
prakaraṇāt iti darśitam
 | ato nirākaraṇaṃ pradhānāvadānaviṣaya evāyamabhyāsaḥ na
sviṣṭakṛdavadānāpekṣaḥ pradānasya prakaraṇaṃ yata iti bhāṣye darśitam | ato heto-
rāptyarthatvādasya nirākaraṇam | tasmāt kṛṣṇalānāṃ strucyasaṃsarga evopastaraṇābhidhāraṇa-
yorbādhe(rabhāve)hetuḥ | tayossaṃsarganivṛttiprayojanatvāt | kasmāttadeva prayojana-
mityatrāha---na ca dṛṣṭe saṃsarganirākaraṇe prayojane sambhavati prakṛtāvapi
kāryāntarakalpanā śakyate vaktumiti
 yuktamuktaṃ asaṃsarga eva bādhaheturiti |

nanu yadyupastaraṇābhidhāraṇaṃ nāsti tadā pradhānahaviṣṣu2 dvayavadāne sati
caturavattatvaṃ kathaṃ setsyatītyatrāha---yatpunaścaturavattaṃ juhotīti tadāptyā
sampāditamityadoṣaḥ
 | catvāri kṛṣṇalānyavadyati caturavattasyāptyai ityanenaiva

|| 3 || bhakṣāṇāṃ tu prītyarthatvādakarma syāt || 13 ||

nanu bhakṣaṇaṃ dravyapratipattyarthaṃ prakṛtau | keyaṃ prītyarthaṃtā bādhahetutve-
nāśaṅkyate
 | samādhatte---satyaṃ dravyapratipattyarthaṃ kintu bhakṣaṇaṃ sat prati-

1. A stutiprasaṅga eva nāsti | 2. haviṣo R

[p. 337]

pattyartham
 | svarūpāparityāgena pratipattyarthatetyarthaḥ | bhakṣaṇaṃ ca prītyavinābhūtaṃ,
abhyavahāryaviṣayaṃ hi talloke dṛśyate | yadeva pārthivaṃ rasavad dravyaṃ tadviṣayameva loke
bhakṣaṇamucyate | tacca prītyā tṛptirūpayā’vinābhūtam | tasmāttasyāḥ prīterabhāvāt
padārthasvarūpābhāvādeva bhakṣā nivartanta iti yuktamiti pūrvapakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntastu---vacanādbhakṣo bhavatīti
 | tadākṣipati---nanvevaṃ sati
vacanamapyanarthakaṃ prāpnoti aśakyārthavidhānāt
 | rāddhāntī pariharati---
nānarthakaṃ padārthamātrasya śakyatvāt
 | prītyarthe’pi bhakṣe yo’sau vyāpāraḥ sa hi
bhakṣayatinā ucyate | tataśca tadvacanena vidhānaṃ bhaviṣyati | tasya kṛṣṇaleṣvapi
śakyatvāt | nanu cābhyavahāryaviṣayaṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ loke dṛṣṭam | tacca prītyavinābhāvi |
na ca kṛṣṇaleṣu prītirastītyuktam | satyaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ na tu vidheḥ viṣayatāṃ pratipadyate |
kintu vyāpāramātrameva | tacca kṛṣṇaleṣvapyasti | tadāha---vidhiviṣayaṃ ca bhakṣaṇaṃ
kṛṣṇaleṣvapi vidyate
 | viṣayaśabdena bhakṣaṇaṃ parāmṛśyamiti viṣayamiti napuṃsakanirdeśa
iti yuktaṃ vacanam | tacca kṛṣṇalaviṣayameva | na ghṛtaviṣayaṃ tasya guṇatvāt pradhānatvācca
kṛṣṇalānāmityuktaṃ bhāṣye |

nanu ca ghṛtaviṣameva bhakṣaṇavidhānametaditi yuktam | tasmin bhakṣaṇe
cucchūṣādīnāmanuvādasambhavāt | hiraṇye tu bhakṣyamāṇe cucchūṣādīnāmaprāpteḥ tepi
vidhātavyā iti vidhigauravamityatrāha---cucchūṣādīnāmanuvādāsambhavāt
 | hiraṇye
śrapaṇādhārājyasaṃsṛṣṭe bhakṣyamāṇe prāptameva cucchūṣākaraṇamiti na viśiṣṭavidhi-
gauravāpattiḥ |

|| 4 || ekadhopahāre sahatvaṃ brahmabhakṣāṇāṃ prakṛtau vihitatvāt || 17 ||

atra bhāṣyakāraḥ 1sakṛttvapakṣe codakaprāpte bhakṣāntarabādhaprasaṅgena sahatvapakṣa-
māśritya coditavān | “nanu vācanika evāyaṃ bhakṣo na codakaprāpta”
 iti | tatra
punarevamuktavāt---dravyapratipattyarthatayā codakaprāpta evāyaṃ bhakṣaḥ | vacanaṃ cucchūṣādi-
vidhānārthamiti | tadākṣipati---pūrvādhikaraṇe vācanikaṃ bhakṣamabhidhāya kathaṃ
pratipattyarthatvena punaḥ prāptirucyate | kathaṃ vā cucchūṣādīnāṃ anuvāda-

1. A. sakṛdbhakṣye

[p. 338]

mabhidhāya punarniyamo’bhidhīyate
 | samādhatte---nāyaṃ virodhaḥ sambhavati
prakṛtau yathā laukikasya bhakṣasyābhyavahāryaviṣayasya yat kāryaṃ prītirūpaṃ tasya

darśanāt tadabhāvena vikṛtau na vidheyaprāptirityadhastāduktam | vacanaṃ ca vidheya-
viṣayatayopapadyate pākavat | iha punaridamucyate yadi vikṛtāvetadeva vyāpāramātraṃ
vidheyam | vidheśca kāryakalpanā na padārthasya | sambhavati cātrāpi | prakṛtāvapi
tadvidheḥ kāryaṃ dravyapratirūpaṃ tasmānna bādhaḥ śakyate vaktum | tena bhakṣaścodakenaiva
prāpto 1na vedhayaḥ | cucchuṣādīnāmeva vacanaviṣayatvam | cucchuṣādīnāṃ cāni-
tyatvāt nityārthaṃ vacanaviṣayatāpi śakyate vaktumiti sūktam | etaduktaṃ bhavati---
yathā vikṛtau vyāpāramātrasya vidhānaṃ tathā prakṛtāvapyastu | tasya ca dravyapratipattiḥ
pradhānaṃ phalaṃ ānuṣaṅgikī puruṣaprītiriti codakato’styeva bhakṣaprāptiḥ | vacanaṃ tu
prakāraviśeṣaniyamārthaṃ vacanaviśeṣaniyamārtham | kathaṃ tarhi prāgvācanikī prāptiruktā |
ata āha---ādhastyamadhikaraṇamidānīṃ kimarthaṃ ?
 uttaraṃ---etadyukti-
prasiddhayartham
 | etāmeva yuktiṃ prasādhayituṃ tadityarthaḥ | vyāpāramātrameva vidhiviṣaya
ityetatsādhane tatra tātparyam na punarvācaniko bhakṣa ityatra | tena nāsti doṣaḥ |
yuktisādhanaṃ kimarthamityatrāha---na hyaprasādhitā yuktiryuktirbhavati kāryākaraṇāt

|| 5 || sarvatvaṃ ca teṣāmadhikārāt syāt || 18 ||

kasyacidapariharaṇīyasya parihāravidhānaṃ sambhavatīti pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ
 |
yadyeṣā vacanavyaktiḥ yadbrahmaṇe pariharati tatsarvamiti tathāpi brahmabhāgabhūtasyāpi yasya
pariharaṇīyatvaṃ prāptaṃ tasya parihārarthaṃ sarvamiti vidhīyate2 | yo’pi brahmabhāgaḥ sa na
sarvo niyamena brahmaṇe deya iti na prāpta eva | ekadeśadvāreṇāpi tādarthyopapatteḥ |
tathā yadi yadbrahmaṇe sarvaṃ tatpariharatīti vacanavyaktiḥ tathāpi apariharaṇīyapari-
hārārthameva pariharatīti vidheyamityarthaḥ | yadyeka evārtho vacanavyaktidvayepi kimarthaṃ
tarhi dvayamuktamityatrāha---vacanavyaktipradarśanaṃ tu sambhavamātrakathanam
 |

naya eva
 | asyārthaḥ---yadbrahmaṇe pariharati tatsarvamityasyāṃ vacanavyaktau sarvaśabdārtho
1. R. na vidheyaḥ
2. R. vidheyaṃ

[p. 339]

na vidheyaḥ prāptatvāt, yatkhalu brahmaṇe parihārārthaṃ tatsarvameva parihārārthaṃ prāptaṃ dravya-
pratipattyarthatvāt parihārasya yāvaddravyameva parihāro yuktaḥ yadi hi dravyaṃ guṇabhūtaṃ
syāt tadā ekadeśenāpi vyāpāre kṛtaḥ śāstrārtho bhavet | prādhānye tu dravyasya
yāvaddravyameva parihāro yuktaḥ | anyathā’nena saṃskāreṇa tasya varjanaṃ syāt |
tathā yadbrahmaṇe tatsarvaṃ pariharati ityasyāmapi vacanavyaktau sarvapadārthasyānupapattireva
viśeṣyāparijñānāt | tasmāt sarvaṃ caruṃ brahmaṇe pariharati iti | yadi syāt yatsarvaṃ
pariharati tadabrahmaṇa iti anyataravacanavyaktyāśrayaṇena puruṣāntarabhāgānāmapanayo’yaṃ
vidhīyate asambhavaśca sarvaśabdārthasya sarvāsu vacanavyaktiṣviti darśitam1 |

|| 6 || puruṣāpanayāt svakālatvam || 20 ||

kiyadanena bādhyat iti cintyate
 | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī yāvacchravaṇa-
mabādhaṃ manvānaḥ pūrvapakṣitavān | sarvaṃ brahmaṇe pariharatīti śruteḥ ekasminneva kāle
pariharaṇaṃ pratīyata iti kālo’pi bādhyata iti pūrvapakṣitam |

rāddhāntastu---ekārthatvānniyogasya nārthāntaraṃ sambhavatīti apana-
yamātreṇa paryavasānam yadanyat tadavādhitatvāt kartavyam
 | asyārthaḥ---
yadyapyekakālatā śrūyate tathāpi niyogasyānekārthavidhāyakatvāsambhavāt bhāgāpanayamātraṃ
vidhīyate | na kālo’pi | tena kālāntarāvidhānāt yathāprāptasya kālasyāvādhi-
tatvāt svakāla eva pariharaṇaṃ kartavyam |

|| 16 || dveṣye ca cācodanāddakṣiṇāpanayaḥ syāt || 46 ||

ṛtvigbhavati tadā bhavedidaṃ ṛtvigdānaṃ, na ca ṛtvik dveṣyaḥ
 na dveṣaṇīya
ityarthaḥ | smṛtivacanāt ṛtvigācāryau nāticaritavyau iti | tena dveṣyasya-
rtvijo’bhāvāt nedamṛtvije dānam |

nanu laṅghitaśāstrārthasya kadācit ṛtvigapi dveṣyaḥ sambhavati
 |
yata eva dveṣyasambhavaḥ ata eva pratiṣedhaśāstram | anyathā’prasaktau pratiṣevacanaṃ
nāvataret | pariharati---śāstrācca tadviṣayadveṣanāśanaṃ 1yatrataḥ prāptam
 | asyārthaḥ---
śāstrasthān gṛhītvā śāstravyāpāraḥ | na ca śāstrasthasya ṛtvigdveṣaṇīyaḥ | yaḥ punaḥ
śāstraṃ nādriyate tasya varaṇepyanadhikāra eva 2ucchṛṅkhalatvāt | etadevāha---
na cecchāmtramanuvartate vṛṇoti vā kimiti
 | śāstrāccet kṛtaniścayaḥ na tarhi
dveṣya ṛtvik | tasmāt dveṣyasyartvijo’sambhavāt nedaṃ naimittikamānatyarthaṃ dānamiti |
śāstrāntaramadṛṣṭāyeti sthitam |

|| 17 || asthiyajño’vipratiṣedhāditareṣāṃ syādvipratiṣedhāda-
sthnām || 47 ||

kathaṃ punarasthnāṃ yāgāśaṅgā acetanatvāt | na hyacetano yagaṃ kartuṃ śakrati |
na cādhikāro’pyacetanānāmupapadyeta | boddhā hyadhikriyate | nāpi tāni niyujyante |
ata eva tiraścāmanadhikāra ityatrāha---atra śrutibalena abhidhānaviniyogayoḥ
niyogasya ca śaktyapekṣatvāt śrutāparityāgena yathā śakyate tathā niyo-
gārtho’nuṣṭhātavyaḥ iti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhidhānaḥ
 |

asyārthaḥ---nāsthnāmadhikāraḥ śakyate, aṅgatvādasthiyajñasya | asthnāṃ
yāge kartṛtvāśaṅkā abhidhānaviniyogayoḥ śrutibalena | asthīni yājayet ityami-
dhānaṃ tāvadasthiṣu śrautam | tathā yājanānvayo’pyasthnāṃ śrauta eva | tena yāga-
kartṛtā tāvadasthnāṃ śrautī | yo’pi cāyaṃ niyogārtho’sthikartṛke yāge’bhūtena yathā
śakyate tathā’nuṣṭhātavyaḥ yasmāt satre niyogaḥ śaktyapekṣaḥ | pradarśitā ca yathāśakti
1. R. hānaṃ
2. ullaṅghitaśāstratvāt

[p. 348]

śavadravyaṃ gṛhītvā puruṣāntareṣu yāgaṃ kurvāṇeṣu bhavati yāge asthāni kartṝṇīti pūrvapakṣa-
vādino’bhimānaḥ | liṅgaṃ ca darśitam | yajamānabhūtānāṃ stotrānte’sthnāmupasthitiḥ
asthīni yajamānāni darśayati |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---satyaṃ śaktyanurodhī niyogaḥ | kintu
abhidhānaviniyogayoreva śrautārtho na sambhavati
 asthnāṃ yāge kartṛtvā-
sambhavāt | na ca parikradvārā kartṛtā parikrayābhāvāt | yadi hi tāni yājayataḥ
puruṣān parikrīṇīta sa tadā saṃbhavedapi parikrayaḥ | nacaitadasti | na cāsthnāṃ
svamasti yena parikrayaḥ syāt na ca tadviṣayā yathāśaktiḥ | na hyaśavatyanyaḥ
śāstrārtho bhavati |

kathaṃ tarhi pratinidhau yathāśakti
 | uttaraṃ---sā niyogasyaiveti
varṇitā | niyogārtha eva yathā śakyate tathā’nuṣṭhīyate | sadṛśagrahaṇaṃ tu
abhidhānasampattyartham yathābhihitārthasampattaye
 | na punararthāntarābhidhānasya
sampattaye | tasmādabhidhānaviniyogayoreva yathāśrutanimittasāmañjasyaṃ na sambhavatīti
gaṅgāyāṃ dhoṣa itivat lakṣaṇā āśrayaṇīyā | asthiśabdena asthimantaḥ jīvantaḥ
satriṇa eva lakṣaṇīyāḥ | ato yena saha satriṇaḥ pravṛttāḥ tadaṅgavatāṃ tadasthimatāṃ
kartṛtvamiti sāmañjasyam | tasmāt jīvatāmeva yāgaṃ iti yuktam | liṅgaṃ punaḥ
tatsarūpaṃ yājamānakumbhopasthitisarūpaṃ vācanikamityuktaṃ bhāṣye
 |

|| 21 || kāmyāni tu na vidyante kāmājñānādyathetarasyānu-

[p. 350]

|| 26 || kriyā syāddharmamātrāṇām || 62 ||

ākṣeptuṃ sandehamanubhāṣate tāvat---dvādaśāhamṛddhikāmā upeyuriti

 | asyārthaḥ---laukikanyāyānusāreṇārthasya lopaṃ manyate pūrva-
pakṣavādī | loke hi yadanyasmai dīyate dānaṃ sāmaprayogaśca tatrāpi bhṛtyānāṃ bhavatyeva
dātāraṃ pratyānatiḥ | tathāhi---bhṛtyāḥ sādhurayaṃ priyaṃvada udāro dānaśīlaśceti

|| 27 || guṇalope ca mukhyasya || 63 ||

sahaivāgnihotrahavaṇyā nirvāpaśāstraṃ parisamāpyate
 | asyārthaḥ-agnihotra-
havaṇyā havīṃṣi nirvapatīti nirvāpaśāstraṃ agnihotrahavaṇyā saha samāpyate | tathāpi
kimityāha---na ca śāstrārthasya vibhāgo vidyate
 | śāstraikye śāstrārtho’pyekaḥ |
padārthā hi vibhaktāḥ na tu śāstrārthaḥ | agnihotrahavaṇyā vinā śāstrārtha eva nāstīti
pavamāneṣṭiṣu nirvāpasyākaraṇaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | kevalo’pi nirvāpaḥ kṛto’pyakṛta
eva aśāstrārthatvāt |

rāddhāntastu satyaṃ yadyupadeśato’nuṣṭhānaṃ syāt | iha tu kāryato’nuṣṭhānaṃ
kāryaikadeśasya ca sambhave tu tattyāgo niṣpramāṇakaḥ
 | agnihotrahavaṇyā
abhāve’pi tāvat nirvāya/?/sya kāryaṃ sambhavati | na ca sambhavataḥ kāryaikadeśasya tyāge
pramāṇamasti | tattyāge ca tajjanakatayā nirvāpo’pi kartavya eva kāryaprayuktatvādanu-
ṣṭhānasya | yadyupadeśasampādanāyānuṣṭhānaṃ syāt syādapyananuṣṭhānam | iha tu kāryasampā-
danāyaivānuṣṭhānamiti kartavyo nirvāpaḥ | yadi cāgnihotrahavaṇyeva nirvāpena saṃskriyate
tadā saṃskāryābhāve nirvāpasyākaraṇaṃ syāt na ca tadastītyāha---na(nu) cāgnihotraha-
vaṇī saṃskāryā yena
 tadabhāve kāryāvalopaḥ syāt
 | nirvāpasya tu nirupyamāṇadravyaṃ
saṃskāryaṃ1 kāryam | alupte ca tasmin kārye na bādhaḥ śakyate vaktumiti
sthito nirvāpaḥ

nanu caturo muṣṭīn nirvapatīti catussaṅkhyā muṣṭayaśca nirvāpe

anyatarasyāpi lopaḥ kena hetunetyabhiprāyaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---yadyapi nirvāpa-

puruṣārthena kratvarthasya camasasya saptadaśasaṅkhyāyāḥ śarāvadravyeṇa catussaṅkhyāyāḥ
muṣṭidravyasya ca bādho yuktaḥ | yadi punaḥ kāryataḥ samānaviṣayatā nāśrīyate tadā
anyatarasya bādho rāddhāntābhimato na siddhayatīti purvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ |

pāditam | tatrāpi grahaṇameva mukhyasyetyuktam | yathā ādyasyopādānaṃ antyasya

tato dravyamevānuruddhayeta saṅkhyā ca lupyeta | atra tu bhinnaprātipadikābhidheyā saṅkhyā

|| 31 || pareṣāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ syāt || 71 ||

khadirādayastāvadākṣiptaviśeṣavacanatvānnivṛttā ityuktam dravyāntaravidhāne dravyā-
ntaranivṛtteḥ | idānīṃ takṣaṇādayaścintyante kiṃ khalevālyāṃ kartavyā neti |
tatra pūrvapakṣavādī upadeśāvirodhāt kartavyā iti manyate
 | khalevālyāṃ
yūpakāryaṃ kartavyamiti yo’yamupadeśārthaḥ asau takṣaṇādiṣu kriyamāṇeṣvapi na viruddhayata
iti kartavyāstakṣaṇādayaḥ na hyatropadeśena virodhaḥ khadirādīnāmiva |

nanu kāryaṃ nāsti takṣaṇādīnāṃ kimatrāvirodhaḥ kariṣyati
 | joṣaṇa-
chedane tāvat joṣitāyāṃ chinnāyāṃ ca khalevālyāṃ luptakārye | takṣaṇamapi khale-
vālīvirodhādavidyamānakāryaṃ kimatrāvirodhaḥ kariṣyati | uttaraṃ---tadeva tu kāryama-
virodhe sati nivṛttamiti na śakyate vaktumityabhiprāyaḥ
 | ayamabhiprāyaḥ---
svarūpatastāvadvirodho nāsti | tenāvirodhe sati kartavyatayā pratibhāsamānānāmeṣāṃ
kāryaṃ kalpayitavyam | tenādṛṣṭārthe na punarapi joṣaṇachedane kariṣyete adṛṣṭārthaṃ ca
takṣaṇamiti | atrocyate | dṛṣṭakāryatvāt tasya khalevālyāṃ cāśakyatvāt na
kāryāntarākāṅkṣā
 | asyārthaḥ---klṛptakāryāṇāṃ prakṛtitaḥ prāptānāṃ tadeva kāryaṃ
pratibhātīti na kāryāntarākāṅakṣāsti yenāvirodhena kāryāntaraṃ kalpyeta | tasmānna
kāryāvirodhamātreṇa kāryāstitvaṃ śakyate vaktum | prākṛtaṃ ca kāryaṃ yattadeṣāṃ luptam |
yadā caiṣāṃ prākṛtameva kāryaṃ tadā prakṛtau yūpotpattyarthatvāt ihāpi yūpotpattyarthairevai-
tairbhavitavyam | na cātra yūpena prayojanaṃ ayūpasya tatkārye vidhānāt khalevālyā yūpasya
ca bhinnatvāt | tataśca kāryābhāvānna kāryāstakṣaṇādayaḥ | tadāha-kiñca yūpo-
tpattyarthāstakṣaṇādaya iti prakṛtāvuktam | ayūpaścāyaṃ niyojane vihita
iti na takṣaṇādayo bhavitumarhanti joṣaṇādayaśca kṛtatvānna kartavyā iti
pradarśitam
 |

[p. 362]

|| 32 || arthābhāve saṃskāratvaṃ syāt || 73 ||

kṛtatvāt kāryasya kāryāntarākāṅkṣā nāstīti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ
 | adbhi-
rava secanādibhiḥ kṛtaṃ tatkāryamiti punaḥ kāryamaparaṃ nākāṅkṣate | tena nivṛttireṣāmiti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | satyaṃ yadi dṛṣṭameva kāryaṃ na syāt
 | dṛṣṭe punaḥ kārye kṛta-
mityeva nāsti khalevāraṇaśithilīkṛtāyāṃ khalevālyām
 | 1asti ca
dārḍhyāpādanaṃ kāryamiti kṛtakāryatā nāsti kṛtasyāpyananuvartanāt | tasmādaluptārthānāṃ
teṣāmabādhaḥ
 |

|| 33 || arthena ca viparyāse tādarthyāt tattvameva syāt || 74 ||

iha pūrvavādī kramapadārtho’pi bādhyata iti manyate
 | asyārthaḥ---
prakṛtau pākātpūrvamavaghātaḥ iha pākottarakālatā’vaghātasya syāditi kramabādhaḥ | tena
krame bādhite’vaghāto’pi bādhyata iti manyate pūrvapakṣavādī |

nanu cāgnihotrahavaṇyā nirvapatīti na guṇānurodhena pradhānaṃ bādhyata
iti sthitameva | guṇaśca kramaḥ padārthāḥ pradhānabhūtāḥ | tena 2guṇabādhepi
pradhānaṃ kartavyameva kāryataścātra prāptirityuktam
 | tasya ca tuṣakaṇavimoka-
lakṣaṇasya ca kāryasya darśanādalopo’rthasyeti yuktamevāvaghātānuṣṭhānam | tasmāt
gatārthamevedaṃ pratibhāti uktatvānnyāyasya
 |

atrocyate---vidhivitānamātraṃ krama ityuktam | na kramo nāmāgni-
hotrahavaṇīvat kaścidvayatirikto yasya pṛthagbādhaṃ varṇayāmaḥ
 | tasmādvitānā-
nyatve padārthabādha aivati pūrvapakṣaḥ |

yadi hi svatantraḥ padārthakramaḥ syāt tadā tadabhāve padārthasvarūpānapāyāt
kriyetaivānuṣṭhānam | vitānastu padārthasya svarūpānupraviṣṭaḥ padārthā eva vitatāḥ
pratīyanta iti tadabhāve padārthasvarūpasyāpyabhāva iti kramabādhe padārtho’pi yathā-
codito bādhyate |

1. R. astye va
2. R. guṇābhāvepi

[p. 363]

rāddhāntastu---kāryadarśane sati prayuktaṃ bādhituṃ[na]śakyate
 | tuṣakaṇa-
vimocanasya kāryasya sambhavādavaghāto na śakyate bādhitum | yadi kāryaprayukto-
’vaghāto na bādhyate kasya tarhi bādhaḥ ?
 uttaraṃ---anuṣṭhānābhyāsasyeti
vadāmaḥ
 | prakṛtai triḥphalīkaraṇavidhānāt sābhyāso’vadhātaḥ | iha dhānāsvabhyāse
tasmin kriyamāṇe saktavo bhaveyuḥ tena svarūpavirodhādavaghātasyābhyāso bādhyate |
sakṛdavadhātastu kriyate tāvanmātreṇa yāvattuṣakaṇavimocanaṃ tāvadeva kāryaṃ kriyate |
sa cābhyāso’rthaprayuktatvāt guṇa iti śakyate vaktuṃ avaghātābhyāsamantareṇa samyak
taṇḍulanirvṛttyabhāvāt arthākṣipto’vaghātābhyāsa iti guṇatvādbādhyate |

kaḥpunarayaṃ vyatikramo bhāṣyakāreṇocyate
 ? yadi vyatikramaḥ
tadā pūrvoktanyāyenāvadhātasyāpi bādhaḥ prāpnoti | uttaraṃ---śrutadravyābhiprāyeṇa
taducyate
 | yacchrutaṃ dhānādravyaṃ tadabhiprāyeṇedamucyate | prakṛtau pākātprāgavaghātaḥ
kriyate | iha tu pākottarakālamiti vyatikramo bhaṇyate |

paramārthatastu vyatikrama evāyaṃ na bhavati yadeva dravyaṃ śrutaṃ tasyaiva nirvāpa-
prokṣaṇānantaramavaghātaḥ | iha ca na pūrvāvasthā dhānānāṃ śrutā kintu dhānāvasthaiva |
tadāha---aśrutā tu pūrvāvasthā pradhānānām | tasmātsūktaṃ dhānāsvavahantiḥ
kartavya iti
 |

iti ṛjuvimalāyāṃ pañcikāyāṃ

daśamasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

[p. 364]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

|| daśamādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || vikṛtau śabdavattvātpradhānasya guṇānāmadhikotpattiḥ
sannidhānāt || 1 ||

atra bhāṣyakāreṇa yāsu vikṛtiṣu prākṛteṣvaṅgeṣu guṇavidhayaḥ tā udāhṛtāḥ |
tatra yathāśrutamātragrāhī kaścit tanmātrameva māgrahīt ityāha---upadeśavidhāna-
yuktāni sarvāṇyeva vaikṛtānyudāharaṇāni
 | rāddhānte nāmno’pyanekārthakalpanā
syādityabhyuccayopapattyarthaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇodāharaṇaviśeṣaḥ parigṛhītaḥ |

kaḥpunaratra pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ ?
 uttaraṃ---ayamabhiprāyaḥ-
kāryataścodakaprāptiḥ
 | tathāpi kimityāha---kāryataścet tadā tatprāptyaiva
nirākāṅkṣaṃ pradhānam
 | kāryato hi karaṇopakāralakṣaṇāt yā prāptiḥ sā upakāra-
janakatayaiva | tataścopakārajanakapadārthaprāptyaiva nirākāṅkṣaṃ pradhānaṃ, nirākāṅkṣe ca
tasmin vihitasyāpi guṇasya pradhānavidhinā sambandhābhāvāt ananuṣṭhānāpattiḥ1,
guṇavidhyabhāve cānarthakatvaṃ vākyasya | na ca guṇe’dhikārāntarakalpanā sambhavati
prakṛtādhikāraikyāvagamāt | tasmāt guṇavidhyasambhavāt karmāntarāṇyevaitānīti yuktam |

atra codayati---karmāntarakalpanāyāṃ vā kathaṃ codakasya nirodhaḥ
asati ca nirodhe’rthavattā durbhaṇaiva | tasmāt karmāntaravādināpi pradhāna-
sambandhe samādhirvaktavyaḥ
 | ucyate | ayamasti samādhiḥ | pradhānaṃ hi vṛttyu-
pāyāpekṣam | yāvadupadeśavidhānanivṛttaṃ prākṛtamupāyamālocayati tāvada-
trāpi prakṛtivadupadeśa evāstīti na paropadeśanibandhanamupāyamapekṣate
 |
asyārthaḥ---karmāntaravidhipakṣe niyogaikyena pratipannakāryānupraveśānāṃ prākṛtetikarta-
vyatāsvīkāreṇa nirapekṣatayā aupadeśikānāmanupraveśabādho mābhūditi codako
nāṅgīkriyate | kintu darvihomanyāyena yāvacchrutairupakāraḥ kalpyate; guṇa-
vidhau punaḥ kāryābhinirvṛtternāyaṃ parihārassambhavati
 |

1. R āyatteḥ

[p. 365]

asyārthaḥ---guṇavidhipakṣe svāśrayatayā prakṛtapadārthatayā prākṛtapadārthagrahaṇamanu-
mantavyam | tataśca prākṛtenaiva padārthavargeṇa nirākāṅkṣatayā punargaṇānupraveśo na
sambhavati ityukto viśeṣaḥ | prāgeva prayājādayaśca śabdāḥ sphuṭe karmāntaratve prakṛtāviva
bahūnāṃ vācakā ihāpi bhavitumarhanti anekārthatvābhyupagamāt | yathā eka eva
prayājaśabdaḥ prakṛtau bahūnāṃ samidādīnāṃ vācakaḥ tathā’mīṣāmapi karmāntarāṇāmutpadya-
mānānāṃ vācakaḥ prayogabalenānekārthatvāt |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate-satyaṃ kāryataḥ kāraṇopakāralakṣaṇāt prākṛtānāṃ
padārthānāṃ atideśo vikṛtyapūrvatādarthyenābhisambandhaḥ, satyaṃ ca nirjñātopā-
yatvāt nairākāṅkṣyam | niyogaikyamapi tu sannidhānānnaivāpanhotuṃ śakyate

1prakṛtivadeva | yathā prakṛtau sannidhānamātreṇāṅgānāṃ pradhānānāṃ ca eka eva niyogaḥ
sādhyo’bhyupeyate tathā vikṛtāvapi tāvattathaiva pratīyate tadaikyāccādhikāṅgopasaṃhāro na
niṣpramāṇakaḥ niyogaikye’vagate yathopadiṣṭāṅgānāmeva prākṛtopakāraṃ prati janakatā
parikalpyate | asati hyupadeśe prākṛtenaiva padārthajātena tasyopakārasyehāpi siddhira-
vasīyate prakṛtiklṛptamevopakārasādhanaṃ vaikṛto vidhiranumanyate | upadiṣṭeṣvadhikeṣvaṅgeṣu
tadanupraveśasiddhayarthaṃ teṣāmapi tadupakārajananānupraveśaḥ kalpyate | na ca śaṅkayaṃ
codaka eva na pravartiṣyata iti, niyamānavāpteḥ | bhavanti cātra gāthāḥ---

niyamānavāptivikalo liṅgabalopa(hitaṃ)sthitaṃ vidhiḥ prathamam |

upakāraṃ parakīyaṃ svīkurute tāvaditi siddham || 1 ||

upakārajanakabhūtaḥ padārthavargo vivakṣitaḥ prakṛtau |

tādarthyamātreṇa prakārataḥ sannidhimupaiti vikṛtau || 2 ||

upadeśanipatitāḥ śrūyante katipaye ca ye dharmāḥ |

2te niyogaikyavaśāt tādarthyaṃ tatra gacchanti || 3 ||

prākṛtapadārtharāśeḥ vaikṛtamupade(śikīṃ)śaḍhaukitaṃ tu tataḥ |

vidhirupakārajanane sahāyabhāvena kalpayati || 4 ||

1. R prakṛtivadekavadeva
2. A. tepi niyo

[p. 366]

nanu guṇavidhau vākyena vidhānaṃ, karmavidhau tu śrutyetyatrāha-na ca guṇe karmaṇi
viniyogasya kaścidviśeṣaḥ | guṇavidhāvapi bhāvārthaviṣaya eva vidhirityuktaṃ
yataḥ | ato guṇavidhi1sarūpeṣu vākyeṣu sambhavatpadārtheṣu na viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ
vidhigauravāpādakaṃ ātmānaṃ labhate
 | codakāccoktaḥ sambhavaḥ | tasmādguṇa-
vidhānānyevaitānīti yuktam | śeṣaṃ sanidarśanamuktam
 |

|| 2 || hiraṇyagarme pūrvasya mantraliṅgāt || 13 ||

liṅgāt prakāśanasāmarthyātmakāt pūrvasyaivādhārasya mantravidhiriti
pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | anyathā hi adṛṣṭārtho mantraḥ syāt | indraprakāśa-
nāsāmarthyāt
 | dvitīye ādhāre etasyaiva viniyogābhyupagame dṛṣṭārtho mantraḥ syāt |
‘ūrdhvo adhvara’
 iti mantrāntarasya codakaprāptasya bādhaśca na kalpito bhavati |
atatkāryakaraṇenābhyupagatena tatkāryakaraṇe tvabhyupagamyamāne mantrāntareṇa
mantrāntarasya bādhaḥ syāt
 |

evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate | na kāryāntarakalpanā sambhavati | upāyā-
ntaramātravidhānopakṣīṇatvādupadeśasya
 | asyārthaḥ-dvitīya ādhāre mantrakāryaṃ
klṛptamasti pūrvasmiṃścādhāre mantrāntarakāryaṃ kalpayitavyam | na ca kalpanāyāṃ pramāṇa-
masti mantrāntaravidhimātreṇopadeśasyopapatteḥ mantrasya cendrābhidhānepi tulyatvāt |
darśitaṃ ca bhāṣyakāreṇa tulyatvaṃ ca | tasmāduttarasyaiva mantrāntaravidhiriti
sūktam
 |

|| 3 || saṃskare tu kriyāntaraṃ tasya vidhāyakatvāt || 18 ||

nanu ca ekādaśa prayājān yajatītyanenaiva gatam | guṇavidhau
sambhavati na viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ sambhavatīti
 | asyārthaḥ---prākṛtayorevāsādananiyo-
janayorutkaraparidhilakṣaṇaguṇavidhānena vākyayorupapannatvāt na viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ yukta-
māśrayitum | samādhatte---satyametadevaṃ aśakyatvāttu punarāśaṅkyate
 yathāvagata-
svarūpayorutkaraparidhyoruttuṅgatīkṣṇāgrakṛśayoḥ prākṛtamāsādanaṃ niyojanaṃ ca dṛṣṭārthaṃ-
maśakyamiti adṛṣṭārthaṃ karmāntaravidhānamiti punarāśaṅkā | tadāha---nāsādana
-
1. svarūpeṣu

[p. 367]

niyojane prākṛte dṛṣṭārthe tūtkaraparidhyoḥ
 tuṅgatīkṣṇāgrakṛśayoḥ sambhavata ityabhi-
prāyaḥ | etacca guṇavidhāvapi vākyabhedo nāstītyabhyupagamyaiva pūrvakṣitam |

rāddhāntastu---yāvacchakyaṃ niyogārtho’nuṣṭheyaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---prākṛta-
yoreva guṇavidhirastu | tatra yacchakyaṃ tadanuṣṭhāsyate tatra tuṅgāgratā kṛśatā ca
aśakyeti nāpekṣyate pṛthvagratā sthavimā ca kalpayiṣyate yathā bhaviṣyati śaktiḥ |
tadāha---[sa] ca pṛthvagrakaraṇenotkarasya sthavimnā ca paridheḥ niyogārtho
’nuṣṭhāsyate
 | ato nādṛṣṭaprayojanakalpanā yuktā, kartavyatāniṣṭhatvānniyogāvagateḥ |
kartavyatāniṣṭho hi niyogo na prayojananiṣṭhaḥ | tena prayojanāntarakalpanā nāsti |
klṛptakāryāśrayaṇepi anuṣṭhānopapatteḥ | tasmādatrāpi guṇavidhireva nyāyyaḥ |

evaṃ samāpte’dhikaraṇe kṛtvācintāmuddhāṭayati---viśiṣṭānuvādādi cātra
nāśaṅkanīyam
 | nirjñātakāryaviṣayatvādevamāderupadeśasya viśiṣṭavidhānepyadoṣaḥ |
yadyapyanekaguṇaśravaṇāt nāsādananiyojanāntaravidhiḥ tathāpi prākṛtakārye
vidhānānna doṣaḥ phalasya tulyatvāt
 | guṇavidhau yādṛśaṃ phalaṃ tādṛśameva viśiṣṭa-
vidhāvapi | sarvatra cedamuktam |

|| 4 || ṣaṅbhirdīkṣayatīti tāsāṃ mantravikāraḥ
śrutisaṃyogāt || 23 ||

atra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ vaikṛtāstāvanmantrāḥ prākṛtakārya eva viniyuktāḥ
śarā iva barhiṣāṃ kārye | ataḥ kāryāpahārāt prākṛtānāṃ nivṛttiḥ
 |

nanu homānāmabhyāsāt prākṛtā vaikṛtāśca mantrāḥ samuccīyanta ityatrāha---
na cābhyāse kāryāntaraṃ mantrāṇāṃ sambhavati homānāmabhyāsepi
 mantrāṇāṃ
kāryāntaraṃ nāsti kāryānyatve hi abhyāsānupapa(ttiḥ)tteḥ tathābhūtasyaiva punaranuṣṭhāna-
mabhyāsaḥ | mantrakāryabhede ca na tathābhūtatvaṃ ekatve ca kāryasya tasminneva mantrāntara-
vidhānam | tasmādvādho nyāyyaḥ |

rāddhāntastu satyaṃ prākṛtakārye vidhānaṃ kṛtatvāttu kāryasya na mantrā-
bhyāsaṃ nyāyyaṃ manyāmahe
 | sakṛdupadiṣṭaiḥ kārye kṛte’rthavattā tadupadeśasya jāteti
na mantrābhyāso yuktaḥ kāryābhyāsepi | nanu tasminneva kārye satyabhyāsa upa
-

[p. 368]

padyate na kāryāntare;
 pariharati---etadapi satyaṃ na kāryāntare satyabhyāsa
iti | kimiti tarhyanabhyāso mantrāṇām
 | kāryavanmantrābhyāso’pi 1nyāyya
iti bhāvaḥ | uttaraṃ---kṛtaṃ tadupadeśeneti vadāmaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---mantropadeśena
mantrānuṣṭhānaṃ kṛtam | akṛte ca tasmin mantrābhyāse na pramāṇamasti | upadeśa-
sampattyartho hyatra kāryayogo vaikṛtānām | na kāryataḥ kāryayogaḥ
 | kārya-
mastītyetāvatā kāryaṃ na kartavyam kintūpadeśasampattaye | tasmāt sakṛtkaraṇenaivo-
padeśaḥ sampanna ityabhyāsāntare bādhāyālaṃ bhavitumarhati | yadi punaḥ kāryata eva
kāryaṃ syāt tadā kāryasya vidyamānatvāt mantraprāpteḥ prākṛtabādha eva syāt | na
caitadasti tadāha---kāryatastu bādha eva | na cāyaṃ kāryata ityuktam |
tasmātsamuccayaḥ | śeṣamuktam
 |

|| 5 || agnayādheyasya naimittike guṇavikāre dakṣiṇādānamadhikaṃ
syādvākyasaṃyogāt || 30 ||

atra ekasmin kārye vidhānādanabhyāsācca nivṛttiriti sthite kathaṃ
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ ? punarādhāne yā dakṣiṇā sā prākṛtadakṣiṇākārye vihitā | na
ca punarādheyasyābhyāso’stīti bādha eva yukto na samuccaya iti sthite
kathaṃ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ
 ? samādhatte---vacanabhrāntyā ubhayīrdadātīti vacanamiti
bhrāmyataḥ pūrvapakṣaḥ | bhrāntipradarśanena ca rāddhāntaḥ
 | vacanamidamiti bhrānti-
reṣā | kintvanuvādo’yaṃ agnyādheye āgnyādheyikīrdadāti, punarādheye paunarādheyikī-
riti rāddhāntaḥ | liṅgamuktam
 |

|| 6 || vāsovatsaṃ ca sāpnānyāt || 34 ||

uttaravivakṣayeti
---satyamuttaranyāyaṃ vaktumidamuktamiti satyaṃ nānyat |

|| 7 || arthāpattestaddharmā syānnimittasyābhisaṃyogāt || 35 ||

atra taddharmatve na tābannyāyo’bhidhīyata ityuktaṃ bhāṣye | tadayuktam |
uktaṃ hi yo yasya kārye vartate sa taddharmān labhata iti navame
 | tasmādayuktai-
1. R. kārya iti

[p. 369]

vānvāhāryadharmabādhāśaṅkā vāsovatsayoḥ | ekagranthenāha---yadi dharmaprāptirnāsti
kathaṃ prāptipūrvako bādhaḥ
 | prāptiścet siddhaṃ tarhi taddharmatvam | samādhatte---prāpti-
ruttaravivakṣayetyetadarthameva taddraṣṭavyam | kathaṃ tarhi pūrvamadhikaraṇam ? prāptiḥ
smāraṇīyeti
 | prāptiṃ smārayitumeva pūrvamadhikaraṇam | 1yadi prāptiḥ smaryate tadā
tatsmṛtau pūrvapakṣāśrayaṇamayuktam | siddha evārthaḥ smaraṇīyaḥ | samādhatte---nāyuktaṃ
pūrvapakṣasmāraṇam
 | sahetukaṃ hi smāraṇaṃ nirviśaṅkamuttaropayogi bhavatīti sapratipakṣa-
muktam | pūrvapakṣe hyasmārite tannirākaraṇapūrvakaḥ siddhāntaheturna śakyate vaditum
anukte ca siddhāntahetau mandabuddheḥ kathametadevamiti śaṅkā syāt | tataścottaracintā
na śakyā kartumiti sapratipakṣamupanyasyate |

|| 8 || dāne pāko’rthalakṣaṇaḥ || 36 ||

yadarthā prāptiḥ smāritā tadevedānīṃ cintyate | tatra 2vatse tāvat pākaści-
ntyate---kiṃ kartavyo neti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī nyāyyaṃ pākaṃ manyate | evaṃ
prākṛtaṃ annaṃ sādhanaṃ yasya tadannasādhanaṃ dakṣiṇādānaṃ na pīḍitaṃ bhaviṣyati
 |

nanu vatso dakṣiṇeti śrūyate kathaṃ tanmāṃsaṃ dakṣiṇā tatrāha---vatsopadeśaśca
prakṛtidravyatayāpyupapanno bhavati vrīhibhiryajetetivat
 |

nanu cātra vatsaḥ pacyamāno’pi nānnaṃ bhavatītyatrāha---bhavati ca māṃse
annaśabdaḥ paramaṃ vā etadannaṃ yanmāṃsaṃ iti
 |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---vatso dakṣiṇeti vatsasya dakṣi
ṇātvaṃ śrūyate | yā
ca dakṣakaraṇī sā dakṣiṇā | yacca dīyate tat dakṣakaram | yadyatra śrapaṇena vatsamāṃsaṃ
dīyate na tarhi vatso dakṣiṇā | na ca pradeyaprakṛtitayā pradeyatā bhavati | tasmānna
māṃsalakṣaṇārtho vatsaśabdo varṇanīyaḥ | vrīhibhiryatetyatra tu yāgaṃ prati karaṇatvaṃ
brīhīṇāmavagamyate | bhavati ca puroḍāśaniṣpattiṃ avāntaravyāpārīkṛtya karaṇatā
vrīhīṇāmapi | tena na tatra lakṣaṇā | iha tu darśitā lakṣaṇā | na ca kāraṇādṛte
lakṣaṇā nyāyyetyuktam |

1. R. yadi prāptimeva smārayituṃ
2. A. vāsovatsayoḥ

[p. 370]

nanu prakṛtivaditi lakṣaṇākāraṇamupanyastam | na kāraṇaṃ bhavitumarhati prakṛti-
vaditi kāryamātrasyātideśāt | anvāhāryakāryaṃ prakṛtivadityatidiśyate tadākṣepeṇacā-
nvāhāryaṃ tatra | vatsenānvāhāryakārye vihite bādhite’nvāhārye na lakṣaṇākāryamasti
anannasyaivānnakārye vidhānāt | tadāha---vatsena ca tasyānvāhāryakāryasya

 | tasmāt śrutopa-
deśasiddhayarthaṃ vatsa eva dātavyaḥ
 | pāke hi upadiṣṭārthavināśaḥ syāt |

 |

 | na

|| 9 || pākasya cānnakāritatvāt || 37 ||

yuktaṃ vatse upadeśārthavināśāt pāko na kriyata iti
 | ekagranthenāha
---vāsasi tarhi avināśāt kriyatāṃ pākaḥ
 | pakvamapi vāso vāsa eva |

 siddhaiva | pākakārya-

ca vāsasyapi pākādvikāraḥ tena nāsti dvāralopa iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntastu viśiṣṭavikārārtho loke pākaḥprasiddhaḥ | ko’sau viśiṣṭo
vikāraḥ ? annaśabdābhidhānanimittamiti brūmaḥ
 | yasmin sati annaśabdavācyatā
