|| 10 || tathā’bhidhāraṇasya || 38 ||

[p. 371]

abhidhāraṇamidānīṃ cintyate - kiṃ vatse vāsasi cābhidhāraṇaṃ kartavyaṃ
neti | atra pūrvapakṣavādī adṛṣṭasaṃskāramabhidhāraṇenānvāhāryasya manvāno-
’bhidhāraṇasya kartavyatāṃ manyate |

rāddhāntastu---na hi dṛṣṭe sambhavati adṛṣṭakalpanā nyāyyeti hetoḥ
svādimaivābhidhāraṇasya prayojanaṃ ṛtvijāmānatāvupayogāt
 | yathā hi
svādunā’nnenānamante na tathetareṇeti lokaprasiddham | na ca tena svādimnā vatse vāsasi
ca prayojanamasti anadanīyatvāttayoḥ | adanīyadravyavartī hi svādimā puruṣānatā-
vupayujyate | na punaranadanīyavartyapi iti prayojanābhāvādabhidhāraṇaṃ bādhyate |

|| 11 || dravyavidhisannidhau saṅkhyā teṣāṃ guṇatvātsyāt || 39 ||

kaḥpunarasyādhikaraṇasya bādhalakṣaṇe prasaṅgaḥ ?
 uttaraṃ---dakṣiṇābādha-
siddhayarthamaupoddhātikamidamadhikaraṇam
 | varṇayiṣyate hi saṅakhyāyuktānāṃ bhāgaśaḥ
kārtsnyena ca bādhaḥ | yadi hi gavāṃ saṅakhyāsambandhaḥ tadā vikṛtiṣu saṅakhyāntaraśravaṇe
tāsāmeva bādhaḥ | tatrartvigbhāge saṅakhyāntaraśravaṇe gogatasyaiva bhāgasya bādhaḥ kevala-
saṅkhyāntaraśravaṇe kṛtsnānāmeva gavāṃ bādha iti | na kevalamidamadhikaraṇamevamarthaṃ
vibhāgena ca dānamidamarthatayaiva cintyate
 | pariṣadadhikaraṇamapyupoddhātabhūta-
mityarthaḥ | tasmādasti bādhalakṣaṇe prasaṅgo’syādhikaraṇasya |
 
nani ‘tasya dvādaśaśataṃ’
 iti tacchabdena 1prakṛtatvāt kraturevocyate na gavādiḥ
tatkathaṃ gavādiparaṃ tacchabdaṃ gṛhītvā vicāra iti tatrāha---tasya śabdaṃ kratorapanī-
yaiṣā cintā
 | dravyāṇāmeva tacchabdenāvamarśa iti cinteyam | abhyupagamyāpi
tasyaśabdāntaḥ pātināṃ dravyāṇāṃ gavāmeva saṅkhyāsambandha iti vaktuṃ kṛtvā cinteyam |
tatrāgṛhyamāṇaviśeṣo vā sarveṣāṃ saṅkhyā sambandhahetuḥ | tena pratyekaṃ
saṅkhyāsambandhaḥ ekena vā kṛtārthatvāt yena kenacit ekenetyayameva
viśeṣahetuḥ |
 asyārthaḥ---saṅkhyā gavādayaśca dakṣiṇayā sambadhyante | tatrāruṇaika-
hāyanīnyāyena dravyamāśritya saṅkhyāyā anvayo’nubhavanīya iti yatkiñcid dravya-
māśrīyate na sarvāṇīti | caśabdayogādvā saṃhatiḥ saṅkhyāsambandhinīti pratibhā |
1. R. prākṛta

[p. 372]

yadyapi saṅkhyā āśrayamātramapekṣate tathāpi caśabdaśravaṇe dravyāṇāṃ samuccayo’vagamyate |
sa eva ca saṅkhyāsambandhī bhaviṣyatīti pratibhāmātram |
 
ānantaryādvā bhāṣāḥ sambandhinaḥ tadanantaraṃ saṅkhyāśravaṇāt |

saṅkhyāmātravyāvahārikatayā prāṇinaḥ saṅkhyāsambandhinaḥ | saṅkhyāmātreṇa vyāvahā-
rikāḥ vyavahārāya prabhavanti prāṇinaḥ na bhāṣādaya iva kuḍabādyavacchedāpekṣiṇaḥ | tena
sāpekṣatvāt vrīhyādīnāṃ na saṅkhyāsambandhaḥ | kintu prāṇināmeva | upakārādhikyādvā
goṣvaśveṣu ca prasaktiḥ | ṛtvijo hi mahatyupakāre vartante | upakurvatāṃ copakāraḥ
kartavya iti sadācāraḥ | tena mahopakārā gāvo’śvā vā saṅkhyāsambandhinaḥ | asmiṃśca
pakṣe’śvaḥ pratiṣedhānnivartate | ubhayatodatāṃ vyavahāro hi vipratiṣiddha iti na
pracuropayogā aśvāḥ | tena te’pi saṅkhyayā na sambadhyante | kintu gāva eva
pracuropayogitvāt | mahābhāgā hi tāḥ |

atrādhikaraṇe pratyekaṃ 1saṅkhyāsambandho dravyaprādhānyenāśaṅkitaḥ | tatra
codyamāha---nanu dravyaprādhānyapakṣopanyāso’sakṛnnavame nirākṛtaḥ | tena
kimityupanyasyate |
 dravyamātrasambandhe hi ānarthakyamuditam | parihāramāha---
upādānata ānarthakyaṃ parihiṛyata ityabhiprāyo dravyaprādhānyavādinaḥ |

yathā paśvādīnāmekatvādikaṃ dravyamātrasambandhyupādānato’narthakaṃ bhavati upādānaśeṣa-
tvāt tatheyamapi saṅkhyeti | kathaṃ tarhi ayaṃ pakṣo nirākriyate nirākaraṇīya-
ścāyam | anirākṛte hi aruṇānyāyo nāvatarati
 | anavatīrṇe ca tasmin
gomātrasambandho na saṅkhyāyāḥ syādityatrāha---prātipadikopāttatayā nopā-
dānataḥ śeṣatvaṃ sambhavatīti nirākaraṇam |
 prātipadikopāttā hi saṅkhyā
kriyayaiva śabdāt sambandhyate ityaruṇānyāyena viniyogaśeṣatvameva syāt,
nopādānaśeṣatvam | vibhaktyabhihitā tu prātipadikamātrasambandhinī śrutyaiva vibhaktyā-
’bhidhīyata iti bhavatyupādānaśeṣaḥ | bhavatvaruṇānyāyaḥ tathāpi caśabdārthaḥ kimiti
saṅkhyāsanbandhī na bhavatītyatrāha---caśabdārthaḥ punaḥ naivānvayī | anvitārthābhi-
dhāyino hyete |
 asyārthaḥ---caśabdasyārtho na padārthānvayabhāgbhavati samuccaya-
śabdavat niṣkṛṣṭasyārthasya caśabdenānabhidhānāt | na hi bhavati caḥ śobhana iti |
kintu parānvitaṃ paropaśliṣṭaṃ svārtha nipātā abhidadhati |

1. R dravyaṃ prādhānyenāśaṅkitam

[p. 373]

nanu ca yadīha na samuccitānāṃ saṅkhyāsambandhaḥ kutastarhi teṣāṃ samuccayaśca-
śabdena dyotyata ityatrāha---dakṣiṇānvayena caśabdaścaritārthaḥ samuccitānāṃ
dakṣiṇātvamiti vakṣyate
 iti caśabdārtho nirastaḥ | saṅkhyāsambandhitayā
vyavahārābhāvāt aprāṇito nirastāḥ | na hi kuḍabādinirapekṣaṃ kevalasaṅkhyā-
mātrāvacchinnaiḥ vrīhyādibījaiḥ loke vyavahāro’sti | tena vyavahārānanurūpatvāt na
teṣāṃ saṅkhyāsambandhaḥ |

nanu sarve prāṇinaḥ saṃhatāḥ saṅkhyayā kimiti na sambadhyante ityatrāha---
ekenāpi prāṇinā saṅkhyopapadyata iti prāṇimātranirāsaḥ | mātraśabdaścātra
sākalyārthaḥ | niraste ca prāṇimātre ekaprāṇimātrasambandhe sthite mukhya-
tvāt prathamopanipātitvāt mahopakāritvāt apratiṣiddhatvācca gāva eva
saṅkhyāsambandhinyaḥ iti nirūpitam |
 tathā ca darśanamityuktam |

|| 12 || tatra dānaṃ vibhāgena pradānānāṃ pṛthaktvāt || 50 ||

nanu parasvatvāpādanānto dadātiriti loke prasiddhatvāt pṛthaktvena
cartvijāmutpatteḥ pūrvapakṣa evātra na saṃvartate
 | yadi hi svatvatyāgamātrarūpo
dadātyarthaḥ syāt tadā vibhāgamantareṇāpi tyāgasya śakyatvāt aniyamena dānaṃ
yuktamevāśaṅikatum | na ca tadasti | na hi tyāgamātre dānaśabdaṃ lokaḥ prayuṅkte |
kintu ya eva parasvatvāpādanāntaḥ tyāgaḥ tatraiva dānaśabdo loke prasiddhaḥ | yadi ca
bhedenartvijāmutpattiḥ na syāt eka evartvik 1tadāpi vibhāgāniyamena dānaṃ
saṃvarteta | bhinnāścartvijaḥ | tān pratyavibhāgena na śakyaṃ dānaṃ kartu, (m) miti |

nanu kathamaśaktiḥ ? yadi tāvat svatvāpādanāntatvaṃ na sambhavatītyucyeta
kathamasambhavaḥ | bhavati hi avibhaktamapi madhyagaṃ dravyaṃ 2sambhūyasamutthāyināṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ ca
pitāputrayordempatyośca | anyathā hi kulāyayajñādīnāmanutpattireva3 ārjanaṃ svatvā-
pādakamityuktaṃ caturthe | yadā’’rjanameva sādhāraṇaṃ tatra svatvamapi sādhāraṇameva
kathamanyadīyamanyasyeti nāśaṅkanīyam | pratipattivirodho’yaṃ na vastuvirodhaḥ | tatra
1. A tada vibhaktamapi madhyagāniyame prāpte
2. R. saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ
3. R. manutpatti

[p. 074]

yanmadhyagaṃ svaṃ tatrānyadīyasya sato nānyadīyatayā’vagatiḥ kintu yugapadeva sādhāraṇa-
tayā; yathā---yadātithyāyāṃ barhiriti |

athocyeta yadyapi bhavati kiñcitsvaṃ madhyagamapi tathāpi yugapat bahūn
svāminaḥ prati na śakyate svatvamāpādayitumiti nāśaktau kāraṇaṃ vācyam |

idamucyate---paramuddiṃśya tyāgaṃ kurvāṇo hi dātā parasvatvāpādanāntaṃ dadā-
tyarthamabhiniṣpādayati | na ca yugapat bahava ekenoddeṣṭuṃ śakyante anyonyanira-
pekṣāḥ anāpannasamudāyabhāvāśca | deyāstu bahavo’nyonyanirapekṣā api saṅkhyādyavacche-
dāt | gavādayaśca śataṃ sahasraṃ vā śakyanta eva yugapad‌buddhisthīkartum1 paramuddiśya ca
tyaktum | krameṇa tūddiśya tyāge tyaktasya punastyāgānupapatteḥ dvitīyādityāgānupapattiḥ |
nāpyanyonyasāpekṣān yugapaduddiśya tyāgopapattiḥ | tathābhāvena ca teṣāṃ anapekṣa-
tvāt | caitanyena hi svāmī | na caitanye’nyonyāpekṣatvaṃ puruṣāṇām | ato’nyo-
nyāpekṣāṇāṃ svāmitvaṃ na sambhavatīti pūrvapakṣa evātrādhikaraṇe na saṃvartate | samādhatte---
satyaṃ tathāpi loka2vyavahāro’yathārtho dṛśyate
 vyāmohamūlaḥ | yathā kenacinmī-
māṃsājñānavikalacetasā pariṣade dīyate’dṛṣṭārtham | yadā ca kṛpayā dṛṣṭārthaṃ mahājanāya
dīyate | teca svayameva vibhajanti vibhāgena ca karma kurvate | taddarśanāt kasya
cinmandadhiyaḥ iyamāśaṅkodayate---avibhajyāpi ca dakṣiṇā dātavyeti | tasmāt
vyāvahārādvyāmohamūlāt3 kasyacidavibhajyāpi dātavyeti bhrāntirudayate | tadapanaya-
nārthamidamadhikaraṇam |

atra codayati---yadyevaṃ yadi loke pariṣadepi dānavyavahāro’sti
tadā kathamidamupagamyate pratyekaṃ dānapadārtho4 niṣpadyate na pariṣadīti |
yāvatā lokavyavahārabalena evameva kimiti na bhavati |
 pariharati---atra
padārthavidaḥ svīkaraṇaparyantaṃ dadātyarthaṃ bruvate |
 parasvīkāraparyanto dadātyartha iti
śabdārthaṃvido vadanti | kiṃpunastatra bhavatāṃ pramāṇaṃ
 svīkāraparyanto dadātirityatreti |
1. P. buddhayā svīkartum
2. A vyavahāre
3. R vyavahāramūlatayā
4. P dadātyarthī

[p. 375]

ṣaṣṭhyantāditarebhyopi dṛśyata iti
 tral | uttaraṃ---loka evaiṣāṃ pramāṇam tyāga-
mātre dadāteraprayogāt
 | bhavatu parasvīkaraṇānto dadātiḥ tathāpi kathaṃ pariṣade dānā-
nupapattiḥ ityatrāha-pariṣade yaddattaṃ tatra pariṣadino vaktāro bhavanti pareṇa
prāpitaṃ nāsmākamidamadyāpi svamiti
 | tathā ca lokayātrāvidaḥ pariṣadyekena
kṛtaṃ dattamakṛtamadattaṃ manyante | tatkasya hetoḥ asvāmitvāditi gamyate | datte pariṣadaḥ1
svāmitvābhāvāt na svīkaraṇānto dadātiḥ pariṣadyupapadyate | pratyekadāne naitad‌dvayaṃ
pūrvoktamasti | tatra pratigrahītāro’smākamidamiti manyante | lokayātrāvidaśca
dattametaditi manyante | tena tatra svīkaraṇamasti | tasmāt padārthavidāṃ sādhunirṇayaḥ
svīkaraṇaparyanto dadātiḥ iti | sa ca pariṣadi nopapadyata iti | yāveva codyaparihārau
tāveva pūrvottarapakṣāviti | śeṣamuktam |

atra codayati---yuktametatpariṣadi svatvaṃ na niṣpadyata2 iti dānā-
nirvṛttistu kathaṃ ?
 tyāgamātraṃ dānaṃ manvānasya codyam | tadevāha---ubhayatra
dattamiti prayuñjate dātāraḥ pratigrahītāraśca
 yatpariṣade dattaṃ yacca pratyekaṃ tatrobhayatrāpi
dattamiti prayogaḥ tena pariṣadyapi dānaṃ niṣpadyata eva | tasmānnāyaṃ nirṇayahetuḥ |

atrocyate---ayameva nirṇayahetuḥ yāpūrvamuktaḥ yadi hi svīkaraṇādṛtepi
dānānirvṛttiḥ tadā tyāgadānaśabdau paryāyau syātām | paryāyatvaṃ loke prasiddham | nacā-
nyathā paryāyatvasiddhiḥ | tasmāt svīkaraṇānto dadātiḥ | tathā ca laukikāḥ
pareṇāsvīkṛte dattepi svāminā saṅkalpitamidaṃ nādyāpi dīyata iti bruvate | tasmāt
svīkaraṇānto dadātiḥ | ataḥ pariṣade dattamiti yaḥ prayogaḥ asāvupakrama eva samāpti-
paryantasya vācakaḥ prayujyate | tyāgopakramaḥ svīkaraṇaparyanto dadātiriti | tena
tyāgamātre dānopakrame lakṣaṇayā prayujyate | na kevalaṃ lakṣaṇayā tyaktamātre dattaśabdaḥ
api tu saṅkalpite’pi dadātiṃ prayuñjānā dṛśyante dattamedāyuṣmatā yatsaṅkalpitamiti |
tasmāllakṣaṇayāpi prayogadarśanāt na prayogamātrācchabdārthavyavasthitiḥ | yadi na
prayogācchabdārthavyavasthitiḥ kathaṃ tarhi ?
 uttaraṃ---yatra na sambandhyantara-
balādavagatiḥ sa śabdārthaḥ iti |
 yaḥ padārthāntarasambandhānapekṣaḥ śabdādartho-
1. R tena pariṣādināṃ
2 R. nopapadyata iti

[p. 376]

’vagamyate sa śabdārthaḥ | yaḥ punaḥ sambandhyantarabalenārtho’vagamyate sa gauṇo
lākṣaṇiko vā bhavati | atra ca devadattāya gāvo dīyantāṃ ityukte svīkaraṇānta eva
dadātyarthaḥ pratīyate parityāgamātrepyananvayo nāsti | tathāpi yeyaṃ svīkaraṇāntapratītiḥ
saiva śabdamātranibandhaneti gamyate | pariṣade dattamiti yā tyāgamātrāvagatiḥ pariṣa-
tpadārthasambandhabalāditi nāsau śabdamātranibandhanā | yadi pariṣade dānānupapattiḥ
kathaṃ tarhi pariṣade dānaṃ laukikānām
 | uttaraṃ---bhrāntyeti vadāmaḥ caitya-
vandanādivat
 | yathā nirmūlamapi caityavandanāt svargo bhavatīti vyāmohenādriyamāṇā
bhrāntyā caityavandanādikaṃ kurvate tathehāpi nāvaśyamavyāmohādeva laukikavyavahāraḥ
prāyeṇa vyāmohapūrvaka eva laukiko vyavahāraḥ | ata eva parīkṣaṇamapi nyāyavidāṃ
pravṛttam | yadi bhrāntyā loko na vyavaharati tadā kiṃ parīkṣaṇena ? tadāha---
parīkṣaṇaṃ ca nyāyavidāṃ laukikavyavahāramūḍhatayaiva pravṛttam
 | tasmāt sapramāṇa
evāyaṃ nirṇayaḥ padārthavidāṃ svīkaraṇaparyanto dadātiriti sthitam |

|| 13 || samaṃ syādaśrutatvāt || 53 ||

kathaṃ punaḥ pakṣadvayepi samākhyānirdeśaḥ; yadā samaṃ dāna yadā ca karmānurūpyeṇa
tadā kathamardhyādisamākhyānirdeśaḥ ? pariharati---abhyudaya eva dvādaśāha eveti
manyate
 | asyārthaḥ---dvādaśāhe samākhyānirdeśaḥ tena paraṃ tatraiva bhavatveṣā bhāgakalpanā
na punaḥ kratvantare bhavitumarhati | dvividhaśca dvādaśāhaḥ - satramahīnaśca | tatra yadyapi
satre dānānupapattiḥ tathāpyahīne bhaviṣyati | rāddhāntavādī tvavidhānātprākṛtaṃ
tadityāha
 | asyārthaḥ - idaṃ vākyaṃ satrabhūte, tatra na dānabhāgavidhiḥ kintu siddha-
vadbhāgasanbandhenāyamṛtvijāṃ vyapadeśo yajamāne prayuktaḥ | na hyanyathā prākṛtaṃ(tikaṃ)vinā
siddhavadabhidhānopapattiḥ | ataḥ samākhyātaḥ tathābhūto bhāga ityavagamyate |
tathābhūtabhāgasambandhaśca karmakarasyopakāraviśeṣaṃ vinā na bhavatītyupakāraviśeṣo’pi
kalpyate | tadāha---atassamākhyāta upakāravibhāgavat dakṣiṇāvibhāgaḥ |

kathaṃ punarupakāraviśeṣo’vagamyate karmaviśeṣamantareṇetyāśaṅkyāha---sarvaṃ śabdāvagamyaṃ
codanāsviti sthitam
 | yadyalpamapi karma kurvāṇo mahāntamupakāraṃ karotīti
śabdāt pratīyate kaḥśaknotyanyathākartumiti |

|| 14 || tasya dhenuriti gavāṃ prakṛtau vibhaktacoditatvāt
sāmānyāttadvikāraḥ syāt yatheṣṭirguṇaśabdena || 56 ||

[p. 377]

nanu tasya dhenurdakṣiṇeti śrūyate | tataśca sarveṣāṃ gavādīnāṃ dhenvā nivṛttiryuktā |
kathamatra saṃśayaḥ? kiṃ dhenurgavāṃ nivartikā uta kṛtsnakratudākṣiṇyasyeti | atrāha---
atra vibhāgāvibhāganibandhanaḥ saṃśayaḥ
 | yadi vibhaktā dakṣiṇā tadā goranyāpi
dakṣiṇā1 dhenurgavāṃ nivartikā | atha tvavibhaktā tataḥ sarveṣāṃ nivartiketi | vibhāgā-
vibhāgasaṃśayaḥ punaḥ kathaṃ
 ? uttaraṃ---viśeṣānavagamāt
 | ubhayathā ca vākyārthopa-
patteḥ yadi gavādayaḥ ānataya ityucyante tathāpi vākyārtha upapadyate, pratyekaṃ dakṣiṇā-
tvepyupapadyata ityarthaḥ | athānatirgavādaya ityucyate tathāpyupapadyate vākyārthaḥ yadi
samastaṃ dākṣiṇyaṃ tathāpyupapadyata ityarthaḥ | yasmādubhayathāpi vākyārthopapattiḥ tasmādvaraṃ
gavādaya ānataya ityuktam | evaṃ codakānugraho bhaviṣyati dravyāntarāṇāmabādhāt |
svatantranirdeśaśca samarthito bhaviṣyati | gauścāśvaśceti |

nanu śabdānna svatantrāvagatiḥ | apitu samuccīyamānānāmavagatiḥ |

pariharati---nāsvatantrāvagatiḥ |
 avagamyamānanirdeśārthatvāccakārasya yo’rthaḥ
pramāṇāntareṇa prāptaḥ tameva nipātā vadantītyarthaḥ | tadāha---satyanvaye
yo’rtho’vagamyate tasyāyaṃ pratinirdeśakaḥ 2na punaranvayabhājamarthamācaṣṭe |
 
caśabdābhihito nārthāntareṇānvayaṃ bhajate samuccayaśabdavaditi vaidharmyadṛṣṭāntaḥ | darśitaṃ
caitat | na cātra samāsaḥ yenetaretarayuktāḥ samāhṛtāśca dakṣiṇayā sambadhyante |
tasmādvibhaktaṃ dākṣiṇyamiti siddham | ataśca gavāṃ nivartikā dhenuḥ |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---ekaṃ dākṣiṇyaṃ, kutaḥ ? ekavacananirdeśāt
dakṣiṇeti | yadi gavādīnāṃ prādhānyena nirdeśaḥ tadā dakṣiṇāḥ iti
bahuvacanaṃ syāt | guṇavacanānāṃ āśrayato liṅgavacananirdeśāt | yathā
agnīṣomau devateti |
 asyārthaḥ---yadi pratyekaṃ dakṣiṇā syāt tadā gavādīnāṃ
dakṣiṇātvaṃ jñāpanīyam | tataśca gavādayaḥ pradhānaṃ pradhānasaṅkhyānurodhena guṇeṣu
saṅkhyāniveśa iti bahuvacanaṃ syāddakṣiṇā iti | yathā agnīṣomau devate | āgneye
saumye ca karmaṇi agnīṣomīyacodanārthābhiprāyeṇedamucyate iti matvā codayati---

1. R. gomātrasyāpi dakṣiṇātvāt
2. R. kaḥ punaḥ

[p. 378]

nanu cāgnīṣomau devatā kimidamucyate devate iti
 | pariharati---
prādhānyanirdeśapradarśanaparamida bhāṣyakārasya na punaragnīṣomīyacodanārthaparam
 |
tatrāgneḥ somasya ca devatātvaṃ guṇabhūtaṃ nirdiśyate tatpradarśanaparamidaṃ bhāṣyakārasya
vacanaṃ na punaragnīṣomīyaṃ paśumityādicodanāsiddho yo’rthaḥ tatparamidamucyate |
kathamidamavagamyate prādhānyanirdeśapradarśanaparamiti agniradevateti codanārthapare
darśanāt | manotādhikaraṇe’gnīṣomīyacodanārthaṃ pradarśayannagneradevatātvamāha bhāṣyakāraḥ |
tenāgnīṣomīyacodanāsiddhamidaṃ nocyate | anyathāhi tena saha virodhaḥ syāt |
caśabdabalena samuccitaṃ dakṣiṇetyuktamiti manvānaścodayati---nanu na caśabdo’-
nvayabhājaṃ nirdiśati
 | pariharati---kenoktaṃ nirdiśatīti | atrāpya-
nyato’nvitasyaiva1 nirdeśakaḥ
 ekavacanāntadakṣiṇāśabdena yo’vagataḥ sa eva
caśabdena dyotyate |

nanvevaṃ sati itaretarayogaḥ samāhāro vā prāpnoti
 | yadi paraspara-
sāpekṣatvaṃ dākṣiṇye tadā itaretarayogaḥ samāhāro vā prāpnoti | uttaraṃ---prāpnotu
ko doṣaḥ
 | doṣaṃ darśayati---samāsena bhavitavyam
 | pariharati---na bhavitavyaṃ
samāsena vāvacanādbhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ
 | evaṃ caikaṃ dākṣiṇyaṃ dṛṣṭaprayojanaṃ bhavati
itarathā bahūnāṃ madhye ekenaiva dṛṣṭārthasya siddhatvāt anyeṣāṃ dākṣiṇyānāṃ adṛṣṭārtha-
kalpanā |

nanu vikalpo bhaviṣyati | ucyate | upadeśābhiprāyeṇedamucyate
 |
ekasminnupadiṣṭe paropadeśo’dṛṣṭārthaḥ anapekṣitavidhiḥ syādityarthaḥ | eṣa doṣaḥ
parihṛto bhaviṣyati ekasmin dākṣiṇye | ataścaikaṃ dākṣiṇyaṃ kṛtsnasya ca dhenurnivarti-
keti siddham |

|| 15 || ekāṃ pañceti dhenuvat || 59 ||

nanu ca goṣveva saṅkhyāmātravidhiryuktaḥ ataḥ kathaṃ kṛtsnakratu-
dākṣiṇyasya nivṛttyāśaṅkā
 ? yadi ca dākṣiṇye gomātravidhiḥ saṅghyāvidhiśca
tadā vākyabhedaḥ tatrāha---atra viśiṣṭāvidhānāt pūrvapakṣaḥ
 dakṣiṇaiva goviśiṣṭāṃ
1. R. anvaṣāvagatasyaiva

[p. 379]

saṅkhyāviśiṣṭā ca vidhīyate | nanvevaṃ dakṣiṇāntare vihite kathaṃ prākṛtasya nivṛttiḥ
ityatrāha---kṛtatvātprākṛtasya kāryasya dhenvā
 | nanu te lakṣaṇā syāt kratu-
dākṣiṇyasya prākṛtasya nivṛttiḥ |

atra bhāṣyakāraḥ trivatsaśca tathā ca liṅgadarśanam
 iti sūtradvayaṃ antarā-
garbhiṇyāśrayaṇena vyākhyāya punaśca tatparityajya pūrvapakṣe liṅgadarśanaparatvena varṇitavān |
tadāha---antarāgarbhiṇīnyāyamupanyasya tasyānucitatvāt tatparihārāya
liṅgatvena sūtradvayamupavarṇitavān
 | tataśca saṅkhyāmātravidhānena rāddhāntamabhidhāya
goṣu saṅkhyāmātraṃ vidhīyata ityevaṃ rāddhāntamabhidhāya na liṅgavaiṣamyamupavarṇitam
yatpūrvapakṣe sūtradvayena liṅgamuktam tena saha vaiṣamyaṃ noktam | tatrāyamabhiprāyaḥ---
antarāgarbhiṇyaiva vaiṣamyamuktaṃ yā sā pūrvamantarāgarbhiṇyuktā tayaiva liṅgavaiṣamyamuktaṃ
yuktam | sāṇḍasya krayadravyamātranivartakatvaṃ krīṇātisambandhena vidhānāt tasya | iha
tu na dakṣiṇāsambandhena gavāṃ vidhānam | kintu gosambandhitayā saṅkhyāmātravidhānamiti |
nanu tyaktā’ntarāgabhiṇī | kathaṃ tayā vaiṣamyamuktaṃ ityatrāha---iha tvantarāgarbhiṇītvaṃ
nirākriyate na punaryuktyabhidhānam
 | antarānupraveśo nāstītyuktam |
nyāyastu śobhana eva | tena bhavati vaiṣamyamityuktam |

nanu tatrāpi trivatse’pi vayomātravidhānāt ekāṃ pañcetyanena
tulyam
 | yathā goṣu saṅkhyāmātravidhiḥ tathā’tra ṛṣabhe trivatsavidhiḥ | pariharati-
na tulyam viśiṣṭavayovacanatvāt
 | na ca dravyaṃ dravyāntare vidhātuṃ śakyate |
dravye tu saṅkhyāmātravidhānaṃ sukaram | tasmādvaiṣamyam | kevalaṃ tu vayomātravidhāne’ṅgī-
kriyamāṇe’śabdārthataivābhyupagatā bhavati |

|| 17 || prākāśau tatheti cet || 63 ||

nanu hiraṇmayau prākāśāvadhvaryave dadāti ityadhvaryusambandhe’vagamyamāne kathaṃ
kṛtsnasya kratudākṣiṇyasya nivṛttyāśaṅkā yāvatā tadbhāgavikāra evetyāśaṅkyāha---atra
viśiṣṭānuvādabhayātpūrvapakṣaḥ
 | yadyadhvaryuviśiṣṭadānānuvādena prākāśavidhiḥ syāt
tadā viśiṣṭānuvādalakṣaṇo vākyabhedaḥ syāt | tasmādadhvaryuśabdo na viśeṣakaḥ |
tena dānamātre prākāśavidhiḥ ṛtviṅ‌mātrapradarśanārthaścādhvaryuśabdo yaugikaḥ |

[p. 380]

nanu cādhvaryuprākāśasambandha eva vidhīyatāmityātrāha---ānantaryaṃ ca
vibhaktyoḥ sambandhāyogyatvena nādṛtam
 | yadyapyānantaryamanayoḥ tathāpi
dvitīyācaturthyoḥ parasparasambandhāyogyatvānnādhvaryuparityāgena prākāśayossambandhaḥ |
tataścakathaṃ tasya vidhiḥ | tasmāddānamātrasambandhitvena vidhānāt kṛtsnasya
nivartakau prākāśāviti |

rāddhāntastu---pratīyate tāvadvibhāgo dakṣiṇānāṃ na cātra dadātiḥ
padāntaraparityāgenādhvaryuparityāgena prākāśapadena sambadhyata iti pratīti-
rasti
 | na cādhvaryuḥ prākāśapadena na sambadhyate | bhavati hi kārakayorapi kriyā-
nimittakaḥ sambandhaḥ |

nanu vibhaktyartho vihanyate prathamāṣaṣṭhyau hi tadā syātām | na
dvitīyācaturthyāvityuktam
 |

pariharati---na vihanyate’thaprāptatvādvibhaktyarthasya prākāśāvadhbaryu-
sambandhinau kartavyāviti
 vihite’rthe dvitīyācaturthyorarthaprāptiḥ | yadi hi tau
tasmai dadyāt tau tatsambandhinau kuryāt | tasmādārtho vibhaktyartho vibhaktibhyāmanūdyate |

kathaṃ punaradhvaryuprākāśayoḥ sambandha ityatrāha---ānantaryasya sambandhahetu-
tvāt
 | tadidamuktaṃ asatyānantarye sambandho nopapadyata iti | pratyuta caturthīkriyā-
viśeṣasya liṅgaṃ bhaviṣyati | sā kriyā sambandhaheturāśrīyate yasyāṃ satyāṃ caturthyartho
labhyata iti dānameva kriyā kalpyate | eṣā kalpanā’dhvaryuśabdabalenāśritā |
anyathā’vivakṣitārtho’dhvaryuśabdaḥ syāt | rūḍhiṃ ca parityajya yaugikāśrayaṇe
prakārāntaraṃ cā’’ha---viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ vā’bhyupagamyatām
 | dānamevādhvaryu-
prākāśaviśiṣṭaṃ vidhīyate | na punarbhāgāvagatiḥ śakyate’panhotum tasmādadhvaryubhāgasya
pratyāmnāyo dānam | tasyāpi dṛṣṭārthatvena tatkāryakaraṇatvāditi sūktam |

|| 18 || dhenuvaccāśvadakṣiṇā sa brahmaṇa iti puruṣāpanayo
yathā hiraṇyasya || 65 ||

[p. 381]

kathaṃ punaratra brahmabhāgasya nivartako’śva ityetasmin pūrvapakṣe rukma-
lalāṭo dakṣiṇeti vākyam
 | asmin vākye sāmānyena dakṣiṇāsambandhaḥ prati-
bhāti | ataḥ kṛtsnasya kratudākṣiṇyasya nivṛttireva yuktā | uttaraṃ---viśeṣa-
sambandhe’pi sāmānyaṃ na viruddhayata ityabhiprāyaḥ
 | ṛtvigviśeṣadakṣiṇātvepi
dakṣiṇāśabdasya sāmānyavācino na virodhaḥ | ato na kṛtsnasya kratudākṣiṇyasya
prāptasya nivartako’śvaḥ darśanalobhāt bhramaḥ yathā srajamudgātre dadātītyudgātṛbhāgasya
nivartikā tathā kṛtsnasya nivartiketi dṛṣṭāntalobhāt bhramo’yaṃ pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ |
dṛśyate’sminniti darśanam |

rāddhāntastu satyaṃ sāmānyasya viśeṣeṇāvirodhaḥ ekavākyatve hetuḥ
saḥ yathā śābaleyo gauriti
 | vākyabhede punaḥ yathāpratipannameva grahītavyam |
ekavākyatvepi viśeṣānvitameva sāmānyamabhidhīyate | vākyabhede tu svatantrārthadvayā-
vagatiḥ tena prathamavākye’śvasya sāmānyena dakṣiṇātvam | ataḥ kṛtsnasya kratu-
dākṣiṇyasya nivṛttiḥ | dvitīye tu vākye tasyaiva brahmasambandha iti | yadyaśva eva
kṛtsnakratudakṣiṇā vrahmaṇa eva deyaḥ | tadā kathaṃ dakṣiṇāmantareṇa ṛtvigantarāṇāṃ
pravartanam tadāha---kathaṃ punarasmin pakṣe kartrantaraṃ pravartanīyam ?
 uttaraṃ---
nātra ārthasya bādhaḥ | arthādyena kenacitteṣāmānatiḥ vācanikaścāyaṃ pratyā-
mnāyaścintyate
 | vacanādapratihatasvārtho’bhyupāyaḥ |

nanu cārthasyaivāyaṃ niyamaḥ
 | aptyārthaḥ- parakartṛkaṃ karma dakṣiṇāmantareṇa
na śakyate kartumiti arthādyā dakṣiṇā prāptā tatraivāyaṃ niyama iti | kathamapara
ārtho’bhyupāyaḥ
 | pariharati---nāyaṃ niyamaḥ | kutaḥ ? varaṇabharaṇanimittatvā-
tparakartṛkāvagateḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---na sākṣātparakartukatvaṃ śrutau śrūyate yena sāmānye-
nānamane1 adoniyamo bhavet | kintu varaṇabharaṇavidhānādeva parakartṛkatāvagamaḥ
tenāyaṃ niyamo bhavatīti varaṇabharaṇavidhānāt prāk sāmānyenāprāpteriti sūktamuktam |
ataḥ kartrantarāpanaye sarvatra nyāyo draṣṭavyaḥ |

|| 19 || tathā somacamasaḥ || 68 ||

kena viśeṣeṇātra pūrvapakṣa ityatrāha---ekavākyatvenātra sāmānyaṃ viśeṣa-
gataṃ manvānasya pūrvapakṣaḥ
 | evaṃ hi śrūyate---audumbaraḥ somacamaso dakṣiṇā

[p. 382]

sa priyāya sagotrāya brahmaṇe deyaḥ iti | ekavākyatvāt sāmānyaṃ viśeṣaniṣṭhameva,
tena na kṛtsnanivṛttiḥ evaṃ prāptasya bādho’nyasya na kalpito bhavati |

rāddhāntastu-dakṣiṇāśabdaśravaṇāt deyaṃ sampradānaṃ ca kṛtsnasambandhi-
tayā’vagamyate
 | uktaṃ hi avibhaktaṃ dākṣiṇyamiti | yena dakṣiṇāśabda-
śravaṇāt dakṣiṇākārya eva somacamasasyāpi vidhānamavagamyate | tena na dakṣiṇāśabdo
viśeṣe’vasthāpayituṃ śakyate | dvayasya deyasya pradānātmakasya tatsambandhitvenāpi
sāmānyadakṣiṇāsambandhitvenāpyupapatterna lakṣaṇāpramāṇasti | dakṣiṇāśabdena dakṣiṇā-
bhāgalakṣaṇātve pramāṇaṃ nāstīti yuktamuktaṃ kṛtsnasya nivṛttiriti |

nanvevaṃ paurastyamadhikaraṇaṃ dūrotsāritam | naivam | iha dakṣiṇāśabdaśravaṇā-
drāddhāntaḥ | pūrvasminnadhikaraṇe tu dakṣiṇāśabdaśravaṇepi vākyabhede kṛtsnasya nivṛtti-
riti draṣṭavyam |

|| evaṃ vā ||

prayojanatvena “yadi tu brahmaṇa”
 iti pūrvatra dvayamupavarṇya evaṃ veti
vadataḥ ko’bhiprāyaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ-nirākaraṇīyābhāvāt pṛthagadhikaraṇāntaraṃ nārabdha
vyam | uttaraṃ---kāryopadeśaṃ varṇayitvā bādho’pi sayuktika eva vaktavya iti
manyate
 | asyārthaḥ- astyatra nirākaraṇīyaṃ pūrvapakṣī manyate | yadi kṛtsnaṃ dakṣiṇā-
kāryaṃ somacamasena kriyate tadā bhavet kṛtsnanivṛttiḥ | nacaitadasti somacamasena brahmaṇe
dīyamānena na ṛtvigantaravartinyānatiḥ dakṣiṇākāryaṃ sampadyate tasmānna tena kṛtsnani-
vṛttirityasyāmākāṅkṣāyāṃ kṛtsnadakṣiṇākārye somacamasasyopadeśaṃ varṇayitvā bādho’pi
sayuktika eva vaktavyaḥ | ata idamadhikaraṇamārabdham |

kathaṃ punaḥ kṛtsnakārye vidhānam | ucyate | dvividhaṃ hi kāryam | ekaṃ
kratau, aparaṃ tadaṅgeṣu | tatra kratau kāryaṃ kevalāgamādavagamyate | tatra yadyapi
somacamasena kratvantarbhūtapuruṣagataṃ kāryaṃ na kriyate tathāpi kratvaṅge kriyata iti āgama-
prāmāṇyādavagamyate | tadarthaṃ cāṅgataṃ kāryam | tena pradhānakārye somacamasena
siddhe dakṣiṇāntaranivṛttiḥ | yadyatra dakṣiṇākāryasampādakatvābhāvāśaṅkyā pūrvapakṣaḥ kathaṃ
tarhi bhāṣyakāra evamāha---“somacamaso brahmaṇa ityeṣa sambandha iti

[p. 383]

kṛtvā cintā”
 iti
 | evaṃ hi vadan kṛtvācinteti darśayati | na ceyaṃ kṛtvā
cintā | krintu pūrvapakṣabalenaivāyaṃ sambandha iti |

uttaraṃ---evaṃ kṛtvā pūrvapakṣo’tra varṇyata iti varṇanīyamidaṃ bhāṣyam
 |
nyāyabalenaiva somacamaso brahmaṇa iti kṛtvā pūrvapakṣa ityartho bhāṣyasya |

|| 21 || yajuryukte tvadhvaryoḥ dakṣiṇāvikāraḥ syāt || 74 ||

ihādhikaraṇe saptasaptadaśakāni deyāni śrutāni | tathā yajuryuktaṃ rathaṃ
adhvaryave dadātīti | ataśca bhāganiyamo’yaṃ bhavati | atha tu svatantraṃ tato bhāga-
vikāra iti | tatra pūrvapakṣī svātantryaṃ manyate | na hi svātantryye sati pāratantryaṃ
yuktamiti bhāgo’dhvaryoḥ tasyāyaṃ vikāraḥ | punardakṣiṇāvikārapakṣe[rathā]dīnāṃ dakṣiṇā-
sambandhaṃ manyate kasmai tāni deyānauti bhāvaḥ |

uttaraṃ---dīyate tebhyo’dhvaryvādibhyo ye’nye tebhyaḥ tadyugyādika-
miti śakyate vaktum
 | evaṃ pūrvapakṣite ucyate---

nāyaṃ bhāgavikāraśśakyate vaktum | aprāptasya viśeṣāvagatau bhāgavikāro
yuktaḥ | yadi rathadāne’dhvaryuḥ prāptaḥ tadā rathaviśeṣavidhānaṃ bhāgavikārāya syāt |
iha tu prāptānāmadhvaryvādīnāṃ niyamamātreṇaivopadeśasiddheḥ na bhāgāntarānuvṛttiśśakyate
vaktuṃ codakānugrahāya | saptadaśarathā ityetadvākyapūrvakamidamiti rāddhāntaḥ
tāvanmātreṇaivopadeśopapatteḥ |

nanu yadi sāmānyena prāptau satyāṃ viśeṣaśravaṇaṃ niyamamātraphalaṃ, na bhāga-
vikāraḥ tarhi prākāśāvapi niyamārthāveva syātām | aśvamedhepi sāmānyena prākāśa-
vidhirasti hiraṇmayāvadhyaryorniyamyete iti tatrāha---ye punaraśvamedhepyasya prāptiṃ
manyante na te yuktamiva manyante
 | kutaḥ ? nyāyābhidhānamātravivakṣāyāṃ
hi tadanuguṇamudāharaṇaṃ pradarśyate
 | tena santamapi sāmānyaṃ prākāśe ekakāryā-
vagamāt | pariharati---udāharaṇamātraṃ tvetaditi nādartavyam
 adṛṣṭārthamanyārtha-
mapi adṛṣṭārthamapi kṛtvodā [haraṇam] vidhimasantamiva kṛtvodāharaṇaṃ syānna punaḥ
lakṣyanirṇayāyodāharaṇadānam | sāmānyanyāyābhidhitsayedaṃ śāstraṃm | evaṃ sati

[p. 384]

ayaṃ nyāyo bhavatīti yatra sāmānyavacanaṃ nāsti tatra bhāgavikāra iti nyāyaḥ kathyate
na punaḥ granthaviśeṣābhidhitsayā na vākyaviśeṣasyārthaḥ kathyata ityarthaḥ | anyathā hi
paurvāmaryeṇānusaraṇaṃ syāt | yadi hi vedavākyavyākhyārthamidaṃ syāt tadā tathākramaṃ
vedavākyāni vyākhyeyāni nyāyaparatve tu nāyaṃ doṣaḥ | tasmānnyāyaniṣṭhaṃ vyākhyāna-
miti na doṣaḥ |

iti ṛjuvimalāyāṃ pañcikāyāṃ
daśamasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

[p. 385]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

|| daśamādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || prakṛtiliṅgāsaṃyogāt karmasaṃskāraṃ vikṛtāvadhikaṃ syāt || 1 ||

kathaṃ punaruparhomādibhiḥ nāriṣṭhahomādīnāṃ nivṛttirityatrāha---abhidhāna-
sāmānyena tulyārthatāṃ manvānasya pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ - yathaiṣāmabhidhānaṃ
tulyaṃ tathā kāryamapi bhavitumarhati | abhidhānaikye ca kāryabhedānupapattiḥ |

rāddhāntastu vidheyatvādabhidheyānāṃ kāryaikyānavagamācca
 | asyārthaḥ-
yadyapyabhidhānamekaṃ tathāpi abhidheyānāṃ vidheyatvenānyatvameva | anyatvepi ca
yadi kāryamekaṃ, syāt nivṛttiḥ | na ca kāryaikyamavagamyate | yathā ca kāryaikyaṃ
nāvagamyate tathā darśitamadṛṣṭārthānāmekakāryatvānavagamāditi | nanu ca nivīte vāso-
vinyāsasya dṛṣṭārthatvāt vinyāsāntaropamardena ca vinyāsāntarasya ātmalābhādbādha eva
yuktaḥ | tasmādadoṣaḥ |

evaṃ vā

upavarṇanāparihārāt kimabhyadhikamāśaṅkanīyaṃ yannirasyate ?
 uttaraṃ---
siddhe prakṛtiliṅgatve bhavatūpabarṇaṃnāparihāraḥ iha tu prakṛtiliṅgatvaṃ barhirityasya
sādhyata iti
 | viśeṣāntaraṃ vyaktameva barhiṣaḥ prakṛtiliṅgatvam | kiṃ paraṃ sādhyate ?

atastatrāśaṅkā | kā punarasau ? iyam
 | barhiḥ śabdena kuśo’bhidhīyate | tasya
śaramayatā vidhīyate | na punaḥ staraṇe śaravidhirayam | tatra kuśānāṃ śaramayatā
bahutaraśarayogena bhavati “śālimayā māgadhā janapadā”
 iti yathā |

kathaṃ punaridaṃ nirākriyate ?
 ittham | klṛptakāryāvivakṣayā vaikṛtā
vidhayaḥ | tatra yadi varhiḥ kārye śarāṇāṃ vidhiḥ syāt tadā kāryāntara-
kalpanā nāsti | itasthā tu kāryāntaraṃ prakalpyaṃ syāt
 | tena barhiḥ śabdaḥ
kāryasamarpaṇārtha eva | śaramayamiti ca mayaṭpratyayo nityaṃ vṛddhaśarādibhya iti
vikāre | yadyapi staraṇaṃ na vikāraḥ tathāpi śarakāryatayā tadvikāra ityupacaryate |

[p. 386]

evaṃ vā

tatra rathaghoṣeṇeti kiṃ vākyārtho nivartekaḥ uta pratyekaṃ padārtha iti
cintyate
 | ākṣipati---kimidaṃ pralapyate ?
 ekagranthenāha---nanu cobhaya-
trāpi vākyārtha eva nivartakaḥ
 rathaghoṣaśceti pūrvapakṣe’pi vākyārtha eva nivartakaḥ |
sa tu māhendrastotropākaraṇasambandhī nivartakaḥ tathā rāddhānte’pi rathasambandhī śabdo
vākyārtha eva san mantradarbhanivartaka iti pratigkṣatvābhāvāt saṃśayo’yaṃ pralāpabhūtaḥ
samādhatte---satyamevaṃ śrutābhiprāyeṇedamucyate vākyārthaḥ padārtha iti
 | ratha-
sambandhī ghoṣo rathaghoṣaśabdābhyāmucyata iti vākyārtha ityucyate | rathastu rathaśabdena
ghoṣaśca ghoṣaśabdenocyata iti padārtha ityucyate | na punarmāhendrastotropākaraṇasambandhā-
bhiprāyeṇa padārthatvaṃ vākyārthatva vā’bhimatam | etameva vivṛṇoti---kiṃ pratipadaṃ
vākyasamāptiruta samudāya iti
 | ratho’pi stotropākaraṇasambandhī ghoṣo’pīti
pratipadaṃ vākyāsamāptiḥ | yadi rathasambandhī gho/?/ṣaḥ stotropākaraṇasambandhī tadā samudāye
vākyaparisamāptiriti |

kiṃ tāvatprāptam ? pratipadaṃ vākyaparisamāptiḥ
 dvandvasamāso’yam; tena
pratipadaṃ vākyaparisamāptiḥ | kā yuktirityata āha---evaṃ svārthaparatā dvayorapi
padārthayoḥ
 | ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣe tu pūrvapadārtho viśeṣaṇatayā parārthaḥ | svārthatvaṃ parārtha-
tvādyuktam | nanu caivaṃ sati dvivacanena bhavitavyam, rathaghoṣayordvitvāt | na
bhavitavyaṃ dvivacanena dvivacanābhāvāt
 | vede hi pratipattāro vayaṃ na kartāraḥ | tena
dvivacanopadarśane dvitve’pi dvivacanamatra nāstītyavagamyate | dvivacanābhāve kathaṃ tarhi
dvandvāvagatirityatrāha---etasmādeva kāryāt dvandvāvagamaḥ
 | yadidaṃ padānāṃ kāryaṃ
svārthasya kāryatvāpādanaṃ etasmādeva dvandvāvagatiḥ ekavadbhavaṃ vā manyate | tena dvivaca-
nānutpattirna doṣaḥ |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---tatpuruṣo’yaṃ na dvandvanirdeśaḥ | kutaḥ ?
 uttaraṃ
-ekavacananirddeśāt | nanu caitasma devakāryā dvandvāvagatirityuktaḥ parihāraḥ |
nāyaṃ parihāraḥ |
 yadi tasmād‌dvandvā/?/vagatiḥ na tarhi dvandva rthasya vidhānaṃ dvandvatve
pramāṇābhāvāt kāryaṃ cetpramāṇaṃ itaretarāśrayam | dvandve’vagate kāryāvagamaḥ kāryāvaga-
mācca dvandvāvagatiriti tatpuruṣāvagatistu abhidhānādeva ekavacanānu(gyā)jñā nyāyyā |

[p. 387]

yatpunarekavadbhāvādapyupapadyata ityuktaṃ siddhaṃ tarhi samudāyavidhānaṃ
vākyārthavidhānaṃ siddham
 | bhavatvevamityatrāha---iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ dvandve dvayorapi
padārthayoḥ samāhāraparatvāt guṇabhāvo’bhyupagato bhavati
 | tatpuruṣe punaḥ
ekasyāpi tāvat prādhānyamiti nyāyyaṃ tadāśrayaṇam | prayojanamuktam
 | yadi dvandvo
bhavettadā niśśabdo’pi ratho nivartako bhavet anyasambandhyapi ghoṣaḥ | tatpuruṣe tu
rathasambaddhasya nivartakatvamiti |

|| 2 || sarvatra tu grahāmnānamadhikaṃ syāt prakṛtivat || 3 ||

atra kecidevaṃ cintayanti vikṛtiṣu graheṣu guṇavidhānāt kiṃ guṇāntaranivṛttiḥ
neti | tadāśrayaṇena codayati---nanu ca yuktaṃ bārhampatyaṃ gṛhṇātītyudāharaṇam
 |
atra grahānuvādena bṛhaspatividhirāśaṅkituṃ śakyate tenedamudāharaṇaṃ yuktam | ye punaḥ
nāmaviśiṣṭā grahā udāhṛtāḥ arkaṃ gṛhṇātītyevamādayaḥ teṣāṃ saṃjñā cotpatti-
saṃyogāditi karmāntaratvasiddheḥ nodāharaṇatvamupapadyate | kāryāntarāvagamāt
samuccayasyaiva tatra yuktatvāt
 |

paramasiddhāntī vadati---satyamāha bhagavān yadyava guṇanivṛttiścintyate
kiṃ guṇāntaravidhānena prākṛtaguṇanivṛttiruta neti yadyatra cintyate tadā
syādeva pūrvoktadoṣaḥ
 | iha tu grahā eva nivartyā uta neti cintyate | vaikṛtagrahāntara-
vidhānāt kiṃ prākṛtagrahā nivartante neti cintye | tataśca tatrāpi saṃjñāntarayukta-
grahāntaravidheḥ so’pyudāharaṇam | yadyevaṃ yadi grahāntareṇa grahanivṛttireva
cintyate tarhi guṇavidhipradarśanaṃ kimarthaṃ ?
 āgneyā grahā iti pūrvapakṣasiddhānta-
yorasadbhaūtatvāt |

uttaraṃ---bādhaprakāradarśanārthaṃ tat svārtham
 | siddhānte hi samuccayaṃ
varṇayitvā bhāṣyakāreṇa bādhaprakāro darśitaḥ | yadi grahaśabdamuccārya guṇo vidhīyate
tataḥ prākṛtā eva grahāḥ guṇavikṛtā bhaveyuḥ | guṇamātraṃ tadā bādhyeta | atha
grahasambandhenāgrahā vidhīyante tato’graho grahakāryeṃ vartata iti prākṛto nivartate | tatra
yo’yaṃ bādhaprakāraḥ pradarśitaḥ tatsiddhayarthaṃma gneyā grahā ityuktam | yāvat guṇamātra-
vidhiratra na sādhyate tāvadidaṃ bādhaprakāradarśanaṃ na ghaṭata iti guṇamātraviviṃ sādhayitvā
āgneyā iti guṇavikṛtipradarśanaṃ kṛtam, na punaḥ pūrvapakṣadṛṣṭāntatvena | yadyatra guṇa-

[p. 388]

nivṛttireva na cintyate kimarthaṃ punaḥ bārhaspatyapunarupanyāsaḥ ? kimiti
uttara vidhātavyam nātrāsyābhidheyaikyamasti
 | asyābhidheyena saha ekatvaṃ
nāsti kalpanāvirodhastvatantramityuktam | tasmāt bārhaspatyaṃ gṛhṇātīti karmāntaravidhā-
namatra | yadyatra karmāntaravidhānaṃ kathaṃ tarhi idaṃ “ekādaśa prayājān yajati”

iti guṇavidhānaṃ
 ? uttaraṃ---na saṅkhyā’nurañjiketyuktaṃ dvitīye
 | pṛthaktva-
niveśitvamātraṃ hi vidheyasaṅkhyā prabhavati kartuṃ na punaḥ svānukāri karmāvaga-
mayati yathādravyam
 | asyārthaḥ---saṅkhyāyāṃ śrutāyāṃ tāvatsaṅkhyākāni karmāṇya-
vagamyante na punasteṣāṃ saṅkhyāto rūpaviśeṣapratipattiḥ | yathā dravyaṃ svānuraktaṃ karmāva-
gamayati na tathā saṅkhyā, ananurañjakatvāt | tatra ca niyogavyāpāra eva vardhate eka
eva niyogo bahūni karmāṇi vidhatta iti | bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi kimitaravidhinetyatrāha---
śaknotyatideśe’pi kalpanāṃ viṣayaviṣayiṇīṃ vārayitum |
 asyārthaḥ
---arūpatvātsaṅkhyāyāḥ arūpasya ca yajyarthasya vidhānānupapatteḥ rūpavān yāgo
viṣayabhūtaḥ kalpayitavya | tatra cātideśo’pi kalpanāṃ niroddhauṃ śaknotīti |
yadyupadeśaprāpta eva viṣayo na viṣayāntaraṃ kathaṃ tarhi saṅkhyā śāstrāntaratve hetuḥ
ekādaśa prayājān yajatīti nedaṃ śāstrāntaraṃ syāt
 | anuvādaśāstramevedaṃ
bhavatītyarthaḥ |

pariharati---kena coktaṃ saṅkhyā śāstrāntaratve heturbhavatīti
 | bhavatye-
vetyarthaḥ | codayitā pṛcchati kathaṃ viṣayādṛte saṅkhyā śāstraṃ bhinatti
 |
samādhātā uttaramāha---viṣayābhyāsādatra saviṣayaḥ siddhayati | abhyāsaṃ vidadhadidaṃ
śāstrāntaraṃ bhavati | yathā---ekādaśa prayājāt kuryādityetāvanmātraṃ śrūyate | tatra
yathā’bhyāsaviṣayastathehāpi | yataḥ evamādiṣvabhyāsavidhānaṃ ata eva bhagavataḥ
pāṇineḥ kṛtvasuj vidhānaṃ yathārthamupapannaṃ bhavati anyathā hi pratikṣaṇama-
pavargavataḥ karmaṇā’bhyāsa eva nāsti kriyābhyāsāvṛttyasambhavāt ayathārthaṃ
pāṇinervacamaṃ syāt---“saṅkhyāḥ kriyābhyāvṛttigaṇane kṛtvasuc”
 iti tathā
stome ḍavidha namapyevamepo/?/papadyate saptadaśānyājyānītyāvṛttividhānāt stomāvṛttau iti
vārtikamupapadyate |

eva svamataṃ nibandhanakāro darśayitvā paramatamāha-atra punaḥ kecit ekā-
daśa prayājāniti parihārāntara bruvate- nāmayogāt prakṛteṣu saṅkhyāvidhānam
 |

[p. 389]

anyathāhi nāmno’nekārthakalpanā syāt | iha punarnāmāntaraṃ grahaśabde nāstīti dhātvartha-
vidhānameva | yataśca grahaśabdābhāvāt karmavidhitayā āgneyā grahā bhavantīti | yatra graha-
śabdāḥ śrūyate tatra guṇavikārassiddho bhavatīti | śaknoti hi vidheyāvagatikalpanāṃ
atideśo vārayitumiti vadantaḥ |

kecid‌bruvanta iti pūrveṇa sambandhaḥ | pariharati---tadayuktamiti bhāṣya-
vidaḥ
 prabhākaramiśrāḥ | ihāpi bārhaspatye grahaśabdaśravaṇāt nāmayogāt guṇavidhiḥ
syāt | kiñca dadhigrahe grahaśabdaśravaṇādabhidheyanirodhaḥ syāt | tataśca grahaṇe
kratvaṅgasaṃskārarūpe dadhiśravaṇāt kratāveva dravyāntaravidhāne dadhnā somasya vikāraḥ syāt
pūtīkairivābhiṣave śrutairdravyāntarasya pratinidheyasya vikāraḥ |

nanu somābhāve tena vikāraḥ tena nāsti tulyatā dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭānti-
kayoḥ
 | pariharati---nātraitadvivakṣitavyam etadabhāve sati vikāra iti na

vivakṣitavyam | kintu dravyavidhānaṃ prāpnotīti | tataśca dravyāntaraṃ vikriyate |
codakādvā prāptamupādānato vā | sambhavāditi ko viśeṣo vikārāvagatau | tasmāt sahaiva
grahaśabdena pūrvoktaḥ parihāraḥ | yadi vikṛtau karmāntaravidhiḥ kastarhi puroḍāśa-
devatayoḥ havirantara ca sautrāmaṇyāṃ vidhīyate
 | puroḍāśe’gnīṣomīye codaka-
prāpte devatāntarāṇāmindrādīnāṃ vidhānam devatāyāṃ cāgnīṣomayoḥ havirantarasya grahasya
vidhānaṃ sautrāmaṇyāmayuktam | tatra karmāntaravidhireva yuktaḥ | [uttaraṃ]---yuktamuktaṃ
paśupuroḍāśādhikaraṇe kāryapratyabhijñānāt sa evāyaṃ yāga iti
 | agneyā
grahā iti kathaṃ tarhi guṇavidhirityatrāha---āgneyā grahā iti vidheyābhidhānamati-
deśo’pi śaknoti niroddhum
 | vidheyābhidhānamatra śrūyate grahā ityabhidhānārthatvāt
tena bhāvārtho’tra kalpayitavyaḥ tatkalpanāmatideśo vārayituṃ śaknoti | liṅgamuktam
viraṇyādarśanam
 | nanu ca yaduktamādhastye’dhikaraṇe pṛthak homavidhānānna mantravidhiḥ |
samantraśravaṇe tu mantravidhireveti | tadayuktam | samantrakaśravaṇepi karmāntaravidhireva
syāt yadyasau na nāriṣṭhahome guṇavidhirityāśaṅkyāha---yuktamagnau yaduktaṃ bhāṣya-
kāreṇa yasmāt prakṛtireva hi sā | jyotiṣṭoma evāsau
 | tenopadeśataḥ
sannihiteṣu homeṣu samantrakaśravaṇe mantramātravidhireva syāt yadyasau prakṛtiḥ | kathaṃ
tarhi codakaprāpto nāriṣṭhahoma ityucyate tatrāha---naimittikaṃ tvabhyadhikam
 |
asyārthaḥ---naimittiko’yaṃ tasyaiva jyotiṣṭomasya guṇavikāraḥ | tena prakṛtirapi vikṛti-

[p. 390]

rityucyate | yathāgnihotrahomo dadhnondriyakāmasyeti dadhivikṛtau vikṛtirbhavatīti |
tena na doṣaḥ | nanvevamapi grahāṇāṃ samuccayo na yuktaḥ kintu ekakāryatvādbādha ityā-
śaṅkyāha---kāryaṃ punaḥ yāganirvṛttirgrahāṇāṃ na tāṃ grahacodanayaiveti vaiṣamyaṃ

na grahā yāgoddeśena vidhīyante | kintu yāgārthadravyasambandhena teṣāṃ kratvanupraveśaḥ |
tena grahāṇāṃ satāṃ yāganirvṛttiḥ kāryaṃ, na punaḥ grahacodanābhireva yāgasādhanatvena
vidhānam | ataśca samuccayasteṣāmiti darśitaṃ dvitīye iti vaiṣamyaṃ sākṣādyāgasādhana-
tvenopadiṣṭaiḥ dravyādibhiḥ |

|| 3 || prājāpatyeṣu cāmnānāt || 6 ||

paśuśabdaḥ kāryaṃ lakṣayatīti manvānaḥ pūrvapakṣayati
 | asyārthaḥ---sapta-
daśa prājāpatyān paśūnālabhata iti paśuśabdena prākṛtān kratupaśūn lakṣayitvā
prajāpatidevatāyā vidhiḥ tena paśuśabdaḥ uddeśyaviśeṣaṃ samarpayan prakṛtakārya eva
vidhānametaditi jñāpayati | tenābhiprāyeṇocyate paśuśabdaḥ kāryaṃ lakṣayatīti | nanu
ca saṅkhyāvidhānaṃ nopapadyate devatāyāṃ vidhīyamānāyām kuta ityāha---tadvidhāne ca
vākyabhedaḥ prasajyate yataḥ
 | pariharati---naiva saṅkhyā vidheyeti manyate
 |
kathamityāha---karmaṇi saṅkhyā naiva vidhīyate
 yathā tistra āhutīrjuhotīti |
yathā vā’sminnevodāharaṇe saṅkhyādhikaraṇe dvitīye uktam---karmagatā saṅakhyā naiva
vidhīyata iti |

rāddhāntastu satyaṃ na vidheyā saṅakhyā yadi karmāntaravidhānaṃ syāt
 |
yadi prajāpatidevatākapaśudravyakakarmavidhiḥsyāt tadā karmabhedakārikā saṅkhyā na
vidhīyeta | karmāntaratve ca paśuśabdopalakṣaṇatve nopapadyate vidheyatvenāvagamāt |
devatāmātravidhāne punaravidhīyamānārthaḥ saṅkhyāśabdo naivānvetītyasāvapi saṅkhyā
na vidhiśabdārtho vidhātavyaḥ | tataśca vākyabhedaḥ | ataḥ saṅkhyāśabdaśravaṇāt karmā-
ntaravidhānamevaitaditi sthitam | karmāntaravidhāne ca naiva prākṛtakāryāpattiravagamyate
pramāṇābhāvāt | tatra[kathaṃ]guṇavidhirāśaṅkitaḥ pūrvapakṣānaupayikatvāt ? uttaraṃ---
prakāranidarśane sati prasaṅgādidamupajātam
 | bhāṣyakāreṇa prathamaṃ bādhaprakāre
darśite prasaṅgādidamupajātaṃ prāsaṅgikamidaṃ na punaḥ pūrvapakṣaupayikamityarthaḥ | kaḥ
prasaṅgo yenedamākhyātam
 ? uttaraṃ---yadyayaṃ bādhaprakāraḥ syāt yo’yaṃ bhāṣya
-

[p. 391]

kāreṇoktaḥ
 | yadi grahaśabdamuccārya guṇo vidhīyeta tataḥ prākṛtā eva grahāḥ guṇa-
vikṛtā bhaveyuriti | na tarhi bārhaspatye samuccayaḥ tatrāpi guṇavikāra eva syāt
 |
bhavatu kā kṣatirityatrāha samuccayārthaṃ caitadudāhṛtam | bṛhaspatisave śrūyate---
bārhaspatyaṃ gṛhṇātīti | tasmānna śukravat bārhaspatye samuccayaḥ sambhavati

yathāśukre samuccayaḥ tathā bārhaspatye na sambhavati guṇamātravikāropapatteriti | kathaṃ
tarhi samuccayasiddhirityatrāha---

rāddhāntastu bārhaspatye satyaṃ na siddhayati samuccayaḥ yadi gṛhṇātī-
tyasyāṃ codanāyāṃ nāmni guṇe vā kaścidviśeṣaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ - gṛhṇa tītyasyāṃ
codanāyāṃ śrutyā grahavidhāna bhātīti rāddhāntayuktiḥ yā, sā guṇaśravaṇe nāmamātra-
śravaṇe vā’viśiṣṭā, tena bārhaspatyepi samuccayaḥ | kathaṃ punaraviśeṣaṃ manyate
yāvatā ādhastya evādhikaraṇe uktam---pṛthageva homavidhānaṃ na mantravidhā-
namiti
 | tathāpi kimityāha---yadi samantrakaṃ syāt syānmantravidhiriti
 |
bhāṣyakāreṇa mantrarahitahomadhidhānāt na mantravidhimātraṃ bhavatīti vadatā uktaṃ---
vikṛtā/?/vapi yatra guṇaḥ śrūyate tatra guṇavidhirbhavati | api ca dadhnā juhotītyevamādibhiḥ
sahāviśeṣo bārhaspatyaṃ gṛhṇātītyasya | yathā tatra homānuvādena dadhividhiḥ tathehāpi
grahānuvādena guṇavidhireva yuktaḥ |

athāyamabhiprāyaḥ---tatra dadhnā juhotītyevamādau upadeśādeva prakṛtatvāddhomasya
yuktaṃ guṇavidhānaṃ iha punaḥ kāryataḥ prāptau satyāṃ nānuvādo dhātvarthasyeti | yāvadeva
hi kāryataḥ prāptimāśritya guṇavidhirna śakyate darśayituṃ tasya jaghanyatvāditi | tadayu-
ktam | ekādaśa prayājān yajatīti kā kathā | tatrāpi tarhi yāgavidhireva syānna
saṅkhyāvidhiḥ | iṣyate ca saṅkhyāvidhiḥ | kathaṃ vā somaraso grāhyaḥ yadi kāryataḥ
prāptimāśrityopadeśavarṇanā nāsti tadā kathaṃ somaraso vaikṛteṣu graheṣu gṛhyate |
tasmānnātīva viśeṣo lakṣyate upadeśakāryataḥ prāptayoḥ |

evaṃ tāvat bārhaspatye samuccayānupapattiḥ yatrāpi śukraṃ gṛhṇātīti saguṇaśravaṇaṃ
tatrāpi nāmaśravaṇaṃ nopapadyata ityāha---śabdasya ca grahalakṣaṇārthatvaṃ na śakyate-
’pahnotum
 | grahaśabdeta prākṛtaṃ grahakāryaṃ lakṣayitvā grahāntaravidhiryuktaḥ klṛptakārya-
viṣayatvādvaikṛtānāmupadeśānāṃ prakṛtito’pyarthavatā bhavati | prakṛtau hi grahaśabdaḥ
pravṛttiviśeṣakaro na bhavati | iha tu lakṣaṇa(ke)tvena pravṛttiviśeṣakaratvenārthavān

[p. 392]

bhavati | prakṛtau hi arthāsambhavāt kiṃ kriyatām | iha punaḥ sambhavatyarthe nānarthako
grahaśabdo yukta iti pūrvapakṣa eva śreyāniti bruvate |

atrābhidhīyate - gṛhṇātyarthāviśeṣāt grahaśabdasya keyamarthāntara-
kalpanā
 | asyārthaḥ- yathā prakṛtāvaindravāyavaṃ gṛhṇātīti gṛṇhātiḥ grahaṇaṃ brūte tathā
vikṛtāvapi | (ta)yathā grahaṇaṃ graha iti vyutpattyā prakṛtau grahaśabdo mukhyārthaḥ tathe-
hāpi yuktaḥ na punaryathā grahaśabdo grahaṇamātraṃ brūte tathā kāryamityāha---prayojanaṃ
hi grahakāryaṃ grahaśabdasya nābhidheyaṃ
 grahakārye lakṣyamāṇe grahaśabdo’rthavān bhavatīti 
prayojanaṃ grahakāryaṃ grahaśabdasya nābhidheyam | abhidheyaṃ tu grahaṇameva | tasmin
vidyamāne na lakṣaṇāpramāṇamasti | abhidheyārthāparityāgena ca kāryaṃ sarve śabdā
lakṣayitu kṣamāḥ na parityajyābhiṃdheyārthamanarthakā lakṣayeyuḥ | atrāpi cāyaṃ gṛhṇātiḥ
śrūyate | abhinnārthaścāyaṃ gṛhṇātinā grahaśabdaḥ prakṛtivat | na cāyaṃ[kiṃ cāyaṃ]
gṛhṇātiḥ yadi kāryalakṣaṇārthastadā grahaśabdā’pi | atha tu vidheyārthaḥ saḥ tadā
sagrahaśabdaka eva vidheyārthaḥ na tu grahaśabdaṃ parityajya | nāmayogāccāvaśyaṃ śukrādiṣu
gṛhṇātipūrvo vidhātavtaḥ | uktaṃ dvitīye - saṃjñā śāstraṃ minattīti | na ca vidheyā-
dbhinnādṛte śāstrabhedaḥ sambhavati, anubandhasya śāstrabhedahetutvāditi siddhaṃ gṛhṇāti-
vidhānam | grahaśabdaśca tatsamānādhikaraṇo na kāryalakṣaṇārthaḥ | kintu mukhya-
vṛttireva | pūrvapakṣyāha---yuktaṃ sāmasu, barhaspatye tu kā kathā
 tatra guṇavidhānameva
yuktamityarthaḥ | sauvarṇarajatābhyāmiti prākṛtayorbhedo’dhvaryorabhāvāt yuktaḥ guṇavidhi-
parihāra iti manyate | yadi dvau syātāṃ tatra sauvarṇarājatamiti vidhiḥ syāt yathā
vārttadhnī paurṇamāsyāmanūcyete iti | na ca prakṛtau dvau sta iti | tadayuktam | aṃśvadā-
bhyayorvidyamānatvāt aśvadābhyaustaḥ tatra guṇavidhiḥ sambhavatīti | rāddhāntī tvāha---
mahimānāvityanenaikavākyatvātparihāraḥ kevala bārhaspatye parihāro vaktavyaḥ
so’yamabhidhīyate
 | dadhnā juhotīti dhātvarthavidhānaṃ guṇānuraktamiṣṭam | na kevalaṃ
guṇavidhānaṃ kriyāviṣayatvānniyogānām | yadi homavidhānaṃ tarhi agnihotrahomāddho-
māntaraṃ syādityatrāha---anurajyamānaṃ tu svarūpata evopadiṣṭamastviti kriyā-
ntaravidhānam
 | prakṛte hi juhotistasmin vartate | ata eva guṇānurakto
vidheyaḥ tadrūpasya tasya vihitatvāditi na homāntaravidhānam |

[p. 393]

nanvatrāpyevaṃ bhavatvityatrāha---upadeśaścopadeśamupodbalayati
 tad‌buddhihetu-
tvānnātideśaḥ upadeśena sannihite home punarupadeśāntare juhotiḥ śrūyamāṇaḥ tatraiva
buddhiṃ janayatītyupadeśāntarasahāyo bhavati | punaratideśaḥ kāryaniṣṭhatvāt
 kārya-
pradhānatvāt bhavatu kāryaḥ tathāpi kimityāha---na kāryaṃ karmaṇi buddhimutpādayituṃ
kṣamam atatsvābhāvyāt
 | aptyārthaḥ---atideśena prāptaṃ tāvat karaṇopakāralakṣaṇaṃ
kāryam na prākṛtakarmaviśeṣe buddhiṃ janayituṃ kṣamam | akarmasvabhāvatvāt | etaduktaṃ
bhavati---vikṛtiṣu śrutamākhyātaṃ, tena kāryabalena padārthā upatiṣṭhanānā na pratyekaṃ
bhedenopatiṣṭhante iti nākhyātaṃ viśeṣe’vasthāpayituṃ śaktuvanti |

nanvevamapi prākṛte padārthe vidyamāne kimiti padārthāntaramabhyupagamyate tena
guṇavidhireva yuktaḥ na karmāntaravidhiḥ | dadhnā juhotītyatrāpi prakṛtābhidhāne ayameva
hetuḥ sannihite home karmāntarakalpanā nopapadyata iti yo manyate tatrāha---na ca
kalpanānirodho dadhnā juhotītyekābhidheyatā agnihotraṃ juhotītyanena
 | kiṃ
tahiṃ tadbuddhayaiva | na cātra tadbuddhirasti kāraṇābhāvāt |

yadi kalpanāvirodho na bhavati yuktiḥ kimiti tatra bhāṣyakāra evamāha---
“klṛpte na kalpanopapadyata”
 iti atrāha---yatpunaḥ kalpaneti kācidvacovyaktiḥ
sā niyogaviṣayeti mantavyam
 | klṛpte’dhikāraniyoge niyogāntarakalpanā na
yukteti tatrārthaḥ | iha ca guṇāditi samuccayaḥ kratupaśubhissaha prājāpatyā
nām | liṅgamuktam
 |

|| 4 || āmane liṅgadarśanāt || 7 ||

atra saṅkhyā prakṛtiliṅgatve nopapadyata iti manvāno mantravidhānācco-
padeśopapattiṃ anūyājānāṃ nivṛttiṃ manyate
 | asyārthaḥ---āmanamasyāmanasya
devā ye
 sajātā iti tisra āhutīriti tisraḥ śabdena trayāṇāmanūyājānāṃ kāryaṃ
lakṣayitvā mantraviśeṣaktya āhutayo vidhīyante tenānūyājānāṃ nivṛttiriti pūrvapakṣaḥ |
athavā mantra evānūyājāhutiṣu vidhīyata iti prākṛtamantravattayā anūyājānāṃ
nivṛttiriti |

rāddhāntastu - na tāvat saṅkhyānūyājakāryaṃ triśabdena lakṣyeta saṅkhyā-
mātravacanastvayaṃ triśabdaḥ
 | [trisaṅkhyā] nāmāhutīnāṃ....laukikasya karmaṇa

[p. 394]

upapadyata iti na triśabdenānūyājānāmevopādānamiti na tatkārye homāntara-
vidhānam |

nanu na laukike triśabdo vartate yasmāt prakaraṇasambandhe triśabdo vartata iti
cet tadāha-prakaraṇādavaccheda iti cet
 | uttaraṃ---vidheye’pi tulyaṃ prakaraṇaṃ
yataḥ
 | śratra bhāṣyam-liṅgaṃ tvidaṃ prāptirucyatāṃ”
 iti | tadākṣipati---evamuktena
nyāyena triśabdasya prakṛtiliṅgatve niraste “liṅgaṃ tvidaṃ prāptirvaktavyā”

iti vadataḥ ko’bhiprāyaḥ ? ekagranthenāha---nanu prakṛtiliṅgāsaṃyogāduktaiva
prāptiḥ | uktāyāṃ ca punaḥ
 praśno nopapadyate | samādhatte---ayamabhiprāyaḥ
kimiti saṅkhyāvantamanṛdya guṇo vidhātuṃ na śakyate
 | ekagranthenāha---na
nāma saṅkhyā kāryaṃ lakṣayati | saṅkhyāvanta eva hi triśabdāt pratīyante
na saṅkhyāvatāṃ kāryam yajjuhoti tadāmanamasyāmanasya devā ityanena
mantreṇeti | tenānūyājeṣveva mantramātravidhānāt na samuccaya ityucyate
bhāṣyakāreṇeti sambandhaḥ
 |

parihārastu gṛhṇātivajjuhoteḥ śrutyarthatvādvidhānamityuktam
 | yathā
bārhaspatyaṃ gṛhṇātīti śrutyā grahaṇavidhānaṃ tathehāpi homavidhāta/?/meva na mantrabivānamiti
parihāraḥ | vājapeyavacca vākyabhedaḥ saṅkhyāmantrayorvidhānāt
 | yadi tu
rtriśabdārtho na vidhīyeta kintu āhutīrviśinaṣṭi tadā viśiṣṭānuvādalakṣaṇo vākya-
bhedaḥ | tasmāt karmāntarāṇyevaitāni |

evaṃ ca sati yadā karmāntaravidhānaṃ tadā vidhīyamānakarmāntarabhedāpādakatayāpi
saṅkhyā samarthitaiveti | samuccayaliṅgamuktam
 |

|| 5 || upageṣu śaravatsyāt prakṛtiliṅgasaṃyogāt || 8 ||

atra bhāṣyakāraḥ patnībhirupagāyantībhiḥ ṛtvijo nivartyante neti cintayati
tadyathāśrutamiti manvāno nyāyyaṃ sandehamāha---prākṛtamupagānaṃ vikriyate neti
cintyata iti
 | kathaṃ punaryathāśrutamayuktam ? ucyate | yadi hi prākṛte upagāne
patnīmātravidhimāśritya pūrvapakṣāṃ bhavet apūrvopa/?/gānena ca rāddhāntaḥ tadā yukto
yathāśrutassandehaḥ | yadātu (apameva) pūrvameva gānāpacayakaraṃ gānamāśritya pūrvapakṣitaṃ

[p. 395]

tadaṅgīkāreṇaiva ca rāddhāntitaṃ tadā nibandhanokta eva sandeho yuktaḥ | bhāṣyavākyāni
upagānanivṛttāvapi ṛtvijāṃ nivṛttirastītyanenaivābhiprāyeṇa varṇanīyāni |

kathaṃ punarupagānaṃ vikriyate kathaṃ vā na
 ? uttaraṃ---yadi prakṛti-
liṅgasaṃyogo’sti tadā vikāraḥ atha nāsti tadā samuccayaḥ | kathaṃ prakṛti-
liṅgasaṃyogo bhavati kathaṃ vā na sambhavati
 | uttaraṃ---yadyatrāpi prakṛti-
gānasyaiva śārīrasya upārtha upacayaścodyate tadā prakṛtiliṅgasaṃyogaḥ
avayavo hyupacayakārye vartata iti yuktam
 | athāpi gṛhṇa tivanna vikṛtau kevalo-
pārthavidhānaṃ sambhavatīti upārthaviśiṣṭadha tvarthavidhāne’pi tulyakāryatvānnivṛttiḥ
prākṛtasyopagānasya | yadyapi gānopacayakari gānāntaraṃ vidhīyate tathāpi tulyakārya-
tvāt prākṛtamupagānaṃ nivartata iti yathāpūrvaprākāśadānavidhāvapi prākṛtadānanivṛttiḥ |
kathaṃ punaḥ tulyakāryatvaṃ
 ? uttaraṃ---upānvayāt | upaśabdo hi upacaya-
kāritāṃ dyotayati
 | nanūpaśabdo’narthako dṛṣṭaḥ yathā gamanamevāpagamanaṃ tadvadava-
kimiti na bhavatītyatrāha---na hyarthe sambhavatyānarthakyaṃ śakyate kalpayitu-
mityubhayathā upārthavidhau dhātvarthavidhau ca prākṛtaṃ nivartayati
 | yadi punarvādi-
trasyopagānaṃ vāditramevopagānamiti tadā prakṛtiliṅgāsaṃyogāt samuccayaḥ | yasmādeta-
sminnubhayavidhepi pakṣe vāditropacayaḥ kāryaṃ na sāmagānopacayaḥ | yadyevaṃ yadyubhayathāpi
samuccayaḥ tadā rāddhānte dhātvarthopanyāsatantrepi gānaśabdo dṛśyate ityetadeva
vaktavyam | tāvataiva rāddha ntasiddheḥ yadā hi kvopa/?/yujyeta
 | bhāṣyakāraḥ
kimiti dhātvarthavidhānamāhetyarthaḥ | dhātvarthavidhānepi hi prakṛtiliṅgasaṃyoga ukta eva |
tasmādvāditragānepi gānaśabdo vartata iti tadeva codakānugrahāya vaikṛte vāditre
upacayaḥ kāryamiti śabdāvirodhenāṅgīkriyata iti |

samādhatte---astyatrābhiprāyaḥ yadi kevalopasargārthaścodyate tadā na
prākṛtādgānāt anyacchakyate vaktum
 | asyārthaḥ---vāditrameva gānamityuktaṃ
tanna siddhayati yadyupasargārthaḥ kevalo vidhīyate | tadā śrau[tagāna] vidhānamityukte
vākyena prākṛta evopagānopacayaḥ[kṛtaḥ] ihāpi tathaiva mā prasāṅkṣīt ityāghāṭalikādi-
vidhiḥ | dhātvarthavidhāne punaḥ kāryāntareṇāpyupārthānvayaḥ sambhavatīti nāvaśyaṃ prākṛta-
meva kāryaṃ gānopacayakāri | dhātvarthe vidhīyamāne upacayaḥ kāryabhūta āśrayaṇīyaḥ |

[p. 396]

na ca sa prākṛta eva, dikṣu dundubhayo nadanti ityatrāpi upacayasya kāryasya
sambhavāt | sa eva codakānugrahāyāśrīyate | tadidamuktaṃ vāditrasyopagānamiti |

kathaṃ tarhi upasargārthavidhānepi rāddhānto darśita ityatrāha---athāti prakṛta-
tvādva ditrasya tasyāpi gānaśabdavācyatvāt vāditra evopacaye
 upagāyati-
rvartate tadā vāditrasya upārthavidhānepyetatsiddhayati vāditropagānamiti | tasmāt
sarvaṃ sūktam | pūrvapakṣottarapakṣāvapi viṣayābhidhānenaivopavarṇitāvityuparamyate | tatra
śārīrameva gānaṃ na vāditramiti pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ | na hi vādayan gāyatītyucyate |

rāddhāntastu vāditramapi gānamucyate, svaraviśiṣṭābhivyaktirhi gānaṃ
sā ca vāditre’pi vidyate tadapi gānamiti
 |

|| 6 || saṃskāre cānyasaṃyogāt || 10 ||

atra añjata ityupadeśārthatvāt tulyaṃ kāryaṃ manvānasya pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ
 |
gauggulavena prātassavane’ñjate ityañjā/?/syeha lepanamātrameva kāryam | navanītasyāpi
lepa/?/nameva kāryamiti manvānaḥ pūrvapakṣī navanītasya nivṛttimāha |

nanu dīkṣākāle navanītenābhyañjanaṃ sutyākāle ca gauggulavaṃ kaṣāyāñjanaṃ
iti kathaṃ tasya tena nivṛttirityatrāha---tulyakāryatve ca na kālānyatvamupaghāte
hetuḥ
 kālānyattvaṃ nāvanītasya dhopaghāte hetuḥ prayājavat | yathā kālāntarīyairapi
pāśukaiḥ prayājaiḥ kṛte kārye pauroḍāśikaprayājanivṛttiḥ tathehāpītyabhiprāyaḥ |

rāddhāntastu nopadeśārtha eva prakṛtau kāryaṃ nāvanītasyābhyañjanasya |
kiṃ tarhi vaiśadyamaṅgānāṃ snehanena navanītena kṛtam
 | iha gauggulave kaṣāye
snehanaṃ kāryaṃ nāstīti kāryāntarakalpanā | tacca kāryaṃ dṛṣṭamadṛṣṭaṃ vā yathārthaṃ
arthasvarūpānatikrameṇa kalpanīyam | ataḥ kālāntarātsamuccayaḥ na tu kāryānyatvameva
samuccayahetuḥ |

kimidaṃ kālānyatvātsamuccaya ityucyate
 | samādhatte-ekakālīnānāṃ
hi kāryabhede’pi kālāntarābhāvāt bhavati bādha iti pradarśitaṃ bhavati
 |
bhinnakārye api navanītāñjanagauggulavāñjate vaiśadyavirūkṣakaraṇe samānakāle virudhyete

[p. 397]

iti vā etat tyāditi darśayituṃ kālabhede uktaḥ | navanītāñjane sthite gauggulavāñjanaṃ
paścāt virūkṣīkaraṇamiti |

|| 7 || ācchādane tvaikārthyāt prākṛtasya vikāraḥ syāt || 13 ||

atra kaupīnācchādanārthena vāsaso dṛṣṭārthatvāt bādhaṃ manyate pūrva-
pakṣavādī
 | tārpyādibhiḥ kaupīnasya guhyadeśācchāditatvāt ahataṃ vāso nivartate |
dṛṣṭaṃ caitat | idameva kāryaṃ vāsaso’pi kaupīnācchādanamiti rāddhāntavādī manyate |

uktaṃ vaikṛtamantrepadeśe ṣaṅ‌bhirdīkṣayatītyatra nābhyāsomantrāṇām upadeśārtha-
vattvāya hi te kriyante | kāryaprayuktatvena kāryamastītyetāvatā na kriyante | kintu
upadeśamyārthavattvāyeti | evamatrāpyupadeśasyārthavattā karaṇīyā upadeśataḥ prāpteḥ
svārthādīnām | paridhānaṃ copadeśārthaḥ |

|| 8 || sāmasvarthāntaraśruteravikāraḥ pratīyeta || 16 ||

sāmāntarāṇiṃ nivartanta iti | ko’bhiprāyo’syāścintāyāḥ ?
yāvatā yuktaiva nivṛttiḥ stutau sādhanāntaravidhānena sādhanāntarasya
 |
uttaraṃ---nātra kiñcit prākṛtaliṅgaṃ śrūyate yena prākṛtanivṛttiḥ | stutyartha-
syānavagamāt
 | yadi hi stutimuddiśya sāmāntaravidhānaṃ syāt syāt sāmāntara-
nivṛttiḥ | na caitadasti | tasmāt parisāmādāvanuccāraṇamātraṃ vidhīyamānasāmānta-
rāṇāmadṛṣṭāyeti samuccaya eva yukta iti pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ | rāddhāntastu uktaṃ tāvatsāmnāma-
viśeṣeṇa saṃskārakatvam tena krauñcādayo’pi saṃskārakārakā eva | sāmānyena cāyaṃ
nyāyaḥ---svabhāvataḥ kāryasiddhau sambhavantyāṃ padārthasvabhāvatyāgena kāryāntarakalpanā
na yukteti | evaṃ cākṣarābhivyaktyā stutyabhinirvṛtyā cādṛṣṭe upadeśārthavattve
sambhavati nādṛṣṭakalpanā dravyāṇāṃ sambhavati
 | asyārthaḥ---sāmnāṃ hi
sādhanatvaṃ guṇābhidhānasamarthākṣarābhivyaktidvāreṇa | tacca svabhāvāparityāgāt dṛṣṭenaiva
vyāpāreṇa | uccāraṇamātreṇa tvadṛṣṭārthatvam | svabhāvaparityāgena hi tatrākṣarābhi-
vyañjakatvamupayogi | tasmāt stuti [rā]śrīyatām na punaruccāraṇamātreṇādṛṣṭam |
nanu ca kāryapravṛttirasti
 | asyārthaḥ---yadyapi sāmnāmupadeśastathāpi kāryato’pi
sāmāntarāṇāṃ prāptatvāt taissaha samuccaya eva nyāyyaḥ | na bā/?/dhaḥ | uttaraṃ---

[p. 398]

bāḍhamasti kāryāt prāptiḥ
 | sā tu kāryasiddhāvasatyām | iha tu upadeśata eva
siddhaṃ kāryamiti prati(nivṛttitaṃ)nivartitaṃ vidhestaṃ prati prayojakatvam | tasmād‌bādho
yuktaḥ | ayaṃ ca sarvatra nyāyo draṣṭavyaḥ prākṛtapadārthaprayuktibādhena vaikṛtaṃ tatkārye
sādhanāntaraṃ vidhīyata iti |

nanvevaṃ tarhi parisāmasvapi stutireva syāt ityatrāha---parisāmasu punarupa-
deśāntare vidhānāt dṛṣṭaṃ na sambhavatītyadṛṣṭameva kāryamiti yukta
 upadeśāntare
kārye stuteranyasmin kāryāntare vacanenaiva vidhānāt yuktaṃ parisāmnāmadṛṣṭaṃ kāryam |
yadasmābhiruktaṃ tadidamuktaṃ devadattavadyajñadattastaileneti | kathamanenaitaduktamityatrāha---
svabhāvāparityāgena tailaṃ snehanarūpaṃ na kāryāntare vartate ityartho’sya
tailadṛṣṭāntasya
 | tathāpi svabhāvāparityāgenāpi upadeśe siddhe sarveṣāṃ bhāṣyakārīye
sandehe kāraṇasandehamāha-atredānīṃ sthite sāmāntarabādhakatve cintyate | kiṃ

kāryaviśeṣājñānāt stutimātrakāryavijñānāt (sarvakārya) prākṛtasāmakāryārthatvāt
vaikṛtānāṃ sāmnāmupadeśasya upadeśārthanivṛttimātramevānuṣṭhānenopadeśārthaḥ svīkṛto bhavati
tāvadevānuṣṭhānaṃ kāryamiti |

nanu cāyamasmadukto’rthaḥ upadeśārthanirvṛttimātramevānuṣṭhānamiti
kiṃ cintyate nirṇīṃtatvāt
 |

pari/?/harati---satyamuktam kāryāviśeṣānavagamādupadeśamātramevāpratipannaṃ
manyate
 | yathā ṣaḍ‌ni/?/rdīkṣayatītyādau kāryaviśeṣo jñātaḥ tatheha kāryaviśeṣajñānaṃ nāsti
tenopadeśārtha eva na pratipannaḥ | tena sāmānyupadiṣṭāni tāvatāṃ prayoge yatkāryaṃ
tadetadeva tatkāryaṃ na kāryata upadeśanirṇayaḥ parivāsasyapyupapadyata iti na kāryārthaṃ
kāryataḥ prāptamahataṃ vāso na vihanti kaupīnācchādanādanyad dṛṣṭamapi kāryaṃ sambhavati |
paridhānasya sambhavāduparivāsasyapi | upadeśe hi śabdārthānuṣṭhānaṃ tataḥkāryam | na
kāryato’nuṣṭhānaṃ yathā kāryataḥ pravṛttānāṃ kāryato’nuṣṭhānaṃ na tathā sākṣādupadiṣṭānāṃ,
kintu teṣāṃ kāryaṃ kalpyam | na ca śabdārthaviśiṣṭasya dadhātyarthasya kaupīnācchādanenaiva
kāryam kāryāntarasyāpyuparivāsasi darśanāt | tenānaikāntikatvāt na kāryaviśeṣā-
vadhāraṇam | darśanamuktam
 | adṛṣṭārthe hi noparivāsasāpi saha samuccaya iti
vāsāṃsīti bahuvacanaṃ liṅgam |

[p. 399]

|| 8 || sāmasvarthāntaraśruteravikāraḥ pratīyeta || 16 ||

atra kālavidhānameva manvāno na śaknoti pūrvapakṣayitum | vikṛtau
bhāvārthavidhānāt | darśanagrahādhikaraṇavadapratipakṣatvācca bhāvārthavidhānasye-
tyuktam
 | anekaguṇaśravaṇāccetyapi draṣṭavyam | ataḥ stutyantaramevedaṃ sāmaviśiṣṭaṃ
vidhīyate dānavaddānāntarakārye, stutirapi stutyantarakārye vartata iti pūrvapakṣayati---
samānajātīyatvāt stutyantarakārye vartata iti śakyate vaktumiti prākṛta-
stutinivṛttiḥ
 |

rāddhāntastu syādetadevaṃ yadi guṇamātravidhiḥ stāt krauñcādivat
iha tu viśiṣṭavidhānam
 | anyathā yadi viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ nāśrīyate tadā yathāśrutānva-
yānupapattiḥ deśaviśiṣṭasyāpyanvayāvagamāt | tataḥ kiṃ ? yadi viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ na
stutyantarakārye vartata iti śakyate vaktuṃ adṛṣṭārthatvāt stutīnām | dānena dṛṣṭārtha-
tayā yukto dānāntareṇa vaikṛtena prākṛtadānapratyāmnāyaḥ |

nanu ca bhāṣyakāreṇa sāmnāṃ nivṛttirityuktaṃ na stutyantareṇa
stutīnāṃ nivṛttirityuktam | ato bhāṣyavirodhaḥ
 | uttaraṃ - phalatastadva

[canam] |

|| atra pātaḥ ||

[p. 400]

ṛjuvimalāyāṃ

|| dvādaśādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||

atra bhāṣyakāraḥ phalamāha---‘ekādaśādhyāye tantrāvāpalakṣaṇaṃ vyākhyātam |
adhunā dvādaśe prasaṅgalakṣaṇaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ’
 taccaitadvṛttavartiṣyamāṇānukīrtanaṃ sambandha-
kathanārtham | tatra pṛcchati---kaḥpunaranayorlakṣaṇayoḥ sambandhaḥ yatkathanārthaṃidaṃ
bhāṣyamiti
 | pṛcchataścāyamāśayaḥ tantre’pi sakṛdanuṣṭhānaṃ prasaṅge’pi | tadanayorna hetu-
hetumadbhāvassambhavati | na ca sambandhāntarasya śaṅkāpi yukteti | uttaraṃ---hetuhetu-
madbhāvakṛta eva sambandha iti
 | kuta ityāha---sthite hyekaprayogavacanāda-
bhinnākāśakālakartṛtve tantramanuṣṭhānamagṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāditi idānīṃ gṛhya-
māṇe viśeṣe anyato’pi prayogasiddheḥ deśakālakartṛtvābhede kiṃ tantramevā-
ṣṭhānaṃ uta bhedeneti cintyate
 | asyārthaḥ-darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetyeka-
smāt prayogavacanāt prayogaviviśeṣabhūtānāṃ deśakālakartṝṇāmabhede sthite prayājādī-
nāmaṅgānāṃ tantramanuṣṭhānamiti yat tadagṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāt na prayājādyanuṣṭhānasya viśeṣo
gṛhyate asya pradhānasyedaṃ nāsyeti |

etaduktaṃ bhavati---yadyekaḥ prayogavacano na syāt tadā prayogacodanāśeṣabhūtā
deśakālakartāro’pi bhedena sambadhyen sauryāryamaṇaprājāpatyeṣviva; yadā tveka
eva prayogavacanaḥ tadā deśatvaṃ deśasya kālatvaṃ kālasya kartṛtvaṃ karturna bhidyate |
tataśca prayogānubandhabhūtānāṃ teṣāṃ bhedādeka eva prayogo gṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāditi |

idānīṃ paśvarthamanuṣṭhānaṃ na puroḍāśārthamiti gṛhyamāṇepi viśeṣe anya-
to’pi prayogasiddheḥ anuṣṭhānasyāgṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāt deśakālakartṛtvābheda-
sādhakādanyataḥ tantramadhyavidhānādekaprayogasiddheḥ deśakālakartṛtvābhedepi
kiṃ tantrameva sakṛdevānuṣṭhānaṃ prayājādeḥ uta bhedeneti cintyate
 yataḥ tato
hetuhetumadbhāvo’stīti | etacca grahaṇavākyam |

etadeva codyaparihāradvāreṇa vivarītuṃ codayati---kaḥpunaratra pūrvasya
hetvarthaḥ | heturhi bhavan kañcidarthamupakurvan heturbhavati
 | atra pūrvasya ko
hetvarthaḥ | ekadeśyāha---ekadeśakālakartṛtvasiddhiḥ
 | deśakālakartṝṇāmekatā

[p. 401]

pūrvādhyāye siddhayati | bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi kena hetvarthatetyatrāha---tasyāṃ hi satyāṃ
gṛhyamāṇe viśeṣe sakṛdanuṣṭhānam
 |

yadyapi krama(saṅkhyā) [samākhyā ?] apyaviśeṣeṇa gṛhyante viśeṣa[ta]ḥ
pāśukamidamanuṣṭhānamiti tathāpi deśakālakartrekatayā sakṛdanuṣṭhānam | nanvevamapi kathaṃ
pūrvasya hetutvamityatrāha---sā caikaprayogavacanatayā sādhitā’dhastāt
 |

ākṣeptā’ha---nanvatrāpi tulyam
 | asyārthaḥ-yadidaṃ ekaprayogavacanatayā
deśādīnāmekatvasādhanaṃ tattulyamatrāpi | jyotiṣṭoma eva prayogaḥ paśoḥ puroḍāśasya
ca jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajetetyanena pradhānasyāṅgānāṃ ca prayoga ucyata iti |
upadeśāt jyotiṣṭomāṅgasya paśupuroḍāśasya ca paśvaṅgasyaikaḥ prayogavacanaḥ | pāśuka
eva vā puroḍāśasya tadaṅgatvāt
 | yadvā pāśuka eva prayogavacanaḥ agnīṣomīyaṃ
paśumālabhata iti puroḍāśasthaprayogaṃ vakti | puroḍāśasya paśvaṅgatvāt sāṅgasya ca
pradhānasya codanāyaiva prayukto’yaṃ iti codayati---sa eva tadaṅgamapīti
 | yata
evamekaprayogavacanatā’trāpi tannivandhanaśca deśādīnāmabhedaḥ tannibandhanaṃ ca sakṛdanuṣṭhānaṃ
ataḥ tantraprasaṅgyoḥ bheda eva durlabhaḥ hetuhetumadbhāvastu dūrata eva nāstītyāha---
tasmānnānayorhetuhetumadbhābassambhavati
 |

idānīmākṣeptā tantraprasaṅgyoḥ bhedamāśaṅkate---athātrāṅgaprāptirbhinnaniyoga-
labhyā prasaṅge | tantre tu punarekaniyogalabhyā iti bhedaḥ
 | kiṃ tau paśuniyoga-
puroḍāśaniyogāvatideśataḥ prāpayataḥ prasaṅge, tantre punareka eva darśapūrṇamāsādhikāra-
niyogaḥ prayājādikamaṅgajātaṃ prati karaṇaṃ prāpayati | tenaikaniyogaprāpitānāṃ sakṛda-
nuṣṭhānaṃ tantram | bhinnaniyogaprāpitānāṃ tu prasaṅga iti viśeṣaḥ | nirākaroti---
ayamapi viśeṣo naiva śakyate vaktuṃ yasmāt jyotiṣṭomaniyogalabhyaiveya-
mubhayoritikartavyatā
 | asyārthaḥ - yadyapi prāpakabhedaḥ tathāpi prayojakabhedastā-
vannāsti | prasaṅgepi jyotiṣṭomaniyogasya yattantraṃ prayuktiḥ tallabhyaiva tatprayojyaivobhayoḥ
paśupuroḍāśayoritikartavyatā | prayojakaviśeṣanibandhanaścānuṣṭhānaviśeṣo nyāyyaḥ tadadhīna-
tvāttasya | tantraprasaṅgau ca anuṣṭhānasya viśeṣau | tenobhayatrāpi prayojakaviśeṣā-
bhāvānnānuṣṭhānaviśeṣo yuktaḥ | evameva cāyaṃ grantho vyākhyeyaḥ | na jyotiṣṭomasya
prāpakatvamāśritya kṛtsnavidhānādhikaraṇe tasya prāpakatvaniṣedhāt | na cānena prasaṅga
tantrayoḥ viśeṣeṇa hetuhetumadbhāvasiddhiḥ |

[p. 402]

punarapi tantraprasaṅgayoḥ viśeṣamāśaṅkate---athedamucyate guṇādivikārepi
tulyamanuṣṭhānamiti prasaṅgaḥ
 | yadyapi vṛttādinā saṅkhyayā ca ekādaśādikayā
vikṛtāḥ padārthāḥ tathāpyekamevānuṣṭhānamityevaṃrūpaḥ prasaṅgaḥ | vikāre tu tantramiti kiṃ
tantraprasaṅgayorviśeṣaḥ iti | nirākaroti---yadyapyayaṃ viśeṣaḥ tathāpi hetuhetu-
madbhāvo vaktavyaḥ
 | sa evāha---kaḥ khalu
 kṛtasya vikāre hetubhāvaḥ |

nanu kimatra hetuhetumadbhāvenetyatrāhaṃ-na ca hetuhetumadbhāvaṃ vinā idaṃ vṛttaṃ
idaṃ vatiṃṣyamāṇamiti yuktaṃ vaktuṃ
 sambandhāntarasya[ā]vidyamānatvāt | ākṣeptaiva
śaṅkāmāha---athāṅgānāṃ sakṛdanuṣṭhānalakṣaṇassambandha ucyate
 | asyārthaḥ-
tantrepi sakṛdanuṣṭhānaṃ prasaṅgepi | tena sakṛdanuṣṭhānarūpatantraprasaṅgāt prasaṅgaścintyata
iti | tena prāsaṅgikassambandha iti | nirākaroti---tathāpi vṛttagrahaṇamakiñci-
tkaram viparītasyāpi prasaṅgasya sambhavāt prasaṅgaprasaṅgena tantrameva
kasmānnocyate
 tena tantre vṛtte prasaṅga iti vṛttagrahaṇamakiñcitkaram | tattu evamupayogi
syādyadi vṛtta eva tasminnidaṃ vicāryate | yāvatā vartiṣyamāṇepi tasminnidaṃ śakyameva
cintayitum |

dūṣaṇāntaramāha---taddhetūpajīvanaṃ cātra lakṣyate
 | 1taddhetūpajīvanañca
prasaṅge kṛte | kasyāsau taddheturityatrāha---abhinnadeśakālakartṛtvamiti
 | tantre
prasaṅge ca tadeva heturiti samānahetukatayā parasparaṃ hetuhetumadbhāvo nāstītyabhiprāyaḥ |
upasaṃharati---tasmādvaktavyo’tra hetuhetumadbhāvaḥ
 |

samarthayitā āha---ayamabhidhīyate
 | hetuhetumadbhāvadeśakālakartṛśāstrārthā-
viśeṣeṇa hi tantre sakṛdanuṣṭhānāt tantreṇa kāryasiddhiruktā anuṣṭhānoddeśaviśeṣāgrahaṇā-
diti | same darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajeta paurṇamāsyāṃ paurṇamāsyā yajeta darśapūrṇamāsayorya-
jñakratvoḥ catvāraṛtvija iti yāni deśakālakartṝṇāṃ śāstrāṇi teṣāṃ yo’rthaḥ
tasyāviśeṣeṇa prayājādīnāṃ sakṛdanuṣṭhānāt kāryasiddhiḥ tantramityanuṣṭhānoddeśe anuṣṭhāne
yaddeśaḥ asmā idamanuṣṭhānamiti tasya viśeṣāgrahaṇāditi tantre tantralakṣaṇe uktam |

idānīmanuṣṭhānoddeśe viśeṣagrahaṇepi deśakālakartṛśāstrārthaviśeṣāt
aṅgakāryasya kimanyatvaṃ bhavati neti saṃśayaḥ
 | yadi hi aṅgakāryasya bhedo
1. R taddheturupa

[p. 403]

bhavati ekasyārthena kṛtamaṅgajātaṃ aparasyopakartuṃ na śaknuyāt tadā bhedenāṅgānā-
manuṣṭhānaṃ bhavati | atha na bhinnaṃ kāryaṃ tadā sakṛdanuṣṭhānamiti anuṣṭhānabhedābhedena
kāryasya bhedābhedau cintyete |

nanvevamapi pūrvasya kathaṃ hetuhetumadbhāvaḥ ityatrāha---satyaṃ uddeśyāviśeṣeṇa
sarvārthamanuṣṭhānamiti sthitam | kāryāviśeṣastu kartrādyabhedanibandhana eva
iti anuṣṭhānaviśeṣeṇa sādhitastantre prasaṅge hetutāṃ pratipadyata iti so’yaṃ hetu-
hetumadbhāvaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ---satyaṃ tatroddeśyāviśeṣeṇa sarvārthamanuṣṭhānamiti sthitam |
kāryāviśeṣastu yastatra kartrādyabhedanibandhana eva | kartrādīnāṃ hi prayogacodanāśeṣatvāt
sapta[rva]padārtheṣbabhedena kartṛtvaṃ deśatvaṃ kālatvaṃ ca śāstrārthaḥ | tatra yeṣāṃ padārthānāṃ
svarūpabhedaḥ tatra pradhānabhedādaṅgabhūtānāṃ kartrādīnāṃ kartṛtvādikamāvartate pratipradhānamā-
vartante guṇā iti nyāyāt | ye tu padārthāḥ na svarūpato bhinnāḥ tān prati kartṛtvā-
dikaṃ na bhidyata iti balāt sakṛdevānuṣṭhānam | anyathā hi kartṛtvādikamapi bhidyate |
tataśca sakṛdanuṣṭhāne niścite kāryamapyaṅgānāṃ tantrameveti niścīyate anuṣṭhitānāṃ
kāryāsādhakatvāt | pratipradhānaṃ cāṅgānāṃ viniyogādavaśyābhyupagamanīyatvāt kārya-
syeti | ata idamucyate---kāryādyabhedastu kartrādyabhedanibandhana eveti yo’yaṃ
kāryāviśeṣastantre tantralakṣaṇe anuṣṭhānāviśeṣeṇa sādhitaḥ anuṣṭhānoddeśāviśeṣāt
kartrādyabhedaḥ tadabhedādanuṣṭhānābhedaḥ anuṣṭhānābhedāt kāryābheda iti matvā uktamanu-
ṣṭhāna[ā]viśeṣeṇa sādhitastantre kāryāviśeṣa iti |

sa cāyaṃ kāryāviśeṣaḥ aṅgakāryaṃ tantratā tantralakṣaṇena sādhitā | sati(tī)-
ca prasaṅge paroddeśānuṣṭhitenāpi paropakāralābhe niścetavye hetutāṃ pratipadyata iti
so’yaṃ hetuhetumadbhāvaḥ |

tadeṣa saṅkṣepārthaḥ---paśūddeśenānuṣṭhitānāmapyaṅgānāṃ yadavāntakāryaṃ tat paśupuro-
ḍāśayordvayorapi sādhāraṇamiti sthite aṅgāvāntarakāryatantratve tantralakṣaṇe siddhe parārthā-
ṣṭhitairapi padārthaiḥ parasyopakāralakṣaṇakāryasiddheḥ udāsīnā pradhānāntaraprayuktirbhavatīti
prasaṅganiścayo jāyata iti bhavati tantrasya prasaṅgaṃ prati hetubhāvaḥ | dṛṣṭaṃ hi parārthānu-
ṣṭhitasyāpi paraṃ pratyupakāratvamiti |

[p. 404]

tatra bhavanti ślokāḥ---

paroddeśapravṛttānāmaṅgānāṃ yatparān prati |

mahopakārasāmarthya/?/ sa prasaṅga udāhṛtaḥ || 1 ||

aṅgāvāntarakāryāṇāṃ tantratā tasya bodhikā |

hetutā hetumattā ca tena tantraprasaṅgyoḥ || 2 ||

kartṛtvādyaviśeṣeṇa kartrādīnāṃ prasiddhayati |

anuṣṭhānasakṛdbhāvaḥ tena kāryasya tantratā || 3 ||

anuṣṭhānāviśeṣeṇa tantre kartrādyabhinnatā |

tantramadhyanipātena prasaṅge sā ca siddhayati || 4 ||

ekaprayogatāsiddhiḥ tantramadhyavidhānataḥ |

aikyena ca prayogasya kartrādīnāmabhinnatā || 5 ||

yata eva tantre anuṣṭhānaviśeṣāgrahaṇāt kāryāviśeṣaḥ ata evātra pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ anuṣṭhāne
viśeṣāgrahaṇāt kāryaviśeṣo nāstītyevaṃrūpaḥ | paśūddeśena hi kriyamāṇāḥ prayā-
jādayaḥ paśūpakārānuguṇamevāvāntarakāryaṃ kurvanti iti yuktam | tena puroḍāśārthaṃ
bhedenānuṣṭhātavyā iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntastu---satyaṃ anuṣṭhāne viśeṣo gṛhyate na punaḥ deśakālakartṝṇāṃ
viśeṣaḥ tatkṛtaścāyaṃ kāryāviśeṣaḥ
 | asyārthaḥ - yadyapyuddeśe viśeṣo gṛhyate
tathāpi deśādīnāṃ deśatvā(nya)dyabhedāt sakṛdanuṣṭhānena bhavitavyam | tataścāṅga-
kāryeṇāpi sakṛdevetyaṅgāvāntaratantratā tāvadgamyate | tataśca paramopakāro’pi tad‌dvāra-
katvāt puroḍāśasyāpi tena siddhayati yathā prāsādoddeśenāpi kṛtasya pradīpasya yadavāntara-
kāryaṃ prabhālakṣaṇaṃ tasya sādhāraṇatvāt gamanakāryajanakatā rājamārgepi bhavati |
tathehāpi praṣṭavyam |

yadyanuṣṭhānaviśeṣagrahaṇe’pi kāryāviśeṣaḥ kimarthaṃ tarhi tantre anuṣṭhāna-
viśeṣāgrahaṇaṃ prayatnenocyate
 | uttaraṃ---kartrādyaviśeṣasiddhaye
 | yatra hyanuṣṭhāne
viśeṣo gṛhyate tatra kartrādīnāṃ kartṛtvādikaṃ bhidyate yathā saṃsparśiṣu [saṃsarpiṣu ?]
yathā vā āgneyakṛṣṇagrīvayoḥ | tena kartrādīnāmabhedasiddhayarthaṃ viśeṣāgrahaṇaṃ sādhyate |

[p. 405]

yadi viśeṣāgrahaṇe kartrādīnāmabhedaḥ iha tarhi tadabhāvāt viśeṣāgrahaṇā-
bhāvāt kartrādyabhedaḥ [na]prāpnoti
 | tataśca kutaḥkāryāviśeṣaḥ kutastarāṃ ca prasaṅgaḥ
iti | pariharati---anyataḥ prayogaikyasiddheḥ na prāpnoti kartrādīnāṃ bhedaḥ |
anyataḥ tantramadhya
vidhānāt prayogaikyaṃ paśupuroḍāśayoḥ avagamyate | ekaprayogatve
ca prayogaśeṣāṇāṃ kartrādīnāmabhedo’vagamyate | ataḥ aṅgeṣu siddhepi prayogaviśeṣagrahaṇe
anyataḥ kartrādyabhedalābhāt kāryāviśeṣāt tantravatprasaṅgasiddhiriti sthitam |

tantrepi sakṛdanuṣṭhānenopakārasiddhirevābhedānuṣṭhānahetuḥ ihāpi coktena nyāyena
asti sakṛdanuṣṭhānenāpyupakārasiddhiriti yuktamabhedenānuṣṭhānam | yata eva kartrādīnāma-
bhedenānuṣṭhānasyaikyaniścayaḥ ata eva padārthadvārakāḥ kramasaṅkhyādayaḥ na padārthaṃ-
kāryaṃ vihantuṃ kṣamāḥ tadānuguṇyāt
 | asyārthaḥ---pūrvapakṣiṇā hyetaduktam tiṣṭhantaṃ
paśuṃ prayajati pṛṣadājyenānūyān yajati ekādaśa prayājān yajati iti paśau prayā-
jādīnāṃ guṇakālavikāravidhānāt vikṛtāḥ paśorupakurvanti na puroḍāśānāmiti |

tatredamucyate---yadā tantramadhyavidhānasiddhaikaprayoganibandhanakartrādyabhedabalādā-
yātaḥ kāryāviśeṣaḥ paśau puroḍāśe ca prayājādīnāṃ tadā ye padārthadvārakāḥ kramasaṅkhyā-
dayaḥ te padārthakāryaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ na vihantuṃ kṣamāḥ kāryānuguṇatvāt guṇavidhānasya |
ye hi prayogādvārakāḥ te prayogabhedamāvahanto bhavantyeva kāryābhedavighātakāḥ | yā tu
saṅkhyā yacca dravyaṃ tanna prayogadvārakam | yaḥpunaḥ tiṣṭhantaṃ paśuṃ prayajatīti kālavidhiḥ
sa kālasya vacanāntareṇa sutyālakṣaṇasya vihitatvāt kramamātrapara eveti na bhedenānu-
ṣṭhānamāpādayituṃ kṣamaḥ | ata eva kramasaṅkhyādaya iti kramagrahaṇaṃ kṛtamiti hetoḥ
nānuṣṭhānāntaramāvahanti kramasaṅkhyādayaḥ |

yatpunaridaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa padārthāntaranirākṛtau prayājādayaḥ saṅkhyā-
bhede’pi na prākṛtebhyo’rthāntarabhūtā ityatra yatitaṃ prayājādiṣu
 | yatiḥ
prakṛtyantaramasti na punaryatī prayatne ityasyedaṃ rūpam, śvīdita iti iḍabhāvaprasaṅgāt |
tadabuddhipūrvakamiva pratibhāti | padārthāntaratvepi paśāveva tatkāryaṃ bhaviṣyati

tasya padārthāntarasya kāryaṃ paśāveva bhaviṣyati paśoreva tasya vihitatvāt saṅkhyādī-
nāmiva | yathā saṅkhyādīnāṃ paśāveva kāryaṃ tasmāt karmāntaratvepi na puroḍāśe
prakṛtivatkāryavighātaḥ śakyate vaktum
 | asyārthaḥ---prākṛtānāṃ prayājādīnāṃ
paśvarthaṃmanuṣṭhitānāṃ puroḍāśe prakṛtivatkāryavighāto na śakyate vaktuṃ ye prayājādayaḥ

[p. 406]

prakṛtāḥ tadabhiprāyeṇāpi prasaṅgo bhavatyeveti kiṃ padārthāntaranirākaraṇaprayatneneti
bhāvaḥ |

pariharati---satyamāha bhavān kintvatra guṇavikārastantramityuktam |
tatsiddhayarthaṃ prayājādīnāṃ svarūpavat kāryamapi nānyadbhavitumarhati prāpti-
siddhayartho’yaṃ yatnaḥ
 |

asyārthaḥ---yadyapi padārthāntaratvepi pṛṣadājyenānūyājān yajatītyevamādīnāṃ
prasaṅgaścintayituṃ śakyate tathāpi bhāṣyakāreṇāpi tu guṇavikārastantramityuktam | guṇena
pṛṣattādinā anūyājādīnāṃ vikāro yasmin aṅgāvāntaropakāre sa guṇavikārastantra-
mityuktam tasya tantratvasiddhayartham | kena hetunā tatsiddhayarthatvaṃ ityāha---svarūpavat
kāryamapi nānyadbhavitumarhati iti
 | yathā svarūpaṃ padārthasya tadevetyekarūpatayā
kāryasya tantratāṃ sādhayituṃ prāptisiddhayartho anuyājādiprāptipratipādanārtho’yaṃ bhāṣya-
kārasya padārthāntaranirākaraṇe yatnaḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ prāptisiddhayā kāryaikyasiddhirityatrāha
---kāryaviśeṣe hi prāptiḥ yadā prakṛtivikṛtyoḥ | aṅgakāryāviśeṣo
bhavati tadā’stu tāvadaṅgānāṃ prāptirbhavati vikṛtibhūtaśca paśuḥ
 |

etāvatyeva mātṛkā ||
